+

-**Proof sketch.** Write `f = f(x, y)`. Alice's view in the hybrid execution is
-`⟨1^n, ⟨x, []⟩, r_A, [m_B]⟩` (her OT answers are empty), Bob's is `⟨1^n, ⟨y, ans⟩, [], [m_A]⟩`
-with `ans` the bits of the keys `k_{inA + i}^{y_i}` (`yaoHybrid_answersB_getD`). Let `Bad` be the
-event that the garbling is not good or some output vertex has two equal keys; by
-`yaoSeed_good_whp` it has probability at most `δ(n)`, and off `Bad` both outputs are `f`
-(`yaoHybrid_correct`).
-1. *`S_A(1^n, ⟨x, f⟩)`*, with `yaoCoins(n)` coins `r`: the view `⟨1^n, ⟨x, []⟩, r, [f]⟩`. The
-   source's `S_A` also simulates the OT transcripts with Proposition 161.5's simulator; in the
-   hybrid model there are none. Off `Bad`, `m_B = f` and Bob outputs `f`, so the real and ideal
-   samplers agree outside an event of probability at most `δ(n)`; every distinguisher has
-   advantage at most `δ(n)`, for all inputs.
-2. *`S_B(1^n, ⟨y, f⟩)`*, with `yaoCoins(n)` coins: read a seed `s = yaoSeed` and let the active
-   key of every wire be `k_w = k_w^0`. The fake garbled circuit has the real enumeration and
-   shifts, but every row of gate `i` encrypts the active key `k_{inA + inB + i}` under that row's
-   argument keys. Alice's input keys are the active keys, and so are Bob's answers. The pair of
-   output `j` is `⟨k_o^0, k_o^1⟩` if `f_j = 0` and `⟨k_o^1, k_o^0⟩` otherwise, so the active key
-   decodes to `f_j`. This is the source's "garbled circuit that always outputs `f(x, y)`", in the
-   one-active-key form of [LP09]. `S_B` outputs `⟨1^n, ⟨y, ans'⟩, [], [m'_A]⟩`.
-3. *Bob's side, the hybrid over gates* [PS10, "using a hybrid argument …"].
-   - `H₀` is the real sampler.
-   - In `H₁`, wire `w` with value `v_w` on `(x, y)` has active key `a_w = k_w^{v_w}` and inactive
-     key `ι_w = k_w^{1−v_w}`, and gate `i` the shift `λ_i ⊕ v_{args}`. This is a bijection of
-     Alice's coins, so `H₁ = H₀`. In these terms the opened row of every gate is the row of the
-     all-active assignment; its position is uniform, given by the shift. Bob holds only active
-     keys, and the active key of output `j` decodes to `f_j`.
-   - `H₂,ᵢ` makes the rows of the gates below `i` encrypt the active output key. Then
-     `H₂,₀ = H₁`, and `H₂,|gates|` is `S_B`'s output up to renaming the keys.
-   - `H₂,ᵢ ≈ H₂,ᵢ₊₁`: the rows that change are encrypted under an inactive key `ι_u` of an
-     argument `u` of gate `i`: in the outer layer if `u` is the first argument, else in the inner
-     one. In `H₂,ᵢ`, `ι_u` is only an encryption key, of rows of the gates that read `u`:
-     - it is no plaintext: if `u` is a gate, it precedes gate `i` and its table is already fake;
-     - it is in no decoding pair: no gate reads an output vertex (`YaoCircuit.outs_sink`);
-     - it is not among Alice's keys or Bob's answers, which are active.
-
-     A distinguisher between the two gives one against the multi-message security of
-     Algorithm 157.2 under `ι_u`: the reduction samples everything else and obtains the rows
-     under `ι_u` from its challenge, with plaintexts `ι_{inA + inB + i}` versus
-     `a_{inA + inB + i}` for the changed rows. For the inner layer this is Claim 157.a (i)
-     (`specialScheme_multiMessageSecure`, `n`-bit messages with `G₁`). For the outer layer it is
-     the same claim for `(4n + 2)`-bit messages with `G₂`. Since `ι_u` may key both layers, the
-     reduction needs the two schemes' ciphertexts under one key: the same proof, with the
-     joint PRF `hG₁₂` replaced by a random function (not yet stated in the library).
-   - The reductions are non-uniform: their advice at `n` is the worst inputs `(x, y)` and the
-     hybrid index. This makes the bound `2 · S(n) · ε_MM(n)` uniform over the inputs.
-   - Bob's real output pair adds `Bad`: the total is `δ(n) + 2 · S(n) · ε_MM(n)`, negligible.
-4. *Efficiency of the simulators.* `S_A` copies its input and coins. `S_B` builds `C_n` with the
-   uniformity map and encrypts with `E₁` and `E₂`; both are uniform PPT machines. -/
+**Proof sketch.** Fix the uniform copying simulator for Alice and fake garbler for Bob.
+The frozen good-seed statement supplies one negligible bad-event bound before any inputs.
+Outside that event, correctness gives both prescribed functionality outputs.
+The copying-view coupling simulates Alice; the same event permits Bob's output switch.
+The actual two-message view and ideal OT answers identify Bob's selected-key real view.
+An exact permutation of the full coin tape identifies this view with stage zero.
+The gate hybrid has two substeps per gate, replacing rows under an inactive argument key.
+Each neighbor reduces to the frozen joint multi-message encryption statement.
+Coordinate swapping preserves the seed law, and reconstruction gives the exact challenge.
+Finite worst-input and stage advice yields a negligible bound uniform over both inputs.
+The last-stage payload is the fixed Bob simulator's canonical prescribed-output view.
+Exact endpoint transport and the statistical bound give their negligible sum for both sides. -/

+  obtain ⟨δ, hδ, hWHP⟩ := yaoSeed_good_whp Y E₁ E₂ G₁ G₂ hGE₁ hGE₂ hG₁ hG₂
+  let BadWord n q := ¬ (Good (nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ n) (Y.circ n) (yaoSeed Y n q) ∧
+    ∀ j, keyAt (Y.circ n) (yaoSeed Y n q) (Y.outs n j) false ≠
+      keyAt (Y.circ n) (yaoSeed Y n q) (Y.outs n j) true)
+  let switched n x y := fun r : (yaoHybrid Y E₁ E₂).Coins n => pairEncode
+    ((yaoHybrid Y E₁ E₂).main.viewV (List.replicate n true)
+      ((yaoHybrid Y E₁ E₂).privA n (List.ofFn x) (List.ofFn y)
+        (List.ofFn r.1) (List.ofFn r.2))
+      ((yaoHybrid Y E₁ E₂).privB n (List.ofFn x) (List.ofFn y)
+        (List.ofFn r.1) (List.ofFn r.2)) r).encode
+    (List.ofFn ((yaoFunctionality Y).f n x y).1)
+  let v n (x : Fin (Y.inA.eval n) → Bool) (y : Fin (Y.inB.eval n) → Bool) w :=
+    ((Y.circ n).values (Fin.append x y)).getD w false
+  let game n x y stage := fun r :
+      Fin (10 * Y.sizeBound.eval n * n + 2 * Y.sizeBound.eval n) → Bool =>
+    GateHybridMessage.sample (nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ n) (Y.circ n) (yaoSeed Y n (List.ofFn r))
+      (Y.inA.eval n) (Y.inB.eval n) (Y.outLen.eval n) (Y.outs n) (v n x y)
+      (List.replicate n true) (List.ofFn y) [] (List.ofFn (Y.output n x y)) stage
+  have hAlice := AliceStatistical.distAdv_real_ideal_le (yaoHybrid Y E₁ E₂)
+    (yaoFunctionality Y) (yaoCoins Y) rfl (yaoMain_rounds Y E₁ E₂)
+    (yaoMain_verifierFirst Y E₁ E₂) BadWord δ hWHP (fun _ _ _ => rfl)
+    (fun n x y r hr => (yaoHybrid_correct Y E₁ E₂ n x y r
+      (not_not.mp hr).1 (not_not.mp hr).2).1)
+  have hSwitch := BobStatistical.statClose_real_output_switch
+    (yaoHybrid Y E₁ E₂) (yaoFunctionality Y) (yaoCoins Y) rfl BadWord δ hWHP
+    (fun n x y r hr => (yaoHybrid_correct Y E₁ E₂ n x y r
+      (not_not.mp hr).1 (not_not.mp hr).2).2)
+  -- The joint slice-security consumer supplies the complete computational hybrid.
+  have hBob : ∀ D : NUPPT, ∃ μ : ℕ → ℝ, Negligible μ ∧ ∀ n x y,
+      distAdv (game n x y 0) (game n x y (2 * (Y.circ n).gates.length))
+        (D.distinguisherL n) ≤ μ n := by
+    intro D
+    obtain ⟨μ, hμ, hBound⟩ := GateHybridSecurity.endpoint_negligible
+      (L := .add (.add .var .var) (.add .var innerLen))
+      (I := fun n => (Fin (Y.inA.eval n) → Bool) × (Fin (Y.inB.eval n) → Bool))
+      Y.circ Y.sizeBound Y.inA Y.inB Y.outLen Y.outLen (yaoSeed Y)
+      (fun n z => v n z.1 z.2) Y.outs (fun _ z => List.ofFn z.2)
+      (fun n z => List.ofFn (Y.output n z.1 z.2)) Y.size_le Y.fanin
+      (fun n z => GateHybrids.values_consistent (Y.circ n) (Fin.append z.1 z.2)) Y.outs_sink
+      (fun n z => by simp only [List.length_ofFn, le_refl])
+      (fun n z => by simp only [List.length_ofFn, le_refl])
+      (fun n s x => Fin.append (G₁ n s x) (G₂ n s x)) G₁ G₂ E₁ E₂ hE₂
+      (fun n s x => List.ofFn_fin_append (G₁ n s x) (G₂ n s x)) hGE₁ hGE₂
+      (fun n => by
+        simp only [PolyExpr.eval_add, innerLen_eq, PolyExpr.eval_mul,
+          PolyExpr.eval_const, PolyExpr.eval_var]
+        omega)
+      (sliceMultiMessageSecure_of_prfOut hG₁₂)
+      (fun n q w b t => by rw [hSeedK, readBits_apply])
+      (fun n q i a t => by simp only [hSeedR₀, readBits_apply, assignIdx_eq])
+      (fun n q i a t => by simp only [hSeedR₁, readBits_apply, assignIdx_eq])
+      (fun n q i a t => by simp only [yaoSeed_snd_snd, xorShift_apply, readBits_apply]) D
+    exact ⟨μ, hμ, fun n x y => hBound n (x, y)⟩
+  refine ⟨AliceSimulator.simulator (yaoCoins Y), SB, simulators_of_output_switch
+    (yaoHybrid Y E₁ E₂) (yaoFunctionality Y) _ SB switched δ hδ hAlice hSwitch ?_⟩
+  intro D; obtain ⟨μ, hμ, hBound⟩ := hBob D
+  refine ⟨μ, hμ, ?_⟩; intro n x y
+  have hvA (i : Fin (Y.inA.eval n)) : v n x y i = (List.ofFn x).getD i false := by
+    rw [List.getD_eq_getElem _ _ (by simpa only [List.length_ofFn] using i.isLt),
+      List.getElem_ofFn]
+    simpa only [Fin.coe_castAdd, Fin.append_left] using
+      (Y.circ n).values_getD_input (Fin.append x y) (Fin.castAdd (Y.inB.eval n) i)
+  have hvB (i : Fin (Y.inB.eval n)) : v n x y (Y.inA.eval n + i) =
+      (List.ofFn y).getD i false := by
+    rw [List.getD_eq_getElem _ _ (by simpa only [List.length_ofFn] using i.isLt),
+      List.getElem_ofFn]
+    simpa only [Fin.coe_natAdd, Fin.append_right] using
+      (Y.circ n).values_getD_input (Fin.append x y) (Fin.natAdd (Y.inA.eval n) i)
+  have hStart := BobStageZero.acceptProb_eq_stage0 (Y.circ n) (Y.sizeBound.eval n) n
+    (hFan n) (v n x y) (Y.size_le n) (yaoSeed Y n)
+    (fun q w b t => by rw [hSeedK, readBits_apply])
+    (fun q i a t => by simp only [hSeedR₀, readBits_apply, assignIdx_eq])
+    (fun q i a t => by simp only [hSeedR₁, readBits_apply, assignIdx_eq])
+    (fun q i a t => by simp only [yaoSeed_snd_snd, xorShift_apply, readBits_apply])
+    (nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ n) (Y.inA.eval n) (Y.inB.eval n) (Y.outLen.eval n) (Y.outs n)
+    (yaoHybrid Y E₁ E₂) (yaoMsg Y E₁ E₂) rfl rfl rfl (yaoAlice_next_nil Y E₁ E₂)
+    (List.ofFn x) (List.ofFn y) (List.ofFn ((yaoFunctionality Y).f n x y).1)
+    (by rw [Strategy.coinLen_eq, List.length_replicate, yaoHybrid_main, yaoMain_P,
+      yaoAlice_coins, yaoCoins_eval])
+    (by rw [yaoHybrid_calls, PolyExpr.eval_mul, PolyExpr.eval_var]) hvB
+    (fun q r => yaoHybrid_answersB_getD Y E₁ E₂ n _ _ q r)
+    (fun r => by simp only [yaoMsg_eq, yaoTables_eq, yaoKeysA_eq, yaoPairs_eq,
+      encodeMsgs_eq_encWords, ← hvA]) (D.distinguisherL n)
+  let e := (Equiv.arrowCongr (finCongr (yaoCoins_eval Y n).symm) (Equiv.refl Bool)).trans
+    (NUPPT.coinsEquivPoly hSBcoins n).symm
+  have hEnd := SamplerEndpoints.acceptProb_end_eq_ideal (nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ n) (Y.circ n)
+    (fun r => yaoSeed Y n (List.ofFn r)) (Y.inA.eval n) (Y.inB.eval n) (Y.outLen.eval n)
+    (Y.outs n) (v n x y) (fun j => (List.ofFn ((yaoFunctionality Y).f n x y).2).getD j false)
+    (fun j => by
+      change v n x y (Y.outs n j) =
+        (List.ofFn ((yaoFunctionality Y).f n x y).2).getD j false
+      rw [List.getD_eq_getElem _ _ (by simpa only [List.length_ofFn] using j.isLt),
+        List.getElem_ofFn]; rfl) (List.replicate n true) (List.ofFn y) []
+    (List.ofFn ((yaoFunctionality Y).f n x y).1) e ((yaoFunctionality Y).idealB SB n x y)
+    (fun r => by
+      simpa only [e, Equiv.trans_apply, NUPPT.coinsEquivPoly_symm_ofFn,
+        ofFn_arrowCongr_finCongr] using
+        (hIdeal (yaoFunctionality Y) n x y (e r))) (D.distinguisherL n)
+  rw [distAdv_def, hStart, ← hEnd]
+  have hb := hBound n x y
+  rw [distAdv_def] at hb
+  exact hb
 
 include hGE₁ hGE₂ in

+/-!
+# Helpers for Yao’s protocol in the OT-hybrid model
+
+These generic contracts provide total garbling/evaluation programs, message bounds,
+simulator machines, exact seed relabelings and the probability framework for gate hybrids.
+They do not import the concrete Yao protocol.
+
+## Contents
+
+* `AliceProgram`: polynomial-time Alice messages from generic circuit and seed readers.
+* `AliceSimulator`: a copying simulator for an Alice-first two-message view.
+* `AliceStatistical`: statistical simulation of the sender's two-message view.
+* `AllTables`: polynomial-time assembly of all garbled tables.
+* `BobIO`: reading the output of an encoded garbled evaluation.
+* `BobInit`: polynomial-time initial keys for a garbled evaluation.
+* `BobProgram`: the complete generic polynomial-time garbled-circuit evaluator.
+* `BobSimulator`: a polynomial-time Bob simulator from generic fake-garbling interfaces.
+* `BobStageZero`: the actual receiver view at the first garbling hybrid.
+* `BobStatistical`: statistical switching of the receiver's paired output.
+* `BobTables`: a total row-table selector for the garbled-evaluation host.
+* `BobViewShape`: the exact Bob-view shape of an OT-hybrid two-message protocol.
+* `CallEquations`: the bit-OT input machines and a product call index.
+* `ChunkScanner`: finite scanners with word-valued emissions.
+* `CoinGarbling`: polynomial-time garbling coin addresses.
+* `CoinRead`: polynomial-time windows of a coin string.
+* `CoinRelabel`: exact coin-block bijections for garbling.
+* `CoinSeedRelabel`: the tape-to-seed coin relabeling bridge.
+* `CoordinateSwap`: re-randomizing a partial family of seed coordinates.
+* `Decryption`: tagged special decryption at arbitrary message lengths.
+* `DescriptionDecoder`: a polynomial-time host for circuit descriptions.
+* `EvalGate`: polynomial-time garbled-gate evaluation.
+* `FakeMessage`: the canonical encoded simulated garbling message.
+* `FakeObjects`: active-key simulated garblings.
+* `FakeProgram`: polynomial-time simulated garbling programs.
+* `FakeSimulator`: a fixed uniform Bob simulator from circuit and seed readers.
+* `FakeSimulatorFactory`: fixed simulator contracts from supplied circuit and seed families.
+* `FirstTagged`: first successful tagged row in polynomial time.
+* `GarbledRun`: the generic polynomial-time garbled-evaluation host.
+* `Garbling`: polynomial-time leaves for circuit garbling.
+* `GarblingProgram`: generic garbling programs from uniform circuit descriptions.
+* `GateHybridChain`: summing present-argument bounds along the gate hybrid.
+* `GateHybridMessage`: encoded endpoints of the garbled-gate hybrid.
+* `GateHybridSecurity`: joint slice security for the complete actual gate hybrid.
+* `GateHybrids`: two-stage garbled-gate hybrids.
+* `GateOccurrences`: all occurrences of a selected inactive wire label.
+* `GateStepCases`: present and absent argument cases for the two-step gate clock.
+* `Lengths`: length bounds for garbled messages.
+* `NonceCoordinates`: partial inverse coordinates for selected row nonces.
+* `OTAnswerWords`: bit-transfer answers as active wire labels.
+* `OTInputs`: polynomial-time inputs for bit oblivious transfers.
+* `ProtocolEfficiency`: message bounds for an Alice-first two-move protocol.
+* `RelabelGarbling`: seed-by-seed relabeling of a generic garbling.
+* `RowFromCoins`: a polynomial-time row garbler from coin words.
+* `RunWithHost`: a polynomial-time host for data-dependent gate evaluation.
+* `SamplerEndpoints`: exact sampler transport at the gate-hybrid endpoints.
+* `SeedCoordinates`: coordinates of a garbling seed and its selected challenge.
+* `SelectedAdviceBounds`: polynomial advice bounds for selected-key reconstruction.
+* `SelectedFields`: fields unaffected by selected inactive-key replacement.
+* `SelectedLaw`: fresh selected coordinates in the actual tape distribution.
+* `SelectedPlan`: fixed selected-key slice messages and table descriptors.
+* `SelectedRows`: precomputed messages and reconstruction records for selected rows.
+* `SelectedSeed`: filling one selected key and all its encryption nonces.
+* `SelectedSlices`: the two slice shapes in a selected-wire reduction.
+* `SelectedStep`: a common reconstruction for both sides of a selected gate substep.
+* `SelectedStepChoices`: finite advice choices for all selected hybrid substeps.
+* `SelectedStepSecurity`: one uniform negligible bound for all selected neighboring hybrids.
+* `SelectedTables`: exact selected-key table and view reconstruction.
+* `SimulationBound`: combining the two simulation bounds.
+* `SliceReconstruction`: reconstruction from a tuple of slice ciphertexts.
+* `SliceReduction`: advice and averaging for a slice-encryption reduction.
+* `StatisticalSwitch`: statistical switches outside a bad event.
+* `Strategies`: efficient first-message and reply strategies.
+* `TablesFromCoins`: polynomial-time garbled tables from coin words.
+* `TapeValues`: actual key and nonce tables separated from tape parameters.
+* `TwoMoveViews`: views of an Alice-first two-message protocol.
+* `WordLists`: polynomial-time encoded word vectors.
+
+## References
+
+* [PS10] R. Pass, a. shelat, *A Course in Cryptography*, lecture notes, 3rd ed.,
+  January 2010, Protocol 162.6 and Theorem 163.7.
+-/

+import TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.Yao.HybridProof.GarblingProgram

+/-!
+# Polynomial-time Alice messages from generic circuit and seed readers
+
+The interface assembles the actual encoded garbled tables, the selected input keys, and
+the output decoding pairs. Circuit families, raw selectors, description maps, and seed block
+equations are parameters. No definition from `Yao.lean` is visible.
+
+## Main definitions
+
+None.
+
+## Main results
+
+* `AliceProgram.selectedKeys_poly`: encoded selected input keys are polynomial-time.
+* `AliceProgram.outputPairs_poly`: encoded decoding pairs are polynomial-time.
+* `AliceProgram.message_poly`: the complete first garbling message is polynomial-time.
+
+## References
+
+* [PS10] R. Pass, a. shelat, *A Course in Cryptography*, lecture notes, 3rd ed., January
+  2010, Protocol 162.6 (the first message of Alice).
+-/
+
+namespace TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof.AliceProgram
+
+open Complexity Turing BoolCircuit TCSlib.Cryptography.EncodingMachines
+
+/-- The encoded list of `keyAt C s v (xs.getD v false)` for `v < count` is polynomial-time
+when the unary width/bound/count maps and raw input/coin words are polynomial-time,
+`count ≤ inputs`, and the seed satisfies its addressed-key equation.
+[PS10, Protocol 162.6], Alice's selected input keys.
+
+**Proof sketch.** On an indexed retained input, the index length is the input vertex and
+a polynomial-time bit read selects its input bit. The generic addressed-key contract
+computes the corresponding key word. Encode this indexed word vector with the public
+range assembler; input-vertex bounds allow the supplied key reader at every genuine index. -/
+theorem selectedKeys_poly (width bound count inputs : List Bool → ℕ)
+    (xs coins : List Bool → List Bool)
+    (hwidth : PolyTimeComputable fun z => List.replicate (width z) true)
+    (hbound : PolyTimeComputable fun z => List.replicate (bound z) true)
+    (hcount : PolyTimeComputable fun z => List.replicate (count z) true)
+    (hxs : PolyTimeComputable xs) (hcoins : PolyTimeComputable coins)
+    (C : ∀ z, DAGCircuit (inputs z))
+    (s : ∀ z, GarbleSeed (C z) (Fin (width z) → Bool)
+      ((Fin (width z) → Bool) × (Fin (width z) → Bool)))
+    (hcount_le : ∀ z, count z ≤ inputs z)
+    (hkey : ∀ z (v : ℕ), v < (C z).size → ∀ b,
+      keyAt (C z) (s z) v b = fun t : Fin (width z) =>
+        (coins z).getD ((b.toNat * bound z + v) * width z + t) false) :
+    PolyTimeComputable fun z => BinArith.encWords (List.ofFn fun v : Fin (count z) =>
+      List.ofFn (keyAt (C z) (s z) v ((xs z).getD v false))) := by
+  let entry := fun z v => List.ofFn fun t : Fin (width z) => (coins z).getD
+    ((((xs z).getD v false).toNat * bound z + v) * width z + t) false
+  have hw : PolyTimeComputable fun w => List.replicate (width (pairSndD w)) true :=
+    hwidth.comp polyTimeComputable_pairSndD
+  have hb : PolyTimeComputable fun w => List.replicate (bound (pairSndD w)) true :=
+    hbound.comp polyTimeComputable_pairSndD
+  have hbit : PolyTimeComputable fun w =>
+      [(xs (pairSndD w)).getD (pairFstD w).length false] :=
+    getBit_poly (hxs.comp polyTimeComputable_pairSndD)
+      (unaryLength_poly polyTimeComputable_pairFstD)
+  have he : PolyTimeComputable fun w => entry (pairSndD w) (pairFstD w).length :=
+    CoinGarbling.keyWord_poly (hcoins.comp polyTimeComputable_pairSndD) hw hb
+      (unaryLength_poly polyTimeComputable_pairFstD) hbit
+      (key := fun w (t : Fin (width (pairSndD w))) => (coins (pairSndD w)).getD
+        ((((xs (pairSndD w)).getD (pairFstD w).length false).toNat * bound (pairSndD w) +
+          (pairFstD w).length) * width (pairSndD w) + t) false) (fun _ _ => rfl)
+  refine WordLists.ofFnTable_poly _ entry hcount he ?_
+  intro z v
+  have hv : (v : ℕ) < (C z).size := by
+    rw [DAGCircuit.size_eq]
+    have := hcount_le z
+    omega
+  exact congrArg List.ofFn (hkey z v hv ((xs z).getD v false))
+
+/-- The encoded output-key pairs `⟨keyAt C s (output j) false, keyAt C s (output j) true⟩`
+are polynomial-time from a polynomial-time description/coin map, unary width/bound/count
+maps, a canonical output-index word list, valid output vertices, and the seed key equation.
+[PS10, Protocol 162.6], the output decoding information.
+`PolyTimeComputable fun z => BinArith.encWords (List.ofFn fun j : Fin (count z) => pairEncode
+(List.ofFn (keyAt (C z) (s z) (output z j) false)) (List.ofFn (keyAt (C z) (s z) (output z j)
+true)))`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** On a unary index, read the corresponding output-index word from the
+description's second component. Dropping one symbol from its unary terminated code leaves
+a word whose length is the vertex number. The key-window contract computes both Boolean
+keys at this vertex. Encode these pairs over the output count; canonical lookup and the
+valid-vertex hypotheses identify every pair with the supplied seed's output keys. -/
+theorem outputPairs_poly (width bound count inputs : List Bool → ℕ)
+    (description coins : List Bool → List Bool)
+    (hwidth : PolyTimeComputable fun z => List.replicate (width z) true)
+    (hbound : PolyTimeComputable fun z => List.replicate (bound z) true)
+    (hcount : PolyTimeComputable fun z => List.replicate (count z) true)
+    (hdescription : PolyTimeComputable description) (hcoins : PolyTimeComputable coins)
+    (C : ∀ z, DAGCircuit (inputs z))
+    (s : ∀ z, GarbleSeed (C z) (Fin (width z) → Bool)
+      ((Fin (width z) → Bool) × (Fin (width z) → Bool)))
+    (output : ∀ z, Fin (count z) → ℕ)
+    (houtsWord : ∀ z, pairSndD (description z) =
+      BinArith.encWords (List.ofFn fun j => encodeNat (output z j)))
+    (houtput_lt : ∀ z j, output z j < (C z).size)
+    (hkey : ∀ z (v : ℕ), v < (C z).size → ∀ b,
+      keyAt (C z) (s z) v b = fun t : Fin (width z) =>
+        (coins z).getD ((b.toNat * bound z + v) * width z + t) false) :
+    PolyTimeComputable fun z => BinArith.encWords (List.ofFn fun j : Fin (count z) =>
+      pairEncode (List.ofFn (keyAt (C z) (s z) (output z j) false))
+        (List.ofFn (keyAt (C z) (s z) (output z j) true))) := by
+  let select := fun z j =>
+    ((BinArith.getW (pairEncode (List.replicate j true) (pairSndD (description z)))).drop 1).length
+  let entry := fun z j => pairEncode
+    (RowFromCoins.keyWord width bound (fun v => select v j) (fun _ => false) coins z)
+    (RowFromCoins.keyWord width bound (fun v => select v j) (fun _ => true) coins z)
+  have hw : PolyTimeComputable fun w => List.replicate (width (pairSndD w)) true :=
+    hwidth.comp polyTimeComputable_pairSndD
+  have hb : PolyTimeComputable fun w => List.replicate (bound (pairSndD w)) true :=
+    hbound.comp polyTimeComputable_pairSndD
+  have hlookup := BinArith.polyTimeComputable_getW.comp
+    ((polyTimeComputable_unary.comp polyTimeComputable_pairFstD).pairEncode
+      ((polyTimeComputable_pairSndD.comp hdescription).comp polyTimeComputable_pairSndD))
+  have hv : PolyTimeComputable fun w =>
+      List.replicate (select (pairSndD w) (pairFstD w).length) true :=
+    unaryLength_poly (polyTimeComputable_tail.comp hlookup)
+  have h₀ := RowFromCoins.keyWord_poly (hcoins.comp polyTimeComputable_pairSndD) hw hb hv
+    (polyTimeComputable_const [false])
+  have h₁ := RowFromCoins.keyWord_poly (hcoins.comp polyTimeComputable_pairSndD) hw hb hv
+    (polyTimeComputable_const [true])
+  have he : PolyTimeComputable fun w => entry (pairSndD w) (pairFstD w).length := by
+    refine (h₀.pairEncode h₁).congr fun w => ?_
+    dsimp only [entry]
+    rw [RowFromCoins.keyWord_eq, RowFromCoins.keyWord_eq,
+      RowFromCoins.keyWord_eq, RowFromCoins.keyWord_eq]
+    simp only [Function.comp_apply]
+  refine WordLists.ofFnTable_poly _ entry hcount he ?_
+  intro z j
+  -- The canonical unary code determines the output vertex at this index.
+  have hcode : BinArith.getW (pairEncode (List.replicate j true) (pairSndD (description z))) =
+      encodeNat (output z j) := by
+    rw [houtsWord z, BinArith.getW_pairEncode, List.length_replicate]
+    rw [List.getD_eq_getElem _ _ (by rw [List.length_ofFn]; exact j.isLt), List.getElem_ofFn]
+  have hvertex : select z j = output z j := by
+    dsimp only [select]
+    rw [hcode, encodeNat_def, List.length_drop, List.length_append,
+      List.length_replicate, List.length_singleton]
+    omega
+  dsimp only [entry]
+  apply congrArg₂ pairEncode
+  · rw [RowFromCoins.keyWord_eq, hvertex]
+    exact congrArg List.ofFn (hkey z (output z j) (houtput_lt z j) false)
+  · rw [RowFromCoins.keyWord_eq, hvertex]
+    exact congrArg List.ofFn (hkey z (output z j) (houtput_lt z j) true)
+
+/-- Alice's complete garbling message is polynomial-time: the pair of the actual encoded
+nested-special garbled tables and the pair of her selected input-key vector and the output
+decoding-pair vector. The hypotheses are polynomial-time raw selectors, canonical uniform
+circuit descriptions, valid output vertices, fan-in at most two, and the supplied seed block
+and XOR-shift readers. [PS10, Protocol 162.6]
+`PolyTimeComputable fun z => pairEncode (BinArith.encWords (List.ofFn fun i => BinArith.encWords
+(garbledTable (nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ (width z)) (C z) (s z) i))) (pairEncode (BinArith.encWords
+(List.ofFn fun v : Fin (inA z) => List.ofFn (keyAt (C z) (s z) v ((xs z).getD v false))))
+(BinArith.encWords (List.ofFn fun j : Fin (outLen z) => pairEncode (List.ofFn (keyAt (C z) (s z)
+(output z j) false)) (List.ofFn (keyAt (C z) (s z) (output z j) true)))))`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** The generic garbling program proves polynomial time for the actual table
+vector from description and seed readers. `selectedKeys_poly` gives Alice's input-key vector,
+and `outputPairs_poly` gives the decoding-pair vector from the same description. Two public
+pairing closure applications assemble the message. -/
+theorem message_poly {E₁ E₂ : List Bool → List Bool} (hE₁ : PolyTimeComputable E₁)
+    (hE₂ : PolyTimeComputable E₂) (width bound inputs inA outLen : List Bool → ℕ)
+    (description xs coins : List Bool → List Bool)
+    (hwidth : PolyTimeComputable fun z => List.replicate (width z) true)
+    (hbound : PolyTimeComputable fun z => List.replicate (bound z) true)
+    (hinputs : PolyTimeComputable fun z => List.replicate (inputs z) true)
+    (hinA : PolyTimeComputable fun z => List.replicate (inA z) true)
+    (houtLen : PolyTimeComputable fun z => List.replicate (outLen z) true)
+    (hdescription : PolyTimeComputable description) (hxs : PolyTimeComputable xs)
+    (hcoins : PolyTimeComputable coins) (C : ∀ z, DAGCircuit (inputs z))
+    (s : ∀ z, GarbleSeed (C z) (Fin (width z) → Bool)
+      ((Fin (width z) → Bool) × (Fin (width z) → Bool)))
+    (output : ∀ z, Fin (outLen z) → ℕ)
+    (hshape : ∀ z, description z = pairEncode (C z).encode
+      (BinArith.encWords (List.ofFn fun j => encodeNat (output z j))))
+    (hinA_le : ∀ z, inA z ≤ inputs z)
+    (houtput_lt : ∀ z j, output z j < (C z).size)
+    (hfan : ∀ z (i : Fin (C z).gates.length), ((C z).gates[i]).args.length ≤ 2)
+    (hkey : ∀ z (v : ℕ), v < (C z).size → ∀ b,
+      keyAt (C z) (s z) v b = fun t : Fin (width z) =>
+        (coins z).getD ((b.toNat * bound z + v) * width z + t) false)
+    (hr₀ : ∀ z (i : Fin (C z).gates.length) a t, ((s z).2.1 i a).1 t = (coins z).getD
+      (2 * bound z * width z +
+        (4 * i + (List.ofFn a).foldr (fun b k => b.toNat + 2 * k) 0) *
+          (2 * width z) + t) false)
+    (hr₁ : ∀ z (i : Fin (C z).gates.length) a t, ((s z).2.1 i a).2 t = (coins z).getD
+      (2 * bound z * width z +
+        (4 * i + (List.ofFn a).foldr (fun b k => b.toNat + 2 * k) 0) *
+          (2 * width z) + width z + t) false)
+    (hperm : ∀ z (i : Fin (C z).gates.length) a t, (s z).2.2 i a t = xor (a t)
+      ((coins z).getD (10 * bound z * width z + 2 * i + t) false)) :
+    PolyTimeComputable fun z => pairEncode
+      (BinArith.encWords (List.ofFn fun i =>
+        BinArith.encWords (garbledTable (nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ (width z)) (C z) (s z) i)))
+      (pairEncode
+        (BinArith.encWords (List.ofFn fun v : Fin (inA z) =>
+          List.ofFn (keyAt (C z) (s z) v ((xs z).getD v false))))
+        (BinArith.encWords (List.ofFn fun j : Fin (outLen z) =>
+          pairEncode (List.ofFn (keyAt (C z) (s z) (output z j) false))
+            (List.ofFn (keyAt (C z) (s z) (output z j) true))))) := by
+  have ht := GarblingProgram.tables_poly hE₁ hE₂ width bound inputs description
+    (fun z => BinArith.encWords (List.ofFn fun j => encodeNat (output z j))) coins
+    hwidth hbound hinputs hdescription hcoins C s hshape hfan hkey hr₀ hr₁ hperm
+  have hk := selectedKeys_poly width bound inA inputs xs coins hwidth hbound hinA hxs hcoins
+    C s hinA_le hkey
+  have houtsWord (z : List Bool) : pairSndD (description z) =
+      BinArith.encWords (List.ofFn fun j => encodeNat (output z j)) := by
+    rw [hshape z, pairSndD_pairEncode]
+  have hp := outputPairs_poly width bound outLen inputs description coins
+    hwidth hbound houtLen hdescription hcoins C s output houtsWord houtput_lt hkey
+  exact ht.pairEncode (hk.pairEncode hp)
+
+end TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof.AliceProgram

+import TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.Defs
+import TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.Yao.HybridProof.StatisticalSwitch
+import TCSlib.Complexity.Randomized.Closure

+set_option maxHeartbeats 0
+set_option relaxedAutoImplicit false
+set_option autoImplicit false

+/-!
+# A copying simulator for an Alice-first two-message view
+
+The simulator constructs the view `⟨1^n, ⟨input, []⟩, coins, [output]⟩` using only
+its own input, prescribed output and random tape. Its polynomial-time raw machine
+works on every string. A generic coupling contract compares any real sampler with
+this ideal sampler when the two agree outside a bad event in Alice's coins.
+
+## Main definitions
+
+* `raw`: the all-input word function constructing the encoded view.
+* `simulator`: the uniform machine for `raw`, with a supplied coin polynomial.
+
+## Main results
+
+* `polyTimeComputable_raw`: constructing the view is polynomial-time on every word.
+* `simulator_uniform`, `simulator_coins`, `simulator_runAtL`: the machine contracts.
+* `statClose_real_ideal`: an off-bad real-view identity gives statistical simulation.
+* `distAdv_real_ideal_le`: every randomized distinguisher inherits that bound.
+
+## References
+
+* [PS10] R. Pass, a. shelat, *A Course in Cryptography*, lecture notes, 3rd ed.,
+  January 2010, Theorem 163.7 (Alice's simulator in the OT-hybrid model).
+-/
+
+namespace TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof.AliceSimulator
+
+open Complexity Turing TCSlib.Cryptography
+
+/-- The raw copying simulator returns the encoding of
+`⟨inParam z, ⟨fst (inArg z), []⟩, inCoins z, [snd (inArg z)]⟩`.
+It reads no circuit and makes no ideal calls. [PS10, Theorem 163.7]
+`raw z = pairEncode (pairEncode (NUPPT.inParam z) (pairEncode (pairFstD (NUPPT.inArg z)) []))
+(pairEncode (NUPPT.inCoins z) (Strategy.encodeMsgs [pairSndD (NUPPT.inArg z)]))`. -/
+def raw (z : List Bool) : List Bool :=
+  pairEncode (pairEncode (NUPPT.inParam z) (pairEncode (pairFstD (NUPPT.inArg z)) []))
+    (pairEncode (NUPPT.inCoins z) (Strategy.encodeMsgs [pairSndD (NUPPT.inArg z)]))
+
+/-- `raw z = ⟨⟨inParam z, ⟨fst (inArg z), []⟩⟩,
+⟨inCoins z, encodeMsgs [snd (inArg z)]⟩⟩`. -/
+theorem raw_eq (z : List Bool) :
+    raw z =
+      pairEncode (pairEncode (NUPPT.inParam z) (pairEncode (pairFstD (NUPPT.inArg z)) []))
+        (pairEncode (NUPPT.inCoins z) (Strategy.encodeMsgs [pairSndD (NUPPT.inArg z)])) := rfl
+
+/-- The raw view-copying function is polynomial-time on every string:
+`PolyTimeComputable raw`. [PS10, Theorem 163.7] -/
+theorem polyTimeComputable_raw : PolyTimeComputable raw := by
+  have hnil := polyTimeComputable_const ([] : List Bool)
+  have hx := polyTimeComputable_pairFstD.comp NUPPT.polyTimeComputable_inArg
+  have hf := polyTimeComputable_pairSndD.comp NUPPT.polyTimeComputable_inArg
+  have hleft := NUPPT.polyTimeComputable_inParam.pairEncode (hx.pairEncode hnil)
+  have hright := NUPPT.polyTimeComputable_inCoins.pairEncode (hf.pairEncode hnil)
+  refine (hleft.pairEncode hright).congr fun z => ?_
+  simp only [Function.comp_apply, raw_eq, Strategy.encodeMsgs_cons, Strategy.encodeMsgs_nil]
+
+/-- The uniform copying simulator uses the supplied polynomial number of coins:
+`simulator coins = NUPPT.ofPolyTimeUniform polyTimeComputable_raw coins`.
+[PS10, Theorem 163.7] -/
+noncomputable def simulator (coins : PolyExpr) : NUPPT :=
+  NUPPT.ofPolyTimeUniform polyTimeComputable_raw coins
+
+/-- `simulator coins = NUPPT.ofPolyTimeUniform polyTimeComputable_raw coins`. -/
+theorem simulator_eq (coins : PolyExpr) :
+    simulator coins = NUPPT.ofPolyTimeUniform polyTimeComputable_raw coins := rfl
+
+/-- `simulator coins` is uniform for every coin polynomial `coins`.
+`(simulator coins).Uniform`. -/
+theorem simulator_uniform (coins : PolyExpr) : (simulator coins).Uniform := by
+  rw [simulator_eq]
+  exact NUPPT.ofPolyTimeUniform_uniform polyTimeComputable_raw coins
+
+/-- The simulator keeps the supplied coin polynomial: `(simulator coins).coins = coins`. -/
+theorem simulator_coins (coins : PolyExpr) : (simulator coins).coins = coins := by
+  rw [simulator_eq]
+  exact NUPPT.coins_ofPolyTimeUniform polyTimeComputable_raw coins
+
+/-- On every parameter `n`, input `x`, prescribed output `f` and tape `r`,
+`simulator coins` returns `View.encode ⟨1^n, ⟨x, []⟩, r, [f]⟩`.
+The equation holds for arbitrary word lengths. [PS10, Theorem 163.7]
+`(simulator coins).runAtL n (pairEncode x f) r = (⟨List.replicate n true, pairEncode x [], r, [f]⟩ :
+View).encode`. -/
+theorem simulator_runAtL (coins : PolyExpr) (n : ℕ) (x f r : List Bool) :
+    (simulator coins).runAtL n (pairEncode x f) r =
+      (⟨List.replicate n true, pairEncode x [], r, [f]⟩ : View).encode := by
+  rw [simulator_eq, NUPPT.runAtL_ofPolyTimeUniform, raw_eq, View.encode_mk]
+  simp only [NUPPT.inParam_eq, NUPPT.inArg_eq, NUPPT.inCoins_eq, NUPPT.advInput_eq,
+    pairFstD_pairEncode, pairSndD_pairEncode]
+
+/-- Given a coin equivalence `e` to the simulator's tape, a real sampler on
+`σ × τ` that equals the encoded simulated Alice view paired with `fB` outside
+`Bad` is `δ`-close to `F.idealA (simulator coins) n x y` when `Pr[Bad] ≤ δ`.
+The simulator is fixed by `coins`, independently of the inputs and distinguisher.
+[PS10, Theorem 163.7]
+
+**Proof sketch.** Lift the bad event to the joint coin space; its probability is
+unchanged by the independent second tape. The simulator run contract identifies
+the ideal sample with the given real-view expression outside this event. Apply
+the same-coins statistical switch, then remove the unused second tape and
+transport the first tape by the supplied coin equivalence. -/
+theorem statClose_real_ideal (coins : PolyExpr) (F : Functionality) (n : ℕ)
+    (x : Fin (F.inA.eval n) → Bool) (y : Fin (F.inB.eval n) → Bool)
+    {σ τ : Type*} [Fintype σ] [Fintype τ] [Nonempty τ]
+    (e : σ ≃ (simulator coins).Coins n) (real : σ × τ → List Bool)
+    (Bad : σ → Prop) (δ : ℝ) (hBad : uprob Bad ≤ δ)
+    (hReal : ∀ r, ¬ Bad r.1 → real r =
+      pairEncode (⟨List.replicate n true, pairEncode (List.ofFn x) [],
+        List.ofFn (e r.1), [List.ofFn (F.f n x y).1]⟩ : View).encode
+        (List.ofFn (F.f n x y).2)) :
+    StatClose real (F.idealA (simulator coins) n x y) δ := by
+  classical
+  have hBad' : uprob (fun r : σ × τ => Bad r.1) ≤ δ := by
+    rw [uprob_fst]
+    exact hBad
+  have h : StatClose real
+      (fun r : σ × τ => F.idealA (simulator coins) n x y (e r.1)) δ :=
+    StatisticalSwitch.statClose_of_eq_off real _ (fun r => Bad r.1) δ hBad' fun r hr => by
+      rw [hReal r hr, Functionality.idealA_eq, NUPPT.runAt_eq, simulator_runAtL]
+  rw [statClose_iff] at h ⊢
+  intro T
+  have hT := h T
+  rw [uprob_fst (β := τ) (fun t : σ => T (F.idealA (simulator coins) n x y (e t))),
+    uprob_comp_equiv e (fun t => T (F.idealA (simulator coins) n x y t))] at hT
+  exact hT
+
+/-- Under the off-bad real-view identity and `Pr[Bad] ≤ δ`, every randomized
+`D` has `distAdv real (F.idealA (simulator coins) n x y) D ≤ δ`.
+The same machine `simulator coins` works for all inputs and all distinguishers.
+[PS10, Theorem 163.7] -/
+theorem distAdv_real_ideal_le (coins : PolyExpr) (F : Functionality) (n : ℕ)
+    (x : Fin (F.inA.eval n) → Bool) (y : Fin (F.inB.eval n) → Bool)
+    {σ τ ρ : Type*} [Fintype σ] [Fintype τ] [Nonempty τ] [Fintype ρ]
+    (e : σ ≃ (simulator coins).Coins n) (real : σ × τ → List Bool)
+    (Bad : σ → Prop) (δ : ℝ) (hBad : uprob Bad ≤ δ)
+    (hReal : ∀ r, ¬ Bad r.1 → real r =
+      pairEncode (⟨List.replicate n true, pairEncode (List.ofFn x) [],
+        List.ofFn (e r.1), [List.ofFn (F.f n x y).1]⟩ : View).encode
+        (List.ofFn (F.f n x y).2)) (D : List Bool → ρ → Bool) :
+    distAdv real (F.idealA (simulator coins) n x y) D ≤ δ :=
+  distAdv_le_of_statClose real _ (statClose_real_ideal coins F n x y e real Bad δ hBad hReal) D
+
+end TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof.AliceSimulator

+import TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.Yao.HybridProof.AliceSimulator
+import TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.Yao.HybridProof.OTAnswerWords
+import TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.Yao.HybridProof.SimulationBound
+import TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.Yao.HybridProof.TwoMoveViews

+set_option maxHeartbeats 0
+set_option relaxedAutoImplicit false
+set_option autoImplicit false

+/-!
+# Statistical simulation of the sender's two-message view
+
+In an Alice-first, two-message bit-transfer hybrid, the sender receives no ideal answers.
+If Bob's output is the shared functionality output outside a bounded bad event in Alice's
+tape, the copying simulator has that same statistical simulation bound.
+
+## Main definitions
+
+There are no new definitions.
+
+## Main results
+
+* `distAdv_real_ideal_le`: a generic protocol correctness bound yields Alice simulation.
+
+## References
+
+* [PS10] R. Pass, a. shelat, *A Course in Cryptography*, lecture notes, 3rd ed.,
+  January 2010, Theorem 163.7 (Alice's simulator in the OT-hybrid model).
+-/
+
+namespace TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof.AliceStatistical
+
+open Complexity Turing
+
+/-- Suppose a bit-transfer hybrid main phase has two moves with Alice first and Alice's
+coin polynomial is `p`. If both functionality outputs are the same word, and Bob outputs
+that word outside an Alice-tape event of probability at most `δ(n)`, then the fixed copying
+simulator with polynomial `p` has advantage at most `δ(n)` for all inputs and tests.
+[PS10, Theorem 163.7]
+`∀ (D : NUPPT) n x y, distAdv (H.realA F n x y) (F.idealA (AliceSimulator.simulator p) n x y)
+(D.distinguisherL n) ≤ δ n`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** Normalize Alice's tape with a bit-word-preserving equivalence and lift
+its bad-event bound to the actual tape type. The two-move view and output readers identify
+Alice's sole received message with Bob's output. Bit transfer gives Alice no ideal answers.
+Outside the bad event, correctness and equality of the functionality output words identify
+the real view with the copying simulator view. Apply the proved copying-simulator coupling.
+No encryption or circuit definition is used. -/
+theorem distAdv_real_ideal_le (H : HybridProtocol otFunctionality) (F : Functionality)
+    (p : PolyExpr) (hCoins : H.main.P.coins = p)
+    (hRounds : H.main.rounds = .const 2) (hFirst : H.main.verifierFirst = false)
+    (BadWord : ℕ → List Bool → Prop) (δ : ℕ → ℝ)
+    (hBad : ∀ n, uprob (fun r : Fin (p.eval n) → Bool => BadWord n (List.ofFn r)) ≤ δ n)
+    (hOutputs : ∀ n x y, List.ofFn (F.f n x y).1 = List.ofFn (F.f n x y).2)
+    (hCorrect : ∀ n x y (r : H.Coins n), ¬ BadWord n (List.ofFn r.1) →
+      H.main.outV H.main.P (List.replicate n true)
+        (H.privA n (List.ofFn x) (List.ofFn y) (List.ofFn r.1) (List.ofFn r.2))
+        (H.privB n (List.ofFn x) (List.ofFn y) (List.ofFn r.1) (List.ofFn r.2)) r =
+          List.ofFn (F.f n x y).2) :
+    ∀ (D : NUPPT) n x y,
+      distAdv (H.realA F n x y) (F.idealA (AliceSimulator.simulator p) n x y)
+        (D.distinguisherL n) ≤ δ n := by
+  intro D n x y
+  let σ := H.main.P.Coins (List.replicate n true).length
+  have hLen : H.main.P.coinLen (List.replicate n true).length = p.eval n := by
+    rw [Strategy.coinLen_eq, hCoins, List.length_replicate]
+  let e₀ := strategyTapeEquiv H.main.P (List.replicate n true).length (p.eval n) hLen
+  let e := e₀.trans (NUPPT.coinsEquivPoly (AliceSimulator.simulator_coins p) n).symm
+  have he₀ (r : σ) : List.ofFn (e₀ r) = List.ofFn r :=
+    strategyTapeEquiv_ofFn _ _ _ hLen r
+  have he (r : σ) : List.ofFn (e r) = List.ofFn r := by
+    rw [Equiv.trans_apply, NUPPT.coinsEquivPoly_symm_ofFn, he₀]
+  have hBad' : uprob (fun r : σ => BadWord n (List.ofFn r)) ≤ δ n := by
+    have hb := hBad n
+    rw [← uprob_comp_equiv e₀] at hb
+    simpa only [he₀] using hb
+  apply AliceSimulator.distAdv_real_ideal_le p F n x y e (H.realA F n x y)
+    (fun r : σ => BadWord n (List.ofFn r)) (δ n) hBad'
+  intro r hr
+  have hv := hCorrect n x y r hr
+  let first := fun w z r => H.main.P.next w z r []
+  let reply := fun w z r a => H.main.V.next w z r [a]
+  rw [TwoMoveViews.outV_eq H.main first reply hRounds hFirst
+    (fun _ _ _ => rfl) (fun _ _ _ _ => rfl)] at hv
+  rw [TwoMoveViews.realA_eq first reply H hRounds hFirst
+    (fun _ _ _ => rfl) (fun _ _ _ _ => rfl)]
+  simp only [HybridProtocol.privA_eq, OTAnswerWords.answersA_nil] at hv ⊢
+  rw [hv]
+  simp only [View.encode_mk, he, hOutputs n x y]
+
+end TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof.AliceStatistical

+/-
+Copyright (c) 2026 TCSlib contributors. All rights reserved.
+Released under Apache 2.0 license as described in the file LICENSE.

+import TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.Yao.HybridProof.TablesFromCoins
+import TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.Yao.HybridProof.WordLists
+
+set_option maxHeartbeats 0
+set_option relaxedAutoImplicit false
+set_option autoImplicit false
+
+/-!
+# Polynomial-time assembly of all garbled tables
+
+The indexed adapter reads one gate record from an encoded circuit gate list and supplies
+its kind/argument fields to the total row/table machine. The outer machine counts gates
+and encodes the table word at each index. Both machines work on arbitrary raw words.
+
+## Main definitions
+
+* `AllTables.entryWord`: one table at the unary index of a retained input word.
+* `AllTables.allTablesWord`: the encoded list of all table words.
+
+## Main results
+
+* `AllTables.entryWord_poly`, `allTablesWord_poly`: polynomial-time assembly.
+* `AllTables.allTablesWord_garbledTables`: correctness from indexed table contracts.
+
+## References
+
+* [PS10] R. Pass, a. shelat, *A Course in Cryptography*, lecture notes, 3rd ed., January
+  2010, Protocol 162.6 (transmission of the garbled circuit).
+-/
+
+namespace TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof.AllTables
+
+open Complexity Turing BoolCircuit TCSlib.Cryptography.EncodingMachines
+
+/-- On `⟨1^i, z⟩`, read gate `i` from the supplied encoded gate list, then build its encoded
+row table from the retained unary data and coin word. [PS10, Protocol 162.6]
+`entryWord E₁ E₂ width bound inputs gates coins w = let record := fun v => BinArith.getW (pairEncode
+(pairFstD v) (gates (pairSndD v))); TablesFromCoins.tableWord E₁ E₂ (fun v => width (pairSndD v))
+(fun v => bound (pairSndD v)) (fun v => inputs (pairSndD v)) (fun v => (pairFstD v).length) (fun v
+=> pairFstD (record v)) (fun v => pairSndD (record v)) (fun v => coins (pairSndD v)) w`. -/
+noncomputable def entryWord (E₁ E₂ : List Bool → List Bool)
+    (width bound inputs : List Bool → ℕ) (gates coins : List Bool → List Bool)
+    (w : List Bool) : List Bool :=
+  let record := fun v => BinArith.getW (pairEncode (pairFstD v) (gates (pairSndD v)))
+  TablesFromCoins.tableWord E₁ E₂
+    (fun v => width (pairSndD v)) (fun v => bound (pairSndD v))
+    (fun v => inputs (pairSndD v)) (fun v => (pairFstD v).length)
+    (fun v => pairFstD (record v)) (fun v => pairSndD (record v))
+    (fun v => coins (pairSndD v)) w
+
+/-- `entryWord ... w` applies the total table adapter to the selected gate record and
+retained numeric/coin data of `pairSndD w`, with gate index `|pairFstD w|`.
+`entryWord E₁ E₂ width bound inputs gates coins w = let record := fun v => BinArith.getW (pairEncode
+(pairFstD v) (gates (pairSndD v))); TablesFromCoins.tableWord E₁ E₂ (fun v => width (pairSndD v))
+(fun v => bound (pairSndD v)) (fun v => inputs (pairSndD v)) (fun v => (pairFstD v).length) (fun v
+=> pairFstD (record v)) (fun v => pairSndD (record v)) (fun v => coins (pairSndD v)) w`. -/
+theorem entryWord_eq (E₁ E₂ : List Bool → List Bool)
+    (width bound inputs : List Bool → ℕ) (gates coins : List Bool → List Bool)
+    (w : List Bool) :
+    entryWord E₁ E₂ width bound inputs gates coins w =
+      let record := fun v => BinArith.getW (pairEncode (pairFstD v) (gates (pairSndD v)))
+      TablesFromCoins.tableWord E₁ E₂
+        (fun v => width (pairSndD v)) (fun v => bound (pairSndD v))
+        (fun v => inputs (pairSndD v)) (fun v => (pairFstD v).length)
+        (fun v => pairFstD (record v)) (fun v => pairSndD (record v))
+        (fun v => coins (pairSndD v)) w := rfl
+
+/-- The indexed table adapter is polynomial-time when its evaluators, encoded gate list,
+coin word, and unary numeric maps are polynomial-time. [PS10, Protocol 162.6]
+`PolyTimeComputable (entryWord E₁ E₂ width bound inputs gates coins)`. -/
+theorem entryWord_poly {E₁ E₂ : List Bool → List Bool} (hE₁ : PolyTimeComputable E₁)
+    (hE₂ : PolyTimeComputable E₂) {width bound inputs : List Bool → ℕ}
+    {gates coins : List Bool → List Bool}
+    (hwidth : PolyTimeComputable fun z => List.replicate (width z) true)
+    (hbound : PolyTimeComputable fun z => List.replicate (bound z) true)
+    (hinputs : PolyTimeComputable fun z => List.replicate (inputs z) true)
+    (hgates : PolyTimeComputable gates) (hcoins : PolyTimeComputable coins) :
+    PolyTimeComputable (entryWord E₁ E₂ width bound inputs gates coins) := by
+  have hw : PolyTimeComputable fun v => List.replicate (width (pairSndD v)) true :=
+    hwidth.comp polyTimeComputable_pairSndD
+  have hb : PolyTimeComputable fun v => List.replicate (bound (pairSndD v)) true :=
+    hbound.comp polyTimeComputable_pairSndD
+  have hi : PolyTimeComputable fun v => List.replicate (inputs (pairSndD v)) true :=
+    hinputs.comp polyTimeComputable_pairSndD
+  have hrecord := BinArith.polyTimeComputable_getW.comp
+    (polyTimeComputable_pairFstD.pairEncode (hgates.comp polyTimeComputable_pairSndD))
+  exact TablesFromCoins.tableWord_poly hE₁ hE₂ hw hb hi
+    (unaryLength_poly polyTimeComputable_pairFstD)
+    (polyTimeComputable_pairFstD.comp hrecord) (polyTimeComputable_pairSndD.comp hrecord)
+    (hcoins.comp polyTimeComputable_pairSndD)
+
+/-- The encoded table list contains the indexed table words for every decoded gate:
+`encWords ((range |decWords (gates z)|).map (fun i => entryWord ... ⟨1^i, z⟩))`.
+[PS10, Protocol 162.6] -/
+noncomputable def allTablesWord (E₁ E₂ : List Bool → List Bool)
+    (width bound inputs : List Bool → ℕ) (gates coins : List Bool → List Bool)
+    (z : List Bool) : List Bool :=
+  BinArith.encWords ((List.range (BinArith.decWords (gates z)).length).map fun i =>
+    entryWord E₁ E₂ width bound inputs gates coins (pairEncode (List.replicate i true) z))
+
+/-- `allTablesWord ... z` encodes every indexed table word of the decoded gate list.
+`allTablesWord E₁ E₂ width bound inputs gates coins z = BinArith.encWords ((List.range
+(BinArith.decWords (gates z)).length).map fun i => entryWord E₁ E₂ width bound inputs gates coins
+(pairEncode (List.replicate i true) z))`. -/
+theorem allTablesWord_eq (E₁ E₂ : List Bool → List Bool)
+    (width bound inputs : List Bool → ℕ) (gates coins : List Bool → List Bool)
+    (z : List Bool) :
+    allTablesWord E₁ E₂ width bound inputs gates coins z =
+      BinArith.encWords ((List.range (BinArith.decWords (gates z)).length).map fun i =>
+        entryWord E₁ E₂ width bound inputs gates coins (pairEncode (List.replicate i true) z)) :=
+  rfl
+
+/-- The complete encoded table list is polynomial-time when its evaluators, encoded gate
+list, coin word, and unary numeric maps are polynomial-time. [PS10, Protocol 162.6]
+`PolyTimeComputable (allTablesWord E₁ E₂ width bound inputs gates coins)`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** Count the encoded gate records in unary. Normalize an indexed input's
+counter to unary and apply the polynomial-time indexed table adapter. The public
+data-dependent range-list assembler encodes these table words at every gate index. -/
+theorem allTablesWord_poly {E₁ E₂ : List Bool → List Bool} (hE₁ : PolyTimeComputable E₁)
+    (hE₂ : PolyTimeComputable E₂) {width bound inputs : List Bool → ℕ}
+    {gates coins : List Bool → List Bool}
+    (hwidth : PolyTimeComputable fun z => List.replicate (width z) true)
+    (hbound : PolyTimeComputable fun z => List.replicate (bound z) true)
+    (hinputs : PolyTimeComputable fun z => List.replicate (inputs z) true)
+    (hgates : PolyTimeComputable gates) (hcoins : PolyTimeComputable coins) :
+    PolyTimeComputable (allTablesWord E₁ E₂ width bound inputs gates coins) := by
+  have hcount : PolyTimeComputable fun z =>
+      List.replicate (BinArith.decWords (gates z)).length true := by
+    convert BinArith.polyTimeComputable_countW.comp hgates using 1
+    funext z
+    rw [Function.comp_apply, BinArith.countW_eq]
+  have he := (entryWord_poly hE₁ hE₂ hwidth hbound hinputs hgates hcoins).comp
+    ((polyTimeComputable_unary.comp polyTimeComputable_pairFstD).pairEncode
+      polyTimeComputable_pairSndD)
+  exact WordLists.rangeTable_poly
+    (entry := fun z i => entryWord E₁ E₂ width bound inputs gates coins
+      (pairEncode (List.replicate i true) z)) hcount he
+
+/-- If the gate word encodes `C.gates` and each indexed adapter supplies its encoded garbled
+table, the outer adapter is exactly `encWords (ofFn (fun i => encWords (garbledTable ... i)))`.
+[PS10, Protocol 162.6] -/
+theorem allTablesWord_garbledTables (E₁ E₂ : List Bool → List Bool)
+    (width bound inputs : List Bool → ℕ) (gates coins : List Bool → List Bool)
+    (gateCode : DAGGate → List Bool) (z : List Bool) (C : DAGCircuit (inputs z))
+    (s : GarbleSeed C (Fin (width z) → Bool)
+      ((Fin (width z) → Bool) × (Fin (width z) → Bool)))
+    (hgates : gates z = BinArith.encWords (C.gates.map gateCode))
+    (hentry : ∀ i : Fin C.gates.length,
+      entryWord E₁ E₂ width bound inputs gates coins (pairEncode (List.replicate i true) z) =
+        BinArith.encWords (garbledTable (nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ (width z)) C s i)) :
+    allTablesWord E₁ E₂ width bound inputs gates coins z =
+      BinArith.encWords (List.ofFn fun i =>
+        BinArith.encWords (garbledTable (nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ (width z)) C s i)) := by
+  rw [allTablesWord_eq, hgates, BinArith.decWords_encWords, List.length_map]
+  apply congrArg BinArith.encWords
+  symm
+  apply ofFn_eq_map_range
+  intro i
+  exact (hentry i).symm
+
+
+/-- On a canonical indexed input `⟨1^i, z⟩`, the adapter equals the table machine on the
+retained data with gate index fixed to `i` and the selected record's kind/argument fields.
+This equation includes arbitrary malformed retained words.
+`entryWord E₁ E₂ width bound inputs gates coins (pairEncode (List.replicate i true) z) =
+TablesFromCoins.tableWord E₁ E₂ width bound inputs (fun _ => i) (fun v => pairFstD (BinArith.getW
+(pairEncode (List.replicate i true) (gates v)))) (fun v => pairSndD (BinArith.getW (pairEncode
+(List.replicate i true) (gates v)))) coins z`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** Normalize the argument count before comparing the dependent finite
+assignment lists. Both branches then use the same enumeration. At each row, freeze the
+supplied data maps to their current values; the public pair readers identify these values
+with the retained context and unary index. -/
+theorem entryWord_pair (E₁ E₂ : List Bool → List Bool)
+    (width bound inputs : List Bool → ℕ) (gates coins : List Bool → List Bool)
+    (i : ℕ) (z : List Bool) :
+    entryWord E₁ E₂ width bound inputs gates coins (pairEncode (List.replicate i true) z) =
+      TablesFromCoins.tableWord E₁ E₂ width bound inputs (fun _ => i)
+        (fun v => pairFstD (BinArith.getW (pairEncode (List.replicate i true) (gates v))))
+        (fun v => pairSndD (BinArith.getW (pairEncode (List.replicate i true) (gates v))))
+        coins z := by
+  let w := pairEncode (List.replicate i true) z
+  let record := fun v => BinArith.getW (pairEncode (pairFstD v) (gates (pairSndD v)))
+  let record' := fun v => BinArith.getW (pairEncode (List.replicate i true) (gates v))
+  let count := (BinArith.decWords (pairSndD (record' z))).length
+  let left (l : ℕ) := BinArith.encWords
+    ((Finset.univ : Finset (Fin l → Bool)).toList.map fun a =>
+      RowFromCoins.rowWord E₁ E₂ (fun v => width (pairSndD v))
+        (fun v => bound (pairSndD v)) (fun v => inputs (pairSndD v))
+        (fun v => (pairFstD v).length) (fun v => pairFstD (record v))
+        (fun v => pairSndD (record v)) (fun v => coins (pairSndD v)) l a w)
+  let right (l : ℕ) := BinArith.encWords
+    ((Finset.univ : Finset (Fin l → Bool)).toList.map fun a =>
+      RowFromCoins.rowWord E₁ E₂ width bound inputs (fun _ => i)
+        (fun v => pairFstD (record' v)) (fun v => pairSndD (record' v)) coins l a z)
+  have hrecord : record w = record' z := by
+    simp only [record, record', w, pairFstD_pairEncode, pairSndD_pairEncode]
+  rw [entryWord_eq, TablesFromCoins.tableWord_eq, TablesFromCoins.tableWord_eq]
+  change (if (BinArith.decWords (pairSndD (record w))).length ≤ 2 then
+    left (min (BinArith.decWords (pairSndD (record w))).length 2) else []) =
+      if count ≤ 2 then right (min count 2) else []
+  -- Normalize the natural arity before opening its dependent finite enumeration.
+  rw [hrecord]
+  change (if count ≤ 2 then left (min count 2) else []) =
+    if count ≤ 2 then right (min count 2) else []
+  by_cases h : count ≤ 2
+  · rw [if_pos h, if_pos h]
+    dsimp only [left, right]
+    apply congrArg BinArith.encWords
+    apply List.map_congr_left
+    intro a _
+    -- Freeze row data first, so paired-field readers never enter a dependent domain.
+    conv_lhs => rw [RowFromCoins.rowWord_value]
+    conv_rhs => rw [RowFromCoins.rowWord_value]
+    simp only [record, record', w, pairFstD_pairEncode, pairSndD_pairEncode,
+      List.length_replicate]
+  · rw [if_neg h, if_neg h]
+
+end TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof.AllTables

+/-
+Copyright (c) 2026 TCSlib contributors. All rights reserved.
+Released under Apache 2.0 license as described in the file LICENSE.
+-/
+import TCSlib.Cryptography.Asymptotic.EnsemblesLemmas
+import TCSlib.Cryptography.ZeroKnowledge.EncodingMachines
+
+set_option maxHeartbeats 0
+set_option relaxedAutoImplicit false
+set_option autoImplicit false
+
+/-!
+# Reading the output of an encoded garbled evaluation
+
+The output reader takes a context `⟨1^n, ⟨values, ⟨pairs, outputs⟩⟩⟩`. The value list and
+pair list use `BinArith.encWords`; the output-vertex list uses that encoding on unary numbers
+with their terminating `false` bit. Every raw input has a total interpretation. A missing key
+is padded to `n` bits before it is compared with the decoding pair.
+
+## Main definitions
+
+* `outputBit`: decode one output vertex, given its unary position in the output list.
+* `outputWord`: decode a polynomial number of output positions.
+
+## Main results
+
+* `outputBit_poly`: the one-bit output reader is polynomial-time on every word.
+* `outputWord_poly`: a polynomial number of decoded output bits is polynomial-time.
+
+## References
+
+* [PS10] R. Pass, a. shelat, *A Course in Cryptography*, lecture notes, 3rd ed.,
+  January 2010, Protocol 162.6 (reading the output of the garbled circuit).
+-/
+
+namespace TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof.BobIO
+
+open Complexity Turing
+
+/-- On `⟨⟨u, ⟨values, ⟨pairs, outputs⟩⟩⟩, 1^j⟩`, read output vertex `outputs[j]`, pad its
+key from `values` to `|u|` bits, and decode it against `pairs[j]`: first key gives `false`,
+otherwise equality to the second key gives `true`. All projections and lookups are total.
+`outputBit z = let ctx := pairFstD z; let j := pairSndD z; let vals := pairFstD (pairSndD ctx); let
+pairs := pairFstD (pairSndD (pairSndD ctx)); let outs := pairSndD (pairSndD (pairSndD ctx)); let v
+:= BinArith.uHead (BinArith.getW (pairEncode j outs)); let k := EnsembleLemmas.padL (pairFstD
+ctx).length (BinArith.getW (pairEncode v vals)); let p := BinArith.getW (pairEncode j pairs); if k =
+pairFstD p then false else decide (k = pairSndD p)`. -/
+def outputBit (z : List Bool) : Bool :=
+  let ctx := pairFstD z
+  let j := pairSndD z
+  let vals := pairFstD (pairSndD ctx)
+  let pairs := pairFstD (pairSndD (pairSndD ctx))
+  let outs := pairSndD (pairSndD (pairSndD ctx))
+  let v := BinArith.uHead (BinArith.getW (pairEncode j outs))
+  let k := EnsembleLemmas.padL (pairFstD ctx).length (BinArith.getW (pairEncode v vals))
+  let p := BinArith.getW (pairEncode j pairs)
+  if k = pairFstD p then false else decide (k = pairSndD p)
+
+/-- The bit reader is the comparison of the padded value at the selected output vertex
+with the two components of the selected decoding pair.
+`outputBit z = let ctx := pairFstD z; let j := pairSndD z; let vals := pairFstD (pairSndD ctx); let
+pairs := pairFstD (pairSndD (pairSndD ctx)); let outs := pairSndD (pairSndD (pairSndD ctx)); let v
+:= BinArith.uHead (BinArith.getW (pairEncode j outs)); let k := EnsembleLemmas.padL (pairFstD
+ctx).length (BinArith.getW (pairEncode v vals)); let p := BinArith.getW (pairEncode j pairs); if k =
+pairFstD p then false else decide (k = pairSndD p)`. -/
+theorem outputBit_eq (z : List Bool) :
+    outputBit z =
+      let ctx := pairFstD z
+      let j := pairSndD z
+      let vals := pairFstD (pairSndD ctx)
+      let pairs := pairFstD (pairSndD (pairSndD ctx))
+      let outs := pairSndD (pairSndD (pairSndD ctx))
+      let v := BinArith.uHead (BinArith.getW (pairEncode j outs))
+      let k := EnsembleLemmas.padL (pairFstD ctx).length (BinArith.getW (pairEncode v vals))
+      let p := BinArith.getW (pairEncode j pairs)
+      if k = pairFstD p then false else decide (k = pairSndD p) := rfl
+
+/-- The output word at context `z` has `p(|fst z|)` bits; bit `j` is
+`outputBit ⟨z, 1^j⟩`. [PS10, Protocol 162.6]
+`outputWord p z = List.ofFn fun j : Fin (p.eval (pairFstD z).length) => outputBit (pairEncode z
+(List.replicate j true))`. -/
+def outputWord (p : PolyExpr) (z : List Bool) : List Bool :=
+  List.ofFn fun j : Fin (p.eval (pairFstD z).length) =>
+    outputBit (pairEncode z (List.replicate j true))
+
+/-- The output reader's word is the list of `outputBit ⟨z, 1^j⟩` for
+`j < p(|fst z|)`. -/
+theorem outputWord_eq (p : PolyExpr) (z : List Bool) :
+    outputWord p z = List.ofFn fun j : Fin (p.eval (pairFstD z).length) =>
+      outputBit (pairEncode z (List.replicate j true)) := rfl
+
+/-- The one-bit output reader is polynomial-time on every raw word:
+`PolyTimeComputable (fun z => [outputBit z])`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** Use unary word-list lookup for the output vertex and the decoding pair.
+The vertex's key is another word-list lookup, padded to the parameter length. Two word
+comparisons implement the decoding rule. Each lookup and padding operation is total, so
+malformed words require no separate machine. -/
+theorem outputBit_poly : PolyTimeComputable fun z => [outputBit z] := by
+  let ctx := pairFstD
+  let vals := fun z => pairFstD (pairSndD (ctx z))
+  let pairs := fun z => pairFstD (pairSndD (pairSndD (ctx z)))
+  let outs := fun z => pairSndD (pairSndD (pairSndD (ctx z)))
+  have hc : PolyTimeComputable ctx := polyTimeComputable_pairFstD
+  have hv : PolyTimeComputable vals :=
+    polyTimeComputable_pairFstD.comp (polyTimeComputable_pairSndD.comp hc)
+  have hp : PolyTimeComputable pairs :=
+    polyTimeComputable_pairFstD.comp
+      (polyTimeComputable_pairSndD.comp (polyTimeComputable_pairSndD.comp hc))
+  have ho : PolyTimeComputable outs :=
+    polyTimeComputable_pairSndD.comp
+      (polyTimeComputable_pairSndD.comp (polyTimeComputable_pairSndD.comp hc))
+  let vertex := fun z => BinArith.uHead (BinArith.getW (pairEncode (pairSndD z) (outs z)))
+  have hvertex : PolyTimeComputable vertex := BinArith.polyTimeComputable_uHead.comp
+    (BinArith.polyTimeComputable_getW.comp (polyTimeComputable_pairSndD.pairEncode ho))
+  let key := fun z => EnsembleLemmas.padL (pairFstD (ctx z)).length
+    (BinArith.getW (pairEncode (vertex z) (vals z)))
+  have hk0 := EnsembleLemmas.polyTimeComputable_padL .var
+    (u := fun z => pairFstD (ctx z))
+    (a := fun z => BinArith.getW (pairEncode (vertex z) (vals z)))
+    (polyTimeComputable_pairFstD.comp' hc)
+    (BinArith.polyTimeComputable_getW.comp' (hvertex.pairEncode hv))
+  have hk : PolyTimeComputable key := hk0.congr (g := key) fun z => by
+    simp only [key, PolyExpr.eval_var]
+  let pair := fun z => BinArith.getW (pairEncode (pairSndD z) (pairs z))
+  have hpair : PolyTimeComputable pair :=
+    BinArith.polyTimeComputable_getW.comp (polyTimeComputable_pairSndD.pairEncode hp)
+  have heq0 : PolyTimeComputable fun z => [decide (key z = pairFstD (pair z))] :=
+    (polyTimeComputable_pairEq.comp
+      (hk.pairEncode (polyTimeComputable_pairFstD.comp hpair))).congr fun z => by
+        simp only [Function.comp_apply, pairFstD_pairEncode, pairSndD_pairEncode]
+  have heq1 : PolyTimeComputable fun z => [decide (key z = pairSndD (pair z))] :=
+    (polyTimeComputable_pairEq.comp
+      (hk.pairEncode (polyTimeComputable_pairSndD.comp hpair))).congr fun z => by
+        simp only [Function.comp_apply, pairFstD_pairEncode, pairSndD_pairEncode]
+  refine (polyTimeComputable_ite heq0 (polyTimeComputable_const [false]) heq1).congr
+    (g := fun z => [outputBit z]) ?_
+  intro z
+  by_cases h : key z = pairFstD (pair z) <;>
+    simp only [outputBit_eq, ctx, vals, pairs, outs, vertex, key, pair] at h ⊢ <;>
+    simp only [h, decide_true, decide_false, Bool.false_eq_true,
+      if_true, if_false]
+
+/-- The decoded output word has exactly `p(|fst z|)` bits.
+`(outputWord p z).length = p.eval (pairFstD z).length`. -/
+theorem length_outputWord (p : PolyExpr) (z : List Bool) :
+    (outputWord p z).length = p.eval (pairFstD z).length := List.length_ofFn
+
+/-- Reading position `j` of the output word gives `outputBit ⟨z, 1^j⟩` in range and
+`false` otherwise.
+`(outputWord p z).getD j false = if j < p.eval (pairFstD z).length then outputBit (pairEncode z
+(List.replicate j true)) else false`. -/
+theorem outputWord_getD (p : PolyExpr) (z : List Bool) (j : ℕ) :
+    (outputWord p z).getD j false =
+      if j < p.eval (pairFstD z).length then
+        outputBit (pairEncode z (List.replicate j true)) else false := by
+  rw [outputWord_eq, List.getD_eq_getElem?_getD, List.getElem?_ofFn]
+  split_ifs <;> rfl
+
+/-- Decoding `p(|fst z|)` output bits is polynomial-time on every context word:
+`PolyTimeComputable (outputWord p)`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** The length of the output is the unary polynomial evaluator at the
+parameter word. The output bit at a unary index is the polynomial-time bit reader, guarded
+by the test that the index is below this length. Apply the published construction of a word
+from its polynomial-time length and indexed-bit function. -/
+theorem outputWord_poly (p : PolyExpr) : PolyTimeComputable (outputWord p) := by
+  have hn : PolyTimeComputable fun w =>
+      List.replicate (p.eval (pairFstD (pairSndD w)).length) true :=
+    (polyTimeComputable_unaryPoly p).comp
+      (polyTimeComputable_pairFstD.comp polyTimeComputable_pairSndD)
+  have ht := EncodingMachines.ltTest_poly
+    (EncodingMachines.unaryLength_poly polyTimeComputable_pairFstD) hn
+  have hb : PolyTimeComputable fun w =>
+      [outputBit (pairEncode (pairSndD w)
+        (List.replicate (pairFstD w).length true))] :=
+    outputBit_poly.comp (polyTimeComputable_pairSndD.pairEncode
+      (polyTimeComputable_unary.comp polyTimeComputable_pairFstD))
+  refine EncodingMachines.polyTimeComputable_of_getD (F := outputWord p) ?_ ?_
+  · refine (polyTimeComputable_ite ht hb (polyTimeComputable_const [false])).congr ?_
+    intro w
+    by_cases h : (pairFstD w).length < p.eval (pairFstD (pairSndD w)).length <;>
+      simp only [outputWord_getD, h, decide_true, decide_false,
+        Bool.false_eq_true, if_true, if_false]
+  · exact ((polyTimeComputable_unaryPoly p).comp polyTimeComputable_pairFstD).congr
+      fun z => by simp only [Function.comp_apply, length_outputWord]
+
+/-- On encoded `n`-bit values, decoding pairs and unary output vertices, the bit reader
+compares the value at `outs j` with the `j`-th pair. The vertex must be in the value list.
+`outputBit (pairEncode (pairEncode (List.replicate n true) (pairEncode (BinArith.encWords (vs.map
+List.ofFn)) (pairEncode (BinArith.encWords ps) (BinArith.encWords (List.ofFn fun i =>
+BoolCircuit.encodeNat (outs i)))))) (List.replicate j true)) = if List.ofFn (vs.getD (outs j)
+default) = pairFstD (ps.getD j []) then false else decide (List.ofFn (vs.getD (outs j) default) =
+pairSndD (ps.getD j []))`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** The public word-list lookup equations read the output vertex and the
+pair. The vertex is encoded in unary, so its leading ones give its value. In-range lookup
+in the encoded value list returns the list of the selected bit vector, and padding this
+list leaves it unchanged. -/
+theorem outputBit_layout {n k : ℕ} (vs : List (Fin n → Bool)) (ps : List (List Bool))
+    (outs : Fin k → ℕ) (j : Fin k) (hv : outs j < vs.length) :
+    outputBit (pairEncode
+        (pairEncode (List.replicate n true) (pairEncode (BinArith.encWords (vs.map List.ofFn))
+          (pairEncode (BinArith.encWords ps)
+            (BinArith.encWords (List.ofFn fun i => BoolCircuit.encodeNat (outs i))))))
+        (List.replicate j true)) =
+      if List.ofFn (vs.getD (outs j) default) = pairFstD (ps.getD j []) then false
+      else decide (List.ofFn (vs.getD (outs j) default) = pairSndD (ps.getD j [])) := by
+  have ho : (List.ofFn fun i => BoolCircuit.encodeNat (outs i)).getD j [] =
+      BoolCircuit.encodeNat (outs j) := by
+    rw [List.getD_eq_getElem _ _ (by simpa using j.isLt), List.getElem_ofFn]
+  have hk : (vs.map List.ofFn).getD (outs j) [] = List.ofFn (vs.getD (outs j) default) := by
+    rw [List.getD_eq_getElem _ _ (by simpa using hv), List.getElem_map,
+      List.getD_eq_getElem _ _ hv]
+  have hu := BinArith.uHead_encodeNat (outs j) []
+  rw [List.append_nil] at hu
+  simp only [outputBit_eq, pairFstD_pairEncode, pairSndD_pairEncode,
+    BinArith.getW_pairEncode, List.length_replicate, ho, hu]
+  rw [hk, ← EnsembleLemmas.ofFn_padFin_eq_padL, padFin_ofFn]
+
+/-- On encoded `n`-bit values, decoding pairs and `p(n)` unary output vertices,
+`outputWord p` is the vector of comparisons with the selected decoding pairs. Every
+output vertex must be present in the value list. [PS10, Protocol 162.6]
+`outputWord p (pairEncode (List.replicate n true) (pairEncode (BinArith.encWords (vs.map List.ofFn))
+(pairEncode (BinArith.encWords ps) (BinArith.encWords (List.ofFn fun j => BoolCircuit.encodeNat
+(outs j)))))) = List.ofFn fun j => if List.ofFn (vs.getD (outs j) default) = pairFstD (ps.getD j [])
+then false else decide (List.ofFn (vs.getD (outs j) default) = pairSndD (ps.getD j []))`. -/
+theorem outputWord_layout (p : PolyExpr) {n : ℕ} (vs : List (Fin n → Bool))
+    (ps : List (List Bool)) (outs : Fin (p.eval n) → ℕ)
+    (hv : ∀ j, outs j < vs.length) :
+    outputWord p
+        (pairEncode (List.replicate n true) (pairEncode (BinArith.encWords (vs.map List.ofFn))
+          (pairEncode (BinArith.encWords ps)
+            (BinArith.encWords (List.ofFn fun j => BoolCircuit.encodeNat (outs j)))))) =
+      List.ofFn fun j =>
+        if List.ofFn (vs.getD (outs j) default) = pairFstD (ps.getD j []) then false
+        else decide (List.ofFn (vs.getD (outs j) default) = pairSndD (ps.getD j [])) := by
+  simp only [outputWord_eq, pairFstD_pairEncode, List.length_replicate, Fin.coe_cast]
+  apply congrArg List.ofFn
+  funext j
+  exact outputBit_layout vs ps outs j (hv j)
+
+end TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof.BobIO

+/-

+Released under Apache 2.0 license as described in the file LICENSE.

+import TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.Yao.HybridProof.CoinRead
+import TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.Yao.HybridProof.FirstTagged
+import TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.Yao.HybridProof.WordLists

+set_option maxHeartbeats 0
+set_option relaxedAutoImplicit false
+set_option autoImplicit false
