+/-!
+# Polynomial-time initial keys for a garbled evaluation
+
+A context has shape `⟨N, ⟨A, ⟨B, ⟨message, answers⟩⟩⟩⟩`. The lengths of `N`, `A` and `B`
+are the key width and the two input counts. At a position below `|A|`, the reader pads the
+corresponding key of the message. At a later position it reads the corresponding key-width
+block of the answer string. Every raw context and every index have a total interpretation.
+
+## Main definitions
+
+* `initEntry`: one initial key, selected by a unary index.
+* `initWords`: the encoded list of the `|A| + |B|` initial keys.
+
+## Main results
+
+* `initEntry_poly`, `initWords_poly`: both operations are polynomial-time on all inputs.
+* `initEntry_layout`, `initWords_layout`: the exact contracts on the context layout.
+
+## References
+
+* [PS10] R. Pass, a. shelat, *A Course in Cryptography*, lecture notes, 3rd ed.,
+  January 2010, Protocol 162.6 (the evaluating party's initial wire keys).
+-/
+
+namespace TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof.BobInit
+
+open Complexity Turing
+
+/-- On `⟨1^v, ⟨N, ⟨A, ⟨B, ⟨message, answers⟩⟩⟩⟩⟩`, return the padded message key
+at `v < |A|`, otherwise the `|N|`-bit answer block from `(v - |A|) |N|`. -/
+def initEntry (z : List Bool) : List Bool :=
+  let ctx := pairSndD z
+  let N := pairFstD ctx
+  let A := pairFstD (pairSndD ctx)
+  let msg := pairFstD (pairSndD (pairSndD (pairSndD ctx)))
+  let ans := pairSndD (pairSndD (pairSndD (pairSndD ctx)))
+  if (pairFstD z).length < A.length then
+    EnsembleLemmas.padL N.length
+      (BinArith.getW (pairEncode (pairFstD z)
+        (BinArith.encWords (BinArith.decWords (pairFstD (pairSndD msg))))))
+  else List.ofFn fun t : Fin N.length =>
+    ans.getD (((pairFstD z).length - A.length) * N.length + t) false
+
+/-- The entry reader selects the padded message key below `|A|`, and an answer block
+otherwise, through total pair and encoded-list readers.
+`initEntry z = let ctx := pairSndD z; let N := pairFstD ctx; let A := pairFstD (pairSndD ctx); let
+msg := pairFstD (pairSndD (pairSndD (pairSndD ctx))); let ans := pairSndD (pairSndD (pairSndD
+(pairSndD ctx))); if (pairFstD z).length < A.length then EnsembleLemmas.padL N.length (BinArith.getW
+(pairEncode (pairFstD z) (BinArith.encWords (BinArith.decWords (pairFstD (pairSndD msg)))))) else
+List.ofFn fun t : Fin N.length => ans.getD (((pairFstD z).length - A.length) * N.length + t)
+false`. -/
+theorem initEntry_eq (z : List Bool) :
+    initEntry z =
+      let ctx := pairSndD z
+      let N := pairFstD ctx
+      let A := pairFstD (pairSndD ctx)
+      let msg := pairFstD (pairSndD (pairSndD (pairSndD ctx)))
+      let ans := pairSndD (pairSndD (pairSndD (pairSndD ctx)))
+      if (pairFstD z).length < A.length then
+        EnsembleLemmas.padL N.length
+          (BinArith.getW (pairEncode (pairFstD z)
+            (BinArith.encWords (BinArith.decWords (pairFstD (pairSndD msg))))))
+      else List.ofFn fun t : Fin N.length =>
+        ans.getD (((pairFstD z).length - A.length) * N.length + t) false := rfl
+
+/-- The initial encoded word list has `|A| + |B|` entries, entry `v` being
+`initEntry ⟨1^v, context⟩`. [PS10, Protocol 162.6]
+`initWords z = BinArith.encWords ((List.range ((pairFstD (pairSndD z)).length + (pairFstD (pairSndD
+(pairSndD z))).length)).map fun v => initEntry (pairEncode (List.replicate v true) z))`. -/
+def initWords (z : List Bool) : List Bool :=
+  BinArith.encWords
+    ((List.range ((pairFstD (pairSndD z)).length +
+      (pairFstD (pairSndD (pairSndD z))).length)).map fun v =>
+        initEntry (pairEncode (List.replicate v true) z))
+
+/-- The initial encoded word list is the encoding of the entry reader over the range
+`0, …, |A| + |B| - 1`.
+`initWords z = BinArith.encWords ((List.range ((pairFstD (pairSndD z)).length + (pairFstD (pairSndD
+(pairSndD z))).length)).map fun v => initEntry (pairEncode (List.replicate v true) z))`. -/
+theorem initWords_eq (z : List Bool) :
+    initWords z = BinArith.encWords
+      ((List.range ((pairFstD (pairSndD z)).length +
+        (pairFstD (pairSndD (pairSndD z))).length)).map fun v =>
+          initEntry (pairEncode (List.replicate v true) z)) := rfl
+
+/-- Reading one initial key is polynomial-time on every raw context and unary index:
+`PolyTimeComputable initEntry`. [PS10, Protocol 162.6]
+
+**Proof sketch.** Normalize the message's encoded key list, look up the selected word,
+and pad it to the key width. The other branch is a padded coin window at the unary offset
+`(v - |A|) |N|`. Unary subtraction, multiplication and the comparison `v < |A|` are
+published polynomial-time operations. They assemble the two branches without assuming
+that any input or message is well formed. -/
+theorem initEntry_poly : PolyTimeComputable initEntry := by
+  let ctx := pairSndD
+  let N := fun z => pairFstD (ctx z)
+  let A := fun z => pairFstD (pairSndD (ctx z))
+  let msg := fun z => pairFstD (pairSndD (pairSndD (pairSndD (ctx z))))
+  let ans := fun z => pairSndD (pairSndD (pairSndD (pairSndD (ctx z))))
+  have hc : PolyTimeComputable ctx := polyTimeComputable_pairSndD
+  have hN : PolyTimeComputable N := polyTimeComputable_pairFstD.comp' hc
+  have hA : PolyTimeComputable A :=
+    polyTimeComputable_pairFstD.comp' (polyTimeComputable_pairSndD.comp' hc)
+  have hm : PolyTimeComputable msg := polyTimeComputable_pairFstD.comp'
+    (polyTimeComputable_pairSndD.comp'
+      (polyTimeComputable_pairSndD.comp' (polyTimeComputable_pairSndD.comp' hc)))
+  have ha : PolyTimeComputable ans := polyTimeComputable_pairSndD.comp'
+    (polyTimeComputable_pairSndD.comp'
+      (polyTimeComputable_pairSndD.comp' (polyTimeComputable_pairSndD.comp' hc)))
+  let raw := fun z => BinArith.getW (pairEncode (pairFstD z)
+    (BinArith.encWords (BinArith.decWords (pairFstD (pairSndD (msg z))))))
+  have hr : PolyTimeComputable raw := BinArith.polyTimeComputable_getW.comp'
+    (polyTimeComputable_pairFstD.pairEncode (polyTimeComputable_reencodeWords.comp'
+      (polyTimeComputable_pairFstD.comp' (polyTimeComputable_pairSndD.comp' hm))))
+  have hpad0 := EnsembleLemmas.polyTimeComputable_padL .var (u := N) (a := raw) hN hr
+  have hpad : PolyTimeComputable fun z => EnsembleLemmas.padL (N z).length (raw z) :=
+    hpad0.congr fun z => by simp only [PolyExpr.eval_var]
+  have hi := EncodingMachines.unaryLength_poly polyTimeComputable_pairFstD
+  have hcount := EncodingMachines.unaryLength_poly hA
+  have hwidth := EncodingMachines.unaryLength_poly hN
+  have hw := polyTimeComputable_window ha
+    (EncodingMachines.unaryMul_poly (EncodingMachines.unarySub_poly hi hcount) hwidth) hwidth
+  have ht := EncodingMachines.ltTest_poly hi hcount
+  refine (polyTimeComputable_ite ht hpad hw).congr (g := initEntry) fun z => ?_
+  simp only [initEntry_eq, ctx, N, A, msg, ans, raw, decide_eq_true_eq]
+
+/-- Constructing the entire encoded initial-key list is polynomial-time on every raw
+context: `PolyTimeComputable initWords`. [PS10, Protocol 162.6]
+
+**Proof sketch.** The lengths of the two input-count words are polynomial-time unary
+counts. Add them, then apply the shared ordered-list encoder to the polynomial-time
+entry reader, supplying each index in unary. -/
+theorem initWords_poly : PolyTimeComputable initWords := by
+  have hA := polyTimeComputable_pairFstD.comp' polyTimeComputable_pairSndD
+  have hB := polyTimeComputable_pairFstD.comp'
+    (polyTimeComputable_pairSndD.comp' polyTimeComputable_pairSndD)
+  have hc := EncodingMachines.unaryAdd_poly
+    (EncodingMachines.unaryLength_poly hA) (EncodingMachines.unaryLength_poly hB)
+  have he := initEntry_poly.comp'
+    ((polyTimeComputable_unary.comp' polyTimeComputable_pairFstD).pairEncode
+      polyTimeComputable_pairSndD)
+  exact (WordLists.rangeTable_poly
+    (count := fun z => (pairFstD (pairSndD z)).length +
+      (pairFstD (pairSndD (pairSndD z))).length)
+    (entry := fun z v => initEntry (pairEncode (List.replicate v true) z)) hc he).congr
+      (g := initWords) fun z => (initWords_eq z).symm
+
+/-- On the stated context layout, entry `v` is the `v`-th padded key decoded from the
+message below `|A|`, and the answer block from `(v - |A|) |N|` otherwise.
+`initEntry (pairEncode (List.replicate v true) (pairEncode N (pairEncode A (pairEncode B (pairEncode
+msg ans))))) = if v < A.length then List.ofFn (padFin N.length ((BinArith.decWords (pairFstD
+(pairSndD msg))).getD v [])) else List.ofFn fun t : Fin N.length => ans.getD ((v - A.length) *
+N.length + t) false`. -/
+theorem initEntry_layout (N A B msg ans : List Bool) (v : ℕ) :
+    initEntry (pairEncode (List.replicate v true)
+        (pairEncode N (pairEncode A (pairEncode B (pairEncode msg ans))))) =
+      if v < A.length then
+        List.ofFn (padFin N.length
+          ((BinArith.decWords (pairFstD (pairSndD msg))).getD v []))
+      else List.ofFn fun t : Fin N.length =>
+        ans.getD ((v - A.length) * N.length + t) false := by
+  simp only [initEntry_eq, pairFstD_pairEncode, pairSndD_pairEncode,
+    List.length_replicate, BinArith.getW_pairEncode, Fin.coe_cast,
+    ← EnsembleLemmas.ofFn_padFin_eq_padL]
+
+/-- On the context layout, the initial word list is the encoded vector of `|A| + |B|`
+keys: the first `|A|` are padded message keys, and the rest are answer blocks.
+`initWords (pairEncode N (pairEncode A (pairEncode B (pairEncode msg ans)))) = BinArith.encWords
+(List.ofFn fun v : Fin (A.length + B.length) => if (v : ℕ) < A.length then List.ofFn (padFin
+N.length ((BinArith.decWords (pairFstD (pairSndD msg))).getD v [])) else List.ofFn fun t : Fin
+N.length => ans.getD (((v : ℕ) - A.length) * N.length + t) false)`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** Replace the unary range map by the equal finite vector using the public
+range/vector identity. Then apply the entry reader's layout contract at every position. -/
+theorem initWords_layout (N A B msg ans : List Bool) :
+    initWords (pairEncode N (pairEncode A (pairEncode B (pairEncode msg ans)))) =
+      BinArith.encWords (List.ofFn fun v : Fin (A.length + B.length) =>
+        if (v : ℕ) < A.length then
+          List.ofFn (padFin N.length
+            ((BinArith.decWords (pairFstD (pairSndD msg))).getD v []))
+        else List.ofFn fun t : Fin N.length =>
+          ans.getD (((v : ℕ) - A.length) * N.length + t) false) := by
+  rw [initWords_eq]
+  simp only [pairFstD_pairEncode, pairSndD_pairEncode]
+  apply congrArg BinArith.encWords
+  refine Eq.trans (ofFn_eq_map_range
+    (fun v : Fin (A.length + B.length) => initEntry (pairEncode (List.replicate v true)
+      (pairEncode N (pairEncode A (pairEncode B (pairEncode msg ans))))))
+    (fun v => initEntry (pairEncode (List.replicate v true)
+      (pairEncode N (pairEncode A (pairEncode B (pairEncode msg ans))))))
+    (fun _ => rfl)).symm ?_
+  apply congrArg List.ofFn
+  funext v
+  exact initEntry_layout N A B msg ans v
+
+end TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof.BobInit

+import TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.Yao.HybridProof.DescriptionDecoder
+import TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.Yao.HybridProof.BobInit
+import TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.Yao.HybridProof.BobTables
+import TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.Yao.HybridProof.BobIO
+import TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.Yao.HybridProof.GarbledRun
+
+set_option maxHeartbeats 0
+set_option relaxedAutoImplicit false
+set_option autoImplicit false
+
+/-!
+# The complete generic polynomial-time garbled-circuit evaluator
+
+A uniform circuit-description generator, initial-key constructor, table selector and
+output reader assemble into one polynomial-time output function. The common-input,
+message and answer selectors are arbitrary polynomial-time word maps. Only the
+length of the common-input selector matters; its bits need not form a unary word.
+The initial keys, tables and decoding pairs enter through explicit interface equations.
+
+## Main definitions
+
+None. The theorem uses the library's `runWith`, `evalGate` and nested special encryption.
+
+## Main results
+
+* `BobProgram.output_poly`: the complete encoded output-comparison vector is polynomial-time.
+
+## References
+
+* [PS10] R. Pass, a. shelat, *A Course in Cryptography*, lecture notes, 3rd ed., January
+  2010, Protocol 162.6 (the evaluating party's computation).
+-/
+
+namespace TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof.BobProgram
+
+open Complexity Turing BoolCircuit
+
+/-- For a uniform circuit family with valid output vertices, polynomial-time evaluators
+and arbitrary polynomial-time parameter/message/answer selectors, the output-comparison
+vector after garbled evaluation is polynomial-time. Initial keys, row tables and decoding
+pairs are supplied through the stated reader equations. No well-formed-message hypothesis
+is imposed. [PS10, Protocol 162.6]
+`PolyTimeComputable fun z => let vals := runWith (fun g vs => evalGate (nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ (N
+z).length) (T z) vs.length g vs) (circ (N z).length).gates (init z); List.ofFn fun j : Fin
+(outLen.eval (N z).length) => if List.ofFn (vals.getD (outs (N z).length j) default) = pairFstD
+((pairs z).getD j []) then false else decide (List.ofFn (vals.getD (outs (N z).length j) default) =
+pairSndD ((pairs z).getD j []))`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** Normalize the parameter to unary before calling the uniform generator.
+The public description decoder supplies the gate records. The initial-key machine and
+raw table selector satisfy the generic garbled-run contracts, so the encoded complete
+vertex list is polynomial-time. Normalize the message's decoding-pair list and retain the
+uniform description's output list. The public output reader computes the desired vector.
+The output vertices are available because `runWith` adds one value per gate and the initial
+list has the circuit's input count. -/
+theorem output_poly (inA inB outLen : PolyExpr)
+    (circ : ∀ n, DAGCircuit (inA.eval n + inB.eval n))
+    (outs : ∀ n, Fin (outLen.eval n) → ℕ)
+    (houts : ∀ n j, outs n j < (circ n).size)
+    {gen E₁ E₂ N msg ans : List Bool → List Bool}
+    (hgen : PolyTimeComputable gen)
+    (hgen_eq : ∀ n, gen (List.replicate n true) =
+      pairEncode (circ n).encode
+        (BinArith.encWords (List.ofFn fun j => encodeNat (outs n j))))
+    (hE₁ : PolyTimeComputable E₁) (hE₂ : PolyTimeComputable E₂)
+    (hN : PolyTimeComputable N) (hmsg : PolyTimeComputable msg)
+    (hans : PolyTimeComputable ans)
+    (init : ∀ z, List (Fin (N z).length → Bool))
+    (T : List Bool → ℕ → List (List Bool)) (pairs : List Bool → List (List Bool))
+    (hinit : ∀ z, BinArith.encWords ((init z).map List.ofFn) =
+      BobInit.initWords (pairEncode (List.replicate (N z).length true)
+        (pairEncode (List.replicate (inA.eval (N z).length) true)
+          (pairEncode (List.replicate (inB.eval (N z).length) true)
+            (pairEncode (msg z) (ans z))))))
+    (hinit_length : ∀ z, (init z).length = inA.eval (N z).length + inB.eval (N z).length)
+    (hT : ∀ z v, T z v =
+      if inA.eval (N z).length + inB.eval (N z).length ≤ v then
+        BinArith.decWords ((BinArith.decWords (pairFstD (msg z))).getD
+          (v - (inA.eval (N z).length + inB.eval (N z).length)) []) else [])
+    (hpairs : ∀ z, pairs z = BinArith.decWords (pairSndD (pairSndD (msg z)))) :
+    PolyTimeComputable fun z =>
+      let vals := runWith
+        (fun g vs => evalGate (nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ (N z).length) (T z) vs.length g vs)
+        (circ (N z).length).gates (init z)
+      List.ofFn fun j : Fin (outLen.eval (N z).length) =>
+        if List.ofFn (vals.getD (outs (N z).length j) default) =
+            pairFstD ((pairs z).getD j []) then false
+        else decide (List.ofFn (vals.getD (outs (N z).length j) default) =
+          pairSndD ((pairs z).getD j [])) := by
+  -- Decode the uniform description without imposing any raw-input shape.
+  have hNu := polyTimeComputable_unary.comp' hN
+  have hdesc := hgen.comp' hNu
+  have hgateRaw := polyTimeComputable_pairSndD.comp'
+    (DescriptionDecoder.readCircuit_poly.comp' (polyTimeComputable_pairFstD.comp' hdesc))
+  have hgs : PolyTimeComputable fun z =>
+      BinArith.encWords ((circ (N z).length).gates.map DescriptionDecoder.gateRecord) :=
+    hgateRaw.congr fun z => by
+      rw [hgen_eq, pairFstD_pairEncode, DescriptionDecoder.readCircuit_encode,
+        pairSndD_pairEncode]
+  have houtsCode : PolyTimeComputable fun z =>
+      BinArith.encWords (List.ofFn fun j => encodeNat (outs (N z).length j)) :=
+    (polyTimeComputable_pairSndD.comp' hdesc).congr fun z => by
+      rw [hgen_eq, pairSndD_pairEncode]
+  have hA := (polyTimeComputable_unaryPoly inA).comp' hN
+  have hB := (polyTimeComputable_unaryPoly inB).comp' hN
+  have hiRaw := BobInit.initWords_poly.comp'
+    (hNu.pairEncode (hA.pairEncode (hB.pairEncode (hmsg.pairEncode hans))))
+  have hi : PolyTimeComputable fun z => BinArith.encWords ((init z).map List.ofFn) :=
+    hiRaw.congr fun z => (hinit z).symm
+  -- The current-table machine discharges the generic traversal's reconstruction premise.
+  let m : List Bool → List Bool := fun z =>
+    List.replicate (inA.eval (N z).length + inB.eval (N z).length) true
+  have hm : PolyTimeComputable m := EncodingMachines.unaryAdd_poly hA hB
+  have hargs : ∀ g, pairSndD (DescriptionDecoder.gateRecord g) =
+      BinArith.encWords (g.args.map fun a => List.replicate a true) := fun g => by
+    rw [DescriptionDecoder.gateRecord_eq, pairSndD_pairEncode]
+  have ht : ∀ z g (vs : List (Fin (N z).length → Bool)),
+      BobTables.tableSelector m msg (pairEncode z
+        (pairEncode (DescriptionDecoder.gateRecord g) (BinArith.encWords (vs.map List.ofFn)))) =
+          BinArith.encWords (T z vs.length) := by
+    intro z g vs
+    rw [BobTables.tableSelector_host]
+    simp only [m, List.length_replicate]
+    rw [hT]
+  let vals := fun z => runWith
+    (fun g vs => evalGate (nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ (N z).length) (T z) vs.length g vs)
+    (circ (N z).length).gates (init z)
+  have hv : PolyTimeComputable fun z => BinArith.encWords ((vals z).map List.ofFn) :=
+    garbledRunWith_poly hE₁ hE₂ hN DescriptionDecoder.gateRecord
+      (fun z => (circ (N z).length).gates) init T hgs hi
+      (BobTables.tableSelector_poly hm hmsg) hargs ht
+  -- The circuit-size reader proves that every requested output is present.
+  have hrange : ∀ z j, outs (N z).length j < (vals z).length := by
+    intro z j
+    have h := houts (N z).length j
+    rw [DAGCircuit.size_eq] at h
+    simpa only [vals, length_runWith, hinit_length] using h
+  have hp : PolyTimeComputable fun z => BinArith.encWords (pairs z) :=
+    (polyTimeComputable_reencodeWords.comp'
+      (polyTimeComputable_pairSndD.comp' (polyTimeComputable_pairSndD.comp' hmsg))).congr
+        fun z => congrArg BinArith.encWords (hpairs z).symm
+  have hcontext := hNu.pairEncode (hv.pairEncode (hp.pairEncode houtsCode))
+  have hout := ((BobIO.outputWord_poly outLen).comp' hcontext).congr fun z =>
+    BobIO.outputWord_layout outLen (vals z) (pairs z) (outs (N z).length) (hrange z)
+  simpa only [vals] using hout
+
+end TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof.BobProgram

+import TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.Defs
+import TCSlib.Complexity.Randomized.Closure

+set_option maxHeartbeats 0
+set_option relaxedAutoImplicit false
+set_option autoImplicit false

+/-!
+# A polynomial-time Bob simulator from generic fake-garbling interfaces
+
+Two fixed polynomial-time word maps supply a fake garbled message and the active
+oblivious-transfer answers. The simulator places them in Bob's encoded view, retaining
+his input and an empty private tape. Its machine and coin polynomial are fixed before
+any distinguisher is introduced. The leaf contracts are independent of the construction
+of a particular fake garbling.
+
+## Main definitions
+
+* `BobSimulator.raw`: the total word map assembling the simulated view.
+* `BobSimulator.simulator`: the uniform machine with a supplied coin polynomial.
+
+## Main results
+
+* `BobSimulator.raw_poly`: total polynomial time from the two leaf interfaces.
+* `BobSimulator.simulator_uniform`, `simulator_coins`: uniformity and coin budget.
+* `BobSimulator.simulator_runAtL`, `simulator_runAt`: exact canonical-layout view contracts.
+* `BobSimulator.idealB_eq`: the ideal sampler's exact endpoint, retaining Alice's true output.
+
+## References
+
+* [PS10] R. Pass, a. shelat, *A Course in Cryptography*, lecture notes, 3rd ed., January
+  2010, Theorem 163.7 (Bob's simulator in the OT-hybrid model).
+* [LP09] Y. Lindell, B. Pinkas, *A proof of security of Yao's protocol for two-party
+  computation*, Journal of Cryptology 22(2), 2009, pp. 161–188 (one active key per wire).
+-/
+
+namespace TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof.BobSimulator
+
+open Complexity Turing TCSlib.Cryptography
+
+/-- Assemble Bob's simulated view
+`⟨inParam z, ⟨fst (inArg z), activeAnswers z⟩, [], [fakeMessage z]⟩`.
+The prescribed output is available to the leaves as `snd (inArg z)`.
+[PS10, Theorem 163.7; LP09]
+`raw fakeMessage activeAnswers z = (⟨NUPPT.inParam z, pairEncode (pairFstD (NUPPT.inArg z))
+(activeAnswers z), [], [fakeMessage z]⟩ : View).encode`. -/
+def raw (fakeMessage activeAnswers : List Bool → List Bool) (z : List Bool) : List Bool :=
+  (⟨NUPPT.inParam z, pairEncode (pairFstD (NUPPT.inArg z)) (activeAnswers z),
+    [], [fakeMessage z]⟩ : View).encode
+
+/-- For every word `z`, `raw fakeMessage activeAnswers z` is the encoded view with
+common input `inParam z`, private input `⟨fst (inArg z), activeAnswers z⟩`, empty
+private tape and sole received message `fakeMessage z`.
+`raw fakeMessage activeAnswers z = (⟨NUPPT.inParam z, pairEncode (pairFstD (NUPPT.inArg z))
+(activeAnswers z), [], [fakeMessage z]⟩ : View).encode`. -/
+theorem raw_eq (fakeMessage activeAnswers : List Bool → List Bool) (z : List Bool) :
+    raw fakeMessage activeAnswers z =
+      (⟨NUPPT.inParam z, pairEncode (pairFstD (NUPPT.inArg z)) (activeAnswers z),
+        [], [fakeMessage z]⟩ : View).encode := rfl
+
+/-- For fixed polynomial-time fake-message and active-answer maps, assembling
+Bob's simulated view is polynomial-time on every raw input word.
+[PS10, Theorem 163.7]
+`PolyTimeComputable (raw fakeMessage activeAnswers)`. -/
+theorem raw_poly {fakeMessage activeAnswers : List Bool → List Bool}
+    (hM : PolyTimeComputable fakeMessage) (hA : PolyTimeComputable activeAnswers) :
+    PolyTimeComputable (raw fakeMessage activeAnswers) := by
+  have hnil := polyTimeComputable_const ([] : List Bool)
+  have hy := polyTimeComputable_pairFstD.comp' NUPPT.polyTimeComputable_inArg
+  have hl := NUPPT.polyTimeComputable_inParam.pairEncode (hy.pairEncode hA)
+  have hr := hnil.pairEncode (hM.pairEncode hnil)
+  refine (hl.pairEncode hr).congr (g := raw fakeMessage activeAnswers) fun z => ?_
+  rw [raw_eq, View.encode_mk]
+  simp only [Strategy.encodeMsgs_cons, Strategy.encodeMsgs_nil]
+
+/-- The Bob simulator is the uniform machine for `raw fakeMessage activeAnswers`
+with the supplied coin polynomial. Both leaf maps and the coin polynomial are
+fixed independently of a distinguisher. [PS10, Theorem 163.7]
+`simulator hM hA coins = NUPPT.ofPolyTimeUniform (raw_poly hM hA) coins`. -/
+noncomputable def simulator {fakeMessage activeAnswers : List Bool → List Bool}
+    (hM : PolyTimeComputable fakeMessage) (hA : PolyTimeComputable activeAnswers)
+    (coins : PolyExpr) : NUPPT :=
+  NUPPT.ofPolyTimeUniform (raw_poly hM hA) coins
+
+/-- `simulator hM hA coins = ofPolyTimeUniform (raw_poly hM hA) coins`. -/
+theorem simulator_eq {fakeMessage activeAnswers : List Bool → List Bool}
+    (hM : PolyTimeComputable fakeMessage) (hA : PolyTimeComputable activeAnswers)
+    (coins : PolyExpr) :
+    simulator hM hA coins = NUPPT.ofPolyTimeUniform (raw_poly hM hA) coins := rfl
+
+/-- The fixed-leaf Bob simulator is uniform for every supplied coin polynomial:
+`(simulator hM hA coins).Uniform`. -/
+theorem simulator_uniform {fakeMessage activeAnswers : List Bool → List Bool}
+    (hM : PolyTimeComputable fakeMessage) (hA : PolyTimeComputable activeAnswers)
+    (coins : PolyExpr) : (simulator hM hA coins).Uniform := by
+  rw [simulator_eq]
+  exact NUPPT.ofPolyTimeUniform_uniform (raw_poly hM hA) coins
+
+/-- The Bob simulator keeps the supplied coin polynomial:
+`(simulator hM hA coins).coins = coins`. -/
+theorem simulator_coins {fakeMessage activeAnswers : List Bool → List Bool}
+    (hM : PolyTimeComputable fakeMessage) (hA : PolyTimeComputable activeAnswers)
+    (coins : PolyExpr) : (simulator hM hA coins).coins = coins :=
+  (congrArg NUPPT.coins (simulator_eq hM hA coins)).trans
+    (NUPPT.coins_ofPolyTimeUniform (raw_poly hM hA) coins)
+
+/-- On parameter `n`, private input `y`, prescribed output `f`, and any tape `r`,
+the simulator returns Bob's view with the leaves evaluated at the canonical word
+`NUPPT.layout (1^n) ⟨y,f⟩ [] r`. Its private tape field is empty.
+[PS10, Theorem 163.7; LP09]
+`(simulator hM hA coins).runAtL n (pairEncode y f) r = let w := NUPPT.layout (List.replicate n true)
+(pairEncode y f) [] r; (⟨List.replicate n true, pairEncode y (activeAnswers w), [], [fakeMessage w]⟩
+: View).encode`. -/
+theorem simulator_runAtL {fakeMessage activeAnswers : List Bool → List Bool}
+    (hM : PolyTimeComputable fakeMessage) (hA : PolyTimeComputable activeAnswers)
+    (coins : PolyExpr) (n : ℕ) (y f r : List Bool) :
+    (simulator hM hA coins).runAtL n (pairEncode y f) r =
+      let w := NUPPT.layout (List.replicate n true) (pairEncode y f) [] r
+      (⟨List.replicate n true, pairEncode y (activeAnswers w),
+        [], [fakeMessage w]⟩ : View).encode := by
+  rw [simulator_eq, NUPPT.runAtL_ofPolyTimeUniform, raw_eq]
+  simp only [NUPPT.inParam_eq, NUPPT.inArg_eq, NUPPT.advInput_eq, NUPPT.layout_eq,
+    pairFstD_pairEncode, pairSndD_pairEncode]
+
+/-- On a typed simulator tape `r`, the view contract uses the canonical layout
+with coin word `List.ofFn r`; the actual tape field in Bob's view remains empty.
+[PS10, Theorem 163.7]
+`(simulator hM hA coins).runAt n (pairEncode y f) r = let w := NUPPT.layout (List.replicate n true)
+(pairEncode y f) [] (List.ofFn r); (⟨List.replicate n true, pairEncode y (activeAnswers w), [],
+[fakeMessage w]⟩ : View).encode`. -/
+theorem simulator_runAt {fakeMessage activeAnswers : List Bool → List Bool}
+    (hM : PolyTimeComputable fakeMessage) (hA : PolyTimeComputable activeAnswers)
+    (coins : PolyExpr) (n : ℕ) (y f : List Bool) (r : (simulator hM hA coins).Coins n) :
+    (simulator hM hA coins).runAt n (pairEncode y f) r =
+      let w := NUPPT.layout (List.replicate n true) (pairEncode y f) [] (List.ofFn r)
+      (⟨List.replicate n true, pairEncode y (activeAnswers w),
+        [], [fakeMessage w]⟩ : View).encode := by
+  rw [NUPPT.runAt_eq, simulator_runAtL]
+
+/-- Bob's ideal sampler is the simulated view from input `y` and prescribed output
+`(F.f n x y).2`, paired with the actual Alice output `(F.f n x y).1`. The leaves
+receive only the canonical layout of Bob's input, prescribed output and simulator coins.
+[PS10, Theorem 163.7]
+`F.idealB (simulator hM hA coins) n x y r = let w := NUPPT.layout (List.replicate n true)
+(pairEncode (List.ofFn y) (List.ofFn (F.f n x y).2)) [] (List.ofFn r); pairEncode (⟨List.replicate n
+true, pairEncode (List.ofFn y) (activeAnswers w), [], [fakeMessage w]⟩ : View).encode (List.ofFn
+(F.f n x y).1)`.
+
+This is an endpoint identity, independent of any distinguisher or hybrid argument. -/
+theorem idealB_eq {fakeMessage activeAnswers : List Bool → List Bool}
+    (hM : PolyTimeComputable fakeMessage) (hA : PolyTimeComputable activeAnswers)
+    (coins : PolyExpr) (F : Functionality) (n : ℕ)
+    (x : Fin (F.inA.eval n) → Bool) (y : Fin (F.inB.eval n) → Bool)
+    (r : (simulator hM hA coins).Coins n) :
+    F.idealB (simulator hM hA coins) n x y r =
+      let w := NUPPT.layout (List.replicate n true)
+        (pairEncode (List.ofFn y) (List.ofFn (F.f n x y).2)) [] (List.ofFn r)
+      pairEncode
+        (⟨List.replicate n true, pairEncode (List.ofFn y) (activeAnswers w),
+          [], [fakeMessage w]⟩ : View).encode
+        (List.ofFn (F.f n x y).1) := by
+  rw [Functionality.idealB_eq, simulator_runAt]
+
+end TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof.BobSimulator

+import TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.Yao.HybridProof.SamplerEndpoints
+import TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.Yao.HybridProof.BobViewShape
+import TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.Yao.HybridProof.OTAnswerWords
+import TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.Yao.HybridProof.SimulationBound

+set_option maxHeartbeats 0
+set_option relaxedAutoImplicit false
+set_option autoImplicit false

+/-!
+# The actual receiver view at the first garbling hybrid
+
+This consumer combines the established view shape, bit-transfer answer words, tape
+normalization and complete-tape relabeling. The caller supplies only protocol and seed
+readers and the first-message payload equation. No protocol or message is defined here.
+
+## Main definitions
+
+None.
+
+## Main results
+
+* `acceptProb_eq_stage0`: the actual view paired with any prescribed sender output has
+  exactly the acceptance probability of the first garbling hybrid.
+
+## References
+
+* [PS10] R. Pass, a. shelat, *A Course in Cryptography*, lecture notes, 3rd ed., January
+  2010, Protocol 162.6 and Theorem 163.7 (receiver-view coin relabeling).
+-/
+
+namespace TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof.BobStageZero
+
+open Complexity Turing BoolCircuit TCSlib.Cryptography
+
+variable {inputs : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit inputs) (S n : ℕ)
+  (hA : ∀ i : Fin C.gates.length, (C.gates[i]).args.length ≤ 2) (v : ℕ → Bool)
+  (hS : C.size ≤ S)
+  (seed : List Bool → GarbleSeed C (Fin n → Bool) ((Fin n → Bool) × (Fin n → Bool)))
+  (hK : ∀ q (w : Fin C.size) b (t : Fin n),
+    (seed q).1 w b t = q.getD ((b.toNat * S + w) * n + t) false)
+  (hR₀ : ∀ q (i : Fin C.gates.length) (a : Fin (C.gates[i]).args.length → Bool)
+      (t : Fin n),
+    ((seed q).2.1 i a).1 t = q.getD (2 * S * n + (4 * i +
+      (List.ofFn a).foldr (fun b k => b.toNat + 2 * k) 0) * (2 * n) + t) false)
+  (hR₁ : ∀ q (i : Fin C.gates.length) (a : Fin (C.gates[i]).args.length → Bool)
+      (t : Fin n),
+    ((seed q).2.1 i a).2 t = q.getD (2 * S * n + (4 * i +
+      (List.ofFn a).foldr (fun b k => b.toNat + 2 * k) 0) * (2 * n) + n + t) false)
+  (hP : ∀ q (i : Fin C.gates.length) (a : Fin (C.gates[i]).args.length → Bool)
+      (t : Fin (C.gates[i]).args.length),
+    (seed q).2.2 i a t = xor (a t) (q.getD (10 * S * n + 2 * i + t) false))
+  (M : MultiEnc (Fin n → Bool) (List Bool) ((Fin n → Bool) × (Fin n → Bool)))
+  (inA inB outLen : ℕ) (output : Fin outLen → ℕ)
+  (H : HybridProtocol otFunctionality)
+  (first : ℕ → List Bool → List Bool → List Bool)
+  (hRounds : H.main.rounds = .const 2) (hFirst : H.main.verifierFirst = false)
+  (hBobCoins : H.main.V.coins = .const 0)
+  (hSender : ∀ w z r, H.main.P.next w z r [] = first w.length (pairFstD z) r)
+  (xs ys aliceOutput : List Bool)
+  (hLen : H.main.P.coinLen (List.replicate n true).length = 10 * S * n + 2 * S)
+  (hCalls : H.calls.eval n = inB * n)
+  (hvB : ∀ i : Fin inB, v (inA + i) = ys.getD i false)
+  (hpoint : ∀ rA rB (i : Fin inB) (t : Fin n),
+    (H.answersB n xs ys rA rB).getD (i * n + t) false =
+      keyAt C (seed rA) (inA + i) (ys.getD i false) t)
+  (hMessage : ∀ r, first n xs r =
+    pairEncode (BinArith.encWords (List.ofFn fun i =>
+      BinArith.encWords (garbledTable M C (seed r) i)))
+      (pairEncode
+        (BinArith.encWords (List.ofFn fun w : Fin inA =>
+          List.ofFn (keyAt C (seed r) w (v w))))
+        (BinArith.encWords (List.ofFn fun j =>
+          pairEncode (List.ofFn (keyAt C (seed r) (output j) false))
+            (List.ofFn (keyAt C (seed r) (output j) true))))))
+
+include hA hS hK hR₀ hR₁ hP hRounds hFirst hBobCoins hSender hLen hCalls hvB hpoint hMessage
+
+/-- If an Alice-first two-move bit-transfer hybrid has a zero-coin receiver, the supplied
+payload and addressed seed readers, then its actual encoded receiver view paired with
+`aliceOutput` has exactly the acceptance probability of stage zero over `10*S*n+2*S` bits.
+The receiver's selected wire values agree with `ys`, and no positivity premise is needed.
+[PS10, Protocol 162.6 and Theorem 163.7]
+`acceptProb (fun r : H.Coins n => pairEncode (H.main.viewV (List.replicate n true) (H.privA n xs ys
+(List.ofFn r.1) (List.ofFn r.2)) (H.privB n xs ys (List.ofFn r.1) (List.ofFn r.2)) r).encode
+aliceOutput) D = acceptProb (fun r : Fin (10 * S * n + 2 * S) → Bool => GateHybridMessage.sample M C
+(seed (List.ofFn r)) inA inB outLen output v (List.replicate n true) ys [] aliceOutput 0) D`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** Form a word sampler over the full normalized first tape, using the
+supplied first-message function and the answers selected by the wire values. The answer
+reader and bit-transfer call count identify the actual answer word with the active keys
+under the relabeled seed. Read that seed through the existing active-answer relabeling
+formula. The two-message view contract then gives the actual receiver view, including its
+empty receiver tape. Normalize the first tape using the supplied length equality; its
+word is preserved, so the existing first-tape probability contract drops the unused second
+tape. Finally replace the first message by its supplied payload equation and invoke the
+established complete-tape relabeling endpoint. Thus both probability changes use existing
+uniform transport lemmas rather than any new summation or re-randomization argument. -/
+theorem acceptProb_eq_stage0 {κ : Type*} [Fintype κ]
+    (D : List Bool → κ → Bool) :
+    acceptProb (fun r : H.Coins n => pairEncode
+      (H.main.viewV (List.replicate n true)
+        (H.privA n xs ys (List.ofFn r.1) (List.ofFn r.2))
+        (H.privB n xs ys (List.ofFn r.1) (List.ofFn r.2)) r).encode aliceOutput) D =
+    acceptProb (fun r : Fin (10 * S * n + 2 * S) → Bool =>
+      GateHybridMessage.sample M C (seed (List.ofFn r)) inA inB outLen output v
+        (List.replicate n true) ys [] aliceOutput 0) D := by
+  let real := fun r : Fin (10 * S * n + 2 * S) → Bool =>
+    pairEncode (⟨List.replicate n true,
+      pairEncode ys (List.ofFn fun t : Fin (inB * n) =>
+        keyAt C (seed (List.ofFn r)) (inA + t.divNat) (v (inA + t.divNat)) t.modNat), [],
+      [first n xs (List.ofFn r)]⟩ : View).encode aliceOutput
+  have hView (r : H.Coins n) :
+      (H.main.viewV (List.replicate n true)
+        (H.privA n xs ys (List.ofFn r.1) (List.ofFn r.2))
+        (H.privB n xs ys (List.ofFn r.1) (List.ofFn r.2)) r).encode =
+      (⟨List.replicate n true,
+        pairEncode ys (List.ofFn fun t : Fin (inB * n) =>
+          keyAt C (seed (List.ofFn r.1)) (inA + t.divNat) (v (inA + t.divNat)) t.modNat), [],
+        [first n xs (List.ofFn r.1)]⟩ : View).encode := by
+    have hAns := OTAnswerWords.answersB_eq_active H n xs ys
+      (List.ofFn r.1) (List.ofFn r.2) C (seed (List.ofFn r.1)) inA inB v hCalls hvB
+      (hpoint (List.ofFn r.1) (List.ofFn r.2))
+    rw [BobViewShape.encode_viewV_eq H first hRounds hFirst hBobCoins hSender,
+      hAns, GateHybridMessage.activeAnswers_relabel]
+  trans acceptProb real D
+  · apply acceptProb_firstTape H n _ hLen _ real _ D
+    intro r
+    simp only [hView, real, strategyTapeEquiv_ofFn]
+  · apply SamplerEndpoints.acceptProb_real_eq_stage0 C S n hA v hS seed hK hR₀ hR₁ hP M
+      inA inB outLen output (List.replicate n true) ys [] aliceOutput real _ D
+    intro r
+    simp only [real, hMessage]
+
+end TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof.BobStageZero

+/-!
+# Statistical switching of the receiver's paired output
+
+For any hybrid protocol, an event on Alice's bit tape can control replacement
+of the outer Alice output in Bob's real experiment. Bob's actual complete
+encoded view is retained. The canonical tape probability is transported by
+the existing strategy-tape equivalence and independent-tape marginal reader.
+
+## Main definitions
+
+None.
+
+## Main results
+
+* `statClose_real_output_switch`: an off-bad Alice-output equation gives the
+  statistical bound uniformly in the parameter and private inputs.
+
+## References
+
+* [PS10] R. Pass, a. shelat, *A Course in Cryptography*, lecture notes, 3rd ed.,
+  January 2010, Theorem 163.7 (statistical correctness switch for Bob's view).
+-/
+
+namespace TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof.BobStatistical
+
+open Complexity Turing TCSlib.Cryptography
+
+/-- If Alice's actual output is her functionality output outside an event on
+her tape of probability at most `δ(n)`, Bob's real experiment is `δ(n)`-close
+to his unchanged actual encoded view paired with that functionality output.
+The tape bound is stated on the canonical polynomial-length bit tape, and
+Bob's independent tape can have any length. [PS10, Theorem 163.7]
+`∀ n x y, StatClose (H.realB F n x y) (fun r => pairEncode (H.main.viewV (List.replicate n true)
+(H.privA n (List.ofFn x) (List.ofFn y) (List.ofFn r.1) (List.ofFn r.2)) (H.privB n (List.ofFn x)
+(List.ofFn y) (List.ofFn r.1) (List.ofFn r.2)) r).encode (List.ofFn (F.f n x y).1)) (δ n)`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** Use the sender's coin-polynomial equation to normalize her
+actual tape to the canonical bit tape without changing its word. Pull back
+the supplied bad-event bound through that equivalence and drop the independent
+Bob tape by the existing marginal equation. The published real-output switch
+then applies directly with the supplied off-bad output equality. No new
+probability inequality, simulator or protocol definition is introduced. -/
+theorem statClose_real_output_switch {G : Functionality} (H : HybridProtocol G)
+    (F : Functionality) (p : PolyExpr) (hCoins : H.main.P.coins = p)
+    (BadWord : ℕ → List Bool → Prop) (δ : ℕ → ℝ)
+    (hBad : ∀ n, uprob (fun r : Fin (p.eval n) → Bool => BadWord n (List.ofFn r)) ≤ δ n)
+    (hCorrect : ∀ n x y (r : H.Coins n), ¬ BadWord n (List.ofFn r.1) →
+      H.main.outP (List.replicate n true)
+        (H.privA n (List.ofFn x) (List.ofFn y) (List.ofFn r.1) (List.ofFn r.2))
+        (H.privB n (List.ofFn x) (List.ofFn y) (List.ofFn r.1) (List.ofFn r.2)) r =
+          List.ofFn (F.f n x y).1) :
+    ∀ n x y, StatClose (H.realB F n x y)
+      (fun r => pairEncode
+        (H.main.viewV (List.replicate n true)
+          (H.privA n (List.ofFn x) (List.ofFn y) (List.ofFn r.1) (List.ofFn r.2))
+          (H.privB n (List.ofFn x) (List.ofFn y) (List.ofFn r.1) (List.ofFn r.2)) r).encode
+        (List.ofFn (F.f n x y).1)) (δ n) := by
+  intro n x y
+  refine realB_output_switch H F n x y
+    (fun r => BadWord n (List.ofFn r.1)) (δ n) ?_ (hCorrect n x y)
+  rw [uprob_fst (β := H.main.V.Coins (List.replicate n true).length)
+    (fun r : H.main.P.Coins (List.replicate n true).length => BadWord n (List.ofFn r))]
+  have hLen : H.main.P.coinLen (List.replicate n true).length = p.eval n := by
+    rw [Strategy.coinLen_eq, hCoins, List.length_replicate]
+  have h := hBad n
+  rw [← uprob_comp_equiv (strategyTapeEquiv
+    H.main.P (List.replicate n true).length (p.eval n) hLen)] at h
+  simpa only [strategyTapeEquiv_ofFn] using h
+
+end TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof.BobStatistical

+/-!
+# A total row-table selector for the garbled-evaluation host
+
+The host retains input `x` and the encoded current vertex values. The selectors
+`m x` and `msg x` provide an input-vertex count in unary and an arbitrary message.
+Counting the current values gives the current vertex. A guarded unary subtraction
+selects its table, with word-list normalization before either public lookup.
+
+## Main definitions
+
+* `BobTables.tableSelector`: the raw-word table selector for the generic evaluation host.
+
+## Main results
+
+* `BobTables.tableSelector_poly`: polynomial time on every host word.
+* `BobTables.tableSelector_host`: its exact equation on a canonical host state.
+
+## References
+
+* [PS10] R. Pass, a. shelat, *A Course in Cryptography*, lecture notes, 3rd ed.,
+  January 2010, Protocol 162.6 (reading a garbled table).
+-/
+
+namespace TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof.BobTables
+
+open Complexity Turing
+
+/-- At `⟨x, ⟨gateRecord, values⟩⟩`, count the vertex values. If their count `v`
+is at least `|m x|`, select table `v - |m x|` from the message's first component
+and normalize its rows; otherwise return the empty encoded list.
+`tableSelector m msg w = let x := pairFstD w; let v := (BinArith.decWords (pairSndD (pairSndD
+w))).length; if (m x).length ≤ v then BinArith.encWords (BinArith.decWords (BinArith.getW
+(pairEncode (List.replicate (v - (m x).length) true) (BinArith.encWords (BinArith.decWords (pairFstD
+(msg x))))))) else []`. -/
+def tableSelector (m msg : List Bool → List Bool) (w : List Bool) : List Bool :=
+  let x := pairFstD w
+  let v := (BinArith.decWords (pairSndD (pairSndD w))).length
+  if (m x).length ≤ v then
+    BinArith.encWords (BinArith.decWords (BinArith.getW
+      (pairEncode (List.replicate (v - (m x).length) true)
+        (BinArith.encWords (BinArith.decWords (pairFstD (msg x)))))))
+  else []
+
+/-- For every `w`, `tableSelector m msg w` compares the input-count word's
+length with the current decoded-value count and normalizes the selected row table.
+`tableSelector m msg w = let x := pairFstD w; let v := (BinArith.decWords (pairSndD (pairSndD
+w))).length; if (m x).length ≤ v then BinArith.encWords (BinArith.decWords (BinArith.getW
+(pairEncode (List.replicate (v - (m x).length) true) (BinArith.encWords (BinArith.decWords (pairFstD
+(msg x))))))) else []`. -/
+theorem tableSelector_eq (m msg : List Bool → List Bool) (w : List Bool) :
+    tableSelector m msg w =
+      let x := pairFstD w
+      let v := (BinArith.decWords (pairSndD (pairSndD w))).length
+      if (m x).length ≤ v then
+        BinArith.encWords (BinArith.decWords (BinArith.getW
+          (pairEncode (List.replicate (v - (m x).length) true)
+            (BinArith.encWords (BinArith.decWords (pairFstD (msg x)))))))
+      else [] := rfl
+
+/-- For polynomial-time selectors `m` and `msg`, `tableSelector m msg` is
+polynomial-time on every input word, including malformed messages and states.
+`PolyTimeComputable (tableSelector m msg)`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** Count the current encoded values and compute the unary input
+count. The existing unary subtraction gives their index difference. Normalize
+the message's table list, use public word lookup at this index, and normalize
+the selected rows. A polynomial-time length comparison guards the lookup result. -/
+theorem tableSelector_poly {m msg : List Bool → List Bool}
+    (hm : PolyTimeComputable m) (hmsg : PolyTimeComputable msg) :
+    PolyTimeComputable (tableSelector m msg) := by
+  have hvraw := BinArith.polyTimeComputable_countW.comp
+    (polyTimeComputable_pairSndD.comp polyTimeComputable_pairSndD)
+  have hv : PolyTimeComputable fun w =>
+      List.replicate (BinArith.decWords (pairSndD (pairSndD w))).length true :=
+    hvraw.congr (fun w => by simp only [Function.comp_apply, BinArith.countW_eq])
+  have hmraw := hm.comp polyTimeComputable_pairFstD
+  have hmlen := polyTimeComputable_unary.comp hmraw
+  have hindex := EncodingMachines.unarySub_poly hv hmlen
+  have htables := polyTimeComputable_reencodeWords.comp
+    (polyTimeComputable_pairFstD.comp (hmsg.comp polyTimeComputable_pairFstD))
+  have hrows := polyTimeComputable_reencodeWords.comp
+    (BinArith.polyTimeComputable_getW.comp (hindex.pairEncode htables))
+  have htest := polyTimeComputable_lenLe.comp (hv.pairEncode hmraw)
+  have h := polyTimeComputable_ite htest hrows (polyTimeComputable_const [])
+  convert h using 1
+  funext w
+  simp only [tableSelector_eq, Function.comp_apply, pairFstD_pairEncode,
+    pairSndD_pairEncode, List.length_replicate, decide_eq_true_eq]
+
+/-- On `⟨x, ⟨gate, encWords (vs.map code)⟩⟩`, the selector returns the
+encoded rows of message table `|vs| - |m x|` when `|m x| ≤ |vs|`, and the
+empty encoded row list otherwise. The gate-record payload is immaterial. -/
+theorem tableSelector_host {α : Type} (m msg : List Bool → List Bool)
+    (x gate : List Bool) (vs : List α) (code : α → List Bool) :
+    tableSelector m msg (pairEncode x
+        (pairEncode gate (BinArith.encWords (vs.map code)))) =
+      BinArith.encWords (if (m x).length ≤ vs.length then
+        BinArith.decWords ((BinArith.decWords (pairFstD (msg x))).getD
+          (vs.length - (m x).length) []) else []) := by
+  rw [tableSelector_eq]
+  simp only [pairFstD_pairEncode, pairSndD_pairEncode, BinArith.decWords_encWords,
+    List.length_map, BinArith.getW_pairEncode, List.length_replicate]
+  split_ifs <;> simp only [BinArith.encWords_nil]
+
+end TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof.BobTables

+/-!
+# The exact Bob-view shape of an OT-hybrid two-message protocol
+
+A deterministic Bob receives only Alice's first main-phase message. His complete private
+input contains his original input and the ideal OT answers. This interface extracts that
+encoded view from the existing two-message view contract, with arbitrary raw input words.
+
+## Main definitions
+
+None.
+
+## Main results
+
+* `BobViewShape.encode_viewV_eq`: the actual encoded view has the canonical hybrid shape.
+
+## References
+
+* [PS10] R. Pass, a. shelat, *A Course in Cryptography*, lecture notes, 3rd ed., January
+  2010, Protocol 162.6 and Theorem 163.7 (Bob's OT-hybrid view).
+-/
+
+namespace TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof.BobViewShape
+
+open Complexity Turing TCSlib.Cryptography
+
+/-- In an Alice-first two-message OT-hybrid protocol with a zero-coin Bob, if Alice's
+empty-history message is `first |w| (fst zA) rA`, then Bob's actual encoded view is
+`View.encode ⟨1^n, ⟨ys, H.answersB ...⟩, [], [first n xs rA]⟩`. The inputs `xs` and `ys`
+are arbitrary raw words, and no hypothesis on Bob's reply is required.
+[PS10, Protocol 162.6 and Theorem 163.7]
+`(H.main.viewV (List.replicate n true) (H.privA n xs ys (List.ofFn r.1) (List.ofFn r.2)) (H.privB n
+xs ys (List.ofFn r.1) (List.ofFn r.2)) r).encode = (⟨List.replicate n true, pairEncode ys
+(H.answersB n xs ys (List.ofFn r.1) (List.ofFn r.2)), [], [first n xs (List.ofFn r.1)]⟩ :
+View).encode`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** The public coin-length equation turns Bob's constant-zero coin polynomial
+into a zero-length tape, whose list encoding is empty. Normalize the unary parameter length
+only inside the nondependent message function, by congruence, so no typed execution tape
+is transported. Instantiate the existing two-message encoded-view contract with Alice's
+supplied sender equation and Bob's actual next function on a singleton history. The latter
+satisfies its reply equation reflexively. Finally read the hybrid private-input equations:
+Alice's first projection is `xs`, and Bob's private input pairs `ys` with his ideal answers. -/
+theorem encode_viewV_eq (H : HybridProtocol otFunctionality)
+    (first : ℕ → List Bool → List Bool → List Bool)
+    (hRounds : H.main.rounds = .const 2) (hFirst : H.main.verifierFirst = false)
+    (hBobCoins : H.main.V.coins = .const 0)
+    (hSender : ∀ w z r, H.main.P.next w z r [] = first w.length (pairFstD z) r)
+    (n : ℕ) (xs ys : List Bool) (r : H.Coins n) :
+    (H.main.viewV (List.replicate n true)
+      (H.privA n xs ys (List.ofFn r.1) (List.ofFn r.2))
+      (H.privB n xs ys (List.ofFn r.1) (List.ofFn r.2)) r).encode =
+      (⟨List.replicate n true,
+        pairEncode ys (H.answersB n xs ys (List.ofFn r.1) (List.ofFn r.2)), [],
+        [first n xs (List.ofFn r.1)]⟩ : View).encode := by
+  have hBob : List.ofFn r.2 = [] := by
+    apply List.eq_nil_of_length_eq_zero
+    rw [List.length_ofFn]
+    rw [Strategy.coinLen_eq, hBobCoins, PolyExpr.eval_const]
+  have hMsg : first (List.replicate n true).length xs (List.ofFn r.1) =
+      first n xs (List.ofFn r.1) :=
+    congrArg (fun k => first k xs (List.ofFn r.1))
+      (List.length_replicate (n := n) (a := true))
+  rw [TwoMoveViews.encode_viewV_eq H.main
+    (fun w z r => first w.length (pairFstD z) r)
+    (fun w z r a => H.main.V.next w z r [a]) hRounds hFirst hSender
+    (fun _ _ _ _ => rfl)]
+  simp only [HybridProtocol.pairFstD_privA, hMsg, hBob, HybridProtocol.privB_eq,
+    View.encode_mk]
+
+end TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof.BobViewShape

+/-!
+# The bit-OT input machines and a product call index
+
+The input machines use a flat unary call index, while a circuit protocol labels a call
+by an input-wire index and a key-bit index. The public finite-product equivalence relates
+these forms. The contracts below take the circuit protocol's input-bit equations as
+hypotheses, without referring to any concrete protocol or seed definition.
+
+## Main definitions
+
+None.
+
+## Main results
+
+* `CallEquations.inputA_eq`: the two sender bits agree with the flat coin-reading machine.
+* `CallEquations.inputB_eq`: the receiver bit agrees with the unary-quotient machine.
+
+## References
+
+* [PS10] R. Pass, a. shelat, *A Course in Cryptography*, lecture notes, 3rd ed.,
+  January 2010, Protocol 162.6 (one ideal bit transfer per bit of each input-wire key).
+-/
+
+namespace TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof.CallEquations
+
+open Complexity Turing
+
+/-- If call `(i,t)` reads its two bits at `(b S(n) + A(n) + i) n + t`,
+then `otInputA A S ⟨1^n, ⟨x, ⟨r, 1^j⟩⟩⟩ = List.ofFn (call j)`.
+This includes `n = 0`, when there is no call index. [PS10, Protocol 162.6] -/
+theorem inputA_eq (A S : PolyExpr) (n m : ℕ) (x r : List Bool)
+    (call : Fin (m * n) → Fin 2 → Bool)
+    (hcall : ∀ (i : Fin m) (t : Fin n) (c : Fin 2),
+      call (finProdFinEquiv (i, t)) c =
+        r.getD (((decide ((c : ℕ) = 1)).toNat * S.eval n + A.eval n + i) * n + t) false)
+    (j : Fin (m * n)) :
+    OTInputs.otInputA A S (pairEncode (List.replicate n true)
+      (pairEncode x (pairEncode r (List.replicate j true)))) = List.ofFn (call j) := by
+  obtain ⟨⟨i, t⟩, rfl⟩ :=
+    (finProdFinEquiv (m := m) (n := n)).surjective j
+  rw [OTInputs.otInputA_layout]
+  norm_num only [List.ofFn_succ, List.ofFn_zero, hcall, finProdFinEquiv_apply_val,
+    Fin.val_zero, Fin.val_succ, decide_false, decide_true, Bool.toNat_false, Bool.toNat_true]
+  congr 2 <;> ring
+
+/-- If call `(i,t)` chooses `x[i]`, then the receiver's unary-quotient machine returns
+that call's singleton bit: `otInputB ⟨1^n, ⟨x, ⟨r, 1^j⟩⟩⟩ = List.ofFn (call j)`.
+This includes `n = 0`, when there is no call index. [PS10, Protocol 162.6] -/
+theorem inputB_eq (n m : ℕ) (x r : List Bool)
+    (call : Fin (m * n) → Fin 1 → Bool)
+    (hcall : ∀ (i : Fin m) (t : Fin n) (c : Fin 1),
+      call (finProdFinEquiv (i, t)) c = x.getD i false)
+    (j : Fin (m * n)) :
+    OTInputs.otInputB (pairEncode (List.replicate n true)
+      (pairEncode x (pairEncode r (List.replicate j true)))) = List.ofFn (call j) := by
+  obtain ⟨⟨i, t⟩, rfl⟩ :=
+    (finProdFinEquiv (m := m) (n := n)).surjective j
+  rw [OTInputs.otInputB_layout, finProdFinEquiv_apply_val]
+  have ht := t.isLt
+  have hdiv : ((t : ℕ) + n * i) / n = i := by
+    rw [Nat.add_mul_div_left _ _ (by omega), Nat.div_eq_of_lt ht, Nat.zero_add]
+  rw [hdiv]
+  simp only [List.ofFn_succ, List.ofFn_zero, hcall]
+
+end TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof.CallEquations

+import TCSlib.Complexity.ClassP.BinaryArith.Words

+set_option autoImplicit false

+/-!
+# Finite scanners with word-valued emissions
+
+This construction extends the existing one-bit transducer to a bounded word per input bit.
+It uses the verified emitting loop, with a three-bit control and a shrinking input suffix.
+The circuit-description decoder uses its seven control phases.
+
+## Main definitions
+
+* `ChunkScanner.Phase`: three bits of finite control.
+* `ChunkScanner.scan`: concatenate the emitted words while advancing the control.
+
+## Main results
+
+* `ChunkScanner.scan_poly`: every such scanner is polynomial-time.
+
+## References
+
+Generic parsing infrastructure for [AB09, §6.2], circuit descriptions. The machine assembly
+uses the emitting-loop host of `Complexity.polyTimeComputable_emitIter`.
+-/
+
+namespace TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof.ChunkScanner
+
+open Complexity Turing
+
+/-- The scanner has three bits of finite control: `Phase = Bool × Bool × Bool`. -/
+abbrev Phase := Bool × Bool × Bool
+
+/-- The phase code is the three-bit word `[q.1, q.2.1, q.2.2]`.
+`phaseCode q = [q.1, q.2.1, q.2.2]`. -/
+def phaseCode (q : Phase) : List Bool := [q.1, q.2.1, q.2.2]
+
+/-- `phaseCode q = [q.1, q.2.1, q.2.2]`. -/
+theorem phaseCode_eq (q : Phase) : phaseCode q = [q.1, q.2.1, q.2.2] := rfl
+
+/-- Every phase code has length three: `|phaseCode q| = 3`. -/
+theorem length_phaseCode (q : Phase) : (phaseCode q).length = 3 := rfl
+
+/-- Read the first three bits, defaulting each missing bit to false.
+`readPhase w = (w.headD false, (w.drop 1).headD false, (w.drop 2).headD false)`. -/
+def readPhase (w : List Bool) : Phase :=
+  (w.headD false, (w.drop 1).headD false, (w.drop 2).headD false)
+
+/-- `readPhase w = (head w, head (drop 1 w), head (drop 2 w))`, with false defaults. -/
+theorem readPhase_eq (w : List Bool) :
+    readPhase w = (w.headD false, (w.drop 1).headD false, (w.drop 2).headD false) := rfl
+
+/-- Reading the phase code recovers the phase: `readPhase (phaseCode q) = q`. -/
+theorem readPhase_code (q : Phase) : readPhase (phaseCode q) = q := rfl
+
+/-- Concatenate the word `o q b` emitted at each input bit, changing control by `δ q b`.
+`scan δ o q [] = []` and `scan δ o q (b :: w) = o q b ++ scan δ o (δ q b) w`. -/
+def scan (δ : Phase → Bool → Phase) (o : Phase → Bool → List Bool) :
+    Phase → List Bool → List Bool
+  | _, [] => []
+  | q, b :: w => o q b ++ scan δ o (δ q b) w
+
+/-- `scan δ o q [] = []`. -/
+theorem scan_nil (δ : Phase → Bool → Phase) (o : Phase → Bool → List Bool) (q : Phase) :
+    scan δ o q [] = [] := rfl
+
+/-- `scan δ o q (b :: w) = o q b ++ scan δ o (δ q b) w`. -/
+theorem scan_cons (δ : Phase → Bool → Phase) (o : Phase → Bool → List Bool) (q : Phase)
+    (b : Bool) (w : List Bool) :
+    scan δ o q (b :: w) = o q b ++ scan δ o (δ q b) w := rfl
+
+/-- A scanner round stores the next three-bit control and removes one input bit.
+`step δ w = pairEncode (phaseCode (δ (readPhase (pairFstD w)) ((pairSndD w).headD false)))
+((pairSndD w).drop 1)`. -/
+def step (δ : Phase → Bool → Phase) (w : List Bool) : List Bool :=
+  pairEncode (phaseCode (δ (readPhase (pairFstD w)) ((pairSndD w).headD false)))
+    ((pairSndD w).drop 1)
+
+/-- `step δ w` pairs the next phase code with `(pairSndD w).drop 1`.
+`step δ w = pairEncode (phaseCode (δ (readPhase (pairFstD w)) ((pairSndD w).headD false)))
+((pairSndD w).drop 1)`. -/
+theorem step_eq (δ : Phase → Bool → Phase) (w : List Bool) :
+    step δ w =
+      pairEncode (phaseCode (δ (readPhase (pairFstD w)) ((pairSndD w).headD false)))
+        ((pairSndD w).drop 1) := rfl
+
+/-- A scanner emits nothing on an empty suffix, and otherwise emits `o q (head suffix)`.
+`emit o w = if pairSndD w = [] then [] else o (readPhase (pairFstD w)) ((pairSndD w).headD
+false)`. -/
+def emit (o : Phase → Bool → List Bool) (w : List Bool) : List Bool :=
+  if pairSndD w = [] then [] else o (readPhase (pairFstD w)) ((pairSndD w).headD false)
+
+/-- `emit o w` is empty on an empty second component and otherwise is the table entry.
+`emit o w = if pairSndD w = [] then [] else o (readPhase (pairFstD w)) ((pairSndD w).headD
+false)`. -/
+theorem emit_eq (o : Phase → Bool → List Bool) (w : List Bool) :
+    emit o w =
+      if pairSndD w = [] then []
+      else o (readPhase (pairFstD w)) ((pairSndD w).headD false) := rfl
+
+/-- A fixed table of four polynomial-time Boolean tests is polynomial-time.
+
+**Proof sketch.** Branch on the four bits in turn using the existing polynomial-time
+conditional combinator. Each of the sixteen leaves is the corresponding constant word;
+case analysis on the four bits identifies the resulting decision tree with the table. -/
+private theorem table4_poly (F : Bool → Bool → Bool → Bool → List Bool)
+    {a b c d : List Bool → Bool}
+    (ha : PolyTimeComputable fun w => [a w])
+    (hb : PolyTimeComputable fun w => [b w])
+    (hc : PolyTimeComputable fun w => [c w])
+    (hd : PolyTimeComputable fun w => [d w]) :
+    PolyTimeComputable fun w => F (a w) (b w) (c w) (d w) := by
+  convert (polyTimeComputable_ite ha
+    (polyTimeComputable_ite hb
+    (polyTimeComputable_ite hc
+    (polyTimeComputable_ite hd
+    (polyTimeComputable_const (F true true true true))
+    (polyTimeComputable_const (F true true true false)))
+    (polyTimeComputable_ite hd
+    (polyTimeComputable_const (F true true false true))
+    (polyTimeComputable_const (F true true false false))))
+    (polyTimeComputable_ite hc
+    (polyTimeComputable_ite hd
+    (polyTimeComputable_const (F true false true true))
+    (polyTimeComputable_const (F true false true false)))
+    (polyTimeComputable_ite hd
+    (polyTimeComputable_const (F true false false true))
+    (polyTimeComputable_const (F true false false false)))))
+    (polyTimeComputable_ite hb
+    (polyTimeComputable_ite hc
+    (polyTimeComputable_ite hd
+    (polyTimeComputable_const (F false true true true))
+    (polyTimeComputable_const (F false true true false)))
+    (polyTimeComputable_ite hd
+    (polyTimeComputable_const (F false true false true))
+    (polyTimeComputable_const (F false true false false))))
+    (polyTimeComputable_ite hc
+    (polyTimeComputable_ite hd
+    (polyTimeComputable_const (F false false true true))
+    (polyTimeComputable_const (F false false true false)))
+    (polyTimeComputable_ite hd
+    (polyTimeComputable_const (F false false false true))
+    (polyTimeComputable_const (F false false false false)))))) using 1
+  funext w
+  cases a w <;> cases b w <;> cases c w <;> cases d w <;> rfl
+
+/-- The three-bit phase type is the Boolean triple: `Phase = Bool × Bool × Bool`. -/
+theorem phase_eq : Phase = (Bool × Bool × Bool) := rfl
+
+/-- Each scanner round is polynomial-time: `PolyTimeComputable (step δ)`. -/
+theorem step_poly (δ : Phase → Bool → Phase) : PolyTimeComputable (step δ) := by
+  exact (table4_poly (fun a b c d => phaseCode (δ (a, b, c) d))
+    (polyTimeComputable_headD.comp polyTimeComputable_pairFstD)
+    (polyTimeComputable_headD.comp
+      ((BinArith.polyTimeComputable_drop 1).comp polyTimeComputable_pairFstD))
+    (polyTimeComputable_headD.comp
+      ((BinArith.polyTimeComputable_drop 2).comp polyTimeComputable_pairFstD))
+    (polyTimeComputable_headD.comp polyTimeComputable_pairSndD)).pairEncode
+      (polyTimeComputable_tail.comp polyTimeComputable_pairSndD)
+
+/-- Every finite emission table is polynomial-time: `PolyTimeComputable (emit o)`. -/
+theorem emit_poly (o : Phase → Bool → List Bool) : PolyTimeComputable (emit o) := by
+  convert polyTimeComputable_ite
+    (polyTimeComputable_isNil.comp polyTimeComputable_pairSndD)
+    (polyTimeComputable_const [])
+    (table4_poly (fun a b c d => o (a, b, c) d)
+      (polyTimeComputable_headD.comp polyTimeComputable_pairFstD)
+      (polyTimeComputable_headD.comp
+        ((BinArith.polyTimeComputable_drop 1).comp polyTimeComputable_pairFstD))
+      (polyTimeComputable_headD.comp
+        ((BinArith.polyTimeComputable_drop 2).comp polyTimeComputable_pairFstD))
+      (polyTimeComputable_headD.comp polyTimeComputable_pairSndD)) using 1
+  funext w
+  simp only [emit_eq, readPhase_eq, decide_eq_true_eq, Function.comp_apply]
+
+/-- On a phase/suffix pair the round advances its phase and drops one suffix bit.
+`step δ (pairEncode (phaseCode q) w) = pairEncode (phaseCode (δ q (w.headD false))) (w.drop 1)`. -/
+theorem step_pair (δ : Phase → Bool → Phase) (q : Phase) (w : List Bool) :
+    step δ (pairEncode (phaseCode q) w) =
+      pairEncode (phaseCode (δ q (w.headD false))) (w.drop 1) := by
+  rw [step_eq, pairFstD_pairEncode, pairSndD_pairEncode, readPhase_code]
+
+/-- On a phase/suffix pair emission is empty exactly for the exhausted suffix.
+`emit o (pairEncode (phaseCode q) w) = if w = [] then [] else o q (w.headD false)`. -/
+theorem emit_pair (o : Phase → Bool → List Bool) (q : Phase) (w : List Bool) :
+    emit o (pairEncode (phaseCode q) w) =
+      if w = [] then [] else o q (w.headD false) := by
+  rw [emit_eq, pairFstD_pairEncode, pairSndD_pairEncode, readPhase_code]
+
+/-- The input suffix does not grow in one round:
+`|pairSndD (step δ w)| ≤ |pairSndD w|`. -/
+theorem length_snd_step (δ : Phase → Bool → Phase) (w : List Bool) :
+    (pairSndD (step δ w)).length ≤ (pairSndD w).length := by
+  rw [step_eq, pairSndD_pairEncode, List.length_drop]
+  omega
+
+/-- Every iterated suffix stays within the original word:
+`|pairSndD ((step δ)^[i] w)| ≤ |w|`. -/
+theorem length_snd_iterate (δ : Phase → Bool → Phase) (w : List Bool) (i : ℕ) :
+    (pairSndD ((step δ)^[i] w)).length ≤ w.length := by
+  induction i with
+  | zero => exact length_pairSndD_le w
+  | succ i ih =>
+    rw [Function.iterate_succ_apply']
+    exact (length_snd_step δ _).trans ih
+
+/-- Every scanner state has a linear envelope:
+`|((step δ)^[i]) w| ≤ 9 · (|w| + 1)`. -/
+theorem length_iterate (δ : Phase → Bool → Phase) (w : List Bool) (i : ℕ) :
+    ((step δ)^[i] w).length ≤ 9 * (w.length + 1) := by
+  cases i with
+  | zero => simp only [Function.iterate_zero_apply]; omega
+  | succ i =>
+    rw [Function.iterate_succ_apply', step_eq, length_pairEncode, length_phaseCode,
+      List.length_drop]
+    have h := length_snd_iterate δ w i
+    omega
+
+/-- Emitting along at least `|w|` rounds of the encoded scanner yields `scan δ o q w`.
+`(List.range N).flatMap (fun i => emit o ((step δ)^[i] (pairEncode (phaseCode q) w))) = scan δ o q
+w`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** Induct on the round budget. The first round emits the head's table entry,
+then the next state holds the remaining input. Once the suffix is empty all later emissions
+are empty, so any budget at least the input length gives the same result. -/
+theorem flatMap_scan (δ : Phase → Bool → Phase) (o : Phase → Bool → List Bool)
+    (N : ℕ) (q : Phase) (w : List Bool) (hN : w.length ≤ N) :
+    (List.range N).flatMap
+        (fun i => emit o ((step δ)^[i] (pairEncode (phaseCode q) w))) =
+      scan δ o q w := by
+  induction N generalizing q w with
+  | zero =>
+    have hw : w = [] := List.eq_nil_of_length_eq_zero (by omega)
+    subst hw
+    rfl
+  | succ N ih =>
+    -- Peel the first round and run the remaining budget from the next state.
+    rw [List.range_succ_eq_map, List.flatMap_cons, List.flatMap_map]
+    simp only [Function.iterate_zero_apply, Function.iterate_succ_apply]
+    rw [emit_pair, step_pair]
+    cases w with
+    | nil =>
+      simp only [List.headD_nil, List.drop_nil, scan_nil]
+      exact ih (δ q false) [] (by simp)
+    | cons b w =>
+      simp only [List.headD_cons, List.drop_succ_cons, List.drop_zero,
+        List.cons_ne_nil, if_false, scan_cons]
+      rw [ih (δ q b) w (by simpa using hN)]
+
+/-- A finite word-emitting scanner is polynomial-time:
+`PolyTimeComputable (scan δ o q)`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** Use the public emitting-loop host on the encoded phase and suffix.
+The round and emission tables are polynomial-time finite Boolean tables; every round's
+state length is bounded by `9 · (|input| + 1)`. The initial pair supplies more rounds than
+there are input bits, and `flatMap_scan` identifies the emitted word exactly. -/
+theorem scan_poly (δ : Phase → Bool → Phase) (o : Phase → Bool → List Bool) (q : Phase) :
+    PolyTimeComputable (scan δ o q) := by
+  have h := polyTimeComputable_emitIter (step_poly δ) (emit_poly o) 1 1 9 1
+    (fun w i => by simpa only [Nat.pow_one] using length_iterate δ w i)
+  have hpre : PolyTimeComputable fun w => pairEncode (phaseCode q) w :=
+    (polyTimeComputable_const (phaseCode q)).pairEncode polyTimeComputable_id
+  convert h.comp hpre using 1
+  funext w
+  simp only [Function.comp_apply, Nat.one_mul, Nat.pow_one]
+  symm
+  apply flatMap_scan
+  rw [length_pairEncode]
+  omega
+
+/-- One clean finite machine installs the scanner's value and emits nothing within
+`A · (|arg| + 1)^d` steps. Every work head of that same machine remains within that bound
+at every time. The machine is the public H14 clean-install module for `scan δ o q`.
+`∃ (M : FinTM Bool) (A d : ℕ), ModuleHost.Clean M (scan δ o q) (fun _ => []) (fun n => A * (n + 1) ^
+d) ∧ ∀ (x arg : List Bool) (v : ℕ) (j : Fin M.k), |(M.tm.runFrom (Cfg.ofWords (input := x) M.tm.q₀
+(stateWord M.k arg)) v).workTapePos j| ≤ (A * (arg.length + 1) ^ d : ℕ)`. -/
+theorem scan_host (δ : Phase → Bool → Phase) (o : Phase → Bool → List Bool) (q : Phase) :
+    ∃ (M : FinTM Bool) (A d : ℕ),
+      ModuleHost.Clean M (scan δ o q) (fun _ => []) (fun n => A * (n + 1) ^ d) ∧
+      ∀ (x arg : List Bool) (v : ℕ) (j : Fin M.k),
+        |(M.tm.runFrom
+          (Cfg.ofWords (input := x) M.tm.q₀ (stateWord M.k arg)) v).workTapePos j| ≤
+            (A * (arg.length + 1) ^ d : ℕ) := by
+  obtain ⟨M, A, d, hM⟩ := ModuleHost.clean_install (scan_poly δ o q)
+  exact ⟨M, A, d, hM, fun x arg v j => hM.envelope x arg v j⟩
+
+end TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof.ChunkScanner

+/-
+Copyright (c) 2026 TCSlib contributors. All rights reserved.
+Released under Apache 2.0 license as described in the file LICENSE.
+-/
+import TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.Yao.HybridProof.CoinRead
+import TCSlib.Cryptography.ZeroKnowledge.EncodingMachines
+
+set_option maxHeartbeats 0
+set_option relaxedAutoImplicit false
+set_option autoImplicit false

+/-!
+# Polynomial-time garbling coin addresses
+
+These contracts are generic in polynomial-time numeric and word maps. A consumer supplies
+its own key, nonce, and shift equations. No seed or circuit-family definition is duplicated.
+
+## Main definitions
+
+None.
+
+## Main results
+
+* `CoinGarbling.keyWord_poly`: an addressed key block is polynomial-time.
+* `CoinGarbling.assignmentIndex_poly`: a two-bit assignment index is polynomial-time in unary.
+* `CoinGarbling.rowCoinWord_poly`: either half of an addressed row nonce is polynomial-time.
+* `CoinGarbling.shiftBit_poly`: a fixed position of a gate's XOR shift is polynomial-time.
+
+## References
+
+* [PS10] R. Pass, a. shelat, *A Course in Cryptography*, lecture notes, 3rd ed., January
+  2010, Protocol 162.6 (keys and independent encryption coins).
+-/
+
+namespace TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof.CoinGarbling
+
+open Complexity Turing TCSlib.Cryptography.EncodingMachines
+
+/-- A supplied key with block equation
+`key z t = (coins z).getD (((bit z).toNat · bound z + vertex z) · width z + t) false`
+is polynomial-time as a word when its coin, unary-number, and bit maps are polynomial-time.
+[PS10, Protocol 162.6], addressing one of the two keys at a vertex. -/
+theorem keyWord_poly {coins : List Bool → List Bool} {width bound vertex : List Bool → ℕ}
+    {bit : List Bool → Bool} {key : ∀ z, Fin (width z) → Bool}
+    (hcoins : PolyTimeComputable coins)
+    (hwidth : PolyTimeComputable fun z => List.replicate (width z) true)
+    (hbound : PolyTimeComputable fun z => List.replicate (bound z) true)
+    (hvertex : PolyTimeComputable fun z => List.replicate (vertex z) true)
+    (hbit : PolyTimeComputable fun z => [bit z])
+    (hkey : ∀ z t, key z t =
+      (coins z).getD (((bit z).toNat * bound z + vertex z) * width z + t) false) :
+    PolyTimeComputable fun z => List.ofFn (key z) := by
+  have hoff := unaryMul_poly
+    (unaryAdd_poly (unaryMul_poly (unaryOfTest_poly hbit) hbound) hvertex) hwidth
+  refine (polyTimeComputable_window hcoins hoff hwidth).congr fun z => ?_
+  apply congrArg List.ofFn
+  funext t
+  exact (hkey z t).symm
+
+/-- The unary two-bit assignment index `b₀ + 2 b₁` is polynomial-time when both bits are:
+`z ↦ 1^((b₀ z).toNat + 2 · (b₁ z).toNat)`. [PS10, Protocol 162.6]
+`PolyTimeComputable fun z => List.replicate ((b₀ z).toNat + 2 * (b₁ z).toNat) true`. -/
+theorem assignmentIndex_poly {b₀ b₁ : List Bool → Bool}
+    (h₀ : PolyTimeComputable fun z => [b₀ z])
+    (h₁ : PolyTimeComputable fun z => [b₁ z]) :
+    PolyTimeComputable fun z =>
+      List.replicate ((b₀ z).toNat + 2 * (b₁ z).toNat) true :=
+  unaryAdd_poly (unaryOfTest_poly h₀) (unaryMul_poly (unaryConst_poly 2) (unaryOfTest_poly h₁))
+
+/-- A supplied row nonce with block equation
+`nonce z t = (coins z).getD (2 · bound z · width z +
+ (4 · gate z + index z) · (2 · width z) + half.toNat · width z + t) false`
+is polynomial-time as a word when the coin and unary-number maps are polynomial-time.
+The fixed bit `half` selects the first or second nonce. [PS10, Protocol 162.6] -/
+theorem rowCoinWord_poly {coins : List Bool → List Bool}
+    {width bound gate index : List Bool → ℕ} {nonce : ∀ z, Fin (width z) → Bool}
+    (half : Bool) (hcoins : PolyTimeComputable coins)
+    (hwidth : PolyTimeComputable fun z => List.replicate (width z) true)
+    (hbound : PolyTimeComputable fun z => List.replicate (bound z) true)
+    (hgate : PolyTimeComputable fun z => List.replicate (gate z) true)
+    (hindex : PolyTimeComputable fun z => List.replicate (index z) true)
+    (hnonce : ∀ z t, nonce z t =
+      (coins z).getD (2 * bound z * width z + (4 * gate z + index z) * (2 * width z) +
+        half.toNat * width z + t) false) :
+    PolyTimeComputable fun z => List.ofFn (nonce z) := by
+  have hoff := unaryAdd_poly
+    (unaryAdd_poly (unaryMul_poly (unaryMul_poly (unaryConst_poly 2) hbound) hwidth)
+      (unaryMul_poly (unaryAdd_poly (unaryMul_poly (unaryConst_poly 4) hgate) hindex)
+        (unaryMul_poly (unaryConst_poly 2) hwidth)))
+    (unaryMul_poly (unaryConst_poly half.toNat) hwidth)
+  refine (polyTimeComputable_window hcoins hoff hwidth).congr fun z => ?_
+  apply congrArg List.ofFn
+  funext t
+  exact (hnonce z t).symm
+
+/-- Position `j` of a gate's shift is polynomial-time:
+`z ↦ [(coins z).getD (10 · bound z · width z + 2 · gate z + j) false]`,
+when the coin and unary-number maps are polynomial-time. [PS10, Protocol 162.6]
+`PolyTimeComputable fun z => [(coins z).getD (10 * bound z * width z + 2 * gate z + j) false]`. -/
+theorem shiftBit_poly {coins : List Bool → List Bool} {width bound gate : List Bool → ℕ}
+    (j : ℕ) (hcoins : PolyTimeComputable coins)
+    (hwidth : PolyTimeComputable fun z => List.replicate (width z) true)
+    (hbound : PolyTimeComputable fun z => List.replicate (bound z) true)
+    (hgate : PolyTimeComputable fun z => List.replicate (gate z) true) :
+    PolyTimeComputable fun z =>
+      [(coins z).getD (10 * bound z * width z + 2 * gate z + j) false] := by
+  exact getBit_poly hcoins
+    (unaryAdd_poly
+      (unaryAdd_poly (unaryMul_poly (unaryMul_poly (unaryConst_poly 10) hbound) hwidth)
+        (unaryMul_poly (unaryConst_poly 2) hgate)) (unaryConst_poly j))
+
+end TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof.CoinGarbling

+/-

+Released under Apache 2.0 license as described in the file LICENSE.

+import TCSlib.Cryptography.Asymptotic.EnsemblesLemmas
+
+set_option maxHeartbeats 0
+set_option relaxedAutoImplicit false
+set_option autoImplicit false
+
+/-!
+# Polynomial-time windows of a coin string
+
+The window operations below take their offsets and widths in unary. They work on arbitrary
+words, including words shorter than the requested window, by padding the missing bits with
+`false`. The contracts therefore apply to the malformed inputs included in strategy efficiency.
+
+## Main definitions
+
+No new data representation is introduced.
+
+## Main results
+
+* `polyTimeComputable_window`: a padded window whose word, offset and width are polynomial-time.
+
+## References
+
+* [PS10] R. Pass, a. shelat, *A Course in Cryptography*, lecture notes, 3rd ed.,
+  January 2010, Protocol 162.6 (fixed blocks of the parties' random tapes).
+-/
+
+namespace TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof
+
+open Complexity Turing
+
+/-- A padded window is polynomial-time when its word and its unary offset and width are:
+`z ↦ List.ofFn (fun t : Fin (len z) => (w z).getD (off z + t) false)`.
+The contract includes zero widths and windows extending beyond the word. -/
+theorem polyTimeComputable_window {w : List Bool → List Bool} {off len : List Bool → ℕ}
+    (hw : PolyTimeComputable w)
+    (hoff : PolyTimeComputable fun z => List.replicate (off z) true)
+    (hlen : PolyTimeComputable fun z => List.replicate (len z) true) :
+    PolyTimeComputable fun z =>
+      List.ofFn (fun t : Fin (len z) => (w z).getD (off z + t) false) := by
+  have hdrop : PolyTimeComputable fun z => (w z).drop (off z) :=
+    ((polyTimeComputable_pairDropPoly .var).comp (hoff.pairEncode hw)).congr fun z => by
+      simp only [Function.comp_apply, pairFstD_pairEncode, pairSndD_pairEncode,
+        PolyExpr.eval_var, List.length_replicate]
+  have hp := EnsembleLemmas.polyTimeComputable_padL .var hlen hdrop
+  refine hp.congr fun z => ?_
+  simp only [PolyExpr.eval_var, List.length_replicate, ← EnsembleLemmas.ofFn_padFin_eq_padL]
+  apply congrArg List.ofFn
+  funext t
+  rw [padFin_apply, List.getD_eq_getElem?_getD, List.getElem?_drop,
+    List.getD_eq_getElem?_getD]
+
+end TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof

+/-
+Copyright (c) 2026 TCSlib contributors. All rights reserved.
+Released under Apache 2.0 license as described in the file LICENSE.
