+import TCSlib.Cryptography.Asymptotic.Adversary
+import TCSlib.Cryptography.Foundations.BitLists
+import Mathlib.Logic.Equiv.Fin.Basic
+import Mathlib.Tactic.Ring
+
+set_option maxHeartbeats 0
+set_option relaxedAutoImplicit false
+set_option autoImplicit false
+
+/-!
+# Exact coin-block bijections for garbling
+
+The tape of `10 S n + 2 S` bits consists of two `S`-by-`n` key tables,
+`S`-by-four rows of `2 n` bits, and `S` two-bit shifts. The equivalences below
+include zero-width key and row tables. Relabeling permutes key choices and row
+blocks and XORs shift bits; callers can fix unused coordinates explicitly.
+
+## Main definitions
+
+* `Blocks`: the typed key, row and shift tables.
+* `splitTape`: the three contiguous regions of the tape.
+* `blocksEquiv`: the complete tape as its three typed tables.
+* `relabel`: a coin bijection obtained by relabeling those tables.
+
+## Main results
+
+* `blocksEquiv_key`, `blocksEquiv_row`, `blocksEquiv_shift`: exact offset equations.
+* `relabel_blocks`: conjugation through the typed block representation.
+
+## References
+
+* [PS10] R. Pass, a. shelat, *A Course in Cryptography*, lecture notes, 3rd ed.,
+  January 2010, Theorem 163.7 (the coin relabeling in the garbling hybrid).
+* [BMR90] D. Beaver, S. Micali, P. Rogaway, *The round complexity of secure
+  protocols*, STOC 1990, pp.503–513 (point-and-permute).
+-/
+
+namespace TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof.CoinRelabel
+
+open TCSlib.Cryptography
+
+/-- `Blocks S n` is a key table `Bool → Fin S → Fin n → Bool`, a row table
+`Fin S → Fin 4 → Fin (2 n) → Bool`, and shifts `Fin S → Fin 2 → Bool`. -/
+abbrev Blocks (S n : ℕ) :=
+  (Bool → Fin S → Fin n → Bool) ×
+    (Fin S → Fin 4 → Fin (2 * n) → Bool) × (Fin S → Fin 2 → Bool)
+
+/-- `Blocks S n` is the product of its key, row and shift function types.
+`Blocks S n = ((Bool → Fin S → Fin n → Bool) × (Fin S → Fin 4 → Fin (2 * n) → Bool) × (Fin S → Fin 2
+→ Bool))`. -/
+theorem Blocks_eq (S n : ℕ) :
+    Blocks S n = ((Bool → Fin S → Fin n → Bool) ×
+      (Fin S → Fin 4 → Fin (2 * n) → Bool) × (Fin S → Fin 2 → Bool)) := rfl
+
+/-- Split `10 S n + 2 S` coin bits into regions of lengths `2 S n`, `4 S (2 n)`
+and `2 S`, by two append equivalences. Zero widths are included.
+`splitTape S n r = ((fun i : Fin ((2 * S) * n) => (List.ofFn r).getD i false), (fun i : Fin
+((S * 4) * (2 * n)) => (List.ofFn r).getD (2 * S * n + i) false), (fun i : Fin (S * 2) => (List.ofFn
+r).getD (10 * S * n + i) false))`. -/
+def splitTape (S n : ℕ) :
+    (Fin (10 * S * n + 2 * S) → Bool) ≃
+      (Fin ((2 * S) * n) → Bool) ×
+        (Fin ((S * 4) * (2 * n)) → Bool) × (Fin (S * 2) → Bool) :=
+  let h : 10 * S * n + 2 * S = (2 * S) * n + ((S * 4) * (2 * n) + S * 2) := by ring
+  (Equiv.arrowCongr (finCongr h) (Equiv.refl Bool)).trans
+    ((Fin.appendEquiv ((2 * S) * n) ((S * 4) * (2 * n) + S * 2)).symm.trans
+      ((Equiv.refl _).prodCongr (Fin.appendEquiv ((S * 4) * (2 * n)) (S * 2)).symm))
+
+/-- The key region of `splitTape S n r` reads the tape at its unchanged offset:
+`(splitTape S n r).1 i = (ofFn r).getD i false`. -/
+theorem splitTape_key (S n : ℕ) (r : Fin (10 * S * n + 2 * S) → Bool)
+    (i : Fin ((2 * S) * n)) :
+    (splitTape S n r).1 i = (List.ofFn r).getD i false := by
+  have hsum : 10 * S * n + 2 * S = (2 * S) * n + ((S * 4) * (2 * n) + S * 2) := by
+    ring
+  have hi := i.isLt
+  have hb : (i : ℕ) < 10 * S * n + 2 * S := by omega
+  rw [List.getD_eq_getElem _ _ (by rw [List.length_ofFn]; exact hb), List.getElem_ofFn]
+  rfl
+
+/-- The row region of `splitTape S n r` begins at `2 S n`:
+`(splitTape S n r).2.1 i = (ofFn r).getD (2*S*n+i) false`. -/
+theorem splitTape_row (S n : ℕ) (r : Fin (10 * S * n + 2 * S) → Bool)
+    (i : Fin ((S * 4) * (2 * n))) :
+    (splitTape S n r).2.1 i = (List.ofFn r).getD (2 * S * n + i) false := by
+  have hsum : 10 * S * n + 2 * S = (2 * S) * n + ((S * 4) * (2 * n) + S * 2) := by
+    ring
+  have hi := i.isLt
+  have hb : 2 * S * n + i < 10 * S * n + 2 * S := by omega
+  rw [List.getD_eq_getElem _ _ (by rw [List.length_ofFn]; exact hb), List.getElem_ofFn]
+  rfl
+
+/-- The shift region of `splitTape S n r` begins at `10 S n`:
+`(splitTape S n r).2.2 i = (ofFn r).getD (10*S*n+i) false`. -/
+theorem splitTape_shift (S n : ℕ) (r : Fin (10 * S * n + 2 * S) → Bool)
+    (i : Fin (S * 2)) :
+    (splitTape S n r).2.2 i = (List.ofFn r).getD (10 * S * n + i) false := by
+  have hi := i.isLt
+  have hb : 10 * S * n + i < 10 * S * n + 2 * S := by omega
+  rw [List.getD_eq_getElem _ _ (by rw [List.length_ofFn]; exact hb), List.getElem_ofFn]
+  apply congrArg r
+  apply Fin.ext
+  change (2 * S) * n + ((S * 4) * (2 * n) + (i : ℕ)) = 10 * S * n + i
+  ring
+
+/-- `splitTape S n r` is the triple of windows at offsets `0`, `2 S n`, and
+`10 S n`, of lengths `2 S n`, `4 S (2 n)`, and `2 S`, respectively.
+`splitTape S n r = ((fun i : Fin ((2 * S) * n) => (List.ofFn r).getD i false), (fun i : Fin
+((S * 4) * (2 * n)) => (List.ofFn r).getD (2 * S * n + i) false), (fun i : Fin (S * 2) => (List.ofFn
+r).getD (10 * S * n + i) false))`. -/
+theorem splitTape_eq (S n : ℕ) (r : Fin (10 * S * n + 2 * S) → Bool) :
+    splitTape S n r =
+      ((fun i : Fin ((2 * S) * n) => (List.ofFn r).getD i false),
+        (fun i : Fin ((S * 4) * (2 * n)) => (List.ofFn r).getD (2 * S * n + i) false),
+        (fun i : Fin (S * 2) => (List.ofFn r).getD (10 * S * n + i) false)) := by
+  apply Prod.ext
+  · funext i
+    exact splitTape_key S n r i
+  · apply Prod.ext
+    · funext i
+      exact splitTape_row S n r i
+    · funext i
+      exact splitTape_shift S n r i
+
+/-- The complete tape of `10 S n + 2 S` bits is equivalent to its typed key, row,
+and shift tables. The construction uses only splitting, existing block equivalences,
+and reindexing; in particular it includes `S = 0` and `n = 0`.
+[PS10, Theorem 163.7, coin relabeling] -/
+def blocksEquiv (S n : ℕ) : (Fin (10 * S * n + 2 * S) → Bool) ≃ Blocks S n :=
+  let eK : (Fin ((2 * S) * n) → Bool) ≃ (Bool → Fin S → Fin n → Bool) :=
+    ((FunFamily.blocks (2 * S) n).trans (blocksEquivX 2 S (Fin n → Bool))).trans
+      (Equiv.arrowCongr finTwoEquiv (Equiv.refl _))
+  let eR : (Fin ((S * 4) * (2 * n)) → Bool) ≃ (Fin S → Fin 4 → Fin (2 * n) → Bool) :=
+    (FunFamily.blocks (S * 4) (2 * n)).trans (blocksEquivX S 4 (Fin (2 * n) → Bool))
+  (splitTape S n).trans (eK.prodCongr (eR.prodCongr (FunFamily.blocks S 2)))
+
+/-- Key bit `t` of choice `b` at vertex `v` is tape bit `(b*S+v)*n+t`:
+`(blocksEquiv S n r).1 b v t = (ofFn r).getD ((b.toNat*S+v)*n+t) false`.
+The equation includes zero-width key tables. -/
+theorem blocksEquiv_key (S n : ℕ) (r : Fin (10 * S * n + 2 * S) → Bool)
+    (b : Bool) (v : Fin S) (t : Fin n) :
+    (blocksEquiv S n r).1 b v t =
+      (List.ofFn r).getD ((b.toNat * S + v) * n + t) false := by
+  change blocksEquivX 2 S (Fin n → Bool)
+    (FunFamily.blocks (2 * S) n ((splitTape S n r).1)) (finTwoEquiv.symm b) v t = _
+  rw [blocksEquivX_apply, FunFamily.blocks_apply, splitTape_key]
+  congr 1
+  simp only [finProdFinEquiv_apply_val]
+  have hb : (finTwoEquiv.symm b : ℕ) = b.toNat := by cases b <;> rfl
+  rw [hb]
+  ring
+
+/-- Row-coin bit `t` of assignment index `a` at gate `i` is tape bit
+`2 S n + (4 i + a) (2 n) + t`, for every `S` and `n`.
+`(blocksEquiv S n r).2.1 i a t = (List.ofFn r).getD (2 * S * n + (4 * i + a) * (2 * n) + t)
+false`. -/
+theorem blocksEquiv_row (S n : ℕ) (r : Fin (10 * S * n + 2 * S) → Bool)
+    (i : Fin S) (a : Fin 4) (t : Fin (2 * n)) :
+    (blocksEquiv S n r).2.1 i a t =
+      (List.ofFn r).getD (2 * S * n + (4 * i + a) * (2 * n) + t) false := by
+  change blocksEquivX S 4 (Fin (2 * n) → Bool)
+    (FunFamily.blocks (S * 4) (2 * n) ((splitTape S n r).2.1)) i a t = _
+  rw [blocksEquivX_apply, FunFamily.blocks_apply, splitTape_row]
+  congr 1
+  simp only [finProdFinEquiv_apply_val]
+  ring
+
+/-- Shift bit `t` at gate `i` is tape bit `10 S n + 2 i + t`, including `n = 0`. -/
+theorem blocksEquiv_shift (S n : ℕ) (r : Fin (10 * S * n + 2 * S) → Bool)
+    (i : Fin S) (t : Fin 2) :
+    (blocksEquiv S n r).2.2 i t =
+      (List.ofFn r).getD (10 * S * n + 2 * i + t) false := by
+  change FunFamily.blocks S 2 ((splitTape S n r).2.2) i t = _
+  rw [FunFamily.blocks_apply, splitTape_shift]
+  congr 1
+  rw [finProdFinEquiv_apply_val]
+  ring
+
+/-- `blocksEquiv S n r` is exactly the key, row and shift tables at their
+specified tape offsets.
+`blocksEquiv S n r = ((fun (b : Bool) (v : Fin S) (t : Fin n) => (List.ofFn r).getD ((b.toNat * S +
+v) * n + t) false), (fun (i : Fin S) (a : Fin 4) (t : Fin (2 * n)) => (List.ofFn r).getD (2 * S *
+n + (4 * i + a) * (2 * n) + t) false), (fun (i : Fin S) (t : Fin 2) => (List.ofFn r).getD (10 * S *
+n + 2 * i + t) false))`. -/
+theorem blocksEquiv_eq (S n : ℕ) (r : Fin (10 * S * n + 2 * S) → Bool) :
+    blocksEquiv S n r =
+      ((fun (b : Bool) (v : Fin S) (t : Fin n) =>
+          (List.ofFn r).getD ((b.toNat * S + v) * n + t) false),
+        (fun (i : Fin S) (a : Fin 4) (t : Fin (2 * n)) =>
+          (List.ofFn r).getD (2 * S * n + (4 * i + a) * (2 * n) + t) false),
+        (fun (i : Fin S) (t : Fin 2) =>
+          (List.ofFn r).getD (10 * S * n + 2 * i + t) false)) := by
+  apply Prod.ext
+  · funext b v t
+    exact blocksEquiv_key S n r b v t
+  · apply Prod.ext
+    · funext i a t
+      exact blocksEquiv_row S n r i a t
+    · funext i t
+      exact blocksEquiv_shift S n r i t
+
+/-- Relabel a key table by changing choice `b` at `v` to `b XOR values v`.
+This is an involutive permutation of complete key tables.
+`keyRelabel S n values k b v t = k (xor b (values v)) v t`. -/
+def keyRelabel (S n : ℕ) (values : Fin S → Bool) :
+    Equiv.Perm (Bool → Fin S → Fin n → Bool) :=
+  Function.Involutive.toPerm
+    (fun k b v t => k (xor b (values v)) v t) fun k => by
+      funext b v t
+      simp only [Bool.xor_assoc, Bool.xor_self, Bool.xor_false]
+
+/-- `keyRelabel S n values k b v t = k (b XOR values v) v t`. -/
+theorem keyRelabel_eq (S n : ℕ) (values : Fin S → Bool)
+    (k : Bool → Fin S → Fin n → Bool) (b : Bool) (v : Fin S) (t : Fin n) :
+    keyRelabel S n values k b v t = k (xor b (values v)) v t := rfl
+
+/-- Relabel row tables by the supplied assignment-index permutation at each gate:
+`rowRelabel S n rows k i a t = k i (rows i a) t`. -/
+def rowRelabel (S n : ℕ) (rows : Fin S → Equiv.Perm (Fin 4)) :
+    Equiv.Perm (Fin S → Fin 4 → Fin (2 * n) → Bool) :=
+  Equiv.piCongrRight fun i => Equiv.arrowCongr (rows i).symm (Equiv.refl _)
+
+/-- `rowRelabel S n rows k i a t = k i (rows i a) t`. -/
+theorem rowRelabel_eq (S n : ℕ) (rows : Fin S → Equiv.Perm (Fin 4))
+    (k : Fin S → Fin 4 → Fin (2 * n) → Bool) (i : Fin S) (a : Fin 4)
+    (t : Fin (2 * n)) : rowRelabel S n rows k i a t = k i (rows i a) t := rfl
+
+/-- XOR each two-bit shift with its supplied mask, reusing the public `xorPerm`:
+`shiftRelabel S mask k i t = k i t XOR mask i t`. -/
+def shiftRelabel (S : ℕ) (mask : Fin S → Fin 2 → Bool) :
+    Equiv.Perm (Fin S → Fin 2 → Bool) :=
+  Equiv.piCongrRight fun i => xorPerm (mask i)
+
+/-- `shiftRelabel S mask k i t = k i t XOR mask i t`. -/
+theorem shiftRelabel_eq (S : ℕ) (mask : Fin S → Fin 2 → Bool)
+    (k : Fin S → Fin 2 → Bool) (i : Fin S) (t : Fin 2) :
+    shiftRelabel S mask k i t = xor (k i t) (mask i t) := by
+  change xorPerm (mask i) (k i) t = _
+  exact congrFun (xorPerm_apply (mask i) (k i)) t
+
+/-- Relabel the key, row and shift tables independently by their supplied choice
+flips, row permutations and shift masks. [PS10, Theorem 163.7]
+`blockRelabel S n values rows mask q = (keyRelabel S n values q.1, rowRelabel S n rows q.2.1,
+shiftRelabel S mask q.2.2)`. -/
+def blockRelabel (S n : ℕ) (values : Fin S → Bool)
+    (rows : Fin S → Equiv.Perm (Fin 4)) (mask : Fin S → Fin 2 → Bool) :
+    Equiv.Perm (Blocks S n) :=
+  (keyRelabel S n values).prodCongr ((rowRelabel S n rows).prodCongr (shiftRelabel S mask))
+
+/-- `blockRelabel` applies `keyRelabel`, `rowRelabel` and `shiftRelabel` to the
+three components of the block triple.
+`blockRelabel S n values rows mask q = (keyRelabel S n values q.1, rowRelabel S n rows q.2.1,
+shiftRelabel S mask q.2.2)`. -/
+theorem blockRelabel_eq (S n : ℕ) (values : Fin S → Bool)
+    (rows : Fin S → Equiv.Perm (Fin 4)) (mask : Fin S → Fin 2 → Bool) (q : Blocks S n) :
+    blockRelabel S n values rows mask q =
+      (keyRelabel S n values q.1, rowRelabel S n rows q.2.1, shiftRelabel S mask q.2.2) := rfl
+
+/-- Conjugating `blockRelabel` by `blocksEquiv` gives a permutation of all
+`10 S n + 2 S` tape bits. [PS10, Theorem 163.7; BMR90]
+`relabel S n values rows mask r = (blocksEquiv S n).symm (blockRelabel S n values rows mask
+(blocksEquiv S n r))`. -/
+def relabel (S n : ℕ) (values : Fin S → Bool)
+    (rows : Fin S → Equiv.Perm (Fin 4)) (mask : Fin S → Fin 2 → Bool) :
+    Equiv.Perm (Fin (10 * S * n + 2 * S) → Bool) :=
+  (blocksEquiv S n).trans ((blockRelabel S n values rows mask).trans (blocksEquiv S n).symm)
+
+/-- `relabel S n values rows mask r` is the inverse block encoding of the
+relabeled block triple of `r`.
+`relabel S n values rows mask r = (blocksEquiv S n).symm (blockRelabel S n values rows mask
+(blocksEquiv S n r))`. -/
+theorem relabel_eq (S n : ℕ) (values : Fin S → Bool)
+    (rows : Fin S → Equiv.Perm (Fin 4)) (mask : Fin S → Fin 2 → Bool)
+    (r : Fin (10 * S * n + 2 * S) → Bool) :
+    relabel S n values rows mask r =
+      (blocksEquiv S n).symm (blockRelabel S n values rows mask (blocksEquiv S n r)) := rfl
+
+/-- Encoding `relabel r` as blocks gives exactly `blockRelabel (blocksEquiv r)`:
+`blocksEquiv S n (relabel S n values rows mask r) =
+ blockRelabel S n values rows mask (blocksEquiv S n r)`. -/
+theorem relabel_blocks (S n : ℕ) (values : Fin S → Bool)
+    (rows : Fin S → Equiv.Perm (Fin 4)) (mask : Fin S → Fin 2 → Bool)
+    (r : Fin (10 * S * n + 2 * S) → Bool) :
+    blocksEquiv S n (relabel S n values rows mask r) =
+      blockRelabel S n values rows mask (blocksEquiv S n r) := by
+  rw [relabel_eq, Equiv.apply_symm_apply]
+
+/-- In the relabeled tape, key choice `b` at `v` reads the original key choice
+`b XOR values v` at that same vertex.
+`(blocksEquiv S n (relabel S n values rows mask r)).1 b v t = (blocksEquiv S n r).1 (xor b (values
+v)) v t`. -/
+theorem relabel_key (S n : ℕ) (values : Fin S → Bool)
+    (rows : Fin S → Equiv.Perm (Fin 4)) (mask : Fin S → Fin 2 → Bool)
+    (r : Fin (10 * S * n + 2 * S) → Bool) (b : Bool) (v : Fin S) (t : Fin n) :
+    (blocksEquiv S n (relabel S n values rows mask r)).1 b v t =
+      (blocksEquiv S n r).1 (xor b (values v)) v t := by
+  simp only [relabel_blocks, blockRelabel_eq, keyRelabel_eq]
+
+/-- Row `a` of gate `i` in the relabeled tape reads original row `rows i a`,
+with the within-row nonce coordinate unchanged.
+`(blocksEquiv S n (relabel S n values rows mask r)).2.1 i a t = (blocksEquiv S n r).2.1 i (rows i a)
+t`. -/
+theorem relabel_row (S n : ℕ) (values : Fin S → Bool)
+    (rows : Fin S → Equiv.Perm (Fin 4)) (mask : Fin S → Fin 2 → Bool)
+    (r : Fin (10 * S * n + 2 * S) → Bool) (i : Fin S) (a : Fin 4) (t : Fin (2 * n)) :
+    (blocksEquiv S n (relabel S n values rows mask r)).2.1 i a t =
+      (blocksEquiv S n r).2.1 i (rows i a) t := by
+  simp only [relabel_blocks, blockRelabel_eq, rowRelabel_eq]
+
+/-- Shift bit `t` at gate `i` in the relabeled tape is the original shift bit
+XOR the supplied mask bit `mask i t`.
+`(blocksEquiv S n (relabel S n values rows mask r)).2.2 i t = xor ((blocksEquiv S n r).2.2 i t)
+(mask i t)`. -/
+theorem relabel_shift (S n : ℕ) (values : Fin S → Bool)
+    (rows : Fin S → Equiv.Perm (Fin 4)) (mask : Fin S → Fin 2 → Bool)
+    (r : Fin (10 * S * n + 2 * S) → Bool) (i : Fin S) (t : Fin 2) :
+    (blocksEquiv S n (relabel S n values rows mask r)).2.2 i t =
+      xor ((blocksEquiv S n r).2.2 i t) (mask i t) := by
+  simp only [relabel_blocks, blockRelabel_eq, shiftRelabel_eq]
+
+/-- At exact tape offsets, relabeling replaces key choice `b` by
+`b XOR values v`: the coordinate `(b*S+v)*n+t` reads `((b XOR values v)*S+v)*n+t`.
+`(List.ofFn (relabel S n values rows mask r)).getD ((b.toNat * S + v) * n + t) false = (List.ofFn
+r).getD (((xor b (values v)).toNat * S + v) * n + t) false`. -/
+theorem relabel_key_getD (S n : ℕ) (values : Fin S → Bool)
+    (rows : Fin S → Equiv.Perm (Fin 4)) (mask : Fin S → Fin 2 → Bool)
+    (r : Fin (10 * S * n + 2 * S) → Bool) (b : Bool) (v : Fin S) (t : Fin n) :
+    (List.ofFn (relabel S n values rows mask r)).getD ((b.toNat * S + v) * n + t) false =
+      (List.ofFn r).getD (((xor b (values v)).toNat * S + v) * n + t) false := by
+  rw [← blocksEquiv_key S n (relabel S n values rows mask r) b v t,
+    relabel_key, blocksEquiv_key]
+
+/-- At exact row-coin offsets, relabeling replaces assignment code `a` by
+`rows i a` and keeps nonce bit `t` unchanged.
+`(List.ofFn (relabel S n values rows mask r)).getD (2 * S * n + (4 * i + a) * (2 * n) + t) false =
+(List.ofFn r).getD (2 * S * n + (4 * i + (rows i a : ℕ)) * (2 * n) + t) false`. -/
+theorem relabel_row_getD (S n : ℕ) (values : Fin S → Bool)
+    (rows : Fin S → Equiv.Perm (Fin 4)) (mask : Fin S → Fin 2 → Bool)
+    (r : Fin (10 * S * n + 2 * S) → Bool) (i : Fin S) (a : Fin 4) (t : Fin (2 * n)) :
+    (List.ofFn (relabel S n values rows mask r)).getD
+        (2 * S * n + (4 * i + a) * (2 * n) + t) false =
+      (List.ofFn r).getD (2 * S * n + (4 * i + (rows i a : ℕ)) * (2 * n) + t) false := by
+  rw [← blocksEquiv_row S n (relabel S n values rows mask r) i a t,
+    relabel_row, blocksEquiv_row]
+
+/-- At tape offset `10 S n + 2 i + t`, relabeling XORs the existing shift bit
+with `mask i t`, including when the key length is zero.
+`(List.ofFn (relabel S n values rows mask r)).getD (10 * S * n + 2 * i + t) false = xor ((List.ofFn
+r).getD (10 * S * n + 2 * i + t) false) (mask i t)`. -/
+theorem relabel_shift_getD (S n : ℕ) (values : Fin S → Bool)
+    (rows : Fin S → Equiv.Perm (Fin 4)) (mask : Fin S → Fin 2 → Bool)
+    (r : Fin (10 * S * n + 2 * S) → Bool) (i : Fin S) (t : Fin 2) :
+    (List.ofFn (relabel S n values rows mask r)).getD (10 * S * n + 2 * i + t) false =
+      xor ((List.ofFn r).getD (10 * S * n + 2 * i + t) false) (mask i t) := by
+  rw [← blocksEquiv_shift S n (relabel S n values rows mask r) i t,
+    relabel_shift, blocksEquiv_shift]
+
+/-- A key block at a vertex whose value mask is `false` is unchanged by relabeling.
+This preserves unused key blocks when their value masks are set to `false`.
+`(List.ofFn (relabel S n values rows mask r)).getD ((b.toNat * S + v) * n + t) false = (List.ofFn
+r).getD ((b.toNat * S + v) * n + t) false`. -/
+theorem relabel_key_fixed (S n : ℕ) (values : Fin S → Bool)
+    (rows : Fin S → Equiv.Perm (Fin 4)) (mask : Fin S → Fin 2 → Bool)
+    (r : Fin (10 * S * n + 2 * S) → Bool) (b : Bool) (v : Fin S) (t : Fin n)
+    (hv : values v = false) :
+    (List.ofFn (relabel S n values rows mask r)).getD ((b.toNat * S + v) * n + t) false =
+      (List.ofFn r).getD ((b.toNat * S + v) * n + t) false := by
+  rw [relabel_key_getD, hv, Bool.xor_false]
+
+/-- A row coordinate fixed by `rows i` keeps every nonce bit. In particular,
+callers preserve unused assignment codes by fixing them in the supplied permutation.
+`(List.ofFn (relabel S n values rows mask r)).getD (2 * S * n + (4 * i + a) * (2 * n) + t) false =
+(List.ofFn r).getD (2 * S * n + (4 * i + a) * (2 * n) + t) false`. -/
+theorem relabel_row_fixed (S n : ℕ) (values : Fin S → Bool)
+    (rows : Fin S → Equiv.Perm (Fin 4)) (mask : Fin S → Fin 2 → Bool)
+    (r : Fin (10 * S * n + 2 * S) → Bool) (i : Fin S) (a : Fin 4) (t : Fin (2 * n))
+    (ha : rows i a = a) :
+    (List.ofFn (relabel S n values rows mask r)).getD
+        (2 * S * n + (4 * i + a) * (2 * n) + t) false =
+      (List.ofFn r).getD (2 * S * n + (4 * i + a) * (2 * n) + t) false := by
+  rw [relabel_row_getD, ha]
+
+/-- A shift bit with a `false` mask is unchanged. Callers can therefore preserve
+unused shift positions by setting exactly those mask bits to `false`.
+`(List.ofFn (relabel S n values rows mask r)).getD (10 * S * n + 2 * i + t) false = (List.ofFn
+r).getD (10 * S * n + 2 * i + t) false`. -/
+theorem relabel_shift_fixed (S n : ℕ) (values : Fin S → Bool)
+    (rows : Fin S → Equiv.Perm (Fin 4)) (mask : Fin S → Fin 2 → Bool)
+    (r : Fin (10 * S * n + 2 * S) → Bool) (i : Fin S) (t : Fin 2)
+    (ht : mask i t = false) :
+    (List.ofFn (relabel S n values rows mask r)).getD (10 * S * n + 2 * i + t) false =
+      (List.ofFn r).getD (10 * S * n + 2 * i + t) false := by
+  rw [relabel_shift_getD, ht, Bool.xor_false]
+
+end TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof.CoinRelabel

+/-

+import TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.Yao.HybridProof.CoinRelabel
+import TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.Yao.HybridProof.RelabelGarbling
+import TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.Yao.HybridProof.RowFromCoins
+
+set_option maxHeartbeats 0
+set_option relaxedAutoImplicit false
+set_option autoImplicit false
+
+/-!
+# The tape-to-seed coin relabeling bridge
+
+The seed function is a parameter, specified by its key, nonce and shift-bit
+readers. Relabeling its complete tape agrees with the generic seed relabeling.
+Assignment XOR is extended to four row codes by the existing finite-embedding
+permutation extension, which fixes unused codes. Unused key and shift positions
+are padded with false masks.
+
+## Main definitions
+
+* `assignmentEmbedding`, `assignmentPerm`: assignment folds and their extended XOR permutation.
+* `keyMask`, `rowPerm`, `shiftMask`: the padded coordinate actions on complete tapes.
+
+## Main results
+
+* `seed_relabel`: the seed of the relabeled tape is the relabeling of the original seed.
+
+## References
+
+* [PS10] R. Pass, a. shelat, *A Course in Cryptography*, lecture notes, 3rd ed.,
+  January 2010, Protocol 162.6 and Theorem 163.7 (the coin relabeling).
+-/
+
+namespace TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof.CoinSeedRelabel
+
+open BoolCircuit TCSlib.Cryptography
+
+/-- The binary fold of an assignment to at most two argument positions is below four.
+This is the first conjunct of the published `GarblingProbability.assignmentFold_small`.
+[PS10, Protocol 162.6]
+`(List.ofFn a).foldr (fun b k => b.toNat + 2 * k) 0 < 4`. -/
+theorem assignmentFold_lt_four {l : ℕ} (a : Fin l → Bool) (hl : l ≤ 2) :
+    (List.ofFn a).foldr (fun b k => b.toNat + 2 * k) 0 < 4 :=
+  (GarblingProbability.assignmentFold_small l hl).1 a
+
+/-- For `l ≤ 2`, the assignment fold is below the exact code count `2^l`.
+Thus unused codes begin at one for arity zero and at two for arity one. -/
+theorem assignmentFold_lt_pow {l : ℕ} (a : Fin l → Bool) (hl : l ≤ 2) :
+    (List.ofFn a).foldr (fun b k => b.toNat + 2 * k) 0 < 2 ^ l := by
+  have hc : l = 0 ∨ l = 1 ∨ l = 2 := by omega
+  rcases hc with rfl | rfl | rfl
+  · rw [RowFromCoins.assignmentFold_eq_two a (by decide),
+      List.getD_eq_default (List.ofFn a) false (n := 0) (by rw [List.length_ofFn]),
+      List.getD_eq_default (List.ofFn a) false (n := 1) (by rw [List.length_ofFn]; omega)]
+    decide
+  · rw [RowFromCoins.assignmentFold_eq_two a (by decide),
+      List.getD_eq_default (List.ofFn a) false (n := 1) (by rw [List.length_ofFn])]
+    simp only [Bool.toNat_false, Nat.mul_zero, Nat.add_zero]
+    exact Bool.toNat_lt _
+  · exact assignmentFold_lt_four a (by decide)
+
+/-- For at most two positions, equal assignment folds imply equal assignments. This is the
+second conjunct of the published `GarblingProbability.assignmentFold_small`.
+`Function.Injective (fun a : Fin l → Bool => (List.ofFn a).foldr (fun b k => b.toNat + 2 * k)
+0)`. -/
+theorem assignmentFold_injective (l : ℕ) (hl : l ≤ 2) :
+    Function.Injective (fun a : Fin l → Bool =>
+      (List.ofFn a).foldr (fun b k => b.toNat + 2 * k) 0) :=
+  (GarblingProbability.assignmentFold_small l hl).2
+
+/-- Embed an assignment to `l ≤ 2` positions in four row codes by its binary fold. -/
+def assignmentEmbedding (l : ℕ) (hl : l ≤ 2) : (Fin l → Bool) ↪ Fin 4 where
+  toFun a := ⟨(List.ofFn a).foldr (fun b k => b.toNat + 2 * k) 0,
+    assignmentFold_lt_four a hl⟩
+  inj' _ _ h := assignmentFold_injective l hl (congrArg Fin.val h)
+
+/-- The natural value of `assignmentEmbedding l hl a` is its assignment fold.
+`(assignmentEmbedding l hl a : ℕ) = (List.ofFn a).foldr (fun b k => b.toNat + 2 * k) 0`. -/
+theorem assignmentEmbedding_eq (l : ℕ) (hl : l ≤ 2) (a : Fin l → Bool) :
+    (assignmentEmbedding l hl a : ℕ) =
+      (List.ofFn a).foldr (fun b k => b.toNat + 2 * k) 0 := rfl
+
+/-- The embedded code is below `2^l`: `(assignmentEmbedding l hl a : ℕ) < 2^l`. -/
+theorem assignmentEmbedding_lt_pow (l : ℕ) (hl : l ≤ 2) (a : Fin l → Bool) :
+    (assignmentEmbedding l hl a : ℕ) < 2 ^ l := assignmentFold_lt_pow a hl
+
+/-- Extend assignment XOR to four row codes with the existing embedding extension.
+Every code outside the assignment embedding's range is fixed.
+`assignmentPerm l hl mask = (xorPerm mask).viaFintypeEmbedding (assignmentEmbedding l hl)`. -/
+def assignmentPerm (l : ℕ) (hl : l ≤ 2) (mask : Fin l → Bool) : Equiv.Perm (Fin 4) :=
+  (xorPerm mask).viaFintypeEmbedding (assignmentEmbedding l hl)
+
+/-- `assignmentPerm l hl mask` is `xorPerm mask` extended through the fold embedding.
+`assignmentPerm l hl mask = (xorPerm mask).viaFintypeEmbedding (assignmentEmbedding l hl)`. -/
+theorem assignmentPerm_eq (l : ℕ) (hl : l ≤ 2) (mask : Fin l → Bool) :
+    assignmentPerm l hl mask =
+      (xorPerm mask).viaFintypeEmbedding (assignmentEmbedding l hl) := rfl
+
+/-- The extended permutation sends the code of `a` to the code of `a XOR mask`.
+`assignmentPerm l hl mask (assignmentEmbedding l hl a) = assignmentEmbedding l hl (xorPerm mask
+a)`. -/
+theorem assignmentPerm_code (l : ℕ) (hl : l ≤ 2) (mask a : Fin l → Bool) :
+    assignmentPerm l hl mask (assignmentEmbedding l hl a) =
+      assignmentEmbedding l hl (xorPerm mask a) := by
+  rw [assignmentPerm_eq, Equiv.Perm.viaFintypeEmbedding_apply_image]
+
+/-- The extension fixes every code `a ≥ 2^l`. In particular, arity zero fixes
+codes one through three, and arity one fixes codes two and three. -/
+theorem assignmentPerm_unused (l : ℕ) (hl : l ≤ 2) (mask : Fin l → Bool)
+    (a : Fin 4) (ha : 2 ^ l ≤ (a : ℕ)) : assignmentPerm l hl mask a = a := by
+  rw [assignmentPerm_eq]
+  apply Equiv.Perm.viaFintypeEmbedding_apply_notMem_range
+  rintro ⟨b, hb⟩
+  have hval := congrArg Fin.val hb
+  have hlt := assignmentEmbedding_lt_pow l hl b
+  omega
+
+/-- The padded key-choice mask is `v w` at real vertices and `false` elsewhere.
+`keyMask C S v w = if (w : ℕ) < C.size then v w else false`. -/
+def keyMask {m : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit m) (S : ℕ) (v : ℕ → Bool) (w : Fin S) : Bool :=
+  if (w : ℕ) < C.size then v w else false
+
+/-- `keyMask C S v w = if w < C.size then v w else false`. -/
+theorem keyMask_eq {m : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit m) (S : ℕ) (v : ℕ → Bool) (w : Fin S) :
+    keyMask C S v w = if (w : ℕ) < C.size then v w else false := rfl
+
+/-- A real vertex's padded key mask is its wire value: `keyMask ... w = v w`. -/
+theorem keyMask_vertex {m : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit m) (S : ℕ) (v : ℕ → Bool)
+    (hS : C.size ≤ S) (w : Fin C.size) :
+    keyMask C S v (Fin.castLE hS w) = v w := by
+  change (if (w : ℕ) < C.size then v w else false) = v w
+  rw [if_pos w.isLt]
+
+/-- The padded row permutation extends assignment XOR at each real gate and
+is the identity at unused gate coordinates.
+`rowPerm C S hA v i = if hi : (i : ℕ) < C.gates.length then assignmentPerm (C.gates[(i :
+ℕ)]'hi).args.length (hA ⟨i, hi⟩) (RelabelGarbling.argValues C v ⟨i, hi⟩) else Equiv.refl _`. -/
+def rowPerm {m : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit m) (S : ℕ)
+    (hA : ∀ i : Fin C.gates.length, (C.gates[i]).args.length ≤ 2)
+    (v : ℕ → Bool) (i : Fin S) : Equiv.Perm (Fin 4) :=
+  if hi : (i : ℕ) < C.gates.length then
+    assignmentPerm (C.gates[(i : ℕ)]'hi).args.length (hA ⟨i, hi⟩)
+      (RelabelGarbling.argValues C v ⟨i, hi⟩)
+  else Equiv.refl _
+
+/-- `rowPerm C S hA v i` is the real gate's extended assignment XOR, or the identity.
+`rowPerm C S hA v i = if hi : (i : ℕ) < C.gates.length then assignmentPerm (C.gates[(i :
+ℕ)]'hi).args.length (hA ⟨i, hi⟩) (RelabelGarbling.argValues C v ⟨i, hi⟩) else Equiv.refl _`. -/
+theorem rowPerm_eq {m : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit m) (S : ℕ)
+    (hA : ∀ i : Fin C.gates.length, (C.gates[i]).args.length ≤ 2)
+    (v : ℕ → Bool) (i : Fin S) :
+    rowPerm C S hA v i =
+      if hi : (i : ℕ) < C.gates.length then
+        assignmentPerm (C.gates[(i : ℕ)]'hi).args.length (hA ⟨i, hi⟩)
+          (RelabelGarbling.argValues C v ⟨i, hi⟩)
+      else Equiv.refl _ := rfl
+
+/-- At a real gate, the padded row permutation is precisely that gate's
+assignment-XOR extension.
+`rowPerm C S hA v (Fin.castLE hG i) = assignmentPerm (C.gates[i]).args.length (hA i)
+(RelabelGarbling.argValues C v i)`. -/
+theorem rowPerm_gate {m : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit m) (S : ℕ)
+    (hA : ∀ i : Fin C.gates.length, (C.gates[i]).args.length ≤ 2) (v : ℕ → Bool)
+    (hG : C.gates.length ≤ S) (i : Fin C.gates.length) :
+    rowPerm C S hA v (Fin.castLE hG i) =
+      assignmentPerm (C.gates[i]).args.length (hA i) (RelabelGarbling.argValues C v i) := by
+  change (if hi : (i : ℕ) < C.gates.length then
+    assignmentPerm (C.gates[(i : ℕ)]'hi).args.length (hA ⟨i, hi⟩)
+      (RelabelGarbling.argValues C v ⟨i, hi⟩)
+    else Equiv.refl _) = _
+  rw [dif_pos i.isLt]
+  simp only [Fin.getElem_fin]
+
+/-- On an embedded assignment code, the padded row permutation returns the
+binary fold of the XORed assignment at that gate.
+`(rowPerm C S hA v (Fin.castLE hG i) (assignmentEmbedding (C.gates[i]).args.length (hA i) a) : ℕ) =
+(List.ofFn (xorPerm (RelabelGarbling.argValues C v i) a)).foldr (fun b k => b.toNat + 2 * k) 0`. -/
+theorem rowPerm_fold {m : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit m) (S : ℕ)
+    (hA : ∀ i : Fin C.gates.length, (C.gates[i]).args.length ≤ 2) (v : ℕ → Bool)
+    (hG : C.gates.length ≤ S) (i : Fin C.gates.length)
+    (a : Fin (C.gates[i]).args.length → Bool) :
+    (rowPerm C S hA v (Fin.castLE hG i)
+        (assignmentEmbedding (C.gates[i]).args.length (hA i) a) : ℕ) =
+      (List.ofFn (xorPerm (RelabelGarbling.argValues C v i) a)).foldr
+        (fun b k => b.toNat + 2 * k) 0 := by
+  rw [rowPerm_gate, assignmentPerm_code, assignmentEmbedding_eq]
+
+/-- The padded shift mask is the value of the argument at a real gate/position,
+and is `false` at unused gate or argument positions.
+`shiftMask C S v i t = if hi : (i : ℕ) < C.gates.length then if ht : (t : ℕ) < (C.gates[(i :
+ℕ)]'hi).args.length then v ((C.gates[(i : ℕ)]'hi).args[(t : ℕ)]'ht) else false else false`. -/
+def shiftMask {m : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit m) (S : ℕ) (v : ℕ → Bool)
+    (i : Fin S) (t : Fin 2) : Bool :=
+  if hi : (i : ℕ) < C.gates.length then
+    if ht : (t : ℕ) < (C.gates[(i : ℕ)]'hi).args.length then
+      v ((C.gates[(i : ℕ)]'hi).args[(t : ℕ)]'ht)
+    else false
+  else false
+
+/-- `shiftMask` reads the real argument's wire value and returns `false` elsewhere.
+`shiftMask C S v i t = if hi : (i : ℕ) < C.gates.length then if ht : (t : ℕ) < (C.gates[(i :
+ℕ)]'hi).args.length then v ((C.gates[(i : ℕ)]'hi).args[(t : ℕ)]'ht) else false else false`. -/
+theorem shiftMask_eq {m : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit m) (S : ℕ) (v : ℕ → Bool)
+    (i : Fin S) (t : Fin 2) :
+    shiftMask C S v i t =
+      if hi : (i : ℕ) < C.gates.length then
+        if ht : (t : ℕ) < (C.gates[(i : ℕ)]'hi).args.length then
+          v ((C.gates[(i : ℕ)]'hi).args[(t : ℕ)]'ht)
+        else false
+      else false := rfl
+
+/-- At a real gate and argument position, the padded shift mask is the value
+of that argument's wire.
+`shiftMask C S v (Fin.castLE hG i) (Fin.castLE (hA i) t) = v ((C.gates[i]).args[t])`. -/
+theorem shiftMask_argument {m : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit m) (S : ℕ)
+    (hA : ∀ i : Fin C.gates.length, (C.gates[i]).args.length ≤ 2) (v : ℕ → Bool)
+    (hG : C.gates.length ≤ S) (i : Fin C.gates.length)
+    (t : Fin (C.gates[i]).args.length) :
+    shiftMask C S v (Fin.castLE hG i) (Fin.castLE (hA i) t) =
+      v ((C.gates[i]).args[t]) := by
+  change (if hi : (i : ℕ) < C.gates.length then
+    if ht : (t : ℕ) < (C.gates[(i : ℕ)]'hi).args.length then
+      v ((C.gates[(i : ℕ)]'hi).args[(t : ℕ)]'ht)
+    else false
+    else false) = _
+  rw [dif_pos i.isLt]
+  simp only [Fin.getElem_fin] at t ⊢
+  rw [dif_pos t.isLt]
+
+/-- Every unused key coordinate has mask `false`, so its entire block is fixed.
+`keyMask C S v w = false`. -/
+theorem keyMask_unused {m : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit m) (S : ℕ) (v : ℕ → Bool)
+    (w : Fin S) (hw : C.size ≤ (w : ℕ)) : keyMask C S v w = false := by
+  rw [keyMask_eq, if_neg (Nat.not_lt.mpr hw)]
+
+/-- At an unused gate coordinate, the padded row action is the identity.
+`rowPerm C S hA v i = Equiv.refl _`. -/
+theorem rowPerm_unused_gate {m : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit m) (S : ℕ)
+    (hA : ∀ i : Fin C.gates.length, (C.gates[i]).args.length ≤ 2) (v : ℕ → Bool)
+    (i : Fin S) (hi : C.gates.length ≤ (i : ℕ)) :
+    rowPerm C S hA v i = Equiv.refl _ := by
+  rw [rowPerm_eq, dif_neg (Nat.not_lt.mpr hi)]
+
+/-- At a real gate, every unused row code `a ≥ 2^arity` is fixed. -/
+theorem rowPerm_unused_code {m : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit m) (S : ℕ)
+    (hA : ∀ i : Fin C.gates.length, (C.gates[i]).args.length ≤ 2) (v : ℕ → Bool)
+    (hG : C.gates.length ≤ S) (i : Fin C.gates.length) (a : Fin 4)
+    (ha : 2 ^ (C.gates[i]).args.length ≤ (a : ℕ)) :
+    rowPerm C S hA v (Fin.castLE hG i) a = a := by
+  rw [rowPerm_gate]
+  exact assignmentPerm_unused _ (hA i) _ a ha
+
+/-- At an unused gate coordinate, every shift mask bit is `false`.
+`shiftMask C S v i t = false`. -/
+theorem shiftMask_unused_gate {m : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit m) (S : ℕ) (v : ℕ → Bool)
+    (i : Fin S) (t : Fin 2) (hi : C.gates.length ≤ (i : ℕ)) :
+    shiftMask C S v i t = false := by
+  rw [shiftMask_eq, dif_neg (Nat.not_lt.mpr hi)]
+
+/-- At a real gate but unused argument coordinate, the shift mask bit is `false`.
+`shiftMask C S v (Fin.castLE hG i) t = false`. -/
+theorem shiftMask_unused_argument {m : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit m) (S : ℕ) (v : ℕ → Bool)
+    (hG : C.gates.length ≤ S) (i : Fin C.gates.length) (t : Fin 2)
+    (ht : (C.gates[i]).args.length ≤ (t : ℕ)) :
+    shiftMask C S v (Fin.castLE hG i) t = false := by
+  change (if hi : (i : ℕ) < C.gates.length then
+    if ht' : (t : ℕ) < (C.gates[(i : ℕ)]'hi).args.length then
+      v ((C.gates[(i : ℕ)]'hi).args[(t : ℕ)]'ht')
+    else false
+    else false) = false
+  rw [dif_pos i.isLt]
+  simp only [Fin.getElem_fin] at ht
+  rw [dif_neg (Nat.not_lt.mpr ht)]
+
+/-- At each real vertex, relabeling changes the key tape address from choice `b`
+to choice `b XOR v w`, retaining its vertex and within-key coordinate.
+`(List.ofFn (CoinRelabel.relabel S n (keyMask C S v) (rowPerm C S hA v) (shiftMask C S v) r)).getD
+((b.toNat * S + w) * n + t) false = (List.ofFn r).getD (((xor b (v w)).toNat * S + w) * n + t)
+false`. -/
+theorem relabel_key_getD {m : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit m) (S n : ℕ)
+    (hA : ∀ i : Fin C.gates.length, (C.gates[i]).args.length ≤ 2) (v : ℕ → Bool)
+    (hS : C.size ≤ S) (r : Fin (10 * S * n + 2 * S) → Bool)
+    (w : Fin C.size) (b : Bool) (t : Fin n) :
+    (List.ofFn (CoinRelabel.relabel S n (keyMask C S v)
+      (rowPerm C S hA v) (shiftMask C S v) r)).getD
+        ((b.toNat * S + w) * n + t) false =
+      (List.ofFn r).getD (((xor b (v w)).toNat * S + w) * n + t) false := by
+  have h := CoinRelabel.relabel_key_getD S n (keyMask C S v)
+    (rowPerm C S hA v) (shiftMask C S v) r b (Fin.castLE hS w) t
+  rw [keyMask_vertex C S v hS w] at h
+  exact h
+
+/-- At a real gate, relabeling sends a row's nonce address to the binary fold
+of its argument-value-XORed assignment, leaving the within-row bit unchanged.
+`(List.ofFn (CoinRelabel.relabel S n (keyMask C S v) (rowPerm C S hA v) (shiftMask C S v) r)).getD
+(2 * S * n + (4 * i + (List.ofFn a).foldr (fun b k => b.toNat + 2 * k) 0) * (2 * n) + t) false =
+(List.ofFn r).getD (2 * S * n + (4 * i + (List.ofFn (xorPerm (RelabelGarbling.argValues C v i)
+a)).foldr (fun b k => b.toNat + 2 * k) 0) * (2 * n) + t) false`. -/
+theorem relabel_row_getD {m : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit m) (S n : ℕ)
+    (hA : ∀ i : Fin C.gates.length, (C.gates[i]).args.length ≤ 2) (v : ℕ → Bool)
+    (hG : C.gates.length ≤ S) (r : Fin (10 * S * n + 2 * S) → Bool)
+    (i : Fin C.gates.length) (a : Fin (C.gates[i]).args.length → Bool)
+    (t : Fin (2 * n)) :
+    (List.ofFn (CoinRelabel.relabel S n (keyMask C S v)
+      (rowPerm C S hA v) (shiftMask C S v) r)).getD
+        (2 * S * n + (4 * i +
+          (List.ofFn a).foldr (fun b k => b.toNat + 2 * k) 0) * (2 * n) + t) false =
+      (List.ofFn r).getD (2 * S * n + (4 * i +
+        (List.ofFn (xorPerm (RelabelGarbling.argValues C v i) a)).foldr
+          (fun b k => b.toNat + 2 * k) 0) * (2 * n) + t) false := by
+  have h := CoinRelabel.relabel_row_getD S n (keyMask C S v)
+    (rowPerm C S hA v) (shiftMask C S v) r (Fin.castLE hG i)
+      (assignmentEmbedding (C.gates[i]).args.length (hA i) a) t
+  rw [rowPerm_fold, assignmentEmbedding_eq] at h
+  exact h
+
+/-- At a real gate and argument, the relabeled shift tape bit is the original
+bit XOR the value of the wire at that argument. The result includes `n = 0`. -/
+theorem relabel_shift_getD {m : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit m) (S n : ℕ)
+    (hA : ∀ i : Fin C.gates.length, (C.gates[i]).args.length ≤ 2) (v : ℕ → Bool)
+    (hG : C.gates.length ≤ S) (r : Fin (10 * S * n + 2 * S) → Bool)
+    (i : Fin C.gates.length) (t : Fin (C.gates[i]).args.length) :
+    (List.ofFn (CoinRelabel.relabel S n (keyMask C S v)
+      (rowPerm C S hA v) (shiftMask C S v) r)).getD
+        (10 * S * n + 2 * i + t) false =
+      xor ((List.ofFn r).getD (10 * S * n + 2 * i + t) false)
+        (v ((C.gates[i]).args[t])) := by
+  have h := CoinRelabel.relabel_shift_getD S n (keyMask C S v)
+    (rowPerm C S hA v) (shiftMask C S v) r (Fin.castLE hG i) (Fin.castLE (hA i) t)
+  rw [shiftMask_argument] at h
+  exact h
+
+/-- A seed specified by the key, two nonce halves and permutation-bit readers
+commutes exactly with complete-tape relabeling. No positive key width is assumed.
+This identifies the actual sampler with the generic seed relabeling, rather than
+introducing a second sampler. [PS10, Theorem 163.7]
+`seed (List.ofFn (CoinRelabel.relabel S n (keyMask C S v) (rowPerm C S hA v) (shiftMask C S v) r)) =
+RelabelGarbling.relabelSeed C v (seed (List.ofFn r))`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** Equality of seeds reduces to equality of keys, row-coin pairs
+and row permutations. Each key coordinate follows the key-address transformation.
+Each nonce half follows the row-address transformation, using the embedded binary
+assignment code. The shift-bit transformation composes the old assignment XOR
+with argument-value XOR, which is exactly the relabeled permutation. -/
+theorem seed_relabel {m : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit m) (S n : ℕ)
+    (hA : ∀ i : Fin C.gates.length, (C.gates[i]).args.length ≤ 2) (v : ℕ → Bool)
+    (hS : C.size ≤ S)
+    (seed : List Bool → GarbleSeed C (Fin n → Bool) ((Fin n → Bool) × (Fin n → Bool)))
+    (hK : ∀ q (w : Fin C.size) b (t : Fin n),
+      (seed q).1 w b t = q.getD ((b.toNat * S + w) * n + t) false)
+    (hR₀ : ∀ q (i : Fin C.gates.length) (a : Fin (C.gates[i]).args.length → Bool)
+        (t : Fin n),
+      ((seed q).2.1 i a).1 t = q.getD (2 * S * n + (4 * i +
+        (List.ofFn a).foldr (fun b k => b.toNat + 2 * k) 0) * (2 * n) + t) false)
+    (hR₁ : ∀ q (i : Fin C.gates.length) (a : Fin (C.gates[i]).args.length → Bool)
+        (t : Fin n),
+      ((seed q).2.1 i a).2 t = q.getD (2 * S * n + (4 * i +
+        (List.ofFn a).foldr (fun b k => b.toNat + 2 * k) 0) * (2 * n) + n + t) false)
+    (hP : ∀ q (i : Fin C.gates.length) (a : Fin (C.gates[i]).args.length → Bool)
+        (t : Fin (C.gates[i]).args.length),
+      (seed q).2.2 i a t = xor (a t) (q.getD (10 * S * n + 2 * i + t) false))
+    (r : Fin (10 * S * n + 2 * S) → Bool) :
+    seed (List.ofFn (CoinRelabel.relabel S n (keyMask C S v)
+        (rowPerm C S hA v) (shiftMask C S v) r)) =
+      RelabelGarbling.relabelSeed C v (seed (List.ofFn r)) := by
+  have hG : C.gates.length ≤ S := by
+    rw [DAGCircuit.size_eq] at hS
+    omega
+  apply Prod.ext
+  · funext w b t
+    rw [RelabelGarbling.relabelSeed_key, hK, hK]
+    exact relabel_key_getD C S n hA v hS r w b t
+  · apply Prod.ext
+    · funext i a
+      rw [RelabelGarbling.relabelSeed_coin]
+      apply Prod.ext
+      · funext t
+        rw [hR₀, hR₀]
+        exact relabel_row_getD C S n hA v hG r i a
+          ⟨t, by have := t.isLt; omega⟩
+      · funext t
+        rw [hR₁, hR₁]
+        simpa only [Nat.add_assoc] using
+          relabel_row_getD C S n hA v hG r i a
+            ⟨n + t, by have := t.isLt; omega⟩
+    · funext i
+      apply Equiv.ext
+      intro a
+      funext t
+      rw [RelabelGarbling.relabelSeed_perm]
+      simp only [xorPerm_apply, hP, relabel_shift_getD C S n hA v hG r i t,
+        RelabelGarbling.argValues_apply, Bool.xor_assoc]
+
+end TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof.CoinSeedRelabel

+Released under Apache 2.0 license as described in the file LICENSE.

+import TCSlib.Cryptography.Pseudorandom.Indistinguishability
+
+set_option maxHeartbeats 0
+set_option relaxedAutoImplicit false
+set_option autoImplicit false

+/-!
+# Re-randomizing a partial family of seed coordinates
+
+An unchanged control value chooses a partial matching between full-seed coordinates and
+independent challenge coordinates. Swapping each matched pair is an involution. Unmatched
+full coordinates and unused padded challenge slots remain unchanged. Values are never
+compared, so repeated actual key or nonce values need no separate treatment.
+
+## Main definitions
+
+* `CoordinateSwap.swap`: the simultaneous matched-coordinate exchange.
+* `CoordinateSwap.equiv`: its self-inverse joint-seed bijection.
+
+## Main results
+
+* `CoordinateSwap.swap_involutive`: the exchange is its own inverse.
+* `acceptProb_swap`, `acceptProb_fill`: exact generic and transported acceptance laws.
+
+## References
+
+* [PS10] R. Pass, a. shelat, *A Course in Cryptography*, lecture notes, 3rd ed., January
+  2010, Theorem 163.7 (independent inactive-key and encryption-coin resampling).
+
+This is a technical coordinate formulation for that simulation. It reuses the existing
+finite-probability relabeling contracts rather than proving a new counting identity.
+-/
+
+namespace TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof.CoordinateSwap
+
+variable {P I J V : Type*}
+
+/-- Exchange every full coordinate selected by `pick` with its challenge coordinate,
+using `place` for the reverse direction. The control value stays fixed, and `none` retains
+that side's original value, including every unused padded challenge slot.
+`swap pick place s = ((s.1.1, fun i => (pick s.1.1 i).elim (s.1.2 i) (fun j => s.2 j)), fun j =>
+(place s.1.1 j).elim (s.2 j) (fun i => s.1.2 i))`. -/
+@[simps (config := { isSimp := false })]
+def swap (pick : P → I → Option J) (place : P → J → Option I)
+    (s : (P × (I → V)) × (J → V)) : (P × (I → V)) × (J → V) :=
+  ((s.1.1, fun i => (pick s.1.1 i).elim (s.1.2 i) (fun j => s.2 j)),
+    fun j => (place s.1.1 j).elim (s.2 j) (fun i => s.1.2 i))
+
+/-- `swap pick place s` retains `s.control`, exchanges matched full/challenge coordinates,
+and retains a side's original coordinate when its selector returns `none`.
+`swap pick place s = ((s.1.1, fun i => (pick s.1.1 i).elim (s.1.2 i) (fun j => s.2 j)), fun j =>
+(place s.1.1 j).elim (s.2 j) (fun i => s.1.2 i))`. -/
+theorem swap_eq (pick : P → I → Option J) (place : P → J → Option I)
+    (s : (P × (I → V)) × (J → V)) :
+    swap pick place s =
+      ((s.1.1, fun i => (pick s.1.1 i).elim (s.1.2 i) (fun j => s.2 j)),
+        fun j => (place s.1.1 j).elim (s.2 j) (fun i => s.1.2 i)) := rfl
+
+/-- If `pick p i = some j` exactly when `place p j = some i`, the simultaneous exchange
+is an involution. No distinctness or decidable equality of coordinate values is required.
+
+**Proof sketch.** The control value is unchanged. At a selected full coordinate, the first
+exchange reads the challenge coordinate, and the matching condition makes the second read
+its original full value. An unselected coordinate is retained twice. The same two cases
+on a challenge coordinate recover its original value; this includes every padded slot
+whose reverse selector is `none`. Function and product extensionality assemble the result. -/
+theorem swap_involutive (pick : P → I → Option J) (place : P → J → Option I)
+    (hmatch : ∀ p i j, pick p i = some j ↔ place p j = some i) :
+    Function.Involutive (swap pick place : ((P × (I → V)) × (J → V)) → _) := by
+  rintro ⟨⟨p, full⟩, challenge⟩
+  apply Prod.ext
+  · apply Prod.ext
+    · rfl
+    · funext i
+      cases h : pick p i with
+      | none => simp only [swap_eq, h, Option.elim_none]
+      | some j =>
+        have h' := (hmatch p i j).mp h
+        simp only [swap_eq, h, h', Option.elim_some]
+  · funext j
+    cases h : place p j with
+    | none => simp only [swap_eq, h, Option.elim_none]
+    | some i =>
+      have h' := (hmatch p i j).mpr h
+      simp only [swap_eq, h, h', Option.elim_some]
+
+/-- The matched-coordinate exchange is a bijection of the joint full/challenge seed space.
+Its inverse is the same exchange; the control value remains fixed.
+`equiv pick place hmatch s = swap pick place s` and `(equiv pick place hmatch).symm s = swap pick
+place s`. -/
+@[simps (config := { isSimp := false }) apply symm_apply]
+def equiv (pick : P → I → Option J) (place : P → J → Option I)
+    (hmatch : ∀ p i j, pick p i = some j ↔ place p j = some i) :
+    ((P × (I → V)) × (J → V)) ≃ ((P × (I → V)) × (J → V)) where
+  toFun := swap pick place
+  invFun := swap pick place
+  left_inv := swap_involutive pick place hmatch
+  right_inv := swap_involutive pick place hmatch
+
+/-- Applying `equiv pick place hmatch` is exactly `swap pick place`.
+`equiv pick place hmatch s = swap pick place s`. -/
+theorem equiv_apply_eq (pick : P → I → Option J) (place : P → J → Option I)
+    (hmatch : ∀ p i j, pick p i = some j ↔ place p j = some i)
+    (s : (P × (I → V)) × (J → V)) :
+    equiv pick place hmatch s = swap pick place s := rfl
+
+/-- Applying the inverse of `equiv pick place hmatch` is the same `swap pick place`.
+`(equiv pick place hmatch).symm s = swap pick place s`. -/
+theorem equiv_symm_apply_eq (pick : P → I → Option J) (place : P → J → Option I)
+    (hmatch : ∀ p i j, pick p i = some j ↔ place p j = some i)
+    (s : (P × (I → V)) × (J → V)) :
+    (equiv pick place hmatch).symm s = swap pick place s := rfl
+
+/-- Replacing the matched coordinates of an independent uniform full seed by uniform
+challenge coordinates preserves every sampler acceptance probability. Unmatched challenge
+slots may be padded, and coordinate values may repeat. Only the full coordinate spaces need
+finite enumerations; the control and address types are not separately enumerated.
+[PS10, Theorem 163.7]
+`acceptProb (fun s : (P × (I → V)) × (J → V) => X (swap pick place s).1) D = acceptProb X D`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** Relabel the joint uniform sample by the proved exchange bijection.
+The sampler then sees just the original full-seed component. Remove its independent unused
+challenge component with the existing ignored-coordinate probability contract. -/
+theorem acceptProb_swap [Fintype (P × (I → V))] [Fintype (J → V)]
+    [Nonempty (J → V)] {γ ρ : Type*} [Fintype ρ]
+    (pick : P → I → Option J) (place : P → J → Option I)
+    (hmatch : ∀ p i j, pick p i = some j ↔ place p j = some i)
+    (X : (P × (I → V)) → γ) (D : γ → ρ → Bool) :
+    acceptProb (fun s : (P × (I → V)) × (J → V) => X (swap pick place s).1) D =
+      acceptProb X D := by
+  have h := acceptProb_comp_equiv (equiv pick place hmatch)
+    (fun s : (P × (I → V)) × (J → V) => X s.1) D
+  simp only [equiv_apply_eq] at h
+  exact h.trans (acceptProb_fst X D)
+
+/-- A concrete fill preserves the full seed's acceptance law when coordinate equivalences
+identify it with the matched exchange. The challenge may have any equivalent finite shape,
+including a key paired with padded nonce slots. [PS10, Theorem 163.7]
+`acceptProb (fun z : S × T => X (fill z.1 z.2)) D = acceptProb X D`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** Relabel the concrete full/challenge product by its two coordinate
+bijections. The supplied fill equation identifies its full output with the first component
+of the generic exchange. Apply the proved exchange law, then relabel the full coordinates
+back to the original seed type. All probability steps use existing equivalence contracts. -/
+theorem acceptProb_fill [Fintype P] [Fintype I] [Fintype J] [Fintype V]
+    [DecidableEq I] [DecidableEq J]
+    {S T γ ρ : Type*} [Fintype S] [Fintype T] [Nonempty T] [Fintype ρ]
+    (pick : P → I → Option J) (place : P → J → Option I)
+    (hmatch : ∀ p i j, pick p i = some j ↔ place p j = some i)
+    (seedEquiv : S ≃ (P × (I → V))) (challengeEquiv : T ≃ (J → V))
+    (fill : S → T → S)
+    (hfill : ∀ s c, seedEquiv (fill s c) =
+      (swap pick place (seedEquiv s, challengeEquiv c)).1)
+    (X : S → γ) (D : γ → ρ → Bool) :
+    acceptProb (fun z : S × T => X (fill z.1 z.2)) D = acceptProb X D := by
+  letI : Nonempty (J → V) := Nonempty.map challengeEquiv (inferInstance : Nonempty T)
+  have hRelabel := acceptProb_comp_equiv (seedEquiv.prodCongr challengeEquiv)
+    (fun w : (P × (I → V)) × (J → V) => X (seedEquiv.symm (swap pick place w).1)) D
+  have hPoint : (fun z : S × T => X (fill z.1 z.2)) =
+      (fun z => X (seedEquiv.symm
+        (swap pick place ((seedEquiv.prodCongr challengeEquiv) z)).1)) := by
+    funext z
+    rw [Equiv.prodCongr_apply, Prod.map_apply', ← hfill z.1 z.2, Equiv.symm_apply_apply]
+  rw [hPoint]
+  exact hRelabel.trans ((acceptProb_swap pick place hmatch
+    (fun w => X (seedEquiv.symm w)) D).trans (acceptProb_comp_equiv seedEquiv.symm X D))
+
+end TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof.CoordinateSwap

+/-
+Copyright (c) 2026 TCSlib contributors. All rights reserved.
+Released under Apache 2.0 license as described in the file LICENSE.
+-/
+import TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.GarblingSpecial
+import TCSlib.Complexity.ClassP.BinaryArith.Words
+
+set_option maxHeartbeats 0
+set_option relaxedAutoImplicit false
+set_option autoImplicit false
+
+/-!
+# Tagged special decryption at arbitrary message lengths
+
+The published decryption adapter specializes the message length to the key length.
+A nested garbled row also needs the outer message length `4n + 2`. This module
+provides one adapter whose key and message lengths are separate unary input words.
+Failure is `[]` and a successful message `m` is `true :: m`, including the empty message.
+
+## Main definitions
+
+* `YaoHybridProof.specialDecTagged`: tagged decryption on `⟨N, ⟨L, ⟨k, c⟩⟩⟩`.
+
+## Main results
+
+* `YaoHybridProof.specialDecTagged_poly`: polynomial time on every input word.
+
+## References
+
+* [PS10] R. Pass, a. shelat, *A Course in Cryptography*, lecture notes, 3rd ed.,
+  January 2010, Algorithm 157.2 and Protocol 162.6.
+-/
+
+namespace TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof
+
+open Complexity Turing
+
+/-- Tagged special decryption on `⟨N, ⟨L, ⟨k, c⟩⟩⟩`: run `spDecStr E |N| |L| k c`,
+returning `[]` on failure and `true :: m` on success with message `m`.
+The unary lengths are separate, so the adapter covers either encryption layer.
+[PS10, Algorithm 157.2]
+`specialDecTagged E z = match spDecStr E (pairFstD z).length (pairFstD (pairSndD z)).length
+(pairFstD (pairSndD (pairSndD z))) (pairSndD (pairSndD (pairSndD z))) with | none => [] | some m =>
+true :: m`. -/
+def specialDecTagged (E : List Bool → List Bool) (z : List Bool) : List Bool :=
+  match spDecStr E (pairFstD z).length (pairFstD (pairSndD z)).length
+      (pairFstD (pairSndD (pairSndD z))) (pairSndD (pairSndD (pairSndD z))) with
+  | none => []
+  | some m => true :: m
+
+/-- For every `z`, `specialDecTagged E z` encodes the result of decryption with
+key length `|fst z|`, message length `|fst (snd z)|`, key `fst (snd (snd z))`
+and ciphertext `snd (snd (snd z))` by `none ↦ []`, `some m ↦ true :: m`.
+`specialDecTagged E z = match spDecStr E (pairFstD z).length (pairFstD (pairSndD z)).length
+(pairFstD (pairSndD (pairSndD z))) (pairSndD (pairSndD (pairSndD z))) with | none => [] | some m =>
+true :: m`. -/
+theorem specialDecTagged_eq (E : List Bool → List Bool) (z : List Bool) :
+    specialDecTagged E z =
+      match spDecStr E (pairFstD z).length (pairFstD (pairSndD z)).length
+          (pairFstD (pairSndD (pairSndD z))) (pairSndD (pairSndD (pairSndD z))) with
+      | none => []
+      | some m => true :: m := rfl
+
+/-- For a polynomial-time evaluator `E`, `specialDecTagged E` is polynomial-time
+on every word, with independent unary key and message lengths.
+[PS10, Algorithm 157.2]
+`PolyTimeComputable (specialDecTagged E)`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** Read the two unary lengths, key, nonce and ciphertext body.
+Test the three shape equalities. Compute the pad and compare its key-length
+prefix with the ciphertext prefix. If both tests pass, append a success marker
+to the XOR of the suffixes; otherwise return the empty failure word. Each
+operation uses the published pairing, length, cut and XOR closure contracts. -/
+theorem specialDecTagged_poly {E : List Bool → List Bool} (hE : PolyTimeComputable E) :
+    PolyTimeComputable (specialDecTagged E) := by
+  have hN := polyTimeComputable_pairFstD
+  have hL := polyTimeComputable_pairFstD.comp polyTimeComputable_pairSndD
+  have hkc := polyTimeComputable_pairSndD.comp polyTimeComputable_pairSndD
+  have hk := polyTimeComputable_pairFstD.comp hkc
+  have hct := polyTimeComputable_pairSndD.comp hkc
+  have hr := polyTimeComputable_pairFstD.comp hct
+  have hc := polyTimeComputable_pairSndD.comp hct
+  have hp := hE.comp (hk.pairEncode hr)
+  have h1 := polyTimeComputable_lenEq.comp (hk.pairEncode hN)
+  have h2 := polyTimeComputable_lenEq.comp (hr.pairEncode hN)
+  have h3 := polyTimeComputable_lenEq.comp (hc.pairEncode (hN.append hL))
+  have hguard := polyTimeComputable_and h1 (polyTimeComputable_and h2 h3)
+  have hctake := (polyTimeComputable_pairTakePoly .var).comp (hN.pairEncode hc)
+  have hptake := (polyTimeComputable_pairTakePoly .var).comp (hN.pairEncode hp)
+  have hcDrop := (polyTimeComputable_pairDropPoly .var).comp (hN.pairEncode hc)
+  have hpDrop := (polyTimeComputable_pairDropPoly .var).comp (hN.pairEncode hp)
+  have heq := polyTimeComputable_pairEq.comp (hctake.pairEncode hptake)
+  have hxor := polyTimeComputable_pairXor.comp (hcDrop.pairEncode hpDrop)
+  have hgood := (polyTimeComputable_prepend [true]).comp hxor
+  have h := polyTimeComputable_ite hguard
+    (polyTimeComputable_ite heq hgood (polyTimeComputable_const []))
+    (polyTimeComputable_const [])
+  convert h using 1
+  funext z
+  simp only [specialDecTagged_eq, spDecStr_eq, Function.comp_apply,
+    pairFstD_pairEncode, pairSndD_pairEncode, PolyExpr.eval_var, List.length_append,
+    ← Bool.decide_and, decide_eq_true_eq]
+  split_ifs <;> rfl
+
+/-- On the explicit layout, `specialDecTagged E ⟨N, ⟨L, ⟨k, c⟩⟩⟩` is
+`[]` if `spDecStr E |N| |L| k c` fails and `true :: m` if it returns `m`.
+`specialDecTagged E (pairEncode N (pairEncode L (pairEncode k c))) = match spDecStr E N.length
+L.length k c with | none => [] | some m => true :: m`. -/
+theorem specialDecTagged_pairEncode (E : List Bool → List Bool) (N L k c : List Bool) :
+    specialDecTagged E (pairEncode N (pairEncode L (pairEncode k c))) =
+      match spDecStr E N.length L.length k c with
+      | none => []
+      | some m => true :: m := by
+  rw [specialDecTagged_eq]
+  simp only [pairFstD_pairEncode, pairSndD_pairEncode]
+
+/-- Apply a word function `F` to the payload of a successful tagged word:
+`bindTagged F [] = []`, and `bindTagged F (b :: m) = F m`. -/
+def bindTagged (F : List Bool → List Bool) (w : List Bool) : List Bool :=
+  if w = [] then [] else F (w.drop 1)
+
+/-- For every `w`, `bindTagged F w = if w = [] then [] else F (w.drop 1)`. -/
+theorem bindTagged_eq (F : List Bool → List Bool) (w : List Bool) :
+    bindTagged F w = if w = [] then [] else F (w.drop 1) := rfl
+
+/-- On a tagged optional payload, `bindTagged` applies `F` exactly in the success case.
+`bindTagged F (match o with | none => [] | some m => true :: m) = match o with | none => [] | some m
+=> F m`. -/
+theorem bindTagged_option (F : List Bool → List Bool) (o : Option (List Bool)) :
+    bindTagged F (match o with | none => [] | some m => true :: m) =
+      match o with | none => [] | some m => F m := by
+  cases o with
+  | none => rw [bindTagged_eq, if_pos rfl]
+  | some m =>
+    rw [bindTagged_eq, if_neg (List.cons_ne_nil true m)]
+    rfl
+
+/-- If `F` is polynomial-time, then `bindTagged F` is polynomial-time on all words.
+`PolyTimeComputable (bindTagged F)`. -/
+theorem bindTagged_poly {F : List Bool → List Bool} (hF : PolyTimeComputable F) :
+    PolyTimeComputable (bindTagged F) := by
+  simpa only [bindTagged_eq, Function.comp_apply, decide_eq_true_eq] using
+    polyTimeComputable_ite polyTimeComputable_isNil (polyTimeComputable_const [])
+      (hF.comp polyTimeComputable_tail)
+
+/-- Check a tagged word at `⟨N, w⟩`: reject the empty failure word and
+wrong-length payloads, and return `true :: payload` when its length is `|N|`.
+`checkFinTagged z = if pairSndD z = [] then [] else if ((pairSndD z).drop 1).length = (pairFstD
+z).length then true :: (pairSndD z).drop 1 else []`. -/
+def checkFinTagged (z : List Bool) : List Bool :=
+  if pairSndD z = [] then []
+  else if ((pairSndD z).drop 1).length = (pairFstD z).length then
+    true :: (pairSndD z).drop 1 else []
+
+/-- For every `z`, `checkFinTagged z` succeeds exactly when `snd z` is nonempty
+and its payload `drop 1 (snd z)` has length `|fst z|`.
+`checkFinTagged z = if pairSndD z = [] then [] else if ((pairSndD z).drop 1).length = (pairFstD
+z).length then true :: (pairSndD z).drop 1 else []`. -/
+theorem checkFinTagged_eq (z : List Bool) :
+    checkFinTagged z = if pairSndD z = [] then []
+      else if ((pairSndD z).drop 1).length = (pairFstD z).length then
+        true :: (pairSndD z).drop 1 else [] := rfl
+
+/-- Checking the tagged encoding of `o : Option (List Bool)` produces the tagged
+encoding of `o.bind (decodeFin |N|)`, with a decoded key represented by `List.ofFn`.
+`checkFinTagged (pairEncode N (match o with | none => [] | some m => true :: m)) = match o.bind
+(TCSlib.Cryptography.decodeFin N.length) with | none => [] | some k => true :: List.ofFn k`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** Failure stays failure. For a successful string, split on its
+`decodeFin` result. A failed decode has the wrong length by the decoder reader;
+a successful decode identifies the string with `List.ofFn` of its key by the
+published exact-decoding characterization. -/
+theorem checkFinTagged_option (N : List Bool) (o : Option (List Bool)) :
+    checkFinTagged (pairEncode N (match o with | none => [] | some m => true :: m)) =
+      match o.bind (TCSlib.Cryptography.decodeFin N.length) with
+      | none => []
+      | some k => true :: List.ofFn k := by
+  cases o with
+  | none =>
+    rw [checkFinTagged_eq, pairSndD_pairEncode, if_pos rfl]
+    rfl
+  | some m =>
+    rw [checkFinTagged_eq, pairFstD_pairEncode, pairSndD_pairEncode,
+      if_neg (List.cons_ne_nil true m), List.drop_succ_cons, List.drop_zero, Option.bind_some]
+    cases hd : TCSlib.Cryptography.decodeFin N.length m with
+    | none =>
+      have hne : m.length ≠ N.length := by
+        intro hm
+        rw [TCSlib.Cryptography.decodeFin_eq, dif_pos hm] at hd
+        contradiction
+      rw [if_neg hne]
+    | some k =>
+      have hm := (TCSlib.Cryptography.decodeFin_eq_some_iff N.length m k).mp hd
+      rw [hm, List.length_ofFn, if_pos rfl]
+
+/-- The exact-length tagged key check `checkFinTagged` is polynomial-time on all words.
+`PolyTimeComputable checkFinTagged`. -/
+theorem checkFinTagged_poly : PolyTimeComputable checkFinTagged := by
+  have hp := polyTimeComputable_tail.comp polyTimeComputable_pairSndD
+  have ht := polyTimeComputable_lenEq.comp (hp.pairEncode polyTimeComputable_pairFstD)
+  have hg := (polyTimeComputable_prepend [true]).comp hp
+  have h := polyTimeComputable_ite
+    (polyTimeComputable_isNil.comp polyTimeComputable_pairSndD)
+    (polyTimeComputable_const [])
+    (polyTimeComputable_ite ht hg (polyTimeComputable_const []))
+  convert h using 1
+  funext z
+  simp only [checkFinTagged_eq, Function.comp_apply, pairFstD_pairEncode,
+    pairSndD_pairEncode, decide_eq_true_eq]
+  split_ifs <;> rfl
+
+/-- Nested special decryption on `⟨N, ⟨keys, c⟩⟩`, with `keys` an encoded word list.
+Zero keys check the cleartext; one key uses `E₁`; two keys remove the `E₂` outer
+layer at message length `4|N| + 2`, then the `E₁` inner layer. Other key counts
+fail. Successful keys are tagged as `true :: key`; failure is `[]`.
+[PS10, Protocol 162.6]
+`nestedDecTagged E₁ E₂ z = let N := pairFstD z; let keys := pairFstD (pairSndD z); let c := pairSndD
+(pairSndD z); if keys = [] then checkFinTagged (pairEncode N (true :: c)) else if pairSndD keys = []
+then checkFinTagged (pairEncode N (specialDecTagged E₁ (pairEncode N (pairEncode N (pairEncode
+(pairFstD keys) c))))) else if pairSndD (pairSndD keys) = [] then let outer := specialDecTagged E₂
+(pairEncode N (pairEncode (List.replicate (innerLen.eval N.length) true) (pairEncode (pairFstD keys)
+c))); if outer = [] then [] else checkFinTagged (pairEncode N (specialDecTagged E₁ (pairEncode N
+(pairEncode N (pairEncode (pairFstD (pairSndD keys)) (outer.drop 1)))))) else []`. -/
+def nestedDecTagged (E₁ E₂ : List Bool → List Bool) (z : List Bool) : List Bool :=
+  let N := pairFstD z
+  let keys := pairFstD (pairSndD z)
+  let c := pairSndD (pairSndD z)
+  if keys = [] then checkFinTagged (pairEncode N (true :: c))
+  else if pairSndD keys = [] then
+    checkFinTagged (pairEncode N
+      (specialDecTagged E₁ (pairEncode N (pairEncode N (pairEncode (pairFstD keys) c)))))
+  else if pairSndD (pairSndD keys) = [] then
+    let outer := specialDecTagged E₂ (pairEncode N
+      (pairEncode (List.replicate (innerLen.eval N.length) true)
+        (pairEncode (pairFstD keys) c)))
+    if outer = [] then [] else
+      checkFinTagged (pairEncode N
+        (specialDecTagged E₁ (pairEncode N
+          (pairEncode N (pairEncode (pairFstD (pairSndD keys)) (outer.drop 1))))))
+  else []
+
+/-- For every input `z`, `nestedDecTagged E₁ E₂ z` branches on zero, one or two
+encoded keys and performs the corresponding tagged special decryptions and
+exact key-length check.
+`nestedDecTagged E₁ E₂ z = let N := pairFstD z; let keys := pairFstD (pairSndD z); let c := pairSndD
+(pairSndD z); if keys = [] then checkFinTagged (pairEncode N (true :: c)) else if pairSndD keys = []
+then checkFinTagged (pairEncode N (specialDecTagged E₁ (pairEncode N (pairEncode N (pairEncode
+(pairFstD keys) c))))) else if pairSndD (pairSndD keys) = [] then let outer := specialDecTagged E₂
+(pairEncode N (pairEncode (List.replicate (innerLen.eval N.length) true) (pairEncode (pairFstD keys)
+c))); if outer = [] then [] else checkFinTagged (pairEncode N (specialDecTagged E₁ (pairEncode N
+(pairEncode N (pairEncode (pairFstD (pairSndD keys)) (outer.drop 1)))))) else []`. -/
+theorem nestedDecTagged_eq (E₁ E₂ : List Bool → List Bool) (z : List Bool) :
+    nestedDecTagged E₁ E₂ z =
+  let N := pairFstD z
+  let keys := pairFstD (pairSndD z)
+  let c := pairSndD (pairSndD z)
+  if keys = [] then checkFinTagged (pairEncode N (true :: c))
+  else if pairSndD keys = [] then
+    checkFinTagged (pairEncode N
+      (specialDecTagged E₁ (pairEncode N (pairEncode N (pairEncode (pairFstD keys) c)))))
+  else if pairSndD (pairSndD keys) = [] then
+    let outer := specialDecTagged E₂ (pairEncode N
+      (pairEncode (List.replicate (innerLen.eval N.length) true)
+        (pairEncode (pairFstD keys) c)))
+    if outer = [] then [] else
+      checkFinTagged (pairEncode N
+        (specialDecTagged E₁ (pairEncode N
+          (pairEncode N (pairEncode (pairFstD (pairSndD keys)) (outer.drop 1))))))
+  else [] := rfl
+
+/-- On the encoded key list `ks`, `nestedDecTagged E₁ E₂ ⟨N, ⟨keys, c⟩⟩`
+is `[]` when `(nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ |N|).dec ks c` fails and is
+`true :: List.ofFn k` when it returns key `k`. [PS10, Protocol 162.6]
+`nestedDecTagged E₁ E₂ (pairEncode N (pairEncode (BinArith.encWords (ks.map List.ofFn)) c)) = match
+(nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ N.length).dec ks c with | none => [] | some k => true :: List.ofFn k`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** Split into zero, one, two and more keys. The list-code readers
+select the corresponding branch. The exact-length checker implements `decodeFin`.
+In the two-key case split on the outer decryption result; failure is preserved,
+while success exposes exactly the ciphertext on which the inner layer is called. -/
+theorem nestedDecTagged_encoded (E₁ E₂ : List Bool → List Bool) (N : List Bool)
+    (ks : List (Fin N.length → Bool)) (c : List Bool) :
+    nestedDecTagged E₁ E₂
+        (pairEncode N (pairEncode (BinArith.encWords (ks.map List.ofFn)) c)) =
+      match (nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ N.length).dec ks c with
+      | none => []
+      | some k => true :: List.ofFn k := by
+  cases ks with
+  | nil =>
+    rw [nestedDecTagged_eq, nestedSpecial_dec]
+    simp only [List.map_nil, BinArith.encWords_nil, pairFstD_pairEncode,
+      pairSndD_pairEncode, if_true]
+    exact checkFinTagged_option N (some c)
+  | cons k₁ ks =>
+    cases ks with
+    | nil =>
+      rw [nestedDecTagged_eq, nestedSpecial_dec]
+      simp only [List.map_nil, List.map_cons, BinArith.encWords_nil,
+        BinArith.encWords_cons, pairFstD_pairEncode, pairSndD_pairEncode,
+        pairEncode_ne_nil, if_false, if_true]
+      rw [specialDecTagged_pairEncode]
+      exact checkFinTagged_option N (spDecStr E₁ N.length N.length (List.ofFn k₁) c)
+    | cons k₂ ks =>
+      cases ks with
+      | nil =>
+        rw [nestedDecTagged_eq, nestedSpecial_dec]
+        simp only [List.map_nil, List.map_cons, BinArith.encWords_nil,
+          BinArith.encWords_cons, pairFstD_pairEncode, pairSndD_pairEncode,
+          pairEncode_ne_nil, if_false, if_true, specialDecTagged_pairEncode,
+          List.length_replicate]
+        cases ho : spDecStr E₂ N.length (innerLen.eval N.length) (List.ofFn k₁) c with
+        | none => simp only [if_true, Option.bind_none]
+        | some m =>
+          simp only [List.cons_ne_nil, if_false, List.drop_succ_cons, List.drop_zero,
+            Option.bind_some]
+          exact checkFinTagged_option N (spDecStr E₁ N.length N.length (List.ofFn k₂) m)
+      | cons k₃ ks =>
+        rw [nestedDecTagged_eq, nestedSpecial_dec]
+        simp only [List.map_cons, BinArith.encWords_cons, pairFstD_pairEncode,
+          pairSndD_pairEncode, pairEncode_ne_nil, if_false]
+
+/-- If `E₁` and `E₂` are polynomial-time, then `nestedDecTagged E₁ E₂`
+is polynomial-time on every input, including malformed key lists and ciphertexts.
+[PS10, Protocol 162.6]
+`PolyTimeComputable (nestedDecTagged E₁ E₂)`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** Pair projections read the parameter, keys and ciphertext.
+Emptiness tests distinguish the four key-count cases. The no-key branch uses the
+exact-length decoder; the one-key branch calls the inner decryption adapter.
+The two-key branch computes the unary outer-message length `4n + 2`, calls the
+outer adapter, preserves its failure marker and otherwise calls the inner one.
+All branches use the already proved total tagged adapters and FP closure. -/
+theorem nestedDecTagged_poly {E₁ E₂ : List Bool → List Bool}
+    (hE₁ : PolyTimeComputable E₁) (hE₂ : PolyTimeComputable E₂) :
+    PolyTimeComputable (nestedDecTagged E₁ E₂) := by
+  have hN := polyTimeComputable_pairFstD
+  have hkeys := polyTimeComputable_pairFstD.comp polyTimeComputable_pairSndD
+  have hc := polyTimeComputable_pairSndD.comp polyTimeComputable_pairSndD
+  have hk₁ := polyTimeComputable_pairFstD.comp hkeys
+  have hrest := polyTimeComputable_pairSndD.comp hkeys
+  have hk₂ := polyTimeComputable_pairFstD.comp hrest
+  have hrest₂ := polyTimeComputable_pairSndD.comp hrest
+  have hL := (polyTimeComputable_unaryPoly innerLen).comp hN
+  have houter := (specialDecTagged_poly hE₂).comp
+    (hN.pairEncode (hL.pairEncode (hk₁.pairEncode hc)))
+  have hinner₁ := (specialDecTagged_poly hE₁).comp
+    (hN.pairEncode (hN.pairEncode (hk₁.pairEncode hc)))
+  have hinner₂ := (specialDecTagged_poly hE₁).comp
+    (hN.pairEncode (hN.pairEncode
+      (hk₂.pairEncode (polyTimeComputable_tail.comp houter))))
+  have hzero := checkFinTagged_poly.comp
+    (hN.pairEncode ((polyTimeComputable_prepend [true]).comp hc))
+  have hone := checkFinTagged_poly.comp (hN.pairEncode hinner₁)
+  have htwo := polyTimeComputable_ite (polyTimeComputable_isNil.comp houter)
+    (polyTimeComputable_const []) (checkFinTagged_poly.comp (hN.pairEncode hinner₂))
+  have h := polyTimeComputable_ite (polyTimeComputable_isNil.comp hkeys) hzero
+    (polyTimeComputable_ite (polyTimeComputable_isNil.comp hrest) hone
+      (polyTimeComputable_ite (polyTimeComputable_isNil.comp hrest₂) htwo
+        (polyTimeComputable_const [])))
+  convert h using 1
+  funext z
+  simp only [nestedDecTagged_eq, Function.comp_apply, decide_eq_true_eq]
+  split_ifs <;> rfl
+
+end TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof
