+import TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.Yao.HybridProof.ChunkScanner
+import TCSlib.Complexity.CircuitComplexity.DAGCircuitSatLang
+import TCSlib.Cryptography.Encryption.PrivateKey.FromPRF.ReductionMachine
+
+set_option maxHeartbeats 0
+set_option relaxedAutoImplicit false
+set_option autoImplicit false
+
+/-!
+# A polynomial-time host for circuit descriptions
+
+The decoder converts the unary circuit-description grammar to the library's encoded lists
+of words. It supplies the input count, gate kinds, and argument indices as independently
+accessible words. A supplied output list is retained verbatim. The conversion is total on
+malformed descriptions as well; its correctness contract concerns every encoded circuit.
+
+`DAGCircuit.decode_encode` already validates and reconstructs canonical descriptions. This
+host instead supplies a polynomial-time representation for subsequent traversal, without
+needing to unfold that decoder or copy its validity checks.
+
+## Main definitions
+
+* `DescriptionDecoder.gateRecord`: a kind word paired with encoded unary argument words.
+* `DescriptionDecoder.readCircuit`: a unary input count paired with encoded gate records.
+* `DescriptionDecoder.decodeDescription`: parse the circuit half and retain the output half.
+
+## Main results
+
+* `DescriptionDecoder.readCircuit_encode`: correctness on every circuit encoding.
+* `DescriptionDecoder.decodeDescription_host`: one clean machine with polynomial time and
+  work-head envelopes,
+  together with correctness on circuit descriptions carrying an arbitrary output word.
+
+## References
+
+* [AB09] S. Arora, B. Barak, *Computational Complexity: A Modern Approach*, Cambridge
+  University Press, 2009, §6.2 (descriptions of uniform circuit families).
+* [PS10] R. Pass, a. shelat, *A Course in Cryptography*, lecture notes, 3rd ed., January
+  2010, Protocol 162.6 (the canonical circuit description).
+-/
+
+namespace TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof.DescriptionDecoder
+
+open Complexity Turing BoolCircuit ChunkScanner
+
+-- Control phases: input unary, gate marker, kind bit one, kind bit two,
+-- argument marker, argument unary, done. The eighth code is also absorbing.
+private def next : Phase → Bool → Phase
+  | (false, false, false), b => if b then (false, false, false) else (false, false, true)
+  | (false, false, true), b => if b then (false, true, false) else (true, true, false)
+  | (false, true, false), _ => (false, true, true)
+  | (false, true, true), _ => (true, false, false)
+  | (true, false, false), b => if b then (true, false, true) else (false, false, true)
+  | (true, false, true), b => if b then (true, false, true) else (true, false, false)
+  | (true, true, c), _ => (true, true, c)
+
+private def output : Phase → Bool → List Bool
+  | (false, false, false), b => if b then [true, true] else [false, true]
+  | (false, false, true), _ => []
+  | (false, true, false), b => List.replicate 4 b
+  | (false, true, true), b => List.replicate 4 b ++ [false, false, true, true]
+  | (true, false, false), b => if b then [] else [false, true]
+  | (true, false, true), b =>
+      if b then [true, true, true, true] else [false, false, true, true]
+  | (true, true, _), _ => []
+
+/-- A gate record is `⟨g.kind.encode, encWords (g.args.map (fun a => 1^a))⟩`.
+[PS10, Protocol 162.6], a representation for decoding the canonical circuit. -/
+def gateRecord (g : DAGGate) : List Bool :=
+  pairEncode g.kind.encode (BinArith.encWords (g.args.map fun a => List.replicate a true))
+
+/-- `gateRecord g = ⟨g.kind.encode, encWords (g.args.map (fun a => 1^a))⟩`. -/
+theorem gateRecord_eq (g : DAGGate) :
+    gateRecord g =
+      pairEncode g.kind.encode
+        (BinArith.encWords (g.args.map fun a => List.replicate a true)) := rfl
+
+/-- Parse the circuit grammar into `⟨1^inputCount, encWords gateRecords⟩`.
+[AB09, §6.2], using the seven finite control phases described above.
+`readCircuit w = ChunkScanner.scan next output (false, false, false) w`. -/
+def readCircuit (w : List Bool) : List Bool :=
+  ChunkScanner.scan next output (false, false, false) w
+
+/-- `readCircuit w = ChunkScanner.scan next output (false, false, false) w`. -/
+theorem readCircuit_eq (w : List Bool) :
+    readCircuit w = ChunkScanner.scan next output (false, false, false) w := rfl
+
+/-- Circuit-description parsing is polynomial-time: `PolyTimeComputable readCircuit`. -/
+theorem readCircuit_poly : PolyTimeComputable readCircuit :=
+  ChunkScanner.scan_poly next output (false, false, false)
+
+/-- Parse a description pair, retaining its output word:
+`decodeDescription w = ⟨readCircuit (pairFstD w), pairSndD w⟩`. -/
+def decodeDescription (w : List Bool) : List Bool :=
+  pairEncode (readCircuit (pairFstD w)) (pairSndD w)
+
+/-- `decodeDescription w = ⟨readCircuit (pairFstD w), pairSndD w⟩`. -/
+theorem decodeDescription_eq (w : List Bool) :
+    decodeDescription w = pairEncode (readCircuit (pairFstD w)) (pairSndD w) := rfl
+
+/-- The total description decoder is polynomial-time:
+`PolyTimeComputable decodeDescription`. -/
+theorem decodeDescription_poly : PolyTimeComputable decodeDescription :=
+  (readCircuit_poly.comp polyTimeComputable_pairFstD).pairEncode polyTimeComputable_pairSndD
+
+private theorem dbl_pair (a b : List Bool) :
+    dbl (pairEncode a b) =
+      dbl (dbl a) ++ [false, false, true, true] ++ dbl b := by
+  rw [pairEncode_eq_dbl, MultiMsg.dbl_append, MultiMsg.dbl_append]
+  simp only [dbl_cons, dbl_nil, List.append_assoc]
+
+private theorem scan_done (w : List Bool) :
+    ChunkScanner.scan next output (true, true, false) w = [] := by
+  induction w with
+  | nil => exact ChunkScanner.scan_nil _ _ _
+  | cons b w ih =>
+    rw [ChunkScanner.scan_cons]
+    simpa only [next, output, List.nil_append] using ih
+
+private theorem scan_input (n : ℕ) (r : List Bool) :
+    ChunkScanner.scan next output (false, false, false)
+        (List.replicate n true ++ false :: r) =
+      dbl (List.replicate n true) ++ [false, true] ++
+        ChunkScanner.scan next output (false, false, true) r := by
+  induction n with
+  | zero =>
+    simp only [List.replicate_zero, List.nil_append, ChunkScanner.scan_cons, next,
+      output, Bool.false_eq_true, if_false, dbl_nil]
+  | succ n ih =>
+    simp only [List.replicate_succ, List.cons_append, ChunkScanner.scan_cons, next,
+      output, if_true, dbl_cons, ih, List.cons_append, List.nil_append]
+
+private theorem scan_argument (n : ℕ) (r : List Bool) :
+    ChunkScanner.scan next output (true, false, true)
+        (List.replicate n true ++ false :: r) =
+      dbl (dbl (List.replicate n true)) ++ [false, false, true, true] ++
+        ChunkScanner.scan next output (true, false, false) r := by
+  induction n with
+  | zero =>
+    simp only [List.replicate_zero, List.nil_append, ChunkScanner.scan_cons, next,
+      output, Bool.false_eq_true, if_false, dbl_nil]
+  | succ n ih =>
+    simp only [List.replicate_succ, List.cons_append, ChunkScanner.scan_cons, next,
+      output, if_true, dbl_cons, ih, List.cons_append, List.nil_append]
+
+private theorem scan_arguments (args : List ℕ) (r : List Bool) :
+    ChunkScanner.scan next output (true, false, false) (encodeList encodeNat args ++ r) =
+      dbl (BinArith.encWords (args.map fun a => List.replicate a true)) ++ [false, true] ++
+        ChunkScanner.scan next output (false, false, true) r := by
+  induction args with
+  | nil =>
+    simp only [encodeList_nil, List.singleton_append, ChunkScanner.scan_cons, next,
+      output, Bool.false_eq_true, if_false, List.map_nil, BinArith.encWords_nil,
+      dbl_nil, List.nil_append]
+  | cons a args ih =>
+    rw [encodeList_cons, List.cons_append, ChunkScanner.scan_cons]
+    simp only [next, output, if_true, List.nil_append]
+    simp only [encodeNat_def, List.append_assoc, List.cons_append, List.nil_append]
+    rw [scan_argument, ih, List.map_cons, BinArith.encWords_cons, dbl_pair]
+    simp only [List.append_assoc, List.cons_append, List.nil_append]
+
+private theorem scan_gate (g : DAGGate) (r : List Bool) :
+    ChunkScanner.scan next output (false, true, false) (g.encode ++ r) =
+      dbl (gateRecord g) ++ [false, true] ++
+        ChunkScanner.scan next output (false, false, true) r := by
+  cases g with
+  | mk k args =>
+    cases k <;>
+      simp only [DAGGate.encode_eq, GateKind.encode_and, GateKind.encode_or,
+        GateKind.encode_not, List.cons_append, List.nil_append, ChunkScanner.scan_cons,
+        next, output, List.replicate_succ, List.replicate_zero, scan_arguments,
+        gateRecord_eq, dbl_pair, dbl_cons, dbl_nil, List.append_assoc]
+
+private theorem scan_gates (gs : List DAGGate) (r : List Bool) :
+    ChunkScanner.scan next output (false, false, true) (encodeList DAGGate.encode gs ++ r) =
+      BinArith.encWords (gs.map gateRecord) := by
+  induction gs with
+  | nil =>
+    simp only [encodeList_nil, List.singleton_append, ChunkScanner.scan_cons, next,
+      output, Bool.false_eq_true, if_false, scan_done, List.map_nil,
+      BinArith.encWords_nil, List.nil_append]
+  | cons g gs ih =>
+    rw [encodeList_cons, List.cons_append, ChunkScanner.scan_cons]
+    simp only [next, output, if_true, List.nil_append]
+    rw [List.append_assoc, scan_gate, ih, List.map_cons, BinArith.encWords_cons,
+      pairEncode_eq_dbl]
+
+/-- On every circuit, parsing returns the unary input count and every gate record:
+`readCircuit C.encode = ⟨1^n, encWords (C.gates.map gateRecord)⟩`. [AB09, §6.2]
+
+**Proof sketch.** The input-count phase doubles the unary count and emits the pair
+separator. Each gate's two kind bits become the first field of its gate record; each unary
+argument becomes one encoded word. The gate-list terminator enters an absorbing phase,
+so the circuit's distinguished output field does not affect the list. Induction on the
+argument and gate lists proves these identities without any fan-in assumption. -/
+theorem readCircuit_encode {n : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit n) :
+    readCircuit C.encode =
+      pairEncode (List.replicate n true) (BinArith.encWords (C.gates.map gateRecord)) := by
+  rw [readCircuit_eq, DAGCircuit.encode_parts]
+  simp only [encodeNat_def, List.append_assoc, List.singleton_append]
+  rw [scan_input, scan_gates, pairEncode_eq_dbl]
+
+/-- On a circuit description paired with any output word, the host returns
+`⟨⟨1^n, encWords (C.gates.map gateRecord)⟩, outs⟩`.
+[PS10, Protocol 162.6], decoding the canonical description with an output list.
+`decodeDescription (pairEncode C.encode outs) = pairEncode (pairEncode (List.replicate n true)
+(BinArith.encWords (C.gates.map gateRecord))) outs`. -/
+theorem decodeDescription_pair {n : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit n) (outs : List Bool) :
+    decodeDescription (pairEncode C.encode outs) =
+      pairEncode
+        (pairEncode (List.replicate n true) (BinArith.encWords (C.gates.map gateRecord)))
+        outs := by
+  rw [decodeDescription_eq, pairFstD_pairEncode, pairSndD_pairEncode, readCircuit_encode]
+
+/-- **The circuit-description decoder host**: one clean finite machine installs
+`decodeDescription arg` and emits nothing within `A · (|arg| + 1)^c` steps. Every work head
+of that same machine remains within this envelope at every time. On `⟨C.encode, outs⟩` the
+installed word is `⟨⟨1^n, encWords (C.gates.map gateRecord)⟩, outs⟩`.
+[AB09, §6.2; PS10, Protocol 162.6]
+`∃ (M : FinTM Bool) (A c : ℕ), ModuleHost.Clean M decodeDescription (fun _ => []) (fun n => A *
+(n + 1) ^ c) ∧ (∀ (x arg : List Bool) (v : ℕ) (j : Fin M.k), |(M.tm.runFrom (Cfg.ofWords (input :=
+x) M.tm.q₀ (stateWord M.k arg)) v).workTapePos j| ≤ (A * (arg.length + 1) ^ c : ℕ)) ∧ ∀ (n : ℕ) (C :
+DAGCircuit n) (outs : List Bool), decodeDescription (pairEncode C.encode outs) = pairEncode
+(pairEncode (List.replicate n true) (BinArith.encWords (C.gates.map gateRecord))) outs`.
+
+The machine uses the registered H14 clean-install host; the work-head bound is its
+`Clean.envelope` contract, so correctness, time, and work heads refer to the same machine. -/
+theorem decodeDescription_host :
+    ∃ (M : FinTM Bool) (A c : ℕ),
+      ModuleHost.Clean M decodeDescription (fun _ => []) (fun n => A * (n + 1) ^ c) ∧
+      (∀ (x arg : List Bool) (v : ℕ) (j : Fin M.k),
+        |(M.tm.runFrom
+          (Cfg.ofWords (input := x) M.tm.q₀ (stateWord M.k arg)) v).workTapePos j| ≤
+            (A * (arg.length + 1) ^ c : ℕ)) ∧
+      ∀ (n : ℕ) (C : DAGCircuit n) (outs : List Bool),
+        decodeDescription (pairEncode C.encode outs) =
+          pairEncode
+            (pairEncode (List.replicate n true) (BinArith.encWords (C.gates.map gateRecord)))
+            outs := by
+  obtain ⟨M, A, c, hM⟩ := ModuleHost.clean_install decodeDescription_poly
+  exact ⟨M, A, c, hM, (fun x arg v j => hM.envelope x arg v j),
+    fun _ C outs => decodeDescription_pair C outs⟩
+
+end TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof.DescriptionDecoder

+/-

+import TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.Yao.HybridProof.Decryption
+import TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.Yao.HybridProof.FirstTagged
+import TCSlib.Cryptography.Asymptotic.EnsemblesLemmas
+import TCSlib.Cryptography.Games.BitCodec
+
+set_option maxHeartbeats 0
+set_option relaxedAutoImplicit false
+set_option autoImplicit false
+
+/-!
+# Polynomial-time garbled-gate evaluation
+
+A gate's argument list is represented by an encoded list of unary indices.
+The current vertex keys and its row table are encoded word lists. Padded lookup
+reproduces the default key at an unavailable vertex. The published first-index
+host finds the first row that nested special decryption opens.
+
+## Main definitions
+
+* `YaoHybridProof.keyLookup`: padded lookup in an encoded vertex-key list.
+* `YaoHybridProof.argKeysCode`: encoded keys of the gate's arguments.
+* `YaoHybridProof.nestedRowTagged`: the row adapter for first-success search.
+* `YaoHybridProof.untagDefault`: the default key on search failure.
+* `YaoHybridProof.evalGateWord`: encoded garbled-gate evaluation.
+
+## Main results
+
+* `YaoHybridProof.evalGateWord_poly`: polynomial time on every input word.
+* `YaoHybridProof.evalGateWord_encoded`: correctness for `evalGate (nestedSpecial …)`.
+
+## References
+
+* [PS10] R. Pass, a. shelat, *A Course in Cryptography*, lecture notes, 3rd ed.,
+  January 2010, Protocol 162.6 and §6.2.3.
+-/
+
+namespace TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof
+
+open Complexity Turing BoolCircuit TCSlib.Cryptography
+
+/-- On `⟨⟨N, values⟩, index⟩`, read the `|index|`-th word from `values`
+and pad or truncate it to `|N|` bits.
+`keyLookup z = EnsembleLemmas.padL (pairFstD (pairFstD z)).length (BinArith.getW (pairEncode
+(pairSndD z) (pairSndD (pairFstD z))))`. -/
+def keyLookup (z : List Bool) : List Bool :=
+  EnsembleLemmas.padL (pairFstD (pairFstD z)).length
+    (BinArith.getW (pairEncode (pairSndD z) (pairSndD (pairFstD z))))
+
+/-- For every `z`, `keyLookup z` is the padded `getW` lookup at its unary index.
+`keyLookup z = EnsembleLemmas.padL (pairFstD (pairFstD z)).length (BinArith.getW (pairEncode
+(pairSndD z) (pairSndD (pairFstD z))))`. -/
+theorem keyLookup_eq (z : List Bool) :
+    keyLookup z = EnsembleLemmas.padL (pairFstD (pairFstD z)).length
+      (BinArith.getW (pairEncode (pairSndD z) (pairSndD (pairFstD z)))) := rfl
+
+/-- Padded encoded-key lookup is polynomial-time on every word.
+`PolyTimeComputable keyLookup`. -/
+theorem keyLookup_poly : PolyTimeComputable keyLookup := by
+  have hN := polyTimeComputable_pairFstD.comp polyTimeComputable_pairFstD
+  have hv := polyTimeComputable_pairSndD.comp polyTimeComputable_pairFstD
+  have hraw := BinArith.polyTimeComputable_getW.comp
+    (polyTimeComputable_pairSndD.pairEncode hv)
+  simpa only [keyLookup_eq, PolyExpr.eval_var, Function.comp_apply] using
+    EnsembleLemmas.polyTimeComputable_padL .var hN hraw
+
+/-- Lookup from the encoded vector list returns the encoding of its defaulted entry:
+`keyLookup ⟨⟨N, encWords (vs.map ofFn)⟩, 1^a⟩ = ofFn (vs.getD a default)`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** Read the list lookup through its public encoded-list contract.
+For an available vertex, padding inverts `List.ofFn`. For an unavailable vertex,
+the empty lookup pads to the all-false key, which is the default vector. -/
+theorem keyLookup_encoded (N : List Bool) (vs : List (Fin N.length → Bool)) (a : ℕ) :
+    keyLookup (pairEncode (pairEncode N (BinArith.encWords (vs.map List.ofFn)))
+        (List.replicate a true)) = List.ofFn (vs.getD a default) := by
+  rw [keyLookup_eq, pairFstD_pairEncode, pairSndD_pairEncode, pairFstD_pairEncode,
+    pairSndD_pairEncode, BinArith.getW_pairEncode, List.length_replicate,
+    ← EnsembleLemmas.ofFn_padFin_eq_padL]
+  apply congrArg List.ofFn
+  simp only [List.getD_eq_getElem?_getD, List.getElem?_map]
+  cases h : vs[a]? with
+  | none =>
+    simp only [Option.map_none, Option.getD_none]
+    rw [Games.padFin_nil]
+    funext i
+    rw [Games.zeroBits_apply]
+    rfl
+  | some k => simp only [Option.map_some, Option.getD_some, padFin_ofFn]
+
+/-- On `⟨N, ⟨values, args⟩⟩`, map padded key lookup over the unary argument indices.
+`argKeysCode z = BinArith.mapW keyLookup (pairEncode (pairEncode (pairFstD z) (pairFstD (pairSndD
+z))) (pairSndD (pairSndD z)))`. -/
+def argKeysCode (z : List Bool) : List Bool :=
+  BinArith.mapW keyLookup
+    (pairEncode (pairEncode (pairFstD z) (pairFstD (pairSndD z)))
+      (pairSndD (pairSndD z)))
+
+/-- For every `z`, `argKeysCode z` is `mapW keyLookup` with the parameter and
+encoded vertex values as retained context and the argument indices as the list.
+`argKeysCode z = BinArith.mapW keyLookup (pairEncode (pairEncode (pairFstD z) (pairFstD (pairSndD
+z))) (pairSndD (pairSndD z)))`. -/
+theorem argKeysCode_eq (z : List Bool) :
+    argKeysCode z = BinArith.mapW keyLookup
+      (pairEncode (pairEncode (pairFstD z) (pairFstD (pairSndD z)))
+        (pairSndD (pairSndD z))) := rfl
+
+/-- The encoded argument-key map `argKeysCode` is polynomial-time on every word.
+`PolyTimeComputable argKeysCode`. -/
+theorem argKeysCode_poly : PolyTimeComputable argKeysCode :=
+  (BinArith.polyTimeComputable_mapW keyLookup_poly).comp
+    ((polyTimeComputable_pairFstD.pairEncode
+      (polyTimeComputable_pairFstD.comp polyTimeComputable_pairSndD)).pairEncode
+        (polyTimeComputable_pairSndD.comp polyTimeComputable_pairSndD))
+
+/-- On encoded vertices and unary arguments, `argKeysCode` is the encoded list
+of the defaulted vertex keys at those argument indices.
+`argKeysCode (pairEncode N (pairEncode (BinArith.encWords (vs.map List.ofFn)) (BinArith.encWords
+(args.map fun a => List.replicate a true)))) = BinArith.encWords ((args.map fun a => vs.getD a
+default).map List.ofFn)`. -/
+theorem argKeysCode_encoded (N : List Bool) (vs : List (Fin N.length → Bool))
+    (args : List ℕ) :
+    argKeysCode (pairEncode N (pairEncode (BinArith.encWords (vs.map List.ofFn))
+        (BinArith.encWords (args.map fun a => List.replicate a true)))) =
+      BinArith.encWords ((args.map fun a => vs.getD a default).map List.ofFn) := by
+  simp only [argKeysCode_eq, pairFstD_pairEncode, pairSndD_pairEncode,
+    BinArith.mapW_pairEncode, BinArith.decWords_encWords, List.map_map]
+  congr 1
+  apply List.map_congr_left
+  intro a _
+  exact keyLookup_encoded N vs a
+
+/-- Adapt `⟨⟨N, keys⟩, row⟩` to the nested-decryption input `⟨N, ⟨keys, row⟩⟩`.
+`nestedRowTagged E₁ E₂ z = nestedDecTagged E₁ E₂ (pairEncode (pairFstD (pairFstD z)) (pairEncode
+(pairSndD (pairFstD z)) (pairSndD z)))`. -/
+def nestedRowTagged (E₁ E₂ : List Bool → List Bool) (z : List Bool) : List Bool :=
+  nestedDecTagged E₁ E₂ (pairEncode (pairFstD (pairFstD z))
+    (pairEncode (pairSndD (pairFstD z)) (pairSndD z)))
+
+/-- For every `z`, `nestedRowTagged E₁ E₂ z` runs `nestedDecTagged` with
+parameter `fst (fst z)`, keys `snd (fst z)` and row `snd z`.
+`nestedRowTagged E₁ E₂ z = nestedDecTagged E₁ E₂ (pairEncode (pairFstD (pairFstD z)) (pairEncode
+(pairSndD (pairFstD z)) (pairSndD z)))`. -/
+theorem nestedRowTagged_eq (E₁ E₂ : List Bool → List Bool) (z : List Bool) :
+    nestedRowTagged E₁ E₂ z =
+      nestedDecTagged E₁ E₂ (pairEncode (pairFstD (pairFstD z))
+        (pairEncode (pairSndD (pairFstD z)) (pairSndD z))) := rfl
+
+/-- For polynomial-time evaluators, the nested row adapter is polynomial-time.
+`PolyTimeComputable (nestedRowTagged E₁ E₂)`. -/
+theorem nestedRowTagged_poly {E₁ E₂ : List Bool → List Bool}
+    (hE₁ : PolyTimeComputable E₁) (hE₂ : PolyTimeComputable E₂) :
+    PolyTimeComputable (nestedRowTagged E₁ E₂) :=
+  (nestedDecTagged_poly hE₁ hE₂).comp
+    ((polyTimeComputable_pairFstD.comp polyTimeComputable_pairFstD).pairEncode
+      ((polyTimeComputable_pairSndD.comp polyTimeComputable_pairFstD).pairEncode
+        polyTimeComputable_pairSndD))
+
+/-- On `⟨N, tagged⟩`, return `|N|` false bits when the tag is empty and
+otherwise remove its first bit.
+`untagDefault z = if pairSndD z = [] then List.replicate (pairFstD z).length false else (pairSndD
+z).drop 1`. -/
+def untagDefault (z : List Bool) : List Bool :=
+  if pairSndD z = [] then List.replicate (pairFstD z).length false
+  else (pairSndD z).drop 1
+
+/-- For every `z`, `untagDefault z` is the zero key on empty tag and the
+payload `drop 1 (snd z)` otherwise.
+`untagDefault z = if pairSndD z = [] then List.replicate (pairFstD z).length false else (pairSndD
+z).drop 1`. -/
+theorem untagDefault_eq (z : List Bool) :
+    untagDefault z = if pairSndD z = [] then List.replicate (pairFstD z).length false
+      else (pairSndD z).drop 1 := rfl
+
+/-- Removing the tag and supplying the default zero key is polynomial-time.
+`PolyTimeComputable untagDefault`. -/
+theorem untagDefault_poly : PolyTimeComputable untagDefault := by
+  simpa only [untagDefault_eq, PolyExpr.eval_var, Function.comp_apply, decide_eq_true_eq] using
+    polyTimeComputable_ite (polyTimeComputable_isNil.comp polyTimeComputable_pairSndD)
+      (EnsembleLemmas.polyTimeComputable_zeros .var polyTimeComputable_pairFstD)
+      (polyTimeComputable_tail.comp polyTimeComputable_pairSndD)
+
+/-- Untagging the encoded optional key gives the encoding of `o.getD default`,
+including `N.length = 0`. -/
+theorem untagDefault_encoded (N : List Bool) (o : Option (Fin N.length → Bool)) :
+    untagDefault (pairEncode N (match o with | none => [] | some k => true :: List.ofFn k)) =
+      List.ofFn (o.getD default) := by
+  cases o with
+  | none =>
+    rw [untagDefault_eq, pairFstD_pairEncode, pairSndD_pairEncode, if_pos rfl]
+    change List.replicate N.length false = List.ofFn (fun _ : Fin N.length => false)
+    rw [List.ofFn_const]
+  | some k =>
+    rw [untagDefault_eq, pairFstD_pairEncode, pairSndD_pairEncode,
+      if_neg (List.cons_ne_nil true (List.ofFn k))]
+    rfl
+
+/-- On `⟨N, ⟨args, ⟨values, rows⟩⟩⟩`, look up the gate's argument keys, scan
+its rows for the first successful nested special decryption, and return the
+resulting key or the default zero key. [PS10, Protocol 162.6]
+`evalGateWord E₁ E₂ z = let N := pairFstD z; let args := pairFstD (pairSndD z); let values :=
+pairFstD (pairSndD (pairSndD z)); let rows := pairSndD (pairSndD (pairSndD z)); let keys :=
+argKeysCode (pairEncode N (pairEncode values args)); untagDefault (pairEncode N (firstTagged
+(nestedRowTagged E₁ E₂) (pairEncode (pairEncode N keys) rows)))`. -/
+noncomputable def evalGateWord (E₁ E₂ : List Bool → List Bool) (z : List Bool) : List Bool :=
+  let N := pairFstD z
+  let args := pairFstD (pairSndD z)
+  let values := pairFstD (pairSndD (pairSndD z))
+  let rows := pairSndD (pairSndD (pairSndD z))
+  let keys := argKeysCode (pairEncode N (pairEncode values args))
+  untagDefault (pairEncode N
+    (firstTagged (nestedRowTagged E₁ E₂) (pairEncode (pairEncode N keys) rows)))
+
+/-- For every `z`, `evalGateWord E₁ E₂ z` reads its parameter, argument indices,
+vertex keys and row table, computes `argKeysCode`, applies `firstTagged` with
+the nested-decryption row adapter and removes the tag with `untagDefault`.
+`evalGateWord E₁ E₂ z = let N := pairFstD z; let args := pairFstD (pairSndD z); let values :=
+pairFstD (pairSndD (pairSndD z)); let rows := pairSndD (pairSndD (pairSndD z)); let keys :=
+argKeysCode (pairEncode N (pairEncode values args)); untagDefault (pairEncode N (firstTagged
+(nestedRowTagged E₁ E₂) (pairEncode (pairEncode N keys) rows)))`. -/
+theorem evalGateWord_eq (E₁ E₂ : List Bool → List Bool) (z : List Bool) :
+    evalGateWord E₁ E₂ z =
+  let N := pairFstD z
+  let args := pairFstD (pairSndD z)
+  let values := pairFstD (pairSndD (pairSndD z))
+  let rows := pairSndD (pairSndD (pairSndD z))
+  let keys := argKeysCode (pairEncode N (pairEncode values args))
+  untagDefault (pairEncode N
+    (firstTagged (nestedRowTagged E₁ E₂) (pairEncode (pairEncode N keys) rows))) := rfl
+
+/-- For polynomial-time evaluators `E₁` and `E₂`, `evalGateWord E₁ E₂`
+is polynomial-time on every input word. [PS10, Protocol 162.6]
+`PolyTimeComputable (evalGateWord E₁ E₂)`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** Read the four fields by pair projections. The argument-key map,
+nested-decryption adapter and first-index adapter are polynomial-time on raw
+words. Pair their inputs and compose their contracts, finishing with the
+default-key untagging adapter. -/
+theorem evalGateWord_poly {E₁ E₂ : List Bool → List Bool}
+    (hE₁ : PolyTimeComputable E₁) (hE₂ : PolyTimeComputable E₂) :
+    PolyTimeComputable (evalGateWord E₁ E₂) := by
+  have hN := polyTimeComputable_pairFstD
+  have hargs := polyTimeComputable_pairFstD.comp polyTimeComputable_pairSndD
+  have htail := polyTimeComputable_pairSndD.comp polyTimeComputable_pairSndD
+  have hvalues := polyTimeComputable_pairFstD.comp htail
+  have hrows := polyTimeComputable_pairSndD.comp htail
+  have hkeys := argKeysCode_poly.comp (hN.pairEncode (hvalues.pairEncode hargs))
+  have hsearch := (firstTagged_poly (nestedRowTagged_poly hE₁ hE₂)).comp
+    ((hN.pairEncode hkeys).pairEncode hrows)
+  simpa only [evalGateWord_eq, Function.comp_apply] using
+    untagDefault_poly.comp (hN.pairEncode hsearch)
+
+/-- For every gate, vertex-value list and row table, the encoded evaluator returns
+`List.ofFn (evalGate (nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ |N|) T v g vs)` on the corresponding
+unary-argument and word-list encodings. No well-formedness or ciphertext-validity
+hypothesis is needed. [PS10, Protocol 162.6]
+`evalGateWord E₁ E₂ (pairEncode N (pairEncode (BinArith.encWords (g.args.map fun a => List.replicate
+a true)) (pairEncode (BinArith.encWords (vs.map List.ofFn)) (BinArith.encWords (T v))))) = List.ofFn
+(evalGate (nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ N.length) T v g vs)`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** The argument-key map gives exactly the defaulted keys read by
+`evalGate`. The nested row adapter tags precisely the optional decryption result.
+The first-success contract therefore tags the table's `findSome?` result.
+Removing its tag supplies exactly the defaulted key in `evalGate`'s public equation. -/
+theorem evalGateWord_encoded (E₁ E₂ : List Bool → List Bool) (N : List Bool)
+    (T : ℕ → List (List Bool)) (v : ℕ) (g : DAGGate)
+    (vs : List (Fin N.length → Bool)) :
+    evalGateWord E₁ E₂ (pairEncode N
+        (pairEncode (BinArith.encWords (g.args.map fun a => List.replicate a true))
+          (pairEncode (BinArith.encWords (vs.map List.ofFn)) (BinArith.encWords (T v))))) =
+      List.ofFn (evalGate (nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ N.length) T v g vs) := by
+  rw [evalGateWord_eq]
+  simp only [pairFstD_pairEncode, pairSndD_pairEncode]
+  rw [argKeysCode_encoded]
+  have hsearch := firstTagged_encoded (β := Fin N.length → Bool) (nestedRowTagged E₁ E₂)
+    (pairEncode N (BinArith.encWords
+      ((g.args.map fun a => vs.getD a default).map List.ofFn))) (T v)
+    ((nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ N.length).dec (g.args.map fun a => vs.getD a default))
+    (fun k : Fin N.length → Bool => List.ofFn k) (fun row => by
+      rw [nestedRowTagged_eq]
+      simp only [pairFstD_pairEncode, pairSndD_pairEncode]
+      have hh := nestedDecTagged_encoded E₁ E₂ N (g.args.map fun a => vs.getD a default) row
+      cases hd : (nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ N.length).dec
+          (g.args.map fun a => vs.getD a default) row with
+      | none => simpa only [hd] using hh
+      | some k => simpa only [hd] using hh)
+  rw [hsearch, evalGate_eq]
+  have hh := untagDefault_encoded N
+    ((T v).findSome? ((nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ N.length).dec
+      (g.args.map fun a => vs.getD a default)))
+  cases hd : (T v).findSome? ((nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ N.length).dec
+      (g.args.map fun a => vs.getD a default)) with
+  | none => simpa only [hd] using hh
+  | some k => simpa only [hd] using hh
+
+/-- Append the encoded evaluated key to the input value list in
+`⟨N, ⟨args, ⟨values, rows⟩⟩⟩`.
+`evalGateAppendWord E₁ E₂ z = pairFstD (pairSndD (pairSndD z)) ++ pairEncode (evalGateWord E₁ E₂ z)
+[]`. -/
+noncomputable def evalGateAppendWord (E₁ E₂ : List Bool → List Bool) (z : List Bool) : List Bool :=
+  pairFstD (pairSndD (pairSndD z)) ++ pairEncode (evalGateWord E₁ E₂ z) []
+
+/-- For every `z`, `evalGateAppendWord E₁ E₂ z` appends the pair-encoded output
+of `evalGateWord E₁ E₂ z` to `fst (snd (snd z))`.
+`evalGateAppendWord E₁ E₂ z = pairFstD (pairSndD (pairSndD z)) ++ pairEncode (evalGateWord E₁ E₂ z)
+[]`. -/
+theorem evalGateAppendWord_eq (E₁ E₂ : List Bool → List Bool) (z : List Bool) :
+    evalGateAppendWord E₁ E₂ z =
+      pairFstD (pairSndD (pairSndD z)) ++ pairEncode (evalGateWord E₁ E₂ z) [] := rfl
+
+/-- For polynomial-time `E₁` and `E₂`, appending the evaluated key to the
+encoded value list is polynomial-time.
+`PolyTimeComputable (evalGateAppendWord E₁ E₂)`. -/
+theorem evalGateAppendWord_poly {E₁ E₂ : List Bool → List Bool}
+    (hE₁ : PolyTimeComputable E₁) (hE₂ : PolyTimeComputable E₂) :
+    PolyTimeComputable (evalGateAppendWord E₁ E₂) :=
+  (polyTimeComputable_pairFstD.comp
+      (polyTimeComputable_pairSndD.comp polyTimeComputable_pairSndD)).append
+    ((evalGateWord_poly hE₁ hE₂).pairEncode (polyTimeComputable_const []))
+
+/-- On the encoded gate arguments, values and table, appending the evaluated key
+produces the encoding of `vs ++ [evalGate (nestedSpecial …) T v g vs]`.
+`evalGateAppendWord E₁ E₂ (pairEncode N (pairEncode (BinArith.encWords (g.args.map fun a =>
+List.replicate a true)) (pairEncode (BinArith.encWords (vs.map List.ofFn)) (BinArith.encWords (T
+v))))) = BinArith.encWords ((vs ++ [evalGate (nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ N.length) T v g vs]).map
+List.ofFn)`. -/
+theorem evalGateAppendWord_encoded (E₁ E₂ : List Bool → List Bool) (N : List Bool)
+    (T : ℕ → List (List Bool)) (v : ℕ) (g : DAGGate)
+    (vs : List (Fin N.length → Bool)) :
+    evalGateAppendWord E₁ E₂ (pairEncode N
+        (pairEncode (BinArith.encWords (g.args.map fun a => List.replicate a true))
+          (pairEncode (BinArith.encWords (vs.map List.ofFn)) (BinArith.encWords (T v))))) =
+      BinArith.encWords ((vs ++ [evalGate (nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ N.length) T v g vs]).map
+        List.ofFn) := by
+  rw [evalGateAppendWord_eq]
+  simp only [pairFstD_pairEncode, pairSndD_pairEncode]
+  rw [evalGateWord_encoded, List.map_append, List.map_singleton,
+    BinArith.encWords_append_singleton]
+
+end TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof

+import TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.Yao.HybridProof.FakeObjects
+import TCSlib.Complexity.ClassP.BinaryArith.Words

+set_option maxHeartbeats 0
+set_option relaxedAutoImplicit false
+set_option autoImplicit false

+/-!
+# The canonical encoded simulated garbling message
+
+This lightweight semantic interface gives the shared endpoint used by the simulated garbler
+and the gate hybrids. Its three components are the simulated tables, Alice's false input
+keys, and output decoding pairs ordered by the prescribed result bits.
+
+## Main definitions
+
+* `FakeObjects.message`: the complete simulated first message.
+
+## Main results
+
+* `FakeObjects.message_eq`: its exact table/key/pair encoding.
+
+## References
+
+* [PS10] R. Pass, a. shelat, *A Course in Cryptography*, lecture notes, 3rd ed., January
+  2010, Theorem 163.7 (Bob's simulated garbling message).
+-/
+
+namespace TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof.FakeObjects
+
+open Complexity Turing BoolCircuit
+
+/-- The simulated first message is the encoded table vector paired with Alice's encoded
+false-key vector and the encoded output pairs ordered by the prescribed result bits.
+[PS10, Theorem 163.7]
+`message M C s inA outLen output result = pairEncode (BinArith.encWords (List.ofFn fun i =>
+BinArith.encWords (table M C s i))) (pairEncode (BinArith.encWords (List.ofFn fun v : Fin inA =>
+List.ofFn (keyAt C s v false))) (BinArith.encWords (List.ofFn fun j => pairEncode (List.ofFn
+(decodePair C s (output j) (result j)).1) (List.ofFn (decodePair C s (output j) (result
+j)).2))))`. -/
+noncomputable def message {inputs width : ℕ} {ρ : Type}
+    (M : MultiEnc (Fin width → Bool) (List Bool) ρ) (C : DAGCircuit inputs)
+    (s : GarbleSeed C (Fin width → Bool) ρ) (inA outLen : ℕ)
+    (output : Fin outLen → ℕ) (result : Fin outLen → Bool) : List Bool :=
+  pairEncode (BinArith.encWords (List.ofFn fun i => BinArith.encWords (table M C s i)))
+    (pairEncode
+      (BinArith.encWords (List.ofFn fun v : Fin inA => List.ofFn (keyAt C s v false)))
+      (BinArith.encWords (List.ofFn fun j =>
+        pairEncode (List.ofFn (decodePair C s (output j) (result j)).1)
+          (List.ofFn (decodePair C s (output j) (result j)).2))))
+
+/-- `message M C s inA outLen output result` is
+`⟨encWords (ofFn (encWords ∘ table)), ⟨encWords (ofFn key0), encWords (ofFn decodePair)⟩⟩`,
+where keys are encoded by `List.ofFn` and each decoding pair by `pairEncode`.
+`message M C s inA outLen output result = pairEncode (BinArith.encWords (List.ofFn fun i =>
+BinArith.encWords (table M C s i))) (pairEncode (BinArith.encWords (List.ofFn fun v : Fin inA =>
+List.ofFn (keyAt C s v false))) (BinArith.encWords (List.ofFn fun j => pairEncode (List.ofFn
+(decodePair C s (output j) (result j)).1) (List.ofFn (decodePair C s (output j) (result
+j)).2))))`. -/
+theorem message_eq {inputs width : ℕ} {ρ : Type}
+    (M : MultiEnc (Fin width → Bool) (List Bool) ρ) (C : DAGCircuit inputs)
+    (s : GarbleSeed C (Fin width → Bool) ρ) (inA outLen : ℕ)
+    (output : Fin outLen → ℕ) (result : Fin outLen → Bool) :
+    message M C s inA outLen output result =
+      pairEncode (BinArith.encWords (List.ofFn fun i => BinArith.encWords (table M C s i)))
+        (pairEncode
+          (BinArith.encWords (List.ofFn fun v : Fin inA => List.ofFn (keyAt C s v false)))
+          (BinArith.encWords (List.ofFn fun j =>
+            pairEncode (List.ofFn (decodePair C s (output j) (result j)).1)
+              (List.ofFn (decodePair C s (output j) (result j)).2)))) := rfl
+
+end TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof.FakeObjects

+/-
+Copyright (c) 2026 TCSlib contributors. All rights reserved.
+Released under Apache 2.0 license as described in the file LICENSE.
+-/
+import TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.Garbling
+
+set_option maxHeartbeats 0
+set_option relaxedAutoImplicit false
+set_option autoImplicit false
+
+/-!
+# Active-key simulated garblings
+
+These are new simulator objects. Every row encrypts its output vertex's false key, and
+an output decoding pair is swapped according to the prescribed output bit. The ordinary
+argument-key and shuffled-enumeration interfaces come from the public garbling layer.
+
+## Main definitions
+
+* `FakeObjects.row`, `table`: rows and shuffled tables with false active output keys.
+* `FakeObjects.decodePair`: the output-key pair ordered by a prescribed bit.
+* `FakeObjects.activeAnswers`: consecutive false-key blocks for selected input vertices.
+
+## Main results
+
+* The `_eq` readers expose every new object's formula without unfolding it.
+
+## References
+
+* [PS10] R. Pass, a. shelat, *A Course in Cryptography*, lecture notes, 3rd ed., January
+  2010, Theorem 163.7 (simulation for Bob in the OT-hybrid garbling protocol).
+-/
+
+namespace TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof.FakeObjects
+
+open BoolCircuit
+
+variable {K Cipher ρ : Type} [Inhabited K] {inputs : ℕ}
+
+/-- A simulated row encrypts the false key of vertex `inputs + i` under the assignment's
+ordinary argument keys, using that row's seed coins. [PS10, Theorem 163.7]
+`row M C s i a = M.enc (argKeys C s i a) (keyAt C s (inputs + i) false) (s.2.1 i a)`. -/
+def row (M : MultiEnc K Cipher ρ) (C : DAGCircuit inputs) (s : GarbleSeed C K ρ)
+    (i : Fin C.gates.length) (a : Fin (C.gates[i]).args.length → Bool) : Cipher :=
+  M.enc (argKeys C s i a) (keyAt C s (inputs + i) false) (s.2.1 i a)
+
+/-- `row M C s i a = M.enc (argKeys C s i a) (keyAt C s (inputs + i) false) (s.2.1 i a)`. -/
+theorem row_eq (M : MultiEnc K Cipher ρ) (C : DAGCircuit inputs) (s : GarbleSeed C K ρ)
+    (i : Fin C.gates.length) (a : Fin (C.gates[i]).args.length → Bool) :
+    row M C s i a = M.enc (argKeys C s i a) (keyAt C s (inputs + i) false) (s.2.1 i a) := rfl
+
+/-- A simulated table enumerates all assignments in the public fixed order, applies the
+seed row permutation, and emits a simulated row at every position. [PS10, Theorem 163.7]
+`table M C s i = (Finset.univ : Finset (Fin (C.gates[i]).args.length → Bool)).toList.map fun a =>
+row M C s i (s.2.2 i a)`. -/
+noncomputable def table (M : MultiEnc K Cipher ρ) (C : DAGCircuit inputs)
+    (s : GarbleSeed C K ρ) (i : Fin C.gates.length) : List Cipher :=
+  (Finset.univ : Finset (Fin (C.gates[i]).args.length → Bool)).toList.map
+    fun a => row M C s i (s.2.2 i a)
+
+/-- `table M C s i` is the fixed assignment enumeration mapped to `row M C s i (s.perm i a)`.
+`table M C s i = (Finset.univ : Finset (Fin (C.gates[i]).args.length → Bool)).toList.map fun a =>
+row M C s i (s.2.2 i a)`. -/
+theorem table_eq (M : MultiEnc K Cipher ρ) (C : DAGCircuit inputs)
+    (s : GarbleSeed C K ρ) (i : Fin C.gates.length) :
+    table M C s i =
+      (Finset.univ : Finset (Fin (C.gates[i]).args.length → Bool)).toList.map
+        fun a => row M C s i (s.2.2 i a) := rfl
+
+/-- The simulated decoding pair at vertex `v` is `(key_v(b), key_v(!b))`, placing the
+false active key at the position named by the prescribed result `b`. [PS10, Theorem 163.7]
+`decodePair C s v b = (keyAt C s v b, keyAt C s v (!b))`. -/
+def decodePair (C : DAGCircuit inputs) (s : GarbleSeed C K ρ) (v : ℕ) (b : Bool) : K × K :=
+  (keyAt C s v b, keyAt C s v (!b))
+
+/-- `decodePair C s v b = (keyAt C s v b, keyAt C s v (!b))`. -/
+theorem decodePair_eq (C : DAGCircuit inputs) (s : GarbleSeed C K ρ) (v : ℕ) (b : Bool) :
+    decodePair C s v b = (keyAt C s v b, keyAt C s v (!b)) := rfl
+
+/-- The simulated OT answer concatenates `count` false-key blocks beginning at input
+vertex `first`: position `t` reads key `first + t.divNat` at coordinate `t.modNat`.
+[PS10, Theorem 163.7]
+`activeAnswers C s first count t = keyAt C s (first + t.divNat) false t.modNat`. -/
+def activeAnswers {width : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit inputs)
+    (s : GarbleSeed C (Fin width → Bool) ρ) (first count : ℕ) :
+    Fin (count * width) → Bool :=
+  fun t => keyAt C s (first + t.divNat) false t.modNat
+
+/-- `activeAnswers C s first count t = keyAt C s (first + t.divNat) false t.modNat`. -/
+theorem activeAnswers_eq {width : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit inputs)
+    (s : GarbleSeed C (Fin width → Bool) ρ) (first count : ℕ) (t : Fin (count * width)) :
+    activeAnswers C s first count t = keyAt C s (first + t.divNat) false t.modNat := rfl
+
+end TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof.FakeObjects

+/-
+Copyright (c) 2026 TCSlib contributors. All rights reserved.
+Released under Apache 2.0 license as described in the file LICENSE.
+-/
+import TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.Yao.HybridProof.AliceProgram
+import TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.Yao.HybridProof.FakeObjects
+import TCSlib.Cryptography.Asymptotic.OneWay
+
+set_option maxHeartbeats 0
+set_option relaxedAutoImplicit false
+set_option autoImplicit false
+
+/-!
+# Polynomial-time simulated garbling programs
+
+The simulated garbler shares the real garbler's addressed key, nonce, row, and bounded table
+machines. Its output-key bit is always false; output decoding pairs use the prescribed
+result bits. All circuit families and seed equations remain parameters.
+
+## Main definitions
+
+None.
+
+## Main results
+
+* `FakeProgram.activeAnswers_word`, `activeAnswers_poly`: canonical flat active-key answers.
+* `FakeProgram.inputKeys_poly`, `outputPairs_poly`: active input keys and swapped output pairs.
+* `FakeProgram.tables_poly`, `message_poly`: the complete actual simulated garbling program.
+
+## References
+
+* [PS10] R. Pass, a. shelat, *A Course in Cryptography*, lecture notes, 3rd ed., January
+  2010, Theorem 163.7 (simulation for Bob).
+-/
+
+namespace TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof.FakeProgram
+
+open Complexity Turing BoolCircuit TCSlib.Cryptography.EncodingMachines
+
+/-- The canonical simulated answer is the concatenation of the false-key blocks beginning
+at vertex `first`: `ofFn (activeAnswers C s first count) = flatten (ofFn (ofFn ∘ key0))`.
+This reuses the published generic tuple-block identity. [PS10, Theorem 163.7] -/
+theorem activeAnswers_word {inputs width : ℕ} {ρ : Type} (C : DAGCircuit inputs)
+    (s : GarbleSeed C (Fin width → Bool) ρ) (first count : ℕ) :
+    List.ofFn (FakeObjects.activeAnswers C s first count) =
+      (List.ofFn fun i : Fin count => List.ofFn (keyAt C s (first + i) false)).flatten := by
+  have heq : FakeObjects.activeAnswers C s first count =
+      (FunFamily.blocks count width).symm (fun i => keyAt C s (first + i) false) := by
+    funext t
+    rw [FakeObjects.activeAnswers_eq, FunFamily.blocks_symm_apply]
+  rw [heq, FunFamily.ofFn_blocks_symm]
+
+/-- The canonical flat false-key answer is polynomial-time from unary width, bound,
+starting vertex and count maps, a polynomial-time coin word, a valid input range, and the
+seed's addressed-key equation. [PS10, Theorem 163.7]
+`PolyTimeComputable fun z => List.ofFn (FakeObjects.activeAnswers (C z) (s z) (first z) (count z))`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** On an indexed retained input, add the unary index to the starting vertex
+and read its false-key window. The public data-dependent concatenation loop emits one such
+block per requested vertex. The generic tuple-block identity identifies this concatenation
+with the canonical finite answer; the valid-range bound justifies every seed key reader. -/
+theorem activeAnswers_poly (width bound first count inputs : List Bool → ℕ)
+    (coins : List Bool → List Bool)
+    (hwidth : PolyTimeComputable fun z => List.replicate (width z) true)
+    (hbound : PolyTimeComputable fun z => List.replicate (bound z) true)
+    (hfirst : PolyTimeComputable fun z => List.replicate (first z) true)
+    (hcount : PolyTimeComputable fun z => List.replicate (count z) true)
+    (hcoins : PolyTimeComputable coins) (C : ∀ z, DAGCircuit (inputs z))
+    (s : ∀ z, GarbleSeed (C z) (Fin (width z) → Bool)
+      ((Fin (width z) → Bool) × (Fin (width z) → Bool)))
+    (hrange : ∀ z, first z + count z ≤ inputs z)
+    (hkey : ∀ z (v : ℕ), v < (C z).size → ∀ b,
+      keyAt (C z) (s z) v b = fun t : Fin (width z) =>
+        (coins z).getD ((b.toNat * bound z + v) * width z + t) false) :
+    PolyTimeComputable fun z =>
+      List.ofFn (FakeObjects.activeAnswers (C z) (s z) (first z) (count z)) := by
+  let entry := fun z i => RowFromCoins.keyWord width bound (fun v => first v + i)
+    (fun _ => false) coins z
+  have hv := unaryAdd_poly (hfirst.comp polyTimeComputable_pairSndD)
+    (unaryLength_poly polyTimeComputable_pairFstD)
+  have he : PolyTimeComputable fun w => entry (pairSndD w) (pairFstD w).length := by
+    refine (RowFromCoins.keyWord_poly (hcoins.comp polyTimeComputable_pairSndD)
+      (hwidth.comp polyTimeComputable_pairSndD) (hbound.comp polyTimeComputable_pairSndD)
+      hv (polyTimeComputable_const [false])).congr fun w => ?_
+    simp only [entry, RowFromCoins.keyWord_eq, Function.comp_apply]
+  refine (ColMachines.polyTimeComputable_flatMap_range he hcount).congr fun z => ?_
+  rw [activeAnswers_word, List.flatMap_def]
+  apply congrArg List.flatten
+  symm
+  apply ofFn_eq_map_range
+  intro i
+  dsimp only [entry]
+  rw [RowFromCoins.keyWord_eq]
+  apply congrArg List.ofFn
+  apply hkey
+  rw [DAGCircuit.size_eq]
+  have := hrange z
+  omega
+
+/-- Swapping each encoded pair according to the corresponding bit of a polynomial-time
+result word preserves polynomial time. The pair vector is supplied by a polynomial-time
+canonical encoding, and its unary length is polynomial-time. [PS10, Theorem 163.7]
+`PolyTimeComputable fun z => BinArith.encWords (List.ofFn fun j => if (result z).getD j false then
+pairEncode (value z j).2 (value z j).1 else pairEncode (value z j).1 (value z j).2)`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** At a unary index, read the pair from its canonical word vector and read
+the corresponding result bit. A conditional either swaps the two public pair projections
+or retains the original pair. The existing indexed word-vector assembler encodes all these
+entries; canonical list lookup identifies each entry with the requested pair. -/
+theorem swapPairs_poly (count : List Bool → ℕ) (pairs result : List Bool → List Bool)
+    (value : ∀ z, Fin (count z) → List Bool × List Bool)
+    (hcount : PolyTimeComputable fun z => List.replicate (count z) true)
+    (hpairs : PolyTimeComputable pairs) (hresult : PolyTimeComputable result)
+    (hshape : ∀ z, pairs z = BinArith.encWords (List.ofFn fun j =>
+      pairEncode (value z j).1 (value z j).2)) :
+    PolyTimeComputable fun z => BinArith.encWords (List.ofFn fun j =>
+      if (result z).getD j false then pairEncode (value z j).2 (value z j).1
+      else pairEncode (value z j).1 (value z j).2) := by
+  let lookup := fun z i => BinArith.getW (pairEncode (List.replicate i true) (pairs z))
+  let entry := fun z i => if (result z).getD i false then
+    pairEncode (pairSndD (lookup z i)) (pairFstD (lookup z i)) else lookup z i
+  have hl : PolyTimeComputable fun w => lookup (pairSndD w) (pairFstD w).length :=
+    BinArith.polyTimeComputable_getW.comp
+      ((polyTimeComputable_unary.comp polyTimeComputable_pairFstD).pairEncode
+        (hpairs.comp polyTimeComputable_pairSndD))
+  have hb : PolyTimeComputable fun w =>
+      [(result (pairSndD w)).getD (pairFstD w).length false] :=
+    getBit_poly (hresult.comp polyTimeComputable_pairSndD)
+      (unaryLength_poly polyTimeComputable_pairFstD)
+  have he : PolyTimeComputable fun w => entry (pairSndD w) (pairFstD w).length :=
+    polyTimeComputable_ite hb
+      ((polyTimeComputable_pairSndD.comp hl).pairEncode
+        (polyTimeComputable_pairFstD.comp hl)) hl
+  refine WordLists.ofFnTable_poly _ entry hcount he ?_
+  intro z j
+  have hlookup : lookup z j = pairEncode (value z j).1 (value z j).2 := by
+    dsimp only [lookup]
+    rw [hshape z, BinArith.getW_pairEncode, List.length_replicate]
+    rw [List.getD_eq_getElem _ _ (by rw [List.length_ofFn]; exact j.isLt), List.getElem_ofFn]
+  dsimp only [entry]
+  rw [hlookup, pairFstD_pairEncode, pairSndD_pairEncode]
+
+/-- The encoded simulated decoding-pair vector is polynomial-time from the real output
+pair readers and a polynomial-time prescribed result word. Each result bit swaps the
+corresponding two keys. [PS10, Theorem 163.7]
+`PolyTimeComputable fun z => BinArith.encWords (List.ofFn fun j : Fin (count z) => pairEncode
+(List.ofFn (FakeObjects.decodePair (C z) (s z) (output z j) ((result z).getD j false)).1) (List.ofFn
+(FakeObjects.decodePair (C z) (s z) (output z j) ((result z).getD j false)).2))`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** Compute the real canonical output-pair vector once. Apply the generic
+indexed pair-swap contract to that vector and the result word. A Boolean case split on
+each prescribed output bit identifies the swapped pair with the simulated pair reader. -/
+theorem outputPairs_poly (width bound count inputs : List Bool → ℕ)
+    (description coins result : List Bool → List Bool)
+    (hwidth : PolyTimeComputable fun z => List.replicate (width z) true)
+    (hbound : PolyTimeComputable fun z => List.replicate (bound z) true)
+    (hcount : PolyTimeComputable fun z => List.replicate (count z) true)
+    (hdescription : PolyTimeComputable description) (hcoins : PolyTimeComputable coins)
+    (hresult : PolyTimeComputable result) (C : ∀ z, DAGCircuit (inputs z))
+    (s : ∀ z, GarbleSeed (C z) (Fin (width z) → Bool)
+      ((Fin (width z) → Bool) × (Fin (width z) → Bool)))
+    (output : ∀ z, Fin (count z) → ℕ)
+    (houtsWord : ∀ z, pairSndD (description z) =
+      BinArith.encWords (List.ofFn fun j => encodeNat (output z j)))
+    (houtput_lt : ∀ z j, output z j < (C z).size)
+    (hkey : ∀ z (v : ℕ), v < (C z).size → ∀ b,
+      keyAt (C z) (s z) v b = fun t : Fin (width z) =>
+        (coins z).getD ((b.toNat * bound z + v) * width z + t) false) :
+    PolyTimeComputable fun z => BinArith.encWords (List.ofFn fun j : Fin (count z) =>
+      pairEncode (List.ofFn (FakeObjects.decodePair (C z) (s z) (output z j)
+        ((result z).getD j false)).1)
+        (List.ofFn (FakeObjects.decodePair (C z) (s z) (output z j)
+          ((result z).getD j false)).2)) := by
+  let value := fun z (j : Fin (count z)) =>
+    (List.ofFn (keyAt (C z) (s z) (output z j) false),
+      List.ofFn (keyAt (C z) (s z) (output z j) true))
+  let pairs := fun z => BinArith.encWords (List.ofFn fun j =>
+    pairEncode (value z j).1 (value z j).2)
+  have hp : PolyTimeComputable pairs := AliceProgram.outputPairs_poly
+    width bound count inputs description coins hwidth hbound hcount hdescription hcoins
+    C s output houtsWord houtput_lt hkey
+  refine (swapPairs_poly count pairs result value hcount hp hresult (fun _ => rfl)).congr
+    fun z => ?_
+  apply congrArg BinArith.encWords
+  apply congrArg List.ofFn
+  funext j
+  cases hb : (result z).getD j false <;>
+    simp [value, FakeObjects.decodePair_eq]
+
+/-- The encoded simulated Alice input-key vector contains the false key at every input
+vertex and is polynomial-time under the real selected-key reader hypotheses.
+[PS10, Theorem 163.7]
+`PolyTimeComputable fun z => BinArith.encWords (List.ofFn fun v : Fin (count z) => List.ofFn (keyAt
+(C z) (s z) v false))`. -/
+theorem inputKeys_poly (width bound count inputs : List Bool → ℕ)
+    (coins : List Bool → List Bool)
+    (hwidth : PolyTimeComputable fun z => List.replicate (width z) true)
+    (hbound : PolyTimeComputable fun z => List.replicate (bound z) true)
+    (hcount : PolyTimeComputable fun z => List.replicate (count z) true)
+    (hcoins : PolyTimeComputable coins) (C : ∀ z, DAGCircuit (inputs z))
+    (s : ∀ z, GarbleSeed (C z) (Fin (width z) → Bool)
+      ((Fin (width z) → Bool) × (Fin (width z) → Bool)))
+    (hcount_le : ∀ z, count z ≤ inputs z)
+    (hkey : ∀ z (v : ℕ), v < (C z).size → ∀ b,
+      keyAt (C z) (s z) v b = fun t : Fin (width z) =>
+        (coins z).getD ((b.toNat * bound z + v) * width z + t) false) :
+    PolyTimeComputable fun z => BinArith.encWords (List.ofFn fun v : Fin (count z) =>
+      List.ofFn (keyAt (C z) (s z) v false)) := by
+  simpa only [List.getD_nil] using AliceProgram.selectedKeys_poly width bound count inputs
+    (fun _ => []) coins hwidth hbound hcount (polyTimeComputable_const []) hcoins
+    C s hcount_le hkey
+
+/-- The actual encoded simulated table vector is polynomial-time from the generic uniform
+circuit-description map and the seed's key, nonce, and XOR-permutation readers. Every row
+plaintext is its output vertex's false key. [PS10, Theorem 163.7]
+`PolyTimeComputable fun z => BinArith.encWords (List.ofFn fun i => BinArith.encWords
+(FakeObjects.table (nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ (width z)) (C z) (s z) i))`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** Decode the canonical circuit description to the public gate-record word.
+At a unary index, select its argument-word list and invoke the real garbler's generic row
+core with the constant false output bit. The existing guarded table adapter enumerates at
+most four rows, and the indexed word-vector assembler traverses all gate records. To prove
+correctness, transport the retained context through the generic pointwise table contract.
+Canonical record lookup fixes the argument list. Its fan-in bound removes the guard and
+normalizes the dependent assignment enumeration. The arbitrary-output-bit row theorem then
+identifies every enumerated row with the new simulated semantic object. -/
+theorem tables_poly {E₁ E₂ : List Bool → List Bool} (hE₁ : PolyTimeComputable E₁)
+    (hE₂ : PolyTimeComputable E₂) (width bound inputs : List Bool → ℕ)
+    (description outs coins : List Bool → List Bool)
+    (hwidth : PolyTimeComputable fun z => List.replicate (width z) true)
+    (hbound : PolyTimeComputable fun z => List.replicate (bound z) true)
+    (hinputs : PolyTimeComputable fun z => List.replicate (inputs z) true)
+    (hdescription : PolyTimeComputable description) (hcoins : PolyTimeComputable coins)
+    (C : ∀ z, DAGCircuit (inputs z))
+    (s : ∀ z, GarbleSeed (C z) (Fin (width z) → Bool)
+      ((Fin (width z) → Bool) × (Fin (width z) → Bool)))
+    (hshape : ∀ z, description z = pairEncode (C z).encode (outs z))
+    (hfan : ∀ z (i : Fin (C z).gates.length), ((C z).gates[i]).args.length ≤ 2)
+    (hkey : ∀ z (v : ℕ), v < (C z).size → ∀ b,
+      keyAt (C z) (s z) v b = fun t : Fin (width z) =>
+        (coins z).getD ((b.toNat * bound z + v) * width z + t) false)
+    (hr₀ : ∀ z (i : Fin (C z).gates.length) a t, ((s z).2.1 i a).1 t = (coins z).getD
+      (2 * bound z * width z +
+        (4 * i + (List.ofFn a).foldr (fun b k => b.toNat + 2 * k) 0) *
+          (2 * width z) + t) false)
+    (hr₁ : ∀ z (i : Fin (C z).gates.length) a t, ((s z).2.1 i a).2 t = (coins z).getD
+      (2 * bound z * width z +
+        (4 * i + (List.ofFn a).foldr (fun b k => b.toNat + 2 * k) 0) *
+          (2 * width z) + width z + t) false)
+    (hperm : ∀ z (i : Fin (C z).gates.length) a t, (s z).2.2 i a t = xor (a t)
+      ((coins z).getD (10 * bound z * width z + 2 * i + t) false)) :
+    PolyTimeComputable fun z => BinArith.encWords (List.ofFn fun i =>
+      BinArith.encWords (FakeObjects.table (nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ (width z)) (C z) (s z) i)) := by
+  let gates := GarblingProgram.gatesWord description
+  let record := fun w => BinArith.getW (pairEncode (pairFstD w) (gates (pairSndD w)))
+  let args := fun w => pairSndD (record w)
+  let row := fun l a => RowFromCoins.rowWordWith E₁ E₂
+    (fun w => width (pairSndD w)) (fun w => bound (pairSndD w))
+    (fun w => inputs (pairSndD w)) (fun w => (pairFstD w).length)
+    args (fun w => coins (pairSndD w)) (fun _ => false) l a
+  let entry := TablesFromCoins.tableWordWith args row
+  let entryVal := fun z i => entry (pairEncode (List.replicate i true) z)
+  have hgates : PolyTimeComputable gates := GarblingProgram.gatesWord_poly hdescription
+  have hgs (z : List Bool) :
+      gates z = BinArith.encWords ((C z).gates.map DescriptionDecoder.gateRecord) :=
+    GarblingProgram.gatesWord_description description z (C z) (outs z) (hshape z)
+  have hrecord := BinArith.polyTimeComputable_getW.comp
+    (polyTimeComputable_pairFstD.pairEncode (hgates.comp polyTimeComputable_pairSndD))
+  have hargs : PolyTimeComputable args := polyTimeComputable_pairSndD.comp hrecord
+  have hentry : PolyTimeComputable entry := TablesFromCoins.tableWordWith_poly row hargs
+    (fun l _ a => RowFromCoins.rowWordWith_poly hE₁ hE₂
+      (hwidth.comp polyTimeComputable_pairSndD) (hbound.comp polyTimeComputable_pairSndD)
+      (hinputs.comp polyTimeComputable_pairSndD)
+      (unaryLength_poly polyTimeComputable_pairFstD) hargs
+      (hcoins.comp polyTimeComputable_pairSndD) (polyTimeComputable_const [false]) l a)
+  have hcount : PolyTimeComputable fun z => List.replicate (C z).gates.length true := by
+    refine (BinArith.polyTimeComputable_countW.comp hgates).congr fun z => ?_
+    rw [Function.comp_apply, BinArith.countW_eq, hgs z,
+      BinArith.decWords_encWords, List.length_map]
+  have he : PolyTimeComputable fun w => entryVal (pairSndD w) (pairFstD w).length :=
+    hentry.comp ((polyTimeComputable_unary.comp polyTimeComputable_pairFstD).pairEncode
+      polyTimeComputable_pairSndD)
+  refine WordLists.ofFnTable_poly _ entryVal hcount he ?_
+  intro z i
+  symm
+  let w := pairEncode (List.replicate i true) z
+  let args' := fun v => pairSndD (BinArith.getW
+    (pairEncode (List.replicate i true) (gates v)))
+  let row' := fun l a => RowFromCoins.rowWordWith E₁ E₂ width bound inputs
+    (fun _ => i) args' coins (fun _ => false) l a
+  have ha : args w = args' z := by
+    simp only [args, args', record, w, pairFstD_pairEncode, pairSndD_pairEncode]
+  have hr : ∀ l a, row l a w = row' l a z := by
+    intro l a
+    dsimp only [row, row']
+    conv_lhs => rw [RowFromCoins.rowWordWith_value]
+    conv_rhs => rw [RowFromCoins.rowWordWith_value]
+    simp only [args, args', record, w, pairFstD_pairEncode, pairSndD_pairEncode,
+      List.length_replicate]
+  change TablesFromCoins.tableWordWith args row w = _
+  rw [TablesFromCoins.tableWordWith_congr_at args args' row row' w z ha hr]
+  have hrec : BinArith.getW (pairEncode (List.replicate i true) (gates z)) =
+      DescriptionDecoder.gateRecord ((C z).gates[i]) := by
+    rw [hgs z, BinArith.getW_pairEncode, List.length_replicate]
+    rw [List.getD_eq_getElem _ _ (by rw [List.length_map]; exact i.isLt), List.getElem_map]
+    rfl
+  have hcanonical : args' z =
+      BinArith.encWords (((C z).gates[i]).args.map fun v => List.replicate v true) := by
+    dsimp only [args']
+    rw [hrec, DescriptionDecoder.gateRecord_eq, pairSndD_pairEncode]
+  rw [TablesFromCoins.tableWordWith_of_encoded args' row' z _ (hfan z i) hcanonical,
+    FakeObjects.table_eq]
+  apply congrArg BinArith.encWords
+  apply List.map_congr_left
+  intro a _
+  dsimp only [row']
+  rw [FakeObjects.row_eq]
+  exact RowFromCoins.rowWordWith_enc E₁ E₂ width bound inputs (fun _ => i) args' coins
+    (fun _ => false) z (C z) (s z) i a (hfan z i) rfl hcanonical
+    (hkey z) (hr₀ z i) (hr₁ z i) (hperm z i)
+
+/-- The complete simulated Alice message is polynomial-time: simulated tables with false
+row plaintexts, Alice's false input keys, and output pairs swapped by the prescribed output
+word. The assumptions are the generic uniform-description and seed block readers, valid
+input/output ranges, and polynomial-time raw selectors. [PS10, Theorem 163.7]
+`PolyTimeComputable fun z => pairEncode (BinArith.encWords (List.ofFn fun i => BinArith.encWords
+(FakeObjects.table (nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ (width z)) (C z) (s z) i))) (pairEncode (BinArith.encWords
+(List.ofFn fun v : Fin (inA z) => List.ofFn (keyAt (C z) (s z) v false))) (BinArith.encWords
+(List.ofFn fun j : Fin (outLen z) => pairEncode (List.ofFn (FakeObjects.decodePair (C z) (s z)
+(output z j) ((result z).getD j false)).1) (List.ofFn (FakeObjects.decodePair (C z) (s z) (output z
+j) ((result z).getD j false)).2))))`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** The generic simulated-table program computes all false-output-key tables.
+The real selected-key contract at a constant false input gives Alice's active input keys.
+The real output-pair contract followed by the indexed bit-controlled swap gives the simulated
+decoding information. Two applications of the public pairing closure assemble the message. -/
+theorem message_poly {E₁ E₂ : List Bool → List Bool} (hE₁ : PolyTimeComputable E₁)
+    (hE₂ : PolyTimeComputable E₂) (width bound inputs inA outLen : List Bool → ℕ)
+    (description result coins : List Bool → List Bool)
+    (hwidth : PolyTimeComputable fun z => List.replicate (width z) true)
+    (hbound : PolyTimeComputable fun z => List.replicate (bound z) true)
+    (hinputs : PolyTimeComputable fun z => List.replicate (inputs z) true)
+    (hinA : PolyTimeComputable fun z => List.replicate (inA z) true)
+    (houtLen : PolyTimeComputable fun z => List.replicate (outLen z) true)
+    (hdescription : PolyTimeComputable description) (hresult : PolyTimeComputable result)
+    (hcoins : PolyTimeComputable coins) (C : ∀ z, DAGCircuit (inputs z))
+    (s : ∀ z, GarbleSeed (C z) (Fin (width z) → Bool)
+      ((Fin (width z) → Bool) × (Fin (width z) → Bool)))
+    (output : ∀ z, Fin (outLen z) → ℕ)
+    (hshape : ∀ z, description z = pairEncode (C z).encode
+      (BinArith.encWords (List.ofFn fun j => encodeNat (output z j))))
+    (hinA_le : ∀ z, inA z ≤ inputs z)
+    (houtput_lt : ∀ z j, output z j < (C z).size)
+    (hfan : ∀ z (i : Fin (C z).gates.length), ((C z).gates[i]).args.length ≤ 2)
+    (hkey : ∀ z (v : ℕ), v < (C z).size → ∀ b,
+      keyAt (C z) (s z) v b = fun t : Fin (width z) =>
+        (coins z).getD ((b.toNat * bound z + v) * width z + t) false)
+    (hr₀ : ∀ z (i : Fin (C z).gates.length) a t, ((s z).2.1 i a).1 t = (coins z).getD
+      (2 * bound z * width z +
+        (4 * i + (List.ofFn a).foldr (fun b k => b.toNat + 2 * k) 0) *
+          (2 * width z) + t) false)
+    (hr₁ : ∀ z (i : Fin (C z).gates.length) a t, ((s z).2.1 i a).2 t = (coins z).getD
+      (2 * bound z * width z +
+        (4 * i + (List.ofFn a).foldr (fun b k => b.toNat + 2 * k) 0) *
+          (2 * width z) + width z + t) false)
+    (hperm : ∀ z (i : Fin (C z).gates.length) a t, (s z).2.2 i a t = xor (a t)
+      ((coins z).getD (10 * bound z * width z + 2 * i + t) false)) :
+    PolyTimeComputable fun z => pairEncode
+      (BinArith.encWords (List.ofFn fun i =>
+        BinArith.encWords (FakeObjects.table (nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ (width z)) (C z) (s z) i)))
+      (pairEncode
+        (BinArith.encWords (List.ofFn fun v : Fin (inA z) =>
+          List.ofFn (keyAt (C z) (s z) v false)))
+        (BinArith.encWords (List.ofFn fun j : Fin (outLen z) =>
+          pairEncode
+            (List.ofFn (FakeObjects.decodePair (C z) (s z) (output z j)
+              ((result z).getD j false)).1)
+            (List.ofFn (FakeObjects.decodePair (C z) (s z) (output z j)
+              ((result z).getD j false)).2)))) := by
+  have ht := tables_poly hE₁ hE₂ width bound inputs description
+    (fun z => BinArith.encWords (List.ofFn fun j => encodeNat (output z j))) coins
+    hwidth hbound hinputs hdescription hcoins C s hshape hfan hkey hr₀ hr₁ hperm
+  have hk := inputKeys_poly width bound inA inputs coins hwidth hbound hinA hcoins
+    C s hinA_le hkey
+  have houtsWord (z : List Bool) : pairSndD (description z) =
+      BinArith.encWords (List.ofFn fun j => encodeNat (output z j)) := by
+    rw [hshape z, pairSndD_pairEncode]
+  have hp := outputPairs_poly width bound outLen inputs description coins result
+    hwidth hbound houtLen hdescription hcoins hresult C s output houtsWord houtput_lt hkey
+  exact ht.pairEncode (hk.pairEncode hp)
+
+end TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof.FakeProgram

+import TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.Yao.HybridProof.FakeMessage
+import TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.Yao.HybridProof.FakeProgram
+import TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.Yao.HybridProof.BobSimulator
