+set_option maxHeartbeats 0
+set_option relaxedAutoImplicit false
+set_option autoImplicit false
+
+/-!
+# A fixed uniform Bob simulator from circuit and seed readers
+
+The data specify polynomial circuit counts, a uniform unary description generator, and
+an addressed seed family on arbitrary coin words. The actual fake message and active-answer
+leaves are derived, rather than assumed polynomial-time. The existing Bob simulator then
+supplies one fixed machine before any input or distinguisher is chosen.
+
+## Main definitions
+
+* `FakeSimulator.Data`: the circuit, algorithm, polynomial, and seed-reader interface.
+* `messageAt`, `answersAt`: the actual fake message and active answers at a parameter/tape.
+* `message`, `answers`: their total canonical-layout adapters.
+* `simulator`: the existing Bob simulator instantiated by these proved leaves.
+
+## Main results
+
+* `message_poly`, `answers_poly`: actual polynomial-time leaves derived from the data.
+* `simulator_uniform`, `simulator_coins`: one fixed uniform machine and its coin polynomial.
+* `simulator_runAtL`, `simulator_runAt`, `idealB_eq`: actual simulated-view endpoints.
+* `messageAt_ofFn`: exact typed prescribed-output bits without padding.
+
+## References
+
+* [PS10] R. Pass, a. shelat, *A Course in Cryptography*, lecture notes, 3rd ed., January
+  2010, Theorem 163.7 (one efficient Bob simulator before the distinguisher).
+-/
+
+namespace TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof.FakeSimulator
+
+open Complexity Turing BoolCircuit TCSlib.Cryptography.EncodingMachines
+
+/-- The total raw security parameter is the length of the canonical parameter field:
+`parameter z = (NUPPT.inParam z).length`. -/
+def parameter (z : List Bool) : ℕ := (NUPPT.inParam z).length
+
+/-- `parameter z = (NUPPT.inParam z).length`. -/
+theorem parameter_eq (z : List Bool) : parameter z = (NUPPT.inParam z).length := rfl
+
+/-- On the canonical layout with common input `1^n`, `parameter` is exactly `n`.
+`parameter (NUPPT.layout (List.replicate n true) arg advice coins) = n`. -/
+theorem parameter_layout (n : ℕ) (arg advice coins : List Bool) :
+    parameter (NUPPT.layout (List.replicate n true) arg advice coins) = n := by
+  rw [parameter_eq, NUPPT.inParam_layout, List.length_replicate]
+
+/-- Simulator data consist of polynomial counts and bound, polynomial-time encryption
+algorithms and unary description generator, a circuit/output family, and seed key/nonce/XOR
+readers on every raw tape. The seed depends only on the parameter and tape.
+[PS10, Theorem 163.7]
+`Data` has the fields, in order: `E₁ : List Bool → List Bool`, `E₂ : List Bool → List Bool`,
+`E₁_poly : PolyTimeComputable E₁`, `E₂_poly : PolyTimeComputable E₂`, `bound : PolyExpr`, `inputs :
+PolyExpr`, `inA : PolyExpr`, `inB : PolyExpr`, `outLen : PolyExpr`, `gen : List Bool → List Bool`,
+`gen_poly : PolyTimeComputable gen`, `circuit : ∀ n, DAGCircuit (inputs.eval n)`, `output : ∀ n, Fin
+(outLen.eval n) → ℕ`, `shape : ∀ n, gen (List.replicate n true) = pairEncode (circuit n).encode
+(BinArith.encWords (List.ofFn fun j => encodeNat (output n j)))`, `input_range : ∀ n, inA.eval n +
+inB.eval n ≤ inputs.eval n`, `output_lt : ∀ n j, output n j < (circuit n).size`, `fanin : ∀ n (i :
+Fin (circuit n).gates.length), ((circuit n).gates[i]).args.length ≤ 2`, `seed : ∀ n, List Bool →
+GarbleSeed (circuit n) (Fin n → Bool) ((Fin n → Bool) × (Fin n → Bool))`, `key : ∀ n r (v : ℕ), v <
+(circuit n).size → ∀ b, keyAt (circuit n) (seed n r) v b = fun t : Fin n => r.getD ((b.toNat *
+bound.eval n + v) * n + t) false`, `nonce₀ : ∀ n r (i : Fin (circuit n).gates.length) a t, ((seed n
+r).2.1 i a).1 t = r.getD (2 * bound.eval n * n + (4 * i + (List.ofFn a).foldr (fun b k => b.toNat +
+2 * k) 0) * (2 * n) + t) false`, `nonce₁ : ∀ n r (i : Fin (circuit n).gates.length) a t, ((seed n
+r).2.1 i a).2 t = r.getD (2 * bound.eval n * n + (4 * i + (List.ofFn a).foldr (fun b k => b.toNat +
+2 * k) 0) * (2 * n) + n + t) false` and `permutation : ∀ n r (i : Fin (circuit n).gates.length) a t,
+(seed n r).2.2 i a t = xor (a t) (r.getD (10 * bound.eval n * n + 2 * i + t) false)`. -/
+structure Data where
+  /-- The two special-encryption evaluation maps. -/
+  E₁ : List Bool → List Bool
+  E₂ : List Bool → List Bool
+  /-- Both encryption evaluation maps are polynomial-time on every word. -/
+  E₁_poly : PolyTimeComputable E₁
+  E₂_poly : PolyTimeComputable E₂
+  /-- Polynomial wire bound and input/output counts. -/
+  bound : PolyExpr
+  inputs : PolyExpr
+  inA : PolyExpr
+  inB : PolyExpr
+  outLen : PolyExpr
+  /-- The polynomial-time generator is called on a normalized unary parameter. -/
+  gen : List Bool → List Bool
+  gen_poly : PolyTimeComputable gen
+  /-- The circuit and its ordered output vertices at each parameter. -/
+  circuit : ∀ n, DAGCircuit (inputs.eval n)
+  output : ∀ n, Fin (outLen.eval n) → ℕ
+  /-- Unary generation returns the canonical circuit/output-word description. -/
+  shape : ∀ n, gen (List.replicate n true) = pairEncode (circuit n).encode
+    (BinArith.encWords (List.ofFn fun j => encodeNat (output n j)))
+  /-- Alice and Bob's selected input ranges lie inside the circuit inputs. -/
+  input_range : ∀ n, inA.eval n + inB.eval n ≤ inputs.eval n
+  output_lt : ∀ n j, output n j < (circuit n).size
+  fanin : ∀ n (i : Fin (circuit n).gates.length), ((circuit n).gates[i]).args.length ≤ 2
+  /-- The padded raw-tape seed family, independent of private inputs and output bits. -/
+  seed : ∀ n, List Bool → GarbleSeed (circuit n) (Fin n → Bool)
+    ((Fin n → Bool) × (Fin n → Bool))
+  /-- A valid vertex's Boolean key is its addressed length-`n` coin block. -/
+  key : ∀ n r (v : ℕ), v < (circuit n).size → ∀ b,
+    keyAt (circuit n) (seed n r) v b = fun t : Fin n =>
+      r.getD ((b.toNat * bound.eval n + v) * n + t) false
+  /-- The two row nonces occupy the prescribed consecutive length-`n` blocks. -/
+  nonce₀ : ∀ n r (i : Fin (circuit n).gates.length) a t, ((seed n r).2.1 i a).1 t =
+    r.getD (2 * bound.eval n * n +
+      (4 * i + (List.ofFn a).foldr (fun b k => b.toNat + 2 * k) 0) * (2 * n) + t) false
+  nonce₁ : ∀ n r (i : Fin (circuit n).gates.length) a t, ((seed n r).2.1 i a).2 t =
+    r.getD (2 * bound.eval n * n +
+      (4 * i + (List.ofFn a).foldr (fun b k => b.toNat + 2 * k) 0) * (2 * n) + n + t) false
+  /-- The row permutation is the addressed XOR shift. -/
+  permutation : ∀ n r (i : Fin (circuit n).gates.length) a t,
+    (seed n r).2.2 i a t = xor (a t) (r.getD (10 * bound.eval n * n + 2 * i + t) false)
+
+section Constructor
+
+variable
+    (E₁ : List Bool → List Bool)
+    (E₂ : List Bool → List Bool)
+    (E₁_poly : PolyTimeComputable E₁)
+    (E₂_poly : PolyTimeComputable E₂)
+    (bound : PolyExpr)
+    (inputs : PolyExpr)
+    (inA : PolyExpr)
+    (inB : PolyExpr)
+    (outLen : PolyExpr)
+    (gen : List Bool → List Bool)
+    (gen_poly : PolyTimeComputable gen)
+    (circuit : ∀ n, DAGCircuit (inputs.eval n))
+    (output : ∀ n, Fin (outLen.eval n) → ℕ)
+    (shape : ∀ n, gen (List.replicate n true) = pairEncode (circuit n).encode
+    (BinArith.encWords (List.ofFn fun j => encodeNat (output n j))))
+    (input_range : ∀ n, inA.eval n + inB.eval n ≤ inputs.eval n)
+    (output_lt : ∀ n j, output n j < (circuit n).size)
+    (fanin : ∀ n (i : Fin (circuit n).gates.length), ((circuit n).gates[i]).args.length ≤ 2)
+    (seed : ∀ n, List Bool → GarbleSeed (circuit n) (Fin n → Bool)
+    ((Fin n → Bool) × (Fin n → Bool)))
+    (key : ∀ n r (v : ℕ), v < (circuit n).size → ∀ b,
+    keyAt (circuit n) (seed n r) v b = fun t : Fin n =>
+      r.getD ((b.toNat * bound.eval n + v) * n + t) false)
+    (nonce₀ : ∀ n r (i : Fin (circuit n).gates.length) a t, ((seed n r).2.1 i a).1 t =
+    r.getD (2 * bound.eval n * n +
+      (4 * i + (List.ofFn a).foldr (fun b k => b.toNat + 2 * k) 0) * (2 * n) + t) false)
+    (nonce₁ : ∀ n r (i : Fin (circuit n).gates.length) a t, ((seed n r).2.1 i a).2 t =
+    r.getD (2 * bound.eval n * n +
+      (4 * i + (List.ofFn a).foldr (fun b k => b.toNat + 2 * k) 0) * (2 * n) + n + t) false)
+    (permutation : ∀ n r (i : Fin (circuit n).gates.length) a t,
+    (seed n r).2.2 i a t = xor (a t) (r.getD (10 * bound.eval n * n + 2 * i + t) false))
+
+/-- Store the supplied algorithms, polynomials, circuit/output family and seed readers in
+`Data`. Every computational field equals its supplied argument through the generated
+non-simp projection readers. The pointwise word readers below expose the dependent fields
+together, so downstream consumers need no circuit or seed transport.
+`Data.make E₁ E₂ E₁_poly E₂_poly bound inputs inA inB outLen gen gen_poly circuit output shape
+input_range output_lt fanin seed key nonce₀ nonce₁ permutation = ⟨E₁, E₂, E₁_poly, E₂_poly, bound,
+inputs, inA, inB, outLen, gen, gen_poly, circuit, output, shape, input_range, output_lt, fanin,
+seed, key, nonce₀, nonce₁, permutation⟩`. -/
+@[simps (config := { isSimp := false }) E₁ E₂ bound inputs inA inB outLen gen circuit output seed]
+def Data.make : Data where
+  E₁ := E₁
+  E₂ := E₂
+  E₁_poly := E₁_poly
+  E₂_poly := E₂_poly
+  bound := bound
+  inputs := inputs
+  inA := inA
+  inB := inB
+  outLen := outLen
+  gen := gen
+  gen_poly := gen_poly
+  circuit := circuit
+  output := output
+  shape := shape
+  input_range := input_range
+  output_lt := output_lt
+  fanin := fanin
+  seed := seed
+  key := key
+  nonce₀ := nonce₀
+  nonce₁ := nonce₁
+  permutation := permutation
+
+/-- The normalized raw circuit description is `d.gen (1^(parameter z))`.
+`description d z = d.gen (List.replicate (parameter z) true)`. -/
+def description (d : Data) (z : List Bool) : List Bool :=
+  d.gen (List.replicate (parameter z) true)
+
+/-- `description d z = d.gen (List.replicate (parameter z) true)`. -/
+theorem description_eq (d : Data) (z : List Bool) :
+    description d z = d.gen (List.replicate (parameter z) true) := rfl
+
+/-- The actual simulated message at parameter `n`, raw tape `r`, and prescribed output
+word `f` is `FakeObjects.message` with seed `d.seed n r` and result bits `f.getD j false`.
+[PS10, Theorem 163.7]
+`messageAt d n r f = FakeObjects.message (nestedSpecial d.E₁ d.E₂ n) (d.circuit n) (d.seed n r)
+(d.inA.eval n) (d.outLen.eval n) (d.output n) (fun j => f.getD j false)`. -/
+noncomputable def messageAt (d : Data) (n : ℕ) (r f : List Bool) : List Bool :=
+  FakeObjects.message (nestedSpecial d.E₁ d.E₂ n) (d.circuit n) (d.seed n r)
+    (d.inA.eval n) (d.outLen.eval n) (d.output n) (fun j => f.getD j false)
+
+/-- `messageAt d n r f` is the canonical encoded fake message on `d.seed n r`, with Alice
+count `d.inA(n)`, output vertices `d.output n`, and prescribed bits `f.getD j false`.
+`messageAt d n r f = FakeObjects.message (nestedSpecial d.E₁ d.E₂ n) (d.circuit n) (d.seed n r)
+(d.inA.eval n) (d.outLen.eval n) (d.output n) (fun j => f.getD j false)`. -/
+theorem messageAt_eq (d : Data) (n : ℕ) (r f : List Bool) :
+    messageAt d n r f =
+      FakeObjects.message (nestedSpecial d.E₁ d.E₂ n) (d.circuit n) (d.seed n r)
+        (d.inA.eval n) (d.outLen.eval n) (d.output n) (fun j => f.getD j false) := rfl
+
+/-- The active answer at parameter `n` and tape `r` is the canonical flat vector of
+`d.inB(n)` false-key blocks beginning at input vertex `d.inA(n)`.
+`answersAt d n r = List.ofFn (FakeObjects.activeAnswers (d.circuit n) (d.seed n r) (d.inA.eval n)
+(d.inB.eval n))`. -/
+def answersAt (d : Data) (n : ℕ) (r : List Bool) : List Bool :=
+  List.ofFn (FakeObjects.activeAnswers (d.circuit n) (d.seed n r)
+    (d.inA.eval n) (d.inB.eval n))
+
+/-- `answersAt d n r` is `ofFn (activeAnswers (d.circuit n) (d.seed n r) inA(n) inB(n))`.
+`answersAt d n r = List.ofFn (FakeObjects.activeAnswers (d.circuit n) (d.seed n r) (d.inA.eval n)
+(d.inB.eval n))`. -/
+theorem answersAt_eq (d : Data) (n : ℕ) (r : List Bool) :
+    answersAt d n r = List.ofFn (FakeObjects.activeAnswers (d.circuit n) (d.seed n r)
+      (d.inA.eval n) (d.inB.eval n)) := rfl
+
+/-- The total fake-message leaf reads only the parameter length, raw tape, and prescribed
+output word from the canonical NUPPT fields.
+`message d z = messageAt d (parameter z) (NUPPT.inCoins z) (pairSndD (NUPPT.inArg z))`. -/
+noncomputable def message (d : Data) (z : List Bool) : List Bool :=
+  messageAt d (parameter z) (NUPPT.inCoins z) (pairSndD (NUPPT.inArg z))
+
+/-- `message d z = messageAt d (parameter z) (inCoins z) (snd (inArg z))`. -/
+theorem message_eq (d : Data) (z : List Bool) :
+    message d z = messageAt d (parameter z) (NUPPT.inCoins z)
+      (pairSndD (NUPPT.inArg z)) := rfl
+
+/-- The total active-answer leaf reads only the parameter length and raw simulator tape.
+`answers d z = answersAt d (parameter z) (NUPPT.inCoins z)`. -/
+def answers (d : Data) (z : List Bool) : List Bool :=
+  answersAt d (parameter z) (NUPPT.inCoins z)
+
+/-- `answers d z = answersAt d (parameter z) (inCoins z)`. -/
+theorem answers_eq (d : Data) (z : List Bool) :
+    answers d z = answersAt d (parameter z) (NUPPT.inCoins z) := rfl
+
+/-- The unary raw parameter `1^(parameter z)` is polynomial-time computable.
+`PolyTimeComputable fun z => List.replicate (parameter z) true`. -/
+theorem parameter_unary_poly :
+    PolyTimeComputable fun z => List.replicate (parameter z) true :=
+  unaryLength_poly NUPPT.polyTimeComputable_inParam
+
+/-- For every polynomial `p`, the unary value `1^(p(parameter z))` is polynomial-time.
+`PolyTimeComputable fun z => List.replicate (p.eval (parameter z)) true`. -/
+theorem eval_unary_poly (p : PolyExpr) :
+    PolyTimeComputable fun z => List.replicate (p.eval (parameter z)) true :=
+  (polyTimeComputable_unaryPoly p).comp NUPPT.polyTimeComputable_inParam
+
+/-- The normalized unary description map is polynomial-time for every simulator data set.
+`PolyTimeComputable (description d)`. -/
+theorem description_poly (d : Data) : PolyTimeComputable (description d) :=
+  d.gen_poly.comp parameter_unary_poly
+
+/-- The description at every raw word has the canonical circuit/output encoding at its
+parameter length, including when the raw common-input field is malformed.
+`description d z = pairEncode (d.circuit (parameter z)).encode (BinArith.encWords (List.ofFn fun j
+=> encodeNat (d.output (parameter z) j)))`. -/
+theorem description_shape (d : Data) (z : List Bool) :
+    description d z = pairEncode (d.circuit (parameter z)).encode
+      (BinArith.encWords (List.ofFn fun j => encodeNat (d.output (parameter z) j))) :=
+  d.shape (parameter z)
+
+/-- The actual active-answer leaf is polynomial-time from the polynomial counts and seed
+key reader alone; no answer-computability assumption is required. [PS10, Theorem 163.7]
+`PolyTimeComputable (answers d)`. -/
+theorem answers_poly (d : Data) : PolyTimeComputable (answers d) := by
+  simpa only [answers_eq, answersAt_eq] using FakeProgram.activeAnswers_poly
+    parameter (fun z => d.bound.eval (parameter z)) (fun z => d.inA.eval (parameter z))
+    (fun z => d.inB.eval (parameter z)) (fun z => d.inputs.eval (parameter z)) NUPPT.inCoins
+    parameter_unary_poly (eval_unary_poly d.bound) (eval_unary_poly d.inA)
+    (eval_unary_poly d.inB) NUPPT.polyTimeComputable_inCoins
+    (fun z => d.circuit (parameter z)) (fun z => d.seed (parameter z) (NUPPT.inCoins z))
+    (fun z => d.input_range (parameter z))
+    (fun z => d.key (parameter z) (NUPPT.inCoins z))
+
+/-- The actual fake-message leaf is polynomial-time from the polynomial family, uniform
+description generator, encryption algorithms, and seed block readers. No fake-message
+computability assumption is required. [PS10, Theorem 163.7]
+`PolyTimeComputable (message d)`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** Normalize the parameter before calling the unary uniform generator, so
+every raw word receives the canonical description of the circuit at its parameter length.
+The polynomial counts and bound are computed through the public unary polynomial routine.
+Read the prescribed output from the argument's second component and the random tape from
+its canonical field. Apply the complete fake garbling program to these selectors. Its input
+range and output bounds follow from the family data; all key, nonce, and permutation premises
+are exactly the supplied addressed seed readers. The public message equation identifies
+the resulting actual encoded tables, active keys, and decoding pairs with this leaf. -/
+theorem message_poly (d : Data) : PolyTimeComputable (message d) := by
+  have hinA : ∀ z, d.inA.eval (parameter z) ≤ d.inputs.eval (parameter z) := by
+    intro z
+    have := d.input_range (parameter z)
+    omega
+  have hM := FakeProgram.message_poly d.E₁_poly d.E₂_poly
+    parameter (fun z => d.bound.eval (parameter z)) (fun z => d.inputs.eval (parameter z))
+    (fun z => d.inA.eval (parameter z)) (fun z => d.outLen.eval (parameter z))
+    (description d) (fun z => pairSndD (NUPPT.inArg z)) NUPPT.inCoins
+    parameter_unary_poly (eval_unary_poly d.bound) (eval_unary_poly d.inputs)
+    (eval_unary_poly d.inA) (eval_unary_poly d.outLen) (description_poly d)
+    (polyTimeComputable_pairSndD.comp NUPPT.polyTimeComputable_inArg)
+    NUPPT.polyTimeComputable_inCoins (fun z => d.circuit (parameter z))
+    (fun z => d.seed (parameter z) (NUPPT.inCoins z)) (fun z => d.output (parameter z))
+    (description_shape d) hinA (fun z => d.output_lt (parameter z))
+    (fun z => d.fanin (parameter z)) (fun z => d.key (parameter z) (NUPPT.inCoins z))
+    (fun z => d.nonce₀ (parameter z) (NUPPT.inCoins z))
+    (fun z => d.nonce₁ (parameter z) (NUPPT.inCoins z))
+    (fun z => d.permutation (parameter z) (NUPPT.inCoins z))
+  refine hM.congr fun z => ?_
+  rw [message_eq, messageAt_eq, FakeObjects.message_eq]
+
+/-- On every canonical layout, including arbitrary argument and tape lengths, the raw
+message leaf is exactly the pointwise fake message at `n`, tape `r`, and output word `f`.
+`message d (NUPPT.layout (List.replicate n true) (pairEncode y f) advice r) = messageAt d n r f`. -/
+theorem message_layout (d : Data) (n : ℕ) (y f advice r : List Bool) :
+    message d (NUPPT.layout (List.replicate n true) (pairEncode y f) advice r) =
+      messageAt d n r f := by
+  rw [message_eq, parameter_layout, NUPPT.inCoins_layout,
+    NUPPT.inArg_layout, pairSndD_pairEncode]
+
+/-- The complete encoded simulated-view map is polynomial-time from the actual derived
+message and answer leaves. [PS10, Theorem 163.7]
+`PolyTimeComputable (BobSimulator.raw (message d) (answers d))`. -/
+theorem view_poly (d : Data) :
+    PolyTimeComputable (BobSimulator.raw (message d) (answers d)) :=
+  BobSimulator.raw_poly (message_poly d) (answers_poly d)
+
+/-- The simulator is the existing Bob simulator specialized to the proved actual message
+and answer leaves. Its data and coin polynomial are fixed before any distinguisher.
+[PS10, Theorem 163.7]
+`simulator d coins = BobSimulator.simulator (message_poly d) (answers_poly d) coins`. -/
+noncomputable def simulator (d : Data) (coins : PolyExpr) : NUPPT :=
+  BobSimulator.simulator (message_poly d) (answers_poly d) coins
+
+/-- `simulator d coins = BobSimulator.simulator (message_poly d) (answers_poly d) coins`. -/
+theorem simulator_eq (d : Data) (coins : PolyExpr) :
+    simulator d coins = BobSimulator.simulator (message_poly d) (answers_poly d) coins := rfl
+
+/-- The one fixed simulator is uniform for every supplied polynomial coin budget.
+`(simulator d coins).Uniform`. -/
+theorem simulator_uniform (d : Data) (coins : PolyExpr) : (simulator d coins).Uniform :=
+  BobSimulator.simulator_uniform (message_poly d) (answers_poly d) coins
+
+/-- The fixed simulator's coin polynomial is exactly the supplied `coins`.
+`(simulator d coins).coins = coins`. -/
+theorem simulator_coins (d : Data) (coins : PolyExpr) : (simulator d coins).coins = coins :=
+  BobSimulator.simulator_coins (message_poly d) (answers_poly d) coins
+
+/-- On a canonical layout, the raw active-answer leaf is exactly the pointwise flat
+answer at the parameter and raw simulator tape; it ignores argument and advice fields.
+`answers d (NUPPT.layout (List.replicate n true) arg advice r) = answersAt d n r`. -/
+theorem answers_layout (d : Data) (n : ℕ) (arg advice r : List Bool) :
+    answers d (NUPPT.layout (List.replicate n true) arg advice r) = answersAt d n r := by
+  rw [answers_eq, parameter_layout, NUPPT.inCoins_layout]
+
+/-- On any raw input/output/tape words, the one fixed simulator returns exactly Bob's
+encoded view with the actual pointwise fake message and active answers, and an empty
+private tape. This specializes the existing Bob simulator contract. [PS10, Theorem 163.7]
+`(simulator d coins).runAtL n (pairEncode y f) r = (⟨List.replicate n true, pairEncode y (answersAt
+d n r), [], [messageAt d n r f]⟩ : View).encode`. -/
+theorem simulator_runAtL (d : Data) (coins : PolyExpr) (n : ℕ) (y f r : List Bool) :
+    (simulator d coins).runAtL n (pairEncode y f) r =
+      (⟨List.replicate n true, pairEncode y (answersAt d n r),
+        [], [messageAt d n r f]⟩ : View).encode := by
+  rw [simulator_eq, BobSimulator.simulator_runAtL]
+  simp only [message_layout, answers_layout]
+
+/-- On a typed tape, the fixed simulator returns the same actual fake view with raw tape
+`List.ofFn r`. The only randomness is its polynomial-budget tape. [PS10, Theorem 163.7]
+`(simulator d coins).runAt n (pairEncode y f) r = (⟨List.replicate n true, pairEncode y (answersAt d
+n (List.ofFn r)), [], [messageAt d n (List.ofFn r) f]⟩ : View).encode`. -/
+theorem simulator_runAt (d : Data) (coins : PolyExpr) (n : ℕ) (y f : List Bool)
+    (r : (simulator d coins).Coins n) :
+    (simulator d coins).runAt n (pairEncode y f) r =
+      (⟨List.replicate n true, pairEncode y (answersAt d n (List.ofFn r)),
+        [], [messageAt d n (List.ofFn r) f]⟩ : View).encode := by
+  rw [NUPPT.runAt_eq, simulator_runAtL]
+
+/-- The ideal Bob sample is the actual fake view built from Bob's input, the prescribed
+Bob output and the simulator tape, paired with Alice's true functionality output. The same
+fixed simulator works before every input and distinguisher. [PS10, Theorem 163.7]
+`F.idealB (simulator d coins) n x y r = pairEncode (⟨List.replicate n true, pairEncode (List.ofFn y)
+(answersAt d n (List.ofFn r)), [], [messageAt d n (List.ofFn r) (List.ofFn (F.f n x y).2)]⟩ :
+View).encode (List.ofFn (F.f n x y).1)`. -/
+theorem idealB_eq (d : Data) (coins : PolyExpr) (F : Functionality) (n : ℕ)
+    (x : Fin (F.inA.eval n) → Bool) (y : Fin (F.inB.eval n) → Bool)
+    (r : (simulator d coins).Coins n) :
+    F.idealB (simulator d coins) n x y r =
+      pairEncode
+        (⟨List.replicate n true, pairEncode (List.ofFn y) (answersAt d n (List.ofFn r)),
+          [], [messageAt d n (List.ofFn r) (List.ofFn (F.f n x y).2)]⟩ : View).encode
+        (List.ofFn (F.f n x y).1) := by
+  simpa only [message_layout, answers_layout] using
+    BobSimulator.idealB_eq (message_poly d) (answers_poly d) coins F n x y r
+
+/-- On a typed prescribed output vector, `messageAt` uses its actual bits without padding:
+`messageAt d n r (ofFn f) = FakeObjects.message ... f`. -/
+theorem messageAt_ofFn (d : Data) (n : ℕ) (r : List Bool)
+    (f : Fin (d.outLen.eval n) → Bool) :
+    messageAt d n r (List.ofFn f) =
+      FakeObjects.message (nestedSpecial d.E₁ d.E₂ n) (d.circuit n) (d.seed n r)
+        (d.inA.eval n) (d.outLen.eval n) (d.output n) f := by
+  rw [messageAt_eq]
+  apply congrArg (FakeObjects.message (nestedSpecial d.E₁ d.E₂ n)
+    (d.circuit n) (d.seed n r) (d.inA.eval n) (d.outLen.eval n) (d.output n))
+  funext j
+  rw [List.getD_eq_getElem _ _ (by rw [List.length_ofFn]; exact j.isLt), List.getElem_ofFn]
+
+/-- For the public constructor, the actual fake-message word uses exactly the supplied
+algorithms, circuit, seed, counts, output vertices and prescribed result bits.
+`messageAt (Data.make (E₁ := E₁) (E₂ := E₂) (E₁_poly := E₁_poly) (E₂_poly := E₂_poly) (bound :=
+bound) (inputs := inputs) (inA := inA) (inB := inB) (outLen := outLen) (gen := gen) (gen_poly :=
+gen_poly) (circuit := circuit) (output := output) (shape := shape) (input_range := input_range)
+(output_lt := output_lt) (fanin := fanin) (seed := seed) (key := key) (nonce₀ := nonce₀) (nonce₁ :=
+nonce₁) (permutation := permutation)) n r f = FakeObjects.message (nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ n) (circuit
+n) (seed n r) (inA.eval n) (outLen.eval n) (output n) (fun j => f.getD j false)`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** Read the stored constructor fields in the pointwise message equation;
+all dependent circuit, output, and seed arguments are exposed together. -/
+theorem Data.make_messageAt (n : ℕ) (r f : List Bool) :
+    messageAt (Data.make
+      (E₁ := E₁)
+      (E₂ := E₂)
+      (E₁_poly := E₁_poly)
+      (E₂_poly := E₂_poly)
+      (bound := bound)
+      (inputs := inputs)
+      (inA := inA)
+      (inB := inB)
+      (outLen := outLen)
+      (gen := gen)
+      (gen_poly := gen_poly)
+      (circuit := circuit)
+      (output := output)
+      (shape := shape)
+      (input_range := input_range)
+      (output_lt := output_lt)
+      (fanin := fanin)
+      (seed := seed)
+      (key := key)
+      (nonce₀ := nonce₀)
+      (nonce₁ := nonce₁)
+      (permutation := permutation)) n r f =
+      FakeObjects.message (nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ n) (circuit n) (seed n r)
+        (inA.eval n) (outLen.eval n) (output n) (fun j => f.getD j false) := rfl
+
+/-- For the public constructor, the active-answer word uses exactly the supplied circuit,
+seed and polynomial input ranges.
+`answersAt (Data.make (E₁ := E₁) (E₂ := E₂) (E₁_poly := E₁_poly) (E₂_poly := E₂_poly) (bound :=
+bound) (inputs := inputs) (inA := inA) (inB := inB) (outLen := outLen) (gen := gen) (gen_poly :=
+gen_poly) (circuit := circuit) (output := output) (shape := shape) (input_range := input_range)
+(output_lt := output_lt) (fanin := fanin) (seed := seed) (key := key) (nonce₀ := nonce₀) (nonce₁ :=
+nonce₁) (permutation := permutation)) n r = List.ofFn (FakeObjects.activeAnswers (circuit n) (seed n
+r) (inA.eval n) (inB.eval n))`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** Read the constructor's circuit, seed and input-range fields in the
+pointwise answer equation; both sides are the same finite vector. -/
+theorem Data.make_answersAt (n : ℕ) (r : List Bool) :
+    answersAt (Data.make
+      (E₁ := E₁)
+      (E₂ := E₂)
+      (E₁_poly := E₁_poly)
+      (E₂_poly := E₂_poly)
+      (bound := bound)
+      (inputs := inputs)
+      (inA := inA)
+      (inB := inB)
+      (outLen := outLen)
+      (gen := gen)
+      (gen_poly := gen_poly)
+      (circuit := circuit)
+      (output := output)
+      (shape := shape)
+      (input_range := input_range)
+      (output_lt := output_lt)
+      (fanin := fanin)
+      (seed := seed)
+      (key := key)
+      (nonce₀ := nonce₀)
+      (nonce₁ := nonce₁)
+      (permutation := permutation)) n r =
+      List.ofFn (FakeObjects.activeAnswers (circuit n) (seed n r)
+        (inA.eval n) (inB.eval n)) := rfl
+
+end Constructor
+
+end TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof.FakeSimulator

+/-
+Copyright (c) 2026 TCSlib contributors. All rights reserved.
+Released under Apache 2.0 license as described in the file LICENSE.
+-/
+import TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.Yao.HybridProof.FakeSimulator
+
+set_option maxHeartbeats 0
+set_option relaxedAutoImplicit false
+set_option autoImplicit false
+
+/-!
+# Fixed simulator contracts from supplied circuit and seed families
+
+The factory consumes the existing data constructor once and returns simple word equations
+on an abstract data value. This avoids downstream matching of its dependent constructor.
+
+## Main definitions
+
+None.
+
+## Main results
+
+* `exists_data`: supplied families admit data with the actual pointwise word equations.
+* `exists_simulator`: one fixed uniform machine with actual run and ideal-sample equations.
+
+## References
+
+* [PS10] R. Pass, a. shelat, *A Course in Cryptography*, lecture notes, 3rd ed., January
+  2010, Theorem 163.7 (one fixed efficient simulator).
+-/
+
+namespace TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof.FakeSimulatorFactory
+
+open Complexity Turing BoolCircuit
+
+variable
+    (E₁ : List Bool → List Bool)
+    (E₂ : List Bool → List Bool)
+    (E₁_poly : PolyTimeComputable E₁)
+    (E₂_poly : PolyTimeComputable E₂)
+    (bound : PolyExpr)
+    (inputs : PolyExpr)
+    (inA : PolyExpr)
+    (inB : PolyExpr)
+    (outLen : PolyExpr)
+    (gen : List Bool → List Bool)
+    (gen_poly : PolyTimeComputable gen)
+    (circuit : ∀ n, DAGCircuit (inputs.eval n))
+    (output : ∀ n, Fin (outLen.eval n) → ℕ)
+    (shape : ∀ n, gen (List.replicate n true) = pairEncode (circuit n).encode
+    (BinArith.encWords (List.ofFn fun j => encodeNat (output n j))))
+    (input_range : ∀ n, inA.eval n + inB.eval n ≤ inputs.eval n)
+    (output_lt : ∀ n j, output n j < (circuit n).size)
+    (fanin : ∀ n (i : Fin (circuit n).gates.length), ((circuit n).gates[i]).args.length ≤ 2)
+    (seed : ∀ n, List Bool → GarbleSeed (circuit n) (Fin n → Bool)
+    ((Fin n → Bool) × (Fin n → Bool)))
+    (key : ∀ n r (v : ℕ), v < (circuit n).size → ∀ b,
+    keyAt (circuit n) (seed n r) v b = fun t : Fin n =>
+      r.getD ((b.toNat * bound.eval n + v) * n + t) false)
+    (nonce₀ : ∀ n r (i : Fin (circuit n).gates.length) a t, ((seed n r).2.1 i a).1 t =
+    r.getD (2 * bound.eval n * n +
+      (4 * i + (List.ofFn a).foldr (fun b k => b.toNat + 2 * k) 0) * (2 * n) + t) false)
+    (nonce₁ : ∀ n r (i : Fin (circuit n).gates.length) a t, ((seed n r).2.1 i a).2 t =
+    r.getD (2 * bound.eval n * n +
+      (4 * i + (List.ofFn a).foldr (fun b k => b.toNat + 2 * k) 0) * (2 * n) + n + t) false)
+    (permutation : ∀ n r (i : Fin (circuit n).gates.length) a t,
+    (seed n r).2.2 i a t = xor (a t) (r.getD (10 * bound.eval n * n + 2 * i + t) false))
+
+include E₁_poly E₂_poly gen_poly shape input_range output_lt fanin key nonce₀ nonce₁ permutation
+
+/-- The supplied algorithms, polynomial counts, circuit/output family and seed readers
+admit one data value whose actual answer and message words are exactly those supplied.
+The public data constructor and its word equations are consumed in a generic context.
+[PS10, Theorem 163.7]
+`∃ d : FakeSimulator.Data, (∀ n r, FakeSimulator.answersAt d n r = List.ofFn
+(FakeObjects.activeAnswers (circuit n) (seed n r) (inA.eval n) (inB.eval n))) ∧ ∀ n r f,
+FakeSimulator.messageAt d n r f = FakeObjects.message (nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ n) (circuit n) (seed n r)
+(inA.eval n) (outLen.eval n) (output n) (fun j => f.getD j false)`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** Construct the existing data value from the supplied fields. Apply each
+of its pointwise constructor equations with every argument explicit. The resulting word
+equations expose the dependent families together, without asking consumers to match them. -/
+theorem exists_data :
+    ∃ d : FakeSimulator.Data,
+      (∀ n r, FakeSimulator.answersAt d n r =
+        List.ofFn (FakeObjects.activeAnswers (circuit n) (seed n r)
+          (inA.eval n) (inB.eval n))) ∧
+      ∀ n r f, FakeSimulator.messageAt d n r f =
+        FakeObjects.message (nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ n) (circuit n) (seed n r)
+          (inA.eval n) (outLen.eval n) (output n) (fun j => f.getD j false) := by
+  refine ⟨FakeSimulator.Data.make
+      (E₁ := E₁)
+      (E₂ := E₂)
+      (E₁_poly := E₁_poly)
+      (E₂_poly := E₂_poly)
+      (bound := bound)
+      (inputs := inputs)
+      (inA := inA)
+      (inB := inB)
+      (outLen := outLen)
+      (gen := gen)
+      (gen_poly := gen_poly)
+      (circuit := circuit)
+      (output := output)
+      (shape := shape)
+      (input_range := input_range)
+      (output_lt := output_lt)
+      (fanin := fanin)
+      (seed := seed)
+      (key := key)
+      (nonce₀ := nonce₀)
+      (nonce₁ := nonce₁)
+      (permutation := permutation), ?_, ?_⟩
+  · intro n r
+    exact FakeSimulator.Data.make_answersAt
+      (E₁ := E₁)
+      (E₂ := E₂)
+      (E₁_poly := E₁_poly)
+      (E₂_poly := E₂_poly)
+      (bound := bound)
+      (inputs := inputs)
+      (inA := inA)
+      (inB := inB)
+      (outLen := outLen)
+      (gen := gen)
+      (gen_poly := gen_poly)
+      (circuit := circuit)
+      (output := output)
+      (shape := shape)
+      (input_range := input_range)
+      (output_lt := output_lt)
+      (fanin := fanin)
+      (seed := seed)
+      (key := key)
+      (nonce₀ := nonce₀)
+      (nonce₁ := nonce₁)
+      (permutation := permutation)
+      n r
+  · intro n r f
+    exact FakeSimulator.Data.make_messageAt
+      (E₁ := E₁)
+      (E₂ := E₂)
+      (E₁_poly := E₁_poly)
+      (E₂_poly := E₂_poly)
+      (bound := bound)
+      (inputs := inputs)
+      (inA := inA)
+      (inB := inB)
+      (outLen := outLen)
+      (gen := gen)
+      (gen_poly := gen_poly)
+      (circuit := circuit)
+      (output := output)
+      (shape := shape)
+      (input_range := input_range)
+      (output_lt := output_lt)
+      (fanin := fanin)
+      (seed := seed)
+      (key := key)
+      (nonce₀ := nonce₀)
+      (nonce₁ := nonce₁)
+      (permutation := permutation)
+      n r f
+
+/-- The supplied families admit one fixed uniform simulator with the prescribed coin
+polynomial and exact raw-run and ideal-sampler equations in the original supplied families.
+No downstream dependent-constructor matching or assumed leaf efficiency is needed.
+[PS10, Theorem 163.7]
+`∃ SB : NUPPT, SB.Uniform ∧ SB.coins = coins ∧ (∀ n y f r, SB.runAtL n (pairEncode y f) r =
+(⟨List.replicate n true, pairEncode y (List.ofFn (FakeObjects.activeAnswers (circuit n) (seed n r)
+(inA.eval n) (inB.eval n))), [], [FakeObjects.message (nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ n) (circuit n) (seed n r)
+(inA.eval n) (outLen.eval n) (output n) (fun j => f.getD j false)]⟩ : View).encode) ∧ ∀ (F :
+Functionality) n (x : Fin (F.inA.eval n) → Bool) (y : Fin (F.inB.eval n) → Bool) (r : SB.Coins n),
+F.idealB SB n x y r = pairEncode (⟨List.replicate n true, pairEncode (List.ofFn y) (List.ofFn
+(FakeObjects.activeAnswers (circuit n) (seed n (List.ofFn r)) (inA.eval n) (inB.eval n))), [],
+[FakeObjects.message (nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ n) (circuit n) (seed n (List.ofFn r)) (inA.eval n)
+(outLen.eval n) (output n) (fun j => (List.ofFn (F.f n x y).2).getD j false)]⟩ : View).encode
+(List.ofFn (F.f n x y).1)`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** Obtain the existing data value together with its two plain word equations.
+Choose the previously proved uniform simulator on this data. Its coin contract supplies the
+budget. Specialize its raw-run and ideal-sampler contracts, then replace the pointwise answer
+and message words using the two supplied equations. This keeps the machine fixed before
+any input, functionality, or distinguisher is introduced. -/
+theorem exists_simulator (coins : PolyExpr) :
+    ∃ SB : NUPPT, SB.Uniform ∧ SB.coins = coins ∧
+      (∀ n y f r, SB.runAtL n (pairEncode y f) r =
+        (⟨List.replicate n true, pairEncode y
+          (List.ofFn (FakeObjects.activeAnswers (circuit n) (seed n r)
+            (inA.eval n) (inB.eval n))), [],
+          [FakeObjects.message (nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ n) (circuit n) (seed n r)
+            (inA.eval n) (outLen.eval n) (output n) (fun j => f.getD j false)]⟩ : View).encode) ∧
+      ∀ (F : Functionality) n (x : Fin (F.inA.eval n) → Bool)
+        (y : Fin (F.inB.eval n) → Bool) (r : SB.Coins n),
+        F.idealB SB n x y r =
+          pairEncode
+            (⟨List.replicate n true, pairEncode (List.ofFn y)
+              (List.ofFn (FakeObjects.activeAnswers (circuit n) (seed n (List.ofFn r))
+                (inA.eval n) (inB.eval n))), [],
+              [FakeObjects.message (nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ n) (circuit n) (seed n (List.ofFn r))
+                (inA.eval n) (outLen.eval n) (output n)
+                (fun j => (List.ofFn (F.f n x y).2).getD j false)]⟩ : View).encode
+            (List.ofFn (F.f n x y).1) := by
+  obtain ⟨d, ha, hm⟩ := exists_data
+      (E₁ := E₁)
+      (E₂ := E₂)
+      (E₁_poly := E₁_poly)
+      (E₂_poly := E₂_poly)
+      (bound := bound)
+      (inputs := inputs)
+      (inA := inA)
+      (inB := inB)
+      (outLen := outLen)
+      (gen := gen)
+      (gen_poly := gen_poly)
+      (circuit := circuit)
+      (output := output)
+      (shape := shape)
+      (input_range := input_range)
+      (output_lt := output_lt)
+      (fanin := fanin)
+      (seed := seed)
+      (key := key)
+      (nonce₀ := nonce₀)
+      (nonce₁ := nonce₁)
+      (permutation := permutation)
+  refine ⟨FakeSimulator.simulator d coins, FakeSimulator.simulator_uniform d coins,
+    FakeSimulator.simulator_coins d coins, ?_, ?_⟩
+  · intro n y f r
+    rw [FakeSimulator.simulator_runAtL, ha, hm]
+  · intro F n x y r
+    rw [FakeSimulator.idealB_eq, ha, hm]
+
+end TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof.FakeSimulatorFactory

+/-

+Released under Apache 2.0 license as described in the file LICENSE.

+import TCSlib.Cryptography.ZeroKnowledge.EncodingMachines
+import TCSlib.Cryptography.Foundations.BitLists

+set_option maxHeartbeats 0
+set_option relaxedAutoImplicit false
+set_option autoImplicit false

+/-!
+# First successful tagged row in polynomial time
+
+This adapter uses the existing bounded first-index host. Each row is tested by a
+polynomial-time word function returning `[]` on failure and a nonempty tagged
+payload on success. The result is its first successful payload, or `[]`.
+
+## Main definitions
+
+* `YaoHybridProof.firstTagged`: first nonempty row result, using `EncodingMachines.firstIdx`.
+
+## Main results
+
+* `YaoHybridProof.polyTimeComputable_reencodeWords`: normalize a word-list encoding.
+* `YaoHybridProof.firstTagged_poly`: polynomial time on arbitrary row encodings.
+* `YaoHybridProof.firstTagged_encoded`: agreement with `List.findSome?`.
+
+## References
+
+* [PS10] R. Pass, a. shelat, *A Course in Cryptography*, lecture notes, 3rd ed.,
+  January 2010, Protocol 162.6 (first successful row decryption).
+-/
+
+namespace TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof
+
+open Complexity Turing
+
+/-- Re-encoding the word list read from any word is polynomial-time:
+`w ↦ BinArith.encWords (BinArith.decWords w)`.
+It is `mapW pairSndD` with empty retained context.
+`PolyTimeComputable fun w => BinArith.encWords (BinArith.decWords w)`. -/
+theorem polyTimeComputable_reencodeWords :
+    PolyTimeComputable fun w => BinArith.encWords (BinArith.decWords w) := by
+  have h := (BinArith.polyTimeComputable_mapW polyTimeComputable_pairSndD).comp
+    ((polyTimeComputable_const []).pairEncode polyTimeComputable_id)
+  convert h using 1
+  funext w
+  rw [Function.comp_apply, BinArith.mapW_pairEncode]
+  simp only [pairSndD_pairEncode, id_eq, List.map_id']
+
+/-- On `⟨q, rows⟩`, return the first nonempty `F ⟨q, row⟩` among
+`BinArith.decWords rows`, or `[]` if every result is empty.
+The index is selected by the published bounded first-index host.
+[PS10, Protocol 162.6]
+`firstTagged F z = let rows := BinArith.decWords (pairSndD z); let i := EncodingMachines.firstIdx
+(fun j => !decide (F (pairEncode (pairFstD z) (rows.getD j [])) = [])) rows.length; if rows.length ≤
+i then [] else F (pairEncode (pairFstD z) (rows.getD i []))`. -/
+noncomputable def firstTagged (F : List Bool → List Bool) (z : List Bool) : List Bool :=
+  let rows := BinArith.decWords (pairSndD z)
+  let i := EncodingMachines.firstIdx
+    (fun j => !decide (F (pairEncode (pairFstD z) (rows.getD j [])) = [])) rows.length
+  if rows.length ≤ i then [] else F (pairEncode (pairFstD z) (rows.getD i []))
+
+/-- For every `z`, `firstTagged F z` uses the first index accepted by
+`j ↦ !decide (F ⟨fst z, (decWords (snd z)).getD j []⟩ = [])`,
+and returns `[]` when that index exhausts the row count. -/
+theorem firstTagged_eq (F : List Bool → List Bool) (z : List Bool) :
+    firstTagged F z =
+      let rows := BinArith.decWords (pairSndD z)
+      let i := EncodingMachines.firstIdx
+        (fun j => !decide (F (pairEncode (pairFstD z) (rows.getD j [])) = [])) rows.length
+      if rows.length ≤ i then [] else F (pairEncode (pairFstD z) (rows.getD i [])) := rfl
+
+/-- For polynomial-time `F`, `firstTagged F` is polynomial-time on every input word.
+[PS10, Protocol 162.6]
+`PolyTimeComputable (firstTagged F)`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** Normalize the row encoding so that the public list-lookup reader
+applies even to malformed input words. The bounded first-index host tests whether
+`F` on the selected row is nonempty, using the polynomial-time row count as its
+bound. Read the selected row once more and apply `F`, with an empty result when
+the index reaches the row count. This reuses the existing search host. -/
+theorem firstTagged_poly {F : List Bool → List Bool} (hF : PolyTimeComputable F) :
+    PolyTimeComputable (firstTagged F) := by
+  have hcanon := polyTimeComputable_reencodeWords.comp polyTimeComputable_pairSndD
+  have hlookup : PolyTimeComputable fun w =>
+      (BinArith.decWords (pairSndD (pairSndD w))).getD (pairFstD w).length [] := by
+    have h := BinArith.polyTimeComputable_getW.comp
+      (polyTimeComputable_pairFstD.pairEncode (hcanon.comp polyTimeComputable_pairSndD))
+    convert h using 1
+    funext w
+    simp only [Function.comp_apply, BinArith.getW_pairEncode]
+  have htest := polyTimeComputable_not (polyTimeComputable_isNil.comp
+    (hF.comp ((polyTimeComputable_pairFstD.comp polyTimeComputable_pairSndD).pairEncode
+      hlookup)))
+  have hcount : PolyTimeComputable fun z =>
+      List.replicate (BinArith.decWords (pairSndD z)).length true := by
+    exact (BinArith.polyTimeComputable_countW.comp polyTimeComputable_pairSndD).congr
+      (fun z => by rw [Function.comp_apply, BinArith.countW_eq])
+  have hindex := EncodingMachines.polyTimeComputable_firstIdx
+    (p := fun z j => !decide (F (pairEncode (pairFstD z)
+      ((BinArith.decWords (pairSndD z)).getD j [])) = [])) htest hcount
+  have hchosen := BinArith.polyTimeComputable_getW.comp (hindex.pairEncode hcanon)
+  have hresult := hF.comp (polyTimeComputable_pairFstD.pairEncode hchosen)
+  have hlast := polyTimeComputable_lenLe.comp (hindex.pairEncode hcount)
+  have h := polyTimeComputable_ite hlast (polyTimeComputable_const []) hresult
+  convert h using 1
+  funext z
+  simp only [firstTagged_eq, Function.comp_apply, pairFstD_pairEncode,
+    pairSndD_pairEncode, BinArith.getW_pairEncode, List.length_replicate, decide_eq_true_eq]
+
+/-- If `F ⟨q, row⟩` tags `f row` by `none ↦ []`, `some b ↦ true :: payload b`,
+then `firstTagged F ⟨q, encWords rows⟩` tags `rows.findSome? f` in the same way.
+`firstTagged F (pairEncode q (BinArith.encWords rows)) = match rows.findSome? f with | none => [] |
+some b => true :: payload b`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** If every row fails, the first-index characterization gives the
+row count. Otherwise the published first-success characterization splits the
+list into a failing prefix, the successful row and the remaining suffix. The
+first-index characterization selects exactly the prefix length, whose lookup
+is the successful row. -/
+theorem firstTagged_encoded {β : Type} (F : List Bool → List Bool) (q : List Bool)
+    (rows : List (List Bool)) (f : List Bool → Option β) (payload : β → List Bool)
+    (hspec : ∀ row, F (pairEncode q row) =
+      match f row with | none => [] | some b => true :: payload b) :
+    firstTagged F (pairEncode q (BinArith.encWords rows)) =
+      match rows.findSome? f with | none => [] | some b => true :: payload b := by
+  rw [firstTagged_eq, pairFstD_pairEncode, pairSndD_pairEncode, BinArith.decWords_encWords]
+  dsimp only
+  cases hfind : rows.findSome? f with
+  | none =>
+    have hnone := List.findSome?_eq_none_iff.mp hfind
+    have hidx : EncodingMachines.firstIdx
+        (fun j => !decide (F (pairEncode q (rows.getD j [])) = [])) rows.length =
+          rows.length := by
+      apply EncodingMachines.firstIdx_eq_of_forall
+      intro i hi
+      rw [List.getD_eq_getElem _ [] hi, hspec, hnone _ (List.getElem_mem hi)]
+      rfl
+    rw [hidx, if_pos le_rfl]
+  | some b =>
+    obtain ⟨pre, row, post, rfl, hrow, hpre⟩ := List.findSome?_eq_some_iff.mp hfind
+    have hAt : (pre ++ row :: post).getD pre.length [] = row := by
+      rw [List.getD_append_right _ _ _ _ le_rfl, Nat.sub_self, List.getD_cons_zero]
+    have hidx : EncodingMachines.firstIdx
+        (fun j => !decide (F (pairEncode q ((pre ++ row :: post).getD j [])) = []))
+          (pre ++ row :: post).length = pre.length := by
+      apply EncodingMachines.firstIdx_eq_of
+      · simp only [List.length_append, List.length_cons]
+        omega
+      · rw [hAt, hspec, hrow]
+        rfl
+      · intro i hi
+        rw [List.getD_append _ _ _ _ hi, List.getD_eq_getElem _ [] hi,
+          hspec, hpre _ (List.getElem_mem hi)]
+        rfl
+    rw [hidx, if_neg (by simp only [List.length_append, List.length_cons]; omega),
+      hAt, hspec, hrow]
+
+end TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof

+/-
+Copyright (c) 2026 TCSlib contributors. All rights reserved.
+Released under Apache 2.0 license as described in the file LICENSE.
+-/
+import TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.Yao.HybridProof.RunWithHost
+import TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.Yao.HybridProof.EvalGate
+
+set_option maxHeartbeats 0
+set_option relaxedAutoImplicit false
+set_option autoImplicit false
+
+/-!
+# The generic polynomial-time garbled-evaluation host
+
+The retained input determines a unary key-length word, a decoded gate list and
+initial keys. Gate records expose their unary argument lists as second components.
+A polynomial-time table selector reads the table at the current vertex. The host
+then evaluates every gate by the generic nested-special evaluator.
+
+## Main definitions
+
+* `YaoHybridProof.garbledStep`: append one evaluated key to the encoded value list.
+
+## Main results
+
+* `YaoHybridProof.garbledRunWith_poly`: polynomial time for the complete encoded value list.
+
+## References
+
+* [PS10] R. Pass, a. shelat, *A Course in Cryptography*, lecture notes, 3rd ed.,
+  January 2010, Protocol 162.6 and §6.2.3.
+-/
+
+namespace TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof
+
+open Complexity Turing BoolCircuit
+
+/-- On the host word `⟨x, ⟨gateRecord, values⟩⟩`, append the key evaluated with
+parameter `N x`, argument list `snd gateRecord` and row table `tableWord w`.
+`garbledStep E₁ E₂ N tableWord w = evalGateAppendWord E₁ E₂ (pairEncode (N (pairFstD w)) (pairEncode
+(pairSndD (pairFstD (pairSndD w))) (pairEncode (pairSndD (pairSndD w)) (tableWord w))))`. -/
+noncomputable def garbledStep (E₁ E₂ : List Bool → List Bool)
+    (N tableWord : List Bool → List Bool) (w : List Bool) : List Bool :=
+  evalGateAppendWord E₁ E₂ (pairEncode (N (pairFstD w))
+    (pairEncode (pairSndD (pairFstD (pairSndD w)))
+      (pairEncode (pairSndD (pairSndD w)) (tableWord w))))
+
+/-- For every `w`, `garbledStep E₁ E₂ N tableWord w` calls `evalGateAppendWord`
+on its selected parameter, argument code, encoded values and selected table.
+`garbledStep E₁ E₂ N tableWord w = evalGateAppendWord E₁ E₂ (pairEncode (N (pairFstD w)) (pairEncode
+(pairSndD (pairFstD (pairSndD w))) (pairEncode (pairSndD (pairSndD w)) (tableWord w))))`. -/
+theorem garbledStep_eq (E₁ E₂ : List Bool → List Bool)
+    (N tableWord : List Bool → List Bool) (w : List Bool) :
+    garbledStep E₁ E₂ N tableWord w =
+      evalGateAppendWord E₁ E₂ (pairEncode (N (pairFstD w))
+        (pairEncode (pairSndD (pairFstD (pairSndD w)))
+          (pairEncode (pairSndD (pairSndD w)) (tableWord w)))) := rfl
+
+/-- If the evaluators, parameter selector and table selector are polynomial-time,
+then the generic garbled-evaluation step is polynomial-time.
+`PolyTimeComputable (garbledStep E₁ E₂ N tableWord)`. -/
+theorem garbledStep_poly {E₁ E₂ N tableWord : List Bool → List Bool}
+    (hE₁ : PolyTimeComputable E₁) (hE₂ : PolyTimeComputable E₂)
+    (hN : PolyTimeComputable N) (hT : PolyTimeComputable tableWord) :
+    PolyTimeComputable (garbledStep E₁ E₂ N tableWord) :=
+  (evalGateAppendWord_poly hE₁ hE₂).comp
+    ((hN.comp polyTimeComputable_pairFstD).pairEncode
+      ((polyTimeComputable_pairSndD.comp
+        (polyTimeComputable_pairFstD.comp polyTimeComputable_pairSndD)).pairEncode
+          ((polyTimeComputable_pairSndD.comp polyTimeComputable_pairSndD).pairEncode hT)))
+
+/-- The complete encoded garbled evaluation is polynomial-time when the parameter,
+gate records, initial keys and current-row table selector are polynomial-time.
+Gate records must expose their unary argument lists, and `tableWord` must select
+`T x |vs|` on every host word. The key width may depend on `x`.
+[PS10, Protocol 162.6]
+`PolyTimeComputable fun x => BinArith.encWords ((runWith (fun g vs => evalGate (nestedSpecial E₁ E₂
+(N x).length) (T x) vs.length g vs) (gs x) (init x)).map List.ofFn)`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** Instantiate the generic traversal with the nested-special gate
+step. Polynomial-time parameter, gate and initial-key maps give polynomial bounds
+on their output lengths. Thus the gate count and initial key count are polynomial.
+Each processed gate adds one key of exactly the parameter width, so the encoded
+value list has polynomial length throughout the run. The remaining gate records
+are a suffix and never grow. These bounds discharge the traversal's state envelope;
+the gate-step correctness theorem discharges its leaf contract. -/
+theorem garbledRunWith_poly {E₁ E₂ N tableWord : List Bool → List Bool}
+    (hE₁ : PolyTimeComputable E₁) (hE₂ : PolyTimeComputable E₂)
+    (hN : PolyTimeComputable N) (gateCode : DAGGate → List Bool)
+    (gs : List Bool → List DAGGate)
+    (init : ∀ x, List (Fin (N x).length → Bool))
+    (T : List Bool → ℕ → List (List Bool))
+    (hgs : PolyTimeComputable fun x => BinArith.encWords ((gs x).map gateCode))
+    (hinit : PolyTimeComputable fun x => BinArith.encWords ((init x).map List.ofFn))
+    (hT : PolyTimeComputable tableWord)
+    (hargs : ∀ g, pairSndD (gateCode g) =
+      BinArith.encWords (g.args.map fun a => List.replicate a true))
+    (htable : ∀ x g (vs : List (Fin (N x).length → Bool)),
+      tableWord (pairEncode x
+        (pairEncode (gateCode g) (BinArith.encWords (vs.map List.ofFn)))) =
+          BinArith.encWords (T x vs.length)) :
+    PolyTimeComputable fun x => BinArith.encWords
+      ((runWith (fun g vs => evalGate (nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ (N x).length) (T x) vs.length g vs)
+        (gs x) (init x)).map List.ofFn) := by
+  -- Extract envelopes from the three input-construction contracts.
+  obtain ⟨Cg, cg, hCg⟩ := hgs.output_length_le
+  obtain ⟨Ci, ci, hCi⟩ := hinit.output_length_le
+  obtain ⟨Cn, cn, hCn⟩ := hN.output_length_le
+  let G := PolyExpr.normal Cg cg
+  let I := PolyExpr.normal Ci ci
+  let P := PolyExpr.normal Cn cn
+  have hg x : (BinArith.encWords ((gs x).map gateCode)).length ≤ G.eval x.length := by
+    simpa only [G, PolyExpr.eval_normal] using hCg x
+  have hi x : (BinArith.encWords ((init x).map List.ofFn)).length ≤ I.eval x.length := by
+    simpa only [I, PolyExpr.eval_normal] using hCi x
+  have hn x : (N x).length ≤ P.eval x.length := by
+    simpa only [P, PolyExpr.eval_normal] using hCn x
+  have hgl x : (gs x).length ≤ G.eval x.length := by
+    have h := BinArith.length_decWords_le (BinArith.encWords ((gs x).map gateCode))
+    rw [BinArith.decWords_encWords, List.length_map] at h
+    exact h.trans (hg x)
+  have hil x : (init x).length ≤ I.eval x.length := by
+    have h := BinArith.length_decWords_le (BinArith.encWords ((init x).map List.ofFn))
+    rw [BinArith.decWords_encWords, List.length_map] at h
+    exact h.trans (hi x)
+  let B : PolyExpr := .add (.mul (.const 2) G)
+    (.add (.const 2) (.mul (.add I G) (.add (.mul (.const 2) P) (.const 2))))
+  refine RunWithHost.polyTimeComputable_runWith gateCode
+    (fun _ vs => BinArith.encWords (vs.map List.ofFn)) gs init
+    (fun x g vs => evalGate (nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ (N x).length) (T x) vs.length g vs)
+    hgs hinit (garbledStep_poly hE₁ hE₂ hN hT) ?_ G B hgl ?_
+  · intro x g vs
+    rw [garbledStep_eq]
+    simp only [pairFstD_pairEncode, pairSndD_pairEncode]
+    rw [hargs, htable]
+    exact evalGateAppendWord_encoded E₁ E₂ (N x) (T x) vs.length g vs
+  · intro x i _
+    -- Remaining gate records form a suffix of the original encoded list.
+    have hdrop := BinArith.length_iterate_pairSndD_le
+      (BinArith.encWords ((gs x).map gateCode)) i
+    rw [BinArith.iterate_pairSndD_encWords, ← List.map_drop] at hdrop
+    have hd := hdrop.trans (hg x)
+    -- Every vertex value is one fixed-width key; at most one is added per gate.
+    have hv := BinArith.length_encWords_le
+      ((runWith (fun g vs => evalGate (nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ (N x).length)
+        (T x) vs.length g vs) ((gs x).take i) (init x)).map List.ofFn)
+      (N x).length (fun w hw => by
+        obtain ⟨k, _, rfl⟩ := List.mem_map.mp hw
+        exact (List.length_ofFn).le)
+    rw [List.length_map, length_runWith, List.length_take] at hv
+    have hc : (init x).length + min i (gs x).length ≤ I.eval x.length + G.eval x.length :=
+      Nat.add_le_add (hil x) ((Nat.min_le_right _ _).trans (hgl x))
+    have hp : 2 * (N x).length + 2 ≤ 2 * P.eval x.length + 2 := by
+      have := hn x
+      omega
+    have hval := hv.trans (Nat.mul_le_mul hc hp)
+    rw [length_pairEncode]
+    simp only [B, PolyExpr.eval_add, PolyExpr.eval_mul, PolyExpr.eval_const]
+    omega
+
+end TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof

+import TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.GarblingSpecial
+import TCSlib.Complexity.ClassP.BinaryArith.Words
+import TCSlib.Complexity.CircuitComplexity.Uniform

+set_option maxHeartbeats 0
+set_option relaxedAutoImplicit false
+set_option autoImplicit false

+/-!
+# Polynomial-time leaves for circuit garbling
+
+The encryption adapter operates on word lists and arbitrary input words. Its contract
+identifies it with the frozen nested-encryption interface on zero, one, or two vector keys.
+No circuit-family or seed definition from `Yao.lean` occurs here.
+
+## Main definitions
+
+* `Garbling.nestedWordEnc`: nested special encryption on encoded word lists.
+
+## Main results
+
+* `Garbling.spEncStr_poly`: special encryption of polynomial-time supplied words.
+* `Garbling.nestedWordEnc_poly`: the nested adapter is polynomial-time.
+* `Garbling.nestedWordEnc_vectors`: the adapter agrees with `nestedSpecial.enc`.
+
+## References
+
+* [PS10] R. Pass, a. shelat, *A Course in Cryptography*, lecture notes, 3rd ed., January
+  2010, Algorithm 157.2 and Protocol 162.6 (nested encryption of garbled rows).
+-/
+
+namespace TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof.Garbling
+
+open Complexity Turing BoolCircuit
+
+/-- If `E`, `k`, `m`, and `r` are polynomial-time word maps, so is
+`z ↦ spEncStr E (k z) (m z) (r z)`. [PS10, Algorithm 157.2]
+`PolyTimeComputable fun z => spEncStr E (k z) (m z) (r z)`. -/
+theorem spEncStr_poly {E k m r : List Bool → List Bool} (hE : PolyTimeComputable E)
+    (hk : PolyTimeComputable k) (hm : PolyTimeComputable m) (hr : PolyTimeComputable r) :
+    PolyTimeComputable fun z => spEncStr E (k z) (m z) (r z) := by
+  have h := (polyTimeComputable_spEncFun hE).comp
+    (NUPPT.polyTimeComputable_layout (polyTimeComputable_const []) (hk.pairEncode hm)
+      (polyTimeComputable_const []) hr)
+  convert h using 1
+  funext z
+  simp only [Function.comp_apply]
+  rw [spEncFun_eq, NUPPT.inArg_layout, NUPPT.inCoins_layout, pairFstD_pairEncode,
+    pairSndD_pairEncode]
+
+/-- On `⟨keys, ⟨m, ⟨r₁, r₂⟩⟩⟩`, return `m` for no key, encrypt under the first key
+with `r₁` for one key, or encrypt under the second key with `r₂` and then under the first
+with `r₁` for two keys. Longer encoded lists return `[]`. [PS10, Protocol 162.6]
+`nestedWordEnc E₁ E₂ z = if pairFstD z = [] then pairFstD (pairSndD z) else if pairSndD (pairFstD z)
+= [] then spEncStr E₁ (pairFstD (pairFstD z)) (pairFstD (pairSndD z)) (pairFstD (pairSndD (pairSndD
+z))) else if pairSndD (pairSndD (pairFstD z)) = [] then spEncStr E₂ (pairFstD (pairFstD z))
+(spEncStr E₁ (pairFstD (pairSndD (pairFstD z))) (pairFstD (pairSndD z)) (pairSndD (pairSndD
+(pairSndD z)))) (pairFstD (pairSndD (pairSndD z))) else []`. -/
+def nestedWordEnc (E₁ E₂ : List Bool → List Bool) (z : List Bool) : List Bool :=
+  if pairFstD z = [] then pairFstD (pairSndD z)
+  else if pairSndD (pairFstD z) = [] then
+    spEncStr E₁ (pairFstD (pairFstD z)) (pairFstD (pairSndD z))
+      (pairFstD (pairSndD (pairSndD z)))
+  else if pairSndD (pairSndD (pairFstD z)) = [] then
+    spEncStr E₂ (pairFstD (pairFstD z))
+      (spEncStr E₁ (pairFstD (pairSndD (pairFstD z))) (pairFstD (pairSndD z))
+        (pairSndD (pairSndD (pairSndD z)))) (pairFstD (pairSndD (pairSndD z)))
+  else []
+
+/-- `nestedWordEnc E₁ E₂ z` branches on zero, one, or two encoded keys and performs
+zero, one, or two applications of `spEncStr`, respectively.
+`nestedWordEnc E₁ E₂ z = if pairFstD z = [] then pairFstD (pairSndD z) else if pairSndD (pairFstD z)
+= [] then spEncStr E₁ (pairFstD (pairFstD z)) (pairFstD (pairSndD z)) (pairFstD (pairSndD (pairSndD
+z))) else if pairSndD (pairSndD (pairFstD z)) = [] then spEncStr E₂ (pairFstD (pairFstD z))
+(spEncStr E₁ (pairFstD (pairSndD (pairFstD z))) (pairFstD (pairSndD z)) (pairSndD (pairSndD
+(pairSndD z)))) (pairFstD (pairSndD (pairSndD z))) else []`. -/
+theorem nestedWordEnc_eq (E₁ E₂ : List Bool → List Bool) (z : List Bool) :
+    nestedWordEnc E₁ E₂ z =
+      if pairFstD z = [] then pairFstD (pairSndD z)
+      else if pairSndD (pairFstD z) = [] then
+        spEncStr E₁ (pairFstD (pairFstD z)) (pairFstD (pairSndD z))
+          (pairFstD (pairSndD (pairSndD z)))
+      else if pairSndD (pairSndD (pairFstD z)) = [] then
+        spEncStr E₂ (pairFstD (pairFstD z))
+          (spEncStr E₁ (pairFstD (pairSndD (pairFstD z))) (pairFstD (pairSndD z))
+            (pairSndD (pairSndD (pairSndD z)))) (pairFstD (pairSndD (pairSndD z)))
+      else [] := rfl
+
+/-- If `E₁` and `E₂` are polynomial-time, the total nested adapter is polynomial-time:
+`PolyTimeComputable (nestedWordEnc E₁ E₂)`. [PS10, Protocol 162.6]
+
+**Proof sketch.** Read the key list, message, and two nonce words by pair projections.
+Special encryption is polynomial-time for any supplied word lengths. The zero-, one-,
+and two-key cases are selected by three polynomial-time emptiness tests; malformed input
+words are covered by the same total projections and tests. -/
+theorem nestedWordEnc_poly {E₁ E₂ : List Bool → List Bool}
+    (hE₁ : PolyTimeComputable E₁) (hE₂ : PolyTimeComputable E₂) :
+    PolyTimeComputable (nestedWordEnc E₁ E₂) := by
+  -- The leaf arguments are total polynomial-time pair projections.
+  have hk₁ := polyTimeComputable_pairFstD.comp polyTimeComputable_pairFstD
+  have hk₂ := polyTimeComputable_pairFstD.comp
+    (polyTimeComputable_pairSndD.comp polyTimeComputable_pairFstD)
+  have hm := polyTimeComputable_pairFstD.comp polyTimeComputable_pairSndD
+  have hr₁ := polyTimeComputable_pairFstD.comp
+    (polyTimeComputable_pairSndD.comp polyTimeComputable_pairSndD)
+  have hr₂ := polyTimeComputable_pairSndD.comp
+    (polyTimeComputable_pairSndD.comp polyTimeComputable_pairSndD)
+  -- Three emptiness tests distinguish the supported encoded list lengths.
+  convert polyTimeComputable_ite
+    (polyTimeComputable_isNil.comp polyTimeComputable_pairFstD) hm
+    (polyTimeComputable_ite
+      (polyTimeComputable_isNil.comp
+        (polyTimeComputable_pairSndD.comp polyTimeComputable_pairFstD))
+      (spEncStr_poly hE₁ hk₁ hm hr₁)
+      (polyTimeComputable_ite
+        (polyTimeComputable_isNil.comp (polyTimeComputable_pairSndD.comp
+          (polyTimeComputable_pairSndD.comp polyTimeComputable_pairFstD)))
+        (spEncStr_poly hE₂ hk₁ (spEncStr_poly hE₁ hk₂ hm hr₂) hr₁)
+        (polyTimeComputable_const []))) using 1
+  funext z
+  simp only [nestedWordEnc_eq, decide_eq_true_eq, Function.comp_apply]
+
+/-- On at most two vector keys, the word adapter agrees with nested special encryption:
+`nestedWordEnc E₁ E₂ ⟨encWords (ks.map ofFn), ⟨ofFn o, ⟨ofFn r.1, ofFn r.2⟩⟩⟩ =
+ (nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ n).enc ks o r`. [PS10, Protocol 162.6]
+
+**Proof sketch.** Split by the number of keys. Encoded lists are nested pairs, so the
+emptiness tests select their length correctly, even if an individual key is empty.
+The public `nestedSpecial_enc` reader then gives exactly the corresponding encryption
+expression, including which independent nonce belongs to each encryption layer. -/
+theorem nestedWordEnc_vectors {n : ℕ} (E₁ E₂ : List Bool → List Bool)
+    (ks : List (Fin n → Bool)) (hks : ks.length ≤ 2) (o : Fin n → Bool)
+    (r : (Fin n → Bool) × (Fin n → Bool)) :
+    nestedWordEnc E₁ E₂
+        (pairEncode (BinArith.encWords (ks.map List.ofFn))
+          (pairEncode (List.ofFn o) (pairEncode (List.ofFn r.1) (List.ofFn r.2)))) =
+      (nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ n).enc ks o r := by
+  -- Pair encodings are nonempty even when a vector has width zero.
+  match ks, hks with
+  | [], _ =>
+    simp only [nestedWordEnc_eq, List.map_nil, BinArith.encWords_nil,
+      pairFstD_pairEncode, pairSndD_pairEncode, if_true, nestedSpecial_enc]
+  | [k], _ =>
+    simp only [nestedWordEnc_eq, List.map_cons, List.map_nil, BinArith.encWords_cons,
+      BinArith.encWords_nil, pairFstD_pairEncode, pairSndD_pairEncode,
+      pairEncode_ne_nil, if_false, if_true, nestedSpecial_enc]
+  | [k₁, k₂], _ =>
+    simp only [nestedWordEnc_eq, List.map_cons, List.map_nil, BinArith.encWords_cons,
+      BinArith.encWords_nil, pairFstD_pairEncode, pairSndD_pairEncode,
+      pairEncode_ne_nil, if_false, if_true, nestedSpecial_enc]
+  | _ :: _ :: _ :: _, h =>
+    simp only [List.length_cons] at h
+    omega
+
+
+/-- A garbled row is polynomial-time when its encoded argument keys, output key, and both
+nonce words are polynomial-time, and its gate has at most two arguments.
+[PS10, Protocol 162.6], encryption of one row from supplied seed data.
+`PolyTimeComputable fun z => garbledRow (nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ (width z)) (C z) (s z) (i z) (a z)`. -/
+theorem garbledRow_poly {E₁ E₂ : List Bool → List Bool}
+    (hE₁ : PolyTimeComputable E₁) (hE₂ : PolyTimeComputable E₂)
+    (inputs width : List Bool → ℕ) (C : ∀ z, DAGCircuit (inputs z))
+    (s : ∀ z, GarbleSeed (C z) (Fin (width z) → Bool)
+      ((Fin (width z) → Bool) × (Fin (width z) → Bool)))
+    (i : ∀ z, Fin (C z).gates.length)
+    (a : ∀ z, Fin ((C z).gates[i z]).args.length → Bool)
+    (hfan : ∀ z, ((C z).gates[i z]).args.length ≤ 2)
+    (hkeys : PolyTimeComputable fun z =>
+      BinArith.encWords ((argKeys (C z) (s z) (i z) (a z)).map List.ofFn))
+    (hout : PolyTimeComputable fun z =>
+      List.ofFn (keyAt (C z) (s z) (inputs z + i z)
+        (gateOut ((C z).gates[i z]) (a z))))
+    (hr₁ : PolyTimeComputable fun z => List.ofFn (((s z).2.1 (i z) (a z)).1))
+    (hr₂ : PolyTimeComputable fun z => List.ofFn (((s z).2.1 (i z) (a z)).2)) :
+    PolyTimeComputable fun z =>
+      garbledRow (nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ (width z)) (C z) (s z) (i z) (a z) := by
+  have h := (nestedWordEnc_poly hE₁ hE₂).comp
+    (hkeys.pairEncode (hout.pairEncode (hr₁.pairEncode hr₂)))
+  convert h using 1
+  funext z
+  rw [garbledRow_eq]
+  symm
+  apply nestedWordEnc_vectors
+  rw [argKeys_eq, List.length_ofFn]
+  exact hfan z
+
+
+/-- Encoding a fixed finite list of polynomial-time word maps is polynomial-time:
+`z ↦ encWords (enum.map (fun a => row a z))`. In particular, the enumeration can be any
+fixed list of assignments to zero, one, or two argument positions. [PS10, Protocol 162.6] -/
+theorem fixedTable_poly {α : Type} (enum : List α) (row : α → List Bool → List Bool)
+    (hrow : ∀ a, PolyTimeComputable (row a)) :
+    PolyTimeComputable fun z => BinArith.encWords (enum.map fun a => row a z) := by
+  induction enum with
+  | nil =>
+    simpa only [List.map_nil, BinArith.encWords_nil] using polyTimeComputable_const []
+  | cons a enum ih =>
+    simpa only [List.map_cons, BinArith.encWords_cons] using (hrow a).pairEncode ih
+
+
+/-- For at most two arguments, `gateOut g a` is the conjunction, disjunction, or negated
+conjunction of the first zero, one, or two assignment bits, according to `g.kind`.
+Empty conjunction and disjunction are `true` and `false`. [PS10, Protocol 162.6]
+`gateOut g a = if g.kind = .and then if g.args.length = 0 then true else if g.args.length = 1 then
+(List.ofFn a).getD 0 false else (List.ofFn a).getD 0 false && (List.ofFn a).getD 1 false else if
+g.kind = .or then if g.args.length = 0 then false else if g.args.length = 1 then (List.ofFn a).getD
+0 false else (List.ofFn a).getD 0 false || (List.ofFn a).getD 1 false else !(if g.args.length = 0
+then true else if g.args.length = 1 then (List.ofFn a).getD 0 false else (List.ofFn a).getD 0 false
+&& (List.ofFn a).getD 1 false)`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** Split the argument list into zero, one, two, or at least three entries.
+For each gate kind in the first three cases, the public evaluation equations give the
+displayed Boolean expression. The fan-in bound excludes the final case. -/
+theorem gateOut_small (g : DAGGate) (a : Fin g.args.length → Bool)
+    (hfan : g.args.length ≤ 2) :
+    gateOut g a =
+      if g.kind = .and then
+        if g.args.length = 0 then true else if g.args.length = 1 then
+          (List.ofFn a).getD 0 false
+        else (List.ofFn a).getD 0 false && (List.ofFn a).getD 1 false
+      else if g.kind = .or then
+        if g.args.length = 0 then false else if g.args.length = 1 then
+          (List.ofFn a).getD 0 false
+        else (List.ofFn a).getD 0 false || (List.ofFn a).getD 1 false
+      else !(if g.args.length = 0 then true else if g.args.length = 1 then
+          (List.ofFn a).getD 0 false
+        else (List.ofFn a).getD 0 false && (List.ofFn a).getD 1 false) := by
+  cases g with
+  | mk k args =>
+    cases args with
+    | nil => cases k <;>
+          simp [gateOut_eq_eval, DAGGate.eval_eq]
+    | cons u args =>
+      cases args with
+      | nil => cases k <;>
+          simp [gateOut_eq_eval, DAGGate.eval_eq, List.range_succ]
+      | cons v args =>
+        cases args with
+        | nil => cases k <;>
+          simp [gateOut_eq_eval, DAGGate.eval_eq, List.range_succ]
+        | cons w args => simp only [List.length_cons] at hfan; omega
+
+
+/-- A gate's output assignment bit is polynomial-time when the two kind tests, the
+zero and one argument tests, and the first two assignment-bit maps are polynomial-time,
+provided every gate has at most two arguments. [PS10, Protocol 162.6]
+`PolyTimeComputable fun z => [gateOut (g z) (a z)]`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** Build the conjunction and disjunction of the at most two supplied bits,
+with their empty and one-bit cases. Two kind tests choose conjunction, disjunction, or
+negated conjunction. The bounded-arity equation `gateOut_small` identifies this finite
+Boolean calculation with the public gate-output interface. -/
+theorem gateOut_poly (g : List Bool → DAGGate)
+    (a : ∀ z, Fin (g z).args.length → Bool)
+    (hfan : ∀ z, (g z).args.length ≤ 2)
+    (hkAnd : PolyTimeComputable fun z => [decide ((g z).kind = .and)])
+    (hkOr : PolyTimeComputable fun z => [decide ((g z).kind = .or)])
+    (hzero : PolyTimeComputable fun z => [decide ((g z).args.length = 0)])
+    (hone : PolyTimeComputable fun z => [decide ((g z).args.length = 1)])
+    (h₀ : PolyTimeComputable fun z => [(List.ofFn (a z)).getD 0 false])
+    (h₁ : PolyTimeComputable fun z => [(List.ofFn (a z)).getD 1 false]) :
+    PolyTimeComputable fun z => [gateOut (g z) (a z)] := by
+  -- Compute each kind's output from at most two assignment bits.
+  have hAnd : PolyTimeComputable fun z =>
+      [if (g z).args.length = 0 then true else if (g z).args.length = 1 then
+        (List.ofFn (a z)).getD 0 false
+      else (List.ofFn (a z)).getD 0 false && (List.ofFn (a z)).getD 1 false] := by
+    convert polyTimeComputable_ite hzero (polyTimeComputable_const [true])
+      (polyTimeComputable_ite hone h₀ (polyTimeComputable_and h₀ h₁)) using 1
+    funext z
+    simp only [decide_eq_true_eq]
+    split_ifs <;> rfl
+  have hOr : PolyTimeComputable fun z =>
+      [if (g z).args.length = 0 then false else if (g z).args.length = 1 then
+        (List.ofFn (a z)).getD 0 false
+      else (List.ofFn (a z)).getD 0 false || (List.ofFn (a z)).getD 1 false] := by
+    convert polyTimeComputable_ite hzero (polyTimeComputable_const [false])
+      (polyTimeComputable_ite hone h₀ (polyTimeComputable_or h₀ h₁)) using 1
+    funext z
+    simp only [decide_eq_true_eq]
+    split_ifs <;> rfl
+  have h := polyTimeComputable_ite hkAnd hAnd
+    (polyTimeComputable_ite hkOr hOr (polyTimeComputable_not hAnd))
+  -- The formula uses only the public gate-output equation.
+  refine h.congr fun z => ?_
+  simp only [decide_eq_true_eq]
+  rw [gateOut_small (g z) (a z) (hfan z)]
+  split_ifs <;> rfl
+
+
+/-- A gate-kind equality test is polynomial-time when its kind code is:
+`z ↦ [decide (k z = tag)]`. This applies to all three constructor tags. -/
+theorem gateKind_test_poly (k : List Bool → GateKind)
+    (hcode : PolyTimeComputable fun z => (k z).encode) (tag : GateKind) :
+    PolyTimeComputable fun z => [decide (k z = tag)] := by
+  have h := polyTimeComputable_pairEq.comp (hcode.pairEncode
+    (polyTimeComputable_const tag.encode))
+  refine h.congr fun z => ?_
+  simp only [Function.comp_apply, pairFstD_pairEncode, pairSndD_pairEncode]
+  cases k z <;> cases tag <;>
+    simp [GateKind.encode_and, GateKind.encode_or, GateKind.encode_not]
+
+
+/-- A selector over at most two argument positions has the corresponding short word list.
+
+**Proof sketch.** Split the argument list into its first three possible lengths and use
+the public word-list encoder equations. The position enumeration is a fixed finite list
+in each case. -/
+private theorem keyList_small (args : List ℕ) (a : Fin args.length → Bool)
+    (K : ℕ → Bool → List Bool) (hfan : args.length ≤ 2) :
+    BinArith.encWords (List.ofFn fun t => K args[t] (a t)) =
+      if args.length = 0 then []
+      else if args.length = 1 then
+        pairEncode (K (args.getD 0 0) ((List.ofFn a).getD 0 false)) []
+      else
+        pairEncode (K (args.getD 0 0) ((List.ofFn a).getD 0 false))
+          (pairEncode (K (args.getD 1 0) ((List.ofFn a).getD 1 false)) []) := by
+  cases args with
+  | nil =>
+    simp only [List.ofFn_zero, BinArith.encWords_nil]
+    rfl
+  | cons u args =>
+    cases args with
+    | nil =>
+      simp only [List.ofFn_succ, List.ofFn_zero, BinArith.encWords_cons,
+        BinArith.encWords_nil]
+      rfl
+    | cons v args =>
+      cases args with
+      | nil =>
+        simp only [List.ofFn_succ, List.ofFn_zero, BinArith.encWords_cons,
+          BinArith.encWords_nil]
+        rfl
+      | cons w args => simp only [List.length_cons] at hfan; omega
+
+/-- For a gate with at most two arguments, its encoded argument keys are the zero-, one-,
+or two-entry word list selected by its argument count. The two possible entries read
+argument positions zero and one with defaults. [PS10, Protocol 162.6]
+`BinArith.encWords ((argKeys C s i a).map List.ofFn) = if (C.gates[i]).args.length = 0 then [] else
+if (C.gates[i]).args.length = 1 then pairEncode (List.ofFn (keyAt C s ((C.gates[i]).args.getD 0 0)
+((List.ofFn a).getD 0 false))) [] else pairEncode (List.ofFn (keyAt C s ((C.gates[i]).args.getD 0 0)
+((List.ofFn a).getD 0 false))) (pairEncode (List.ofFn (keyAt C s ((C.gates[i]).args.getD 1 0)
+((List.ofFn a).getD 1 false))) [])`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** Use the published argument-key equation. Split the argument list into
+its zero-, one-, and two-element cases; in each, the fixed-length `ofFn` reads precisely
+the stated positions. Longer lists contradict the fan-in hypothesis. -/
+theorem argKeys_small {m n : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit m)
+    (s : GarbleSeed C (Fin n → Bool) ((Fin n → Bool) × (Fin n → Bool)))
+    (i : Fin C.gates.length) (a : Fin (C.gates[i]).args.length → Bool)
+    (hfan : (C.gates[i]).args.length ≤ 2) :
+    BinArith.encWords ((argKeys C s i a).map List.ofFn) =
+      if (C.gates[i]).args.length = 0 then []
+      else if (C.gates[i]).args.length = 1 then
+        pairEncode (List.ofFn (keyAt C s ((C.gates[i]).args.getD 0 0)
+          ((List.ofFn a).getD 0 false))) []
+      else
+        pairEncode (List.ofFn (keyAt C s ((C.gates[i]).args.getD 0 0)
+          ((List.ofFn a).getD 0 false)))
+          (pairEncode (List.ofFn (keyAt C s ((C.gates[i]).args.getD 1 0)
+            ((List.ofFn a).getD 1 false))) []) := by
+  rw [argKeys_eq]
+  simpa only [List.map_ofFn] using
+    keyList_small (C.gates[i]).args a (fun v b => List.ofFn (keyAt C s v b)) hfan
+
+
+/-- The encoded argument-key list is polynomial-time if its zero and one argument tests
+and the two potential key words are polynomial-time, for fan-in at most two.
+[PS10, Protocol 162.6], collecting the keys which encrypt one garbled row.
+`PolyTimeComputable fun z => BinArith.encWords ((argKeys (C z) (s z) (i z) (a z)).map
+List.ofFn)`. -/
+theorem argKeys_poly (inputs width : List Bool → ℕ) (C : ∀ z, DAGCircuit (inputs z))
+    (s : ∀ z, GarbleSeed (C z) (Fin (width z) → Bool)
+      ((Fin (width z) → Bool) × (Fin (width z) → Bool)))
+    (i : ∀ z, Fin (C z).gates.length)
+    (a : ∀ z, Fin ((C z).gates[i z]).args.length → Bool)
+    (hfan : ∀ z, ((C z).gates[i z]).args.length ≤ 2)
+    (hzero : PolyTimeComputable fun z => [decide (((C z).gates[i z]).args.length = 0)])
+    (hone : PolyTimeComputable fun z => [decide (((C z).gates[i z]).args.length = 1)])
+    (h₀ : PolyTimeComputable fun z =>
+      List.ofFn (keyAt (C z) (s z) (((C z).gates[i z]).args.getD 0 0)
+        ((List.ofFn (a z)).getD 0 false)))
+    (h₁ : PolyTimeComputable fun z =>
+      List.ofFn (keyAt (C z) (s z) (((C z).gates[i z]).args.getD 1 0)
+        ((List.ofFn (a z)).getD 1 false))) :
+    PolyTimeComputable fun z =>
+      BinArith.encWords ((argKeys (C z) (s z) (i z) (a z)).map List.ofFn) := by
+  have h := polyTimeComputable_ite hzero (polyTimeComputable_const [])
+    (polyTimeComputable_ite hone (h₀.pairEncode (polyTimeComputable_const []))
+      (h₀.pairEncode (h₁.pairEncode (polyTimeComputable_const []))))
+  refine h.congr fun z => ?_
+  simp only [decide_eq_true_eq]
+  exact (argKeys_small (C z) (s z) (i z) (a z) (hfan z)).symm
+
+
+/-- A table with zero, one, or two argument positions is polynomial-time for any constant
+assignment enumerations `enum l`, if every corresponding row map is polynomial-time and
+the zero and one arity tests are polynomial-time. [PS10, Protocol 162.6]
+`PolyTimeComputable fun z => BinArith.encWords ((enum (arity z)).map fun a => row (arity z) a z)`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** For each of the three arities, assemble the finite encoded row list with
+`fixedTable_poly`. Two polynomial-time tests select its arity. The fan-in bound ensures
+that the final branch has arity two, so no algorithm for computing the enumeration is
+required, including when the chosen constant lists come from `Finset.univ.toList`. -/
+theorem smallArityTable_poly (arity : List Bool → ℕ)
+    (enum : ∀ l : ℕ, List (Fin l → Bool))
+    (row : ∀ l : ℕ, (Fin l → Bool) → List Bool → List Bool)
+    (hbound : ∀ z, arity z ≤ 2)
+    (hzero : PolyTimeComputable fun z => [decide (arity z = 0)])
+    (hone : PolyTimeComputable fun z => [decide (arity z = 1)])
+    (hrow : ∀ l, l ≤ 2 → ∀ a, PolyTimeComputable (row l a)) :
+    PolyTimeComputable fun z =>
+      BinArith.encWords ((enum (arity z)).map fun a => row (arity z) a z) := by
+  have h₀ := fixedTable_poly (enum 0) (row 0) (hrow 0 (by omega))
+  have h₁ := fixedTable_poly (enum 1) (row 1) (hrow 1 (by omega))
+  have h₂ := fixedTable_poly (enum 2) (row 2) (hrow 2 (by omega))
+  have h := polyTimeComputable_ite hzero h₀ (polyTimeComputable_ite hone h₁ h₂)
+  refine h.congr fun z => ?_
+  simp only [decide_eq_true_eq]
+  generalize hk : arity z = k
+  have hkbound : k ≤ 2 := by have := hbound z; omega
+  have hz : k = 0 ∨ k = 1 ∨ k = 2 := by omega
+  rcases hz with rfl | rfl | rfl <;> simp
+
+end TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof.Garbling

+/-
+Copyright (c) 2026 TCSlib contributors. All rights reserved.
+Released under Apache 2.0 license as described in the file LICENSE.
