+import TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.Yao.HybridProof.AllTables
+import TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.Yao.HybridProof.DescriptionDecoder
+
+set_option maxHeartbeats 0
+set_option relaxedAutoImplicit false
+set_option autoImplicit false
+
+/-!
+# Generic garbling programs from uniform circuit descriptions
+
+The interface takes a circuit family, a polynomial-time description map, numeric/coin maps,
+and a seed satisfying its key, row-nonce, and XOR-shift block equations. It does not refer
+to any definition from `Yao.lean`.
+
+## Main definitions
+
+* `GarblingProgram.gatesWord`: the gate-record word extracted by the public decoder.
+
+## Main results
+
+* `GarblingProgram.tables_poly`: polynomial time for the actual encoded garbled tables.
+
+## References
+
+* [PS10] R. Pass, a. shelat, *A Course in Cryptography*, lecture notes, 3rd ed., January
+  2010, Protocol 162.6 (garbling the canonical uniform circuit).
+-/
+
+namespace TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof.GarblingProgram
+
+open Complexity Turing BoolCircuit TCSlib.Cryptography.EncodingMachines
+
+/-- The gate-record list extracted from a circuit-description pair:
+`gatesWord description z = pairSndD (readCircuit (pairFstD (description z)))`. -/
+def gatesWord (description : List Bool → List Bool) (z : List Bool) : List Bool :=
+  pairSndD (DescriptionDecoder.readCircuit (pairFstD (description z)))
+
+/-- `gatesWord description z = pairSndD (readCircuit (pairFstD (description z)))`. -/
+theorem gatesWord_eq (description : List Bool → List Bool) (z : List Bool) :
+    gatesWord description z =
+      pairSndD (DescriptionDecoder.readCircuit (pairFstD (description z))) := rfl
+
+/-- Extracting the gate-record word is polynomial-time from a polynomial-time description.
+`PolyTimeComputable (gatesWord description)`. -/
+theorem gatesWord_poly {description : List Bool → List Bool}
+    (hdescription : PolyTimeComputable description) : PolyTimeComputable (gatesWord description) :=
+  polyTimeComputable_pairSndD.comp
+    (DescriptionDecoder.readCircuit_poly.comp (polyTimeComputable_pairFstD.comp hdescription))
+
+/-- A canonical description gives exactly its encoded gate-record list:
+`gatesWord description z = encWords (C.gates.map gateRecord)`. -/
+theorem gatesWord_description (description : List Bool → List Bool) (z : List Bool)
+    {m : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit m) (outs : List Bool)
+    (hdescription : description z = pairEncode C.encode outs) :
+    gatesWord description z = BinArith.encWords (C.gates.map DescriptionDecoder.gateRecord) := by
+  rw [gatesWord_eq, hdescription, pairFstD_pairEncode,
+    DescriptionDecoder.readCircuit_encode, pairSndD_pairEncode]
+
+/-- The actual encoded garbled-table vector is polynomial-time from a polynomial-time
+circuit-description pair, polynomial-time unary/coin maps, fan-in at most two, and the
+supplied seed's key, row-nonce, and XOR-shift equations. [PS10, Protocol 162.6; BMR90]
+`PolyTimeComputable fun z => BinArith.encWords (List.ofFn fun i => BinArith.encWords (garbledTable
+(nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ (width z)) (C z) (s z) i))`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** Decode the description to its gate-record word and apply the total raw
+all-table machine. At a canonical index, the public context-transport equation exposes the
+selected gate record. Canonical list lookup supplies its kind and unary argument words.
+The row-machine seed contract identifies every encrypted row, the table contract identifies
+the enumerated table, and the outer word-vector contract identifies the complete table list.
+No unproved indexed reconstruction hypothesis is left to the consumer. -/
+theorem tables_poly {E₁ E₂ : List Bool → List Bool} (hE₁ : PolyTimeComputable E₁)
+    (hE₂ : PolyTimeComputable E₂) (width bound inputs : List Bool → ℕ)
+    (description outs coins : List Bool → List Bool)
+    (hwidth : PolyTimeComputable fun z => List.replicate (width z) true)
+    (hbound : PolyTimeComputable fun z => List.replicate (bound z) true)
+    (hinputs : PolyTimeComputable fun z => List.replicate (inputs z) true)
+    (hdescription : PolyTimeComputable description) (hcoins : PolyTimeComputable coins)
+    (C : ∀ z, DAGCircuit (inputs z))
+    (s : ∀ z, GarbleSeed (C z) (Fin (width z) → Bool)
+      ((Fin (width z) → Bool) × (Fin (width z) → Bool)))
+    (hshape : ∀ z, description z = pairEncode (C z).encode (outs z))
+    (hfan : ∀ z (i : Fin (C z).gates.length), ((C z).gates[i]).args.length ≤ 2)
+    (hkey : ∀ z (v : ℕ), v < (C z).size → ∀ b,
+      keyAt (C z) (s z) v b = fun t : Fin (width z) =>
+        (coins z).getD ((b.toNat * bound z + v) * width z + t) false)
+    (hr₀ : ∀ z (i : Fin (C z).gates.length) a t, ((s z).2.1 i a).1 t = (coins z).getD
+      (2 * bound z * width z +
+        (4 * i + (List.ofFn a).foldr (fun b k => b.toNat + 2 * k) 0) *
+          (2 * width z) + t) false)
+    (hr₁ : ∀ z (i : Fin (C z).gates.length) a t, ((s z).2.1 i a).2 t = (coins z).getD
+      (2 * bound z * width z +
+        (4 * i + (List.ofFn a).foldr (fun b k => b.toNat + 2 * k) 0) *
+          (2 * width z) + width z + t) false)
+    (hperm : ∀ z (i : Fin (C z).gates.length) a t, (s z).2.2 i a t = xor (a t)
+      ((coins z).getD (10 * bound z * width z + 2 * i + t) false)) :
+    PolyTimeComputable fun z => BinArith.encWords (List.ofFn fun i =>
+      BinArith.encWords (garbledTable (nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ (width z)) (C z) (s z) i)) := by
+  let gates := gatesWord description
+  have hgates : PolyTimeComputable gates := gatesWord_poly hdescription
+  have hgs (z : List Bool) :
+      gates z = BinArith.encWords ((C z).gates.map DescriptionDecoder.gateRecord) :=
+    gatesWord_description description z (C z) (outs z) (hshape z)
+  have hraw := AllTables.allTablesWord_poly hE₁ hE₂ hwidth hbound hinputs hgates hcoins
+  refine hraw.congr fun z => ?_
+  refine AllTables.allTablesWord_garbledTables E₁ E₂ width bound inputs gates coins
+    DescriptionDecoder.gateRecord z (C z) (s z) (hgs z) ?_
+  intro i
+  -- Transport the indexed context before reading the selected canonical gate record.
+  rw [AllTables.entryWord_pair]
+  have hrecord : BinArith.getW (pairEncode (List.replicate i true) (gates z)) =
+      DescriptionDecoder.gateRecord ((C z).gates[i]) := by
+    rw [hgs z, BinArith.getW_pairEncode, List.length_replicate]
+    rw [List.getD_eq_getElem _ _ (by rw [List.length_map]; exact i.isLt), List.getElem_map]
+    rfl
+  have hk : pairFstD (BinArith.getW (pairEncode (List.replicate i true) (gates z))) =
+      ((C z).gates[i]).kind.encode := by
+    rw [hrecord, DescriptionDecoder.gateRecord_eq, pairFstD_pairEncode]
+  have ha : pairSndD (BinArith.getW (pairEncode (List.replicate i true) (gates z))) =
+      BinArith.encWords (((C z).gates[i]).args.map fun v => List.replicate v true) := by
+    rw [hrecord, DescriptionDecoder.gateRecord_eq, pairSndD_pairEncode]
+  -- The seed equations supply the entire row-to-table semantic contract.
+  refine TablesFromCoins.tableWord_garbledTable E₁ E₂ width bound inputs (fun _ => i)
+    (fun v => pairFstD (BinArith.getW (pairEncode (List.replicate i true) (gates v))))
+    (fun v => pairSndD (BinArith.getW (pairEncode (List.replicate i true) (gates v))))
+    coins z (C z) (s z) i (hfan z i) ha ?_
+  intro a
+  exact RowFromCoins.rowWord_garbledRow E₁ E₂ width bound inputs (fun _ => i)
+    (fun v => pairFstD (BinArith.getW (pairEncode (List.replicate i true) (gates v))))
+    (fun v => pairSndD (BinArith.getW (pairEncode (List.replicate i true) (gates v))))
+    coins z (C z) (s z) i a (hfan z i) rfl hk ha (hkey z) (hr₀ z i) (hr₁ z i) (hperm z i)
+
+end TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof.GarblingProgram

+/-
+Copyright (c) 2026 TCSlib contributors. All rights reserved.
+Released under Apache 2.0 license as described in the file LICENSE.
+-/
+import TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.Yao.HybridProof.GateStepCases
+import TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.Yao.HybridProof.SliceReduction
+
+set_option maxHeartbeats 0
+set_option relaxedAutoImplicit false
+set_option autoImplicit false
+
+/-!
+# Summing present-argument bounds along the gate hybrid
+
+The selected-step security result bounds only actual arguments. Missing argument positions
+leave the stage labels unchanged. This consumer combines that structural dichotomy with
+the existing polynomial-chain theorem, uniformly over the same arbitrary input family.
+
+## Main definitions
+
+None.
+
+## Main results
+
+* `endpoint_negligible`: a uniform negligible selected-step bound gives one negligible
+  endpoint bound for a polynomial-size family of two-step-per-gate hybrids.
+
+## References
+
+* [PS10] R. Pass, a. shelat, *A Course in Cryptography*, lecture notes, 3rd ed., January
+  2010, Theorem 163.7 (the gate hybrid and polynomial summation).
+-/
+
+namespace TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof.GateHybridChain
+
+open Complexity BoolCircuit TCSlib.Cryptography
+
+/-- Suppose a fan-in-two circuit family has size at most `S(n)`, consistent wire values,
+and a sampler `H` whose stage labels determine its value. If every substep at an actual
+argument has advantage at most one nonnegative negligible `μ(n)`, uniformly over `z`,
+then the advantage from stage zero to stage `2*gatecount` has one negligible bound,
+uniformly over that same family. The input family need not be finite or nonempty.
+[PS10, Theorem 163.7]
+`∃ ε : ℕ → ℝ, Negligible ε ∧ ∀ n z, distAdv (H n z 0) (H n z (2 * (C n).gates.length))
+(D.distinguisherL n) ≤ ε n`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** Twice the gate count is bounded by twice the circuit size and hence
+by the polynomial twice `S`. Apply the established negligible-chain theorem with this
+envelope. For each step, the proved gate-step dichotomy either identifies the stage-label
+maps or names an actual argument at the step's gate. In the first case the sampler
+congruence identifies both distributions, so its advantage is zero and the nonnegative
+bound applies. In the second case the supplied selected-argument bound applies at the
+exact clock index. The existing chain theorem performs the summation and negligible
+closure; no probability identity or hybrid summation is re-derived. -/
+theorem endpoint_negligible {inputs : ℕ → ℕ} {Z σ : ℕ → Type*}
+    [∀ n, Fintype (σ n)] (C : ∀ n, DAGCircuit (inputs n)) (S : PolyExpr)
+    (hSize : ∀ n, (C n).size ≤ S.eval n) (v : ∀ n, Z n → ℕ → Bool)
+    (hA : ∀ n (i : Fin (C n).gates.length), ((C n).gates[i]).args.length ≤ 2)
+    (hConsistent : ∀ n z (i : Fin (C n).gates.length),
+      gateOut ((C n).gates[i]) (RelabelGarbling.argValues (C n) (v n z) i) =
+        v n z (inputs n + i))
+    (H : ∀ n, Z n → ℕ → σ n → List Bool)
+    (hSameStage : ∀ n z t t', GateHybrids.stageBit (C n) (v n z) t =
+      GateHybrids.stageBit (C n) (v n z) t' → H n z t = H n z t')
+    (D : NUPPT) {μ : ℕ → ℝ} (hμ : Negligible μ) (hNonneg : ∀ n, 0 ≤ μ n)
+    (hSelected : ∀ n z (i : Fin (C n).gates.length)
+      (p : Fin ((C n).gates[i]).args.length), p.val < 2 →
+      distAdv (H n z (2 * i.val + p.val)) (H n z (2 * i.val + p.val + 1))
+        (D.distinguisherL n) ≤ μ n) :
+    ∃ ε : ℕ → ℝ, Negligible ε ∧ ∀ n z,
+      distAdv (H n z 0) (H n z (2 * (C n).gates.length)) (D.distinguisherL n) ≤ ε n := by
+  have hSteps n : 2 * (C n).gates.length ≤ (PolyExpr.mul (.const 2) S).eval n := by
+    rw [PolyExpr.eval_mul, PolyExpr.eval_const]
+    have hs := hSize n
+    rw [DAGCircuit.size_eq] at hs
+    omega
+  apply SliceReduction.chain_negligible H D (fun n => 2 * (C n).gates.length)
+    (.mul (.const 2) S) hSteps hμ
+  intro n z t ht
+  rcases GateStepCases.step_cases (C n) (v n z) (hA n) (hConsistent n z) t ht with
+    hSame | ⟨i, p, hClock, hp⟩
+  · rw [hSameStage n z t (t + 1) hSame, distAdv_def, sub_self, abs_zero]
+    exact hNonneg n
+  · rw [hClock]
+    exact hSelected n z i p hp
+
+end TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof.GateHybridChain

+/-
+Copyright (c) 2026 TCSlib contributors. All rights reserved.
+Released under Apache 2.0 license as described in the file LICENSE.
+-/
+import TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.Yao.HybridProof.GateHybrids
+import TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.Yao.HybridProof.FakeMessage
+import TCSlib.Cryptography.ZeroKnowledge.Interaction

+set_option maxHeartbeats 0
+set_option relaxedAutoImplicit false
+set_option autoImplicit false

+/-!
+# Encoded endpoints of the garbled-gate hybrid
+
+The hybrid message uses the shared generic stage tables, active false input
+keys and output decoding pairs. Its last stage is exactly the canonical fake
+message. The first stage is the original real payload after seed relabeling.
+The view sampler keeps the prescribed Alice output outside Bob's encoded view.
+
+## Main definitions
+
+* `message`: the encoded stage table/key/pair payload.
+* `sample`: Bob's view with active answers and a fixed prescribed Alice output.
+
+## Main results
+
+* `message_relabel`, `activeAnswers_relabel`: per-seed first-endpoint identities.
+* `message_end`, `sample_end`: exact canonical fake-message/view endpoints.
+
+## References
+
+* [PS10] R. Pass, a. shelat, *A Course in Cryptography*, lecture notes, 3rd ed.,
+  January 2010, Protocol 162.6 and Theorem 163.7 (the Bob-view hybrid).
+-/
+
+namespace TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof.GateHybridMessage
+
+open Complexity Turing BoolCircuit TCSlib.Cryptography
+
+variable {inputs width : ℕ} {ρ : Type}
+
+/-- The hybrid's first message encodes its stage tables, Alice's false keys,
+and decoding pairs ordered by the prescribed output-wire values.
+[PS10, Theorem 163.7]
+`message M C s inA outLen output v stage = pairEncode (BinArith.encWords (List.ofFn fun i =>
+BinArith.encWords (GateHybrids.tableWith M C s (GateHybrids.stageBit C v stage) i))) (pairEncode
+(BinArith.encWords (List.ofFn fun w : Fin inA => List.ofFn (keyAt C s w false))) (BinArith.encWords
+(List.ofFn fun j => pairEncode (List.ofFn (FakeObjects.decodePair C s (output j) (v (output j))).1)
+(List.ofFn (FakeObjects.decodePair C s (output j) (v (output j))).2))))`. -/
+noncomputable def message (M : MultiEnc (Fin width → Bool) (List Bool) ρ)
+    (C : DAGCircuit inputs) (s : GarbleSeed C (Fin width → Bool) ρ)
+    (inA outLen : ℕ) (output : Fin outLen → ℕ) (v : ℕ → Bool) (stage : ℕ) : List Bool :=
+  pairEncode (BinArith.encWords (List.ofFn fun i =>
+      BinArith.encWords (GateHybrids.tableWith M C s (GateHybrids.stageBit C v stage) i)))
+    (pairEncode
+      (BinArith.encWords (List.ofFn fun w : Fin inA => List.ofFn (keyAt C s w false)))
+      (BinArith.encWords (List.ofFn fun j =>
+        pairEncode (List.ofFn (FakeObjects.decodePair C s (output j) (v (output j))).1)
+          (List.ofFn (FakeObjects.decodePair C s (output j) (v (output j))).2))))
+
+/-- `message ... stage` is the encoded stage-table vector paired with the
+false-key vector and output-value-ordered decoding-pair vector.
+`message M C s inA outLen output v stage = pairEncode (BinArith.encWords (List.ofFn fun i =>
+BinArith.encWords (GateHybrids.tableWith M C s (GateHybrids.stageBit C v stage) i))) (pairEncode
+(BinArith.encWords (List.ofFn fun w : Fin inA => List.ofFn (keyAt C s w false))) (BinArith.encWords
+(List.ofFn fun j => pairEncode (List.ofFn (FakeObjects.decodePair C s (output j) (v (output j))).1)
+(List.ofFn (FakeObjects.decodePair C s (output j) (v (output j))).2))))`. -/
+theorem message_eq (M : MultiEnc (Fin width → Bool) (List Bool) ρ)
+    (C : DAGCircuit inputs) (s : GarbleSeed C (Fin width → Bool) ρ)
+    (inA outLen : ℕ) (output : Fin outLen → ℕ) (v : ℕ → Bool) (stage : ℕ) :
+    message M C s inA outLen output v stage =
+      pairEncode (BinArith.encWords (List.ofFn fun i =>
+          BinArith.encWords (GateHybrids.tableWith M C s
+            (GateHybrids.stageBit C v stage) i)))
+        (pairEncode
+          (BinArith.encWords (List.ofFn fun w : Fin inA => List.ofFn (keyAt C s w false)))
+          (BinArith.encWords (List.ofFn fun j =>
+            pairEncode (List.ofFn (FakeObjects.decodePair C s (output j) (v (output j))).1)
+              (List.ofFn (FakeObjects.decodePair C s (output j) (v (output j))).2)))) := rfl
+
+/-- The stage sampler pairs Bob's encoded view with the prescribed Alice output.
+Bob's private input is his own word paired with the active false-key answers.
+[PS10, Theorem 163.7]
+`sample M C s inA inB outLen output v commonInput bobInput bobCoins aliceOutput stage = pairEncode
+(⟨commonInput, pairEncode bobInput (List.ofFn (FakeObjects.activeAnswers C s inA inB)), bobCoins,
+[message M C s inA outLen output v stage]⟩ : View).encode aliceOutput`. -/
+noncomputable def sample (M : MultiEnc (Fin width → Bool) (List Bool) ρ)
+    (C : DAGCircuit inputs) (s : GarbleSeed C (Fin width → Bool) ρ)
+    (inA inB outLen : ℕ) (output : Fin outLen → ℕ) (v : ℕ → Bool)
+    (commonInput bobInput bobCoins aliceOutput : List Bool) (stage : ℕ) : List Bool :=
+  pairEncode (⟨commonInput,
+    pairEncode bobInput (List.ofFn (FakeObjects.activeAnswers C s inA inB)),
+    bobCoins, [message M C s inA outLen output v stage]⟩ : View).encode aliceOutput
+
+/-- `sample ... stage = pairEncode (view(commonInput,⟨bobInput,activeAnswers⟩,
+bobCoins,[message stage]).encode) aliceOutput`. -/
+theorem sample_eq (M : MultiEnc (Fin width → Bool) (List Bool) ρ)
+    (C : DAGCircuit inputs) (s : GarbleSeed C (Fin width → Bool) ρ)
+    (inA inB outLen : ℕ) (output : Fin outLen → ℕ) (v : ℕ → Bool)
+    (commonInput bobInput bobCoins aliceOutput : List Bool) (stage : ℕ) :
+    sample M C s inA inB outLen output v commonInput bobInput bobCoins aliceOutput stage =
+      pairEncode (⟨commonInput,
+        pairEncode bobInput (List.ofFn (FakeObjects.activeAnswers C s inA inB)),
+        bobCoins, [message M C s inA outLen output v stage]⟩ : View).encode aliceOutput := rfl
+
+/-- False active answers under the relabeled seed are exactly the original
+answers chosen by the wire values, including when the key width is zero.
+`FakeObjects.activeAnswers C (RelabelGarbling.relabelSeed C v s) first count = fun t => keyAt C s
+(first + t.divNat) (v (first + t.divNat)) t.modNat`. -/
+theorem activeAnswers_relabel (C : DAGCircuit inputs)
+    (s : GarbleSeed C (Fin width → Bool) ρ) (v : ℕ → Bool) (first count : ℕ) :
+    FakeObjects.activeAnswers C (RelabelGarbling.relabelSeed C v s) first count =
+      fun t => keyAt C s (first + t.divNat) (v (first + t.divNat)) t.modNat := by
+  funext t
+  simp only [FakeObjects.activeAnswers_eq, RelabelGarbling.keyAt_relabel, Bool.false_xor]
+
+/-- The output-value-ordered decoding pair under the relabeled seed is exactly
+the original `(false key,true key)` pair, including default vertices.
+`FakeObjects.decodePair C (RelabelGarbling.relabelSeed C v s) w (v w) = (keyAt C s w false, keyAt C
+s w true)`. -/
+theorem decodePair_relabel (C : DAGCircuit inputs)
+    (s : GarbleSeed C (Fin width → Bool) ρ) (v : ℕ → Bool) (w : ℕ) :
+    FakeObjects.decodePair C (RelabelGarbling.relabelSeed C v s) w (v w) =
+      (keyAt C s w false, keyAt C s w true) := by
+  simp only [FakeObjects.decodePair_eq, RelabelGarbling.keyAt_relabel,
+    Bool.xor_self, Bool.not_xor_self]
+
+/-- The original real payload is exactly stage zero under the relabeled seed:
+real tables, keys selected by input-wire values, and ordinary output pairs
+become the stage tables, false keys, and output-value-ordered pairs.
+[PS10, Theorem 163.7]
+`pairEncode (BinArith.encWords (List.ofFn fun i => BinArith.encWords (garbledTable M C s i)))
+(pairEncode (BinArith.encWords (List.ofFn fun w : Fin inA => List.ofFn (keyAt C s w (v w))))
+(BinArith.encWords (List.ofFn fun j => pairEncode (List.ofFn (keyAt C s (output j) false))
+(List.ofFn (keyAt C s (output j) true))))) = message M C (RelabelGarbling.relabelSeed C v s) inA
+outLen output v 0`. -/
+theorem message_relabel (M : MultiEnc (Fin width → Bool) (List Bool) ρ)
+    (C : DAGCircuit inputs) (s : GarbleSeed C (Fin width → Bool) ρ)
+    (inA outLen : ℕ) (output : Fin outLen → ℕ) (v : ℕ → Bool) :
+    pairEncode (BinArith.encWords (List.ofFn fun i =>
+        BinArith.encWords (garbledTable M C s i)))
+      (pairEncode
+        (BinArith.encWords (List.ofFn fun w : Fin inA =>
+          List.ofFn (keyAt C s w (v w))))
+        (BinArith.encWords (List.ofFn fun j =>
+          pairEncode (List.ofFn (keyAt C s (output j) false))
+            (List.ofFn (keyAt C s (output j) true))))) =
+      message M C (RelabelGarbling.relabelSeed C v s) inA outLen output v 0 := by
+  simp only [message_eq, ← GateHybrids.tableWith_start M C v s,
+    RelabelGarbling.keyAt_relabel, Bool.false_xor, decodePair_relabel]
+
+/-- The final-stage encoded payload is exactly the shared canonical fake
+message, with result bits read at the output wires. [PS10, Theorem 163.7]
+`message M C s inA outLen output v (2 * C.gates.length) = FakeObjects.message M C s inA outLen
+output (fun j => v (output j))`. -/
+theorem message_end (M : MultiEnc (Fin width → Bool) (List Bool) ρ)
+    (C : DAGCircuit inputs) (s : GarbleSeed C (Fin width → Bool) ρ)
+    (inA outLen : ℕ) (output : Fin outLen → ℕ) (v : ℕ → Bool) :
+    message M C s inA outLen output v (2 * C.gates.length) =
+      FakeObjects.message M C s inA outLen output (fun j => v (output j)) := by
+  simp only [message_eq, FakeObjects.message_eq, GateHybrids.tableWith_end]
+
+/-- The real payload and wire-value-selected OT answers give exactly the
+stage-zero Bob-view sampler after seed relabeling. The prescribed Alice output
+remains outside the encoded view. [PS10, Theorem 163.7]
+`pairEncode (⟨commonInput, pairEncode bobInput (List.ofFn fun t : Fin (inB * width) => keyAt C s
+(inA + t.divNat) (v (inA + t.divNat)) t.modNat), bobCoins, [pairEncode (BinArith.encWords (List.ofFn
+fun i => BinArith.encWords (garbledTable M C s i))) (pairEncode (BinArith.encWords (List.ofFn fun w
+: Fin inA => List.ofFn (keyAt C s w (v w)))) (BinArith.encWords (List.ofFn fun j => pairEncode
+(List.ofFn (keyAt C s (output j) false)) (List.ofFn (keyAt C s (output j) true)))))]⟩ : View).encode
+aliceOutput = sample M C (RelabelGarbling.relabelSeed C v s) inA inB outLen output v commonInput
+bobInput bobCoins aliceOutput 0`. -/
+theorem sample_relabel (M : MultiEnc (Fin width → Bool) (List Bool) ρ)
+    (C : DAGCircuit inputs) (s : GarbleSeed C (Fin width → Bool) ρ)
+    (inA inB outLen : ℕ) (output : Fin outLen → ℕ) (v : ℕ → Bool)
+    (commonInput bobInput bobCoins aliceOutput : List Bool) :
+    pairEncode (⟨commonInput,
+      pairEncode bobInput (List.ofFn fun t : Fin (inB * width) =>
+        keyAt C s (inA + t.divNat) (v (inA + t.divNat)) t.modNat), bobCoins,
+      [pairEncode (BinArith.encWords (List.ofFn fun i =>
+          BinArith.encWords (garbledTable M C s i)))
+        (pairEncode
+          (BinArith.encWords (List.ofFn fun w : Fin inA =>
+            List.ofFn (keyAt C s w (v w))))
+          (BinArith.encWords (List.ofFn fun j =>
+            pairEncode (List.ofFn (keyAt C s (output j) false))
+              (List.ofFn (keyAt C s (output j) true)))))]⟩ : View).encode aliceOutput =
+      sample M C (RelabelGarbling.relabelSeed C v s) inA inB outLen output v
+        commonInput bobInput bobCoins aliceOutput 0 := by
+  rw [sample_eq, activeAnswers_relabel, ← message_relabel]
+
+/-- The last-stage view sampler is exactly Bob's canonical fake-message and
+active-answer view, paired with the prescribed Alice output. [PS10, Theorem 163.7]
+`sample M C s inA inB outLen output v commonInput bobInput bobCoins aliceOutput (2 * C.gates.length)
+= pairEncode (⟨commonInput, pairEncode bobInput (List.ofFn (FakeObjects.activeAnswers C s inA inB)),
+bobCoins, [FakeObjects.message M C s inA outLen output (fun j => v (output j))]⟩ : View).encode
+aliceOutput`. -/
+theorem sample_end (M : MultiEnc (Fin width → Bool) (List Bool) ρ)
+    (C : DAGCircuit inputs) (s : GarbleSeed C (Fin width → Bool) ρ)
+    (inA inB outLen : ℕ) (output : Fin outLen → ℕ) (v : ℕ → Bool)
+    (commonInput bobInput bobCoins aliceOutput : List Bool) :
+    sample M C s inA inB outLen output v commonInput bobInput bobCoins aliceOutput
+        (2 * C.gates.length) =
+      pairEncode (⟨commonInput,
+        pairEncode bobInput (List.ofFn (FakeObjects.activeAnswers C s inA inB)),
+        bobCoins, [FakeObjects.message M C s inA outLen output
+          (fun j => v (output j))]⟩ : View).encode aliceOutput := by
+  rw [sample_eq, message_end]
+
+end TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof.GateHybridMessage

+Released under Apache 2.0 license as described in the file LICENSE.

+import TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.Yao.HybridProof.SelectedStepSecurity
+import TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.Yao.HybridProof.GateHybridChain
+
+set_option maxHeartbeats 0
+set_option relaxedAutoImplicit false
+set_option autoImplicit false

+/-!
+# Joint slice security for the complete actual gate hybrid
+
+The selected-step theorem already supplies one negligible bound uniform over every finite
+input option and every actual argument. The chain consumer handles the unchanged missing
+positions. This module composes those public contracts on the actual uniform-tape sampler.
+
+## Main definitions
+
+None.
+
+## Main results
+
+* `endpoint_negligible`: joint slice security gives one negligible actual-sample endpoint
+  bound, for each fixed distinguisher and uniformly over the finite input family.
+
+## References
+
+* [PS10] R. Pass, a. shelat, *A Course in Cryptography*, lecture notes, 3rd ed., January
+  2010, Theorem 163.7 (the complete inactive-key gate hybrid).
+-/
+
+namespace TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof.GateHybridSecurity
+
+open Complexity Turing BoolCircuit TCSlib.Cryptography
+
+variable {I : ℕ → Type} [∀ n, Fintype (I n)] {inputs : ℕ → ℕ}
+  (C : ∀ n, DAGCircuit (inputs n)) (S A B O R : PolyExpr)
+  (seed : ∀ n, List Bool →
+    GarbleSeed (C n) (Fin n → Bool) ((Fin n → Bool) × (Fin n → Bool)))
+  (v : ∀ n, I n → ℕ → Bool) (output : ∀ n, Fin (O.eval n) → ℕ)
+  (bobWord aliceWord : ∀ n, I n → List Bool)
+  (hSize : ∀ n, (C n).size ≤ S.eval n) (hfanin : ∀ n, (C n).IsFaninTwo)
+  (hEval : ∀ n z (i : Fin (C n).gates.length),
+    gateOut ((C n).gates[i]) (RelabelGarbling.argValues (C n) (v n z) i) =
+      v n z (inputs n + i))
+  (hsink : ∀ n j g, g ∈ (C n).gates → output n j ∉ g.args)
+  (hBob : ∀ n z, (bobWord n z).length ≤ B.eval n)
+  (hAlice : ∀ n z, (aliceWord n z).length ≤ R.eval n)
+  {L : PolyExpr} {L₂ : ℕ → ℕ}
+  (G : ∀ n, (Fin n → Bool) → (Fin n → Bool) → (Fin (L.eval n) → Bool))
+  (G₁ : ∀ n, (Fin n → Bool) → (Fin n → Bool) → (Fin (n + n) → Bool))
+  (G₂ : ∀ n, (Fin n → Bool) → (Fin n → Bool) → (Fin (L₂ n) → Bool))
+  (E₁ E₂ : List Bool → List Bool) (hE₂ : PolyTimeComputable E₂)
+  (hG : ∀ n (s x : Fin n → Bool),
+    List.ofFn (G n s x) = List.ofFn (G₁ n s x) ++ List.ofFn (G₂ n s x))
+  (hGE₁ : ∀ n (s x : Fin n → Bool), E₁ (pairEncode (List.ofFn s) (List.ofFn x)) =
+    List.ofFn (G₁ n s x))
+  (hGE₂ : ∀ n (s x : Fin n → Bool), E₂ (pairEncode (List.ofFn s) (List.ofFn x)) =
+    List.ofFn (G₂ n s x))
+  (hL : ∀ n, 7 * n + 2 ≤ L.eval n) (hSecure : SliceMultiMessageSecure G)
+
+variable
+  (hK : ∀ n q (w : Fin (C n).size) (b : Bool) (t : Fin n),
+    (seed n q).1 w b t = q.getD ((b.toNat * S.eval n + w.val) * n + t.val) false)
+  (hR₀ : ∀ n q (i : Fin (C n).gates.length)
+      (a : Fin (C n).gates[i.val].args.length → Bool) (t : Fin n),
+    ((seed n q).2.1 i a).1 t = q.getD (2 * S.eval n * n + (4 * i.val +
+      (List.ofFn a).foldr (fun b k => b.toNat + 2 * k) 0) * (2 * n) + t.val) false)
+  (hR₁ : ∀ n q (i : Fin (C n).gates.length)
+      (a : Fin (C n).gates[i.val].args.length → Bool) (t : Fin n),
+    ((seed n q).2.1 i a).2 t = q.getD (2 * S.eval n * n + (4 * i.val +
+      (List.ofFn a).foldr (fun b k => b.toNat + 2 * k) 0) * (2 * n) + n + t.val) false)
+  (hP : ∀ n q (i : Fin (C n).gates.length)
+      (a : Fin (C n).gates[i.val].args.length → Bool)
+      (t : Fin (C n).gates[i.val].args.length),
+    (seed n q).2.2 i a t = xor (a t)
+      (q.getD (10 * S.eval n * n + 2 * i.val + t.val) false))
+
+include hSize hfanin hEval hsink hBob hAlice hE₂ hG hGE₁ hGE₂ hL hSecure
+  hK hR₀ hR₁ hP
+
+/-- For the supplied polynomial-size fan-in-two circuit family, addressed seed and
+consistent values, joint slice security gives one negligible bound on the actual encoded
+sample's advantage from stage zero to stage `2*gatecount`, uniformly over every input
+option. The simulator sample is the existing `GateHybridMessage.sample`, including the
+prescribed receiver word and outer sender output. [PS10, Theorem 163.7]
+`∃ ε : ℕ → ℝ, Negligible ε ∧ ∀ n (z : I n), distAdv (fun r : SelectedStepChoices.Tape S n =>
+GateHybridMessage.sample (nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ n) (C n) (seed n (List.ofFn r)) (A.eval n) (B.eval n)
+(O.eval n) (output n) (v n z) (List.replicate n true) (bobWord n z) [] (aliceWord n z) 0) (fun r :
+SelectedStepChoices.Tape S n => GateHybridMessage.sample (nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ n) (C n) (seed n
+(List.ofFn r)) (A.eval n) (B.eval n) (O.eval n) (output n) (v n z) (List.replicate n true) (bobWord
+n z) [] (aliceWord n z) (2 * (C n).gates.length)) (D.distinguisherL n) ≤ ε n`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** Apply the established selected-step theorem to the actual uniform tape.
+It returns a nonnegative negligible bound uniform over all inputs, gates and present
+arguments. Instantiate the generic chain consumer with the actual encoded sample family.
+The published fan-in reader supplies arity at most two. Equality of stage-label families
+passes through the public complete-sample congruence, pointwise on the tape. Finally feed
+the selected-step bound to the chain consumer, ignoring its redundant first-two-position
+premise. The chain theorem already handles absent arguments and polynomial summation;
+no reduction, probability law or sampler definition is reproduced. -/
+theorem endpoint_negligible (D : NUPPT) :
+    ∃ ε : ℕ → ℝ, Negligible ε ∧ ∀ n (z : I n),
+      distAdv
+        (fun r : SelectedStepChoices.Tape S n =>
+          GateHybridMessage.sample (nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ n) (C n) (seed n (List.ofFn r))
+            (A.eval n) (B.eval n) (O.eval n) (output n) (v n z) (List.replicate n true)
+            (bobWord n z) [] (aliceWord n z) 0)
+        (fun r : SelectedStepChoices.Tape S n =>
+          GateHybridMessage.sample (nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ n) (C n) (seed n (List.ofFn r))
+            (A.eval n) (B.eval n) (O.eval n) (output n) (v n z) (List.replicate n true)
+            (bobWord n z) [] (aliceWord n z) (2 * (C n).gates.length))
+        (D.distinguisherL n) ≤ ε n := by
+  obtain ⟨μ, hμ, hNonneg, hSelected⟩ := SelectedStepSecurity.selected_negligible
+    C S A B O R seed v output bobWord aliceWord hSize hfanin hEval hsink hBob hAlice
+    G G₁ G₂ E₁ E₂ hE₂ hG hGE₁ hGE₂ hL hSecure hK hR₀ hR₁ hP D
+  let H := fun n (z : I n) stage (r : SelectedStepChoices.Tape S n) =>
+    GateHybridMessage.sample (nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ n) (C n) (seed n (List.ofFn r))
+      (A.eval n) (B.eval n) (O.eval n) (output n) (v n z) (List.replicate n true)
+      (bobWord n z) [] (aliceWord n z) stage
+  have hA n (i : Fin (C n).gates.length) :=
+    ((DAGCircuit.isFaninTwo_iff (C n)).mp (hfanin n)).2 _ (List.getElem_mem i.isLt)
+  apply GateHybridChain.endpoint_negligible C S hSize v hA hEval H _ D hμ hNonneg
+    (fun n z i p _ => hSelected n z i p)
+  intro n z t t' h
+  funext r
+  exact SelectedFields.sample_eq_of_stageBit_eq (nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ n) (C n)
+    (seed n (List.ofFn r)) (A.eval n) (B.eval n) (O.eval n) (output n) (v n z)
+    (List.replicate n true) (bobWord n z) [] (aliceWord n z) t t' h
+
+end TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof.GateHybridSecurity

+/-
+Copyright (c) 2026 TCSlib contributors. All rights reserved.
+Released under Apache 2.0 license as described in the file LICENSE.

+import TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.Yao.HybridProof.CoinSeedRelabel
+import TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.Yao.HybridProof.FakeObjects
+
+set_option maxHeartbeats 0
+set_option relaxedAutoImplicit false
+set_option autoImplicit false
+
+/-!
+# Two-stage garbled-gate hybrids
+
+Rows are parameterized by their output-key labels. The hybrid first replaces
+rows whose first argument bit is true, then advances to the next gate. For
+arity at most two, the only remaining nonzero assignment in the second step
+has first bit false and second bit true. The all-zero row already has the
+false label by circuit evaluation. No positive gate count is assumed.
+
+## Main definitions
+
+* `rowWith`, `tableWith`: garbled rows and tables with supplied output-key labels.
+* `trueBit`, `outputBit`: the relabeled truth bit and the two-stage hybrid labels.
+* `stageBit`: the natural-indexed family with cutoff `t / 2` and phase `t % 2`.
+
+## Main results
+
+* `trueBit_zero`: the active all-zero assignment encrypts the false output key.
+* The generic rows and tables specialize to the public real and fake objects.
+
+## References
+
+* [PS10] R. Pass, a. shelat, *A Course in Cryptography*, lecture notes, 3rd ed.,
+  January 2010, Protocol 162.6 and Theorem 163.7 (the gate-by-gate hybrid).
+-/
+
+namespace TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof.GateHybrids
+
+open BoolCircuit TCSlib.Cryptography
+
+variable {K Cipher ρ : Type} [Inhabited K] {inputs : ℕ}
+
+/-- A row with output-key label `bit i a` encrypts that key under the ordinary
+argument keys, using the row's existing seed coins. [PS10, Theorem 163.7]
+`rowWith M C s bit i a = M.enc (argKeys C s i a) (keyAt C s (inputs + i) (bit i a)) (s.2.1 i a)`. -/
+def rowWith (M : MultiEnc K Cipher ρ) (C : DAGCircuit inputs) (s : GarbleSeed C K ρ)
+    (bit : ∀ i : Fin C.gates.length, (Fin (C.gates[i]).args.length → Bool) → Bool)
+    (i : Fin C.gates.length) (a : Fin (C.gates[i]).args.length → Bool) : Cipher :=
+  M.enc (argKeys C s i a) (keyAt C s (inputs + i) (bit i a)) (s.2.1 i a)
+
+/-- `rowWith ... bit i a` is the encryption of output key `bit i a` with the
+ordinary argument keys and row coins.
+`rowWith M C s bit i a = M.enc (argKeys C s i a) (keyAt C s (inputs + i) (bit i a)) (s.2.1 i a)`. -/
+theorem rowWith_eq (M : MultiEnc K Cipher ρ) (C : DAGCircuit inputs)
+    (s : GarbleSeed C K ρ)
+    (bit : ∀ i : Fin C.gates.length, (Fin (C.gates[i]).args.length → Bool) → Bool)
+    (i : Fin C.gates.length) (a : Fin (C.gates[i]).args.length → Bool) :
+    rowWith M C s bit i a =
+      M.enc (argKeys C s i a) (keyAt C s (inputs + i) (bit i a)) (s.2.1 i a) := rfl
+
+/-- A table with labels `bit` uses the public fixed assignment enumeration and
+its seed's row permutation. [PS10, Theorem 163.7]
+`tableWith M C s bit i = (Finset.univ : Finset (Fin (C.gates[i]).args.length → Bool)).toList.map fun
+a => rowWith M C s bit i (s.2.2 i a)`. -/
+noncomputable def tableWith (M : MultiEnc K Cipher ρ) (C : DAGCircuit inputs)
+    (s : GarbleSeed C K ρ)
+    (bit : ∀ i : Fin C.gates.length, (Fin (C.gates[i]).args.length → Bool) → Bool)
+    (i : Fin C.gates.length) : List Cipher :=
+  (Finset.univ : Finset (Fin (C.gates[i]).args.length → Bool)).toList.map
+    fun a => rowWith M C s bit i (s.2.2 i a)
+
+/-- `tableWith ... bit i` enumerates `rowWith ... bit i (s.perm i a)` in the
+public fixed assignment order.
+`tableWith M C s bit i = (Finset.univ : Finset (Fin (C.gates[i]).args.length → Bool)).toList.map fun
+a => rowWith M C s bit i (s.2.2 i a)`. -/
+theorem tableWith_eq (M : MultiEnc K Cipher ρ) (C : DAGCircuit inputs)
+    (s : GarbleSeed C K ρ)
+    (bit : ∀ i : Fin C.gates.length, (Fin (C.gates[i]).args.length → Bool) → Bool)
+    (i : Fin C.gates.length) :
+    tableWith M C s bit i =
+      (Finset.univ : Finset (Fin (C.gates[i]).args.length → Bool)).toList.map
+        fun a => rowWith M C s bit i (s.2.2 i a) := rfl
+
+/-- Equal output labels at every assignment of a gate give equal generic tables.
+`tableWith M C s bit i = tableWith M C s bit' i`. -/
+theorem tableWith_congr (M : MultiEnc K Cipher ρ) (C : DAGCircuit inputs)
+    (s : GarbleSeed C K ρ)
+    (bit bit' : ∀ i : Fin C.gates.length, (Fin (C.gates[i]).args.length → Bool) → Bool)
+    (i : Fin C.gates.length) (h : ∀ a, bit i a = bit' i a) :
+    tableWith M C s bit i = tableWith M C s bit' i := by
+  rw [tableWith_eq, tableWith_eq]
+  apply List.map_congr_left
+  intro a _
+  rw [rowWith_eq, rowWith_eq, h]
+
+/-- With the gate's ordinary truth function as labels, `rowWith` equals the
+published real garbled row. This is a specialization, with no new row semantics.
+`rowWith M C s (fun j b => gateOut (C.gates[j]) b) i a = garbledRow M C s i a`. -/
+theorem rowWith_real (M : MultiEnc K Cipher ρ) (C : DAGCircuit inputs)
+    (s : GarbleSeed C K ρ) (i : Fin C.gates.length)
+    (a : Fin (C.gates[i]).args.length → Bool) :
+    rowWith M C s (fun j b => gateOut (C.gates[j]) b) i a = garbledRow M C s i a := by
+  rw [rowWith_eq, garbledRow_eq]
+
+/-- With all-false labels, `rowWith` equals `FakeObjects.row` exactly.
+`rowWith M C s (fun _ _ => false) i a = FakeObjects.row M C s i a`. -/
+theorem rowWith_fake (M : MultiEnc K Cipher ρ) (C : DAGCircuit inputs)
+    (s : GarbleSeed C K ρ) (i : Fin C.gates.length)
+    (a : Fin (C.gates[i]).args.length → Bool) :
+    rowWith M C s (fun _ _ => false) i a = FakeObjects.row M C s i a := by
+  rw [rowWith_eq, FakeObjects.row_eq]
+
+/-- The generic table with ordinary truth labels is exactly `garbledTable`.
+`tableWith M C s (fun j b => gateOut (C.gates[j]) b) i = garbledTable M C s i`. -/
+theorem tableWith_real (M : MultiEnc K Cipher ρ) (C : DAGCircuit inputs)
+    (s : GarbleSeed C K ρ) (i : Fin C.gates.length) :
+    tableWith M C s (fun j b => gateOut (C.gates[j]) b) i = garbledTable M C s i := by
+  simp only [tableWith_eq, garbledTable_eq, rowWith_real]
+
+/-- The generic table with all-false labels is exactly `FakeObjects.table`.
+`tableWith M C s (fun _ _ => false) i = FakeObjects.table M C s i`. -/
+theorem tableWith_fake (M : MultiEnc K Cipher ρ) (C : DAGCircuit inputs)
+    (s : GarbleSeed C K ρ) (i : Fin C.gates.length) :
+    tableWith M C s (fun _ _ => false) i = FakeObjects.table M C s i := by
+  simp only [tableWith_eq, FakeObjects.table_eq, rowWith_fake]
+
+/-- The relabeled truth bit is the original truth bit after argument-value XOR,
+then XORed with the actual output-wire value. [PS10, Theorem 163.7]
+`trueBit C v i a = xor (gateOut (C.gates[i]) (xorPerm (RelabelGarbling.argValues C v i) a)) (v
+(inputs + i))`. -/
+def trueBit (C : DAGCircuit inputs) (v : ℕ → Bool) (i : Fin C.gates.length)
+    (a : Fin (C.gates[i]).args.length → Bool) : Bool :=
+  xor (gateOut (C.gates[i]) (xorPerm (RelabelGarbling.argValues C v i) a)) (v (inputs + i))
+
+/-- `trueBit C v i a = gateOut(g_i)(a XOR v_args) XOR v(inputs+i)`. -/
+theorem trueBit_eq (C : DAGCircuit inputs) (v : ℕ → Bool) (i : Fin C.gates.length)
+    (a : Fin (C.gates[i]).args.length → Bool) :
+    trueBit C v i a =
+      xor (gateOut (C.gates[i]) (xorPerm (RelabelGarbling.argValues C v i) a))
+        (v (inputs + i)) := rfl
+
+/-- At a circuit-consistent wire-value map, the all-zero assignment has the
+false relabeled output bit. This includes constant gates. [PS10, Theorem 163.7]
+`trueBit C v i (fun _ => false) = false`. -/
+theorem trueBit_zero (C : DAGCircuit inputs) (v : ℕ → Bool)
+    (hEval : ∀ i : Fin C.gates.length,
+      gateOut (C.gates[i]) (RelabelGarbling.argValues C v i) = v (inputs + i))
+    (i : Fin C.gates.length) : trueBit C v i (fun _ => false) = false := by
+  have hz : xorPerm (RelabelGarbling.argValues C v i) (fun _ => false) =
+      RelabelGarbling.argValues C v i := by
+    funext t
+    simp only [xorPerm_apply, Bool.false_xor]
+  rw [trueBit_eq, hz, hEval i, Bool.xor_self]
+
+/-- Circuit evaluation provides the gate-consistency equation needed by the
+all-zero-row lemma, using the published circuit-value and gate-output readers.
+`gateOut (C.gates[i]) (RelabelGarbling.argValues C (fun w => (C.values x).getD w false) i) =
+(C.values x).getD (inputs + i) false`. -/
+theorem values_consistent (C : DAGCircuit inputs) (x : Fin inputs → Bool)
+    (i : Fin C.gates.length) :
+    gateOut (C.gates[i])
+        (RelabelGarbling.argValues C (fun w => (C.values x).getD w false) i) =
+      (C.values x).getD (inputs + i) false := by
+  rw [RelabelGarbling.argValues_eq, gateOut_eq]
+  simpa only [Fin.getElem_fin] using (C.values_getD_gate x i.isLt).symm
+
+/-- The original table equals the table with relabeled truth bits under the
+relabeled seed, in the same public enumeration order. [PS10, Theorem 163.7]
+`garbledTable M C s i = tableWith M C (RelabelGarbling.relabelSeed C v s) (trueBit C v) i`. -/
+theorem tableWith_relabel (M : MultiEnc K Cipher ρ) (C : DAGCircuit inputs)
+    (v : ℕ → Bool) (s : GarbleSeed C K ρ) (i : Fin C.gates.length) :
+    garbledTable M C s i =
+      tableWith M C (RelabelGarbling.relabelSeed C v s) (trueBit C v) i := by
+  simpa only [tableWith_eq, rowWith_eq, trueBit_eq] using
+    RelabelGarbling.garbledTable_relabel C v M s i
+
+/-- Completed gates encrypt false keys. At the current gate, nonzero phase also
+replaces first-bit-true rows; all other rows retain the relabeled truth bit.
+Natural cutoff and phase make the definition total for empty circuits.
+`outputBit C v cutoff phase i a = if (i : ℕ) < cutoff ∨ ((i : ℕ) = cutoff ∧ phase ≠ 0 ∧ (List.ofFn
+a).getD 0 false = true) then false else trueBit C v i a`. -/
+def outputBit (C : DAGCircuit inputs) (v : ℕ → Bool) (cutoff phase : ℕ)
+    (i : Fin C.gates.length) (a : Fin (C.gates[i]).args.length → Bool) : Bool :=
+  if (i : ℕ) < cutoff ∨ ((i : ℕ) = cutoff ∧ phase ≠ 0 ∧
+      (List.ofFn a).getD 0 false = true) then false else trueBit C v i a
+
+/-- `outputBit` is false at completed gates and selected current-gate rows,
+and is `trueBit C v i a` at the remaining rows.
+`outputBit C v cutoff phase i a = if (i : ℕ) < cutoff ∨ ((i : ℕ) = cutoff ∧ phase ≠ 0 ∧ (List.ofFn
+a).getD 0 false = true) then false else trueBit C v i a`. -/
+theorem outputBit_eq (C : DAGCircuit inputs) (v : ℕ → Bool) (cutoff phase : ℕ)
+    (i : Fin C.gates.length) (a : Fin (C.gates[i]).args.length → Bool) :
+    outputBit C v cutoff phase i a =
+      if (i : ℕ) < cutoff ∨ ((i : ℕ) = cutoff ∧ phase ≠ 0 ∧
+        (List.ofFn a).getD 0 false = true) then false else trueBit C v i a := rfl
+
+/-- Stage `t` uses cutoff `t / 2` and phase `t % 2`. Thus stages `2i` and
+`2i+1` are the two substeps at gate `i`. [PS10, Theorem 163.7]
+`stageBit C v t = outputBit C v (t / 2) (t % 2)`. -/
+def stageBit (C : DAGCircuit inputs) (v : ℕ → Bool) (t : ℕ)
+    (i : Fin C.gates.length) (a : Fin (C.gates[i]).args.length → Bool) : Bool :=
+  outputBit C v (t / 2) (t % 2) i a
+
+/-- `stageBit C v t = outputBit C v (t/2) (t%2)`. -/
+theorem stageBit_eq (C : DAGCircuit inputs) (v : ℕ → Bool) (t : ℕ) :
+    stageBit C v t = outputBit C v (t / 2) (t % 2) := rfl
+
+/-- Before any gate is processed, the hybrid labels are the relabeled truth bits.
+`outputBit C v 0 0 = trueBit C v`. -/
+theorem outputBit_start (C : DAGCircuit inputs) (v : ℕ → Bool) :
+    outputBit C v 0 0 = trueBit C v := by
+  funext i a
+  rw [outputBit_eq, if_neg (by omega)]
+
+/-- Once the cutoff reaches the number of gates, every output label is false.
+`outputBit C v cutoff phase = fun _ _ => false`. -/
+theorem outputBit_end (C : DAGCircuit inputs) (v : ℕ → Bool) (cutoff phase : ℕ)
+    (hc : C.gates.length ≤ cutoff) :
+    outputBit C v cutoff phase = fun _ _ => false := by
+  funext i a
+  rw [outputBit_eq, if_pos (Or.inl (lt_of_lt_of_le i.isLt hc))]
+
+/-- Even stage `2k` is the start of gate `k`, with phase zero.
+`stageBit C v (2 * k) = outputBit C v k 0`. -/
+theorem stageBit_even (C : DAGCircuit inputs) (v : ℕ → Bool) (k : ℕ) :
+    stageBit C v (2 * k) = outputBit C v k 0 := by
+  rw [stageBit_eq]
+  have hd : 2 * k / 2 = k := by omega
+  have hm : 2 * k % 2 = 0 := by omega
+  rw [hd, hm]
+
+/-- Odd stage `2k+1` is phase one at gate `k`.
+`stageBit C v (2 * k + 1) = outputBit C v k 1`. -/
+theorem stageBit_odd (C : DAGCircuit inputs) (v : ℕ → Bool) (k : ℕ) :
+    stageBit C v (2 * k + 1) = outputBit C v k 1 := by
+  rw [stageBit_eq]
+  have hd : (2 * k + 1) / 2 = k := by omega
+  have hm : (2 * k + 1) % 2 = 1 := by omega
+  rw [hd, hm]
+
+/-- The last natural stage has exactly the existing fake table at every gate.
+The equality also covers empty circuits. [PS10, Theorem 163.7]
+`tableWith M C s (stageBit C v (2 * C.gates.length)) i = FakeObjects.table M C s i`. -/
+theorem tableWith_end (M : MultiEnc K Cipher ρ) (C : DAGCircuit inputs)
+    (s : GarbleSeed C K ρ) (v : ℕ → Bool) (i : Fin C.gates.length) :
+    tableWith M C s (stageBit C v (2 * C.gates.length)) i =
+      FakeObjects.table M C s i := by
+  rw [stageBit_even, outputBit_end C v C.gates.length 0 (by rfl), tableWith_fake]
+
+/-- A completed gate's label is false at every assignment and phase.
+`outputBit C v cutoff phase i a = false`. -/
+theorem outputBit_completed (C : DAGCircuit inputs) (v : ℕ → Bool)
+    (cutoff phase : ℕ) (i : Fin C.gates.length)
+    (a : Fin (C.gates[i]).args.length → Bool) (hi : (i : ℕ) < cutoff) :
+    outputBit C v cutoff phase i a = false := by
+  rw [outputBit_eq, if_pos (Or.inl hi)]
+
+/-- A future gate retains its relabeled truth label at every phase.
+`outputBit C v cutoff phase i a = trueBit C v i a`. -/
+theorem outputBit_future (C : DAGCircuit inputs) (v : ℕ → Bool)
+    (cutoff phase : ℕ) (i : Fin C.gates.length)
+    (a : Fin (C.gates[i]).args.length → Bool) (hi : cutoff < (i : ℕ)) :
+    outputBit C v cutoff phase i a = trueBit C v i a := by
+  rw [outputBit_eq, if_neg (by omega)]
+
+/-- At the current gate, phase zero retains every relabeled truth label.
+`outputBit C v i 0 i a = trueBit C v i a`. -/
+theorem outputBit_current_zero (C : DAGCircuit inputs) (v : ℕ → Bool)
+    (i : Fin C.gates.length) (a : Fin (C.gates[i]).args.length → Bool) :
+    outputBit C v i 0 i a = trueBit C v i a := by
+  rw [outputBit_eq, if_neg (by omega)]
+
+/-- At phase one of the current gate, every first-bit-true row encrypts false.
+`outputBit C v i 1 i a = false`. -/
+theorem outputBit_current_first (C : DAGCircuit inputs) (v : ℕ → Bool)
+    (i : Fin C.gates.length) (a : Fin (C.gates[i]).args.length → Bool)
+    (ha : (List.ofFn a).getD 0 false = true) :
+    outputBit C v i 1 i a = false := by
+  rw [outputBit_eq, if_pos (Or.inr ⟨rfl, by decide, ha⟩)]
+
+/-- At phase one of the current gate, first-bit-false rows retain their truth label.
+`outputBit C v i 1 i a = trueBit C v i a`. -/
+theorem outputBit_current_rest (C : DAGCircuit inputs) (v : ℕ → Bool)
+    (i : Fin C.gates.length) (a : Fin (C.gates[i]).args.length → Bool)
+    (ha : (List.ofFn a).getD 0 false = false) :
+    outputBit C v i 1 i a = trueBit C v i a := by
+  simp only [outputBit_eq, ha, Bool.false_eq_true, and_false, or_false,
+    Nat.lt_irrefl, ite_false]
+
+/-- At a consistent wire-value map, the all-zero row stays false in every stage.
+`outputBit C v cutoff phase i (fun _ => false) = false`. -/
+theorem outputBit_zero (C : DAGCircuit inputs) (v : ℕ → Bool)
+    (hEval : ∀ i : Fin C.gates.length,
+      gateOut (C.gates[i]) (RelabelGarbling.argValues C v i) = v (inputs + i))
+    (cutoff phase : ℕ) (i : Fin C.gates.length) :
+    outputBit C v cutoff phase i (fun _ => false) = false := by
+  simp only [outputBit_eq, trueBit_zero C v hEval i, ite_self]
+
+/-- For arity at most two, two false padded argument bits force the all-zero
+assignment. This reuses the published binary-fold injectivity lemma.
+`a = fun _ => false`. -/
+theorem assignment_zero_of_two {l : ℕ} (hl : l ≤ 2) (a : Fin l → Bool)
+    (h₀ : (List.ofFn a).getD 0 false = false)
+    (h₁ : (List.ofFn a).getD 1 false = false) : a = fun _ => false := by
+  apply CoinSeedRelabel.assignmentFold_injective l hl
+  dsimp only
+  rw [RowFromCoins.assignmentFold_eq_two a hl,
+    RowFromCoins.assignmentFold_eq_two (fun _ : Fin l => false) hl]
+  simp only [h₀, h₁, List.ofFn_const, List.getD_eq_getElem?_getD,
+    List.getElem?_getD_replicate_default_eq, Bool.toNat_false]
+
+/-- The first substep leaves every other gate and every first-bit-false row unchanged.
+`outputBit C v cutoff 0 i a = outputBit C v cutoff 1 i a`. -/
+theorem outputBit_first_unchanged (C : DAGCircuit inputs) (v : ℕ → Bool)
+    (cutoff : ℕ) (i : Fin C.gates.length) (a : Fin (C.gates[i]).args.length → Bool)
+    (h : (i : ℕ) ≠ cutoff ∨ (List.ofFn a).getD 0 false = false) :
+    outputBit C v cutoff 0 i a = outputBit C v cutoff 1 i a := by
+  rcases h with hi | ha
+  · simp only [outputBit_eq, hi, false_and, or_false]
+  · simp only [outputBit_eq, ha, Bool.false_eq_true, and_false, or_false]
+
+/-- Advancing the cutoff leaves every gate other than the current gate unchanged.
+`outputBit C v cutoff 1 i a = outputBit C v (cutoff + 1) 0 i a`. -/
+theorem outputBit_second_other (C : DAGCircuit inputs) (v : ℕ → Bool)
+    (cutoff : ℕ) (i : Fin C.gates.length) (a : Fin (C.gates[i]).args.length → Bool)
+    (hi : (i : ℕ) ≠ cutoff) :
+    outputBit C v cutoff 1 i a = outputBit C v (cutoff + 1) 0 i a := by
+  by_cases hlt : (i : ℕ) < cutoff
+  · rw [outputBit_completed C v cutoff 1 i a hlt,
+      outputBit_completed C v (cutoff + 1) 0 i a (by omega)]
+  · rw [outputBit_future C v cutoff 1 i a (by omega)]
+    rw [outputBit_eq, if_neg (by omega)]
+
+/-- The second substep changes only current-gate rows whose first bit is false
+and second bit is true, when arity is at most two and wire values are consistent.
+[PS10, Theorem 163.7]
+`outputBit C v cutoff 1 i a = outputBit C v (cutoff + 1) 0 i a`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** Other gates are unchanged by the cutoff arithmetic. At the
+current gate, first-bit-true rows were already replaced in phase one. If both
+padded bits are false, fold injectivity identifies the all-zero assignment;
+circuit consistency makes its relabeled truth bit false in every phase. These
+are exactly the complementary cases to first-false/second-true selection. -/
+theorem outputBit_second_unchanged (C : DAGCircuit inputs) (v : ℕ → Bool)
+    (hA : ∀ i : Fin C.gates.length, (C.gates[i]).args.length ≤ 2)
+    (hEval : ∀ i : Fin C.gates.length,
+      gateOut (C.gates[i]) (RelabelGarbling.argValues C v i) = v (inputs + i))
+    (cutoff : ℕ) (i : Fin C.gates.length) (a : Fin (C.gates[i]).args.length → Bool)
+    (h : (i : ℕ) ≠ cutoff ∨ (List.ofFn a).getD 0 false = true ∨
+      (List.ofFn a).getD 1 false = false) :
+    outputBit C v cutoff 1 i a = outputBit C v (cutoff + 1) 0 i a := by
+  by_cases hi : (i : ℕ) = cutoff
+  · subst cutoff
+    rcases h with hne | hfirst | hsecond
+    · exact False.elim (hne rfl)
+    · rw [outputBit_current_first C v i a hfirst,
+        outputBit_completed C v ((i : ℕ) + 1) 0 i a (by omega)]
+    · cases hfirst : (List.ofFn a).getD 0 false with
+      | false =>
+        have hz := assignment_zero_of_two (hA i) a hfirst hsecond
+        rw [hz, outputBit_zero C v hEval, outputBit_zero C v hEval]
+      | true =>
+        rw [outputBit_current_first C v i a hfirst,
+          outputBit_completed C v ((i : ℕ) + 1) 0 i a (by omega)]
+  · exact outputBit_second_other C v cutoff i a hi
+
+/-- Stage zero uses precisely the relabeled truth labels.
+`stageBit C v 0 = trueBit C v`. -/
+theorem stageBit_start (C : DAGCircuit inputs) (v : ℕ → Bool) :
+    stageBit C v 0 = trueBit C v := by
+  rw [stageBit_eq]
+  exact outputBit_start C v
+
+/-- The original real table equals stage zero under the relabeled seed.
+This is a per-seed identity, before any uniform change of coins.
+`garbledTable M C s i = tableWith M C (RelabelGarbling.relabelSeed C v s) (stageBit C v 0) i`. -/
+theorem tableWith_start (M : MultiEnc K Cipher ρ) (C : DAGCircuit inputs)
+    (v : ℕ → Bool) (s : GarbleSeed C K ρ) (i : Fin C.gates.length) :
+    garbledTable M C s i =
+      tableWith M C (RelabelGarbling.relabelSeed C v s) (stageBit C v 0) i := by
+  rw [stageBit_start]
+  exact tableWith_relabel M C v s i
+
+end TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof.GateHybrids

+/-

+Released under Apache 2.0 license as described in the file LICENSE.

+import TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.Garbling
+import Mathlib.Data.List.FinRange
+import Mathlib.Data.List.Nodup
+import Mathlib.Data.Fintype.BigOperators
+import TCSlib.Programs.Util.List
+
+set_option maxHeartbeats 0
+set_option relaxedAutoImplicit false
+set_option autoImplicit false
+
+/-!
+# All occurrences of a selected inactive wire label
+
+The row enumeration and its assignment permutation are parameters. A selected wire
+contributes at most one challenge occurrence per row, because a gate has distinct
+arguments. Every row that uses its inactive label is enumerated, across every gate
+that reads the wire. Fan-in two and a duplicate-free assignment enumeration give
+at most four occurrences per gate. Fixed-width optional slots supply padding.
+
+All no-dependence statements concern symbolic `(vertex, bit)` labels. A key-value
+map may identify distinct labels; no inequality of actual keys is asserted.
+
+## Main definitions
+
+* `GateOccurrences.selectedPosition`: a row's selected inactive argument position.
+* `GateOccurrences.rowSlots`: the selected positions of every row of one gate.
+* `GateOccurrences.occurrences`: all gate/row/argument occurrences.
+* `GateOccurrences.padded`: a fixed `q+1`-slot optional challenge vector.
+
+## Main results
+
+* Exact membership and exhaustion contracts for the occurrence list and padding.
+* The fan-in-two occurrence bound and per-row uniqueness.
+* Acyclicity, prior-fake-plaintext and sink-decoding label separation.
+
+## References
+
+* [PS10] R. Pass, a. shelat, *A Course in Cryptography*, lecture notes, 3rd ed., January
+  2010, Theorem 163.7 (the hybrid over garbled gates).
+* [LP09] Y. Lindell, B. Pinkas, *A proof of security of Yao's protocol for two-party
+  computation*, Journal of Cryptology 22(2), 2009, pp. 161–188.
+-/
+
+namespace TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof.GateOccurrences
+
+open BoolCircuit
+
+/-- Find an argument position whose vertex is `u` and whose actual assignment bit is
+`true`, by searching `finRange g.args.length`.
+`selectedPosition g a u = (List.finRange g.args.length).find? fun p => decide (g.args[p.val] = u ∧ a
+p = true)`. -/
+def selectedPosition (g : DAGGate) (a : Fin g.args.length → Bool) (u : ℕ) :
+    Option (Fin g.args.length) :=
+  (List.finRange g.args.length).find? fun p => decide (g.args[p.val] = u ∧ a p = true)
+
+/-- `selectedPosition g a u` searches all argument positions for
+`g.args[p.val] = u ∧ a p = true`. -/
+theorem selectedPosition_eq (g : DAGGate) (a : Fin g.args.length → Bool) (u : ℕ) :
+    selectedPosition g a u =
+      (List.finRange g.args.length).find? fun p => decide (g.args[p.val] = u ∧ a p = true) :=
+  rfl
+
+/-- For distinct gate arguments, the search returns `p` exactly when its vertex is `u`
+and its actual row bit is `true`. Every qualifying position is therefore selected.
+`selectedPosition g a u = some p ↔ g.args[p.val] = u ∧ a p = true`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** A found position satisfies the search predicate. Conversely a qualifying
+position makes the search succeed. Any found position has the same argument vertex;
+distinctness of the argument list identifies its index with the qualifying position. -/
+theorem selectedPosition_eq_some_iff (g : DAGGate) (a : Fin g.args.length → Bool) (u : ℕ)
+    (hargs : g.args.Nodup) (p : Fin g.args.length) :
+    selectedPosition g a u = some p ↔ g.args[p.val] = u ∧ a p = true := by
+  rw [selectedPosition_eq]
+  constructor
+  · intro h
+    exact of_decide_eq_true (List.find?_some
+      (p := fun q : Fin g.args.length => decide (g.args[q.val] = u ∧ a q = true)) h)
+  · intro hp
+    have hex : ((List.finRange g.args.length).find?
+        (fun p => decide (g.args[p.val] = u ∧ a p = true))).isSome :=
+      List.find?_isSome.mpr ⟨p, List.mem_finRange p, decide_eq_true hp⟩
+    obtain ⟨q, hq⟩ := Option.isSome_iff_exists.mp hex
+    have hqp : g.args[q.val] = u ∧ a q = true :=
+      of_decide_eq_true (List.find?_some
+        (p := fun q : Fin g.args.length => decide (g.args[q.val] = u ∧ a q = true)) hq)
+    have heq : q = p := Fin.ext
+      (hargs.getElem_inj_iff.mp (hqp.1.trans hp.1.symm))
+    exact heq ▸ hq
+
+/-- The row slots of `g` record each row index and the selected argument index after
+applying the actual-assignment permutation `perm`. Rows without that occurrence are omitted.
+`rowSlots g rows perm u = (List.range rows.length).filterMap (fun r => (selectedPosition g (perm
+(rows.getD r (fun _ => false))) u).map fun p => (r, p.val))`. -/
+def rowSlots (g : DAGGate) (rows : List (Fin g.args.length → Bool))
+    (perm : Equiv.Perm (Fin g.args.length → Bool)) (u : ℕ) : List (ℕ × ℕ) :=
+  (List.range rows.length).filterMap fun r =>
+    (selectedPosition g (perm (rows.getD r (fun _ => false))) u).map fun p => (r, p.val)
+
+/-- `rowSlots` filters the row indices by the selected position and retains `(row, position)`.
+`rowSlots g rows perm u = (List.range rows.length).filterMap (fun r => (selectedPosition g (perm
+(rows.getD r (fun _ => false))) u).map fun p => (r, p.val))`. -/
+theorem rowSlots_eq (g : DAGGate) (rows : List (Fin g.args.length → Bool))
+    (perm : Equiv.Perm (Fin g.args.length → Bool)) (u : ℕ) :
+    rowSlots g rows perm u = (List.range rows.length).filterMap (fun r =>
+      (selectedPosition g (perm (rows.getD r (fun _ => false))) u).map fun p => (r, p.val)) :=
+  rfl
+
+/-- The occurrence list concatenates every gate's row slots, retaining the gate index.
+The row enumeration and the permutation describing its actual assignments are parameters.
+`occurrences C rows perm u = (List.finRange C.gates.length).flatMap (fun i => (rowSlots
+C.gates[i.val] (rows i) (perm i) u).map fun rp => (i, rp))`. -/
+def occurrences {m : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit m)
+    (rows : (i : Fin C.gates.length) → List (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool))
+    (perm : (i : Fin C.gates.length) → Equiv.Perm (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool))
+    (u : ℕ) : List (Fin C.gates.length × ℕ × ℕ) :=
+  (List.finRange C.gates.length).flatMap fun i =>
+    (rowSlots C.gates[i.val] (rows i) (perm i) u).map fun rp => (i, rp)
+
+/-- `occurrences` is the concatenation of the indexed `rowSlots` over all gates.
+`occurrences C rows perm u = (List.finRange C.gates.length).flatMap (fun i => (rowSlots
+C.gates[i.val] (rows i) (perm i) u).map fun rp => (i, rp))`. -/
+theorem occurrences_eq {m : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit m)
+    (rows : (i : Fin C.gates.length) → List (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool))
+    (perm : (i : Fin C.gates.length) → Equiv.Perm (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool))
+    (u : ℕ) :
+    occurrences C rows perm u = (List.finRange C.gates.length).flatMap (fun i =>
+      (rowSlots C.gates[i.val] (rows i) (perm i) u).map fun rp => (i, rp)) :=
+  rfl
+
+/-- A slot `(r,p)` occurs exactly when `r` is a row index, `p` is an argument index,
+that argument is `u`, and its actual permuted assignment bit is `true`.
+`(r, p) ∈ rowSlots g rows perm u ↔ r < rows.length ∧ ∃ hp : p < g.args.length, g.args[p] = u ∧ perm
+(rows.getD r (fun _ => false)) ⟨p, hp⟩ = true`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** Membership in a filtered map gives a row and a found position. The exact
+search contract supplies the vertex and bit tests. Conversely those tests force the search
+to select the given position, and the row index belongs to the enumerated range. -/
+theorem mem_rowSlots_iff (g : DAGGate) (rows : List (Fin g.args.length → Bool))
+    (perm : Equiv.Perm (Fin g.args.length → Bool)) (u r p : ℕ) (hargs : g.args.Nodup) :
+    (r, p) ∈ rowSlots g rows perm u ↔ r < rows.length ∧
+      ∃ hp : p < g.args.length, g.args[p] = u ∧
+        perm (rows.getD r (fun _ => false)) ⟨p, hp⟩ = true := by
+  rw [rowSlots_eq, List.mem_filterMap]
+  constructor
+  · rintro ⟨r', hr', h⟩
+    obtain ⟨p', hp', hpair⟩ := Option.map_eq_some_iff.mp h
+    cases hpair
+    exact ⟨List.mem_range.mp hr', p'.isLt,
+      (selectedPosition_eq_some_iff g _ u hargs p').mp hp'⟩
+  · rintro ⟨hr, hp, hvertex, hbit⟩
+    refine ⟨r, List.mem_range.mpr hr, Option.map_eq_some_iff.mpr ?_⟩
+    exact ⟨⟨p, hp⟩, (selectedPosition_eq_some_iff g _ u hargs ⟨p, hp⟩).mpr
+      ⟨hvertex, hbit⟩, rfl⟩
+
+/-- A triple `(i,r,p)` occurs exactly when row `r` at gate `i` uses vertex `u` at
+position `p` with actual assignment bit `true`. This lists occurrences across all gates.
+`(i, r, p) ∈ occurrences C rows perm u ↔ r < (rows i).length ∧ ∃ hp : p <
+C.gates[i.val].args.length, C.gates[i.val].args[p] = u ∧ perm i ((rows i).getD r (fun _ => false))
+⟨p, hp⟩ = true`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** Flat-map membership selects a gate, and mapped-pair membership identifies
+it with `i`. The one-gate slot contract gives the exact row, vertex, and assignment tests.
+For the reverse direction, `i` belongs to the complete gate-index range. -/
+theorem mem_occurrences_iff {m : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit m)
+    (rows : (i : Fin C.gates.length) → List (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool))
+    (perm : (i : Fin C.gates.length) → Equiv.Perm (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool))
+    (u : ℕ) (i : Fin C.gates.length) (r p : ℕ) (hargs : C.gates[i.val].args.Nodup) :
+    (i, r, p) ∈ occurrences C rows perm u ↔ r < (rows i).length ∧
+      ∃ hp : p < C.gates[i.val].args.length, C.gates[i.val].args[p] = u ∧
+        perm i ((rows i).getD r (fun _ => false)) ⟨p, hp⟩ = true := by
+  rw [occurrences_eq, List.mem_flatMap]
+  constructor
+  · rintro ⟨i', _, h⟩
+    obtain ⟨rp, hrp, heq⟩ := List.mem_map.mp h
+    cases heq
+    exact (mem_rowSlots_iff _ _ _ _ _ _ hargs).mp hrp
+  · intro h
+    refine ⟨i, List.mem_finRange i, List.mem_map.mpr ?_⟩
+    exact ⟨(r, p), (mem_rowSlots_iff _ _ _ _ _ _ hargs).mpr h, rfl⟩
+
+/-- Every actual assignment with the selected inactive argument occurs at some row of its
+gate, provided the row enumeration contains every assignment. The returned row's actual
+assignment is exactly the prescribed one, even when `perm` is nontrivial.
+`∃ r, (i, r, p.val) ∈ occurrences C rows perm u ∧ perm i ((rows i).getD r (fun _ => false)) = a`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** Enumerate the inverse image of the prescribed assignment under the row
+permutation. A list-membership witness supplies its row index. Reading that row and applying
+the permutation recovers the prescribed assignment. The membership contract then records
+its selected argument position. -/
+theorem exhaustive {m : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit m)
+    (rows : (i : Fin C.gates.length) → List (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool))
+    (perm : (i : Fin C.gates.length) → Equiv.Perm (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool))
+    (u : ℕ) (i : Fin C.gates.length) (hargs : C.gates[i.val].args.Nodup)
+    (hcomplete : ∀ a, a ∈ rows i) (a : Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool)
+    (p : Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length) (hvertex : C.gates[i.val].args[p.val] = u)
+    (hbit : a p = true) :
+    ∃ r, (i, r, p.val) ∈ occurrences C rows perm u ∧
+      perm i ((rows i).getD r (fun _ => false)) = a := by
+  obtain ⟨r, hr, hrow⟩ := List.mem_iff_getElem.mp (hcomplete ((perm i).symm a))
+  have hactual : perm i ((rows i).getD r (fun _ => false)) = a := by
+    rw [List.getD_eq_getElem _ _ hr, hrow, Equiv.apply_symm_apply]
+  refine ⟨r, (mem_occurrences_iff C rows perm u i r p.val hargs).mpr ?_, hactual⟩
+  exact ⟨hr, p.isLt, hvertex, hactual ▸ hbit⟩
+
+/-- Two selected occurrences at the same gate and row have the same argument index,
+because the gate's argument vertices are distinct.
+`p = p'`. -/
+theorem per_row_unique {m : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit m)
+    (rows : (i : Fin C.gates.length) → List (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool))
+    (perm : (i : Fin C.gates.length) → Equiv.Perm (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool))
+    (u : ℕ) (i : Fin C.gates.length) (r p p' : ℕ) (hargs : C.gates[i.val].args.Nodup)
+    (h : (i, r, p) ∈ occurrences C rows perm u)
+    (h' : (i, r, p') ∈ occurrences C rows perm u) : p = p' := by
+  obtain ⟨_, hp, hvertex, _⟩ := (mem_occurrences_iff C rows perm u i r p hargs).mp h
+  obtain ⟨_, hp', hvertex', _⟩ := (mem_occurrences_iff C rows perm u i r p' hargs).mp h'
+  exact hargs.getElem_inj_iff.mp (hvertex.trans hvertex'.symm)
+
+/-- The row-slot list contains no duplicate pair, since its row indices come from a
+range without repetitions. This holds even without distinct gate arguments.
+`(rowSlots g rows perm u).Nodup`. -/
+theorem rowSlots_nodup (g : DAGGate) (rows : List (Fin g.args.length → Bool))
+    (perm : Equiv.Perm (Fin g.args.length → Bool)) (u : ℕ) :
+    (rowSlots g rows perm u).Nodup := by
+  rw [rowSlots_eq]
+  apply List.Nodup.filterMap ?_ List.nodup_range
+  intro r r' b hb hb'
+  obtain ⟨p, _, hp⟩ := Option.map_eq_some_iff.mp (Option.mem_def.mp hb)
+  obtain ⟨p', _, hp'⟩ := Option.map_eq_some_iff.mp (Option.mem_def.mp hb')
+  exact (congrArg Prod.fst hp).trans (congrArg Prod.fst hp').symm
+
+/-- The full occurrence list has no repeated gate/row/argument triple. Distinct gate
+indices keep their row-slot lists disjoint.
+`(occurrences C rows perm u).Nodup`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** Each row-slot list has no repetitions and adding its gate index is
+injective. The concatenated lists for distinct gates are disjoint because equality of
+triples would identify their first coordinates. The gate-index enumeration has no repeats. -/
+theorem occurrences_nodup {m : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit m)
+    (rows : (i : Fin C.gates.length) → List (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool))
+    (perm : (i : Fin C.gates.length) → Equiv.Perm (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool))
+    (u : ℕ) : (occurrences C rows perm u).Nodup := by
+  rw [occurrences_eq, List.nodup_flatMap]
+  constructor
+  · intro i _
+    exact (rowSlots_nodup _ _ _ _).map (fun _ _ h => congrArg Prod.snd h)
+  · refine (List.nodup_finRange C.gates.length).imp ?_
+    intro i j hij x hx hx'
+    obtain ⟨a, _, ha⟩ := List.mem_map.mp hx
+    obtain ⟨b, _, hb⟩ := List.mem_map.mp hx'
+    exact hij ((congrArg Prod.fst ha).trans (congrArg Prod.fst hb).symm)
+
+/-- A duplicate-free row enumeration for a gate of arity at most two contributes at
+most four selected occurrences, for every assignment permutation.
+`(rowSlots g rows perm u).length ≤ 4`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** Filtering cannot increase the row count. A duplicate-free list of Boolean
+assignments has length at most the cardinality `2^arity`. The arity bound makes this at most
+four. The selected wire and the row permutation do not affect this count. -/
+theorem length_rowSlots_le_four (g : DAGGate) (rows : List (Fin g.args.length → Bool))
+    (perm : Equiv.Perm (Fin g.args.length → Bool)) (u : ℕ)
+    (hrows : rows.Nodup) (hfanin : g.args.length ≤ 2) :
+    (rowSlots g rows perm u).length ≤ 4 := by
+  have hcard : rows.length ≤ 2 ^ g.args.length := by
+    simpa only [Fintype.card_pi_const, Fintype.card_bool] using hrows.length_le_card
+  calc
+    (rowSlots g rows perm u).length ≤ rows.length := by
+      rw [rowSlots_eq]
+      simpa only [List.length_range] using List.length_filterMap_le
+        (fun r => (selectedPosition g (perm (rows.getD r (fun _ => false))) u).map
+          fun p => (r, p.val)) (List.range rows.length)
+    _ ≤ 2 ^ g.args.length := hcard
+    _ ≤ 2 ^ 2 := Nat.pow_le_pow_right Nat.zero_lt_two hfanin
+    _ = 4 := rfl
+
+/-- For a fan-in-two circuit with duplicate-free assignment rows, the complete occurrence
+list has length at most `4 * C.gates.length`. [PS10, Theorem 163.7] This explicit bound
+counts all uses of the selected wire, including uses in different gates.
+`(occurrences C rows perm u).length ≤ 4 * C.gates.length`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** Each gate contributes at most four slots by the assignment-cardinality
+bound. The concatenation length is the sum of these contributions. Bound the sum by four
+times the length of the complete gate-index list, which has one index per gate. -/
+theorem length_occurrences_le {m : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit m)
+    (rows : (i : Fin C.gates.length) → List (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool))
+    (perm : (i : Fin C.gates.length) → Equiv.Perm (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool))
+    (u : ℕ) (hrows : ∀ i, (rows i).Nodup) (hfanin : C.IsFaninTwo) :
+    (occurrences C rows perm u).length ≤ 4 * C.gates.length := by
+  have hbound (i : Fin C.gates.length) :
+      (rowSlots C.gates[i.val] (rows i) (perm i) u).length ≤ 4 :=
+    length_rowSlots_le_four _ _ _ _ (hrows i)
+      (((DAGCircuit.isFaninTwo_iff C).mp hfanin).2 _ (List.getElem_mem i.isLt))
+  rw [occurrences_eq]
+  have h := List.length_flatMap_le (List.finRange C.gates.length)
+    (fun i => (rowSlots C.gates[i.val] (rows i) (perm i) u).map fun rp => (i, rp)) 4
+    (fun i _ => by simpa only [List.length_map] using hbound i)
+  simpa only [List.length_finRange, Nat.mul_comm] using h
+
+/-- Pad `slots` to `q+1` optional entries by reading `slots[j.val]?`. Valid slots are
+`some` values, and every index beyond the list is `none`.
+`padded q slots j = slots[j.val]?`. -/
+def padded {α : Type*} (q : ℕ) (slots : List α) : Fin (q + 1) → Option α :=
+  fun j => slots[j.val]?
+
+/-- The `j`th padded entry is the optional list lookup `slots[j.val]?`.
+`padded q slots j = slots[j.val]?`. -/
+theorem padded_eq {α : Type*} (q : ℕ) (slots : List α) (j : Fin (q + 1)) :
+    padded q slots j = slots[j.val]? := rfl
+
+/-- A value occurs in a padded vector exactly when it belongs to the original list,
+provided the list fits in the `q+1` available slots.
+`(∃ j, padded q slots j = some a) ↔ a ∈ slots`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** A successful padded lookup witnesses list membership. Conversely list
+membership provides an index below the list length. The capacity bound places this index
+in the finite padded domain, where the lookup returns the same value. -/
+theorem padded_some_iff {α : Type*} (q : ℕ) (slots : List α)
+    (hlen : slots.length ≤ q + 1) (a : α) :
+    (∃ j, padded q slots j = some a) ↔ a ∈ slots := by
+  constructor
+  · rintro ⟨j, h⟩
+    rw [padded_eq] at h
+    exact List.mem_iff_getElem?.mpr ⟨j.val, h⟩
+  · intro h
+    obtain ⟨i, hi, hget⟩ := List.mem_iff_getElem.mp h
+    refine ⟨⟨i, lt_of_lt_of_le hi hlen⟩, ?_⟩
+    rw [padded_eq, List.getElem?_eq_getElem hi, hget]
+
+/-- A padded entry is `none` exactly when its index is at least the original list length.
+`padded q slots j = none ↔ slots.length ≤ j.val`. -/
+theorem padded_eq_none_iff {α : Type*} (q : ℕ) (slots : List α) (j : Fin (q + 1)) :
+    padded q slots j = none ↔ slots.length ≤ j.val := by
+  rw [padded_eq, List.getElem?_eq_none_iff]
+
+/-- Padding the full occurrence list loses no occurrence when `q+1` is at least
+`4 * C.gates.length`; padded `some` entries are exactly the original occurrences.
+`(∃ j, padded q (occurrences C rows perm u) j = some s) ↔ s ∈ occurrences C rows perm u`. -/
+theorem padded_occurrences_some_iff {m : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit m)
+    (rows : (i : Fin C.gates.length) → List (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool))
+    (perm : (i : Fin C.gates.length) → Equiv.Perm (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool))
+    (u q : ℕ) (hrows : ∀ i, (rows i).Nodup) (hfanin : C.IsFaninTwo)
+    (hbudget : 4 * C.gates.length ≤ q + 1) (s : Fin C.gates.length × ℕ × ℕ) :
+    (∃ j, padded q (occurrences C rows perm u) j = some s) ↔
+      s ∈ occurrences C rows perm u :=
+  padded_some_iff q _ ((length_occurrences_le C rows perm u hrows hfanin).trans hbudget) s
+
+/-- A wire used by gate `i₀` precedes that gate's output vertex `m+i₀`, by circuit
+acyclicity. [PS10, Theorem 163.7]
+`u < m + i₀.val`. -/
+theorem selected_wire_lt {m : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit m) (i₀ : Fin C.gates.length)
+    (u : ℕ) (hu : u ∈ C.gates[i₀.val].args) : u < m + i₀.val :=
+  C.args_lt i₀.val i₀.isLt u hu
+
+/-- Every hybrid plaintext label `(m+j, if j<i₀ then false else b)` differs from the
+selected inactive label `(u,true)` whenever `u<m+i₀`. [PS10, Theorem 163.7] This is an
+inequality of symbolic labels, so it makes no assumption about collisions of key values.
+
+**Proof sketch.** Before gate `i₀` the plaintext label has bit `false`, which differs from
+`true`. At gate `i₀` and later its vertex is at least `m+i₀`, strictly after `u`.
+Thus in each case one coordinate differs. -/
+theorem hybrid_plaintext_ne (m i₀ u j : ℕ) (hu : u < m + i₀) (b : Bool) :
+    (m + j, if j < i₀ then false else b) ≠ (u, true) := by
+  by_cases hj : j < i₀
+  · intro h
+    exact Bool.false_ne_true (by simpa only [if_pos hj] using congrArg Prod.snd h)
+  · intro h
+    have hv : m + j = u := congrArg Prod.fst h
+    omega
+
+/-- If gate `i₀` reads `u`, every hybrid plaintext label differs from `(u,true)`.
+This is the circuit-indexed form of `hybrid_plaintext_ne`. [PS10, Theorem 163.7]
+`(m + j, if j < i₀.val then false else b) ≠ (u, true)`. -/
+theorem hybrid_plaintext_ne_of_used {m : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit m)
+    (i₀ : Fin C.gates.length) (u : ℕ) (hu : u ∈ C.gates[i₀.val].args) (j : ℕ) (b : Bool) :
+    (m + j, if j < i₀.val then false else b) ≠ (u, true) :=
+  hybrid_plaintext_ne m i₀.val u j (selected_wire_lt C i₀ u hu) b
+
+/-- Every active label `(v,false)` differs from the selected inactive label `(u,true)`,
+including when the two labels refer to the same vertex. [PS10, Theorem 163.7]
+`(v, false) ≠ (u, true)`. -/
+theorem active_label_ne (v u : ℕ) : (v, false) ≠ (u, true) :=
+  fun h => Bool.false_ne_true (congrArg Prod.snd h)
+
+/-- If every designated output is a sink, a wire `u` read by gate `i₀` cannot occur in
+any output decoding label `(outs j,b)`. [PS10, Theorem 163.7] The bit `b` is arbitrary,
+so the statement covers both entries of every decoding pair.
+`(outs j, b) ≠ (u, true)`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** Equality of labels would identify the output vertex with `u`. The selected
+gate reads `u`, whereas the sink hypothesis forbids this output vertex from belonging to
+any gate's argument list. These two membership statements contradict one another. -/
+theorem decoding_label_ne {m k : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit m) (i₀ : Fin C.gates.length)
+    (u : ℕ) (hu : u ∈ C.gates[i₀.val].args) (outs : Fin k → ℕ)
+    (hsink : ∀ j g, g ∈ C.gates → outs j ∉ g.args) (j : Fin k) (b : Bool) :
+    (outs j, b) ≠ (u, true) := by
+  intro h
+  have hv : outs j = u := congrArg Prod.fst h
+  exact hsink j C.gates[i₀.val] (List.getElem_mem i₀.isLt) (hv.symm ▸ hu)
+
+end TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof.GateOccurrences

+/-

+import TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.Yao.HybridProof.SelectedFields
+
+set_option maxHeartbeats 0
+set_option relaxedAutoImplicit false
+set_option autoImplicit false
+
+/-!
+# Present and absent argument cases for the two-step gate clock
+
+A step below twice the gate count either names an actual argument at its
+current gate or has exactly unchanged stage labels. Missing arguments use
+the existing arity-zero/arity-at-most-one equations without selecting a
+fictional wire. No new semantic object or probability result is introduced.
+
+## Main definitions
+
+None.
+
+## Main results
+
+* `step_cases_exact`: the present argument has gate index `t/2` and position `t%2`.
+* `step_cases`: a shorter equality-or-present-argument interface.
+
+`SelectedFields.sample_eq_of_stageBit_eq` already transports the equality
+branch through the complete encoded sample; this module reuses that interface.
+
+## References
+
+* [PS10] R. Pass, a. shelat, *A Course in Cryptography*, lecture notes, 3rd ed.,
+  January 2010, Theorem 163.7 (the gate-by-gate hybrid with two substeps).
+-/
+
+namespace TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof.GateStepCases
+
+open BoolCircuit
+
+variable {inputs : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit inputs) (v : ℕ → Bool)
+  (hA : ∀ i : Fin C.gates.length, (C.gates[i]).args.length ≤ 2)
+  (hconsistency : ∀ i : Fin C.gates.length,
+    gateOut (C.gates[i]) (RelabelGarbling.argValues C v i) = v (inputs + i))
+
+include hA hconsistency
+
+/-- Every clock step `t < 2*gatecount` either leaves the stage-label family
+unchanged or names an actual argument at gate `t/2` and position `t%2`.
+No selected wire is required in the missing-argument branch. [PS10, Theorem 163.7]
+
+**Proof sketch.** Division by two identifies the current gate and the remainder
+identifies its first or second position. If that position is in range, return
+its finite index. Otherwise remainder zero forces arity zero, where the first
+substep changes nothing. Remainder one forces arity at most one, where the
+consistent all-zero-row argument makes the second substep unchanged. -/
+theorem step_cases_exact (t : ℕ) (ht : t < 2 * C.gates.length) :
+    GateHybrids.stageBit C v t = GateHybrids.stageBit C v (t + 1) ∨
+      ∃ i : Fin C.gates.length, ∃ p : Fin (C.gates[i.val]).args.length,
+        i.val = t / 2 ∧ p.val = t % 2 ∧ t = 2 * i.val + p.val ∧ p.val < 2 := by
+  obtain ⟨i, hi⟩ : ∃ i : Fin C.gates.length, i.val = t / 2 :=
+    ⟨⟨t / 2, by omega⟩, rfl⟩
+  by_cases hp : t % 2 < (C.gates[i.val]).args.length
+  · right
+    refine ⟨i, ⟨t % 2, hp⟩, hi, rfl, ?_, ?_⟩
+    · change t = 2 * i.val + t % 2
+      omega
+    · change t % 2 < 2
+      omega
+  · left
+    have hr : t % 2 = 0 ∨ t % 2 = 1 := by omega
+    rcases hr with hr | hr
+    · have hzero : (C.gates[i.val]).args.length = 0 := by omega
+      have ht₀ : t = 2 * i.val := by omega
+      simpa only [ht₀] using SelectedFields.stage_first_of_arity_zero C v i hzero
+    · have hsmall : (C.gates[i.val]).args.length ≤ 1 := by omega
+      have ht₁ : t = 2 * i.val + 1 := by omega
+      simpa only [ht₁, Nat.add_assoc] using
+        SelectedFields.stage_second_of_arity_le_one C v hA hconsistency i hsmall
+
+/-- Every step below twice the gate count either has unchanged labels or is
+the substep of an actual argument at position zero or one. This is the shorter
+consumer interface to `step_cases_exact`. [PS10, Theorem 163.7]
+`GateHybrids.stageBit C v t = GateHybrids.stageBit C v (t + 1) ∨ ∃ i : Fin C.gates.length, ∃ p : Fin
+(C.gates[i.val]).args.length, t = 2 * i.val + p.val ∧ p.val < 2`. -/
+theorem step_cases (t : ℕ) (ht : t < 2 * C.gates.length) :
+    GateHybrids.stageBit C v t = GateHybrids.stageBit C v (t + 1) ∨
+      ∃ i : Fin C.gates.length, ∃ p : Fin (C.gates[i.val]).args.length,
+        t = 2 * i.val + p.val ∧ p.val < 2 :=
+  (step_cases_exact C v hA hconsistency t ht).imp (fun h => h)
+    (fun ⟨i, p, _, _, hclock, hpos⟩ => ⟨i, p, hclock, hpos⟩)
+
+end TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof.GateStepCases

+/-

+import TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.GarblingSpecial
+import TCSlib.Cryptography.ZeroKnowledge.Interaction

+set_option relaxedAutoImplicit false
+set_option autoImplicit false
