+import TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.Yao.HybridProof.Garbling
+import TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.Yao.HybridProof.CoinGarbling
+import TCSlib.Complexity.ClassP.BinaryArith.Loops
+
+set_option maxHeartbeats 0
+set_option relaxedAutoImplicit false
+set_option autoImplicit false
+
+/-!
+# A polynomial-time row garbler from coin words
+
+The row machine receives polynomial-time unary data maps, a kind code, encoded unary
+argument indices, and a coin word. Its arity and pre-shift assignment are fixed constants.
+All maps are total on arbitrary input strings; short coin words are padded with false bits.
+
+## Main definitions
+
+* `RowFromCoins.assignmentBit`: a fixed assignment bit after the supplied XOR shift.
+* `RowFromCoins.gateBit`: the output bit from a kind code and at most two assignment bits.
+* `RowFromCoins.keyWord`, `nonceWord`: addressed key and row-nonce words.
+* `RowFromCoins.rowWord`: assemble the addressed words and perform nested encryption.
+
+## Main results
+
+* `RowFromCoins.rowWord_poly`: polynomial time on every input word.
+
+## References
+
+* [PS10] R. Pass, a. shelat, *A Course in Cryptography*, lecture notes, 3rd ed., January
+  2010, Protocol 162.6 (garbling from keys and encryption coins).
+* [BMR90] D. Beaver, S. Micali, P. Rogaway, *The round complexity of secure protocols*,
+  STOC 1990, pp. 503–513 (point-and-permute row relabelling).
+-/
+
+namespace TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof.RowFromCoins
+
+open Complexity Turing BoolCircuit TCSlib.Cryptography.EncodingMachines
+
+/-- Position `j` of a fixed assignment to `l` positions, XOR its shift coin at
+`10 · bound z · width z + 2 · gate z + j`; absent positions are false. [BMR90]
+`assignmentBit width bound gate coins l a j z = if j < l then xor ((List.ofFn a).getD j false)
+((coins z).getD (10 * bound z * width z + 2 * gate z + j) false) else false`. -/
+def assignmentBit (width bound gate : List Bool → ℕ) (coins : List Bool → List Bool)
+    (l : ℕ) (a : Fin l → Bool) (j : ℕ) (z : List Bool) : Bool :=
+  if j < l then xor ((List.ofFn a).getD j false)
+    ((coins z).getD (10 * bound z * width z + 2 * gate z + j) false) else false
+
+/-- `assignmentBit ... j z` is the assignment/shift XOR for `j < l`, and false otherwise. -/
+theorem assignmentBit_eq (width bound gate : List Bool → ℕ) (coins : List Bool → List Bool)
+    (l : ℕ) (a : Fin l → Bool) (j : ℕ) (z : List Bool) :
+    assignmentBit width bound gate coins l a j z =
+      if j < l then xor ((List.ofFn a).getD j false)
+        ((coins z).getD (10 * bound z * width z + 2 * gate z + j) false) else false := rfl
+
+/-- Every fixed shifted-assignment bit is polynomial-time from polynomial-time unary
+width, bound, and gate maps and a polynomial-time coin word. [BMR90]
+`PolyTimeComputable fun z => [assignmentBit width bound gate coins l a j z]`. -/
+theorem assignmentBit_poly {width bound gate : List Bool → ℕ} {coins : List Bool → List Bool}
+    (hwidth : PolyTimeComputable fun z => List.replicate (width z) true)
+    (hbound : PolyTimeComputable fun z => List.replicate (bound z) true)
+    (hgate : PolyTimeComputable fun z => List.replicate (gate z) true)
+    (hcoins : PolyTimeComputable coins) (l : ℕ) (a : Fin l → Bool) (j : ℕ) :
+    PolyTimeComputable fun z => [assignmentBit width bound gate coins l a j z] := by
+  by_cases h : j < l
+  · simpa only [assignmentBit_eq, if_pos h] using
+      BinArith.polyTimeComputable_xor (polyTimeComputable_const [((List.ofFn a).getD j false)])
+        (CoinGarbling.shiftBit_poly j hcoins hwidth hbound hgate)
+  · simpa only [assignmentBit_eq, if_neg h] using polyTimeComputable_const [false]
+
+/-- A gate's output on `l ≤ 2` supplied bits, selected by its two-bit kind code:
+`00` selects conjunction, `01` disjunction, and other codes negated conjunction.
+The zero- and one-position cases use their usual empty/one-bit values. -/
+def gateBit (kind : List Bool → List Bool) (l : ℕ) (b₀ b₁ : List Bool → Bool)
+    (z : List Bool) : Bool :=
+  if kind z = [false, false] then
+    if l = 0 then true else if l = 1 then b₀ z else b₀ z && b₁ z
+  else if kind z = [false, true] then
+    if l = 0 then false else if l = 1 then b₀ z else b₀ z || b₁ z
+  else !(if l = 0 then true else if l = 1 then b₀ z else b₀ z && b₁ z)
+
+/-- `gateBit kind l b₀ b₁ z` is the conjunction, disjunction, or negated conjunction
+selected by the `00`, `01`, or remaining kind-code cases, with empty/one-bit cases.
+`gateBit kind l b₀ b₁ z = if kind z = [false, false] then if l = 0 then true else if l = 1 then b₀ z
+else b₀ z && b₁ z else if kind z = [false, true] then if l = 0 then false else if l = 1 then b₀ z
+else b₀ z || b₁ z else !(if l = 0 then true else if l = 1 then b₀ z else b₀ z && b₁ z)`. -/
+theorem gateBit_eq (kind : List Bool → List Bool) (l : ℕ) (b₀ b₁ : List Bool → Bool)
+    (z : List Bool) :
+    gateBit kind l b₀ b₁ z =
+      if kind z = [false, false] then
+        if l = 0 then true else if l = 1 then b₀ z else b₀ z && b₁ z
+      else if kind z = [false, true] then
+        if l = 0 then false else if l = 1 then b₀ z else b₀ z || b₁ z
+      else !(if l = 0 then true else if l = 1 then b₀ z else b₀ z && b₁ z) := rfl
+
+/-- The finite gate-output calculation is polynomial-time if the kind word and the two
+supplied bits are polynomial-time: `z ↦ [gateBit kind l b₀ b₁ z]`.
+`PolyTimeComputable fun z => [gateBit kind l b₀ b₁ z]`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** Fixed arity makes conjunction and disjunction either constants, the first
+bit, or one Boolean operation on the two bits. Compare the kind word with its two constant
+codes, and select one of these results or its negation. All word comparisons and Boolean
+branches use the existing polynomial-time closure calculus. -/
+theorem gateBit_poly {kind : List Bool → List Bool} (hkind : PolyTimeComputable kind)
+    (l : ℕ) {b₀ b₁ : List Bool → Bool}
+    (h₀ : PolyTimeComputable fun z => [b₀ z])
+    (h₁ : PolyTimeComputable fun z => [b₁ z]) :
+    PolyTimeComputable fun z => [gateBit kind l b₀ b₁ z] := by
+  -- Fixed arity selects the conjunction and disjunction leaf calculations.
+  have hAnd : PolyTimeComputable fun z =>
+      [if l = 0 then true else if l = 1 then b₀ z else b₀ z && b₁ z] := by
+    by_cases hzero : l = 0
+    · simpa only [if_pos hzero] using polyTimeComputable_const [true]
+    · by_cases hone : l = 1
+      · simpa only [if_neg hzero, if_pos hone] using h₀
+      · simpa only [if_neg hzero, if_neg hone] using polyTimeComputable_and h₀ h₁
+  have hOr : PolyTimeComputable fun z =>
+      [if l = 0 then false else if l = 1 then b₀ z else b₀ z || b₁ z] := by
+    by_cases hzero : l = 0
+    · simpa only [if_pos hzero] using polyTimeComputable_const [false]
+    · by_cases hone : l = 1
+      · simpa only [if_neg hzero, if_pos hone] using h₀
+      · simpa only [if_neg hzero, if_neg hone] using polyTimeComputable_or h₀ h₁
+  -- Kind tests are ordinary word equality tests, including on malformed words.
+  have hkAnd : PolyTimeComputable fun z => [decide (kind z = [false, false])] := by
+    convert polyTimeComputable_pairEq.comp
+      (hkind.pairEncode (polyTimeComputable_const [false, false])) using 1
+    funext z
+    simp only [Function.comp_apply, pairFstD_pairEncode, pairSndD_pairEncode]
+  have hkOr : PolyTimeComputable fun z => [decide (kind z = [false, true])] := by
+    convert polyTimeComputable_pairEq.comp
+      (hkind.pairEncode (polyTimeComputable_const [false, true])) using 1
+    funext z
+    simp only [Function.comp_apply, pairFstD_pairEncode, pairSndD_pairEncode]
+  have h := polyTimeComputable_ite hkAnd hAnd
+    (polyTimeComputable_ite hkOr hOr (polyTimeComputable_not hAnd))
+  refine h.congr fun z => ?_
+  simp only [gateBit_eq, decide_eq_true_eq]
+  split_ifs <;> rfl
+
+/-- The addressed key word consists of `width z` coins from
+`((bit z).toNat · bound z + vertex z) · width z`, padded with false bits.
+`keyWord width bound vertex bit coins z = List.ofFn fun t : Fin (width z) => (coins z).getD (((bit
+z).toNat * bound z + vertex z) * width z + t) false`. -/
+def keyWord (width bound vertex : List Bool → ℕ) (bit : List Bool → Bool)
+    (coins : List Bool → List Bool) (z : List Bool) : List Bool :=
+  List.ofFn fun t : Fin (width z) =>
+    (coins z).getD (((bit z).toNat * bound z + vertex z) * width z + t) false
+
+/-- `keyWord ... z` is the addressed `List.ofFn` coin window of width `width z`.
+`keyWord width bound vertex bit coins z = List.ofFn fun t : Fin (width z) => (coins z).getD (((bit
+z).toNat * bound z + vertex z) * width z + t) false`. -/
+theorem keyWord_eq (width bound vertex : List Bool → ℕ) (bit : List Bool → Bool)
+    (coins : List Bool → List Bool) (z : List Bool) :
+    keyWord width bound vertex bit coins z =
+      List.ofFn fun t : Fin (width z) =>
+        (coins z).getD (((bit z).toNat * bound z + vertex z) * width z + t) false := rfl
+
+/-- The addressed key-word map is polynomial-time from polynomial-time coins, unary
+width/bound/vertex maps, and its selection bit. [PS10, Protocol 162.6]
+`PolyTimeComputable (keyWord width bound vertex bit coins)`. -/
+theorem keyWord_poly {width bound vertex : List Bool → ℕ} {bit : List Bool → Bool}
+    {coins : List Bool → List Bool} (hcoins : PolyTimeComputable coins)
+    (hwidth : PolyTimeComputable fun z => List.replicate (width z) true)
+    (hbound : PolyTimeComputable fun z => List.replicate (bound z) true)
+    (hvertex : PolyTimeComputable fun z => List.replicate (vertex z) true)
+    (hbit : PolyTimeComputable fun z => [bit z]) :
+    PolyTimeComputable (keyWord width bound vertex bit coins) :=
+  CoinGarbling.keyWord_poly hcoins hwidth hbound hvertex hbit
+    (key := fun z t =>
+      (coins z).getD (((bit z).toNat * bound z + vertex z) * width z + t) false)
+    (fun _ _ => rfl)
+
+/-- The selected row-nonce half is `width z` coins from
+`2 · bound z · width z + (4 · gate z + index z) · (2 · width z) + half.toNat · width z`.
+`nonceWord width bound gate index coins half z = List.ofFn fun t : Fin (width z) => (coins z).getD
+(2 * bound z * width z + (4 * gate z + index z) * (2 * width z) + half.toNat * width z + t)
+false`. -/
+def nonceWord (width bound gate index : List Bool → ℕ) (coins : List Bool → List Bool)
+    (half : Bool) (z : List Bool) : List Bool :=
+  List.ofFn fun t : Fin (width z) =>
+    (coins z).getD (2 * bound z * width z + (4 * gate z + index z) * (2 * width z) +
+      half.toNat * width z + t) false
+
+/-- `nonceWord ... half z` is the addressed `List.ofFn` nonce window of width `width z`.
+`nonceWord width bound gate index coins half z = List.ofFn fun t : Fin (width z) => (coins z).getD
+(2 * bound z * width z + (4 * gate z + index z) * (2 * width z) + half.toNat * width z + t)
+false`. -/
+theorem nonceWord_eq (width bound gate index : List Bool → ℕ) (coins : List Bool → List Bool)
+    (half : Bool) (z : List Bool) :
+    nonceWord width bound gate index coins half z =
+      List.ofFn fun t : Fin (width z) =>
+        (coins z).getD (2 * bound z * width z + (4 * gate z + index z) * (2 * width z) +
+          half.toNat * width z + t) false := rfl
+
+/-- Either addressed row-nonce half is polynomial-time from polynomial-time coins and
+unary width/bound/gate/assignment-index maps. [PS10, Protocol 162.6]
+`PolyTimeComputable (nonceWord width bound gate index coins half)`. -/
+theorem nonceWord_poly {width bound gate index : List Bool → ℕ}
+    {coins : List Bool → List Bool} (half : Bool) (hcoins : PolyTimeComputable coins)
+    (hwidth : PolyTimeComputable fun z => List.replicate (width z) true)
+    (hbound : PolyTimeComputable fun z => List.replicate (bound z) true)
+    (hgate : PolyTimeComputable fun z => List.replicate (gate z) true)
+    (hindex : PolyTimeComputable fun z => List.replicate (index z) true) :
+    PolyTimeComputable (nonceWord width bound gate index coins half) :=
+  CoinGarbling.rowCoinWord_poly half hcoins hwidth hbound hgate hindex
+    (nonce := fun z t => (coins z).getD
+      (2 * bound z * width z + (4 * gate z + index z) * (2 * width z) +
+        half.toNat * width z + t) false) (fun _ _ => rfl)
+
+/-- Assemble one garbled row from unary numeric maps, an output-bit map, encoded unary argument
+indices, and a coin word. The fixed assignment is XOR-shifted; its selected argument keys,
+output key for the supplied bit, and two addressed nonce halves are passed to `nestedWordEnc`.
+[PS10, Protocol 162.6; BMR90]
+`rowWordWith E₁ E₂ width bound inputs gate args coins outputBit l a z = let b₀ := assignmentBit
+width bound gate coins l a 0; let b₁ := assignmentBit width bound gate coins l a 1; let k₀ :=
+keyWord width bound (fun w => (BinArith.getW (pairEncode [] (args w))).length) b₀ coins; let k₁ :=
+keyWord width bound (fun w => (BinArith.getW (pairEncode [true] (args w))).length) b₁ coins; let
+keys := if l = 0 then [] else if l = 1 then pairEncode (k₀ z) [] else pairEncode (k₀ z) (pairEncode
+(k₁ z) []); let out := keyWord width bound (fun w => inputs w + gate w) outputBit coins; let index
+:= fun w => (b₀ w).toNat + 2 * (b₁ w).toNat; Garbling.nestedWordEnc E₁ E₂ (pairEncode keys
+(pairEncode (out z) (pairEncode (nonceWord width bound gate index coins false z) (nonceWord width
+bound gate index coins true z))))`. -/
+def rowWordWith (E₁ E₂ : List Bool → List Bool) (width bound inputs gate : List Bool → ℕ)
+    (args coins : List Bool → List Bool) (outputBit : List Bool → Bool) (l : ℕ) (a : Fin l → Bool)
+    (z : List Bool) : List Bool :=
+  let b₀ := assignmentBit width bound gate coins l a 0
+  let b₁ := assignmentBit width bound gate coins l a 1
+  let k₀ := keyWord width bound
+    (fun w => (BinArith.getW (pairEncode [] (args w))).length) b₀ coins
+  let k₁ := keyWord width bound
+    (fun w => (BinArith.getW (pairEncode [true] (args w))).length) b₁ coins
+  let keys := if l = 0 then [] else if l = 1 then pairEncode (k₀ z) []
+    else pairEncode (k₀ z) (pairEncode (k₁ z) [])
+  let out := keyWord width bound (fun w => inputs w + gate w) outputBit coins
+  let index := fun w => (b₀ w).toNat + 2 * (b₁ w).toNat
+  Garbling.nestedWordEnc E₁ E₂
+    (pairEncode keys (pairEncode (out z)
+      (pairEncode (nonceWord width bound gate index coins false z)
+        (nonceWord width bound gate index coins true z))))
+
+/-- `rowWordWith ... z` encrypts its addressed output key under the zero, one, or two addressed
+argument keys with the two addressed nonce halves. All addressing is given explicitly
+by `keyWord_eq`, `nonceWord_eq`, and `assignmentBit_eq`.
+`rowWordWith E₁ E₂ width bound inputs gate args coins outputBit l a z = let b₀ := assignmentBit
+width bound gate coins l a 0; let b₁ := assignmentBit width bound gate coins l a 1; let k₀ :=
+keyWord width bound (fun w => (BinArith.getW (pairEncode [] (args w))).length) b₀ coins; let k₁ :=
+keyWord width bound (fun w => (BinArith.getW (pairEncode [true] (args w))).length) b₁ coins; let
+keys := if l = 0 then [] else if l = 1 then pairEncode (k₀ z) [] else pairEncode (k₀ z) (pairEncode
+(k₁ z) []); let out := keyWord width bound (fun w => inputs w + gate w) outputBit coins; let index
+:= fun w => (b₀ w).toNat + 2 * (b₁ w).toNat; Garbling.nestedWordEnc E₁ E₂ (pairEncode keys
+(pairEncode (out z) (pairEncode (nonceWord width bound gate index coins false z) (nonceWord width
+bound gate index coins true z))))`. -/
+theorem rowWordWith_eq (E₁ E₂ : List Bool → List Bool) (width bound inputs gate : List Bool → ℕ)
+    (args coins : List Bool → List Bool) (outputBit : List Bool → Bool)
+    (l : ℕ) (a : Fin l → Bool) (z : List Bool) :
+    rowWordWith E₁ E₂ width bound inputs gate args coins outputBit l a z =
+      let b₀ := assignmentBit width bound gate coins l a 0
+      let b₁ := assignmentBit width bound gate coins l a 1
+      let k₀ := keyWord width bound
+        (fun w => (BinArith.getW (pairEncode [] (args w))).length) b₀ coins
+      let k₁ := keyWord width bound
+        (fun w => (BinArith.getW (pairEncode [true] (args w))).length) b₁ coins
+      let keys := if l = 0 then [] else if l = 1 then pairEncode (k₀ z) []
+        else pairEncode (k₀ z) (pairEncode (k₁ z) [])
+      let out := keyWord width bound (fun w => inputs w + gate w) outputBit coins
+      let index := fun w => (b₀ w).toNat + 2 * (b₁ w).toNat
+      Garbling.nestedWordEnc E₁ E₂
+        (pairEncode keys (pairEncode (out z)
+          (pairEncode (nonceWord width bound gate index coins false z)
+            (nonceWord width bound gate index coins true z)))) := rfl
+
+/-- Assemble one garbled row from unary numeric maps, a kind word, encoded unary argument
+indices, and a coin word. The fixed assignment is XOR-shifted; its selected argument keys,
+output key, and two addressed nonce halves are passed to `nestedWordEnc`.
+[PS10, Protocol 162.6; BMR90]
+`rowWord E₁ E₂ width bound inputs gate kind args coins l a z = let b₀ := assignmentBit width bound
+gate coins l a 0; let b₁ := assignmentBit width bound gate coins l a 1; let k₀ := keyWord width
+bound (fun w => (BinArith.getW (pairEncode [] (args w))).length) b₀ coins; let k₁ := keyWord width
+bound (fun w => (BinArith.getW (pairEncode [true] (args w))).length) b₁ coins; let keys := if l = 0
+then [] else if l = 1 then pairEncode (k₀ z) [] else pairEncode (k₀ z) (pairEncode (k₁ z) []); let
+out := keyWord width bound (fun w => inputs w + gate w) (gateBit kind l b₀ b₁) coins; let index :=
+fun w => (b₀ w).toNat + 2 * (b₁ w).toNat; Garbling.nestedWordEnc E₁ E₂ (pairEncode keys (pairEncode
+(out z) (pairEncode (nonceWord width bound gate index coins false z) (nonceWord width bound gate
+index coins true z))))`. -/
+def rowWord (E₁ E₂ : List Bool → List Bool) (width bound inputs gate : List Bool → ℕ)
+    (kind args coins : List Bool → List Bool) (l : ℕ) (a : Fin l → Bool)
+    (z : List Bool) : List Bool :=
+  rowWordWith E₁ E₂ width bound inputs gate args coins
+    (gateBit kind l (assignmentBit width bound gate coins l a 0)
+      (assignmentBit width bound gate coins l a 1)) l a z
+
+/-- `rowWord ... z` encrypts its addressed output key under the zero, one, or two addressed
+argument keys with the two addressed nonce halves. All addressing is given explicitly
+by `keyWord_eq`, `nonceWord_eq`, and `assignmentBit_eq`.
+`rowWord E₁ E₂ width bound inputs gate kind args coins l a z = let b₀ := assignmentBit width bound
+gate coins l a 0; let b₁ := assignmentBit width bound gate coins l a 1; let k₀ := keyWord width
+bound (fun w => (BinArith.getW (pairEncode [] (args w))).length) b₀ coins; let k₁ := keyWord width
+bound (fun w => (BinArith.getW (pairEncode [true] (args w))).length) b₁ coins; let keys := if l = 0
+then [] else if l = 1 then pairEncode (k₀ z) [] else pairEncode (k₀ z) (pairEncode (k₁ z) []); let
+out := keyWord width bound (fun w => inputs w + gate w) (gateBit kind l b₀ b₁) coins; let index :=
+fun w => (b₀ w).toNat + 2 * (b₁ w).toNat; Garbling.nestedWordEnc E₁ E₂ (pairEncode keys (pairEncode
+(out z) (pairEncode (nonceWord width bound gate index coins false z) (nonceWord width bound gate
+index coins true z))))`. -/
+theorem rowWord_eq (E₁ E₂ : List Bool → List Bool) (width bound inputs gate : List Bool → ℕ)
+    (kind args coins : List Bool → List Bool) (l : ℕ) (a : Fin l → Bool) (z : List Bool) :
+    rowWord E₁ E₂ width bound inputs gate kind args coins l a z =
+      let b₀ := assignmentBit width bound gate coins l a 0
+      let b₁ := assignmentBit width bound gate coins l a 1
+      let k₀ := keyWord width bound
+        (fun w => (BinArith.getW (pairEncode [] (args w))).length) b₀ coins
+      let k₁ := keyWord width bound
+        (fun w => (BinArith.getW (pairEncode [true] (args w))).length) b₁ coins
+      let keys := if l = 0 then [] else if l = 1 then pairEncode (k₀ z) []
+        else pairEncode (k₀ z) (pairEncode (k₁ z) [])
+      let out := keyWord width bound (fun w => inputs w + gate w) (gateBit kind l b₀ b₁) coins
+      let index := fun w => (b₀ w).toNat + 2 * (b₁ w).toNat
+      Garbling.nestedWordEnc E₁ E₂
+        (pairEncode keys (pairEncode (out z)
+          (pairEncode (nonceWord width bound gate index coins false z)
+            (nonceWord width bound gate index coins true z)))) := rfl
+
+/-- The complete row machine is polynomial-time on every string when its evaluators, coin
+word, output-bit map, argument word, and unary numeric maps are polynomial-time.
+[PS10, Protocol 162.6; BMR90]
+`PolyTimeComputable (rowWordWith E₁ E₂ width bound inputs gate args coins outputBit l a)`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** Read the two shifted assignment bits. The output-key bit is supplied as a
+polynomial-time parameter. Read the two argument vertex numbers from the encoded
+argument-word list; arithmetic on their unary codes supplies polynomial-time key addresses.
+The same arithmetic supplies the output-key address and the two row-nonce addresses. Assemble
+the at most two argument keys as an encoded word list, and apply the polynomial-time nested
+encryption adapter. Every operation uses total projections or false-padded coin windows. -/
+theorem rowWordWith_poly {E₁ E₂ : List Bool → List Bool} (hE₁ : PolyTimeComputable E₁)
+    (hE₂ : PolyTimeComputable E₂) {width bound inputs gate : List Bool → ℕ}
+    {args coins : List Bool → List Bool} {outputBit : List Bool → Bool}
+    (hwidth : PolyTimeComputable fun z => List.replicate (width z) true)
+    (hbound : PolyTimeComputable fun z => List.replicate (bound z) true)
+    (hinputs : PolyTimeComputable fun z => List.replicate (inputs z) true)
+    (hgate : PolyTimeComputable fun z => List.replicate (gate z) true)
+    (hargs : PolyTimeComputable args) (hcoins : PolyTimeComputable coins)
+    (houtputBit : PolyTimeComputable fun z => [outputBit z]) (l : ℕ) (a : Fin l → Bool) :
+    PolyTimeComputable (rowWordWith E₁ E₂ width bound inputs gate args coins outputBit l a) := by
+  -- Read the two assignment bits and the key words at their argument positions.
+  let b₀ := assignmentBit width bound gate coins l a 0
+  let b₁ := assignmentBit width bound gate coins l a 1
+  have h₀ : PolyTimeComputable fun z => [b₀ z] :=
+    assignmentBit_poly hwidth hbound hgate hcoins l a 0
+  have h₁ : PolyTimeComputable fun z => [b₁ z] :=
+    assignmentBit_poly hwidth hbound hgate hcoins l a 1
+  let k₀ := keyWord width bound
+    (fun w => (BinArith.getW (pairEncode [] (args w))).length) b₀ coins
+  let k₁ := keyWord width bound
+    (fun w => (BinArith.getW (pairEncode [true] (args w))).length) b₁ coins
+  have hk₀ : PolyTimeComputable k₀ := keyWord_poly hcoins hwidth hbound
+    (unaryLength_poly (BinArith.polyTimeComputable_getW.comp
+      ((polyTimeComputable_const []).pairEncode hargs))) h₀
+  have hk₁ : PolyTimeComputable k₁ := keyWord_poly hcoins hwidth hbound
+    (unaryLength_poly (BinArith.polyTimeComputable_getW.comp
+      ((polyTimeComputable_const [true]).pairEncode hargs))) h₁
+  have hkeys : PolyTimeComputable fun z =>
+      if l = 0 then [] else if l = 1 then pairEncode (k₀ z) []
+      else pairEncode (k₀ z) (pairEncode (k₁ z) []) := by
+    by_cases hzero : l = 0
+    · simpa only [if_pos hzero] using polyTimeComputable_const []
+    · by_cases hone : l = 1
+      · simpa only [if_neg hzero, if_pos hone] using
+          hk₀.pairEncode (polyTimeComputable_const [])
+      · simpa only [if_neg hzero, if_neg hone] using
+          hk₀.pairEncode (hk₁.pairEncode (polyTimeComputable_const []))
+  -- The plaintext and both nonce halves use polynomial-time numeric addresses.
+  have hout := keyWord_poly hcoins hwidth hbound (unaryAdd_poly hinputs hgate)
+    houtputBit
+  have hindex := CoinGarbling.assignmentIndex_poly h₀ h₁
+  have hr₀ := nonceWord_poly false hcoins hwidth hbound hgate hindex
+  have hr₁ := nonceWord_poly true hcoins hwidth hbound hgate hindex
+  exact (Garbling.nestedWordEnc_poly hE₁ hE₂).comp
+    (hkeys.pairEncode (hout.pairEncode (hr₀.pairEncode hr₁)))
+
+
+/-- The real row machine is polynomial-time on every string when its evaluators, coin
+word, kind/argument words, and unary numeric maps are polynomial-time.
+[PS10, Protocol 162.6; BMR90]
+`PolyTimeComputable (rowWord E₁ E₂ width bound inputs gate kind args coins l a)`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** Apply the shared `rowWordWith_poly` core, using `gateBit_poly` on the
+two polynomial-time shifted assignment bits as its output-key selection bit. -/
+theorem rowWord_poly {E₁ E₂ : List Bool → List Bool} (hE₁ : PolyTimeComputable E₁)
+    (hE₂ : PolyTimeComputable E₂) {width bound inputs gate : List Bool → ℕ}
+    {kind args coins : List Bool → List Bool}
+    (hwidth : PolyTimeComputable fun z => List.replicate (width z) true)
+    (hbound : PolyTimeComputable fun z => List.replicate (bound z) true)
+    (hinputs : PolyTimeComputable fun z => List.replicate (inputs z) true)
+    (hgate : PolyTimeComputable fun z => List.replicate (gate z) true)
+    (hkind : PolyTimeComputable kind) (hargs : PolyTimeComputable args)
+    (hcoins : PolyTimeComputable coins) (l : ℕ) (a : Fin l → Bool) :
+    PolyTimeComputable (rowWord E₁ E₂ width bound inputs gate kind args coins l a) := by
+  exact rowWordWith_poly hE₁ hE₂ hwidth hbound hinputs hgate hargs hcoins
+    (gateBit_poly hkind l (assignmentBit_poly hwidth hbound hgate hcoins l a 0)
+      (assignmentBit_poly hwidth hbound hgate hcoins l a 1)) l a
+
+/-- If the supplied row permutation satisfies the pointwise XOR-shift equation, the raw
+assignment-bit reader is exactly its output word's `j`-th bit, with false defaults. [BMR90]
+`assignmentBit width bound gate coins l a j z = (List.ofFn (p a)).getD j false`. -/
+theorem assignmentBit_of_perm (width bound gate : List Bool → ℕ)
+    (coins : List Bool → List Bool) (l : ℕ) (a : Fin l → Bool)
+    (p : Equiv.Perm (Fin l → Bool)) (z : List Bool) (j : ℕ)
+    (hp : ∀ t, p a t = xor (a t)
+      ((coins z).getD (10 * bound z * width z + 2 * gate z + t) false)) :
+    assignmentBit width bound gate coins l a j z = (List.ofFn (p a)).getD j false := by
+  by_cases h : j < l
+  · rw [assignmentBit_eq, if_pos h]
+    simp only [List.getD_eq_getElem?_getD, List.getElem?_ofFn, dif_pos h, Option.getD_some]
+    exact (hp ⟨j, h⟩).symm
+  · simp only [assignmentBit_eq, if_neg h, List.getD_eq_getElem?_getD,
+      List.getElem?_ofFn, dif_neg h, Option.getD_none]
+
+
+/-- On a genuine gate-kind code and assignment-word bits, the raw finite calculation is
+`gateOut g a` for every gate with at most two arguments. [PS10, Protocol 162.6]
+`gateBit kind g.args.length b₀ b₁ z = gateOut g a`. -/
+theorem gateBit_eq_gateOut (g : DAGGate) (a : Fin g.args.length → Bool)
+    (kind : List Bool → List Bool) (b₀ b₁ : List Bool → Bool) (z : List Bool)
+    (hfan : g.args.length ≤ 2) (hkind : kind z = g.kind.encode)
+    (h₀ : b₀ z = (List.ofFn a).getD 0 false)
+    (h₁ : b₁ z = (List.ofFn a).getD 1 false) :
+    gateBit kind g.args.length b₀ b₁ z = gateOut g a := by
+  rw [gateBit_eq, hkind, h₀, h₁, Garbling.gateOut_small g a hfan]
+  cases g.kind <;> simp [GateKind.encode_and, GateKind.encode_or, GateKind.encode_not]
+
+
+/-- For at most two positions, the assignment's binary fold is its first bit plus twice
+its second bit, with false defaults for absent positions. [PS10, Protocol 162.6]
+`(List.ofFn a).foldr (fun b k => b.toNat + 2 * k) 0 = ((List.ofFn a).getD 0 false).toNat + 2 *
+((List.ofFn a).getD 1 false).toNat`. -/
+theorem assignmentFold_eq_two {l : ℕ} (a : Fin l → Bool) (hl : l ≤ 2) :
+    (List.ofFn a).foldr (fun b k => b.toNat + 2 * k) 0 =
+      ((List.ofFn a).getD 0 false).toNat + 2 * ((List.ofFn a).getD 1 false).toNat := by
+  have hc : l = 0 ∨ l = 1 ∨ l = 2 := by omega
+  rcases hc with rfl | rfl | rfl <;> simp [List.ofFn_succ]
+
+
+/-- The generic raw row encrypts the output key selected by `outputBit z` under the
+permuted assignment's argument keys, with the supplied seed's row coins. The output bit
+is independent of the gate truth function. [PS10, Theorem 163.7; Protocol 162.6]
+`rowWordWith E₁ E₂ width bound inputs gate args coins outputBit (C.gates[i]).args.length a z =
+(nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ (width z)).enc (argKeys C s i (s.2.2 i a)) (keyAt C s (inputs z + i) (outputBit
+z)) (s.2.1 i (s.2.2 i a))`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** Read the two shifted assignment bits through the permutation equation.
+Canonical argument-word lookup gives the two argument vertices. Circuit acyclicity and the
+published size equation put every addressed argument and output key in the seed's range.
+The seed key equation identifies each key window, including the arbitrary output-bit key.
+The bounded-arity assignment fold identifies the nonce address, and both seed nonce equations
+identify its two halves. Finally the generic nested word-encryption contract turns these
+encoded vectors into the multi-key encryption of the requested output key. -/
+theorem rowWordWith_enc (E₁ E₂ : List Bool → List Bool)
+    (width bound inputs gate : List Bool → ℕ) (args coins : List Bool → List Bool)
+    (outputBit : List Bool → Bool)
+    (z : List Bool) (C : DAGCircuit (inputs z))
+    (s : GarbleSeed C (Fin (width z) → Bool)
+      ((Fin (width z) → Bool) × (Fin (width z) → Bool)))
+    (i : Fin C.gates.length) (a : Fin (C.gates[i]).args.length → Bool)
+    (hfan : (C.gates[i]).args.length ≤ 2) (hgate : gate z = i)
+    (hargs : args z = BinArith.encWords ((C.gates[i]).args.map fun v => List.replicate v true))
+    (hkey : ∀ (v : ℕ), v < C.size → ∀ b,
+      keyAt C s v b = fun t : Fin (width z) =>
+        (coins z).getD ((b.toNat * bound z + v) * width z + t) false)
+    (hr₀ : ∀ a t, (s.2.1 i a).1 t = (coins z).getD
+      (2 * bound z * width z +
+        (4 * gate z + (List.ofFn a).foldr (fun b k => b.toNat + 2 * k) 0) *
+          (2 * width z) + t) false)
+    (hr₁ : ∀ a t, (s.2.1 i a).2 t = (coins z).getD
+      (2 * bound z * width z +
+        (4 * gate z + (List.ofFn a).foldr (fun b k => b.toNat + 2 * k) 0) *
+          (2 * width z) + width z + t) false)
+    (hperm : ∀ a t, s.2.2 i a t = xor (a t)
+      ((coins z).getD (10 * bound z * width z + 2 * gate z + t) false)) :
+    rowWordWith E₁ E₂ width bound inputs gate args coins outputBit
+        (C.gates[i]).args.length a z =
+      (nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ (width z)).enc (argKeys C s i (s.2.2 i a))
+        (keyAt C s (inputs z + i) (outputBit z)) (s.2.1 i (s.2.2 i a)) := by
+  let b₀ := assignmentBit width bound gate coins (C.gates[i]).args.length a 0
+  let b₁ := assignmentBit width bound gate coins (C.gates[i]).args.length a 1
+  let a' := s.2.2 i a
+  have hb₀ : b₀ z = (List.ofFn a').getD 0 false :=
+    assignmentBit_of_perm width bound gate coins _ a (s.2.2 i) z 0 (hperm a)
+  have hb₁ : b₁ z = (List.ofFn a').getD 1 false :=
+    assignmentBit_of_perm width bound gate coins _ a (s.2.2 i) z 1 (hperm a)
+  -- Canonical lookup reads argument numbers, including its zero default.
+  have hlookup (j : ℕ) :
+      (BinArith.getW (pairEncode (List.replicate j true) (args z))).length =
+        (C.gates[i]).args.getD j 0 := by
+    rw [hargs, BinArith.getW_pairEncode, List.length_replicate]
+    change (((C.gates[i]).args.map (fun v => List.replicate v true)).getD j
+      (List.replicate 0 true)).length = (C.gates[i]).args.getD j 0
+    rw [List.getD_map, List.length_replicate]
+  have hlookup₀ : (BinArith.getW (pairEncode [] (args z))).length =
+      (C.gates[i]).args.getD 0 0 := by simpa using hlookup 0
+  have hlookup₁ : (BinArith.getW (pairEncode [true] (args z))).length =
+      (C.gates[i]).args.getD 1 0 := by simpa using hlookup 1
+  have houtlt : inputs z + i < C.size := by
+    rw [DAGCircuit.size_eq]
+    exact Nat.add_lt_add_left i.isLt _
+  have hwire (j : ℕ) : (C.gates[i]).args.getD j 0 < C.size := by
+    by_cases hj : j < (C.gates[i]).args.length
+    · rw [List.getD_eq_getElem _ _ hj]
+      exact (C.args_lt i i.isLt _ (List.getElem_mem hj)).trans houtlt
+    · rw [List.getD_eq_default _ _ (by omega), DAGCircuit.size_eq]
+      exact (Nat.zero_le C.output).trans_lt C.output_lt
+  have hkeyWord (vertex : List Bool → ℕ) (bit : List Bool → Bool)
+      (hv : vertex z < C.size) :
+      keyWord width bound vertex bit coins z = List.ofFn (keyAt C s (vertex z) (bit z)) := by
+    rw [keyWord_eq]
+    exact congrArg List.ofFn (hkey (vertex z) hv (bit z)).symm
+  have hk₀ : keyWord width bound
+      (fun w => (BinArith.getW (pairEncode [] (args w))).length) b₀ coins z =
+        List.ofFn (keyAt C s ((C.gates[i]).args.getD 0 0) ((List.ofFn a').getD 0 false)) := by
+    rw [hkeyWord _ _ (by rw [hlookup₀]; exact hwire 0), hlookup₀, hb₀]
+  have hk₁ : keyWord width bound
+      (fun w => (BinArith.getW (pairEncode [true] (args w))).length) b₁ coins z =
+        List.ofFn (keyAt C s ((C.gates[i]).args.getD 1 0) ((List.ofFn a').getD 1 false)) := by
+    rw [hkeyWord _ _ (by rw [hlookup₁]; exact hwire 1), hlookup₁, hb₁]
+  have hkeys :
+      (if (C.gates[i]).args.length = 0 then []
+       else if (C.gates[i]).args.length = 1 then pairEncode
+         (keyWord width bound (fun w =>
+           (BinArith.getW (pairEncode [] (args w))).length) b₀ coins z) []
+       else pairEncode
+         (keyWord width bound (fun w =>
+           (BinArith.getW (pairEncode [] (args w))).length) b₀ coins z)
+         (pairEncode (keyWord width bound (fun w =>
+           (BinArith.getW (pairEncode [true] (args w))).length) b₁ coins z) [])) =
+        BinArith.encWords ((argKeys C s i a').map List.ofFn) := by
+    rw [hk₀, hk₁, Garbling.argKeys_small C s i a' hfan]
+  have hout : keyWord width bound (fun w => inputs w + gate w)
+      outputBit coins z =
+        List.ofFn (keyAt C s (inputs z + i) (outputBit z)) := by
+    rw [hkeyWord _ _ (by simpa only [hgate] using houtlt), hgate]
+  -- The assignment fold gives the exact address of both nonce halves.
+  let index := fun w => (b₀ w).toNat + 2 * (b₁ w).toNat
+  have hindex : index z = (List.ofFn a').foldr (fun b k => b.toNat + 2 * k) 0 := by
+    dsimp only [index]
+    rw [hb₀, hb₁, assignmentFold_eq_two a' hfan]
+  have hrzero : nonceWord width bound gate index coins false z = List.ofFn (s.2.1 i a').1 := by
+    rw [nonceWord_eq, hindex]
+    simp only [Bool.toNat_false, Nat.zero_mul, Nat.add_zero]
+    apply congrArg List.ofFn
+    funext t
+    exact (hr₀ a' t).symm
+  have hrone : nonceWord width bound gate index coins true z = List.ofFn (s.2.1 i a').2 := by
+    rw [nonceWord_eq, hindex]
+    simp only [Bool.toNat_true, Nat.one_mul]
+    apply congrArg List.ofFn
+    funext t
+    exact (hr₁ a' t).symm
+  rw [rowWordWith_eq]
+  dsimp only
+  rw [hkeys, hout, hrzero, hrone]
+  apply Garbling.nestedWordEnc_vectors
+  rw [argKeys_eq, List.length_ofFn]
+  exact hfan
+
+
+/-- The raw row machine equals the garbled row selected by a supplied XOR row permutation,
+when the gate-description fields and the key/nonce block equations hold.
+The contract is generic in the circuit and seed. [PS10, Protocol 162.6; BMR90]
+`rowWord E₁ E₂ width bound inputs gate kind args coins (C.gates[i]).args.length a z = garbledRow
+(nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ (width z)) C s i (s.2.2 i a)`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** The permutation equation identifies the two raw assignment bits with the
+first two bits of the permuted assignment. The kind-code equation then identifies the raw
+output bit with the gate truth function. Apply the arbitrary-output-bit row contract, which
+already identifies every key and nonce window, and read the public garbled-row equation. -/
+theorem rowWord_garbledRow (E₁ E₂ : List Bool → List Bool)
+    (width bound inputs gate : List Bool → ℕ) (kind args coins : List Bool → List Bool)
+    (z : List Bool) (C : DAGCircuit (inputs z))
+    (s : GarbleSeed C (Fin (width z) → Bool)
+      ((Fin (width z) → Bool) × (Fin (width z) → Bool)))
+    (i : Fin C.gates.length) (a : Fin (C.gates[i]).args.length → Bool)
+    (hfan : (C.gates[i]).args.length ≤ 2) (hgate : gate z = i)
+    (hkind : kind z = (C.gates[i]).kind.encode)
+    (hargs : args z = BinArith.encWords ((C.gates[i]).args.map fun v => List.replicate v true))
+    (hkey : ∀ (v : ℕ), v < C.size → ∀ b,
+      keyAt C s v b = fun t : Fin (width z) =>
+        (coins z).getD ((b.toNat * bound z + v) * width z + t) false)
+    (hr₀ : ∀ a t, (s.2.1 i a).1 t = (coins z).getD
+      (2 * bound z * width z +
+        (4 * gate z + (List.ofFn a).foldr (fun b k => b.toNat + 2 * k) 0) *
+          (2 * width z) + t) false)
+    (hr₁ : ∀ a t, (s.2.1 i a).2 t = (coins z).getD
+      (2 * bound z * width z +
+        (4 * gate z + (List.ofFn a).foldr (fun b k => b.toNat + 2 * k) 0) *
+          (2 * width z) + width z + t) false)
+    (hperm : ∀ a t, s.2.2 i a t = xor (a t)
+      ((coins z).getD (10 * bound z * width z + 2 * gate z + t) false)) :
+    rowWord E₁ E₂ width bound inputs gate kind args coins (C.gates[i]).args.length a z =
+      garbledRow (nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ (width z)) C s i (s.2.2 i a) := by
+  let b₀ := assignmentBit width bound gate coins (C.gates[i]).args.length a 0
+  let b₁ := assignmentBit width bound gate coins (C.gates[i]).args.length a 1
+  let a' := s.2.2 i a
+  have hb₀ : b₀ z = (List.ofFn a').getD 0 false :=
+    assignmentBit_of_perm width bound gate coins _ a (s.2.2 i) z 0 (hperm a)
+  have hb₁ : b₁ z = (List.ofFn a').getD 1 false :=
+    assignmentBit_of_perm width bound gate coins _ a (s.2.2 i) z 1 (hperm a)
+  have houtput := gateBit_eq_gateOut (C.gates[i]) a' kind b₀ b₁ z hfan hkind hb₀ hb₁
+  change rowWordWith E₁ E₂ width bound inputs gate args coins
+    (gateBit kind (C.gates[i]).args.length b₀ b₁) (C.gates[i]).args.length a z = _
+  rw [rowWordWith_enc E₁ E₂ width bound inputs gate args coins
+    (gateBit kind (C.gates[i]).args.length b₀ b₁) z C s i a hfan hgate hargs
+    hkey hr₀ hr₁ hperm]
+  rw [houtput, garbledRow_eq]
+
+
+/-- A generic raw row depends only on the data maps and output bit at its input:
+freezing every map to its value at `z` and running at `[]` gives the same encrypted row.
+`rowWordWith E₁ E₂ width bound inputs gate args coins outputBit l a z = rowWordWith E₁ E₂ (fun _ =>
+width z) (fun _ => bound z) (fun _ => inputs z) (fun _ => gate z) (fun _ => args z) (fun _ => coins
+z) (fun _ => outputBit z) l a []`. -/
+theorem rowWordWith_value (E₁ E₂ : List Bool → List Bool)
+    (width bound inputs gate : List Bool → ℕ) (args coins : List Bool → List Bool)
+    (outputBit : List Bool → Bool) (l : ℕ) (a : Fin l → Bool) (z : List Bool) :
+    rowWordWith E₁ E₂ width bound inputs gate args coins outputBit l a z =
+      rowWordWith E₁ E₂ (fun _ => width z) (fun _ => bound z)
+        (fun _ => inputs z) (fun _ => gate z) (fun _ => args z) (fun _ => coins z)
+        (fun _ => outputBit z) l a [] := by
+  rw [rowWordWith_eq, rowWordWith_eq]
+  simp only [keyWord_eq, nonceWord_eq, assignmentBit_eq]
+
+/-- A raw row depends only on the supplied data maps' values at its input:
+freezing each map to its value at `z` and running at `[]` gives the same row.
+This exposes pointwise field normalization without reducing any foreign implementation.
+`rowWord E₁ E₂ width bound inputs gate kind args coins l a z = rowWord E₁ E₂ (fun _ => width z) (fun
+_ => bound z) (fun _ => inputs z) (fun _ => gate z) (fun _ => kind z) (fun _ => args z) (fun _ =>
+coins z) l a []`. -/
+theorem rowWord_value (E₁ E₂ : List Bool → List Bool)
+    (width bound inputs gate : List Bool → ℕ) (kind args coins : List Bool → List Bool)
+    (l : ℕ) (a : Fin l → Bool) (z : List Bool) :
+    rowWord E₁ E₂ width bound inputs gate kind args coins l a z =
+      rowWord E₁ E₂ (fun _ => width z) (fun _ => bound z)
+        (fun _ => inputs z) (fun _ => gate z) (fun _ => kind z)
+        (fun _ => args z) (fun _ => coins z) l a [] := by
+  rw [rowWord_eq, rowWord_eq]
+  simp only [keyWord_eq, nonceWord_eq, assignmentBit_eq, gateBit_eq]
+
+end TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof.RowFromCoins

+/-

+Released under Apache 2.0 license as described in the file LICENSE.

+import TCSlib.Complexity.CircuitComplexity.DAGCircuit
+import TCSlib.Complexity.ClassP.BinaryArith.Words
+import TCSlib.Complexity.Randomized.Repetition
+
+set_option maxHeartbeats 0
+set_option relaxedAutoImplicit false
+set_option autoImplicit false
+
+/-!
+# A polynomial-time host for data-dependent gate evaluation
+
+A gate traversal retains its input and a state consisting of the unprocessed gate records
+and the current encoded vertex data. Its leaf routine receives the retained input, one gate
+record and the vertex data, and returns the vertex data extended by the gate's result.
+The host is generic in the gate records, the vertex-data encoding and the leaf routine.
+
+Unlike the CVAL decider, this host returns the entire encoded value list and permits a
+polynomial-time, input-dependent gate operation. It uses the registered state-loop host;
+there is no new transition table.
+
+## Main definitions
+
+* `RunWithHost.step`: one gate-record traversal step.
+* `RunWithHost.acc`: the test that no gate records remain.
+
+## Main results
+
+* `RunWithHost.orbit_eq`: the traversal state after a gate prefix.
+* `RunWithHost.polyTimeComputable_runWith`: polynomial time under a polynomial state envelope.
+* `RunWithHost.exists_machine`: one finite machine and a uniform polynomial time envelope.
+
+## References
+
+* [AB09] S. Arora, B. Barak, *Computational Complexity: A Modern Approach*,
+  Cambridge University Press, 2009, §6.1 (topologically ordered circuits).
+* [PS10] R. Pass, a. shelat, *A Course in Cryptography*, lecture notes, 3rd ed.,
+  January 2010, Protocol 162.6 (garbled evaluation).
+-/
+
+namespace TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof.RunWithHost
+
+open Complexity Turing BoolCircuit
+
+/-- The traversal step sends `⟨x, ⟨records, values⟩⟩` to
+`⟨tail records, F ⟨x, ⟨head records, values⟩⟩⟩`.
+The outer retained input is maintained by the state-loop host.
+`step F w = pairEncode (pairSndD (pairFstD (pairSndD w))) (F (pairEncode (pairFstD w) (pairEncode
+(pairFstD (pairFstD (pairSndD w))) (pairSndD (pairSndD w)))))`. -/
+def step (F : List Bool → List Bool) (w : List Bool) : List Bool :=
+  pairEncode (pairSndD (pairFstD (pairSndD w)))
+    (F (pairEncode (pairFstD w)
+      (pairEncode (pairFstD (pairFstD (pairSndD w))) (pairSndD (pairSndD w)))))
+
+/-- For every word `w`, `step F w` drops its first gate record and calls `F` on
+`⟨fst w, ⟨head records, values⟩⟩`.
+`step F w = pairEncode (pairSndD (pairFstD (pairSndD w))) (F (pairEncode (pairFstD w) (pairEncode
+(pairFstD (pairFstD (pairSndD w))) (pairSndD (pairSndD w)))))`. -/
+theorem step_eq (F : List Bool → List Bool) (w : List Bool) :
+    step F w = pairEncode (pairSndD (pairFstD (pairSndD w)))
+      (F (pairEncode (pairFstD w)
+        (pairEncode (pairFstD (pairFstD (pairSndD w))) (pairSndD (pairSndD w))))) := rfl
+
+/-- The traversal accepts exactly when its remaining record list is empty:
+`acc w = decide (fst (snd w) = [])`. -/
+def acc (w : List Bool) : Bool := decide (pairFstD (pairSndD w) = [])
+
+/-- For every word `w`, `acc w = decide (pairFstD (pairSndD w) = [])`. -/
+theorem acc_eq (w : List Bool) : acc w = decide (pairFstD (pairSndD w) = []) := rfl
+
+/-- If `F` is polynomial-time, then `step F` is polynomial-time; it consists of
+pair projections, two pairings and one call to `F`.
+`PolyTimeComputable (step F)`. -/
+theorem step_poly {F : List Bool → List Bool} (hF : PolyTimeComputable F) :
+    PolyTimeComputable (step F) :=
+  ((polyTimeComputable_pairSndD.comp
+      (polyTimeComputable_pairFstD.comp polyTimeComputable_pairSndD)).pairEncode
+    (hF.comp (polyTimeComputable_pairFstD.pairEncode
+      ((polyTimeComputable_pairFstD.comp
+          (polyTimeComputable_pairFstD.comp polyTimeComputable_pairSndD)).pairEncode
+        (polyTimeComputable_pairSndD.comp polyTimeComputable_pairSndD)))))
+
+/-- The one-bit acceptance test `w ↦ [acc w]` is polynomial-time.
+`PolyTimeComputable fun w => [acc w]`. -/
+theorem acc_poly : PolyTimeComputable fun w => [acc w] :=
+  polyTimeComputable_isNil.comp
+    (polyTimeComputable_pairFstD.comp polyTimeComputable_pairSndD)
+
+/-- After `i ≤ |gs x|` rounds, the state is the unprocessed gate suffix paired with
+`valueCode x (runWith (f x) ((gs x).take i) (init x))`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** At round zero the prefix is empty. At a successor round the head
+of the remaining encoded list is the next gate record. The leaf contract appends
+its computed value, and the append and singleton equations of `runWith` identify
+the result with evaluation of the next prefix. -/
+theorem orbit_eq {β : List Bool → Type} (gateCode : DAGGate → List Bool)
+    (valueCode : ∀ x, List (β x) → List Bool) (gs : List Bool → List DAGGate)
+    (init : ∀ x, List (β x)) (f : ∀ x, DAGGate → List (β x) → β x)
+    (F : List Bool → List Bool)
+    (hnext : ∀ x g vs,
+      F (pairEncode x (pairEncode (gateCode g) (valueCode x vs))) =
+        valueCode x (vs ++ [f x g vs])) (x : List Bool) :
+    ∀ i, i ≤ (gs x).length →
+      StateLoop.orbit
+          (fun z => pairEncode (BinArith.encWords ((gs z).map gateCode)) (valueCode z (init z)))
+          (step F) x i =
+        pairEncode (BinArith.encWords (((gs x).drop i).map gateCode))
+          (valueCode x (runWith (f x) ((gs x).take i) (init x))) := by
+  intro i
+  induction i with
+  | zero =>
+    intro _
+    rw [StateLoop.orbit_zero, List.drop_zero, List.take_zero, runWith_nil]
+  | succ i ih =>
+    intro hi
+    have hilt : i < (gs x).length := by omega
+    rw [StateLoop.orbit_succ, ih (by omega), step_eq]
+    simp only [pairFstD_pairEncode, pairSndD_pairEncode]
+    rw [List.drop_eq_getElem_cons hilt, List.map_cons, BinArith.encWords_cons]
+    simp only [pairFstD_pairEncode, pairSndD_pairEncode]
+    rw [hnext, List.take_succ_eq_append_getElem hilt, runWith_append, runWith_singleton]
+
+/-- Evaluating `gs x` from `init x` and returning its `valueCode` is polynomial-time
+when the encoded input maps and leaf routine are polynomial-time, the gate count
+is at most `R |x|`, and every encoded prefix state has length at most `B |x|`.
+The leaf receives `⟨x, ⟨gateCode g, valueCode x vs⟩⟩` and returns
+`valueCode x (vs ++ [f x g vs])`. [PS10, Protocol 162.6]
+`PolyTimeComputable fun x => valueCode x (runWith (f x) (gs x) (init x))`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** Use the public state-loop host with the unprocessed gate records
+and current vertex data as state. The orbit equation gives the caller's length
+bound. Before the last round the encoded suffix contains a pair, so it is nonempty;
+at the gate count it is empty. Thus the loop stops at exactly the gate count and
+its second projection is the encoded result of `runWith`. -/
+theorem polyTimeComputable_runWith {β : List Bool → Type} (gateCode : DAGGate → List Bool)
+    (valueCode : ∀ x, List (β x) → List Bool) (gs : List Bool → List DAGGate)
+    (init : ∀ x, List (β x)) (f : ∀ x, DAGGate → List (β x) → β x)
+    {F : List Bool → List Bool}
+    (hgs : PolyTimeComputable fun x => BinArith.encWords ((gs x).map gateCode))
+    (hinit : PolyTimeComputable fun x => valueCode x (init x)) (hF : PolyTimeComputable F)
+    (hnext : ∀ x g vs,
+      F (pairEncode x (pairEncode (gateCode g) (valueCode x vs))) =
+        valueCode x (vs ++ [f x g vs])) (R B : PolyExpr)
+    (hN : ∀ x, (gs x).length ≤ R.eval x.length)
+    (hbound : ∀ x i, i ≤ (gs x).length →
+      (pairEncode (BinArith.encWords (((gs x).drop i).map gateCode))
+        (valueCode x (runWith (f x) ((gs x).take i) (init x)))).length ≤ B.eval x.length) :
+    PolyTimeComputable fun x => valueCode x (runWith (f x) (gs x) (init x)) := by
+  have h := polyTimeComputable_stateFind (hgs.pairEncode hinit) (step_poly hF)
+    acc_poly (polyTimeComputable_pairSndD.comp polyTimeComputable_pairSndD)
+    R B (fun x => (gs x).length) hN
+    (fun x i hi => by
+      rw [orbit_eq gateCode valueCode gs init f F hnext x i hi]
+      exact hbound x i hi)
+    (fun x i hi => by
+      rw [orbit_eq gateCode valueCode gs init f F hnext x i hi.le, acc_eq]
+      simp only [pairFstD_pairEncode, pairSndD_pairEncode]
+      rw [List.drop_eq_getElem_cons hi, List.map_cons, BinArith.encWords_cons]
+      apply decide_eq_false_iff_not.mpr
+      exact pairEncode_ne_nil _ _)
+    (fun x => by
+      rw [orbit_eq gateCode valueCode gs init f F hnext x _ le_rfl, acc_eq]
+      simp only [pairSndD_pairEncode, pairFstD_pairEncode, List.drop_length,
+        List.map_nil, BinArith.encWords_nil, decide_true])
+  convert h using 1
+  funext x
+  rw [orbit_eq gateCode valueCode gs init f F hnext x _ le_rfl]
+  simp only [Function.comp_apply, pairSndD_pairEncode, List.take_length]
+
+/-- There is one clean evaluation machine `M` with a polynomial budget
+`A (|arg| + 1)^d`, installing `valueCode arg (runWith (f arg) (gs arg) (init arg))`
+and emitting nothing. At every time every work head of that same machine is
+within the budget of the origin. The hypotheses are those of
+`polyTimeComputable_runWith`. [PS10, Protocol 162.6]
+`∃ (M : FinTM Bool) (A d : ℕ), ModuleHost.Clean M (fun x => valueCode x (runWith (f x) (gs x) (init
+x))) (fun _ => []) (fun n => A * (n + 1) ^ d) ∧ ∀ (x arg : List Bool) (v : ℕ) (j : Fin M.k),
+|(M.tm.runFrom (Cfg.ofWords (input := x) M.tm.q₀ (stateWord M.k arg)) v).workTapePos j| ≤ (A *
+(arg.length + 1) ^ d : ℕ)`.
+
+The machine is obtained by applying the registered H14 clean-install contract to
+the traversal's polynomial-time function; its head envelope is H14's `Clean.envelope`. -/
+theorem exists_machine {β : List Bool → Type} (gateCode : DAGGate → List Bool)
+    (valueCode : ∀ x, List (β x) → List Bool) (gs : List Bool → List DAGGate)
+    (init : ∀ x, List (β x)) (f : ∀ x, DAGGate → List (β x) → β x)
+    {F : List Bool → List Bool}
+    (hgs : PolyTimeComputable fun x => BinArith.encWords ((gs x).map gateCode))
+    (hinit : PolyTimeComputable fun x => valueCode x (init x)) (hF : PolyTimeComputable F)
+    (hnext : ∀ x g vs,
+      F (pairEncode x (pairEncode (gateCode g) (valueCode x vs))) =
+        valueCode x (vs ++ [f x g vs])) (R B : PolyExpr)
+    (hN : ∀ x, (gs x).length ≤ R.eval x.length)
+    (hbound : ∀ x i, i ≤ (gs x).length →
+      (pairEncode (BinArith.encWords (((gs x).drop i).map gateCode))
+        (valueCode x (runWith (f x) ((gs x).take i) (init x)))).length ≤ B.eval x.length) :
+    ∃ (M : FinTM Bool) (A d : ℕ),
+      ModuleHost.Clean M (fun x => valueCode x (runWith (f x) (gs x) (init x)))
+          (fun _ => []) (fun n => A * (n + 1) ^ d) ∧
+        ∀ (x arg : List Bool) (v : ℕ) (j : Fin M.k),
+          |(M.tm.runFrom
+            (Cfg.ofWords (input := x) M.tm.q₀ (stateWord M.k arg)) v).workTapePos j| ≤
+              (A * (arg.length + 1) ^ d : ℕ) := by
+  obtain ⟨M, A, d, hM⟩ := ModuleHost.clean_install
+    (polyTimeComputable_runWith gateCode valueCode gs init f hgs hinit hF hnext R B hN hbound)
+  exact ⟨M, A, d, hM, fun x arg v j => hM.envelope x arg v j⟩
+
+end TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof.RunWithHost

+/-
+Copyright (c) 2026 TCSlib contributors. All rights reserved.
+Released under Apache 2.0 license as described in the file LICENSE.
+-/
+import TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.Yao.HybridProof.CoinSeedRelabel
+import TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.Yao.HybridProof.GateHybridMessage
+import TCSlib.Cryptography.Pseudorandom.Indistinguishability
+
+set_option maxHeartbeats 0
+set_option relaxedAutoImplicit false
+set_option autoImplicit false
+
+/-!
+# Exact sampler transport at the gate-hybrid endpoints
+
+The real sampler and its encoded-view equation are parameters. Its seed has
+the four addressed tape readers. The proved tape relabeling and per-seed view
+identity therefore identify its acceptance probability with stage zero.
+The last stage is the existing canonical fake view, with prescribed result
+bits. This module defines no protocol, real message, or probability measure.
+
+## Main definitions
+
+None.
+
+## Main results
+
+* `real_eq_stage0_on_tape`: the pointwise complete-tape relabeling identity.
+* `acceptProb_real_eq_stage0`: exact acceptance equality for every distinguisher.
+* `acceptProb_real_eq_stage0_fst`: the same equality with an ignored Bob tape.
+* `sample_end_of_result`: the exact prescribed-result canonical fake endpoint.
+* `acceptProb_end_eq_ideal`: exact final acceptance transport through any coin bijection.
+
+## References
+
+* [PS10] R. Pass, a. shelat, *A Course in Cryptography*, lecture notes, 3rd ed.,
+  January 2010, Protocol 162.6 and Theorem 163.7 (the Bob-view hybrid).
+* [Gar26] S. Garg, *A Course in Theory of Cryptography*, lecture notes,
+  UC Berkeley, draft of 22 January 2026, §3.2, Lemmas 3.2 and 3.3.
+-/
+
+namespace TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof.SamplerEndpoints
+
+open Complexity Turing BoolCircuit TCSlib.Cryptography
+
+section Tape
+
+variable {inputs : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit inputs) (S n : ℕ)
+  (hA : ∀ i : Fin C.gates.length, (C.gates[i]).args.length ≤ 2) (v : ℕ → Bool)
+  (hS : C.size ≤ S)
+  (seed : List Bool → GarbleSeed C (Fin n → Bool) ((Fin n → Bool) × (Fin n → Bool)))
+  (hK : ∀ q (w : Fin C.size) b (t : Fin n),
+    (seed q).1 w b t = q.getD ((b.toNat * S + w) * n + t) false)
+  (hR₀ : ∀ q (i : Fin C.gates.length) (a : Fin (C.gates[i]).args.length → Bool)
+      (t : Fin n),
+    ((seed q).2.1 i a).1 t = q.getD (2 * S * n + (4 * i +
+      (List.ofFn a).foldr (fun b k => b.toNat + 2 * k) 0) * (2 * n) + t) false)
+  (hR₁ : ∀ q (i : Fin C.gates.length) (a : Fin (C.gates[i]).args.length → Bool)
+      (t : Fin n),
+    ((seed q).2.1 i a).2 t = q.getD (2 * S * n + (4 * i +
+      (List.ofFn a).foldr (fun b k => b.toNat + 2 * k) 0) * (2 * n) + n + t) false)
+  (hP : ∀ q (i : Fin C.gates.length) (a : Fin (C.gates[i]).args.length → Bool)
+      (t : Fin (C.gates[i]).args.length),
+    (seed q).2.2 i a t = xor (a t) (q.getD (10 * S * n + 2 * i + t) false))
+  (M : MultiEnc (Fin n → Bool) (List Bool) ((Fin n → Bool) × (Fin n → Bool)))
+  (inA inB outLen : ℕ) (output : Fin outLen → ℕ)
+  (commonInput bobInput bobCoins aliceOutput : List Bool)
+  (real : (Fin (10 * S * n + 2 * S) → Bool) → List Bool)
+  (hReal : ∀ r, real r =
+    pairEncode (⟨commonInput,
+      pairEncode bobInput (List.ofFn fun t : Fin (inB * n) =>
+        keyAt C (seed (List.ofFn r)) (inA + t.divNat) (v (inA + t.divNat)) t.modNat),
+      bobCoins,
+      [pairEncode (BinArith.encWords (List.ofFn fun i =>
+          BinArith.encWords (garbledTable M C (seed (List.ofFn r)) i)))
+        (pairEncode
+          (BinArith.encWords (List.ofFn fun w : Fin inA =>
+            List.ofFn (keyAt C (seed (List.ofFn r)) w (v w))))
+          (BinArith.encWords (List.ofFn fun j =>
+            pairEncode (List.ofFn (keyAt C (seed (List.ofFn r)) (output j) false))
+              (List.ofFn (keyAt C (seed (List.ofFn r)) (output j) true)))))]⟩ : View).encode
+      aliceOutput)
+
+include hA hS hK hR₀ hR₁ hP hReal
+
+/-- The supplied real sampler equals stage zero on the explicitly relabeled
+complete tape, using its four seed readers and encoded-view equation.
+The tape has length `10*S*n+2*S`, including when `n=0`. [PS10, Theorem 163.7] -/
+theorem real_eq_stage0_on_tape (r : Fin (10 * S * n + 2 * S) → Bool) :
+    real r = GateHybridMessage.sample M C
+      (seed (List.ofFn (CoinRelabel.relabel S n (CoinSeedRelabel.keyMask C S v)
+        (CoinSeedRelabel.rowPerm C S hA v) (CoinSeedRelabel.shiftMask C S v) r)))
+      inA inB outLen output v commonInput bobInput bobCoins aliceOutput 0 := by
+  rw [hReal]
+  rw [CoinSeedRelabel.seed_relabel C S n hA v hS seed hK hR₀ hR₁ hP r]
+  exact GateHybridMessage.sample_relabel M C (seed (List.ofFn r)) inA inB outLen
+    output v commonInput bobInput bobCoins aliceOutput
+
+/-- Every randomized distinguisher has exactly the same acceptance probability
+on the supplied real sampler and the uniform stage-zero sampler. This is the
+published uniform change-of-coins lemma specialized to the proved tape bijection.
+[PS10, Theorem 163.7; Gar26, §3.2]
+`acceptProb real D = acceptProb (fun r : Fin (10 * S * n + 2 * S) → Bool => GateHybridMessage.sample
+M C (seed (List.ofFn r)) inA inB outLen output v commonInput bobInput bobCoins aliceOutput 0) D`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** The pointwise reader identifies the real sampler with stage
+zero precomposed by the complete-tape permutation. Apply the existing uniform
+reseeding equation to that permutation and the supplied distinguisher. -/
+theorem acceptProb_real_eq_stage0 {κ : Type*} [Fintype κ]
+    (D : List Bool → κ → Bool) :
+    acceptProb real D = acceptProb
+      (fun r : Fin (10 * S * n + 2 * S) → Bool =>
+        GateHybridMessage.sample M C (seed (List.ofFn r)) inA inB outLen output v
+          commonInput bobInput bobCoins aliceOutput 0) D := by
+  have h : real = fun r => GateHybridMessage.sample M C
+      (seed (List.ofFn (CoinRelabel.relabel S n (CoinSeedRelabel.keyMask C S v)
+        (CoinSeedRelabel.rowPerm C S hA v) (CoinSeedRelabel.shiftMask C S v) r)))
+      inA inB outLen output v commonInput bobInput bobCoins aliceOutput 0 := by
+    funext r
+    exact real_eq_stage0_on_tape C S n hA v hS seed hK hR₀ hR₁ hP M
+      inA inB outLen output commonInput bobInput bobCoins aliceOutput real hReal r
+  rw [h]
+  exact acceptProb_comp_equiv
+    (CoinRelabel.relabel S n (CoinSeedRelabel.keyMask C S v)
+      (CoinSeedRelabel.rowPerm C S hA v) (CoinSeedRelabel.shiftMask C S v))
+    (fun r => GateHybridMessage.sample M C (seed (List.ofFn r)) inA inB outLen output v
+      commonInput bobInput bobCoins aliceOutput 0) D
+
+/-- An independent Bob tape ignored by the real sampler can be dropped before
+the same stage-zero acceptance equality. This directly reuses `acceptProb_fst`.
+[PS10, Theorem 163.7; Gar26, §3.2]
+`acceptProb (fun r : (Fin (10 * S * n + 2 * S) → Bool) × τ => real r.1) D = acceptProb (fun r : Fin
+(10 * S * n + 2 * S) → Bool => GateHybridMessage.sample M C (seed (List.ofFn r)) inA inB outLen
+output v commonInput bobInput bobCoins aliceOutput 0) D`. -/
+theorem acceptProb_real_eq_stage0_fst {τ κ : Type*} [Fintype τ] [Nonempty τ]
+    [Fintype κ] (D : List Bool → κ → Bool) :
+    acceptProb (fun r : (Fin (10 * S * n + 2 * S) → Bool) × τ => real r.1) D =
+      acceptProb (fun r : Fin (10 * S * n + 2 * S) → Bool =>
+        GateHybridMessage.sample M C (seed (List.ofFn r)) inA inB outLen output v
+          commonInput bobInput bobCoins aliceOutput 0) D := by
+  rw [acceptProb_fst]
+  exact acceptProb_real_eq_stage0 C S n hA v hS seed hK hR₀ hR₁ hP M
+    inA inB outLen output commonInput bobInput bobCoins aliceOutput real hReal D
+
+/-- The supplied real and stage-zero samplers have zero distinguishing
+advantage for every randomized distinguisher. [PS10, Theorem 163.7]
+`distAdv real (fun r : Fin (10 * S * n + 2 * S) → Bool => GateHybridMessage.sample M C (seed
+(List.ofFn r)) inA inB outLen output v commonInput bobInput bobCoins aliceOutput 0) D = 0`. -/
+theorem distAdv_real_stage0_zero {κ : Type*} [Fintype κ]
+    (D : List Bool → κ → Bool) :
+    distAdv real (fun r : Fin (10 * S * n + 2 * S) → Bool =>
+      GateHybridMessage.sample M C (seed (List.ofFn r)) inA inB outLen output v
+        commonInput bobInput bobCoins aliceOutput 0) D = 0 := by
+  rw [distAdv_def, acceptProb_real_eq_stage0 C S n hA v hS seed hK hR₀ hR₁ hP M
+    inA inB outLen output commonInput bobInput bobCoins aliceOutput real hReal D,
+    sub_self, abs_zero]
+
+end Tape
+
+variable {inputs width : ℕ} {ρ : Type}
+
+/-- When the output-wire values are the prescribed result bits, the last-stage
+sample is the canonical fake view paired with the prescribed Alice output.
+This is a pointwise identity and does not assume any simulator or coin type.
+[PS10, Theorem 163.7]
+`GateHybridMessage.sample M C s inA inB outLen output v commonInput bobInput bobCoins aliceOutput
+(2 * C.gates.length) = pairEncode (⟨commonInput, pairEncode bobInput (List.ofFn
+(FakeObjects.activeAnswers C s inA inB)), bobCoins, [FakeObjects.message M C s inA outLen output
+result]⟩ : View).encode aliceOutput`. -/
+theorem sample_end_of_result (M : MultiEnc (Fin width → Bool) (List Bool) ρ)
+    (C : DAGCircuit inputs) (s : GarbleSeed C (Fin width → Bool) ρ)
+    (inA inB outLen : ℕ) (output : Fin outLen → ℕ) (v : ℕ → Bool)
+    (result : Fin outLen → Bool) (hResult : ∀ j, v (output j) = result j)
+    (commonInput bobInput bobCoins aliceOutput : List Bool) :
+    GateHybridMessage.sample M C s inA inB outLen output v
+        commonInput bobInput bobCoins aliceOutput (2 * C.gates.length) =
+      pairEncode (⟨commonInput,
+        pairEncode bobInput (List.ofFn (FakeObjects.activeAnswers C s inA inB)),
+        bobCoins, [FakeObjects.message M C s inA outLen output result]⟩ : View).encode
+        aliceOutput := by
+  rw [GateHybridMessage.sample_end, funext hResult]
+
+/-- The final-stage sampler has exactly the acceptance probability of the
+canonical fake view over the same seed family. [PS10, Theorem 163.7]
+`acceptProb (fun r => GateHybridMessage.sample M C (seed r) inA inB outLen output v commonInput
+bobInput bobCoins aliceOutput (2 * C.gates.length)) D = acceptProb (fun r => pairEncode
+(⟨commonInput, pairEncode bobInput (List.ofFn (FakeObjects.activeAnswers C (seed r) inA inB)),
+bobCoins, [FakeObjects.message M C (seed r) inA outLen output result]⟩ : View).encode aliceOutput)
+D`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** Apply the pointwise final-stage equation at every seed, then
+transport the resulting function equality through the existing acceptance
+functional. No summation or probability identity is re-derived. -/
+theorem acceptProb_end_eq_fake {σ κ : Type*} [Fintype σ] [Fintype κ]
+    (M : MultiEnc (Fin width → Bool) (List Bool) ρ) (C : DAGCircuit inputs)
+    (seed : σ → GarbleSeed C (Fin width → Bool) ρ)
+    (inA inB outLen : ℕ) (output : Fin outLen → ℕ) (v : ℕ → Bool)
+    (result : Fin outLen → Bool) (hResult : ∀ j, v (output j) = result j)
+    (commonInput bobInput bobCoins aliceOutput : List Bool) (D : List Bool → κ → Bool) :
+    acceptProb (fun r => GateHybridMessage.sample M C (seed r) inA inB outLen output v
+        commonInput bobInput bobCoins aliceOutput (2 * C.gates.length)) D =
+      acceptProb (fun r => pairEncode (⟨commonInput,
+        pairEncode bobInput (List.ofFn (FakeObjects.activeAnswers C (seed r) inA inB)),
+        bobCoins, [FakeObjects.message M C (seed r) inA outLen output result]⟩ : View).encode
+        aliceOutput) D := by
+  apply congrArg (fun X : σ → List Bool => acceptProb X D)
+  funext r
+  exact sample_end_of_result M C (seed r) inA inB outLen output v result hResult
+    commonInput bobInput bobCoins aliceOutput
+
+section Ideal
+
+variable {σ τ : Type*} [Fintype σ] [Fintype τ]
+  (M : MultiEnc (Fin width → Bool) (List Bool) ρ) (C : DAGCircuit inputs)
+  (seed : σ → GarbleSeed C (Fin width → Bool) ρ)
+  (inA inB outLen : ℕ) (output : Fin outLen → ℕ) (v : ℕ → Bool)
+  (result : Fin outLen → Bool) (hResult : ∀ j, v (output j) = result j)
+  (commonInput bobInput bobCoins aliceOutput : List Bool) (e : σ ≃ τ)
+  (ideal : τ → List Bool)
+  (hIdeal : ∀ r, ideal (e r) = pairEncode (⟨commonInput,
+    pairEncode bobInput (List.ofFn (FakeObjects.activeAnswers C (seed r) inA inB)),
+    bobCoins, [FakeObjects.message M C (seed r) inA outLen output result]⟩ : View).encode
+    aliceOutput)
+
+include hResult hIdeal
+
+/-- A canonical fake-view equation for an ideal sampler, read through any coin
+bijection `e`, identifies its acceptance probability with the last hybrid stage.
+Only the published pointwise endpoint and uniform reseeding lemma are used.
+[PS10, Theorem 163.7; Gar26, §3.2]
+`acceptProb (fun r => GateHybridMessage.sample M C (seed r) inA inB outLen output v commonInput
+bobInput bobCoins aliceOutput (2 * C.gates.length)) D = acceptProb ideal D`. -/
+theorem acceptProb_end_eq_ideal {κ : Type*} [Fintype κ] (D : List Bool → κ → Bool) :
+    acceptProb (fun r => GateHybridMessage.sample M C (seed r) inA inB outLen output v
+      commonInput bobInput bobCoins aliceOutput (2 * C.gates.length)) D =
+      acceptProb ideal D := by
+  calc
+    _ = acceptProb (fun r => ideal (e r)) D := by
+      apply congrArg (fun X : σ → List Bool => acceptProb X D)
+      funext r
+      exact (sample_end_of_result M C (seed r) inA inB outLen output v result hResult
+        commonInput bobInput bobCoins aliceOutput).trans (hIdeal r).symm
+    _ = acceptProb ideal D := acceptProb_comp_equiv e ideal D
+
+/-- The last hybrid stage and the supplied canonical ideal sampler have zero
+distinguishing advantage for every randomized distinguisher.
+This follows directly from their exact acceptance equality. [PS10, Theorem 163.7]
+`distAdv (fun r => GateHybridMessage.sample M C (seed r) inA inB outLen output v commonInput
+bobInput bobCoins aliceOutput (2 * C.gates.length)) ideal D = 0`. -/
+theorem distAdv_end_ideal_zero {κ : Type*} [Fintype κ] (D : List Bool → κ → Bool) :
+    distAdv (fun r => GateHybridMessage.sample M C (seed r) inA inB outLen output v
+      commonInput bobInput bobCoins aliceOutput (2 * C.gates.length)) ideal D = 0 := by
+  rw [distAdv_def, acceptProb_end_eq_ideal M C seed inA inB outLen output v result
+    hResult commonInput bobInput bobCoins aliceOutput e ideal hIdeal D, sub_self, abs_zero]
+
+end Ideal
+
+end TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof.SamplerEndpoints
