+/-
+Copyright (c) 2026 TCSlib contributors. All rights reserved.
+Released under Apache 2.0 license as described in the file LICENSE.
+-/
+import TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.Yao.HybridProof.NonceCoordinates
+
+set_option maxHeartbeats 0
+set_option relaxedAutoImplicit false
+set_option autoImplicit false
+
+/-!
+# Coordinates of a garbling seed and its selected challenge
+
+Existing product, sum, currying and Boolean-function equivalences expose a garbling seed
+as its unchanged assignment permutation and one table of key/nonce values. The challenge
+is one selected key and a padded vector of row nonces. No equality on coordinate values
+is used; repeated actual values are allowed.
+
+## Main definitions
+
+* `SeedCoordinates.Permutations`, `Index`, `ChallengeIndex`: the coordinate types.
+* `SeedCoordinates.seedEquiv`, `challengeEquiv`: the exact coordinate equivalences.
+
+## Main results
+
+* Exact forward and inverse coordinate readers.
+
+## References
+
+* [PS10] R. Pass, a. shelat, *A Course in Cryptography*, lecture notes, 3rd ed., January
+  2010, Theorem 163.7 (fresh selected key and encryption coins).
+-/
+
+namespace TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof.SeedCoordinates
+
+open BoolCircuit
+
+/-- The unchanged seed component is the family of assignment permutations at every gate.
+`Permutations C = ((i : Fin C.gates.length) → Equiv.Perm (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length →
+Bool))`. -/
+abbrev Permutations {m : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit m) :=
+  (i : Fin C.gates.length) → Equiv.Perm (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool)
+
+/-- `Permutations C` is the dependent family of per-gate assignment permutations.
+`Permutations C = ((i : Fin C.gates.length) → Equiv.Perm (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length →
+Bool))`. -/
+theorem Permutations_eq {m : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit m) :
+    Permutations C =
+      ((i : Fin C.gates.length) → Equiv.Perm (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool)) := rfl
+
+/-- A full seed coordinate is either a vertex/bit key label or a row nonce coordinate.
+`Index C = ((Fin C.size × Bool) ⊕ NonceCoordinates.Index C)`. -/
+abbrev Index {m : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit m) :=
+  (Fin C.size × Bool) ⊕ NonceCoordinates.Index C
+
+/-- `Index C` is the sum of key labels and row-nonce coordinates.
+`Index C = ((Fin C.size × Bool) ⊕ NonceCoordinates.Index C)`. -/
+theorem Index_eq {m : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit m) :
+    Index C = ((Fin C.size × Bool) ⊕ NonceCoordinates.Index C) := rfl
+
+/-- Challenge coordinates consist of one selected key slot and `q+1` padded nonce slots.
+`ChallengeIndex q = (Unit ⊕ Fin (q + 1))`. -/
+abbrev ChallengeIndex (q : ℕ) := Unit ⊕ Fin (q + 1)
+
+/-- `ChallengeIndex q = Unit ⊕ Fin (q+1)`. -/
+theorem ChallengeIndex_eq (q : ℕ) : ChallengeIndex q = (Unit ⊕ Fin (q + 1)) := rfl
+
+/-- `Data C V` is the pair of the full two-key table and the full two-half nonce table.
+`Data C V = ((Fin C.size → Bool → V) × ((i : Fin C.gates.length) → (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length →
+Bool) → V × V))`. -/
+abbrev Data {m : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit m) (V : Type) :=
+  (Fin C.size → Bool → V) ×
+    ((i : Fin C.gates.length) → (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool) → V × V)
+
+/-- `Data C V` is the product of its key and nonce function tables.
+`Data C V = ((Fin C.size → Bool → V) × ((i : Fin C.gates.length) → (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length →
+Bool) → V × V))`. -/
+theorem Data_eq {m : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit m) (V : Type) :
+    Data C V = ((Fin C.size → Bool → V) ×
+      ((i : Fin C.gates.length) → (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool) → V × V)) := rfl
+
+/-- Existing currying, Boolean-function and sum-function equivalences identify the key/nonce
+value tables with one coordinate function. No permutation distribution is involved.
+`dataEquiv C V d = Sum.elim (fun label => d.1 label.1 label.2) (fun x => if x.2.2 then (d.2 x.1
+x.2.1).2 else (d.2 x.1 x.2.1).1)`. -/
+def dataEquiv {m : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit m) (V : Type) : Data C V ≃ (Index C → V) :=
+  let key := (Equiv.curry (Fin C.size) Bool V).symm
+  let nonce : ((i : Fin C.gates.length) →
+      (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool) → V × V) ≃ (NonceCoordinates.Index C → V) :=
+    ((Equiv.piCongrRight fun i => Equiv.piCongrRight fun _ :
+        (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool) => (Equiv.boolArrowEquivProd V).symm).trans
+      (Equiv.piCongrRight fun i =>
+        (Equiv.curry (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool) Bool V).symm)).trans
+      (Equiv.piCurry (fun (i : Fin C.gates.length)
+        (_ : (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool) × Bool) => V)).symm
+  (key.prodCongr nonce).trans (Equiv.sumArrowEquivProdArrow _ _ V).symm
+
+/-- The data-coordinate map reads the key table on key labels and the chosen nonce half.
+`dataEquiv C V d = Sum.elim (fun label => d.1 label.1 label.2) (fun x => if x.2.2 then (d.2 x.1
+x.2.1).2 else (d.2 x.1 x.2.1).1)`. -/
+theorem dataEquiv_eq {m : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit m) (V : Type) (d : Data C V) :
+    dataEquiv C V d = Sum.elim (fun label => d.1 label.1 label.2)
+      (fun x => if x.2.2 then (d.2 x.1 x.2.1).2 else (d.2 x.1 x.2.1).1) := rfl
+
+/-- The inverse data-coordinate map restores both key choices and both nonce halves.
+`(dataEquiv C V).symm f = ((fun v b => f (Sum.inl (v, b))), fun i a => (f (Sum.inr ⟨i, (a, false)⟩),
+f (Sum.inr ⟨i, (a, true)⟩)))`. -/
+theorem dataEquiv_symm {m : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit m) (V : Type) (f : Index C → V) :
+    (dataEquiv C V).symm f = ((fun v b => f (Sum.inl (v, b))),
+      fun i a => (f (Sum.inr ⟨i, (a, false)⟩), f (Sum.inr ⟨i, (a, true)⟩))) := rfl
+
+/-- The value-table equivalence retains any unchanged parameter `P`, such as actual XOR
+shifts and unused tape coordinates. No uniformity of a derived permutation is assumed.
+`paramEquiv C V P (p, d) = (p, dataEquiv C V d)`. -/
+def paramEquiv {m : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit m) (V P : Type) :
+    P × Data C V ≃ P × (Index C → V) := (Equiv.refl P).prodCongr (dataEquiv C V)
+
+/-- `paramEquiv` retains `p` and converts only the key/nonce data table.
+`paramEquiv C V P (p, d) = (p, dataEquiv C V d)`. -/
+theorem paramEquiv_eq {m : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit m) (V P : Type) (p : P) (d : Data C V) :
+    paramEquiv C V P (p, d) = (p, dataEquiv C V d) := rfl
+
+/-- The inverse parameterized map retains `p` and restores only its data table.
+`(paramEquiv C V P).symm (p, f) = (p, (dataEquiv C V).symm f)`. -/
+theorem paramEquiv_symm {m : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit m) (V P : Type) (p : P) (f : Index C → V) :
+    (paramEquiv C V P).symm (p, f) = (p, (dataEquiv C V).symm f) := rfl
+
+/-- A full abstract garbling seed is equivalent to its permutation family and key/nonce
+coordinate table. This auxiliary equivalence makes no claim that concrete XOR-shift tapes
+induce uniform samples over all permutations.
+`seedEquiv C V s = (s.2.2, Sum.elim (fun label => s.1 label.1 label.2) (fun x => if x.2.2 then
+(s.2.1 x.1 x.2.1).2 else (s.2.1 x.1 x.2.1).1))`. -/
+def seedEquiv {m : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit m) (V : Type) :
+    GarbleSeed C V (V × V) ≃ Permutations C × (Index C → V) :=
+  ((Equiv.prodAssoc _ _ _).symm.trans (Equiv.prodComm _ _)).trans
+    ((Equiv.refl _).prodCongr (dataEquiv C V))
+
+/-- `seedEquiv` retains the permutations and reads key labels or first/second row nonces.
+`seedEquiv C V s = (s.2.2, Sum.elim (fun label => s.1 label.1 label.2) (fun x => if x.2.2 then
+(s.2.1 x.1 x.2.1).2 else (s.2.1 x.1 x.2.1).1))`. -/
+theorem seedEquiv_eq {m : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit m) (V : Type) (s : GarbleSeed C V (V × V)) :
+    seedEquiv C V s = (s.2.2, Sum.elim (fun label => s.1 label.1 label.2)
+      (fun x => if x.2.2 then (s.2.1 x.1 x.2.1).2 else (s.2.1 x.1 x.2.1).1)) := rfl
+
+/-- The coordinate permutation family is exactly the original seed's permutation family.
+`(seedEquiv C V s).1 = s.2.2`. -/
+theorem seedEquiv_perm {m : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit m) (V : Type) (s : GarbleSeed C V (V × V)) :
+    (seedEquiv C V s).1 = s.2.2 := rfl
+
+/-- A key coordinate reads its original vertex/bit key.
+`(seedEquiv C V s).2 (Sum.inl (v, b)) = s.1 v b`. -/
+theorem seedEquiv_key {m : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit m) (V : Type) (s : GarbleSeed C V (V × V))
+    (v : Fin C.size) (b : Bool) : (seedEquiv C V s).2 (Sum.inl (v, b)) = s.1 v b := rfl
+
+/-- A nonce coordinate reads the first half for `false` and the second half for `true`.
+`(seedEquiv C V s).2 (Sum.inr ⟨i, (a, half)⟩) = if half then (s.2.1 i a).2 else (s.2.1 i a).1`. -/
+theorem seedEquiv_nonce {m : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit m) (V : Type) (s : GarbleSeed C V (V × V))
+    (i : Fin C.gates.length) (a : Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool) (half : Bool) :
+    (seedEquiv C V s).2 (Sum.inr ⟨i, (a, half)⟩) =
+      if half then (s.2.1 i a).2 else (s.2.1 i a).1 := rfl
+
+/-- The inverse coordinate map restores the two key choices, both nonce halves, and the
+unchanged permutation family.
+`(seedEquiv C V).symm (p, f) = ((fun v b => f (Sum.inl (v, b))), ((fun i a => (f (Sum.inr ⟨i, (a,
+false)⟩), f (Sum.inr ⟨i, (a, true)⟩))), p))`. -/
+theorem seedEquiv_symm {m : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit m) (V : Type)
+    (p : Permutations C) (f : Index C → V) :
+    (seedEquiv C V).symm (p, f) =
+      ((fun v b => f (Sum.inl (v, b))),
+        ((fun i a => (f (Sum.inr ⟨i, (a, false)⟩), f (Sum.inr ⟨i, (a, true)⟩))), p)) := rfl
+
+/-- A selected key and its padded nonce vector are one table on the sum of challenge slots.
+`challengeEquiv q V (k, r) = Sum.elim (fun _ => k) r`. -/
+def challengeEquiv (q : ℕ) (V : Type) :
+    V × (Fin (q + 1) → V) ≃ (ChallengeIndex q → V) :=
+  (((Equiv.funUnique Unit V).symm).prodCongr (Equiv.refl _)).trans
+    (Equiv.sumArrowEquivProdArrow Unit (Fin (q + 1)) V).symm
+
+/-- `challengeEquiv q V (k,r)` is `Sum.elim (fun _ => k) r`. -/
+theorem challengeEquiv_eq (q : ℕ) (V : Type) (k : V) (r : Fin (q + 1) → V) :
+    challengeEquiv q V (k, r) = Sum.elim (fun _ => k) r := rfl
+
+/-- The inverse challenge-coordinate map reads the key slot and all padded nonce slots.
+`(challengeEquiv q V).symm f = (f (Sum.inl ()), fun j => f (Sum.inr j))`. -/
+theorem challengeEquiv_symm (q : ℕ) (V : Type) (f : ChallengeIndex q → V) :
+    (challengeEquiv q V).symm f = (f (Sum.inl ()), fun j => f (Sum.inr j)) := rfl
+
+/-- Pick the single key slot at `(u,true)`, or the selected nonce slot given by the nonce
+coordinate map. The map depends on the row permutation and never on coordinate values.
+`pick C rows perm u q x = Sum.elim (fun label => if label = (u, true) then some (Sum.inl () :
+ChallengeIndex q) else none) (fun nonce => (NonceCoordinates.pick C rows perm u.val q nonce).map
+Sum.inr) x`. -/
+noncomputable def pick {m : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit m)
+    (rows : (i : Fin C.gates.length) → List (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool))
+    (perm : Permutations C) (u : Fin C.size) (q : ℕ) (x : Index C) :
+    Option (ChallengeIndex q) :=
+  Sum.elim (fun label => if label = (u, true) then some (Sum.inl () : ChallengeIndex q) else none)
+    (fun nonce => (NonceCoordinates.pick C rows perm u.val q nonce).map Sum.inr) x
+
+/-- `pick` selects the key challenge at `(u,true)` and delegates nonce coordinates to the
+nonce-slot map.
+`pick C rows perm u q x = Sum.elim (fun label => if label = (u, true) then some (Sum.inl () :
+ChallengeIndex q) else none) (fun nonce => (NonceCoordinates.pick C rows perm u.val q nonce).map
+Sum.inr) x`. -/
+theorem pick_eq {m : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit m)
+    (rows : (i : Fin C.gates.length) → List (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool))
+    (perm : Permutations C) (u : Fin C.size) (q : ℕ) (x : Index C) :
+    pick C rows perm u q x =
+      Sum.elim
+        (fun label => if label = (u, true) then some (Sum.inl () : ChallengeIndex q) else none)
+        (fun nonce => (NonceCoordinates.pick C rows perm u.val q nonce).map Sum.inr) x := rfl
+
+/-- Place the selected key at `(u,true)` and each used nonce slot at its selected row half.
+`place C rows perm u q j = Sum.elim (fun _ => some (Sum.inl (u, true) : Index C)) (fun nonce =>
+(NonceCoordinates.place C rows perm u.val q nonce).map Sum.inr) j`. -/
+def place {m : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit m)
+    (rows : (i : Fin C.gates.length) → List (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool))
+    (perm : Permutations C) (u : Fin C.size) (q : ℕ) (j : ChallengeIndex q) : Option (Index C) :=
+  Sum.elim (fun _ => some (Sum.inl (u, true) : Index C))
+    (fun nonce => (NonceCoordinates.place C rows perm u.val q nonce).map Sum.inr) j
+
+/-- `place` retains the distinction between the key challenge and padded nonce challenges.
+`place C rows perm u q j = Sum.elim (fun _ => some (Sum.inl (u, true) : Index C)) (fun nonce =>
+(NonceCoordinates.place C rows perm u.val q nonce).map Sum.inr) j`. -/
+theorem place_eq {m : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit m)
+    (rows : (i : Fin C.gates.length) → List (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool))
+    (perm : Permutations C) (u : Fin C.size) (q : ℕ) (j : ChallengeIndex q) :
+    place C rows perm u q j =
+      Sum.elim (fun _ => some (Sum.inl (u, true) : Index C))
+        (fun nonce => (NonceCoordinates.place C rows perm u.val q nonce).map Sum.inr) j := rfl
+
+/-- Full key/nonce picks and placements are exact partial inverses. The key and nonce
+summands are disjoint, and nonce padding remains unmatched.
+`pick C rows perm u q x = some j ↔ place C rows perm u q j = some x`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** In the key summand there is one matching vertex/bit coordinate and one
+challenge slot. Mixed summands cannot match. Within the nonce summand, injectivity of the
+sum constructor reduces both directions to the existing nonce-coordinate inverse contract. -/
+theorem pick_iff_place {m : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit m)
+    (rows : (i : Fin C.gates.length) → List (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool))
+    (perm : Permutations C) (u : Fin C.size) (q : ℕ)
+    (hargs : ∀ i : Fin C.gates.length, C.gates[i.val].args.Nodup)
+    (hcomplete : ∀ i a, a ∈ rows i) (hrows : ∀ i, (rows i).Nodup)
+    (x : Index C) (j : ChallengeIndex q) :
+    pick C rows perm u q x = some j ↔ place C rows perm u q j = some x := by
+  cases x with
+  | inl label =>
+    cases j with
+    | inl keySlot =>
+      cases keySlot
+      rw [pick_eq, place_eq]
+      change (if label = (u, true) then some (Sum.inl () : ChallengeIndex q) else none) =
+        some (Sum.inl () : ChallengeIndex q) ↔
+        some (Sum.inl (u, true) : Index C) = some (Sum.inl label : Index C)
+      by_cases h : label = (u, true)
+      · subst label
+        constructor
+        · intro _; rfl
+        · intro _; exact if_pos rfl
+      · constructor
+        · intro h'
+          rw [if_neg h] at h'
+          cases h'
+        · intro h'
+          exact (h (Sum.inl.inj (Option.some.inj h')).symm).elim
+    | inr nonce =>
+      rw [pick_eq, place_eq]
+      change (if label = (u, true) then some (Sum.inl () : ChallengeIndex q) else none) =
+        some (Sum.inr nonce : ChallengeIndex q) ↔
+        (NonceCoordinates.place C rows perm u.val q nonce).map Sum.inr =
+          some (Sum.inl label : Index C)
+      constructor
+      · intro h
+        by_cases hc : label = (u, true)
+        · rw [if_pos hc] at h; cases h
+        · rw [if_neg hc] at h; cases h
+      · intro h
+        obtain ⟨_, _, heq⟩ := Option.map_eq_some_iff.mp h
+        cases heq
+  | inr coordinate =>
+    cases j with
+    | inl keySlot =>
+      cases keySlot
+      rw [pick_eq, place_eq]
+      change (NonceCoordinates.pick C rows perm u.val q coordinate).map Sum.inr =
+        some (Sum.inl () : ChallengeIndex q) ↔
+          some (Sum.inl (u, true) : Index C) = some (Sum.inr coordinate : Index C)
+      constructor
+      · intro h
+        obtain ⟨_, _, heq⟩ := Option.map_eq_some_iff.mp h
+        cases heq
+      · intro h; cases h
+    | inr nonce =>
+      rw [pick_eq, place_eq]
+      change (NonceCoordinates.pick C rows perm u.val q coordinate).map Sum.inr =
+        some (Sum.inr nonce : ChallengeIndex q) ↔
+          (NonceCoordinates.place C rows perm u.val q nonce).map Sum.inr =
+            some (Sum.inr coordinate : Index C)
+      constructor
+      · intro h
+        obtain ⟨j, hp, heq⟩ := Option.map_eq_some_iff.mp h
+        have hj : j = nonce := Sum.inr.inj heq
+        subst j
+        exact Option.map_eq_some_iff.mpr ⟨coordinate,
+          (NonceCoordinates.pick_iff_place C rows perm u.val q hargs hcomplete hrows _ _).mp hp,
+          rfl⟩
+      · intro h
+        obtain ⟨x, hp, heq⟩ := Option.map_eq_some_iff.mp h
+        have hx : x = coordinate := Sum.inr.inj heq
+        subst x
+        exact Option.map_eq_some_iff.mpr ⟨nonce,
+          (NonceCoordinates.pick_iff_place C rows perm u.val q hargs hcomplete hrows _ _).mpr hp,
+          rfl⟩
+
+/-- The seed coordinate map is its unchanged permutation paired with the data-coordinate map.
+`seedEquiv C V s = (s.2.2, dataEquiv C V (s.1, s.2.1))`. -/
+theorem seedEquiv_eq_data {m : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit m) (V : Type)
+    (s : GarbleSeed C V (V × V)) :
+    seedEquiv C V s = (s.2.2, dataEquiv C V (s.1, s.2.1)) := rfl
+
+/-- Filling the selected key and nonce vector is exactly the value-coordinate replacement
+prescribed by `pick`. This pointwise identity holds at every value, including collisions,
+and for arbitrary unused padding; no distribution or permutation-uniformity assumption occurs.
+`dataEquiv C V ((SelectedSeed.fill C rows s u q k r).1, (SelectedSeed.fill C rows s u q k r).2.1) =
+fun x => (pick C rows s.2.2 u q x).elim (dataEquiv C V (s.1, s.2.1) x) (challengeEquiv q V (k, r))`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** At key coordinates the filled-key reader changes just `(u,true)`, matching
+the single key slot. At nonce coordinates the nonce-coordinate read contract gives precisely
+the picked challenge value or the retained old value. The challenge sum map reads the key
+in its left summand and the nonce vector in its right summand. -/
+theorem dataEquiv_fill {m : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit m) (V : Type)
+    (rows : (i : Fin C.gates.length) → List (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool))
+    (s : GarbleSeed C V (V × V)) (u : Fin C.size) (q : ℕ) (k : V)
+    (r : Fin (q + 1) → V) :
+    dataEquiv C V ((SelectedSeed.fill C rows s u q k r).1,
+      (SelectedSeed.fill C rows s u q k r).2.1) =
+        fun x => (pick C rows s.2.2 u q x).elim (dataEquiv C V (s.1, s.2.1) x)
+          (challengeEquiv q V (k, r)) := by
+  funext x
+  cases x with
+  | inl label =>
+    rcases label with ⟨v, b⟩
+    simp only [dataEquiv_eq, pick_eq, Sum.elim_inl, SelectedSeed.fill_key,
+      challengeEquiv_eq, Prod.mk.injEq]
+    by_cases h : v = u ∧ b = true
+    · simp only [h, and_self, ite_true, Option.elim_some, Sum.elim_inl]
+    · simp only [h, ite_false, Option.elim_none]
+  | inr coordinate =>
+    rcases coordinate with ⟨i, a, half⟩
+    simp only [dataEquiv_eq, Sum.elim_inr, SelectedSeed.fill_nonce]
+    rw [NonceCoordinates.nonce_read]
+    simp only [pick_eq, challengeEquiv_eq, Sum.elim_inr]
+    cases h : NonceCoordinates.pick C rows s.2.2 u.val q ⟨i, (a, half)⟩ with
+    | none => simp only [Option.map_none, Option.elim_none]
+    | some j => simp only [Option.map_some, Option.elim_some, Sum.elim_inr]
+
+/-- The complete filled abstract seed retains its permutation and performs exactly the
+partial replacement of its key/nonce value coordinates.
+`seedEquiv C V (SelectedSeed.fill C rows s u q k r) = (s.2.2, fun x => (pick C rows s.2.2 u q
+x).elim (dataEquiv C V (s.1, s.2.1) x) (challengeEquiv q V (k, r)))`. -/
+theorem seedEquiv_fill {m : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit m) (V : Type)
+    (rows : (i : Fin C.gates.length) → List (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool))
+    (s : GarbleSeed C V (V × V)) (u : Fin C.size) (q : ℕ) (k : V)
+    (r : Fin (q + 1) → V) :
+    seedEquiv C V (SelectedSeed.fill C rows s u q k r) =
+      (s.2.2, fun x => (pick C rows s.2.2 u q x).elim (dataEquiv C V (s.1, s.2.1) x)
+        (challengeEquiv q V (k, r))) :=
+  (seedEquiv_eq_data C V _).trans (Prod.ext
+    (funext fun i => SelectedSeed.fill_perm C rows s u q k r i)
+    (dataEquiv_fill C V rows s u q k r))
+
+end TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof.SeedCoordinates

+/-

+Released under Apache 2.0 license as described in the file LICENSE.

+import TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.Yao.HybridProof.SelectedPlan
+import TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.Yao.HybridProof.Lengths
+import TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.Yao.HybridProof.GateHybridMessage
+
+set_option maxHeartbeats 0
+set_option relaxedAutoImplicit false
+set_option autoImplicit false
+
+/-!
+# Polynomial advice bounds for selected-key reconstruction
+
+Each unaffected row is one fixed nested ciphertext. Each selected row stores a unary
+challenge index and at most one known key/nonce pair. At most four rows per gate therefore
+give a polynomial descriptor word, with explicit constants independent of all key values.
+The fixed surrounding fields are accounted for separately by their word lengths.
+
+## Main definitions
+
+* `budget`: a polynomial budget for the full reconstruction advice.
+* `slotBudget`, `tailBudget`, `privateBudget`, `sampleBudget`: concrete hybrid-view budgets.
+
+## Main results
+
+* `SelectedAdviceBounds.record_le`: at most `2q+7n+8` bits per row record.
+* Complete descriptor-table and surrounding-advice bounds.
+
+## References
+
+* [PS10] R. Pass, a. shelat, *A Course in Cryptography*, lecture notes, 3rd ed., January
+  2010, Theorem 163.7 (polynomial-length nonuniform reconstruction advice).
+-/
+
+namespace TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof.SelectedAdviceBounds
+
+open Complexity Turing BoolCircuit TCSlib.Cryptography
+
+/-- Every reconstruction record has at most `2q+7n+8` bits when the padded occurrence list
+fits in `q+1` slots. This includes fixed zero/unselected rows and all selected layers.
+`(SelectedPlan.record C rows s u E₁ E₂ o i a).length ≤ 2 * q + 7 * n + 8`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** A fixed record adds two bits to a nested ciphertext, whose length is at
+most `7n+4`. A selected record has index at most `q`, so its direct or wrapped descriptor
+has at most `2q+3n+8` bits. Both bounds are at most the stated common bound. -/
+theorem record_le {m n : ℕ} (E₁ E₂ : List Bool → List Bool) (C : DAGCircuit m)
+    (rows : (i : Fin C.gates.length) → List (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool))
+    (s : GarbleSeed C (Fin n → Bool) ((Fin n → Bool) × (Fin n → Bool)))
+    (u : Fin C.size) (q : ℕ)
+    (o : (i : Fin C.gates.length) → (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool) → (Fin n → Bool))
+    (hargs : ∀ i : Fin C.gates.length, C.gates[i.val].args.Nodup)
+    (hcomplete : ∀ i a, a ∈ rows i)
+    (hcap : (GateOccurrences.occurrences C rows s.2.2 u.val).length ≤ q + 1)
+    (i : Fin C.gates.length) (a : Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool) :
+    (SelectedPlan.record C rows s u E₁ E₂ o i a).length ≤ 2 * q + 7 * n + 8 := by
+  rw [SelectedPlan.record_eq]
+  cases hsel : GateOccurrences.selectedPosition C.gates[i.val] a u.val with
+  | none =>
+    simp only [Option.elim_none]
+    have hc := length_nestedSpecial_enc_le E₁ E₂ n (argKeys C s i a) (o i a) (s.2.1 i a)
+    rw [(SliceReconstruction.record_lengths _ 0 [] []).1]
+    omega
+  | some p =>
+    simp only [Option.elim_some]
+    obtain ⟨hvertex, hbit⟩ :=
+      (GateOccurrences.selectedPosition_eq_some_iff _ _ _ (hargs i) p).mp hsel
+    have hi := lt_of_lt_of_le
+      (SelectedSeed.slotIndex_lt C rows s.2.2 u.val i a p (hargs i)
+        (hcomplete i) hvertex hbit) hcap
+    have hd := SelectedRows.descriptor_length_le n q (argKeys C s i a) (s.2.1 i a)
+      p.val (SelectedSeed.slotIndex C rows s.2.2 u.val i a p) (by omega)
+    exact hd.trans (by omega)
+
+/-- An encoded descriptor table has at most `4(2(2q+7n+8)+2)` bits when its row enumeration
+has length at most four and the occurrence list fits the padded capacity.
+`(BinArith.encWords ((rows i).map fun a => SelectedPlan.record C rows s u E₁ E₂ o i (s.2.2 i
+a))).length ≤ 4 * (2 * (2 * q + 7 * n + 8) + 2)`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** Every row record satisfies the common record bound. The public encoded-list
+length bound charges twice that record length plus two delimiter bits per row. At most four
+rows give the stated total. -/
+theorem table_le {m n : ℕ} (E₁ E₂ : List Bool → List Bool) (C : DAGCircuit m)
+    (rows : (i : Fin C.gates.length) → List (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool))
+    (s : GarbleSeed C (Fin n → Bool) ((Fin n → Bool) × (Fin n → Bool)))
+    (u : Fin C.size) (q : ℕ)
+    (o : (i : Fin C.gates.length) → (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool) → (Fin n → Bool))
+    (hargs : ∀ i : Fin C.gates.length, C.gates[i.val].args.Nodup)
+    (hcomplete : ∀ i a, a ∈ rows i)
+    (hcap : (GateOccurrences.occurrences C rows s.2.2 u.val).length ≤ q + 1)
+    (hrowlen : ∀ i, (rows i).length ≤ 4) (i : Fin C.gates.length) :
+    (BinArith.encWords ((rows i).map fun a =>
+      SelectedPlan.record C rows s u E₁ E₂ o i (s.2.2 i a))).length ≤
+        4 * (2 * (2 * q + 7 * n + 8) + 2) := by
+  have ht := BinArith.length_encWords_le ((rows i).map fun a =>
+    SelectedPlan.record C rows s u E₁ E₂ o i (s.2.2 i a)) (2 * q + 7 * n + 8) (by
+      intro w hw
+      obtain ⟨a, _, rfl⟩ := List.mem_map.mp hw
+      exact record_le E₁ E₂ C rows s u q o hargs hcomplete hcap i (s.2.2 i a))
+  simp only [List.length_map] at ht
+  exact ht.trans (Nat.mul_le_mul_right _ (hrowlen i))
+
+/-- The encoded descriptor-table word has at most
+`C.gates.length * (16*(2q+7n+8)+18)` bits, with at most four rows at each gate.
+`(BinArith.encWords ((SelectedPlan.tables C rows s u E₁ E₂ o).map BinArith.encWords)).length ≤
+C.gates.length * (16 * (2 * q + 7 * n + 8) + 18)`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** Each encoded table has the bound from `table_le`. Apply the encoded-list
+length bound once more to the gate-index vector. Its length is the gate count; simplifying
+the two layers of delimiter costs gives the displayed coefficient. -/
+theorem encodedTables_le {m n : ℕ} (E₁ E₂ : List Bool → List Bool) (C : DAGCircuit m)
+    (rows : (i : Fin C.gates.length) → List (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool))
+    (s : GarbleSeed C (Fin n → Bool) ((Fin n → Bool) × (Fin n → Bool)))
+    (u : Fin C.size) (q : ℕ)
+    (o : (i : Fin C.gates.length) → (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool) → (Fin n → Bool))
+    (hargs : ∀ i : Fin C.gates.length, C.gates[i.val].args.Nodup)
+    (hcomplete : ∀ i a, a ∈ rows i)
+    (hcap : (GateOccurrences.occurrences C rows s.2.2 u.val).length ≤ q + 1)
+    (hrowlen : ∀ i, (rows i).length ≤ 4) :
+    (BinArith.encWords ((SelectedPlan.tables C rows s u E₁ E₂ o).map BinArith.encWords)).length ≤
+      C.gates.length * (16 * (2 * q + 7 * n + 8) + 18) := by
+  rw [SelectedPlan.tables_eq, List.map_ofFn]
+  have ht := BinArith.length_encWords_le
+    (List.ofFn fun i => BinArith.encWords ((rows i).map fun a =>
+      SelectedPlan.record C rows s u E₁ E₂ o i (s.2.2 i a)))
+    (4 * (2 * (2 * q + 7 * n + 8) + 2)) (by
+      intro w hw
+      obtain ⟨i, rfl⟩ := List.mem_ofFn.mp hw
+      exact table_le E₁ E₂ C rows s u q o hargs hcomplete hcap hrowlen i)
+  have hc : 2 * (4 * (2 * (2 * q + 7 * n + 8) + 2)) + 2 =
+      16 * (2 * q + 7 * n + 8) + 18 := by omega
+  simpa only [List.length_ofFn, hc] using ht
+
+/-- Full reconstruction advice has at most
+`2G*(16*(2q+7n+8)+18)+2|tail|+2|privateInput|+|aliceOutput|+6` bits, where `G` is the gate count.
+`(SliceReconstruction.advice (BinArith.encWords ((SelectedPlan.tables C rows s u E₁ E₂ o).map
+BinArith.encWords)) tail privateInput aliceOutput).length ≤ 2 * C.gates.length * (16 * (2 * q + 7 *
+n + 8) + 18) + 2 * tail.length + 2 * privateInput.length + aliceOutput.length + 6`. -/
+theorem advice_le {m n : ℕ} (E₁ E₂ : List Bool → List Bool) (C : DAGCircuit m)
+    (rows : (i : Fin C.gates.length) → List (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool))
+    (s : GarbleSeed C (Fin n → Bool) ((Fin n → Bool) × (Fin n → Bool)))
+    (u : Fin C.size) (q : ℕ)
+    (o : (i : Fin C.gates.length) → (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool) → (Fin n → Bool))
+    (hargs : ∀ i : Fin C.gates.length, C.gates[i.val].args.Nodup)
+    (hcomplete : ∀ i a, a ∈ rows i)
+    (hcap : (GateOccurrences.occurrences C rows s.2.2 u.val).length ≤ q + 1)
+    (hrowlen : ∀ i, (rows i).length ≤ 4) (tail privateInput aliceOutput : List Bool) :
+    (SliceReconstruction.advice
+      (BinArith.encWords ((SelectedPlan.tables C rows s u E₁ E₂ o).map BinArith.encWords))
+      tail privateInput aliceOutput).length ≤
+        2 * C.gates.length * (16 * (2 * q + 7 * n + 8) + 18) +
+          2 * tail.length + 2 * privateInput.length + aliceOutput.length + 6 := by
+  rw [SliceReconstruction.advice_length]
+  have ht := Nat.mul_le_mul_left 2
+    (encodedTables_le E₁ E₂ C rows s u q o hargs hcomplete hcap hrowlen)
+  simp only [Nat.mul_assoc] at ht ⊢
+  omega
+
+/-- The polynomial advice budget for gate bound `S`, slot bound `Q`, and surrounding-field
+bounds `T,I,A` is `2S(16(2Q+7n+8)+18)+2T+2I+A+6`.
+`budget S Q T I A = let row : PolyExpr := .add (.add (.mul (.const 2) Q) (.mul (.const 7) .var))
+(.const 8); let descriptors : PolyExpr := .mul (.mul (.const 2) S) (.add (.mul (.const 16) row)
+(.const 18)); .add (.add (.add (.add descriptors (.mul (.const 2) T)) (.mul (.const 2) I)) A)
+(.const 6)`. -/
+def budget (S Q T I A : PolyExpr) : PolyExpr :=
+  let row : PolyExpr := .add (.add (.mul (.const 2) Q) (.mul (.const 7) .var)) (.const 8)
+  let descriptors : PolyExpr := .mul (.mul (.const 2) S)
+    (.add (.mul (.const 16) row) (.const 18))
+  .add (.add (.add (.add descriptors (.mul (.const 2) T)) (.mul (.const 2) I)) A) (.const 6)
+
+/-- `budget` is the displayed composition of polynomial addition, multiplication and constants.
+`budget S Q T I A = let row : PolyExpr := .add (.add (.mul (.const 2) Q) (.mul (.const 7) .var))
+(.const 8); let descriptors : PolyExpr := .mul (.mul (.const 2) S) (.add (.mul (.const 16) row)
+(.const 18)); .add (.add (.add (.add descriptors (.mul (.const 2) T)) (.mul (.const 2) I)) A)
+(.const 6)`. -/
+theorem budget_eq (S Q T I A : PolyExpr) :
+    budget S Q T I A =
+      let row : PolyExpr := .add (.add (.mul (.const 2) Q) (.mul (.const 7) .var)) (.const 8)
+      let descriptors : PolyExpr := .mul (.mul (.const 2) S)
+        (.add (.mul (.const 16) row) (.const 18))
+      .add (.add (.add (.add descriptors (.mul (.const 2) T)) (.mul (.const 2) I)) A) (.const 6) :=
+  rfl
+
+/-- Evaluating the advice budget gives the explicit natural-number bound
+`2S(n)(16(2Q(n)+7n+8)+18)+2T(n)+2I(n)+A(n)+6`.
+`(budget S Q T I A).eval n = 2 * S.eval n * (16 * (2 * Q.eval n + 7 * n + 8) + 18) + 2 * T.eval n +
+2 * I.eval n + A.eval n + 6`. -/
+theorem budget_eval (S Q T I A : PolyExpr) (n : ℕ) :
+    (budget S Q T I A).eval n =
+      2 * S.eval n * (16 * (2 * Q.eval n + 7 * n + 8) + 18) +
+        2 * T.eval n + 2 * I.eval n + A.eval n + 6 := by
+  simp only [budget_eq, PolyExpr.eval_add, PolyExpr.eval_mul, PolyExpr.eval_const,
+    PolyExpr.eval_var]
+
+/-- The full advice length is bounded by the explicit polynomial `budget S Q T I A` whenever
+the gate count, slot parameter and three fixed surrounding fields have these polynomial
+bounds. This makes the polynomial-advice claim explicit for this encoding.
+[PS10, Theorem 163.7]
+`(SliceReconstruction.advice (BinArith.encWords ((SelectedPlan.tables C rows s u E₁ E₂ o).map
+BinArith.encWords)) tail privateInput aliceOutput).length ≤ (budget S Q T I A).eval n`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** Start with the pointwise advice bound. Enlarge the row-record coefficient
+using the slot polynomial, and enlarge the descriptor-table count using the gate polynomial.
+Monotonicity gives the resulting product bound. Add the bounds for the fixed surrounding
+fields, and evaluate the published budget polynomial to identify the result. -/
+theorem advice_budget {m n : ℕ} (E₁ E₂ : List Bool → List Bool) (C : DAGCircuit m)
+    (rows : (i : Fin C.gates.length) → List (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool))
+    (s : GarbleSeed C (Fin n → Bool) ((Fin n → Bool) × (Fin n → Bool)))
+    (u : Fin C.size) (q : ℕ)
+    (o : (i : Fin C.gates.length) → (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool) → (Fin n → Bool))
+    (hargs : ∀ i : Fin C.gates.length, C.gates[i.val].args.Nodup)
+    (hcomplete : ∀ i a, a ∈ rows i)
+    (hcap : (GateOccurrences.occurrences C rows s.2.2 u.val).length ≤ q + 1)
+    (hrowlen : ∀ i, (rows i).length ≤ 4) (tail privateInput aliceOutput : List Bool)
+    (S Q T I A : PolyExpr) (hS : C.gates.length ≤ S.eval n) (hQ : q ≤ Q.eval n)
+    (hT : tail.length ≤ T.eval n) (hI : privateInput.length ≤ I.eval n)
+    (hA : aliceOutput.length ≤ A.eval n) :
+    (SliceReconstruction.advice
+      (BinArith.encWords ((SelectedPlan.tables C rows s u E₁ E₂ o).map BinArith.encWords))
+      tail privateInput aliceOutput).length ≤ (budget S Q T I A).eval n := by
+  have hrow : 2 * q + 7 * n + 8 ≤ 2 * Q.eval n + 7 * n + 8 := by omega
+  have hcoeff : 16 * (2 * q + 7 * n + 8) + 18 ≤
+      16 * (2 * Q.eval n + 7 * n + 8) + 18 := by omega
+  have hprod := Nat.mul_le_mul hS hcoeff
+  have hscaled := Nat.mul_le_mul_left 2 hprod
+  have hb := advice_le E₁ E₂ C rows s u q o hargs hcomplete hcap hrowlen
+    tail privateInput aliceOutput
+  rw [budget_eval]
+  simp only [Nat.mul_assoc] at hb hscaled ⊢
+  omega
+
+/-- The hybrid message's fixed tail has at most
+`2*(inA*(2n+2))+2+outLen*(6n+6)` bits, independently of its stage tables and all key values.
+The bound reuses the public input-key and output-pair encoding bounds.
+`(pairSndD (GateHybridMessage.message M C s inA outLen output v stage)).length ≤ 2 * (inA * (2 * n +
+2)) + 2 + outLen * (6 * n + 6)`. -/
+theorem messageTail_le {m n : ℕ} {ρ : Type}
+    (M : MultiEnc (Fin n → Bool) (List Bool) ρ) (C : DAGCircuit m)
+    (s : GarbleSeed C (Fin n → Bool) ρ) (inA outLen : ℕ) (output : Fin outLen → ℕ)
+    (v : ℕ → Bool) (stage : ℕ) :
+    (pairSndD (GateHybridMessage.message M C s inA outLen output v stage)).length ≤
+      2 * (inA * (2 * n + 2)) + 2 + outLen * (6 * n + 6) := by
+  have hk : (BinArith.encWords (List.ofFn fun w : Fin inA =>
+      List.ofFn (keyAt C s w false))).length ≤ inA * (2 * n + 2) := by
+    simpa only [encodeMsgs_eq_encWords] using
+      length_encoded_keys_le (fun w : Fin inA => keyAt C s w false)
+  have hp := length_encoded_pairs_le (fun (j : Fin outLen) (b : Bool) =>
+    if b then (FakeObjects.decodePair C s (output j) (v (output j))).2
+    else (FakeObjects.decodePair C s (output j) (v (output j))).1)
+  simp only [encodeMsgs_eq_encWords] at hp
+  rw [GateHybridMessage.message_eq, pairSndD_pairEncode, length_pairEncode]
+  exact Nat.add_le_add (Nat.add_le_add_right (Nat.mul_le_mul_left 2 hk) 2) hp
+
+/-- The private view-input word has exactly `2|bobInput|+2+count*n` bits: the encoded Bob
+input followed by the false-key OT answer vector. Zero key width and zero count are included.
+`(pairEncode bobInput (List.ofFn (FakeObjects.activeAnswers C s first count))).length = 2 *
+bobInput.length + 2 + count * n`. -/
+theorem privateInput_length {m n : ℕ} {ρ : Type} (C : DAGCircuit m)
+    (s : GarbleSeed C (Fin n → Bool) ρ) (first count : ℕ) (bobInput : List Bool) :
+    (pairEncode bobInput (List.ofFn (FakeObjects.activeAnswers C s first count))).length =
+      2 * bobInput.length + 2 + count * n := by
+  rw [length_pairEncode, List.length_ofFn]
+
+/-- The selected-key experiment has `4S(n)+1` padded nonce slots, using slot parameter `4S`.
+`slotBudget S = .mul (.const 4) S`. -/
+def slotBudget (S : PolyExpr) : PolyExpr := .mul (.const 4) S
+
+/-- `slotBudget S = 4*S` as a polynomial expression. -/
+theorem slotBudget_eq (S : PolyExpr) : slotBudget S = .mul (.const 4) S := rfl
+
+/-- The fixed message-tail budget is `2*A(n)*(2n+2)+2+O(n)*(6n+6)`.
+`tailBudget A O = .add (.add (.mul (.const 2) (.mul A (.add (.mul (.const 2) .var) (.const 2))))
+(.const 2)) (.mul O (.add (.mul (.const 6) .var) (.const 6)))`. -/
+def tailBudget (A O : PolyExpr) : PolyExpr :=
+  .add (.add (.mul (.const 2) (.mul A (.add (.mul (.const 2) .var) (.const 2)))) (.const 2))
+    (.mul O (.add (.mul (.const 6) .var) (.const 6)))
+
+/-- `tailBudget` combines the two public key/pair encoding bounds.
+`tailBudget A O = .add (.add (.mul (.const 2) (.mul A (.add (.mul (.const 2) .var) (.const 2))))
+(.const 2)) (.mul O (.add (.mul (.const 6) .var) (.const 6)))`. -/
+theorem tailBudget_eq (A O : PolyExpr) :
+    tailBudget A O =
+      .add (.add (.mul (.const 2) (.mul A (.add (.mul (.const 2) .var) (.const 2)))) (.const 2))
+        (.mul O (.add (.mul (.const 6) .var) (.const 6))) := rfl
+
+/-- The private view-input budget is `2B(n)+2+B(n)*n`.
+`privateBudget B = .add (.add (.mul (.const 2) B) (.const 2)) (.mul B .var)`. -/
+def privateBudget (B : PolyExpr) : PolyExpr :=
+  .add (.add (.mul (.const 2) B) (.const 2)) (.mul B .var)
+
+/-- `privateBudget B` is the polynomial for Bob's input paired with his active answers.
+`privateBudget B = .add (.add (.mul (.const 2) B) (.const 2)) (.mul B .var)`. -/
+theorem privateBudget_eq (B : PolyExpr) :
+    privateBudget B = .add (.add (.mul (.const 2) B) (.const 2)) (.mul B .var) := rfl
+
+/-- The concrete hybrid-view advice budget uses gate bound `S`, input bounds `A,B`,
+output-pair count `O`, and retained Alice-output bound `R`.
+`sampleBudget S A B O R = budget S (slotBudget S) (tailBudget A O) (privateBudget B) R`. -/
+def sampleBudget (S A B O R : PolyExpr) : PolyExpr :=
+  budget S (slotBudget S) (tailBudget A O) (privateBudget B) R
+
+/-- `sampleBudget` instantiates the generic advice polynomial with the fixed hybrid fields.
+`sampleBudget S A B O R = budget S (slotBudget S) (tailBudget A O) (privateBudget B) R`. -/
+theorem sampleBudget_eq (S A B O R : PolyExpr) :
+    sampleBudget S A B O R = budget S (slotBudget S) (tailBudget A O) (privateBudget B) R := rfl
+
+/-- Evaluating the slot polynomial gives `(slotBudget S)(n)=4*S(n)`. -/
+theorem slotBudget_eval (S : PolyExpr) (n : ℕ) :
+    (slotBudget S).eval n = 4 * S.eval n := by
+  simp only [slotBudget_eq, PolyExpr.eval_mul, PolyExpr.eval_const]
+
+/-- Evaluating the fixed message-tail budget gives
+`2*A(n)*(2n+2)+2+O(n)*(6n+6)`.
+`(tailBudget A O).eval n = 2 * (A.eval n * (2 * n + 2)) + 2 + O.eval n * (6 * n + 6)`. -/
+theorem tailBudget_eval (A O : PolyExpr) (n : ℕ) :
+    (tailBudget A O).eval n =
+      2 * (A.eval n * (2 * n + 2)) + 2 + O.eval n * (6 * n + 6) := by
+  simp only [tailBudget_eq, PolyExpr.eval_add, PolyExpr.eval_mul, PolyExpr.eval_const,
+    PolyExpr.eval_var]
+
+/-- Evaluating the private-input polynomial gives `2B(n)+2+B(n)*n`.
+`(privateBudget B).eval n = 2 * B.eval n + 2 + B.eval n * n`. -/
+theorem privateBudget_eval (B : PolyExpr) (n : ℕ) :
+    (privateBudget B).eval n = 2 * B.eval n + 2 + B.eval n * n := by
+  simp only [privateBudget_eq, PolyExpr.eval_add, PolyExpr.eval_mul, PolyExpr.eval_const,
+    PolyExpr.eval_var]
+
+/-- The complete actual hybrid-view advice has the explicit polynomial bound
+`sampleBudget S A B O R`, using `4S(n)+1` padded challenge slots. The circuit, all keys,
+nonces, masks, stage and prescribed outputs may be arbitrary. [PS10, Theorem 163.7]
+`(SelectedPlan.advice C rows s u E₁ E₂ o (pairSndD (GateHybridMessage.message (nestedSpecial E₁ E₂
+n) C s (A.eval n) (O.eval n) output v stage)) (pairEncode bobInput (List.ofFn
+(FakeObjects.activeAnswers C s (A.eval n) (B.eval n)))) aliceOutput).length ≤ (sampleBudget S A B O
+R).eval n`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** Fan-in two supplies distinct arguments and the canonical assignment lists
+have at most four rows by the existing garbled-table bound. The all-gate occurrence bound is
+at most four times the gate count, so the chosen slot polynomial covers it. The published
+message-tail and private-input bounds charge all retained view fields. Substitute these
+bounds into the generic advice polynomial. No key-value inequalities or positive-size
+assumptions are used. -/
+theorem sample_advice_budget {m n : ℕ} (E₁ E₂ : List Bool → List Bool)
+    (C : DAGCircuit m)
+    (rows : (i : Fin C.gates.length) → List (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool))
+    (s : GarbleSeed C (Fin n → Bool) ((Fin n → Bool) × (Fin n → Bool)))
+    (u : Fin C.size)
+    (o : (i : Fin C.gates.length) → (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool) → (Fin n → Bool))
+    (S A B O R : PolyExpr) (output : Fin (O.eval n) → ℕ) (v : ℕ → Bool) (stage : ℕ)
+    (bobInput aliceOutput : List Bool) (hfanin : C.IsFaninTwo)
+    (hS : C.size ≤ S.eval n)
+    (hrows : ∀ i, rows i = (Finset.univ : Finset (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool)).toList)
+    (hBob : bobInput.length ≤ B.eval n) (hAlice : aliceOutput.length ≤ R.eval n) :
+    (SelectedPlan.advice C rows s u E₁ E₂ o
+      (pairSndD (GateHybridMessage.message (nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ n) C s
+        (A.eval n) (O.eval n) output v stage))
+      (pairEncode bobInput (List.ofFn (FakeObjects.activeAnswers C s (A.eval n) (B.eval n))))
+      aliceOutput).length ≤ (sampleBudget S A B O R).eval n := by
+  have hfan := (DAGCircuit.isFaninTwo_iff C).mp hfanin
+  have hargs (i : Fin C.gates.length) : C.gates[i.val].args.Nodup :=
+    (hfan.1 _ (List.getElem_mem i.isLt)).1
+  have hcomplete (i : Fin C.gates.length) (a : Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool) :
+      a ∈ rows i := by
+    rw [hrows i]
+    exact Finset.mem_toList.mpr (Finset.mem_univ a)
+  have hnodup (i : Fin C.gates.length) : (rows i).Nodup := by
+    rw [hrows i]
+    exact Finset.nodup_toList _
+  have hrowlen (i : Fin C.gates.length) : (rows i).length ≤ 4 := by
+    rw [hrows i]
+    simpa only [garbledTable_eq, List.length_map, Fin.getElem_fin] using
+      length_garbledTable_le (nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ n) C s i
+        (hfan.2 _ (List.getElem_mem i.isLt))
+  have hg : C.gates.length ≤ S.eval n := by
+    rw [DAGCircuit.size_eq] at hS
+    omega
+  have hcap : (GateOccurrences.occurrences C rows s.2.2 u.val).length ≤
+      (slotBudget S).eval n + 1 := by
+    have hc := GateOccurrences.length_occurrences_le C rows s.2.2 u.val hnodup hfanin
+    rw [slotBudget_eval]
+    omega
+  have ht := messageTail_le (nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ n) C s (A.eval n) (O.eval n)
+    output v stage
+  have hi : (pairEncode bobInput
+      (List.ofFn (FakeObjects.activeAnswers C s (A.eval n) (B.eval n)))).length ≤
+        (privateBudget B).eval n := by
+    rw [privateInput_length, privateBudget_eval]
+    omega
+  rw [SelectedPlan.advice_eq, sampleBudget_eq]
+  exact advice_budget E₁ E₂ C rows s u ((slotBudget S).eval n) o hargs hcomplete hcap
+    hrowlen _ _ aliceOutput S (slotBudget S) (tailBudget A O) (privateBudget B) R hg
+    (Nat.le_refl _) (by simpa only [tailBudget_eval] using ht) hi hAlice
+
+end TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof.SelectedAdviceBounds

+import TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.Yao.HybridProof.SelectedSeed
+import TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.Yao.HybridProof.GateHybridMessage
+
+set_option maxHeartbeats 0
+set_option relaxedAutoImplicit false
+set_option autoImplicit false
+
+/-!
+# Fields unaffected by selected inactive-key replacement
+
+The selected fill changes the symbolic key label `(u,true)`. False active
+keys, unrelated decoding pairs and the encoded message tail therefore remain
+fixed. These are reader-based consequences, with no key-value inequality or
+encryption hypothesis. Stage output labels are separated from the selected
+argument label by circuit acyclicity and the completed-gate rule.
+
+## Main definitions
+
+None.
+
+## Main results
+
+* `keyAt_of_label_ne`, `keyAt_false`: total key lookup outside the selected label is unchanged.
+* Active answers and unrelated output decoding pairs are unchanged by selected fill.
+* The message tail is independent of selected fill and the hybrid stage.
+* Missing-argument steps are equal without selecting a dummy wire.
+* Symbolic plaintext and unselected-row support holds at both actual argument positions.
+
+## References
+
+* [PS10] R. Pass, a. shelat, *A Course in Cryptography*, lecture notes, 3rd ed.,
+  January 2010, Protocol 162.6 and Theorem 163.7 (the gate-by-gate hybrid).
+-/
+
+namespace TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof.SelectedFields
+
+open Complexity Turing BoolCircuit TCSlib.Cryptography
+
+section Keys
+
+variable {inputs : ℕ} {K ρ : Type} [Inhabited K]
+  (C : DAGCircuit inputs)
+  (rows : (i : Fin C.gates.length) → List (Fin (C.gates[i.val]).args.length → Bool))
+  (s : GarbleSeed C K (ρ × ρ)) (u : Fin C.size) (q : ℕ) (k : K)
+  (rs : Fin (q + 1) → ρ)
+
+/-- Total key lookup is unchanged at every symbolic label other than `(u,true)`,
+including the default region outside the circuit. [PS10, Theorem 163.7]
+`keyAt C (SelectedSeed.fill C rows s u q k rs) w b = keyAt C s w b`. -/
+theorem keyAt_of_label_ne (w : ℕ) (b : Bool) (h : (w, b) ≠ (u.val, true)) :
+    keyAt C (SelectedSeed.fill C rows s u q k rs) w b = keyAt C s w b := by
+  rw [SelectedSeed.fill_keyAt, if_neg h]
+
+/-- False active keys are unchanged at every vertex, including default vertices.
+This is a symbolic-label fact and does not require distinct key values.
+`keyAt C (SelectedSeed.fill C rows s u q k rs) w false = keyAt C s w false`. -/
+theorem keyAt_false (w : ℕ) :
+    keyAt C (SelectedSeed.fill C rows s u q k rs) w false = keyAt C s w false := by
+  simp only [SelectedSeed.fill_keyAt, Prod.mk.injEq, Bool.false_eq_true, and_false, ite_false]
+
+/-- Both key choices are unchanged at a vertex different from the selected vertex.
+`keyAt C (SelectedSeed.fill C rows s u q k rs) w b = keyAt C s w b`. -/
+theorem keyAt_of_ne (w : ℕ) (b : Bool) (hw : w ≠ u.val) :
+    keyAt C (SelectedSeed.fill C rows s u q k rs) w b = keyAt C s w b :=
+  keyAt_of_label_ne C rows s u q k rs w b (fun h => hw (congrArg Prod.fst h))
+
+/-- An output decoding pair at an unrelated vertex is unchanged, for every
+prescribed result bit. No encryption assumption is needed.
+`FakeObjects.decodePair C (SelectedSeed.fill C rows s u q k rs) w b = FakeObjects.decodePair C s w
+b`. -/
+theorem decodePair_fill (w : ℕ) (b : Bool) (hw : w ≠ u.val) :
+    FakeObjects.decodePair C (SelectedSeed.fill C rows s u q k rs) w b =
+      FakeObjects.decodePair C s w b := by
+  rw [FakeObjects.decodePair_eq, FakeObjects.decodePair_eq,
+    keyAt_of_ne C rows s u q k rs w b hw, keyAt_of_ne C rows s u q k rs w (!b) hw]
+
+end Keys
+
+/-- The complete false-key answer vector is unchanged by selected fill for
+all starting vertices and counts, including zero key width.
+`FakeObjects.activeAnswers C (SelectedSeed.fill C rows s u q k rs) first count =
+FakeObjects.activeAnswers C s first count`. -/
+theorem activeAnswers_fill {inputs width : ℕ} {ρ : Type} (C : DAGCircuit inputs)
+    (rows : (i : Fin C.gates.length) → List (Fin (C.gates[i.val]).args.length → Bool))
+    (s : GarbleSeed C (Fin width → Bool) (ρ × ρ)) (u : Fin C.size) (q : ℕ)
+    (k : Fin width → Bool) (rs : Fin (q + 1) → ρ) (first count : ℕ) :
+    FakeObjects.activeAnswers C (SelectedSeed.fill C rows s u q k rs) first count =
+      FakeObjects.activeAnswers C s first count := by
+  funext t
+  rw [FakeObjects.activeAnswers_eq, FakeObjects.activeAnswers_eq, keyAt_false]
+
+/-- The encoded message tail is independent of the hybrid stage: only the
+encoded table head depends on the stage.
+`pairSndD (GateHybridMessage.message M C s inA outLen output v stage) = pairSndD
+(GateHybridMessage.message M C s inA outLen output v stage')`. -/
+theorem message_tail_stage_eq {inputs width : ℕ} {ρ : Type}
+    (M : MultiEnc (Fin width → Bool) (List Bool) ρ) (C : DAGCircuit inputs)
+    (s : GarbleSeed C (Fin width → Bool) ρ) (inA outLen : ℕ)
+    (output : Fin outLen → ℕ) (v : ℕ → Bool) (stage stage' : ℕ) :
+    pairSndD (GateHybridMessage.message M C s inA outLen output v stage) =
+      pairSndD (GateHybridMessage.message M C s inA outLen output v stage') := by
+  simp only [GateHybridMessage.message_eq, pairSndD_pairEncode]
+
+/-- The encoded message tail is unchanged by selected fill when no output
+vertex is the selected argument vertex. [PS10, Theorem 163.7]
+`pairSndD (GateHybridMessage.message M C (SelectedSeed.fill C rows s u q k rs) inA outLen output v
+stage) = pairSndD (GateHybridMessage.message M C s inA outLen output v stage)`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** Read the message pair and discard its table head. Every
+encoded Alice input key is false and is unchanged. Each output decoding pair
+is at an unrelated vertex and is unchanged. Encoding these equal component
+vectors preserves the entire tail. -/
+theorem message_tail_fill {inputs width : ℕ} {ρ : Type}
+    (M : MultiEnc (Fin width → Bool) (List Bool) (ρ × ρ)) (C : DAGCircuit inputs)
+    (rows : (i : Fin C.gates.length) → List (Fin (C.gates[i.val]).args.length → Bool))
+    (s : GarbleSeed C (Fin width → Bool) (ρ × ρ)) (u : Fin C.size) (q : ℕ)
+    (k : Fin width → Bool) (rs : Fin (q + 1) → ρ) (inA outLen : ℕ)
+    (output : Fin outLen → ℕ) (v : ℕ → Bool) (stage : ℕ)
+    (hOut : ∀ j, output j ≠ u.val) :
+    pairSndD (GateHybridMessage.message M C (SelectedSeed.fill C rows s u q k rs)
+      inA outLen output v stage) =
+      pairSndD (GateHybridMessage.message M C s inA outLen output v stage) := by
+  rw [GateHybridMessage.message_eq, GateHybridMessage.message_eq,
+    pairSndD_pairEncode, pairSndD_pairEncode]
+  congr 1
+  · apply congrArg BinArith.encWords
+    apply congrArg List.ofFn
+    funext w
+    rw [keyAt_false]
+  · apply congrArg BinArith.encWords
+    apply congrArg List.ofFn
+    funext j
+    simp only [decodePair_fill C rows s u q k rs (output j) (v (output j)) (hOut j)]
+
+/-- Every hybrid output-key label differs from `(u,true)` when the selected
+wire precedes the current cutoff vertex. This concerns symbolic labels, not
+inequality of key values. [PS10, Theorem 163.7]
+`(inputs + i.val, GateHybrids.outputBit C v cutoff phase i a) ≠ (u, true)`. -/
+theorem outputBit_plaintext_ne_of_lt {inputs : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit inputs)
+    (v : ℕ → Bool) (cutoff phase u : ℕ) (hu : u < inputs + cutoff)
+    (i : Fin C.gates.length) (a : Fin (C.gates[i.val]).args.length → Bool) :
+    (inputs + i.val, GateHybrids.outputBit C v cutoff phase i a) ≠ (u, true) := by
+  have h := GateOccurrences.hybrid_plaintext_ne inputs cutoff u i.val hu
+    (GateHybrids.outputBit C v cutoff phase i a)
+  by_cases hi : i.val < cutoff
+  · rw [GateHybrids.outputBit_completed C v cutoff phase i a hi]
+    simpa only [if_pos hi] using h
+  · simpa only [if_neg hi] using h
+
+/-- Selecting a current gate's argument gives a different symbolic key label
+from every hybrid row plaintext, at every phase. [PS10, Theorem 163.7]
+`(inputs + i.val, GateHybrids.outputBit C v i₀.val phase i a) ≠ (u, true)`. -/
+theorem outputBit_plaintext_ne {inputs : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit inputs) (v : ℕ → Bool)
+    (i₀ : Fin C.gates.length) (phase u : ℕ) (hu : u ∈ (C.gates[i₀.val]).args)
+    (i : Fin C.gates.length) (a : Fin (C.gates[i.val]).args.length → Bool) :
+    (inputs + i.val, GateHybrids.outputBit C v i₀.val phase i a) ≠ (u, true) :=
+  outputBit_plaintext_ne_of_lt C v i₀.val phase u
+    (GateOccurrences.selected_wire_lt C i₀ u hu) i a
+
+/-- At the even stage of a gate, all row plaintext labels avoid its selected
+argument's inactive key label.
+`(inputs + i.val, GateHybrids.stageBit C v (2 * i₀.val) i a) ≠ (u, true)`. -/
+theorem stageBit_plaintext_ne_even {inputs : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit inputs) (v : ℕ → Bool)
+    (i₀ : Fin C.gates.length) (u : ℕ) (hu : u ∈ (C.gates[i₀.val]).args)
+    (i : Fin C.gates.length) (a : Fin (C.gates[i.val]).args.length → Bool) :
+    (inputs + i.val, GateHybrids.stageBit C v (2 * i₀.val) i a) ≠ (u, true) := by
+  rw [GateHybrids.stageBit_even]
+  exact outputBit_plaintext_ne C v i₀ 0 u hu i a
+
+/-- At the odd stage of a gate, all row plaintext labels avoid its selected
+argument's inactive key label.
+`(inputs + i.val, GateHybrids.stageBit C v (2 * i₀.val + 1) i a) ≠ (u, true)`. -/
+theorem stageBit_plaintext_ne_odd {inputs : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit inputs) (v : ℕ → Bool)
+    (i₀ : Fin C.gates.length) (u : ℕ) (hu : u ∈ (C.gates[i₀.val]).args)
+    (i : Fin C.gates.length) (a : Fin (C.gates[i.val]).args.length → Bool) :
+    (inputs + i.val, GateHybrids.stageBit C v (2 * i₀.val + 1) i a) ≠ (u, true) := by
+  rw [GateHybrids.stageBit_odd]
+  exact outputBit_plaintext_ne C v i₀ 1 u hu i a
+
+/-- After completing the current gate, all row plaintext labels still avoid
+its selected argument's inactive key label.
+`(inputs + i.val, GateHybrids.stageBit C v (2 * i₀.val + 2) i a) ≠ (u, true)`. -/
+theorem stageBit_plaintext_ne_next {inputs : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit inputs) (v : ℕ → Bool)
+    (i₀ : Fin C.gates.length) (u : ℕ) (hu : u ∈ (C.gates[i₀.val]).args)
+    (i : Fin C.gates.length) (a : Fin (C.gates[i.val]).args.length → Bool) :
+    (inputs + i.val, GateHybrids.stageBit C v (2 * i₀.val + 2) i a) ≠ (u, true) := by
+  have hlt := GateOccurrences.selected_wire_lt C i₀ u hu
+  have hu' : u < inputs + (i₀.val + 1) := by omega
+  have hstage : 2 * i₀.val + 2 = 2 * (i₀.val + 1) := by omega
+  rw [hstage, GateHybrids.stageBit_even]
+  exact outputBit_plaintext_ne_of_lt C v (i₀.val + 1) 0 u hu' i a
+
+/-- The first substep at an arity-zero gate changes no stage label. The
+statement requires no selected wire because the first argument is absent.
+`GateHybrids.stageBit C v (2 * i₀.val) = GateHybrids.stageBit C v (2 * i₀.val + 1)`. -/
+theorem stage_first_of_arity_zero {inputs : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit inputs) (v : ℕ → Bool)
+    (i₀ : Fin C.gates.length) (hzero : (C.gates[i₀.val]).args.length = 0) :
+    GateHybrids.stageBit C v (2 * i₀.val) =
+      GateHybrids.stageBit C v (2 * i₀.val + 1) := by
+  rw [GateHybrids.stageBit_even, GateHybrids.stageBit_odd]
+  funext i a
+  apply GateHybrids.outputBit_first_unchanged
+  by_cases hi : i = i₀
+  · subst i
+    right
+    exact List.getD_eq_default (List.ofFn a) false (n := 0) (by
+      simpa only [List.length_ofFn, Fin.getElem_fin, hzero] using (Nat.le_refl 0))
+  · exact Or.inl (fun h => hi (Fin.ext h))
+
+/-- The second substep at a gate of arity at most one changes no stage label
+for consistent wire values. No second argument or selected wire is required.
+`GateHybrids.stageBit C v (2 * i₀.val + 1) = GateHybrids.stageBit C v (2 * i₀.val + 2)`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** Other gates are unchanged by cutoff advancement. At the
+current gate the padded second bit is false because the assignment has length
+at most one. The existing second-substep support theorem then applies; its
+all-zero case is justified by the supplied circuit-consistency equation. -/
+theorem stage_second_of_arity_le_one {inputs : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit inputs)
+    (v : ℕ → Bool)
+    (hA : ∀ i : Fin C.gates.length, (C.gates[i]).args.length ≤ 2)
+    (hEval : ∀ i : Fin C.gates.length,
+      gateOut (C.gates[i]) (RelabelGarbling.argValues C v i) = v (inputs + i))
+    (i₀ : Fin C.gates.length) (hsmall : (C.gates[i₀.val]).args.length ≤ 1) :
+    GateHybrids.stageBit C v (2 * i₀.val + 1) =
+      GateHybrids.stageBit C v (2 * i₀.val + 2) := by
+  have hstage : 2 * i₀.val + 2 = 2 * (i₀.val + 1) := by omega
+  rw [GateHybrids.stageBit_odd, hstage, GateHybrids.stageBit_even]
+  funext i a
+  apply GateHybrids.outputBit_second_unchanged C v hA hEval i₀.val i a
+  by_cases hi : i = i₀
+  · subst i
+    right; right
+    exact List.getD_eq_default (List.ofFn a) false (n := 1) (by
+      simpa only [List.length_ofFn, Fin.getElem_fin] using hsmall)
+  · exact Or.inl (fun h => hi (Fin.ext h))
+
+/-- Equal stage-label families give equal complete encoded messages.
+`GateHybridMessage.message M C s inA outLen output v stage = GateHybridMessage.message M C s inA
+outLen output v stage'`. -/
+theorem message_eq_of_stageBit_eq {inputs width : ℕ} {ρ : Type}
+    (M : MultiEnc (Fin width → Bool) (List Bool) ρ) (C : DAGCircuit inputs)
+    (s : GarbleSeed C (Fin width → Bool) ρ) (inA outLen : ℕ)
+    (output : Fin outLen → ℕ) (v : ℕ → Bool) (stage stage' : ℕ)
+    (h : GateHybrids.stageBit C v stage = GateHybrids.stageBit C v stage') :
+    GateHybridMessage.message M C s inA outLen output v stage =
+      GateHybridMessage.message M C s inA outLen output v stage' := by
+  rw [GateHybridMessage.message_eq, GateHybridMessage.message_eq, h]
+
+/-- Equal stage-label families give exactly equal Bob-view samples for every
+seed and prescribed Alice output.
+`GateHybridMessage.sample M C s inA inB outLen output v commonInput bobInput bobCoins aliceOutput
+stage = GateHybridMessage.sample M C s inA inB outLen output v commonInput bobInput bobCoins
+aliceOutput stage'`. -/
+theorem sample_eq_of_stageBit_eq {inputs width : ℕ} {ρ : Type}
+    (M : MultiEnc (Fin width → Bool) (List Bool) ρ) (C : DAGCircuit inputs)
+    (s : GarbleSeed C (Fin width → Bool) ρ) (inA inB outLen : ℕ)
+    (output : Fin outLen → ℕ) (v : ℕ → Bool)
+    (commonInput bobInput bobCoins aliceOutput : List Bool) (stage stage' : ℕ)
+    (h : GateHybrids.stageBit C v stage = GateHybrids.stageBit C v stage') :
+    GateHybridMessage.sample M C s inA inB outLen output v
+        commonInput bobInput bobCoins aliceOutput stage =
+      GateHybridMessage.sample M C s inA inB outLen output v
+        commonInput bobInput bobCoins aliceOutput stage' := by
+  rw [GateHybridMessage.sample_eq, GateHybridMessage.sample_eq,
+    message_eq_of_stageBit_eq M C s inA outLen output v stage stage' h]
+
+/-- If no selected position exists, any argument at the selected wire has
+false assignment bit. This directly specializes the published position reader.
+`a p = false`. -/
+theorem selectedPosition_none_bit (g : DAGGate) (a : Fin g.args.length → Bool)
+    (u : ℕ) (hargs : g.args.Nodup) (p : Fin g.args.length) (hp : g.args[p.val] = u)
+    (hNone : GateOccurrences.selectedPosition g a u = none) : a p = false := by
+  cases hbit : a p with
+  | false => rfl
+  | true =>
+    have hsome := (GateOccurrences.selectedPosition_eq_some_iff g a u hargs p).mpr
+      ⟨hp, hbit⟩
+    rw [hNone] at hsome
+    cases hsome
+
+/-- The encoded assignment bit at a selected wire is false when the selected
+position search returns none. The list lookup uses the existing `ofFn` readers.
+`(List.ofFn a).getD p.val false = false`. -/
+theorem selectedPosition_none_getD (g : DAGGate) (a : Fin g.args.length → Bool)
+    (u : ℕ) (hargs : g.args.Nodup) (p : Fin g.args.length) (hp : g.args[p.val] = u)
+    (hNone : GateOccurrences.selectedPosition g a u = none) :
+    (List.ofFn a).getD p.val false = false := by
+  rw [List.getD_eq_getElem (List.ofFn a) false (n := p.val)
+    (by rw [List.length_ofFn]; exact p.isLt), List.getElem_ofFn]
+  exact selectedPosition_none_bit g a u hargs p hp hNone
+
+/-- In the first substep, rows without the selected first-argument key have
+unchanged stage labels. The actual first argument is supplied, so no absent
+argument is represented by a dummy wire. [PS10, Theorem 163.7]
+`GateHybrids.stageBit C v (2 * i₀.val) i a = GateHybrids.stageBit C v (2 * i₀.val + 1) i a`. -/
+theorem stage_first_unselected {inputs : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit inputs) (v : ℕ → Bool)
+    (i₀ : Fin C.gates.length) (hargs : (C.gates[i₀.val]).args.Nodup)
+    (p : Fin (C.gates[i₀.val]).args.length) (hp : p.val = 0)
+    (u : ℕ) (hu : (C.gates[i₀.val]).args[p.val] = u)
+    (i : Fin C.gates.length) (a : Fin (C.gates[i.val]).args.length → Bool)
+    (hNone : GateOccurrences.selectedPosition (C.gates[i.val]) a u = none) :
+    GateHybrids.stageBit C v (2 * i₀.val) i a =
+      GateHybrids.stageBit C v (2 * i₀.val + 1) i a := by
+  rw [GateHybrids.stageBit_even, GateHybrids.stageBit_odd]
+  apply GateHybrids.outputBit_first_unchanged
+  by_cases hi : i = i₀
+  · subst i
+    right
+    simpa only [hp] using
+      selectedPosition_none_getD (C.gates[i₀.val]) a u hargs p hu hNone
+  · exact Or.inl (fun h => hi (Fin.ext h))
+
+/-- In the second substep, rows without the selected second-argument key
+have unchanged stage labels for consistent wire values. The actual argument
+at position one is supplied. [PS10, Theorem 163.7]
+`GateHybrids.stageBit C v (2 * i₀.val + 1) i a = GateHybrids.stageBit C v (2 * i₀.val + 2) i a`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** Outside the current gate, cutoff advancement changes no row.
+At the current gate, an unsuccessful selected-position search forces the
+second assignment bit false. The existing second-substep support theorem
+therefore gives unchanged output labels, including its active all-zero case. -/
+theorem stage_second_unselected {inputs : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit inputs) (v : ℕ → Bool)
+    (hA : ∀ i : Fin C.gates.length, (C.gates[i]).args.length ≤ 2)
+    (hEval : ∀ i : Fin C.gates.length,
+      gateOut (C.gates[i]) (RelabelGarbling.argValues C v i) = v (inputs + i))
+    (i₀ : Fin C.gates.length) (hargs : (C.gates[i₀.val]).args.Nodup)
+    (p : Fin (C.gates[i₀.val]).args.length) (hp : p.val = 1)
+    (u : ℕ) (hu : (C.gates[i₀.val]).args[p.val] = u)
+    (i : Fin C.gates.length) (a : Fin (C.gates[i.val]).args.length → Bool)
+    (hNone : GateOccurrences.selectedPosition (C.gates[i.val]) a u = none) :
+    GateHybrids.stageBit C v (2 * i₀.val + 1) i a =
+      GateHybrids.stageBit C v (2 * i₀.val + 2) i a := by
+  have hstage : 2 * i₀.val + 2 = 2 * (i₀.val + 1) := by omega
+  rw [GateHybrids.stageBit_odd, hstage, GateHybrids.stageBit_even]
+  apply GateHybrids.outputBit_second_unchanged C v hA hEval i₀.val i a
+  by_cases hi : i = i₀
+  · subst i
+    right; right
+    simpa only [hp] using
+      selectedPosition_none_getD (C.gates[i₀.val]) a u hargs p hu hNone
+  · exact Or.inl (fun h => hi (Fin.ext h))
+
+end TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof.SelectedFields

+import TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.Yao.HybridProof.CoordinateSwap
+import TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.Yao.HybridProof.SeedCoordinates
+import TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.Yao.HybridProof.TapeValues
+
+set_option maxHeartbeats 0
+set_option relaxedAutoImplicit false
+set_option autoImplicit false
+
+/-!
+# Fresh selected coordinates in the actual tape distribution
+
+## Main definitions
+
+None.
+
+## Main results
+
+* `acceptProb_fill`: filling the selected key and row nonces preserves the actual tape law.
+
+## References
+
+* [PS10] R. Pass, a. shelat, *A Course in Cryptography*, lecture notes, 3rd ed.,
+  January 2010, Theorem 163.7 (fixing all other randomness in the gate hybrid).
+-/
+
+namespace TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof.SelectedLaw
+
+open BoolCircuit TCSlib.Cryptography
+
+variable {m : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit m) (S n : ℕ)
+  (hA : ∀ i : Fin C.gates.length, (C.gates[i.val]).args.length ≤ 2) (hS : C.size ≤ S)
+  (seed : List Bool → GarbleSeed C (Fin n → Bool) ((Fin n → Bool) × (Fin n → Bool)))
+  (hK : ∀ q (w : Fin C.size) b (t : Fin n),
+    (seed q).1 w b t = q.getD ((b.toNat * S + w) * n + t) false)
+  (hR₀ : ∀ q (i : Fin C.gates.length) (a : Fin (C.gates[i.val]).args.length → Bool)
+      (t : Fin n),
+    ((seed q).2.1 i a).1 t = q.getD (2 * S * n + (4 * i +
+      (List.ofFn a).foldr (fun b k => b.toNat + 2 * k) 0) * (2 * n) + t) false)
+  (hR₁ : ∀ q (i : Fin C.gates.length) (a : Fin (C.gates[i.val]).args.length → Bool)
+      (t : Fin n),
+    ((seed q).2.1 i a).2 t = q.getD (2 * S * n + (4 * i +
+      (List.ofFn a).foldr (fun b k => b.toNat + 2 * k) 0) * (2 * n) + n + t) false)
+  (hP : ∀ q (i : Fin C.gates.length) (a : Fin (C.gates[i.val]).args.length → Bool)
+      (t : Fin (C.gates[i.val]).args.length),
+    (seed q).2.2 i a t = xor (a t) (q.getD (10 * S * n + 2 * i + t) false))
+
+
+include hA hS hK hR₀ hR₁ hP
+
+/-- For a seed with the four actual addressed tape readers, independently replacing one
+inactive key and every matched row nonce preserves every sampler's acceptance probability.
+All shifts and unused padded coordinates retain their actual distribution; there is no
+uniformity assumption on the derived assignment permutations. No occurrence-capacity or
+key-value-distinctness premise is required. [PS10, Theorem 163.7]
+`acceptProb (fun z : (Fin (10 * S * n + 2 * S) → Bool) × ((Fin n → Bool) × (Fin (q + 1) → Fin n →
+Bool)) => X (SelectedSeed.fill C rows (seed (List.ofFn z.1)) u q z.2.1 z.2.2)) D = acceptProb (fun r
+: Fin (10 * S * n + 2 * S) → Bool => X (seed (List.ofFn r))) D`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** Split the complete tape into its retained shift/unused-block parameter
+and independent actual key/nonce values. Convert the value table and challenge into their
+existing coordinate types. The seed's inverse reader identifies the original sample with
+these coordinates, and the filled-seed reader identifies replacement with the matched
+coordinate exchange. Apply that exchange's proved uniform acceptance law and transport
+back through the two coordinate equivalences. -/
+theorem acceptProb_fill
+    (rows : (i : Fin C.gates.length) → List (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool))
+    (u : Fin C.size) (q : ℕ)
+    (hargs : ∀ i : Fin C.gates.length, C.gates[i.val].args.Nodup)
+    (hcomplete : ∀ i a, a ∈ rows i) (hrows : ∀ i, (rows i).Nodup)
+    {γ κ : Type*} [Fintype κ]
+    (X : GarbleSeed C (Fin n → Bool) ((Fin n → Bool) × (Fin n → Bool)) → γ)
+    (D : γ → κ → Bool) :
+    acceptProb
+      (fun z : (Fin (10 * S * n + 2 * S) → Bool) ×
+          ((Fin n → Bool) × (Fin (q + 1) → Fin n → Bool)) =>
+        X (SelectedSeed.fill C rows (seed (List.ofFn z.1)) u q z.2.1 z.2.2)) D =
+      acceptProb (fun r : Fin (10 * S * n + 2 * S) → Bool => X (seed (List.ofFn r))) D := by
+  classical
+  let P := TapeValues.Rest C S n hA hS
+  let V := Fin n → Bool
+  let e := (TapeValues.equiv C S n hA hS).trans (SeedCoordinates.paramEquiv C V P)
+  let c := SeedCoordinates.challengeEquiv q V
+  letI : Fintype (P × (SeedCoordinates.Index C → V)) :=
+    Fintype.ofEquiv (Fin (10 * S * n + 2 * S) → Bool) e
+  letI : Fintype (SeedCoordinates.ChallengeIndex q → V) :=
+    Fintype.ofEquiv (V × (Fin (q + 1) → V)) c
+  letI : Nonempty (SeedCoordinates.ChallengeIndex q → V) :=
+    ⟨c ((fun _ => false), fun _ _ => false)⟩
+  let perm : P → SeedCoordinates.Permutations C := TapeValues.permutation C S n hA hS
+  let pick := fun p : P => SeedCoordinates.pick C rows (perm p) u q
+  let place := fun p : P => SeedCoordinates.place C rows (perm p) u q
+  have hmatch : ∀ p i j, pick p i = some j ↔ place p j = some i :=
+    fun p => SeedCoordinates.pick_iff_place C rows (perm p) u q hargs hcomplete hrows
+  have hSeed (z : P × (SeedCoordinates.Index C → V)) :
+      seed (List.ofFn (e.symm z)) =
+        (SeedCoordinates.seedEquiv C V).symm (perm z.1, z.2) := by
+    rcases z with ⟨p, full⟩
+    change seed (List.ofFn ((TapeValues.equiv C S n hA hS).symm
+      ((SeedCoordinates.paramEquiv C V P).symm (p, full)))) = _
+    rw [SeedCoordinates.paramEquiv_symm,
+      TapeValues.seed_on_inverse C S n hA hS seed hK hR₀ hR₁ hP]
+    rw [SeedCoordinates.seedEquiv_symm, SeedCoordinates.dataEquiv_symm]
+  have hFilled (z : P × (SeedCoordinates.Index C → V))
+      (t : SeedCoordinates.ChallengeIndex q → V) :
+      SelectedSeed.fill C rows (seed (List.ofFn (e.symm z))) u q
+          (c.symm t).1 (c.symm t).2 =
+        (SeedCoordinates.seedEquiv C V).symm
+          (perm (CoordinateSwap.swap pick place (z, t)).1.1,
+            (CoordinateSwap.swap pick place (z, t)).1.2) := by
+    apply (SeedCoordinates.seedEquiv C V).injective
+    have hs := (congrArg (SeedCoordinates.seedEquiv C V) (hSeed z)).trans
+      (Equiv.apply_symm_apply (SeedCoordinates.seedEquiv C V) (perm z.1, z.2))
+    rw [SeedCoordinates.seedEquiv_eq_data] at hs
+    have hp := congrArg Prod.fst hs
+    have hd := congrArg Prod.snd hs
+    change (seed (List.ofFn (e.symm z))).2.2 = perm z.1 at hp
+    change SeedCoordinates.dataEquiv C V
+      ((seed (List.ofFn (e.symm z))).1, (seed (List.ofFn (e.symm z))).2.1) = z.2 at hd
+    have hc : SeedCoordinates.challengeEquiv q V ((c.symm t).1, (c.symm t).2) = t :=
+      Equiv.apply_symm_apply c t
+    rw [Equiv.apply_symm_apply, SeedCoordinates.seedEquiv_fill, hp, hd, hc,
+      CoordinateSwap.swap_eq]
+  let Z := fun z : P × (SeedCoordinates.Index C → V) =>
+    X ((SeedCoordinates.seedEquiv C V).symm (perm z.1, z.2))
+  have hStart : acceptProb Z D =
+      acceptProb (fun r : Fin (10 * S * n + 2 * S) → Bool => X (seed (List.ofFn r))) D := by
+    have h := acceptProb_comp_equiv e.symm
+      (fun r : Fin (10 * S * n + 2 * S) → Bool => X (seed (List.ofFn r))) D
+    have hf : (fun z : P × (SeedCoordinates.Index C → V) =>
+        X (seed (List.ofFn (e.symm z)))) = Z :=
+      funext fun z => congrArg X (hSeed z)
+    rw [hf] at h
+    exact h
+  have hPoint (z : (Fin (10 * S * n + 2 * S) → Bool) ×
+      (V × (Fin (q + 1) → V))) :
+      X (SelectedSeed.fill C rows (seed (List.ofFn z.1)) u q z.2.1 z.2.2) =
+        Z (CoordinateSwap.swap pick place ((e.prodCongr c) z)).1 := by
+    have h := congrArg X (hFilled (e z.1) (c z.2))
+    rw [Equiv.symm_apply_apply e z.1, Equiv.symm_apply_apply c z.2] at h
+    change X (SelectedSeed.fill C rows (seed (List.ofFn z.1)) u q z.2.1 z.2.2) =
+      X ((SeedCoordinates.seedEquiv C V).symm
+        (perm (CoordinateSwap.swap pick place (e z.1, c z.2)).1.1,
+          (CoordinateSwap.swap pick place (e z.1, c z.2)).1.2))
+    exact h
+  have hTransport := acceptProb_comp_equiv (e.prodCongr c)
+    (fun z : (P × (SeedCoordinates.Index C → V)) ×
+        (SeedCoordinates.ChallengeIndex q → V) => Z (CoordinateSwap.swap pick place z).1) D
+  have hf : (fun z : (Fin (10 * S * n + 2 * S) → Bool) ×
+      (V × (Fin (q + 1) → V)) =>
+      X (SelectedSeed.fill C rows (seed (List.ofFn z.1)) u q z.2.1 z.2.2)) =
+    (fun z => Z (CoordinateSwap.swap pick place ((e.prodCongr c) z)).1) := funext hPoint
+  rw [hf]
+  exact hTransport.trans ((CoordinateSwap.acceptProb_swap pick place hmatch Z D).trans hStart)
+
+end TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof.SelectedLaw
