+import TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.Yao.HybridProof.SelectedSeed
+import TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.Yao.HybridProof.SelectedRows
+
+set_option maxHeartbeats 0
+set_option relaxedAutoImplicit false
+set_option autoImplicit false
+
+/-!
+# Fixed selected-key slice messages and table descriptors
+
+The circuit, original seed, row enumeration, selected wire and plaintext-key family are
+fixed before the challenge key or nonce vector is supplied. The padded occurrence list
+chooses every slice and precomputed message. Unused padding encrypts a fixed zero word.
+Reconstruction records retain unaffected rows and reference the selected rows' challenge slots.
+
+## Main definitions
+
+* `SelectedPlan.record`: one row's fixed or challenge-dependent reconstruction record.
+* `SelectedPlan.slotOffset`, `slotLength`, `slotWord`, `slotMessage`: all padded slice inputs.
+* `SelectedPlan.tables`: the original table/row order of reconstruction records.
+* `SelectedPlan.advice`: their serialized word with fixed surrounding view fields.
+
+## Main results
+
+* Exact padded slice-message lengths and reader contracts.
+
+## References
+
+* [PS10] R. Pass, a. shelat, *A Course in Cryptography*, lecture notes, 3rd ed., January
+  2010, Theorem 163.7 (all ciphertexts under one selected inactive key).
+* [LP09] Y. Lindell, B. Pinkas, *A proof of security of Yao's protocol for two-party
+  computation*, Journal of Cryptology 22(2), 2009, pp. 161–188.
+-/
+
+namespace TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof.SelectedPlan
+
+open Complexity Turing BoolCircuit TCSlib.Cryptography
+
+variable {m n : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit m)
+  (rows : (i : Fin C.gates.length) → List (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool))
+  (s : GarbleSeed C (Fin n → Bool) ((Fin n → Bool) × (Fin n → Bool)))
+  (u : Fin C.size) (q : ℕ)
+
+/-- A row with no selected label retains its precomputed ciphertext. A selected row uses
+the challenge descriptor for its unique selected argument and occurrence rank.
+`record C rows s u E₁ E₂ o i a = (GateOccurrences.selectedPosition C.gates[i.val] a u.val).elim
+(SliceReconstruction.fixedRecord ((nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ n).enc (argKeys C s i a) (o i a) (s.2.1 i
+a))) (fun p => SelectedRows.descriptor n (argKeys C s i a) (s.2.1 i a) p.val (SelectedSeed.slotIndex
+C rows s.2.2 u.val i a p))`. -/
+noncomputable def record (E₁ E₂ : List Bool → List Bool)
+    (o : (i : Fin C.gates.length) → (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool) → (Fin n → Bool))
+    (i : Fin C.gates.length) (a : Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool) : List Bool :=
+  (GateOccurrences.selectedPosition C.gates[i.val] a u.val).elim
+    (SliceReconstruction.fixedRecord ((nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ n).enc
+      (argKeys C s i a) (o i a) (s.2.1 i a)))
+    (fun p => SelectedRows.descriptor n (argKeys C s i a) (s.2.1 i a) p.val
+      (SelectedSeed.slotIndex C rows s.2.2 u.val i a p))
+
+/-- `record` is a fixed ciphertext for an unselected row and the selected-layer descriptor
+at its occurrence rank for a selected row.
+`record C rows s u E₁ E₂ o i a = (GateOccurrences.selectedPosition C.gates[i.val] a u.val).elim
+(SliceReconstruction.fixedRecord ((nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ n).enc (argKeys C s i a) (o i a) (s.2.1 i
+a))) (fun p => SelectedRows.descriptor n (argKeys C s i a) (s.2.1 i a) p.val (SelectedSeed.slotIndex
+C rows s.2.2 u.val i a p))`. -/
+theorem record_eq (E₁ E₂ : List Bool → List Bool)
+    (o : (i : Fin C.gates.length) → (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool) → (Fin n → Bool))
+    (i : Fin C.gates.length) (a : Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool) :
+    record C rows s u E₁ E₂ o i a =
+      (GateOccurrences.selectedPosition C.gates[i.val] a u.val).elim
+        (SliceReconstruction.fixedRecord ((nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ n).enc
+          (argKeys C s i a) (o i a) (s.2.1 i a)))
+        (fun p => SelectedRows.descriptor n (argKeys C s i a) (s.2.1 i a) p.val
+          (SelectedSeed.slotIndex C rows s.2.2 u.val i a p)) := rfl
+
+/-- A challenge slot has the selected row's layer offset, or zero for unused padding.
+`slotOffset C rows s u q j = (GateOccurrences.padded q (GateOccurrences.occurrences C rows s.2.2
+u.val) j).elim 0 (fun t => SelectedRows.offset n (argKeys C s t.1 (s.2.2 t.1 ((rows t.1).getD t.2.1
+(fun _ => false)))) t.2.2)`. -/
+noncomputable def slotOffset (j : Fin (q + 1)) : ℕ :=
+  (GateOccurrences.padded q (GateOccurrences.occurrences C rows s.2.2 u.val) j).elim 0 fun t =>
+    SelectedRows.offset n
+      (argKeys C s t.1 (s.2.2 t.1 ((rows t.1).getD t.2.1 (fun _ => false)))) t.2.2
+
+/-- `slotOffset` reads the selected row's layer offset, with padding offset zero.
+`slotOffset C rows s u q j = (GateOccurrences.padded q (GateOccurrences.occurrences C rows s.2.2
+u.val) j).elim 0 (fun t => SelectedRows.offset n (argKeys C s t.1 (s.2.2 t.1 ((rows t.1).getD t.2.1
+(fun _ => false)))) t.2.2)`. -/
+theorem slotOffset_eq (j : Fin (q + 1)) :
+    slotOffset C rows s u q j =
+      (GateOccurrences.padded q (GateOccurrences.occurrences C rows s.2.2 u.val) j).elim 0
+        (fun t => SelectedRows.offset n
+          (argKeys C s t.1 (s.2.2 t.1 ((rows t.1).getD t.2.1 (fun _ => false)))) t.2.2) := rfl
+
+/-- A challenge slot has its selected row's message length, or `n` for unused padding.
+`slotLength C rows s u q j = (GateOccurrences.padded q (GateOccurrences.occurrences C rows s.2.2
+u.val) j).elim n (fun t => SelectedRows.length n (argKeys C s t.1 (s.2.2 t.1 ((rows t.1).getD t.2.1
+(fun _ => false)))) t.2.2)`. -/
+noncomputable def slotLength (j : Fin (q + 1)) : ℕ :=
+  (GateOccurrences.padded q (GateOccurrences.occurrences C rows s.2.2 u.val) j).elim n fun t =>
+    SelectedRows.length n
+      (argKeys C s t.1 (s.2.2 t.1 ((rows t.1).getD t.2.1 (fun _ => false)))) t.2.2
+
+/-- `slotLength` reads the selected row's message length, with padding length `n`.
+`slotLength C rows s u q j = (GateOccurrences.padded q (GateOccurrences.occurrences C rows s.2.2
+u.val) j).elim n (fun t => SelectedRows.length n (argKeys C s t.1 (s.2.2 t.1 ((rows t.1).getD t.2.1
+(fun _ => false)))) t.2.2)`. -/
+theorem slotLength_eq (j : Fin (q + 1)) :
+    slotLength C rows s u q j =
+      (GateOccurrences.padded q (GateOccurrences.occurrences C rows s.2.2 u.val) j).elim n
+        (fun t => SelectedRows.length n
+          (argKeys C s t.1 (s.2.2 t.1 ((rows t.1).getD t.2.1 (fun _ => false)))) t.2.2) := rfl
+
+/-- A challenge slot's precomputed plaintext word uses only fixed keys and nonce halves;
+unused padding has the fixed `n`-bit zero word.
+`slotWord C rows s u q E₁ o j = (GateOccurrences.padded q (GateOccurrences.occurrences C rows s.2.2
+u.val) j).elim (List.replicate n false) (fun t => let a := s.2.2 t.1 ((rows t.1).getD t.2.1 (fun _
+=> false)); SelectedRows.word E₁ n (argKeys C s t.1 a) (o t.1 a) (s.2.1 t.1 a) t.2.2)`. -/
+noncomputable def slotWord (E₁ : List Bool → List Bool)
+    (o : (i : Fin C.gates.length) → (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool) → (Fin n → Bool))
+    (j : Fin (q + 1)) : List Bool :=
+  (GateOccurrences.padded q (GateOccurrences.occurrences C rows s.2.2 u.val) j).elim
+    (List.replicate n false) fun t =>
+      let a := s.2.2 t.1 ((rows t.1).getD t.2.1 (fun _ => false))
+      SelectedRows.word E₁ n (argKeys C s t.1 a) (o t.1 a) (s.2.1 t.1 a) t.2.2
+
+/-- `slotWord` reads the selected row's precomputed message, with zero-word padding.
+`slotWord C rows s u q E₁ o j = (GateOccurrences.padded q (GateOccurrences.occurrences C rows s.2.2
+u.val) j).elim (List.replicate n false) (fun t => let a := s.2.2 t.1 ((rows t.1).getD t.2.1 (fun _
+=> false)); SelectedRows.word E₁ n (argKeys C s t.1 a) (o t.1 a) (s.2.1 t.1 a) t.2.2)`. -/
+theorem slotWord_eq (E₁ : List Bool → List Bool)
+    (o : (i : Fin C.gates.length) → (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool) → (Fin n → Bool))
+    (j : Fin (q + 1)) :
+    slotWord C rows s u q E₁ o j =
+      (GateOccurrences.padded q (GateOccurrences.occurrences C rows s.2.2 u.val) j).elim
+        (List.replicate n false) (fun t =>
+          let a := s.2.2 t.1 ((rows t.1).getD t.2.1 (fun _ => false))
+          SelectedRows.word E₁ n (argKeys C s t.1 a) (o t.1 a) (s.2.1 t.1 a) t.2.2) := rfl
+
+/-- The typed padded slot message is `padFin` of its precomputed word at its slot length.
+`slotMessage C rows s u q E₁ o j = padFin (slotLength C rows s u q j) (slotWord C rows s u q E₁ o
+j)`. -/
+noncomputable def slotMessage (E₁ : List Bool → List Bool)
+    (o : (i : Fin C.gates.length) → (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool) → (Fin n → Bool))
+    (j : Fin (q + 1)) : Fin (slotLength C rows s u q j) → Bool :=
+  padFin (slotLength C rows s u q j) (slotWord C rows s u q E₁ o j)
+
+/-- `slotMessage` is the padded typed view of the slot's precomputed word.
+`slotMessage C rows s u q E₁ o j = padFin (slotLength C rows s u q j) (slotWord C rows s u q E₁ o
+j)`. -/
+theorem slotMessage_eq (E₁ : List Bool → List Bool)
+    (o : (i : Fin C.gates.length) → (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool) → (Fin n → Bool))
+    (j : Fin (q + 1)) :
+    slotMessage C rows s u q E₁ o j =
+      padFin (slotLength C rows s u q j) (slotWord C rows s u q E₁ o j) := rfl
+
+/-- The descriptor tables retain every gate and every enumerated row in its original order.
+`tables C rows s u E₁ E₂ o = List.ofFn (fun i => (rows i).map fun a => record C rows s u E₁ E₂ o i
+(s.2.2 i a))`. -/
+noncomputable def tables (E₁ E₂ : List Bool → List Bool)
+    (o : (i : Fin C.gates.length) → (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool) → (Fin n → Bool)) :
+    List (List (List Bool)) :=
+  List.ofFn fun i => (rows i).map fun a => record C rows s u E₁ E₂ o i (s.2.2 i a)
+
+/-- `tables` is the `ofFn` list of each gate's enumerated, permuted row records.
+`tables C rows s u E₁ E₂ o = List.ofFn (fun i => (rows i).map fun a => record C rows s u E₁ E₂ o i
+(s.2.2 i a))`. -/
+theorem tables_eq (E₁ E₂ : List Bool → List Bool)
+    (o : (i : Fin C.gates.length) → (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool) → (Fin n → Bool)) :
+    tables C rows s u E₁ E₂ o =
+      List.ofFn (fun i => (rows i).map fun a => record C rows s u E₁ E₂ o i (s.2.2 i a)) := rfl
+
+/-- Every slot word has exactly its prescribed message length, including unused padding.
+`(slotWord C rows s u q E₁ o j).length = slotLength C rows s u q j`. -/
+theorem slotWord_length
+    {G₁ : ∀ k, (Fin k → Bool) → (Fin k → Bool) → (Fin (k + k) → Bool)}
+    {E₁ : List Bool → List Bool}
+    (hGE₁ : ∀ k (key nonce : Fin k → Bool),
+      E₁ (pairEncode (List.ofFn key) (List.ofFn nonce)) = List.ofFn (G₁ k key nonce))
+    (o : (i : Fin C.gates.length) → (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool) → (Fin n → Bool))
+    (j : Fin (q + 1)) :
+    (slotWord C rows s u q E₁ o j).length = slotLength C rows s u q j := by
+  rw [slotWord_eq, slotLength_eq]
+  cases hpad : GateOccurrences.padded q (GateOccurrences.occurrences C rows s.2.2 u.val) j with
+  | none => simp only [Option.elim_none, List.length_replicate]
+  | some t =>
+    simp only [Option.elim_some]
+    exact SelectedRows.word_length hGE₁ n _ _ _ _
+
+/-- Encoding a typed slot message returns its precomputed word exactly, including padding.
+`List.ofFn (slotMessage C rows s u q E₁ o j) = slotWord C rows s u q E₁ o j`. -/
+theorem slotMessage_ofFn
+    {G₁ : ∀ k, (Fin k → Bool) → (Fin k → Bool) → (Fin (k + k) → Bool)}
+    {E₁ : List Bool → List Bool}
+    (hGE₁ : ∀ k (key nonce : Fin k → Bool),
+      E₁ (pairEncode (List.ofFn key) (List.ofFn nonce)) = List.ofFn (G₁ k key nonce))
+    (o : (i : Fin C.gates.length) → (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool) → (Fin n → Bool))
+    (j : Fin (q + 1)) :
+    List.ofFn (slotMessage C rows s u q E₁ o j) = slotWord C rows s u q E₁ o j := by
+  rw [slotMessage_eq, EnsembleLemmas.ofFn_padFin_eq_padL, EnsembleLemmas.padL_eq,
+    ← slotWord_length C rows s u q hGE₁ o j, List.take_left]
+
+/-- At a stored occurrence triple, all three slice readers are the corresponding row's
+precomputed offset, message length and word after its fixed assignment permutation.
+`let a := s.2.2 i ((rows i).getD row (fun _ => false)); slotOffset C rows s u q j =
+SelectedRows.offset n (argKeys C s i a) p ∧ slotLength C rows s u q j = SelectedRows.length n
+(argKeys C s i a) p ∧ slotWord C rows s u q E₁ o j = SelectedRows.word E₁ n (argKeys C s i a) (o i
+a) (s.2.1 i a) p`. -/
+theorem slotReaders_of_padded (E₁ : List Bool → List Bool)
+    (o : (i : Fin C.gates.length) → (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool) → (Fin n → Bool))
+    (j : Fin (q + 1)) (i : Fin C.gates.length) (row p : ℕ)
+    (hpad : GateOccurrences.padded q (GateOccurrences.occurrences C rows s.2.2 u.val) j =
+      some (i, row, p)) :
+    let a := s.2.2 i ((rows i).getD row (fun _ => false))
+    slotOffset C rows s u q j = SelectedRows.offset n (argKeys C s i a) p ∧
+      slotLength C rows s u q j = SelectedRows.length n (argKeys C s i a) p ∧
+      slotWord C rows s u q E₁ o j = SelectedRows.word E₁ n (argKeys C s i a)
+        (o i a) (s.2.1 i a) p := by
+  simp only [slotOffset_eq, slotLength_eq, slotWord_eq, hpad, Option.elim_some, and_self]
+
+/-- Every padded slot's offset and message length fit in the joint pad of length
+`2n+(n+(4n+2))`, including unused slots whose shape is `(0,n)`.
+`slotOffset C rows s u q j + n + slotLength C rows s u q j ≤ (n + n) + (n + (4 * n + 2))`. -/
+theorem slots_fit (j : Fin (q + 1)) :
+    slotOffset C rows s u q j + n + slotLength C rows s u q j ≤
+      (n + n) + (n + (4 * n + 2)) := by
+  rw [slotOffset_eq, slotLength_eq]
+  cases hpad : GateOccurrences.padded q (GateOccurrences.occurrences C rows s.2.2 u.val) j with
+  | none =>
+    simp only [Option.elim_none]
+    exact (SelectedSlices.slice_shapes_fit n).1
+  | some t =>
+    simp only [Option.elim_some]
+    exact SelectedRows.fits n _ _
+
+/-- Two plaintext families give the same row record if they agree on rows with no selected
+inactive argument. Selected-row records themselves do not contain the plaintext.
+`record C rows s u E₁ E₂ o₀ i a = record C rows s u E₁ E₂ o₁ i a`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** For an unselected row, the fixed ciphertexts agree by the plaintext
+hypothesis. For a selected row, both records are the same direct or known-key wrapped
+challenge reference, independent of the plaintext family. -/
+theorem record_congr (E₁ E₂ : List Bool → List Bool)
+    (o₀ o₁ : (i : Fin C.gates.length) →
+      (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool) → (Fin n → Bool))
+    (i : Fin C.gates.length) (a : Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool)
+    (ho : GateOccurrences.selectedPosition C.gates[i.val] a u.val = none → o₀ i a = o₁ i a) :
+    record C rows s u E₁ E₂ o₀ i a = record C rows s u E₁ E₂ o₁ i a := by
+  rw [record_eq, record_eq]
+  cases hsel : GateOccurrences.selectedPosition C.gates[i.val] a u.val with
+  | none => simp only [Option.elim_none]; rw [ho hsel]
+  | some p => simp only [Option.elim_some]
+
+/-- Two plaintext families have the same complete descriptor tables when they agree on
+all rows without a selected inactive argument. Thus both slice experiments use one advice.
+`tables C rows s u E₁ E₂ o₀ = tables C rows s u E₁ E₂ o₁`. -/
+theorem tables_congr (E₁ E₂ : List Bool → List Bool)
+    (o₀ o₁ : (i : Fin C.gates.length) →
+      (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool) → (Fin n → Bool))
+    (ho : ∀ i a, GateOccurrences.selectedPosition C.gates[i.val] a u.val = none →
+      o₀ i a = o₁ i a) : tables C rows s u E₁ E₂ o₀ = tables C rows s u E₁ E₂ o₁ := by
+  rw [tables_eq, tables_eq]
+  apply congrArg List.ofFn
+  funext i
+  apply List.map_congr_left
+  intro a _
+  exact record_congr C rows s u E₁ E₂ o₀ o₁ i (s.2.2 i a) (ho i (s.2.2 i a))
+
+/-- Fixed reconstruction advice serializes the descriptor tables, message tail, private input
+and retained Alice output. No challenge key or nonce is an input to this construction.
+`advice C rows s u E₁ E₂ o tail privateInput aliceOutput = SliceReconstruction.advice
+(BinArith.encWords ((tables C rows s u E₁ E₂ o).map BinArith.encWords)) tail privateInput
+aliceOutput`. -/
+noncomputable def advice (E₁ E₂ : List Bool → List Bool)
+    (o : (i : Fin C.gates.length) → (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool) → (Fin n → Bool))
+    (tail privateInput aliceOutput : List Bool) : List Bool :=
+  SliceReconstruction.advice
+    (BinArith.encWords ((tables C rows s u E₁ E₂ o).map BinArith.encWords))
+    tail privateInput aliceOutput
+
+/-- `advice` is the reconstruction encoding of the descriptor tables and three fixed fields.
+`advice C rows s u E₁ E₂ o tail privateInput aliceOutput = SliceReconstruction.advice
+(BinArith.encWords ((tables C rows s u E₁ E₂ o).map BinArith.encWords)) tail privateInput
+aliceOutput`. -/
+theorem advice_eq (E₁ E₂ : List Bool → List Bool)
+    (o : (i : Fin C.gates.length) → (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool) → (Fin n → Bool))
+    (tail privateInput aliceOutput : List Bool) :
+    advice C rows s u E₁ E₂ o tail privateInput aliceOutput =
+      SliceReconstruction.advice
+        (BinArith.encWords ((tables C rows s u E₁ E₂ o).map BinArith.encWords))
+        tail privateInput aliceOutput := rfl
+
+/-- Two plaintext families agreeing on every unselected row use the identical fixed advice,
+when the surrounding fields are the same.
+`advice C rows s u E₁ E₂ o₀ tail privateInput aliceOutput = advice C rows s u E₁ E₂ o₁ tail
+privateInput aliceOutput`. -/
+theorem advice_congr (E₁ E₂ : List Bool → List Bool)
+    (o₀ o₁ : (i : Fin C.gates.length) →
+      (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool) → (Fin n → Bool))
+    (ho : ∀ i a, GateOccurrences.selectedPosition C.gates[i.val] a u.val = none →
+      o₀ i a = o₁ i a) (tail privateInput aliceOutput : List Bool) :
+    advice C rows s u E₁ E₂ o₀ tail privateInput aliceOutput =
+      advice C rows s u E₁ E₂ o₁ tail privateInput aliceOutput :=
+  congrArg (fun ts : List (List (List Bool)) => SliceReconstruction.advice
+    (BinArith.encWords (ts.map BinArith.encWords)) tail privateInput aliceOutput)
+    (tables_congr C rows s u E₁ E₂ o₀ o₁ ho)
+
+end TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof.SelectedPlan

+/-
+Copyright (c) 2026 TCSlib contributors. All rights reserved.
+Released under Apache 2.0 license as described in the file LICENSE.
+-/
+import TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.Yao.HybridProof.SelectedSlices
+import TCSlib.Cryptography.Asymptotic.EnsemblesLemmas
+
+set_option maxHeartbeats 0
+set_option relaxedAutoImplicit false
+set_option autoImplicit false
+
+/-!
+# Precomputed messages and reconstruction records for selected rows
+
+The fixed argument keys, output key and opposite nonce determine the challenge messages
+and reconstruction records. No selected key or challenge nonce is used to compute them.
+For at most two argument keys, the row uses the inner slice unless position zero of a
+two-key row is selected; that case uses the outer slice and a precomputed inner ciphertext.
+
+## Main definitions
+
+* `SelectedRows.offset`, `length`: the selected encryption layer's slice shape.
+* `SelectedRows.word`, `message`: the deterministic precomputed challenge plaintext.
+* `SelectedRows.descriptor`: direct reading or known-key wrapping of a challenge slot.
+
+## Main results
+
+* The exact message length and typed-message encoding.
+* Pointwise reconstruction of all selected positions in one- and two-key rows.
+
+## References
+
+* [PS10] R. Pass, a. shelat, *A Course in Cryptography*, lecture notes, 3rd ed., January
+  2010, Theorem 163.7, Algorithm 157.2.
+* [LP09] Y. Lindell, B. Pinkas, *A proof of security of Yao's protocol for two-party
+  computation*, Journal of Cryptology 22(2), 2009, pp. 161–188.
+-/
+
+namespace TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof.SelectedRows
+
+open Complexity Turing TCSlib.Cryptography
+
+/-- The selected slice begins at `2n` for position zero in a two-key row, and at zero
+for every other selected position.
+`offset n ks p = if ks.length = 2 ∧ p = 0 then n + n else 0`. -/
+def offset (n : ℕ) (ks : List (Fin n → Bool)) (p : ℕ) : ℕ :=
+  if ks.length = 2 ∧ p = 0 then n + n else 0
+
+/-- `offset n ks p` is `2n` in the outer-layer case and zero otherwise.
+`offset n ks p = if ks.length = 2 ∧ p = 0 then n + n else 0`. -/
+theorem offset_eq (n : ℕ) (ks : List (Fin n → Bool)) (p : ℕ) :
+    offset n ks p = if ks.length = 2 ∧ p = 0 then n + n else 0 := rfl
+
+/-- The selected plaintext length is `4n+2` for an outer-layer occurrence, and `n` otherwise.
+`length n ks p = if ks.length = 2 ∧ p = 0 then 4 * n + 2 else n`. -/
+def length (n : ℕ) (ks : List (Fin n → Bool)) (p : ℕ) : ℕ :=
+  if ks.length = 2 ∧ p = 0 then 4 * n + 2 else n
+
+/-- `length n ks p` is `4n+2` in the outer-layer case and `n` otherwise.
+`length n ks p = if ks.length = 2 ∧ p = 0 then 4 * n + 2 else n`. -/
+theorem length_eq (n : ℕ) (ks : List (Fin n → Bool)) (p : ℕ) :
+    length n ks p = if ks.length = 2 ∧ p = 0 then 4 * n + 2 else n := rfl
+
+/-- The precomputed plaintext is an inner encryption under the known second key for an
+outer-layer occurrence, and the output-key word for an only-key or inner occurrence.
+`word E₁ n ks o nonce p = if ks.length = 2 ∧ p = 0 then spEncStr E₁ (List.ofFn (ks.getD 1 default))
+(List.ofFn o) (List.ofFn nonce.2) else List.ofFn o`. -/
+def word (E₁ : List Bool → List Bool) (n : ℕ) (ks : List (Fin n → Bool))
+    (o : Fin n → Bool) (nonce : (Fin n → Bool) × (Fin n → Bool)) (p : ℕ) : List Bool :=
+  if ks.length = 2 ∧ p = 0 then
+    spEncStr E₁ (List.ofFn (ks.getD 1 default)) (List.ofFn o) (List.ofFn nonce.2)
+  else List.ofFn o
+
+/-- `word` selects the precomputed inner ciphertext or the output-key word by layer.
+`word E₁ n ks o nonce p = if ks.length = 2 ∧ p = 0 then spEncStr E₁ (List.ofFn (ks.getD 1 default))
+(List.ofFn o) (List.ofFn nonce.2) else List.ofFn o`. -/
+theorem word_eq (E₁ : List Bool → List Bool) (n : ℕ) (ks : List (Fin n → Bool))
+    (o : Fin n → Bool) (nonce : (Fin n → Bool) × (Fin n → Bool)) (p : ℕ) :
+    word E₁ n ks o nonce p = if ks.length = 2 ∧ p = 0 then
+      spEncStr E₁ (List.ofFn (ks.getD 1 default)) (List.ofFn o) (List.ofFn nonce.2)
+    else List.ofFn o := rfl
+
+/-- The typed challenge message is the fixed-width padded view of the precomputed word.
+`message E₁ n ks o nonce p = padFin (length n ks p) (word E₁ n ks o nonce p)`. -/
+def message (E₁ : List Bool → List Bool) (n : ℕ) (ks : List (Fin n → Bool))
+    (o : Fin n → Bool) (nonce : (Fin n → Bool) × (Fin n → Bool)) (p : ℕ) :
+    Fin (length n ks p) → Bool :=
+  padFin (length n ks p) (word E₁ n ks o nonce p)
+
+/-- `message` is `padFin (length n ks p) (word E₁ n ks o nonce p)`.
+`message E₁ n ks o nonce p = padFin (length n ks p) (word E₁ n ks o nonce p)`. -/
+theorem message_eq (E₁ : List Bool → List Bool) (n : ℕ) (ks : List (Fin n → Bool))
+    (o : Fin n → Bool) (nonce : (Fin n → Bool) × (Fin n → Bool)) (p : ℕ) :
+    message E₁ n ks o nonce p =
+      padFin (length n ks p) (word E₁ n ks o nonce p) := rfl
+
+/-- Read challenge slot `j` directly, except at position one of a two-key row, where its
+ciphertext is wrapped under the known first key and fixed outer nonce.
+`descriptor n ks nonce p j = if ks.length = 2 ∧ p = 1 then SliceReconstruction.wrappedRecord j
+(List.ofFn (ks.getD 0 default)) (List.ofFn nonce.1) else SliceReconstruction.directRecord j`. -/
+def descriptor (n : ℕ) (ks : List (Fin n → Bool))
+    (nonce : (Fin n → Bool) × (Fin n → Bool)) (p j : ℕ) : List Bool :=
+  if ks.length = 2 ∧ p = 1 then SliceReconstruction.wrappedRecord j
+    (List.ofFn (ks.getD 0 default)) (List.ofFn nonce.1)
+  else SliceReconstruction.directRecord j
+
+/-- `descriptor` is a known-key wrapped record at the second position of a two-key row,
+and a direct challenge record at all other selected positions.
+`descriptor n ks nonce p j = if ks.length = 2 ∧ p = 1 then SliceReconstruction.wrappedRecord j
+(List.ofFn (ks.getD 0 default)) (List.ofFn nonce.1) else SliceReconstruction.directRecord j`. -/
+theorem descriptor_eq (n : ℕ) (ks : List (Fin n → Bool))
+    (nonce : (Fin n → Bool) × (Fin n → Bool)) (p j : ℕ) :
+    descriptor n ks nonce p j = if ks.length = 2 ∧ p = 1 then
+      SliceReconstruction.wrappedRecord j (List.ofFn (ks.getD 0 default)) (List.ofFn nonce.1)
+    else SliceReconstruction.directRecord j := rfl
+
+/-- The deterministic precomputed word has exactly the selected slice's message length,
+provided `E₁` evaluates its prescribed `2n`-bit pad family.
+`(word E₁ n ks o nonce p).length = length n ks p`. -/
+theorem word_length
+    {G₁ : ∀ n, (Fin n → Bool) → (Fin n → Bool) → (Fin (n + n) → Bool)}
+    {E₁ : List Bool → List Bool}
+    (hGE₁ : ∀ n (s x : Fin n → Bool), E₁ (pairEncode (List.ofFn s) (List.ofFn x)) =
+      List.ofFn (G₁ n s x)) (n : ℕ) (ks : List (Fin n → Bool)) (o : Fin n → Bool)
+    (nonce : (Fin n → Bool) × (Fin n → Bool)) (p : ℕ) :
+    (word E₁ n ks o nonce p).length = length n ks p := by
+  rw [word_eq, length_eq]
+  split_ifs
+  · exact SelectedSlices.innerCipher_length hGE₁ n _ o nonce.2
+  · exact List.length_ofFn
+
+/-- The typed message encodes exactly the precomputed word when `E₁` evaluates the pad
+family. Padding has no effect because the word already has the prescribed length.
+`List.ofFn (message E₁ n ks o nonce p) = word E₁ n ks o nonce p`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** The public vector-encoding reader converts padding to list padding. The
+word-length identity identifies the requested width with the actual word length, so taking
+that prefix of the word followed by padding returns the word itself. -/
+theorem message_ofFn
+    {G₁ : ∀ n, (Fin n → Bool) → (Fin n → Bool) → (Fin (n + n) → Bool)}
+    {E₁ : List Bool → List Bool}
+    (hGE₁ : ∀ n (s x : Fin n → Bool), E₁ (pairEncode (List.ofFn s) (List.ofFn x)) =
+      List.ofFn (G₁ n s x)) (n : ℕ) (ks : List (Fin n → Bool)) (o : Fin n → Bool)
+    (nonce : (Fin n → Bool) × (Fin n → Bool)) (p : ℕ) :
+    List.ofFn (message E₁ n ks o nonce p) = word E₁ n ks o nonce p := by
+  rw [message_eq, EnsembleLemmas.ofFn_padFin_eq_padL, EnsembleLemmas.padL_eq,
+    ← word_length hGE₁ n ks o nonce p, List.take_left]
+
+section Joint
+
+variable {L L₂ : ℕ → ℕ}
+  (G : ∀ n, (Fin n → Bool) → (Fin n → Bool) → (Fin (L n) → Bool))
+  (G₁ : ∀ n, (Fin n → Bool) → (Fin n → Bool) → (Fin (n + n) → Bool))
+  (G₂ : ∀ n, (Fin n → Bool) → (Fin n → Bool) → (Fin (L₂ n) → Bool))
+  (E₁ E₂ : List Bool → List Bool)
+  (hG : ∀ n (s x : Fin n → Bool),
+    List.ofFn (G n s x) = List.ofFn (G₁ n s x) ++ List.ofFn (G₂ n s x))
+  (hGE₁ : ∀ n (s x : Fin n → Bool), E₁ (pairEncode (List.ofFn s) (List.ofFn x)) =
+    List.ofFn (G₁ n s x))
+  (hGE₂ : ∀ n (s x : Fin n → Bool), E₂ (pairEncode (List.ofFn s) (List.ofFn x)) =
+    List.ofFn (G₂ n s x))
+
+include hG hGE₁ hGE₂ in
+/-- A selected row reconstructed from its slice-sampler slot is exactly the row with that
+argument key and its encryption nonce replaced by the sampled key and nonce. The other
+key and opposite nonce remain fixed. This holds for every sampler seed, for every valid
+position in a row of at most two keys. [PS10, Theorem 163.7]
+`SliceReconstruction.row E₂ (pairEncode (sliceMultiEncDist G n q offsets lens msgs (k, rs))
+(descriptor n ks nonce p j.val)) = (nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ n).enc (ks.set p k) o (if p = 0 then (rs j,
+nonce.2) else (nonce.1, rs j))`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** A selected position excludes the zero-key case. With one key, position
+zero reads the inner slice directly. With two keys, position zero reads the outer slice
+whose message is the precomputed inner ciphertext; position one wraps the inner slice
+under the known outer key. The three slice identities give the complete nested encryption.
+List-update readers identify its argument keys, and the selected position identifies the
+nonce half. No comparison between actual key values is used. -/
+theorem reconstruct (n q : ℕ) (ks : List (Fin n → Bool)) (o : Fin n → Bool)
+    (nonce : (Fin n → Bool) × (Fin n → Bool)) (p : ℕ) (j : Fin (q + 1))
+    (hks : ks.length ≤ 2) (hp : p < ks.length)
+    (offsets lens : Fin (q + 1) → ℕ) (msgs : ∀ i, Fin (lens i) → Bool)
+    (hOff : offsets j = offset n ks p) (hMsg : List.ofFn (msgs j) = word E₁ n ks o nonce p)
+    (k : Fin n → Bool) (rs : Fin (q + 1) → Fin n → Bool) :
+    SliceReconstruction.row E₂ (pairEncode (sliceMultiEncDist G n q offsets lens msgs (k, rs))
+      (descriptor n ks nonce p j.val)) =
+        (nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ n).enc (ks.set p k) o
+          (if p = 0 then (rs j, nonce.2) else (nonce.1, rs j)) := by
+  cases ks with
+  | nil => simp only [List.length_nil] at hp; omega
+  | cons k₀ rest =>
+    cases rest with
+    | nil =>
+      have hp₀ : p = 0 := by simp only [List.length_cons, List.length_nil] at hp; omega
+      subst p
+      have hd : ¬ ([k₀].length = 2 ∧ (0 : ℕ) = 1) := by
+          simp only [List.length_cons, List.length_nil]; omega
+      have ho : ¬ ([k₀].length = 2 ∧ (0 : ℕ) = 0) := by
+          simp only [List.length_cons, List.length_nil]; omega
+      rw [descriptor_eq, if_neg hd, SelectedSlices.direct_sampler, hOff, hMsg, offset_eq,
+        if_neg ho, word_eq, if_neg ho, List.set_cons_zero, if_pos rfl]
+      exact SelectedSlices.only_row G G₁ G₂ E₁ E₂ hG hGE₁ n k o (rs j) nonce.2
+    | cons k₁ tail =>
+      have htail : tail = [] := List.eq_nil_of_length_eq_zero (by
+        simp only [List.length_cons] at hks; omega)
+      subst tail
+      have hp₂ : p = 0 ∨ p = 1 := by
+        simp only [List.length_cons, List.length_nil] at hp; omega
+      rcases hp₂ with rfl | rfl
+      · have hd : ¬ ([k₀, k₁].length = 2 ∧ (0 : ℕ) = 1) := by
+          simp only [List.length_cons, List.length_nil]; omega
+        have ho : [k₀, k₁].length = 2 ∧ (0 : ℕ) = 0 := ⟨rfl, rfl⟩
+        rw [descriptor_eq, if_neg hd, SelectedSlices.direct_sampler, hOff, hMsg, offset_eq,
+          if_pos ho, word_eq, if_pos ho, List.getD_cons_succ, List.getD_cons_zero,
+          List.set_cons_zero, if_pos rfl]
+        exact SelectedSlices.outer_row G G₁ G₂ E₁ E₂ hG hGE₂ n k k₁ o (rs j) nonce.2
+      · have hd : [k₀, k₁].length = 2 ∧ (1 : ℕ) = 1 := ⟨rfl, rfl⟩
+        have ho : ¬ ([k₀, k₁].length = 2 ∧ (1 : ℕ) = 0) := by
+          simp only [List.length_cons, List.length_nil]; omega
+        rw [descriptor_eq, if_pos hd, SelectedSlices.wrapped_sampler, hOff, hMsg, offset_eq,
+          if_neg ho, word_eq, if_neg ho, List.getD_cons_zero, List.set_cons_succ,
+          List.set_cons_zero, if_neg (by decide : ¬ ((1 : ℕ) = 0))]
+        exact SelectedSlices.inner_row G G₁ G₂ E₁ E₂ hG hGE₁ n k k₀ o nonce.1 (rs j)
+
+end Joint
+
+/-- A selected-row reconstruction record using a slot index `j≤q` has at most
+`2q+3n+8` bits, uniformly in the fixed keys and nonce values. -/
+theorem descriptor_length_le (n q : ℕ) (ks : List (Fin n → Bool))
+    (nonce : (Fin n → Bool) × (Fin n → Bool)) (p j : ℕ) (hj : j ≤ q) :
+    (descriptor n ks nonce p j).length ≤ 2 * q + 3 * n + 8 := by
+  rw [descriptor_eq]
+  split_ifs
+  · rw [(SliceReconstruction.record_lengths [] j _ _).2.2, List.length_ofFn,
+      List.length_ofFn]
+    omega
+  · rw [(SliceReconstruction.record_lengths [] j [] []).2.1]
+    omega
+
+/-- Every selected-row slice fits in the joint pad of length `2n+(n+(4n+2))`, regardless
+of the row's fixed data.
+`offset n ks p + n + length n ks p ≤ (n + n) + (n + (4 * n + 2))`. -/
+theorem fits (n : ℕ) (ks : List (Fin n → Bool)) (p : ℕ) :
+    offset n ks p + n + length n ks p ≤ (n + n) + (n + (4 * n + 2)) := by
+  rw [offset_eq, length_eq]
+  split_ifs
+  · exact (SelectedSlices.slice_shapes_fit n).2
+  · exact (SelectedSlices.slice_shapes_fit n).1
+
+end TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof.SelectedRows

+/-
+Copyright (c) 2026 TCSlib contributors. All rights reserved.
+Released under Apache 2.0 license as described in the file LICENSE.
+-/
+import TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.Yao.HybridProof.GateOccurrences
+
+set_option maxHeartbeats 0
+set_option relaxedAutoImplicit false
+set_option autoImplicit false
+
+/-!
+# Filling one selected key and all its encryption nonces
+
+The assignment rows and permutation are fixed before a selected key and an independent
+nonce vector are supplied. Occurrence ranks address one challenge nonce for every use
+of the selected inactive label. All other key and nonce coordinates stay fixed.
+
+## Main definitions
+
+* `SelectedSeed.rowIndex`: the enumerated row for a prescribed actual assignment.
+* `SelectedSeed.slotIndex`: its index in the all-occurrence list.
+
+## Main results
+
+* Exact row-index and challenge-slot readers.
+
+## References
+
+* [PS10] R. Pass, a. shelat, *A Course in Cryptography*, lecture notes, 3rd ed., January
+  2010, Theorem 163.7 (fixing all randomness except one inactive key and its uses).
+* [LP09] Y. Lindell, B. Pinkas, *A proof of security of Yao's protocol for two-party
+  computation*, Journal of Cryptology 22(2), 2009, pp. 161–188.
+-/
+
+namespace TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof.SelectedSeed
+
+open BoolCircuit
+
+/-- The enumerated row of actual assignment `a` is the index of `perm.symm a` in `rows`.
+`rowIndex C rows perm i a = (rows i).idxOf ((perm i).symm a)`. -/
+noncomputable def rowIndex {m : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit m)
+    (rows : (i : Fin C.gates.length) → List (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool))
+    (perm : (i : Fin C.gates.length) → Equiv.Perm (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool))
+    (i : Fin C.gates.length) (a : Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool) : ℕ :=
+  (rows i).idxOf ((perm i).symm a)
+
+/-- `rowIndex C rows perm i a = (rows i).idxOf ((perm i).symm a)`. -/
+theorem rowIndex_eq {m : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit m)
+    (rows : (i : Fin C.gates.length) → List (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool))
+    (perm : (i : Fin C.gates.length) → Equiv.Perm (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool))
+    (i : Fin C.gates.length) (a : Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool) :
+    rowIndex C rows perm i a = (rows i).idxOf ((perm i).symm a) := rfl
+
+/-- A complete assignment enumeration contains the row index of every actual assignment.
+`rowIndex C rows perm i a < (rows i).length`. -/
+theorem rowIndex_lt {m : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit m)
+    (rows : (i : Fin C.gates.length) → List (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool))
+    (perm : (i : Fin C.gates.length) → Equiv.Perm (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool))
+    (i : Fin C.gates.length) (a : Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool)
+    (hcomplete : ∀ a, a ∈ rows i) : rowIndex C rows perm i a < (rows i).length :=
+  List.idxOf_lt_length_iff.mpr (hcomplete ((perm i).symm a))
+
+/-- Reading the enumerated row at `rowIndex` and applying its permutation recovers the
+prescribed actual assignment exactly.
+`perm i ((rows i).getD (rowIndex C rows perm i a) (fun _ => false)) = a`. -/
+theorem rowIndex_actual {m : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit m)
+    (rows : (i : Fin C.gates.length) → List (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool))
+    (perm : (i : Fin C.gates.length) → Equiv.Perm (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool))
+    (i : Fin C.gates.length) (a : Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool)
+    (hcomplete : ∀ a, a ∈ rows i) :
+    perm i ((rows i).getD (rowIndex C rows perm i a) (fun _ => false)) = a := by
+  rw [rowIndex_eq,
+    List.getD_eq_getElem _ _ (List.idxOf_lt_length_iff.mpr (hcomplete ((perm i).symm a))),
+    List.getElem_idxOf (List.idxOf_lt_length_iff.mpr (hcomplete ((perm i).symm a)))]
+  exact Equiv.apply_symm_apply (perm i) a
+
+/-- The challenge slot for actual assignment `a` and argument position `p` is the index
+of `(i,rowIndex a,p)` in the complete occurrence list.
+`slotIndex C rows perm u i a p = @List.idxOf (Fin C.gates.length × ℕ × ℕ) instBEqOfDecidableEq (i,
+rowIndex C rows perm i a, p.val) (GateOccurrences.occurrences C rows perm u)`. -/
+noncomputable def slotIndex {m : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit m)
+    (rows : (i : Fin C.gates.length) → List (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool))
+    (perm : (i : Fin C.gates.length) → Equiv.Perm (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool))
+    (u : ℕ) (i : Fin C.gates.length) (a : Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool)
+    (p : Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length) : ℕ :=
+  @List.idxOf (Fin C.gates.length × ℕ × ℕ) instBEqOfDecidableEq
+    (i, rowIndex C rows perm i a, p.val) (GateOccurrences.occurrences C rows perm u)
+
+/-- `slotIndex` is the occurrence-list index of the gate, enumerated row, and position.
+`slotIndex C rows perm u i a p = @List.idxOf (Fin C.gates.length × ℕ × ℕ) instBEqOfDecidableEq (i,
+rowIndex C rows perm i a, p.val) (GateOccurrences.occurrences C rows perm u)`. -/
+theorem slotIndex_eq {m : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit m)
+    (rows : (i : Fin C.gates.length) → List (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool))
+    (perm : (i : Fin C.gates.length) → Equiv.Perm (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool))
+    (u : ℕ) (i : Fin C.gates.length) (a : Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool)
+    (p : Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length) :
+    slotIndex C rows perm u i a p =
+      @List.idxOf (Fin C.gates.length × ℕ × ℕ) instBEqOfDecidableEq
+    (i, rowIndex C rows perm i a, p.val) (GateOccurrences.occurrences C rows perm u) := rfl
+
+/-- Every selected inactive argument has a slot strictly below the occurrence-list length.
+Completeness supplies its row even when the assignment permutation is nontrivial.
+`slotIndex C rows perm u i a p < (GateOccurrences.occurrences C rows perm u).length`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** The inverse permutation supplies the row index, whose actual assignment
+is `a`. Its argument and bit tests imply membership in the all-occurrence list. The library
+index-of membership characterization places its index below that list's length. -/
+theorem slotIndex_lt {m : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit m)
+    (rows : (i : Fin C.gates.length) → List (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool))
+    (perm : (i : Fin C.gates.length) → Equiv.Perm (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool))
+    (u : ℕ) (i : Fin C.gates.length) (a : Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool)
+    (p : Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length) (hargs : C.gates[i.val].args.Nodup)
+    (hcomplete : ∀ a, a ∈ rows i) (hvertex : C.gates[i.val].args[p.val] = u)
+    (hbit : a p = true) :
+    slotIndex C rows perm u i a p < (GateOccurrences.occurrences C rows perm u).length := by
+  letI : BEq (Fin C.gates.length × ℕ × ℕ) := instBEqOfDecidableEq
+  rw [slotIndex_eq]
+  apply List.idxOf_lt_length_iff.mpr
+  apply (GateOccurrences.mem_occurrences_iff C rows perm u i _ p.val hargs).mpr
+  refine ⟨rowIndex_lt C rows perm i a hcomplete, p.isLt, hvertex, ?_⟩
+  rw [rowIndex_actual C rows perm i a hcomplete]
+  exact hbit
+
+section Fill
+
+variable {m : ℕ} {K ρ : Type} (C : DAGCircuit m)
+  (rows : (i : Fin C.gates.length) → List (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool))
+  (s : GarbleSeed C K (ρ × ρ)) (u q : ℕ) (r : Fin (q + 1) → ρ)
+
+/-- Fill the selected row's first nonce for argument position zero and its second nonce
+for other positions, using that occurrence's challenge slot. Unselected rows and positions
+whose slot is outside the supplied capacity retain their fixed nonce pair.
+`nonce C rows s u q r i a = (GateOccurrences.selectedPosition C.gates[i.val] a u).elim (s.2.1 i a)
+(fun p => let j := slotIndex C rows s.2.2 u i a p; if h : j < q + 1 then if p.val = 0 then (r ⟨j,
+h⟩, (s.2.1 i a).2) else ((s.2.1 i a).1, r ⟨j, h⟩) else s.2.1 i a)`. -/
+noncomputable def nonce (i : Fin C.gates.length)
+    (a : Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool) : ρ × ρ :=
+  (GateOccurrences.selectedPosition C.gates[i.val] a u).elim (s.2.1 i a) fun p =>
+    let j := slotIndex C rows s.2.2 u i a p
+    if h : j < q + 1 then
+      if p.val = 0 then (r ⟨j, h⟩, (s.2.1 i a).2) else ((s.2.1 i a).1, r ⟨j, h⟩)
+    else s.2.1 i a
+
+/-- `nonce` changes exactly the selected argument's encryption-nonce half, reading the
+independent nonce at its occurrence rank when that rank fits in `q+1` slots.
+`nonce C rows s u q r i a = (GateOccurrences.selectedPosition C.gates[i.val] a u).elim (s.2.1 i a)
+(fun p => let j := slotIndex C rows s.2.2 u i a p; if h : j < q + 1 then if p.val = 0 then (r ⟨j,
+h⟩, (s.2.1 i a).2) else ((s.2.1 i a).1, r ⟨j, h⟩) else s.2.1 i a)`. -/
+theorem nonce_eq (i : Fin C.gates.length) (a : Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool) :
+    nonce C rows s u q r i a =
+      (GateOccurrences.selectedPosition C.gates[i.val] a u).elim (s.2.1 i a) (fun p =>
+        let j := slotIndex C rows s.2.2 u i a p
+        if h : j < q + 1 then
+          if p.val = 0 then (r ⟨j, h⟩, (s.2.1 i a).2) else ((s.2.1 i a).1, r ⟨j, h⟩)
+        else s.2.1 i a) := rfl
+
+/-- A row with no selected inactive argument retains both fixed nonce halves.
+`nonce C rows s u q r i a = s.2.1 i a`. -/
+theorem nonce_of_none (i : Fin C.gates.length) (a : Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool)
+    (h : GateOccurrences.selectedPosition C.gates[i.val] a u = none) :
+    nonce C rows s u q r i a = s.2.1 i a := by
+  rw [nonce_eq, h, Option.elim_none]
+
+/-- A selected row whose occurrence rank fits the capacity receives exactly that independent
+nonce in its selected encryption layer, retaining the opposite fixed nonce half.
+`nonce C rows s u q r i a = if p.val = 0 then (r ⟨slotIndex C rows s.2.2 u i a p, hslot⟩, (s.2.1 i
+a).2) else ((s.2.1 i a).1, r ⟨slotIndex C rows s.2.2 u i a p, hslot⟩)`. -/
+theorem nonce_of_some (i : Fin C.gates.length) (a : Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool)
+    (p : Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length)
+    (h : GateOccurrences.selectedPosition C.gates[i.val] a u = some p)
+    (hslot : slotIndex C rows s.2.2 u i a p < q + 1) :
+    nonce C rows s u q r i a =
+      if p.val = 0 then (r ⟨slotIndex C rows s.2.2 u i a p, hslot⟩, (s.2.1 i a).2)
+      else ((s.2.1 i a).1, r ⟨slotIndex C rows s.2.2 u i a p, hslot⟩) := by
+  rw [nonce_eq, h, Option.elim_some]
+  exact dif_pos hslot
+
+end Fill
+
+/-- Replace only the inactive key at `u`, and fill one independent nonce for every selected
+occurrence. All other keys, opposite nonce halves, and the complete row permutation remain
+fixed. The selected key is not used to compute any occurrence address. [PS10, Theorem 163.7]
+`fill C rows s u q k r = ((fun v b => if v = u ∧ b = true then k else s.1 v b), (nonce C rows s
+u.val q r, s.2.2))`. -/
+noncomputable def fill {m : ℕ} {K ρ : Type} (C : DAGCircuit m)
+    (rows : (i : Fin C.gates.length) → List (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool))
+    (s : GarbleSeed C K (ρ × ρ)) (u : Fin C.size) (q : ℕ) (k : K)
+    (r : Fin (q + 1) → ρ) : GarbleSeed C K (ρ × ρ) :=
+  ((fun v b => if v = u ∧ b = true then k else s.1 v b),
+    (nonce C rows s u.val q r, s.2.2))
+
+/-- `fill` is the product of the one-label key replacement, occurrence-indexed nonce fill,
+and the unchanged assignment permutation.
+`fill C rows s u q k r = ((fun v b => if v = u ∧ b = true then k else s.1 v b), (nonce C rows s
+u.val q r, s.2.2))`. -/
+theorem fill_eq {m : ℕ} {K ρ : Type} (C : DAGCircuit m)
+    (rows : (i : Fin C.gates.length) → List (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool))
+    (s : GarbleSeed C K (ρ × ρ)) (u : Fin C.size) (q : ℕ) (k : K)
+    (r : Fin (q + 1) → ρ) :
+    fill C rows s u q k r =
+      ((fun v b => if v = u ∧ b = true then k else s.1 v b),
+        (nonce C rows s u.val q r, s.2.2)) := rfl
+
+/-- The filled key table changes just `(u,true)` to the independently supplied key `k`.
+`(fill C rows s u q k r).1 v b = if v = u ∧ b = true then k else s.1 v b`. -/
+theorem fill_key {m : ℕ} {K ρ : Type} (C : DAGCircuit m)
+    (rows : (i : Fin C.gates.length) → List (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool))
+    (s : GarbleSeed C K (ρ × ρ)) (u : Fin C.size) (q : ℕ) (k : K)
+    (r : Fin (q + 1) → ρ) (v : Fin C.size) (b : Bool) :
+    (fill C rows s u q k r).1 v b = if v = u ∧ b = true then k else s.1 v b := rfl
+
+/-- The filled row-coin table is exactly the occurrence-indexed `nonce` function.
+`(fill C rows s u q k r).2.1 i a = nonce C rows s u.val q r i a`. -/
+theorem fill_nonce {m : ℕ} {K ρ : Type} (C : DAGCircuit m)
+    (rows : (i : Fin C.gates.length) → List (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool))
+    (s : GarbleSeed C K (ρ × ρ)) (u : Fin C.size) (q : ℕ) (k : K)
+    (r : Fin (q + 1) → ρ) (i : Fin C.gates.length)
+    (a : Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool) :
+    (fill C rows s u q k r).2.1 i a = nonce C rows s u.val q r i a := rfl
+
+/-- The filled seed retains every original assignment permutation.
+`(fill C rows s u q k r).2.2 i = s.2.2 i`. -/
+theorem fill_perm {m : ℕ} {K ρ : Type} (C : DAGCircuit m)
+    (rows : (i : Fin C.gates.length) → List (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool))
+    (s : GarbleSeed C K (ρ × ρ)) (u : Fin C.size) (q : ℕ) (k : K)
+    (r : Fin (q + 1) → ρ) (i : Fin C.gates.length) :
+    (fill C rows s u q k r).2.2 i = s.2.2 i := rfl
+
+/-- Reading the selected inactive coordinate of the filled key table returns `k`.
+`(fill C rows s u q k r).1 u true = k`. -/
+theorem fill_key_selected {m : ℕ} {K ρ : Type} (C : DAGCircuit m)
+    (rows : (i : Fin C.gates.length) → List (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool))
+    (s : GarbleSeed C K (ρ × ρ)) (u : Fin C.size) (q : ℕ) (k : K)
+    (r : Fin (q + 1) → ρ) : (fill C rows s u q k r).1 u true = k :=
+  (fill_key C rows s u q k r u true).trans (if_pos ⟨rfl, rfl⟩)
+
+/-- Every key coordinate with a different symbolic label retains its fixed value.
+No inequality between key values is assumed.
+`(fill C rows s u q k r).1 v b = s.1 v b`. -/
+theorem fill_key_other {m : ℕ} {K ρ : Type} (C : DAGCircuit m)
+    (rows : (i : Fin C.gates.length) → List (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool))
+    (s : GarbleSeed C K (ρ × ρ)) (u : Fin C.size) (q : ℕ) (k : K)
+    (r : Fin (q + 1) → ρ) (v : Fin C.size) (b : Bool) (h : (v, b) ≠ (u, true)) :
+    (fill C rows s u q k r).1 v b = s.1 v b :=
+  (fill_key C rows s u q k r v b).trans (if_neg (fun h' => h (Prod.ext h'.1 h'.2)))
+
+/-- Total wire-key lookup in the filled seed changes exactly the symbolic label `(u,true)`,
+including the default behavior beyond the circuit. Key values may collide.
+`keyAt C (fill C rows s u q k r) v b = if (v, b) = (u.val, true) then k else keyAt C s v b`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** At an in-range vertex, read the filled key table and identify equality of
+finite vertex/bit coordinates with equality of natural vertex/bit labels. Outside the circuit,
+both seeds return the default key, and the label cannot equal the in-range selected label. -/
+theorem fill_keyAt {m : ℕ} {K ρ : Type} [Inhabited K] (C : DAGCircuit m)
+    (rows : (i : Fin C.gates.length) → List (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool))
+    (s : GarbleSeed C K (ρ × ρ)) (u : Fin C.size) (q : ℕ) (k : K)
+    (r : Fin (q + 1) → ρ) (v : ℕ) (b : Bool) :
+    keyAt C (fill C rows s u q k r) v b =
+      if (v, b) = (u.val, true) then k else keyAt C s v b := by
+  by_cases hv : v < C.size
+  · rw [keyAt_eq, dif_pos hv, keyAt_eq, dif_pos hv, fill_key]
+    have hcond : ((⟨v, hv⟩ : Fin C.size) = u ∧ b = true) ↔ (v, b) = (u.val, true) :=
+      ⟨fun h => Prod.ext (congrArg Fin.val h.1) h.2,
+        fun h => ⟨Fin.ext (congrArg Prod.fst h), congrArg Prod.snd h⟩⟩
+    simp only [hcond]
+  · have hne : (v, b) ≠ (u.val, true) := by
+      intro h
+      have hval : v = u.val := congrArg Prod.fst h
+      exact hv (hval.symm ▸ u.isLt)
+    rw [keyAt_eq, dif_neg hv, if_neg hne, keyAt_eq, dif_neg hv]
+
+/-- The filled argument-key list replaces exactly those coordinates bearing symbolic label
+`(u,true)`, with all other wire-key values read from the fixed seed.
+`argKeys C (fill C rows s u q k r) i a = List.ofFn (fun p => if (C.gates[i.val].args[p.val], a p) =
+(u.val, true) then k else keyAt C s C.gates[i.val].args[p.val] (a p))`. -/
+theorem fill_argKeys {m : ℕ} {K ρ : Type} [Inhabited K] (C : DAGCircuit m)
+    (rows : (i : Fin C.gates.length) → List (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool))
+    (s : GarbleSeed C K (ρ × ρ)) (u : Fin C.size) (q : ℕ) (k : K)
+    (r : Fin (q + 1) → ρ) (i : Fin C.gates.length)
+    (a : Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool) :
+    argKeys C (fill C rows s u q k r) i a = List.ofFn (fun p =>
+      if (C.gates[i.val].args[p.val], a p) = (u.val, true) then k
+      else keyAt C s C.gates[i.val].args[p.val] (a p)) :=
+  (argKeys_eq C _ i a).trans (congrArg List.ofFn (funext fun p =>
+    fill_keyAt C rows s u q k r C.gates[i.val].args[p.val] (a p)))
+
+/-- With distinct argument vertices, the selected inactive label occurs at exactly one
+position: `(args[t],a t)=(u,true)` if and only if `t=p`. This excludes double use of the same
+symbolic key within a row without excluding collisions between actual key values. -/
+theorem argument_label_eq_iff (g : DAGGate) (a : Fin g.args.length → Bool) (u : ℕ)
+    (hargs : g.args.Nodup) (p : Fin g.args.length) (hvertex : g.args[p.val] = u)
+    (hbit : a p = true) (t : Fin g.args.length) :
+    (g.args[t.val], a t) = (u, true) ↔ t = p := by
+  constructor
+  · intro h
+    exact Fin.ext (hargs.getElem_inj_iff.mp ((congrArg Prod.fst h).trans hvertex.symm))
+  · rintro rfl
+    exact Prod.ext hvertex hbit
+
+/-- A row with selected position `p` has exactly its `p`th argument key replaced by `k`.
+The other argument key is fixed even if its value happens to equal `k`.
+`argKeys C (fill C rows s u q k r) i a = (argKeys C s i a).set p.val k`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** Compare entries of the two lists. The key-table reader changes an entry
+exactly when its symbolic label is selected. Argument distinctness identifies that event
+with equality to `p`. The list-update reader changes exactly this same position. -/
+theorem fill_argKeys_some {m : ℕ} {K ρ : Type} [Inhabited K] (C : DAGCircuit m)
+    (rows : (i : Fin C.gates.length) → List (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool))
+    (s : GarbleSeed C K (ρ × ρ)) (u : Fin C.size) (q : ℕ) (k : K)
+    (r : Fin (q + 1) → ρ) (i : Fin C.gates.length)
+    (a : Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool) (p : Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length)
+    (hargs : C.gates[i.val].args.Nodup)
+    (h : GateOccurrences.selectedPosition C.gates[i.val] a u.val = some p) :
+    argKeys C (fill C rows s u q k r) i a = (argKeys C s i a).set p.val k := by
+  obtain ⟨hvertex, hbit⟩ :=
+    (GateOccurrences.selectedPosition_eq_some_iff _ _ _ hargs p).mp h
+  rw [fill_argKeys, argKeys_eq]
+  simp only [Fin.getElem_fin]
+  apply List.ext_getElem (by simp only [List.length_ofFn, List.length_set])
+  intro j hj hj'
+  have hjg : j < C.gates[i.val].args.length := by simpa only [List.length_ofFn] using hj
+  simp only [List.getElem_ofFn, List.getElem_set]
+  have hc := argument_label_eq_iff C.gates[i.val] a u.val hargs p hvertex hbit ⟨j, hjg⟩
+  by_cases hjp : j = p.val
+  · have hfin : (⟨j, hjg⟩ : Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length) = p := Fin.ext hjp
+    rw [if_pos (hc.mpr hfin), if_pos hjp.symm]
+  · have hfin : (⟨j, hjg⟩ : Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length) ≠ p :=
+      fun h' => hjp (congrArg Fin.val h')
+    rw [if_neg (fun h' => hfin (hc.mp h')), if_neg (Ne.symm hjp)]
+
+/-- A row with no selected inactive argument keeps its entire argument-key list fixed.
+`argKeys C (fill C rows s u q k r) i a = argKeys C s i a`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** A position bearing the selected label would force the position search
+to return that position, contrary to its `none` result. Thus every coordinate uses the
+unchanged branch of the filled-key reader. -/
+theorem fill_argKeys_none {m : ℕ} {K ρ : Type} [Inhabited K] (C : DAGCircuit m)
+    (rows : (i : Fin C.gates.length) → List (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool))
+    (s : GarbleSeed C K (ρ × ρ)) (u : Fin C.size) (q : ℕ) (k : K)
+    (r : Fin (q + 1) → ρ) (i : Fin C.gates.length)
+    (a : Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool) (hargs : C.gates[i.val].args.Nodup)
+    (h : GateOccurrences.selectedPosition C.gates[i.val] a u.val = none) :
+    argKeys C (fill C rows s u q k r) i a = argKeys C s i a := by
+  rw [fill_argKeys, argKeys_eq]
+  simp only [Fin.getElem_fin]
+  apply congrArg List.ofFn
+  funext t
+  have hne : (C.gates[i.val].args[t.val], a t) ≠ (u.val, true) := by
+    intro hlabel
+    have hs := (GateOccurrences.selectedPosition_eq_some_iff _ _ _ hargs t).mpr
+      ⟨congrArg Prod.fst hlabel, congrArg Prod.snd hlabel⟩
+    rw [h] at hs
+    contradiction
+  exact if_neg hne
+
+/-- Applying a row permutation and then finding its enumerated row recovers the original
+row index, when the enumeration has no repetitions.
+`rowIndex C rows perm i (perm i ((rows i).getD r (fun _ => false))) = r`. -/
+theorem rowIndex_of_row {m : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit m)
+    (rows : (i : Fin C.gates.length) → List (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool))
+    (perm : (i : Fin C.gates.length) → Equiv.Perm (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool))
+    (i : Fin C.gates.length) (r : ℕ) (hr : r < (rows i).length) (hrows : (rows i).Nodup) :
+    rowIndex C rows perm i (perm i ((rows i).getD r (fun _ => false))) = r := by
+  rw [rowIndex_eq, Equiv.symm_apply_apply, List.getD_eq_getElem _ _ hr]
+  exact List.idxOf_getElem hrows r hr
+
+/-- An occurrence read at list index `j` maps back to exactly challenge index `j`.
+The row enumeration's distinctness recovers its row index, and the occurrence enumeration's
+distinctness recovers its challenge index.
+`slotIndex C rows perm u i (perm i ((rows i).getD r (fun _ => false))) p = j`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** The actual assignment at the stored row maps back to that row by the inverse
+row-index reader. The slot index is therefore the index of the looked-up triple. Since the
+occurrence list has no repetitions, index-of the entry at `j` is `j`. -/
+theorem slotIndex_of_lookup {m : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit m)
+    (rows : (i : Fin C.gates.length) → List (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool))
+    (perm : (i : Fin C.gates.length) → Equiv.Perm (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool))
+    (u : ℕ) (i : Fin C.gates.length) (r j : ℕ) (p : Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length)
+    (hr : r < (rows i).length) (hrows : (rows i).Nodup)
+    (hlookup : (GateOccurrences.occurrences C rows perm u)[j]? = some (i, r, p.val)) :
+    slotIndex C rows perm u i (perm i ((rows i).getD r (fun _ => false))) p = j := by
+  obtain ⟨hj, hget⟩ := List.getElem?_eq_some_iff.mp hlookup
+  rw [slotIndex_eq, rowIndex_of_row C rows perm i r hr hrows, ← hget]
+  exact List.idxOf_getElem (GateOccurrences.occurrences_nodup C rows perm u) j hj
+
+/-- Reading a selected argument's padded challenge slot returns its exact occurrence triple.
+This is the forward inverse of the occurrence-rank map.
+`GateOccurrences.padded q (GateOccurrences.occurrences C rows perm u) ⟨slotIndex C rows perm u i a
+p, hslot⟩ = some (i, rowIndex C rows perm i a, p.val)`. -/
+theorem slotIndex_padded {m : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit m)
+    (rows : (i : Fin C.gates.length) → List (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool))
+    (perm : (i : Fin C.gates.length) → Equiv.Perm (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool))
+    (u q : ℕ) (i : Fin C.gates.length) (a : Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool)
+    (p : Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length) (hargs : C.gates[i.val].args.Nodup)
+    (hcomplete : ∀ a, a ∈ rows i) (hvertex : C.gates[i.val].args[p.val] = u)
+    (hbit : a p = true) (hslot : slotIndex C rows perm u i a p < q + 1) :
+    GateOccurrences.padded q (GateOccurrences.occurrences C rows perm u)
+      ⟨slotIndex C rows perm u i a p, hslot⟩ =
+        some (i, rowIndex C rows perm i a, p.val) := by
+  letI : BEq (Fin C.gates.length × ℕ × ℕ) := instBEqOfDecidableEq
+  have hidx := slotIndex_lt C rows perm u i a p hargs hcomplete hvertex hbit
+  rw [GateOccurrences.padded_eq]
+  change (GateOccurrences.occurrences C rows perm u)[slotIndex C rows perm u i a p]? =
+    some (i, rowIndex C rows perm i a, p.val)
+  rw [slotIndex_eq]
+  exact List.getElem?_idxOf (List.idxOf_lt_length_iff.mp hidx)
+
+end TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof.SelectedSeed

+Copyright (c) 2026 TCSlib contributors. All rights reserved.
+Released under Apache 2.0 license as described in the file LICENSE.

+import TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.SpecialEncryptionMulti
+import TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.Yao.HybridProof.SliceReconstruction
+
+set_option maxHeartbeats 0
+set_option relaxedAutoImplicit false
+set_option autoImplicit false

+/-!
+# The two slice shapes in a selected-wire reduction
+
+A selected inactive wire key can occur as an only key, an inner key, or an outer key.
+Only and inner uses occupy the `(0,n)` slice of the joint pad. Outer uses occupy the
+`(2n,4n+2)` slice, whose message is a precomputed inner ciphertext. All other keys and
+nonces are explicit fixed parameters. The identities are pointwise in the challenge key
+and nonce; they do not rely on inequality between distinct key values.
+
+## Main definitions
+
+* `SelectedSlices.innerMessage`: the precomputed outer-slice plaintext vector.
+
+## Main results
+
+* `SelectedSlices.zero_row`: an empty argument list leaves the plaintext unchanged.
+* Slice identities for only, inner, and outer occurrences of the selected key.
+
+## References
+
+* [PS10] R. Pass, a. shelat, *A Course in Cryptography*, lecture notes, 3rd ed., January
+  2010, Theorem 163.7 (the inactive-key hybrid), Algorithm 157.2.
+* [LP09] Y. Lindell, B. Pinkas, *A proof of security of Yao's protocol for two-party
+  computation*, Journal of Cryptology 22(2), 2009, pp. 161–188.
+-/
+
+namespace TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof.SelectedSlices
+
+open Complexity Turing TCSlib.Cryptography
+
+/-- A zero-argument row is exactly its plaintext word, independently of both nonce halves.
+This case contains no selected-key encryption. [PS10, Protocol 162.6]
+`(nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ n).enc [] o (r₁, r₂) = List.ofFn o`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** The public nested-encryption reader at the empty key list returns the
+plaintext directly and ignores the nonce pair. -/
+theorem zero_row (E₁ E₂ : List Bool → List Bool) (n : ℕ) (o r₁ r₂ : Fin n → Bool) :
+    (nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ n).enc [] o (r₁, r₂) = List.ofFn o := by
+  rw [nestedSpecial_enc]
+
+section Joint
+
+variable {L L₂ : ℕ → ℕ}
+  (G : ∀ n, (Fin n → Bool) → (Fin n → Bool) → (Fin (L n) → Bool))
+  (G₁ : ∀ n, (Fin n → Bool) → (Fin n → Bool) → (Fin (n + n) → Bool))
+  (G₂ : ∀ n, (Fin n → Bool) → (Fin n → Bool) → (Fin (L₂ n) → Bool))
+  (E₁ E₂ : List Bool → List Bool)
+  (hG : ∀ n (s x : Fin n → Bool),
+    List.ofFn (G n s x) = List.ofFn (G₁ n s x) ++ List.ofFn (G₂ n s x))
+  (hGE₁ : ∀ n (s x : Fin n → Bool), E₁ (pairEncode (List.ofFn s) (List.ofFn x)) =
+    List.ofFn (G₁ n s x))
+  (hGE₂ : ∀ n (s x : Fin n → Bool), E₂ (pairEncode (List.ofFn s) (List.ofFn x)) =
+    List.ofFn (G₂ n s x))
+
+include hG hGE₁ in
+/-- A `(0,n)` slice ciphertext is the complete row when the selected key is the only
+argument key. The unused second nonce can be fixed arbitrarily. [PS10, Theorem 163.7]
+`sliceEnc G n 0 k r (List.ofFn o) = (nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ n).enc [k] o (r, r₂)`. -/
+theorem only_row (n : ℕ) (k o r r₂ : Fin n → Bool) :
+    sliceEnc G n 0 k r (List.ofFn o) = (nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ n).enc [k] o (r, r₂) := by
+  rw [nestedSpecial_enc]
+  exact sliceEnc_append_left hG hGE₁ n k r (by rw [List.length_ofFn])
+
+include hG hGE₁ in
+/-- If the selected key is the inner argument key, wrap its `(0,n)` challenge ciphertext
+under the known first key with the fixed outer nonce. This is exactly the two-key row,
+for every selected key and inner nonce. [PS10, Theorem 163.7]
+`spEncStr E₂ (List.ofFn outer) (sliceEnc G n 0 k r₂ (List.ofFn o)) (List.ofFn r₁) = (nestedSpecial
+E₁ E₂ n).enc [outer, k] o (r₁, r₂)`. -/
+theorem inner_row (n : ℕ) (k outer o r₁ r₂ : Fin n → Bool) :
+    spEncStr E₂ (List.ofFn outer) (sliceEnc G n 0 k r₂ (List.ofFn o)) (List.ofFn r₁) =
+      (nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ n).enc [outer, k] o (r₁, r₂) := by
+  rw [nestedSpecial_enc, sliceEnc_append_left hG hGE₁ n k r₂ (by rw [List.length_ofFn])]
+
+include hG hGE₂ in
+/-- If the selected key is the outer argument key, its `(2n,4n+2)` slice encrypts the
+inner ciphertext precomputed from the known second key and fixed inner nonce. The result
+is the complete two-key row, for every selected key and outer nonce. [PS10, Theorem 163.7]
+`sliceEnc G n (n + n) k r₁ (spEncStr E₁ (List.ofFn inner) (List.ofFn o) (List.ofFn r₂)) =
+(nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ n).enc [k, inner] o (r₁, r₂)`. -/
+theorem outer_row (n : ℕ) (k inner o r₁ r₂ : Fin n → Bool) :
+    sliceEnc G n (n + n) k r₁
+      (spEncStr E₁ (List.ofFn inner) (List.ofFn o) (List.ofFn r₂)) =
+        (nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ n).enc [k, inner] o (r₁, r₂) := by
+  rw [nestedSpecial_enc, sliceEnc_append_right hG hGE₂]
+
+end Joint
+
+/-- A precomputed inner ciphertext of an `n`-bit output key has exactly `4n+2` bits
+when `E₁` evaluates a `2n`-bit pad family. [PS10, Algorithm 157.2]
+`(spEncStr E₁ (List.ofFn k) (List.ofFn o) (List.ofFn r)).length = 4 * n + 2`. -/
+theorem innerCipher_length
+    {G₁ : ∀ n, (Fin n → Bool) → (Fin n → Bool) → (Fin (n + n) → Bool)}
+    {E₁ : List Bool → List Bool}
+    (hGE₁ : ∀ n (s x : Fin n → Bool), E₁ (pairEncode (List.ofFn s) (List.ofFn x)) =
+      List.ofFn (G₁ n s x)) (n : ℕ) (k o r : Fin n → Bool) :
+    (spEncStr E₁ (List.ofFn k) (List.ofFn o) (List.ofFn r)).length = 4 * n + 2 := by
+  have hp : (List.ofFn r).length + (List.ofFn o).length ≤
+      (E₁ (pairEncode (List.ofFn k) (List.ofFn r))).length := by
+    rw [hGE₁]
+    simp only [List.length_ofFn, Nat.le_refl]
+  rw [length_spEncStr E₁ hp]
+  simp only [List.length_ofFn]
+  omega
+
+/-- View a precomputed inner ciphertext as a `4n+2`-bit message, with false padding if the
+evaluator does not satisfy its prescribed pad-length contract.
+`innerMessage E₁ n k o r t = (spEncStr E₁ (List.ofFn k) (List.ofFn o) (List.ofFn r)).getD t.val
+false`. -/
+def innerMessage (E₁ : List Bool → List Bool) (n : ℕ) (k o r : Fin n → Bool) :
+    Fin (4 * n + 2) → Bool :=
+  fun t => (spEncStr E₁ (List.ofFn k) (List.ofFn o) (List.ofFn r)).getD t.val false
+
+/-- `innerMessage E₁ n k o r t` reads bit `t` of the precomputed inner ciphertext.
+`innerMessage E₁ n k o r t = (spEncStr E₁ (List.ofFn k) (List.ofFn o) (List.ofFn r)).getD t.val
+false`. -/
+theorem innerMessage_eq (E₁ : List Bool → List Bool) (n : ℕ) (k o r : Fin n → Bool)
+    (t : Fin (4 * n + 2)) :
+    innerMessage E₁ n k o r t =
+      (spEncStr E₁ (List.ofFn k) (List.ofFn o) (List.ofFn r)).getD t.val false := rfl
+
+/-- Encoding the precomputed outer-slice message gives exactly the inner ciphertext,
+when `E₁` evaluates its prescribed `2n`-bit pad family.
+`List.ofFn (innerMessage E₁ n k o r) = spEncStr E₁ (List.ofFn k) (List.ofFn o) (List.ofFn r)`. -/
+theorem innerMessage_ofFn
+    {G₁ : ∀ n, (Fin n → Bool) → (Fin n → Bool) → (Fin (n + n) → Bool)}
+    {E₁ : List Bool → List Bool}
+    (hGE₁ : ∀ n (s x : Fin n → Bool), E₁ (pairEncode (List.ofFn s) (List.ofFn x)) =
+      List.ofFn (G₁ n s x)) (n : ℕ) (k o r : Fin n → Bool) :
+    List.ofFn (innerMessage E₁ n k o r) =
+      spEncStr E₁ (List.ofFn k) (List.ofFn o) (List.ofFn r) := by
+  have hlen := innerCipher_length hGE₁ n k o r
+  apply List.ext_getElem (by rw [List.length_ofFn]; exact hlen.symm)
+  intro j _ hj
+  rw [List.getElem_ofFn, innerMessage_eq, List.getD_eq_getElem _ _ hj]
+
+/-- Reading slot `j` from the actual slice multi-message sampler returns precisely its
+slice ciphertext, for every sampled key and nonce vector. [PS10, Theorem 163.7]
+`SliceReconstruction.row E₂ (pairEncode (sliceMultiEncDist G n q offset len m (k, r))
+(SliceReconstruction.directRecord j.val)) = sliceEnc G n (offset j) k (r j) (List.ofFn (m j))`. -/
+theorem direct_sampler {L : ℕ → ℕ}
+    (G : ∀ n, (Fin n → Bool) → (Fin n → Bool) → (Fin (L n) → Bool))
+    (E₂ : List Bool → List Bool) (n q : ℕ) (offset len : Fin (q + 1) → ℕ)
+    (m : ∀ i, Fin (len i) → Bool) (k : Fin n → Bool)
+    (r : Fin (q + 1) → Fin n → Bool) (j : Fin (q + 1)) :
+    SliceReconstruction.row E₂ (pairEncode (sliceMultiEncDist G n q offset len m (k, r))
+      (SliceReconstruction.directRecord j.val)) =
+        sliceEnc G n (offset j) k (r j) (List.ofFn (m j)) := by
+  rw [sliceMultiEncDist_apply, SliceReconstruction.encodeList_eq_encWords,
+    SliceReconstruction.row_directRecord,
+    List.getD_eq_getElem _ _ (by rw [List.length_ofFn]; exact j.isLt), List.getElem_ofFn]
+
+/-- Wrapping sampler slot `j` uses the exact sampled slice ciphertext with the supplied
+known outer key and fixed outer nonce. The selected challenge key is not given to wrapping.
+[PS10, Theorem 163.7]
+`SliceReconstruction.row E₂ (pairEncode (sliceMultiEncDist G n q offset len m (k, r))
+(SliceReconstruction.wrappedRecord j.val outer nonce)) = spEncStr E₂ outer (sliceEnc G n (offset j)
+k (r j) (List.ofFn (m j))) nonce`. -/
+theorem wrapped_sampler {L : ℕ → ℕ}
+    (G : ∀ n, (Fin n → Bool) → (Fin n → Bool) → (Fin (L n) → Bool))
+    (E₂ : List Bool → List Bool) (n q : ℕ) (offset len : Fin (q + 1) → ℕ)
+    (m : ∀ i, Fin (len i) → Bool) (k : Fin n → Bool)
+    (r : Fin (q + 1) → Fin n → Bool) (j : Fin (q + 1)) (outer nonce : List Bool) :
+    SliceReconstruction.row E₂ (pairEncode (sliceMultiEncDist G n q offset len m (k, r))
+      (SliceReconstruction.wrappedRecord j.val outer nonce)) =
+        spEncStr E₂ outer (sliceEnc G n (offset j) k (r j) (List.ofFn (m j))) nonce := by
+  rw [sliceMultiEncDist_apply, SliceReconstruction.encodeList_eq_encWords,
+    SliceReconstruction.row_wrappedRecord,
+    List.getD_eq_getElem _ _ (by rw [List.length_ofFn]; exact j.isLt), List.getElem_ofFn]
+
+/-- Both selected-key slice shapes fit in the joint pad of length
+`2n + (n + (4n+2))`: the `(0,n)` slice and the `(2n,4n+2)` slice.
+[PS10, Theorem 163.7]
+`0 + n + n ≤ (n + n) + (n + (4 * n + 2)) ∧ (n + n) + n + (4 * n + 2) ≤ (n + n) + (n + (4 * n +
+2))`. -/
+theorem slice_shapes_fit (n : ℕ) :
+    0 + n + n ≤ (n + n) + (n + (4 * n + 2)) ∧
+      (n + n) + n + (4 * n + 2) ≤ (n + n) + (n + (4 * n + 2)) := by
+  omega
+
+end TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof.SelectedSlices

+import TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.Yao.HybridProof.SelectedTables
+
+set_option maxHeartbeats 0
+set_option relaxedAutoImplicit false
+set_option autoImplicit false
+
+/-!
+# A common reconstruction for both sides of a selected gate substep
+
+The selected argument is an actual argument of the current gate. The two neighboring
+stage samplers use the same reconstruction machine, slice offsets and lengths, and fixed
+before-stage advice. Only their padded slice message families vary.
+
+## Main definitions
+
+None.
+
+## Main results
+
+* `SelectedStep.plaintext_ne`: all three local stages avoid the selected inactive label.
+* `SelectedStep.advice_next`: both sides use identical fixed advice.
+* `SelectedStep.reconstruct`: the exact pointwise two-sided sampler contract.
+
+## References
+
+* [PS10] R. Pass, a. shelat, *A Course in Cryptography*, lecture notes, 3rd ed., January
+  2010, Theorem 163.7 (one selected inactive-key substep).
+-/
+
+namespace TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof.SelectedStep
+
+open Complexity Turing BoolCircuit TCSlib.Cryptography
+
+/-- Every plaintext label in local stage `2*i0+phase`, for `phase <= 2`, differs from
+`(u,true)` when gate `i0` reads `u`. This combines the three published phase readers and
+uses no comparison of actual key values. [PS10, Theorem 163.7] -/
+theorem plaintext_ne {m : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit m) (v : ℕ → Bool)
+    (i₀ : Fin C.gates.length) (u : ℕ) (hu : u ∈ C.gates[i₀.val].args)
+    (phase : ℕ) (hphase : phase ≤ 2)
+    (i : Fin C.gates.length) (a : Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool) :
+    (m + i.val, GateHybrids.stageBit C v (2 * i₀.val + phase) i a) ≠ (u, true) := by
+  have hc : phase = 0 ∨ phase = 1 ∨ phase = 2 := by omega
+  rcases hc with rfl | rfl | rfl
+  · simpa only [Nat.add_zero] using SelectedFields.stageBit_plaintext_ne_even C v i₀ u hu i a
+  · exact SelectedFields.stageBit_plaintext_ne_odd C v i₀ u hu i a
+  · exact SelectedFields.stageBit_plaintext_ne_next C v i₀ u hu i a
+
+/-- Rows without the selected inactive argument have identical plaintext bits at stages
+`2*i0+p.val` and `2*i0+p.val+1`. The selected position is an actual argument of a gate with
+at most two arguments. [PS10, Theorem 163.7]
+`GateHybrids.stageBit C v (2 * i₀.val + p.val) i a = GateHybrids.stageBit C v (2 * i₀.val +
+p.val + 1) i a`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** The actual position is zero or one. Apply the published first-substep
+support reader in the first case and the second-substep support reader in the second case.
+The latter uses the supplied consistent wire values. No selected wire is fabricated for
+an absent argument. -/
+theorem unselected_eq {m : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit m) (v : ℕ → Bool)
+    (hA : ∀ i : Fin C.gates.length, (C.gates[i]).args.length ≤ 2)
+    (hEval : ∀ i : Fin C.gates.length,
+      gateOut (C.gates[i]) (RelabelGarbling.argValues C v i) = v (m + i))
+    (i₀ : Fin C.gates.length) (hargs : C.gates[i₀.val].args.Nodup)
+    (p : Fin C.gates[i₀.val].args.length) (u : ℕ) (hu : C.gates[i₀.val].args[p.val] = u)
+    (i : Fin C.gates.length) (a : Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool)
+    (hNone : GateOccurrences.selectedPosition C.gates[i.val] a u = none) :
+    GateHybrids.stageBit C v (2 * i₀.val + p.val) i a =
+      GateHybrids.stageBit C v (2 * i₀.val + p.val + 1) i a := by
+  have hp : p.val ≤ 1 := by
+    have ha := hA i₀
+    simp only [Fin.getElem_fin] at ha
+    have hp := p.isLt
+    omega
+  by_cases hzero : p.val = 0
+  · simpa only [hzero, Nat.add_zero] using
+      SelectedFields.stage_first_unselected C v i₀ hargs p hzero u hu i a hNone
+  · have hone : p.val = 1 := by omega
+    have hstage : 2 * i₀.val + 1 + 1 = 2 * i₀.val + 2 := by omega
+    rw [hone, hstage]
+    exact SelectedFields.stage_second_unselected C v hA hEval i₀ hargs p hone u hu i a hNone
+
+/-- Neighboring stages for an actual selected argument have exactly the same fixed
+reconstruction advice, including the complete message tail. Only their challenge messages
+may differ. [PS10, Theorem 163.7]
+`SelectedPlan.advice C rows s u E₁ E₂ (fun i a => keyAt C s (m + i.val) (GateHybrids.stageBit C v
+(2 * i₀.val + p.val) i a)) (pairSndD (GateHybridMessage.message (nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ n) C s inA
+outLen output v (2 * i₀.val + p.val))) privateInput aliceOutput = SelectedPlan.advice C rows s u E₁
+E₂ (fun i a => keyAt C s (m + i.val) (GateHybrids.stageBit C v (2 * i₀.val + p.val + 1) i a))
+(pairSndD (GateHybridMessage.message (nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ n) C s inA outLen output v (2 * i₀.val +
+p.val + 1))) privateInput aliceOutput`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** The published tail reader removes the stage dependence of the retained
+message tail. The common-advice reader then reduces the claim to plaintext-key equality
+on unselected rows. Those rows have equal neighboring stage bits by the first/second
+substep support readers, so their output keys agree. -/
+theorem advice_next {m n : ℕ} (E₁ E₂ : List Bool → List Bool) (C : DAGCircuit m)
+    (rows : (i : Fin C.gates.length) → List (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool))
+    (s : GarbleSeed C (Fin n → Bool) ((Fin n → Bool) × (Fin n → Bool)))
+    (u : Fin C.size) (v : ℕ → Bool) (hfanin : C.IsFaninTwo)
+    (hEval : ∀ i : Fin C.gates.length,
+      gateOut (C.gates[i]) (RelabelGarbling.argValues C v i) = v (m + i))
+    (i₀ : Fin C.gates.length) (p : Fin C.gates[i₀.val].args.length)
+    (hu : C.gates[i₀.val].args[p.val] = u.val) (inA outLen : ℕ)
+    (output : Fin outLen → ℕ) (privateInput aliceOutput : List Bool) :
+    SelectedPlan.advice C rows s u E₁ E₂ (fun i a =>
+      keyAt C s (m + i.val) (GateHybrids.stageBit C v (2 * i₀.val + p.val) i a))
+      (pairSndD (GateHybridMessage.message (nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ n) C s inA outLen
+        output v (2 * i₀.val + p.val))) privateInput aliceOutput =
+    SelectedPlan.advice C rows s u E₁ E₂ (fun i a =>
+      keyAt C s (m + i.val) (GateHybrids.stageBit C v (2 * i₀.val + p.val + 1) i a))
+      (pairSndD (GateHybridMessage.message (nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ n) C s inA outLen
+        output v (2 * i₀.val + p.val + 1))) privateInput aliceOutput := by
+  have hfan := (DAGCircuit.isFaninTwo_iff C).mp hfanin
+  have hA (i : Fin C.gates.length) : (C.gates[i]).args.length ≤ 2 := by
+    simpa only [Fin.getElem_fin] using hfan.2 _ (List.getElem_mem i.isLt)
+  rw [← SelectedFields.message_tail_stage_eq (nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ n) C s inA outLen
+    output v (2 * i₀.val + p.val) (2 * i₀.val + p.val + 1)]
+  apply SelectedPlan.advice_congr
+  intro i a hNone
+  exact congrArg (keyAt C s (m + i.val))
+    (unselected_eq C v hA hEval i₀ (hfan.1 _ (List.getElem_mem i₀.isLt)).1 p u.val hu
+      i a hNone)
+
+section Joint
+
+variable {L L₂ : ℕ → ℕ}
+  (G : ∀ n, (Fin n → Bool) → (Fin n → Bool) → (Fin (L n) → Bool))
+  (G₁ : ∀ n, (Fin n → Bool) → (Fin n → Bool) → (Fin (n + n) → Bool))
+  (G₂ : ∀ n, (Fin n → Bool) → (Fin n → Bool) → (Fin (L₂ n) → Bool))
+  (E₁ E₂ : List Bool → List Bool)
+  (hG : ∀ n (s x : Fin n → Bool),
+    List.ofFn (G n s x) = List.ofFn (G₁ n s x) ++ List.ofFn (G₂ n s x))
+  (hGE₁ : ∀ n (s x : Fin n → Bool), E₁ (pairEncode (List.ofFn s) (List.ofFn x)) =
+    List.ofFn (G₁ n s x))
+  (hGE₂ : ∀ n (s x : Fin n → Bool), E₂ (pairEncode (List.ofFn s) (List.ofFn x)) =
+    List.ofFn (G₂ n s x))
+
+include hG hGE₁ hGE₂ in
+/-- Both neighboring actual hybrid samples reconstruct from the same before-stage advice,
+with the same slice offsets and lengths and only the side-specific challenge messages.
+The equality holds for every challenge key and all padded row nonces. [PS10, Theorem 163.7]
+`let before := 2 * i₀.val + p.val; let stage := before + side.toNat; let plain : (i : Fin
+C.gates.length) → (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool) → (Fin n → Bool) := fun i a => keyAt C s
+(m + i.val) (GateHybrids.stageBit C v stage i a); let beta := SelectedPlan.advice C rows s u E₁ E₂
+(fun i a => keyAt C s (m + i.val) (GateHybrids.stageBit C v before i a)) (pairSndD
+(GateHybridMessage.message (nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ n) C s inA outLen output v before)) (pairEncode
+bobInput (List.ofFn (FakeObjects.activeAnswers C s inA inB))) aliceOutput;
+SliceReconstruction.rebuild E₂ (pairEncode N (pairEncode (sliceMultiEncDist G n q
+(SelectedPlan.slotOffset C rows s u q) (SelectedPlan.slotLength C rows s u q)
+(SelectedPlan.slotMessage C rows s u q E₁ plain) (k, rs)) beta)) = GateHybridMessage.sample
+(nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ n) C (SelectedSeed.fill C rows s u q k rs) inA inB outLen output v N bobInput
+[] aliceOutput stage`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** Fan-in two supplies distinct arguments and an actual position zero or one.
+Canonical rows are complete. Since the selected argument precedes the current gate, every
+plaintext label in either neighboring stage avoids its inactive label. The sink hypothesis
+also excludes the selected wire from both entries of every output decoding pair. Apply the
+exact filled-seed sample reconstruction on the before side. On the after side first replace
+the fixed advice by the equal after-stage advice, then apply the same reconstruction reader.
+All key-value collisions and zero key width are allowed. -/
+theorem reconstruct {m n : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit m)
+    (rows : (i : Fin C.gates.length) → List (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool))
+    (s : GarbleSeed C (Fin n → Bool) ((Fin n → Bool) × (Fin n → Bool)))
+    (u : Fin C.size) (q : ℕ) (v : ℕ → Bool) (hfanin : C.IsFaninTwo)
+    (hEval : ∀ i : Fin C.gates.length,
+      gateOut (C.gates[i]) (RelabelGarbling.argValues C v i) = v (m + i))
+    (i₀ : Fin C.gates.length) (p : Fin C.gates[i₀.val].args.length)
+    (hu : C.gates[i₀.val].args[p.val] = u.val)
+    (hcap : (GateOccurrences.occurrences C rows s.2.2 u.val).length ≤ q + 1)
+    (hrows : ∀ i, rows i = (Finset.univ : Finset (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool)).toList)
+    (inA inB outLen : ℕ) (output : Fin outLen → ℕ)
+    (hsink : ∀ j g, g ∈ C.gates → output j ∉ g.args)
+    (N bobInput aliceOutput : List Bool)
+    (k : Fin n → Bool) (rs : Fin (q + 1) → Fin n → Bool) (side : Bool) :
+    let before := 2 * i₀.val + p.val
+    let stage := before + side.toNat
+    let plain : (i : Fin C.gates.length) →
+        (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool) → (Fin n → Bool) :=
+      fun i a => keyAt C s (m + i.val) (GateHybrids.stageBit C v stage i a)
+    let beta := SelectedPlan.advice C rows s u E₁ E₂ (fun i a =>
+      keyAt C s (m + i.val) (GateHybrids.stageBit C v before i a))
+      (pairSndD (GateHybridMessage.message (nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ n) C s
+        inA outLen output v before))
+      (pairEncode bobInput (List.ofFn (FakeObjects.activeAnswers C s inA inB))) aliceOutput
+    SliceReconstruction.rebuild E₂ (pairEncode N (pairEncode
+      (sliceMultiEncDist G n q (SelectedPlan.slotOffset C rows s u q)
+        (SelectedPlan.slotLength C rows s u q)
+        (SelectedPlan.slotMessage C rows s u q E₁ plain) (k, rs)) beta)) =
+      GateHybridMessage.sample (nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ n) C (SelectedSeed.fill C rows s u q k rs)
+        inA inB outLen output v N bobInput [] aliceOutput stage := by
+  -- Recover the structural hypotheses from the public circuit reader.
+  have hfan := (DAGCircuit.isFaninTwo_iff C).mp hfanin
+  have hargs (i : Fin C.gates.length) : C.gates[i.val].args.Nodup :=
+    (hfan.1 _ (List.getElem_mem i.isLt)).1
+  have hA (i : Fin C.gates.length) : C.gates[i.val].args.length ≤ 2 :=
+    hfan.2 _ (List.getElem_mem i.isLt)
+  have hcomplete (i : Fin C.gates.length) (a : Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool) :
+      a ∈ rows i := by
+    rw [hrows i]
+    exact Finset.mem_toList.mpr (Finset.mem_univ a)
+  have huMem : u.val ∈ C.gates[i₀.val].args := by
+    rw [← hu]
+    exact List.getElem_mem p.isLt
+  -- Sink outputs exclude both decoding labels at the selected wire.
+  have hOut (j : Fin outLen) : output j ≠ u.val := by
+    intro h
+    exact GateOccurrences.decoding_label_ne C i₀ u.val huMem output hsink j true
+      (congrArg (fun w : ℕ => (w, true)) h)
+  have hp : p.val ≤ 1 := by have ha := hA i₀; have hlt := p.isLt; omega
+  -- The after side changes only the challenge messages, using the same advice.
+  dsimp only
+  cases side with
+  | false =>
+    simp only [Bool.toNat_false, Nat.add_zero]
+    apply SelectedTables.sample_reconstruct G G₁ G₂ E₁ E₂ hG hGE₁ hGE₂ C rows s u q
+      inA inB outLen output v (2 * i₀.val + p.val) N bobInput aliceOutput
+      hargs hA hcomplete hcap hrows _ hOut k rs
+    intro i a
+    exact plaintext_ne C v i₀ u.val huMem p.val (by omega) i a
+  | true =>
+    simp only [Bool.toNat_true]
+    rw [advice_next E₁ E₂ C rows s u v hfanin hEval i₀ p hu inA outLen output
+      (pairEncode bobInput (List.ofFn (FakeObjects.activeAnswers C s inA inB))) aliceOutput]
+    apply SelectedTables.sample_reconstruct G G₁ G₂ E₁ E₂ hG hGE₁ hGE₂ C rows s u q
+      inA inB outLen output v (2 * i₀.val + p.val + 1) N bobInput aliceOutput
+      hargs hA hcomplete hcap hrows _ hOut k rs
+    intro i a
+    simpa only [Nat.add_assoc] using
+      plaintext_ne C v i₀ u.val huMem (p.val + 1) (by omega) i a
+
+end Joint
+
+end TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof.SelectedStep
