+import Mathlib.Data.Fintype.Sigma
+import TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.Yao.HybridProof.SelectedStep
+import TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.Yao.HybridProof.SelectedAdviceBounds
+
+set_option maxHeartbeats 0
+set_option relaxedAutoImplicit false
+set_option autoImplicit false
+
+/-!
+# Finite advice choices for all selected hybrid substeps
+
+A choice stores an optional input and actual gate argument, together with the complete
+baseline tape. The absent choice keeps the type nonempty even for an empty circuit. All
+slice messages and advice are fixed before the challenge key and nonces are supplied.
+
+## Main definitions
+
+* `Selection`, `wire`: an actual argument and its vertex.
+* `Tape`, `Choice`: baseline tape and the finite padded advice-option family.
+* `rows`: the canonical complete assignment enumeration.
+* `offset`, `len`, `message`, `advice`: fixed finite-choice reduction data.
+
+## Main results
+
+* Exact public readers, slice-fit and polynomial-advice bounds.
+* `rebuild_some`: both actual filled-seed sampler endpoints at every real choice.
+
+## References
+
+* [PS10] R. Pass, a. shelat, *A Course in Cryptography*, lecture notes, 3rd ed., January
+  2010, Theorem 163.7 (fixing inputs, the selected step and all other coins).
+-/
+
+namespace TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof.SelectedStepChoices
+
+open Complexity Turing BoolCircuit TCSlib.Cryptography
+
+/-- A selection is a gate and one of its actual argument positions.
+`Selection C = (Σ i : Fin C.gates.length, Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length)`. -/
+abbrev Selection {m : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit m) :=
+  Σ i : Fin C.gates.length, Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length
+
+/-- `Selection C` is the dependent pair of a gate index and a valid argument index.
+`Selection C = (Σ i : Fin C.gates.length, Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length)`. -/
+theorem Selection_eq {m : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit m) :
+    Selection C = (Σ i : Fin C.gates.length, Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length) := rfl
+
+/-- The selected vertex is the vertex read at the selected argument position. Acyclicity
+puts this vertex within the circuit's finite key table.
+`(wire C a).val = C.gates[a.1.val].args[a.2.val]`. -/
+def wire {m : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit m) (a : Selection C) : Fin C.size :=
+  ⟨C.gates[a.1.val].args[a.2.val], by
+    have h := GateOccurrences.selected_wire_lt C a.1 _ (List.getElem_mem a.2.isLt)
+    have hi := a.1.isLt
+    rw [DAGCircuit.size_eq]
+    omega⟩
+
+/-- `wire C a` has value `C.gates[a.gate].args[a.position]`.
+`(wire C a).val = C.gates[a.1.val].args[a.2.val]`. -/
+theorem wire_val {m : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit m) (a : Selection C) :
+    (wire C a).val = C.gates[a.1.val].args[a.2.val] := rfl
+
+/-- The full baseline tape has `10*S(n)*n+2*S(n)` bits, including all unused coordinates.
+`Tape S n = (Fin (10 * S.eval n * n + 2 * S.eval n) → Bool)`. -/
+abbrev Tape (S : PolyExpr) (n : ℕ) := Fin (10 * S.eval n * n + 2 * S.eval n) → Bool
+
+/-- `Tape S n` is the complete addressed key/nonce/shift bit-vector type.
+`Tape S n = (Fin (10 * S.eval n * n + 2 * S.eval n) → Bool)`. -/
+theorem Tape_eq (S : PolyExpr) (n : ℕ) :
+    Tape S n = (Fin (10 * S.eval n * n + 2 * S.eval n) → Bool) := rfl
+
+/-- A choice is an optional input and actual selected argument, paired with a full baseline
+tape. `none` permits an advice choice even when no gate or input option exists.
+`Choice I C S n = (Option (I n × Selection (C n)) × Tape S n)`. -/
+abbrev Choice (I : ℕ → Type) {inputs : ℕ → ℕ} (C : ∀ n, DAGCircuit (inputs n))
+    (S : PolyExpr) (n : ℕ) := Option (I n × Selection (C n)) × Tape S n
+
+/-- `Choice I C S n` retains an optional input/argument pair and the full baseline tape.
+`Choice I C S n = (Option (I n × Selection (C n)) × Tape S n)`. -/
+theorem Choice_eq (I : ℕ → Type) {inputs : ℕ → ℕ} (C : ∀ n, DAGCircuit (inputs n))
+    (S : PolyExpr) (n : ℕ) :
+    Choice I C S n = (Option (I n × Selection (C n)) × Tape S n) := rfl
+
+/-- The canonical row list enumerates every Boolean assignment by `Finset.univ.toList`.
+`rows C i = (Finset.univ : Finset (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool)).toList`. -/
+noncomputable def rows {m : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit m) (i : Fin C.gates.length) :
+    List (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool) := Finset.univ.toList
+
+/-- `rows C i = Finset.univ.toList` for the gate's assignment type. -/
+theorem rows_eq {m : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit m) (i : Fin C.gates.length) :
+    rows C i = (Finset.univ : Finset (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool)).toList := rfl
+
+/-- Every Boolean assignment occurs in the canonical row list.
+`a ∈ rows C i`. -/
+theorem rows_complete {m : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit m) (i : Fin C.gates.length)
+    (a : Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool) : a ∈ rows C i :=
+  Eq.mpr (congrArg (fun xs : List (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool) => a ∈ xs)
+    (rows_eq C i)) (Finset.mem_toList.mpr (Finset.mem_univ a))
+
+/-- The canonical row list has no repeated assignments.
+`(rows C i).Nodup`. -/
+theorem rows_nodup {m : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit m) (i : Fin C.gates.length) : (rows C i).Nodup :=
+  Eq.mpr (congrArg List.Nodup (rows_eq C i)) (Finset.nodup_toList _)
+
+/-- A size-S fan-in-two circuit's full selected occurrence list fits in `4*S(n)+1` slots,
+for every row permutation and selected vertex. [PS10, Theorem 163.7]
+`(GateOccurrences.occurrences C (rows C) perm u).length ≤ (SelectedAdviceBounds.slotBudget S).eval
+n + 1`. -/
+theorem capacity {m : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit m) (S : PolyExpr) (n : ℕ)
+    (perm : (i : Fin C.gates.length) → Equiv.Perm (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool))
+    (u : ℕ) (hSize : C.size ≤ S.eval n) (hfanin : C.IsFaninTwo) :
+    (GateOccurrences.occurrences C (rows C) perm u).length ≤
+      (SelectedAdviceBounds.slotBudget S).eval n + 1 := by
+  have hc := GateOccurrences.length_occurrences_le C (rows C) perm u (rows_nodup C) hfanin
+  have hg : C.gates.length ≤ S.eval n := by
+    rw [DAGCircuit.size_eq] at hSize
+    omega
+  rw [SelectedAdviceBounds.slotBudget_eval]
+  omega
+
+section Data
+
+variable {I : ℕ → Type} {inputs : ℕ → ℕ} (C : ∀ n, DAGCircuit (inputs n))
+  (S A B O : PolyExpr)
+  (seed : ∀ n, List Bool →
+    GarbleSeed (C n) (Fin n → Bool) ((Fin n → Bool) × (Fin n → Bool)))
+  (v : ∀ n, I n → ℕ → Bool) (output : ∀ n, Fin (O.eval n) → ℕ)
+  (bobWord aliceWord : ∀ n, I n → List Bool) (E₁ E₂ : List Bool → List Bool)
+
+/-- A real choice uses its selected row offsets; the absent choice uses offset zero.
+`offset (I := I) C S seed n (none, t) = fun _ => 0` and `offset C S seed n (some (z, b), t) =
+SelectedPlan.slotOffset (C n) (rows (C n)) (seed n (List.ofFn t)) (wire (C n) b)
+((SelectedAdviceBounds.slotBudget S).eval n)`. -/
+noncomputable def offset (n : ℕ) (a : Choice I C S n) :
+    Fin ((SelectedAdviceBounds.slotBudget S).eval n + 1) → ℕ :=
+  match a with
+  | (none, _) => fun _ => 0
+  | (some (_, b), t) => SelectedPlan.slotOffset (C n) (rows (C n))
+      (seed n (List.ofFn t)) (wire (C n) b) ((SelectedAdviceBounds.slotBudget S).eval n)
+
+/-- The absent choice's offset vector is constantly zero.
+`offset (I := I) C S seed n (none, t) = fun _ => 0`. -/
+theorem offset_none (n : ℕ) (t : Tape S n) :
+    offset (I := I) C S seed n (none, t) = fun _ => 0 := rfl
+
+/-- A real choice's offsets are exactly the complete selected occurrence plan.
+`offset C S seed n (some (z, b), t) = SelectedPlan.slotOffset (C n) (rows (C n)) (seed n (List.ofFn
+t)) (wire (C n) b) ((SelectedAdviceBounds.slotBudget S).eval n)`. -/
+theorem offset_some (n : ℕ) (z : I n) (b : Selection (C n)) (t : Tape S n) :
+    offset C S seed n (some (z, b), t) = SelectedPlan.slotOffset (C n) (rows (C n))
+      (seed n (List.ofFn t)) (wire (C n) b) ((SelectedAdviceBounds.slotBudget S).eval n) := rfl
+
+/-- A real choice uses the selected row message lengths; the absent choice uses zero.
+`len (I := I) C S seed n (none, t) = fun _ => 0` and `len C S seed n (some (z, b), t) =
+SelectedPlan.slotLength (C n) (rows (C n)) (seed n (List.ofFn t)) (wire (C n) b)
+((SelectedAdviceBounds.slotBudget S).eval n)`. -/
+noncomputable def len (n : ℕ) (a : Choice I C S n) :
+    Fin ((SelectedAdviceBounds.slotBudget S).eval n + 1) → ℕ :=
+  match a with
+  | (none, _) => fun _ => 0
+  | (some (_, b), t) => SelectedPlan.slotLength (C n) (rows (C n))
+      (seed n (List.ofFn t)) (wire (C n) b) ((SelectedAdviceBounds.slotBudget S).eval n)
+
+/-- The absent choice's message-length vector is constantly zero.
+`len (I := I) C S seed n (none, t) = fun _ => 0`. -/
+theorem len_none (n : ℕ) (t : Tape S n) :
+    len (I := I) C S seed n (none, t) = fun _ => 0 := rfl
+
+/-- A real choice's lengths are exactly the complete selected occurrence plan.
+`len C S seed n (some (z, b), t) = SelectedPlan.slotLength (C n) (rows (C n)) (seed n (List.ofFn t))
+(wire (C n) b) ((SelectedAdviceBounds.slotBudget S).eval n)`. -/
+theorem len_some (n : ℕ) (z : I n) (b : Selection (C n)) (t : Tape S n) :
+    len C S seed n (some (z, b), t) = SelectedPlan.slotLength (C n) (rows (C n))
+      (seed n (List.ofFn t)) (wire (C n) b) ((SelectedAdviceBounds.slotBudget S).eval n) := rfl
+
+/-- The chosen side supplies its fixed plaintext keys to every selected occurrence; an
+absent choice supplies the unique empty messages.
+`message C S seed v E₁ side n (none, t) = fun _ _ => false` and `message C S seed v E₁ side n (some
+(z, b), t) = SelectedPlan.slotMessage (C n) (rows (C n)) (seed n (List.ofFn t)) (wire (C n) b)
+((SelectedAdviceBounds.slotBudget S).eval n) E₁ (fun i a => keyAt (C n) (seed n (List.ofFn t))
+(inputs n + i.val) (GateHybrids.stageBit (C n) (v n z) (2 * b.1.val + b.2.val + side.toNat) i
+a))`. -/
+noncomputable def message (side : Bool) (n : ℕ) (a : Choice I C S n) :
+    ∀ j, Fin (len C S seed n a j) → Bool :=
+  match a with
+  | (none, _) => fun _ _ => false
+  | (some (z, b), t) => SelectedPlan.slotMessage (C n) (rows (C n))
+      (seed n (List.ofFn t)) (wire (C n) b) ((SelectedAdviceBounds.slotBudget S).eval n) E₁
+      (fun i a => keyAt (C n) (seed n (List.ofFn t)) (inputs n + i.val)
+        (GateHybrids.stageBit (C n) (v n z) (2 * b.1.val + b.2.val + side.toNat) i a))
+
+/-- The absent choice uses the constant-false function on each empty message type.
+`message C S seed v E₁ side n (none, t) = fun _ _ => false`. -/
+theorem message_none (side : Bool) (n : ℕ) (t : Tape S n) :
+    message C S seed v E₁ side n (none, t) = fun _ _ => false := rfl
+
+/-- A real choice's side-specific messages are exactly the selected occurrence plan at
+stage `2*gate+position+side.toNat`.
+`message C S seed v E₁ side n (some (z, b), t) = SelectedPlan.slotMessage (C n) (rows (C n)) (seed n
+(List.ofFn t)) (wire (C n) b) ((SelectedAdviceBounds.slotBudget S).eval n) E₁ (fun i a => keyAt (C
+n) (seed n (List.ofFn t)) (inputs n + i.val) (GateHybrids.stageBit (C n) (v n z) (2 * b.1.val +
+b.2.val + side.toNat) i a))`. -/
+theorem message_some (side : Bool) (n : ℕ) (z : I n) (b : Selection (C n)) (t : Tape S n) :
+    message C S seed v E₁ side n (some (z, b), t) =
+      SelectedPlan.slotMessage (C n) (rows (C n)) (seed n (List.ofFn t)) (wire (C n) b)
+        ((SelectedAdviceBounds.slotBudget S).eval n) E₁
+        (fun i a => keyAt (C n) (seed n (List.ofFn t)) (inputs n + i.val)
+          (GateHybrids.stageBit (C n) (v n z) (2 * b.1.val + b.2.val + side.toNat) i a)) := rfl
+
+/-- Real advice is the fixed before-stage descriptor/view word. The absent choice uses
+empty advice. Both sides use this same word, independent of the challenge key/nonces.
+`advice C S A B O seed v output bobWord aliceWord E₁ E₂ n (none, t) = []` and `advice C S A B O seed
+v output bobWord aliceWord E₁ E₂ n (some (z, b), t) = SelectedPlan.advice (C n) (rows (C n)) (seed n
+(List.ofFn t)) (wire (C n) b) E₁ E₂ (fun i a => keyAt (C n) (seed n (List.ofFn t)) (inputs n +
+i.val) (GateHybrids.stageBit (C n) (v n z) (2 * b.1.val + b.2.val) i a)) (pairSndD
+(GateHybridMessage.message (nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ n) (C n) (seed n (List.ofFn t)) (A.eval n) (O.eval
+n) (output n) (v n z) (2 * b.1.val + b.2.val))) (pairEncode (bobWord n z) (List.ofFn
+(FakeObjects.activeAnswers (C n) (seed n (List.ofFn t)) (A.eval n) (B.eval n)))) (aliceWord n
+z)`. -/
+noncomputable def advice (n : ℕ) (a : Choice I C S n) : List Bool :=
+  match a with
+  | (none, _) => []
+  | (some (z, b), t) => SelectedPlan.advice (C n) (rows (C n)) (seed n (List.ofFn t))
+      (wire (C n) b) E₁ E₂ (fun i a => keyAt (C n) (seed n (List.ofFn t)) (inputs n + i.val)
+        (GateHybrids.stageBit (C n) (v n z) (2 * b.1.val + b.2.val) i a))
+      (pairSndD (GateHybridMessage.message (nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ n) (C n) (seed n (List.ofFn t))
+        (A.eval n) (O.eval n) (output n) (v n z) (2 * b.1.val + b.2.val)))
+      (pairEncode (bobWord n z)
+        (List.ofFn (FakeObjects.activeAnswers (C n) (seed n (List.ofFn t)) (A.eval n)
+          (B.eval n)))) (aliceWord n z)
+
+/-- The absent choice has empty advice.
+`advice C S A B O seed v output bobWord aliceWord E₁ E₂ n (none, t) = []`. -/
+theorem advice_none (n : ℕ) (t : Tape S n) :
+    advice C S A B O seed v output bobWord aliceWord E₁ E₂ n (none, t) = [] := rfl
+
+/-- Real advice is the complete fixed before-stage selected-plan advice word.
+`advice C S A B O seed v output bobWord aliceWord E₁ E₂ n (some (z, b), t) = SelectedPlan.advice (C
+n) (rows (C n)) (seed n (List.ofFn t)) (wire (C n) b) E₁ E₂ (fun i a => keyAt (C n) (seed n
+(List.ofFn t)) (inputs n + i.val) (GateHybrids.stageBit (C n) (v n z) (2 * b.1.val + b.2.val) i a))
+(pairSndD (GateHybridMessage.message (nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ n) (C n) (seed n (List.ofFn t)) (A.eval n)
+(O.eval n) (output n) (v n z) (2 * b.1.val + b.2.val))) (pairEncode (bobWord n z) (List.ofFn
+(FakeObjects.activeAnswers (C n) (seed n (List.ofFn t)) (A.eval n) (B.eval n)))) (aliceWord n
+z)`. -/
+theorem advice_some (n : ℕ) (z : I n) (b : Selection (C n)) (t : Tape S n) :
+    advice C S A B O seed v output bobWord aliceWord E₁ E₂ n (some (z, b), t) =
+      SelectedPlan.advice (C n) (rows (C n)) (seed n (List.ofFn t)) (wire (C n) b) E₁ E₂
+        (fun i a => keyAt (C n) (seed n (List.ofFn t)) (inputs n + i.val)
+          (GateHybrids.stageBit (C n) (v n z) (2 * b.1.val + b.2.val) i a))
+        (pairSndD (GateHybridMessage.message (nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ n) (C n) (seed n (List.ofFn t))
+          (A.eval n) (O.eval n) (output n) (v n z) (2 * b.1.val + b.2.val)))
+        (pairEncode (bobWord n z)
+          (List.ofFn (FakeObjects.activeAnswers (C n) (seed n (List.ofFn t)) (A.eval n)
+            (B.eval n)))) (aliceWord n z) := rfl
+
+/-- Every choice's slice fits in a joint pad of length at least `7n+2`. The absent choice
+has offset and message length zero; real choices use the existing selected-plan fit.
+`offset C S seed n a j + n + len C S seed n a j ≤ L.eval n`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** Split the optional selection. The empty case needs only n bits and fits
+the supplied lower bound. In the real case the published selected-plan inequality bounds
+the slice endpoint by 7n+2, which the joint pad length bounds in turn. -/
+theorem fits (L : PolyExpr) (hL : ∀ n, 7 * n + 2 ≤ L.eval n)
+    (n : ℕ) (a : Choice I C S n) (j : Fin ((SelectedAdviceBounds.slotBudget S).eval n + 1)) :
+    offset C S seed n a j + n + len C S seed n a j ≤ L.eval n := by
+  rcases a with ⟨a, t⟩
+  cases a with
+  | none =>
+    rw [offset_none, len_none]
+    change 0 + n + 0 ≤ L.eval n
+    have hl := hL n
+    omega
+  | some a =>
+    rcases a with ⟨z, b⟩
+    rw [offset_some, len_some]
+    have hf := SelectedPlan.slots_fit (C n) (rows (C n)) (seed n (List.ofFn t))
+      (wire (C n) b) ((SelectedAdviceBounds.slotBudget S).eval n) j
+    have hl := hL n
+    omega
+
+/-- Every finite advice choice has length at most `sampleBudget S A B O R`, whenever
+circuit size and the retained input/output words have their stated bounds.
+[PS10, Theorem 163.7]
+`(advice C S A B O seed v output bobWord aliceWord E₁ E₂ n a).length ≤
+(SelectedAdviceBounds.sampleBudget S A B O R).eval n`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** Empty advice satisfies every natural-number budget. For a real selection,
+apply the existing complete sample-advice budget to its baseline seed, selected vertex and
+before-stage plaintext family. The row reader supplies the required canonical enumeration. -/
+theorem advice_budget (R : PolyExpr) (hSize : ∀ n, (C n).size ≤ S.eval n)
+    (hfanin : ∀ n, (C n).IsFaninTwo)
+    (hBob : ∀ n z, (bobWord n z).length ≤ B.eval n)
+    (hAlice : ∀ n z, (aliceWord n z).length ≤ R.eval n)
+    (n : ℕ) (a : Choice I C S n) :
+    (advice C S A B O seed v output bobWord aliceWord E₁ E₂ n a).length ≤
+      (SelectedAdviceBounds.sampleBudget S A B O R).eval n := by
+  rcases a with ⟨a, t⟩
+  cases a with
+  | none =>
+    rw [advice_none, List.length_nil]
+    exact Nat.zero_le _
+  | some a =>
+    rcases a with ⟨z, b⟩
+    rw [advice_some]
+    exact SelectedAdviceBounds.sample_advice_budget E₁ E₂ (C n) (rows (C n))
+      (seed n (List.ofFn t)) (wire (C n) b)
+      (fun i a => keyAt (C n) (seed n (List.ofFn t)) (inputs n + i.val)
+        (GateHybrids.stageBit (C n) (v n z) (2 * b.1.val + b.2.val) i a))
+      S A B O R (output n) (v n z) (2 * b.1.val + b.2.val) (bobWord n z) (aliceWord n z)
+      (hfanin n) (hSize n) (rows_eq (C n)) (hBob n z) (hAlice n z)
+
+section Joint
+
+variable {L : PolyExpr} {L₂ : ℕ → ℕ}
+  (G : ∀ n, (Fin n → Bool) → (Fin n → Bool) → (Fin (L.eval n) → Bool))
+  (G₁ : ∀ n, (Fin n → Bool) → (Fin n → Bool) → (Fin (n + n) → Bool))
+  (G₂ : ∀ n, (Fin n → Bool) → (Fin n → Bool) → (Fin (L₂ n) → Bool))
+  (hG : ∀ n (s x : Fin n → Bool),
+    List.ofFn (G n s x) = List.ofFn (G₁ n s x) ++ List.ofFn (G₂ n s x))
+  (hGE₁ : ∀ n (s x : Fin n → Bool), E₁ (pairEncode (List.ofFn s) (List.ofFn x)) =
+    List.ofFn (G₁ n s x))
+  (hGE₂ : ∀ n (s x : Fin n → Bool), E₂ (pairEncode (List.ofFn s) (List.ofFn x)) =
+    List.ofFn (G₂ n s x))
+
+include hG hGE₁ hGE₂ in
+/-- At every real choice, reconstructing either side's slice experiment with the common
+choice advice gives exactly the corresponding actual hybrid sample under the filled seed.
+All structural premises are discharged from circuit size, fan-in, consistency and sinks.
+[PS10, Theorem 163.7]
+`SliceReconstruction.rebuild E₂ (pairEncode (List.replicate n true) (pairEncode (sliceMultiEncDist G
+n ((SelectedAdviceBounds.slotBudget S).eval n) (offset C S seed n (some (z, b), t)) (len C S seed n
+(some (z, b), t)) (message C S seed v E₁ side n (some (z, b), t)) (k, rs)) (advice C S A B O seed v
+output bobWord aliceWord E₁ E₂ n (some (z, b), t)))) = GateHybridMessage.sample (nestedSpecial E₁ E₂
+n) (C n) (SelectedSeed.fill (C n) (rows (C n)) (seed n (List.ofFn t)) (wire (C n) b)
+((SelectedAdviceBounds.slotBudget S).eval n) k rs) (A.eval n) (B.eval n) (O.eval n) (output n) (v n
+z) (List.replicate n true) (bobWord n z) [] (aliceWord n z) (2 * b.1.val + b.2.val + side.toNat)`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** Apply the proved selected-step contract to the actual selected position.
+The finite wire reader identifies its vertex; the canonical row reader fixes the row order,
+and the existing occurrence bound supplies all padded slots. The two side-specific messages
+and common before-stage advice are exactly the public choice maps. -/
+theorem rebuild_some (hSize : ∀ n, (C n).size ≤ S.eval n)
+    (hfanin : ∀ n, (C n).IsFaninTwo)
+    (hEval : ∀ n z (i : Fin (C n).gates.length),
+      gateOut ((C n).gates[i]) (RelabelGarbling.argValues (C n) (v n z) i) =
+        v n z (inputs n + i))
+    (hsink : ∀ n j g, g ∈ (C n).gates → output n j ∉ g.args)
+    (n : ℕ) (z : I n) (b : Selection (C n)) (t : Tape S n)
+    (k : Fin n → Bool)
+    (rs : Fin ((SelectedAdviceBounds.slotBudget S).eval n + 1) → Fin n → Bool)
+    (side : Bool) :
+    SliceReconstruction.rebuild E₂ (pairEncode (List.replicate n true) (pairEncode
+      (sliceMultiEncDist G n ((SelectedAdviceBounds.slotBudget S).eval n)
+        (offset C S seed n (some (z, b), t)) (len C S seed n (some (z, b), t))
+        (message C S seed v E₁ side n (some (z, b), t)) (k, rs))
+      (advice C S A B O seed v output bobWord aliceWord E₁ E₂ n (some (z, b), t)))) =
+      GateHybridMessage.sample (nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ n) (C n)
+        (SelectedSeed.fill (C n) (rows (C n)) (seed n (List.ofFn t)) (wire (C n) b)
+          ((SelectedAdviceBounds.slotBudget S).eval n) k rs)
+        (A.eval n) (B.eval n) (O.eval n) (output n) (v n z) (List.replicate n true)
+        (bobWord n z) [] (aliceWord n z) (2 * b.1.val + b.2.val + side.toNat) :=
+  SelectedStep.reconstruct G G₁ G₂ E₁ E₂ hG hGE₁ hGE₂ (C n) (rows (C n))
+    (seed n (List.ofFn t)) (wire (C n) b) ((SelectedAdviceBounds.slotBudget S).eval n)
+    (v n z) (hfanin n) (hEval n z) b.1 b.2 (wire_val (C n) b).symm
+    (capacity (C n) S n (seed n (List.ofFn t)).2.2 (wire (C n) b).val (hSize n) (hfanin n))
+    (rows_eq (C n)) (A.eval n) (B.eval n) (O.eval n) (output n) (hsink n)
+    (List.replicate n true) (bobWord n z) (aliceWord n z) k rs side
+
+end Joint
+
+end Data
+
+end TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof.SelectedStepChoices

+import TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.Yao.HybridProof.SelectedStepChoices
+import TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.Yao.HybridProof.SliceReduction
+import TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.Yao.HybridProof.SelectedLaw

+set_option relaxedAutoImplicit false
+set_option autoImplicit false

+/-!
+# One uniform negligible bound for all selected neighboring hybrids
+
+The advice family simultaneously ranges over all inputs, actual selected arguments and
+baseline tapes, with an empty option for degenerate circuits. One reconstruction machine
+and the proved polynomial advice bound turn joint slice security into a uniform bound.
+
+## Main definitions
+
+None.
+
+## Main results
+
+* `filled_negligible`: a uniform nonnegative negligible bound with fixed baseline tapes.
+* `selected_negligible`: the bound for every actual uniform-tape selected substep.
+
+## References
+
+* [PS10] R. Pass, a. shelat, *A Course in Cryptography*, lecture notes, 3rd ed., January
+  2010, Theorem 163.7 (the inactive-key hybrid, fixing all other coins).
+-/
+
+namespace TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof.SelectedStepSecurity
+
+open Complexity Turing BoolCircuit TCSlib.Cryptography
+
+section Data
+
+variable {I : ℕ → Type} [∀ n, Fintype (I n)] {inputs : ℕ → ℕ}
+  (C : ∀ n, DAGCircuit (inputs n)) (S A B O R : PolyExpr)
+  (seed : ∀ n, List Bool →
+    GarbleSeed (C n) (Fin n → Bool) ((Fin n → Bool) × (Fin n → Bool)))
+  (v : ∀ n, I n → ℕ → Bool) (output : ∀ n, Fin (O.eval n) → ℕ)
+  (bobWord aliceWord : ∀ n, I n → List Bool)
+  (hSize : ∀ n, (C n).size ≤ S.eval n) (hfanin : ∀ n, (C n).IsFaninTwo)
+  (hEval : ∀ n z (i : Fin (C n).gates.length),
+    gateOut ((C n).gates[i]) (RelabelGarbling.argValues (C n) (v n z) i) =
+      v n z (inputs n + i))
+  (hsink : ∀ n j g, g ∈ (C n).gates → output n j ∉ g.args)
+  (hBob : ∀ n z, (bobWord n z).length ≤ B.eval n)
+  (hAlice : ∀ n z, (aliceWord n z).length ≤ R.eval n)
+  {L : PolyExpr} {L₂ : ℕ → ℕ}
+  (G : ∀ n, (Fin n → Bool) → (Fin n → Bool) → (Fin (L.eval n) → Bool))
+  (G₁ : ∀ n, (Fin n → Bool) → (Fin n → Bool) → (Fin (n + n) → Bool))
+  (G₂ : ∀ n, (Fin n → Bool) → (Fin n → Bool) → (Fin (L₂ n) → Bool))
+  (E₁ E₂ : List Bool → List Bool) (hE₂ : PolyTimeComputable E₂)
+  (hG : ∀ n (s x : Fin n → Bool),
+    List.ofFn (G n s x) = List.ofFn (G₁ n s x) ++ List.ofFn (G₂ n s x))
+  (hGE₁ : ∀ n (s x : Fin n → Bool), E₁ (pairEncode (List.ofFn s) (List.ofFn x)) =
+    List.ofFn (G₁ n s x))
+  (hGE₂ : ∀ n (s x : Fin n → Bool), E₂ (pairEncode (List.ofFn s) (List.ofFn x)) =
+    List.ofFn (G₂ n s x))
+  (hL : ∀ n, 7 * n + 2 ≤ L.eval n) (hSecure : SliceMultiMessageSecure G)
+
+include hSize hfanin hEval hsink hBob hAlice hE₂ hG hGE₁ hGE₂ hL hSecure in
+/-- Joint slice security gives one nonnegative negligible bound for every input, actual
+selected argument and fixed baseline tape, between the two neighboring samples after
+fresh selected-key/nonce fill. The bound is uniform over all these choices.
+[PS10, Theorem 163.7]
+`∃ μ : ℕ → ℝ, Negligible μ ∧ (∀ n, 0 ≤ μ n) ∧ ∀ n (z : I n) (b : SelectedStepChoices.Selection (C
+n)) (t : SelectedStepChoices.Tape S n), distAdv (fun r : (Fin n → Bool) × (Fin
+((SelectedAdviceBounds.slotBudget S).eval n + 1) → Fin n → Bool) => GateHybridMessage.sample
+(nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ n) (C n) (SelectedSeed.fill (C n) (SelectedStepChoices.rows (C n)) (seed n
+(List.ofFn t)) (SelectedStepChoices.wire (C n) b) ((SelectedAdviceBounds.slotBudget S).eval n) r.1
+r.2) (A.eval n) (B.eval n) (O.eval n) (output n) (v n z) (List.replicate n true) (bobWord n z) []
+(aliceWord n z) (2 * b.1.val + b.2.val)) (fun r : (Fin n → Bool) × (Fin
+((SelectedAdviceBounds.slotBudget S).eval n + 1) → Fin n → Bool) => GateHybridMessage.sample
+(nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ n) (C n) (SelectedSeed.fill (C n) (SelectedStepChoices.rows (C n)) (seed n
+(List.ofFn t)) (SelectedStepChoices.wire (C n) b) ((SelectedAdviceBounds.slotBudget S).eval n) r.1
+r.2) (A.eval n) (B.eval n) (O.eval n) (output n) (v n z) (List.replicate n true) (bobWord n z) []
+(aliceWord n z) (2 * b.1.val + b.2.val + 1)) (D.distinguisherL n) ≤ μ n`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** Apply the existing finite-advice reduction to the padded choice family,
+using the complete advice polynomial and the total reconstruction machine. The empty
+choice makes this family nonempty and its nonnegative advantage forces the resulting bound
+to be nonnegative at every parameter. At each real choice, the two pointwise reconstruction
+readers replace the reconstructed slice experiments by the actual filled-seed hybrid samples.
+The advice is identical on both sides and independent of the sampled key and nonces. -/
+theorem filled_negligible (D : NUPPT) :
+    ∃ μ : ℕ → ℝ, Negligible μ ∧ (∀ n, 0 ≤ μ n) ∧
+      ∀ n (z : I n) (b : SelectedStepChoices.Selection (C n))
+        (t : SelectedStepChoices.Tape S n),
+      distAdv
+        (fun r : (Fin n → Bool) ×
+            (Fin ((SelectedAdviceBounds.slotBudget S).eval n + 1) → Fin n → Bool) =>
+          GateHybridMessage.sample (nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ n) (C n)
+            (SelectedSeed.fill (C n) (SelectedStepChoices.rows (C n)) (seed n (List.ofFn t))
+              (SelectedStepChoices.wire (C n) b) ((SelectedAdviceBounds.slotBudget S).eval n)
+              r.1 r.2)
+            (A.eval n) (B.eval n) (O.eval n) (output n) (v n z) (List.replicate n true)
+            (bobWord n z) [] (aliceWord n z) (2 * b.1.val + b.2.val))
+        (fun r : (Fin n → Bool) ×
+            (Fin ((SelectedAdviceBounds.slotBudget S).eval n + 1) → Fin n → Bool) =>
+          GateHybridMessage.sample (nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ n) (C n)
+            (SelectedSeed.fill (C n) (SelectedStepChoices.rows (C n)) (seed n (List.ofFn t))
+              (SelectedStepChoices.wire (C n) b) ((SelectedAdviceBounds.slotBudget S).eval n)
+              r.1 r.2)
+            (A.eval n) (B.eval n) (O.eval n) (output n) (v n z) (List.replicate n true)
+            (bobWord n z) [] (aliceWord n z) (2 * b.1.val + b.2.val + 1))
+        (D.distinguisherL n) ≤ μ n := by
+  classical
+  let Q := SelectedAdviceBounds.slotBudget S
+  let budget := SelectedAdviceBounds.sampleBudget S A B O R
+  letI : ∀ n, Fintype (SelectedStepChoices.Choice I C S n) := fun _ => Fintype.ofFinite _
+  letI : ∀ n, Nonempty (SelectedStepChoices.Choice I C S n) :=
+    fun _ => ⟨(none, fun _ => false)⟩
+  -- One worst finite choice fixes the input, step and all baseline coins.
+  obtain ⟨μ, hμ, hBound⟩ := SliceReduction.reconstruction_negligible hSecure D Q budget
+    (SelectedStepChoices.advice C S A B O seed v output bobWord aliceWord E₁ E₂)
+    (SelectedStepChoices.advice_budget C S A B O seed v output bobWord aliceWord E₁ E₂
+      R hSize hfanin hBob hAlice)
+    (SliceReconstruction.rebuild_poly hE₂)
+    (SelectedStepChoices.offset C S seed) (SelectedStepChoices.len C S seed)
+    (SelectedStepChoices.fits C S seed L hL)
+    (SelectedStepChoices.message C S seed v E₁ false)
+    (SelectedStepChoices.message C S seed v E₁ true)
+  have hNonneg (n : ℕ) : 0 ≤ μ n := by
+    have hb := hBound n ((none, fun _ => false) : SelectedStepChoices.Choice I C S n)
+    rw [distAdv_def] at hb
+    exact (abs_nonneg _).trans hb
+  refine ⟨μ, hμ, hNonneg, ?_⟩
+  intro n z b t
+  -- Both real endpoints use the same advice, offsets and lengths.
+  have hPoint (side : Bool) :
+      (fun r : (Fin n → Bool) × (Fin (Q.eval n + 1) → Fin n → Bool) =>
+        SliceReconstruction.rebuild E₂ (pairEncode (List.replicate n true) (pairEncode
+          (sliceMultiEncDist G n (Q.eval n)
+            (SelectedStepChoices.offset C S seed n (some (z, b), t))
+            (SelectedStepChoices.len C S seed n (some (z, b), t))
+            (SelectedStepChoices.message C S seed v E₁ side n (some (z, b), t)) r)
+          (SelectedStepChoices.advice C S A B O seed v output bobWord aliceWord E₁ E₂
+            n (some (z, b), t))))) =
+      (fun r : (Fin n → Bool) × (Fin (Q.eval n + 1) → Fin n → Bool) =>
+        GateHybridMessage.sample (nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ n) (C n)
+          (SelectedSeed.fill (C n) (SelectedStepChoices.rows (C n)) (seed n (List.ofFn t))
+            (SelectedStepChoices.wire (C n) b) (Q.eval n) r.1 r.2)
+          (A.eval n) (B.eval n) (O.eval n) (output n) (v n z) (List.replicate n true)
+          (bobWord n z) [] (aliceWord n z) (2 * b.1.val + b.2.val + side.toNat)) := by
+    funext r
+    exact SelectedStepChoices.rebuild_some C S A B O seed v output bobWord aliceWord E₁ E₂
+      G G₁ G₂ hG hGE₁ hGE₂ hSize hfanin hEval hsink n z b t r.1 r.2 side
+  have hb := hBound n (some (z, b), t)
+  rw [hPoint false, hPoint true] at hb
+  simpa only [Bool.toNat_false, Bool.toNat_true, Nat.add_zero] using hb
+
+variable
+  (hK : ∀ n q (w : Fin (C n).size) (b : Bool) (t : Fin n),
+    (seed n q).1 w b t = q.getD ((b.toNat * S.eval n + w.val) * n + t.val) false)
+  (hR₀ : ∀ n q (i : Fin (C n).gates.length)
+      (a : Fin (C n).gates[i.val].args.length → Bool) (t : Fin n),
+    ((seed n q).2.1 i a).1 t = q.getD (2 * S.eval n * n + (4 * i.val +
+      (List.ofFn a).foldr (fun b k => b.toNat + 2 * k) 0) * (2 * n) + t.val) false)
+  (hR₁ : ∀ n q (i : Fin (C n).gates.length)
+      (a : Fin (C n).gates[i.val].args.length → Bool) (t : Fin n),
+    ((seed n q).2.1 i a).2 t = q.getD (2 * S.eval n * n + (4 * i.val +
+      (List.ofFn a).foldr (fun b k => b.toNat + 2 * k) 0) * (2 * n) + n + t.val) false)
+  (hP : ∀ n q (i : Fin (C n).gates.length)
+      (a : Fin (C n).gates[i.val].args.length → Bool)
+      (t : Fin (C n).gates[i.val].args.length),
+    (seed n q).2.2 i a t = xor (a t)
+      (q.getD (10 * S.eval n * n + 2 * i.val + t.val) false))
+
+include hSize hfanin hEval hsink hBob hAlice hE₂ hG hGE₁ hGE₂ hL hSecure
+  hK hR₀ hR₁ hP in
+/-- For the actual addressed uniform tape distribution, one nonnegative negligible function
+bounds the advantage of every neighboring hybrid substep with an actual selected argument,
+uniformly in the input, gate and argument. Empty circuits require no selected vertex.
+[PS10, Theorem 163.7]
+`∃ μ : ℕ → ℝ, Negligible μ ∧ (∀ n, 0 ≤ μ n) ∧ ∀ n (z : I n) (i : Fin (C n).gates.length) (p : Fin (C
+n).gates[i.val].args.length), distAdv (fun t : SelectedStepChoices.Tape S n =>
+GateHybridMessage.sample (nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ n) (C n) (seed n (List.ofFn t)) (A.eval n) (B.eval n)
+(O.eval n) (output n) (v n z) (List.replicate n true) (bobWord n z) [] (aliceWord n z) (2 * i.val +
+p.val)) (fun t : SelectedStepChoices.Tape S n => GateHybridMessage.sample (nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ n) (C
+n) (seed n (List.ofFn t)) (A.eval n) (B.eval n) (O.eval n) (output n) (v n z) (List.replicate n
+true) (bobWord n z) [] (aliceWord n z) (2 * i.val + p.val + 1)) (D.distinguisherL n) ≤ μ n`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** Obtain the uniform bound with each baseline tape fixed. Average that tape
+using the existing fiber advantage inequality, which applies because the bound is nonnegative.
+The proved actual-tape fill law then identifies each averaged filled-seed acceptance
+probability with its original uniform-tape acceptance probability. Apply this law separately
+to the before and after stages. All shifts and unused padded coordinates retain their actual
+law; no uniformity claim about the induced permutation or distinctness of key values is used. -/
+theorem selected_negligible (D : NUPPT) :
+    ∃ μ : ℕ → ℝ, Negligible μ ∧ (∀ n, 0 ≤ μ n) ∧
+      ∀ n (z : I n) (i : Fin (C n).gates.length) (p : Fin (C n).gates[i.val].args.length),
+      distAdv
+        (fun t : SelectedStepChoices.Tape S n =>
+          GateHybridMessage.sample (nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ n) (C n) (seed n (List.ofFn t))
+            (A.eval n) (B.eval n) (O.eval n) (output n) (v n z) (List.replicate n true)
+            (bobWord n z) [] (aliceWord n z) (2 * i.val + p.val))
+        (fun t : SelectedStepChoices.Tape S n =>
+          GateHybridMessage.sample (nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ n) (C n) (seed n (List.ofFn t))
+            (A.eval n) (B.eval n) (O.eval n) (output n) (v n z) (List.replicate n true)
+            (bobWord n z) [] (aliceWord n z) (2 * i.val + p.val + 1))
+        (D.distinguisherL n) ≤ μ n := by
+  obtain ⟨μ, hμ, hNonneg, hBound⟩ := filled_negligible C S A B O R seed v output
+    bobWord aliceWord hSize hfanin hEval hsink hBob hAlice G G₁ G₂ E₁ E₂ hE₂
+    hG hGE₁ hGE₂ hL hSecure D
+  refine ⟨μ, hμ, hNonneg, ?_⟩
+  intro n z i p
+  let b : SelectedStepChoices.Selection (C n) := ⟨i, p⟩
+  let u := SelectedStepChoices.wire (C n) b
+  let q := (SelectedAdviceBounds.slotBudget S).eval n
+  let X := fun (stage : ℕ)
+      (s : GarbleSeed (C n) (Fin n → Bool) ((Fin n → Bool) × (Fin n → Bool))) =>
+    GateHybridMessage.sample (nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ n) (C n) s
+      (A.eval n) (B.eval n) (O.eval n) (output n) (v n z) (List.replicate n true)
+      (bobWord n z) [] (aliceWord n z) stage
+  -- Average only the independent baseline tape; the challenge and test coins stay separate.
+  have hAvg := SliceReduction.distAdv_fibers_le
+    (fun (t : SelectedStepChoices.Tape S n)
+        (r : (Fin n → Bool) × (Fin (q + 1) → Fin n → Bool)) =>
+      X (2 * i.val + p.val) (SelectedSeed.fill (C n) (SelectedStepChoices.rows (C n))
+        (seed n (List.ofFn t)) u q r.1 r.2))
+    (fun (t : SelectedStepChoices.Tape S n)
+        (r : (Fin n → Bool) × (Fin (q + 1) → Fin n → Bool)) =>
+      X (2 * i.val + p.val + 1) (SelectedSeed.fill (C n) (SelectedStepChoices.rows (C n))
+        (seed n (List.ofFn t)) u q r.1 r.2))
+    (D.distinguisherL n) (μ n) (hNonneg n) (hBound n z b)
+  have hfan := (DAGCircuit.isFaninTwo_iff (C n)).mp (hfanin n)
+  have hA (j : Fin (C n).gates.length) : (C n).gates[j.val].args.length ≤ 2 :=
+    hfan.2 _ (List.getElem_mem j.isLt)
+  have hargs (j : Fin (C n).gates.length) : (C n).gates[j.val].args.Nodup :=
+    (hfan.1 _ (List.getElem_mem j.isLt)).1
+  -- The same proved fill law applies to either complete sampler endpoint.
+  have hLaw (stage : ℕ) := SelectedLaw.acceptProb_fill (C n) (S.eval n) n hA (hSize n)
+    (seed n) (hK n) (hR₀ n) (hR₁ n) (hP n) (SelectedStepChoices.rows (C n)) u q
+    hargs (SelectedStepChoices.rows_complete (C n)) (SelectedStepChoices.rows_nodup (C n))
+    (X stage) (D.distinguisherL n)
+  rw [distAdv_def] at hAvg ⊢
+  rw [hLaw _, hLaw _] at hAvg
+  exact hAvg
+
+end Data
+
+end TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof.SelectedStepSecurity

+import TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.Yao.HybridProof.SelectedPlan
+import TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.Yao.HybridProof.Lengths
+import TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.Yao.HybridProof.GateHybrids
+import TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.Yao.HybridProof.SelectedFields

+set_option maxHeartbeats 0
+set_option relaxedAutoImplicit false
+set_option autoImplicit false

+/-!
+# Exact selected-key table and view reconstruction
+
+Every challenge message, record and surrounding field is fixed before the sampled key and
+nonce vector are supplied. Reconstruction uses every occurrence of the selected inactive
+label, including occurrences at gates outside the gate currently switched by the hybrid.
+The plaintext-key family is a generic fixed parameter, specialized through the shared
+hybrid objects' public contracts. No protocol-specific circuit or strategy definition occurs here.
+
+## Main definitions
+
+None.
+
+## Main results
+
+* `SelectedTables.record_reconstruct`: exact reconstruction for every sampler seed.
+* Complete table and encoded-view reconstruction from the padded slice experiment.
+
+## References
+
+* [PS10] R. Pass, a. shelat, *A Course in Cryptography*, lecture notes, 3rd ed., January
+  2010, Theorem 163.7 (joint multi-message embedding under one inactive key).
+* [LP09] Y. Lindell, B. Pinkas, *A proof of security of Yao's protocol for two-party
+  computation*, Journal of Cryptology 22(2), 2009, pp. 161–188.
+-/
+
+namespace TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof.SelectedTables
+
+open Complexity Turing BoolCircuit TCSlib.Cryptography
+
+section Joint
+
+variable {L L₂ : ℕ → ℕ}
+  (G : ∀ n, (Fin n → Bool) → (Fin n → Bool) → (Fin (L n) → Bool))
+  (G₁ : ∀ n, (Fin n → Bool) → (Fin n → Bool) → (Fin (n + n) → Bool))
+  (G₂ : ∀ n, (Fin n → Bool) → (Fin n → Bool) → (Fin (L₂ n) → Bool))
+  (E₁ E₂ : List Bool → List Bool)
+  (hG : ∀ n (s x : Fin n → Bool),
+    List.ofFn (G n s x) = List.ofFn (G₁ n s x) ++ List.ofFn (G₂ n s x))
+  (hGE₁ : ∀ n (s x : Fin n → Bool), E₁ (pairEncode (List.ofFn s) (List.ofFn x)) =
+    List.ofFn (G₁ n s x))
+  (hGE₂ : ∀ n (s x : Fin n → Bool), E₂ (pairEncode (List.ofFn s) (List.ofFn x)) =
+    List.ofFn (G₂ n s x))
+
+include hG hGE₁ hGE₂ in
+/-- Every row record reconstructs precisely the nested encryption using the filled seed's
+argument keys and nonce pair and the fixed plaintext key, for every challenge key and nonce
+vector. The capacity covers the complete all-gate occurrence list. [PS10, Theorem 163.7]
+`SliceReconstruction.row E₂ (pairEncode (sliceMultiEncDist G n q (SelectedPlan.slotOffset C rows s u
+q) (SelectedPlan.slotLength C rows s u q) (SelectedPlan.slotMessage C rows s u q E₁ o) (k, rs))
+(SelectedPlan.record C rows s u E₁ E₂ o i a)) = (nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ n).enc (argKeys C
+(SelectedSeed.fill C rows s u q k rs) i a) (o i a) ((SelectedSeed.fill C rows s u q k rs).2.1 i a)`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** An unselected row retains both its argument-key list and nonce pair, so its
+fixed record is already the required row. For a selected argument, its occurrence rank fits
+the padded capacity. Reading that slot recovers the row's exact slice shape and precomputed
+message. The selected-row reconstruction theorem replaces precisely the argument key and
+nonce half described by the filled-seed readers. All gates using the selected key share this
+same sampler key while receiving their own independent slot nonce. -/
+theorem record_reconstruct {m n : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit m)
+    (rows : (i : Fin C.gates.length) → List (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool))
+    (s : GarbleSeed C (Fin n → Bool) ((Fin n → Bool) × (Fin n → Bool)))
+    (u : Fin C.size) (q : ℕ)
+    (o : (i : Fin C.gates.length) → (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool) → (Fin n → Bool))
+    (hargs : ∀ i : Fin C.gates.length, C.gates[i.val].args.Nodup)
+    (hfan : ∀ i : Fin C.gates.length, C.gates[i.val].args.length ≤ 2)
+    (hcomplete : ∀ i a, a ∈ rows i)
+    (hcap : (GateOccurrences.occurrences C rows s.2.2 u.val).length ≤ q + 1)
+    (i : Fin C.gates.length) (a : Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool)
+    (k : Fin n → Bool) (rs : Fin (q + 1) → Fin n → Bool) :
+    SliceReconstruction.row E₂ (pairEncode
+      (sliceMultiEncDist G n q (SelectedPlan.slotOffset C rows s u q)
+        (SelectedPlan.slotLength C rows s u q) (SelectedPlan.slotMessage C rows s u q E₁ o)
+          (k, rs)) (SelectedPlan.record C rows s u E₁ E₂ o i a)) =
+      (nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ n).enc (argKeys C (SelectedSeed.fill C rows s u q k rs) i a)
+        (o i a) ((SelectedSeed.fill C rows s u q k rs).2.1 i a) := by
+  cases hsel : GateOccurrences.selectedPosition C.gates[i.val] a u.val with
+  | none =>
+    rw [SelectedPlan.record_eq, hsel, Option.elim_none, SliceReconstruction.row_fixedRecord,
+      SelectedSeed.fill_argKeys_none C rows s u q k rs i a (hargs i) hsel,
+      SelectedSeed.fill_nonce, SelectedSeed.nonce_of_none C rows s u.val q rs i a hsel]
+  | some p =>
+    obtain ⟨hvertex, hbit⟩ :=
+      (GateOccurrences.selectedPosition_eq_some_iff _ _ _ (hargs i) p).mp hsel
+    have hslot := lt_of_lt_of_le
+      (SelectedSeed.slotIndex_lt C rows s.2.2 u.val i a p (hargs i)
+        (hcomplete i) hvertex hbit) hcap
+    let j : Fin (q + 1) := ⟨SelectedSeed.slotIndex C rows s.2.2 u.val i a p, hslot⟩
+    have hpad := SelectedSeed.slotIndex_padded C rows s.2.2 u.val q i a p (hargs i)
+      (hcomplete i) hvertex hbit hslot
+    have hread := SelectedPlan.slotReaders_of_padded C rows s u q E₁ o j i
+      (SelectedSeed.rowIndex C rows s.2.2 i a) p.val hpad
+    have hactual := SelectedSeed.rowIndex_actual C rows s.2.2 i a (hcomplete i)
+    obtain ⟨hOff, _, hWord⟩ := hread
+    have hOff' := hOff.trans (congrArg
+      (fun b => SelectedRows.offset n (argKeys C s i b) p.val) hactual)
+    have hWord' := hWord.trans (congrArg
+      (fun b => SelectedRows.word E₁ n (argKeys C s i b) (o i b) (s.2.1 i b) p.val) hactual)
+    have hMsg := (SelectedPlan.slotMessage_ofFn C rows s u q hGE₁ o j).trans hWord'
+    have hlen : (argKeys C s i a).length = C.gates[i.val].args.length := by
+      simp only [argKeys_eq, List.length_ofFn, Fin.getElem_fin]
+    have hks : (argKeys C s i a).length ≤ 2 := by rw [hlen]; exact hfan i
+    have hp : p.val < (argKeys C s i a).length := by rw [hlen]; exact p.isLt
+    rw [SelectedPlan.record_eq, hsel, Option.elim_some,
+      SelectedSeed.fill_argKeys_some C rows s u q k rs i a p (hargs i) hsel,
+      SelectedSeed.fill_nonce, SelectedSeed.nonce_of_some C rows s u.val q rs i a p hsel hslot]
+    exact SelectedRows.reconstruct G G₁ G₂ E₁ E₂ hG hGE₁ hGE₂ n q (argKeys C s i a)
+      (o i a) (s.2.1 i a) p.val j hks hp (SelectedPlan.slotOffset C rows s u q)
+      (SelectedPlan.slotLength C rows s u q) (SelectedPlan.slotMessage C rows s u q E₁ o)
+      hOff' hMsg k rs
+
+include hG hGE₁ hGE₂ in
+/-- Reconstructing all descriptor tables yields exactly every filled-seed row in the original
+gate and permuted-row order, for every slice-sampler key and nonce vector.
+[PS10, Theorem 163.7]
+`SliceReconstruction.tables E₂ (pairEncode (sliceMultiEncDist G n q (SelectedPlan.slotOffset C rows
+s u q) (SelectedPlan.slotLength C rows s u q) (SelectedPlan.slotMessage C rows s u q E₁ o) (k, rs))
+(BinArith.encWords ((SelectedPlan.tables C rows s u E₁ E₂ o).map BinArith.encWords))) =
+BinArith.encWords (List.ofFn fun i => BinArith.encWords ((rows i).map fun a => (nestedSpecial E₁ E₂
+n).enc (argKeys C (SelectedSeed.fill C rows s u q k rs) i (s.2.2 i a)) (o i (s.2.2 i a))
+((SelectedSeed.fill C rows s u q k rs).2.1 i (s.2.2 i a))))`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** The public nested-list reconstruction contract maps each stored descriptor
+in place. The descriptor table reader exposes the original gate-index and row enumerations.
+Apply the exact row reconstruction identity at every enumerated assignment. No gate using
+the selected key is omitted from the common slice experiment. -/
+theorem tables_reconstruct {m n : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit m)
+    (rows : (i : Fin C.gates.length) → List (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool))
+    (s : GarbleSeed C (Fin n → Bool) ((Fin n → Bool) × (Fin n → Bool)))
+    (u : Fin C.size) (q : ℕ)
+    (o : (i : Fin C.gates.length) → (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool) → (Fin n → Bool))
+    (hargs : ∀ i : Fin C.gates.length, C.gates[i.val].args.Nodup)
+    (hfan : ∀ i : Fin C.gates.length, C.gates[i.val].args.length ≤ 2)
+    (hcomplete : ∀ i a, a ∈ rows i)
+    (hcap : (GateOccurrences.occurrences C rows s.2.2 u.val).length ≤ q + 1)
+    (k : Fin n → Bool) (rs : Fin (q + 1) → Fin n → Bool) :
+    SliceReconstruction.tables E₂ (pairEncode
+      (sliceMultiEncDist G n q (SelectedPlan.slotOffset C rows s u q)
+        (SelectedPlan.slotLength C rows s u q) (SelectedPlan.slotMessage C rows s u q E₁ o)
+          (k, rs))
+      (BinArith.encWords ((SelectedPlan.tables C rows s u E₁ E₂ o).map BinArith.encWords))) =
+      BinArith.encWords (List.ofFn fun i => BinArith.encWords ((rows i).map fun a =>
+        (nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ n).enc
+          (argKeys C (SelectedSeed.fill C rows s u q k rs) i (s.2.2 i a))
+          (o i (s.2.2 i a)) ((SelectedSeed.fill C rows s u q k rs).2.1 i (s.2.2 i a)))) := by
+  rw [SliceReconstruction.tables_records, SelectedPlan.tables_eq, List.map_ofFn]
+  apply congrArg BinArith.encWords
+  apply congrArg List.ofFn
+  funext i
+  apply congrArg BinArith.encWords
+  rw [List.map_map]
+  apply List.map_congr_left
+  intro a _
+  exact record_reconstruct G G₁ G₂ E₁ E₂ hG hGE₁ hGE₂ C rows s u q o
+    hargs hfan hcomplete hcap i (s.2.2 i a) k rs
+
+include hG hGE₁ hGE₂ in
+/-- With canonical row enumeration, reconstruction gives the shared generic hybrid tables.
+The fixed plaintext family is read from the original seed; symbolic output-label separation
+identifies it with the filled seed's output keys. [PS10, Theorem 163.7]
+`SliceReconstruction.tables E₂ (pairEncode (sliceMultiEncDist G n q (SelectedPlan.slotOffset C rows
+s u q) (SelectedPlan.slotLength C rows s u q) (SelectedPlan.slotMessage C rows s u q E₁ (fun i a =>
+keyAt C s (m + i.val) (bit i a))) (k, rs)) (BinArith.encWords ((SelectedPlan.tables C rows s u E₁ E₂
+(fun i a => keyAt C s (m + i.val) (bit i a))).map BinArith.encWords))) = BinArith.encWords
+(List.ofFn fun i => BinArith.encWords (GateHybrids.tableWith (nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ n) C
+(SelectedSeed.fill C rows s u q k rs) bit i))`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** Apply the complete table reconstruction theorem using the original seed's
+output keys as fixed plaintexts. The canonical enumeration is the hybrid table's enumeration.
+For each row, the filled permutation is unchanged and output-label separation makes its
+plaintext key unchanged. The published hybrid-row reader then identifies the ciphertext. -/
+theorem tablesWith_reconstruct {m n : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit m)
+    (rows : (i : Fin C.gates.length) → List (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool))
+    (s : GarbleSeed C (Fin n → Bool) ((Fin n → Bool) × (Fin n → Bool)))
+    (u : Fin C.size) (q : ℕ)
+    (bit : (i : Fin C.gates.length) → (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool) → Bool)
+    (hargs : ∀ i : Fin C.gates.length, C.gates[i.val].args.Nodup)
+    (hfan : ∀ i : Fin C.gates.length, C.gates[i.val].args.length ≤ 2)
+    (hcomplete : ∀ i a, a ∈ rows i)
+    (hcap : (GateOccurrences.occurrences C rows s.2.2 u.val).length ≤ q + 1)
+    (hrows : ∀ i, rows i = (Finset.univ : Finset (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool)).toList)
+    (hlabel : ∀ i a, (m + i.val, bit i a) ≠ (u.val, true))
+    (k : Fin n → Bool) (rs : Fin (q + 1) → Fin n → Bool) :
+    SliceReconstruction.tables E₂ (pairEncode
+      (sliceMultiEncDist G n q (SelectedPlan.slotOffset C rows s u q)
+        (SelectedPlan.slotLength C rows s u q)
+        (SelectedPlan.slotMessage C rows s u q E₁
+          (fun i a => keyAt C s (m + i.val) (bit i a))) (k, rs))
+      (BinArith.encWords ((SelectedPlan.tables C rows s u E₁ E₂
+        (fun i a => keyAt C s (m + i.val) (bit i a))).map BinArith.encWords))) =
+      BinArith.encWords (List.ofFn fun i => BinArith.encWords
+        (GateHybrids.tableWith (nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ n) C
+          (SelectedSeed.fill C rows s u q k rs) bit i)) := by
+  let o : (i : Fin C.gates.length) → (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool) → (Fin n → Bool) :=
+    fun i a => keyAt C s (m + i.val) (bit i a)
+  have ht := tables_reconstruct G G₁ G₂ E₁ E₂ hG hGE₁ hGE₂ C rows s u q o
+    hargs hfan hcomplete hcap k rs
+  refine ht.trans ?_
+  apply congrArg BinArith.encWords
+  apply congrArg List.ofFn
+  funext i
+  rw [GateHybrids.tableWith_eq, ← hrows i]
+  apply congrArg BinArith.encWords
+  apply List.map_congr_left
+  intro a _
+  rw [GateHybrids.rowWith_eq, SelectedSeed.fill_perm, SelectedSeed.fill_keyAt,
+    if_neg (hlabel i (s.2.2 i a))]
+
+include hG hGE₁ hGE₂ in
+/-- The total reconstruction machine returns the encoded view/output endpoint with the
+filled seed's generic hybrid tables and the supplied fixed surrounding fields.
+[PS10, Theorem 163.7]
+`SliceReconstruction.rebuild E₂ (pairEncode N (pairEncode (sliceMultiEncDist G n q
+(SelectedPlan.slotOffset C rows s u q) (SelectedPlan.slotLength C rows s u q)
+(SelectedPlan.slotMessage C rows s u q E₁ (fun i a => keyAt C s (m + i.val) (bit i a))) (k, rs))
+(SliceReconstruction.advice (BinArith.encWords ((SelectedPlan.tables C rows s u E₁ E₂ (fun i a =>
+keyAt C s (m + i.val) (bit i a))).map BinArith.encWords)) tail privateInput aliceOutput))) =
+pairEncode (⟨N, privateInput, [], [pairEncode (BinArith.encWords (List.ofFn fun i =>
+BinArith.encWords (GateHybrids.tableWith (nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ n) C (SelectedSeed.fill C rows s u q k
+rs) bit i))) tail]⟩ : View).encode aliceOutput`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** The reconstruction layout reader retains the supplied common input,
+private input, message tail and Alice output. Substitute the exact shared hybrid-table
+reconstruction identity into its sole received message. This is a pointwise sampler identity,
+with no distributional or computational hypothesis left for the table reconstruction. -/
+theorem view_reconstruct {m n : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit m)
+    (rows : (i : Fin C.gates.length) → List (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool))
+    (s : GarbleSeed C (Fin n → Bool) ((Fin n → Bool) × (Fin n → Bool)))
+    (u : Fin C.size) (q : ℕ)
+    (bit : (i : Fin C.gates.length) → (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool) → Bool)
+    (hargs : ∀ i : Fin C.gates.length, C.gates[i.val].args.Nodup)
+    (hfan : ∀ i : Fin C.gates.length, C.gates[i.val].args.length ≤ 2)
+    (hcomplete : ∀ i a, a ∈ rows i)
+    (hcap : (GateOccurrences.occurrences C rows s.2.2 u.val).length ≤ q + 1)
+    (hrows : ∀ i, rows i = (Finset.univ : Finset (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool)).toList)
+    (hlabel : ∀ i a, (m + i.val, bit i a) ≠ (u.val, true))
+    (N tail privateInput aliceOutput : List Bool)
+    (k : Fin n → Bool) (rs : Fin (q + 1) → Fin n → Bool) :
+    SliceReconstruction.rebuild E₂ (pairEncode N (pairEncode
+      (sliceMultiEncDist G n q (SelectedPlan.slotOffset C rows s u q)
+        (SelectedPlan.slotLength C rows s u q)
+        (SelectedPlan.slotMessage C rows s u q E₁
+          (fun i a => keyAt C s (m + i.val) (bit i a))) (k, rs))
+      (SliceReconstruction.advice
+        (BinArith.encWords ((SelectedPlan.tables C rows s u E₁ E₂
+          (fun i a => keyAt C s (m + i.val) (bit i a))).map BinArith.encWords))
+        tail privateInput aliceOutput))) =
+      pairEncode (⟨N, privateInput, [], [pairEncode
+        (BinArith.encWords (List.ofFn fun i => BinArith.encWords
+          (GateHybrids.tableWith (nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ n) C
+            (SelectedSeed.fill C rows s u q k rs) bit i))) tail]⟩ : View).encode aliceOutput := by
+  rw [SliceReconstruction.rebuild_layout]
+  exact congrArg (fun tableCode =>
+    pairEncode (⟨N, privateInput, [], [pairEncode tableCode tail]⟩ : View).encode aliceOutput)
+    (tablesWith_reconstruct G G₁ G₂ E₁ E₂ hG hGE₁ hGE₂ C rows s u q bit
+      hargs hfan hcomplete hcap hrows hlabel k rs)
+
+include hG hGE₁ hGE₂ in
+/-- The padded slice experiment reconstructs the actual shared hybrid view sampler under
+the filled seed, with empty Bob coins and the retained Alice output. All fixed fields are
+computed from the original seed. [PS10, Theorem 163.7]
+`SliceReconstruction.rebuild E₂ (pairEncode N (pairEncode (sliceMultiEncDist G n q
+(SelectedPlan.slotOffset C rows s u q) (SelectedPlan.slotLength C rows s u q)
+(SelectedPlan.slotMessage C rows s u q E₁ (fun i a => keyAt C s (m + i.val) (GateHybrids.stageBit C
+v stage i a))) (k, rs)) (SelectedPlan.advice C rows s u E₁ E₂ (fun i a => keyAt C s (m + i.val)
+(GateHybrids.stageBit C v stage i a)) (pairSndD (GateHybridMessage.message (nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ n) C
+s inA outLen output v stage)) (pairEncode bobInput (List.ofFn (FakeObjects.activeAnswers C s inA
+inB))) aliceOutput))) = GateHybridMessage.sample (nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ n) C (SelectedSeed.fill C rows
+s u q k rs) inA inB outLen output v N bobInput [] aliceOutput stage`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** Reconstruct the filled-seed tables using the stage-bit plaintext family.
+False active answers are unchanged by selected fill. Output vertices differ from the selected
+wire, so both entries of every decoding pair, and hence the complete message tail, remain
+unchanged. The public message and sample readers assemble exactly the reconstructed view. -/
+theorem sample_reconstruct {m n : ℕ} (C : DAGCircuit m)
+    (rows : (i : Fin C.gates.length) → List (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool))
+    (s : GarbleSeed C (Fin n → Bool) ((Fin n → Bool) × (Fin n → Bool)))
+    (u : Fin C.size) (q : ℕ) (inA inB outLen : ℕ) (output : Fin outLen → ℕ)
+    (v : ℕ → Bool) (stage : ℕ) (N bobInput aliceOutput : List Bool)
+    (hargs : ∀ i : Fin C.gates.length, C.gates[i.val].args.Nodup)
+    (hfan : ∀ i : Fin C.gates.length, C.gates[i.val].args.length ≤ 2)
+    (hcomplete : ∀ i a, a ∈ rows i)
+    (hcap : (GateOccurrences.occurrences C rows s.2.2 u.val).length ≤ q + 1)
+    (hrows : ∀ i, rows i = (Finset.univ : Finset (Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool)).toList)
+    (hlabel : ∀ (i : Fin C.gates.length) (a : Fin C.gates[i.val].args.length → Bool),
+      (m + i.val, GateHybrids.stageBit C v stage i a) ≠ (u.val, true))
+    (hOut : ∀ j, output j ≠ u.val)
+    (k : Fin n → Bool) (rs : Fin (q + 1) → Fin n → Bool) :
+    SliceReconstruction.rebuild E₂ (pairEncode N (pairEncode
+      (sliceMultiEncDist G n q (SelectedPlan.slotOffset C rows s u q)
+        (SelectedPlan.slotLength C rows s u q)
+        (SelectedPlan.slotMessage C rows s u q E₁ (fun i a =>
+          keyAt C s (m + i.val) (GateHybrids.stageBit C v stage i a))) (k, rs))
+      (SelectedPlan.advice C rows s u E₁ E₂ (fun i a =>
+        keyAt C s (m + i.val) (GateHybrids.stageBit C v stage i a))
+        (pairSndD (GateHybridMessage.message (nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ n) C s
+          inA outLen output v stage))
+        (pairEncode bobInput (List.ofFn (FakeObjects.activeAnswers C s inA inB))) aliceOutput))) =
+      GateHybridMessage.sample (nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ n) C (SelectedSeed.fill C rows s u q k rs)
+        inA inB outLen output v N bobInput [] aliceOutput stage := by
+  rw [SelectedPlan.advice_eq]
+  refine (view_reconstruct G G₁ G₂ E₁ E₂ hG hGE₁ hGE₂ C rows s u q
+    (GateHybrids.stageBit C v stage) hargs hfan hcomplete hcap hrows hlabel N
+    (pairSndD (GateHybridMessage.message (nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ n) C s
+      inA outLen output v stage))
+    (pairEncode bobInput (List.ofFn (FakeObjects.activeAnswers C s inA inB)))
+    aliceOutput k rs).trans ?_
+  rw [GateHybridMessage.sample_eq, SelectedFields.activeAnswers_fill]
+  rw [← SelectedFields.message_tail_fill (nestedSpecial E₁ E₂ n) C rows s u q k rs
+    inA outLen output v stage hOut]
+  simp only [GateHybridMessage.message_eq, pairSndD_pairEncode]
+
+end Joint
+
+end TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof.SelectedTables

+import TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.Hybrid
+import TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.Yao.HybridProof.StatisticalSwitch

+/-!
+# Combining the two simulation bounds
+
+The simulators are fixed before the distinguisher. Alice has a statistical bound;
+Bob has the same statistical output switch followed by a computational hybrid bound.
+Their sum is one negligible function uniform over the parties' inputs.
+
+## Main definitions
+
+* `strategyTapeEquiv`: normalize a strategy tape through a known length equality.
+
+## Main results
+
+* `simulators_of_output_switch`: combine statistical and computational simulation.
+* `realB_output_switch`: replace Alice's real output outside a bounded bad event.
+* `strategyTapeEquiv`, `acceptProb_firstTape`: normalize the first strategy's tape.
+
+## References
+
+* [PS10] R. Pass, a. shelat, *A Course in Cryptography*, lecture notes, 3rd ed.,
+  January 2010, Theorem 163.7 (the two simulators in the OT-hybrid model).
+-/
+
+namespace TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof
+
+open Complexity Turing TCSlib.Cryptography
+
+/-- For fixed simulators `SA`, `SB`, suppose Alice's advantage is bounded by negligible
+`δ`, Bob's real experiment is `δ`-close to an output-switched experiment, and that experiment
+has a negligible simulation bound for every distinguisher uniformly over the inputs.
+Then the same simulators satisfy both simulation clauses with one negligible bound.
+[PS10, Theorem 163.7]
+`∀ D : NUPPT, ∃ ε : ℕ → ℝ, Negligible ε ∧ ∀ n x y, distAdv (H.realA F n x y) (F.idealA SA n x y)
+(D.distinguisherL n) ≤ ε n ∧ distAdv (H.realB F n x y) (F.idealB SB n x y) (D.distinguisherL n) ≤ ε
+n`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** For each distinguisher take Bob's uniform hybrid bound. It is nonnegative
+at every parameter, since all-false inputs exist and an advantage is nonnegative. Add it
+to the statistical bound. Alice's bound only grows; Bob's bound follows from the triangle
+inequality between the real, switched and ideal acceptance probabilities. The existing
+closure theorem proves the sum negligible. -/
+theorem simulators_of_output_switch {G : Functionality} (H : HybridProtocol G)
+    (F : Functionality) (SA SB : NUPPT)
+    (switched : ∀ n, (Fin (F.inA.eval n) → Bool) → (Fin (F.inB.eval n) → Bool) →
+      H.Coins n → List Bool)
+    (δ : ℕ → ℝ) (hδ : Negligible δ)
+    (hAlice : ∀ (D : NUPPT) n x y,
+      distAdv (H.realA F n x y) (F.idealA SA n x y) (D.distinguisherL n) ≤ δ n)
+    (hSwitch : ∀ n x y, StatClose (H.realB F n x y) (switched n x y) (δ n))
+    (hBob : ∀ D : NUPPT, ∃ μ : ℕ → ℝ, Negligible μ ∧ ∀ n x y,
+      distAdv (switched n x y) (F.idealB SB n x y) (D.distinguisherL n) ≤ μ n) :
+    ∀ D : NUPPT, ∃ ε : ℕ → ℝ, Negligible ε ∧ ∀ n x y,
+      distAdv (H.realA F n x y) (F.idealA SA n x y) (D.distinguisherL n) ≤ ε n ∧
+        distAdv (H.realB F n x y) (F.idealB SB n x y) (D.distinguisherL n) ≤ ε n := by
+  intro D
+  obtain ⟨μ, hμ, hB⟩ := hBob D
+  have hNonneg n : 0 ≤ μ n := by
+    have hb := hB n (fun _ => false) (fun _ => false)
+    rw [distAdv_def] at hb
+    exact (abs_nonneg _).trans hb
+  refine ⟨fun n => δ n + μ n, hδ.add hμ, fun n x y => ⟨?_, ?_⟩⟩
+  · exact (hAlice D n x y).trans (le_add_of_nonneg_right (hNonneg n))
+  · have hs := distAdv_le_of_statClose (H.realB F n x y) (switched n x y)
+      (hSwitch n x y) (D.distinguisherL n)
+    have hb := hB n x y
+    rw [distAdv_def] at hs hb ⊢
+    exact (abs_sub_le _ _ _).trans (add_le_add hs hb)
+
+/-- If Alice's actual output equals her functionality output outside a bad event of
+probability at most `δ`, Bob's real experiment is `δ`-close to the same encoded view paired
+with that functionality output. No change is made to Bob's view or its randomness.
+[PS10, Theorem 163.7, Bob's statistical output switch]
+`StatClose (H.realB F n x y) (fun r => pairEncode (H.main.viewV (List.replicate n true) (H.privA n
+(List.ofFn x) (List.ofFn y) (List.ofFn r.1) (List.ofFn r.2)) (H.privB n (List.ofFn x) (List.ofFn y)
+(List.ofFn r.1) (List.ofFn r.2)) r).encode (List.ofFn (F.f n x y).1)) δ`. -/
+theorem realB_output_switch {G : Functionality} (H : HybridProtocol G)
+    (F : Functionality) (n : ℕ) (x : Fin (F.inA.eval n) → Bool)
+    (y : Fin (F.inB.eval n) → Bool) (Bad : H.Coins n → Prop) (δ : ℝ)
+    (hBad : uprob Bad ≤ δ)
+    (hOut : ∀ r, ¬ Bad r →
+      H.main.outP (List.replicate n true)
+        (H.privA n (List.ofFn x) (List.ofFn y) (List.ofFn r.1) (List.ofFn r.2))
+        (H.privB n (List.ofFn x) (List.ofFn y) (List.ofFn r.1) (List.ofFn r.2)) r =
+          List.ofFn (F.f n x y).1) :
+    StatClose (H.realB F n x y)
+      (fun r => pairEncode
+        (H.main.viewV (List.replicate n true)
+          (H.privA n (List.ofFn x) (List.ofFn y) (List.ofFn r.1) (List.ofFn r.2))
+          (H.privB n (List.ofFn x) (List.ofFn y) (List.ofFn r.1) (List.ofFn r.2)) r).encode
+        (List.ofFn (F.f n x y).1)) δ := by
+  apply StatisticalSwitch.statClose_of_eq_off _ _ Bad δ hBad
+  intro r hr
+  rw [HybridProtocol.realB_eq, hOut r hr]
+
+/-- A strategy tape of known length `m` is equivalent to `Fin m → Bool`.
+The equality is supplied explicitly, so no strategy or coin polynomial is unfolded. -/
+def strategyTapeEquiv (S : Strategy) (n m : ℕ) (h : S.coinLen n = m) :
+    S.Coins n ≃ (Fin m → Bool) :=
+  Equiv.arrowCongr (finCongr h) (Equiv.refl Bool)
+
+/-- `strategyTapeEquiv S n m h` is the function-space equivalence induced by `finCongr h`.
+`strategyTapeEquiv S n m h = Equiv.arrowCongr (finCongr h) (Equiv.refl Bool)`. -/
+theorem strategyTapeEquiv_eq (S : Strategy) (n m : ℕ) (h : S.coinLen n = m) :
+    strategyTapeEquiv S n m h = Equiv.arrowCongr (finCongr h) (Equiv.refl Bool) := rfl
+
+/-- Normalizing a strategy tape preserves its bit word:
+`ofFn (strategyTapeEquiv S n m h r) = ofFn r`. -/
+theorem strategyTapeEquiv_ofFn (S : Strategy) (n m : ℕ) (h : S.coinLen n = m)
+    (r : S.Coins n) : List.ofFn (strategyTapeEquiv S n m h r) = List.ofFn r :=
+  ofFn_arrowCongr_finCongr h r
+
+/-- If a hybrid sample depends only on its first tape through a known-length tape
+equivalence, its acceptance probability is that of the normalized first-tape sampler.
+The unused second tape may have any length. [PS10, Theorem 163.7, sampler transport]
+`acceptProb X D = acceptProb Y D`. -/
+theorem acceptProb_firstTape {G : Functionality} (H : HybridProtocol G) (n m : ℕ)
+    (hLen : H.main.P.coinLen (List.replicate n true).length = m)
+    (X : H.Coins n → List Bool) (Y : (Fin m → Bool) → List Bool)
+    (hX : ∀ r, X r = Y (strategyTapeEquiv H.main.P (List.replicate n true).length m hLen r.1))
+    {ρ : Type*} [Fintype ρ] (D : List Bool → ρ → Bool) :
+    acceptProb X D = acceptProb Y D := by
+  rw [show X = (fun r => Y (strategyTapeEquiv H.main.P
+    (List.replicate n true).length m hLen r.1)) from funext hX]
+  exact (acceptProb_comp_equiv
+    ((strategyTapeEquiv H.main.P (List.replicate n true).length m hLen).prodCongr
+      (Equiv.refl (H.main.V.Coins (List.replicate n true).length)))
+    (fun r => Y r.1) D).trans (acceptProb_fst Y D)
+
+end TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.YaoHybridProof

+/-
+Copyright (c) 2026 TCSlib contributors. All rights reserved.
+Released under Apache 2.0 license as described in the file LICENSE.

+import TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.Yao.HybridProof.Garbling
+import TCSlib.Cryptography.SecureComputation.Yao.HybridProof.FirstTagged
+import TCSlib.Cryptography.ZeroKnowledge.Interaction
+import TCSlib.Cryptography.Asymptotic.BlockEncryptor
+
+set_option maxHeartbeats 0
+set_option relaxedAutoImplicit false
+set_option autoImplicit false
